# MusePi Changelog

MusePi 定制版本的发布说明,供启动时的"新功能"面板(`changelog.startup`)与
`/changelog` 展示。上游 oh-my-pi 的变更记录在 `CHANGELOG.md`(本文件存在时
优先读取本文件)。

## [Unreleased]

- **`@` 文件补全包含 gitignore 文件**：`@` 补全对每个根目录先按 `.gitignore` 过滤、再取一次完整树，只出现在完整树里的路径标记为 `ignored` 并排在后面；候选按精确、前缀、路径前缀、子串排序，同质量时未忽略文件优先，文件优先于目录。
  - EN: `@` file completion now includes gitignored files. Each root is scanned twice — once honoring `.gitignore` and once unfiltered — and paths that only the unfiltered scan returns are marked `ignored` and appended. Candidates rank by exact, prefix, path-prefix and substring match; at equal quality non-ignored files come before ignored ones, and files before directories.

- **外观设置新增对话字体与终端字体**：对话正文改用 `--font-transcript`，内置终端改用 `--font-terminal`，两者分别可选并持久化；默认仍继承界面字体与代码字体，启动时随其他外观偏好一起重放。
  - EN: Appearance settings gain a conversation font and a terminal font. Transcript body text uses `--font-transcript` and the integrated terminal uses `--font-terminal`; both are independently selectable and persisted, defaulting to the UI and code faces and replayed with the other appearance preferences at startup.

- **内置浏览器新标签默认静音**：新建与恢复的标签默认静音，并在访客 `dom-ready` 时应用该状态；仍可按标签单独取消静音。
  - EN: New built-in browser tabs start muted. New and restored tabs carry the mute state and apply it when the guest becomes ready; per-tab unmute still works.

- **Google Generative AI 自定义端点自动补齐 API 版本**：自定义 `base_url` 缺少版本段时补 `/v1beta`，已带版本段或尾部斜杠时归一化为单一段，避免 `/v1beta/v1beta` 或缺少版本的请求路径。
  - EN: Custom Google Generative AI endpoints get their API version filled in. A `base_url` without a version segment gains `/v1beta`, while an existing version segment or trailing slash is normalized to a single segment, avoiding `/v1beta/v1beta` or version-less request paths.

- **通知与音效重做**：通知事件改为一行同时管理桌面通知与提示音，音色显示可读名称并支持「无」；新增「通知提示音」开关，系统通知音与应用内音效二选一，修复完成、错误、提问、子任务可能同时响两次的问题。通知事件同时镜像到右下角应用内 toast（可关闭），聚焦时系统通知被抑制仍可见。`tool` 与 `switch` 默认静音，提问、审批、子任务改用独立音色；移除未接线音效墙与「试听全部」；测试通知按钮同时触发系统通知与应用内 toast。
  - EN: Notifications and sound were reworked. Each notification event now has one row for its desktop toast and alert sound, sounds show readable names with an explicit “None”, and a new notification-sound switch chooses between the OS sound and the in-app cue so completion, error, question and subtask can no longer sound twice. Each event also mirrors into the bottom-right in-app toast stack (toggleable), so it stays visible while focused even when the system notification is suppressed. `tool` and `switch` start silent, question/approval/subtask get distinct sounds, and the unwired-sound wall and “audition all” control are gone; the test button exercises both the system notification and the in-app toast.

- **聚焦时的通知行为更明确**：窗口聚焦时仍显示应用内 toast；「聚焦时也发送系统通知」只控制系统通知，不会关闭应用内提示。
  - EN: Focused-window behavior is explicit: the in-app toast still appears while the window is focused, and “System notifications while focused” only controls the OS notification, never the in-app toast.

- **未聚焦时通知 toast 保留到回窗口**：未聚焦期间产生的通知 toast 不再 6 秒消失，而是保留到窗口重新聚焦，再进入短暂自动关闭；聚焦时仍按原 TTL。
  - EN: Unfocused notification toasts no longer expire after six seconds. They stay until the window regains focus and then auto-dismiss shortly after, while focused toasts keep the normal lifetime.

- **修复音效默认音量被当成 0**：从未设置过音量时现在按 100% 处理，开启音效或通知提示音即可听到；显式拖到 0 仍保持静音。
  - EN: The default sound volume is no longer treated as 0. An unset volume now means 100%, so enabling sound effects or notification sounds is audible; an explicit 0 still stays muted.

- **终端标签拖拽排序与右侧停靠**：终端标签支持拖拽与 Alt+左右方向键重排，顺序按项目持久化；外观设置新增「终端位置」（底部/右侧），右侧停靠不重建终端，宽度可用左缘拖拽调整。
  - EN: Terminal tabs support drag and Alt+Left/Right reordering, persisted per project. Appearance gains a terminal position setting (bottom/right); the right-side dock keeps existing terminals alive and its width can be dragged from the left edge.

- **长文本粘贴自动折叠为可编辑芯片**：超过 100 行或 4000 字符的粘贴现在折叠为「粘贴的文本 · N 行 / M 字符」附件芯片，点击查看并可编辑；编辑后重新上传。
  - EN: Long pasted text now collapses into an editable attachment chip showing line and character counts. Click to view and edit; edits are re-uploaded on send.

- **截图编辑器重做为标注画板**：两个输入框的系统截图现在以全屏浮层打开——顶部显示原图尺寸、预览缩放、HEX/RGBA 与全选原图，截图独占舞台，底部一颗悬浮玻璃工具栏提供选区、画笔、箭头、矩形、椭圆、文字、马赛克、橡皮与取色，并带撤销/重做/清空和随工具出现的调色板与粗细滑杆。放大镜跟随光标显示像素网格、坐标与颜色，取色工具点击即按当前格式复制。裁剪会裁掉框外标注，导出始终保持原图像素；笔画尺寸按「屏幕像素 ÷ 预览缩放」存储，导出效果与预览一致。
  - EN: The screenshot editor is now a full-bleed annotation board. The header shows the original pixel size, preview zoom, HEX/RGBA and select-all; the shot owns the stage; one floating glass pill carries crop, pen, arrow, rectangle, ellipse, text, mosaic, eraser and eyedropper plus undo/redo/clear and a contextual palette with a stroke slider. The loupe follows the pointer with the pixel grid, coordinates and colour, and the eyedropper copies in the current format on click. Cropping clips annotations outside the frame, exports always stay at original resolution, and brush size is stored as screen pixels ÷ preview scale so the export matches the preview.

- **更换项目主目录会同步迁移会话**：`workspace.setPrimary` 现在把旧主目录下的会话文件与产物迁移到新主目录，并重载 live 会话配置；桌面端同步刷新项目列表与会话树。
  - EN: Changing the project primary directory now migrates session files and artifacts to the new root and reloads live sessions; the desktop refreshes the project list and session tree.

- **实验性 Aora 头像 provider**：新增 `AoraAvatar`，作为会话头像的角色渲染器；它把同一份 `AgentSignals` 经 `aoraStateFromSignals(signals, "avatar")` 投影成 44 态中适合头像槽的紧凑子集，内置头像预置保持不变。`@aora-bot/react`（含 `@aora-bot/engine`）随桌面端本地打包，运行时不请求 CDN 或头像 API，离线可用；上游署名见 `packages/desktop-app/README.md`。
  - EN: An experimental Aora avatar provider. `AoraAvatar` is a character renderer for the session avatar: it projects the same `AgentSignals` through `aoraStateFromSignals(signals, "avatar")` into the compact subset of Aora's 44 states that fits the avatar slot, leaving the built-in avatar presets unchanged. `@aora-bot/react` (with `@aora-bot/engine`) is bundled locally with the desktop app, so nothing is requested from a CDN or avatar API at runtime and the avatar works offline; the upstream credit is in `packages/desktop-app/README.md`.

- **搜索 provider 可注册，并接入 DeepSeek 搜索**：新增 `web/search/registry.ts`，内置 18 个 provider 与插件注册共用同一张表；扩展通过 `ctx.registerSearchProvider` / `unregisterSearchProvider` 贡献 provider，随扩展实例卸载、热重载与会话关闭释放。候选链为「显式 `provider:` → 用户 order → 内置顺序 → 注册顺序」，`availability` 只影响自动链。新增 `deepseek` provider（`POST https://api.deepseek.com/v1/tools/search`，`DEEPSEEK_API_KEY` 或已存的 deepseek 凭据）。
  - EN: Web search providers are now registrable, and DeepSeek search is wired in. The new `web/search/registry.ts` is the single table the 18 built-ins and plugin registrations share; extensions contribute providers through `ctx.registerSearchProvider` / `unregisterSearchProvider`, released with the extension instance on unload, hot reload and session shutdown. The chain is explicit `provider:` → user order → built-in order → registration order, and `availability` gates only the automatic chain. A `deepseek` provider was added (`POST https://api.deepseek.com/v1/tools/search`, `DEEPSEEK_API_KEY` or a stored deepseek credential).

- **连接器可以先测试再安装**：连接器设置里「测试连接」调用 `mcp.servers.probe`，真实握手后立即释放，显示服务名与工具数或失败原因；不挂载、不缓存、不改变会话工具集。只给名称时，它会先从用户 `mcp.json`、再从该工作区 live manager 的已发现配置里解析出完整条目，经 `prepareConfig` 注入已存 Token/OAuth 后连接，因此 stdio、Token 与 OAuth 行都能测；拿不到 live manager 时返回明确的不可用原因，不会降级成无凭据直连而报出假失败。
  - EN: Connectors can be tested before install. 测试连接 in connector settings calls `mcp.servers.probe`, which performs a real handshake and releases it immediately, showing the server name and tool count or the failure reason — nothing is mounted, cached, or added to the session. Given only a name, it resolves the full entry from the user `mcp.json` and then the workspace's live manager, injects stored tokens/OAuth through `prepareConfig`, and connects, so stdio, token and OAuth rows are covered too; without a live manager it reports a stated unavailable reason instead of falling back to a credential-less connection that would surface a false failure.

- **改动审阅改用会话级 Git 现场并按文件类型分组**：右侧 Git 面板的改动页改为消费 `git.context`：顶部显示分支（detached 显示短 sha）、worktree 标记、base 与 fork point、ahead/behind，文件按 `code/test/docs/config/style/other` 分组并显示每组文件数与 +/− 合计，组内保持路径排序与原有展开语言；点文件仍走 `git.diff`。非 git 目录、超时与 `invalid-base` 有结构化错误行与重试，`truncated`/`timedOut` 以徽标提示。
  - EN: Change review now uses the session git context and groups by file type. The Git panel's changes page consumes `git.context`: the header shows the branch (short sha when detached), worktree marker, base and fork point, and ahead/behind, while files are grouped as `code/test/docs/config/style/other` with per-group counts and +/− totals, keeping path order and the existing expansion language; clicking a file still opens `git.diff`. Not-a-repository, timeouts and `invalid-base` render a structured error row with retry, and `truncated`/`timedOut` surface as badges.

- **会话级 Git 现场（审阅面板的第一层）**：新增 `git.context` RPC，一次返回仓库根与 worktree 归属、分支或 detached 短 sha、base 分支与 merge-base fork point、ahead/behind、以及按 `code/test/docs/config/style/other` 归类的改动文件（含 numstat、未跟踪文件、上限与超时标记）。大仓安全：fork point 只用 merge-base，numstat 按路径限定，不做全历史 diff。
  - EN: A session-level git context, the first layer under a review surface. The new `git.context` RPC returns the repository root and worktree ownership, branch or detached short sha, base branch with its merge-base fork point, ahead/behind, and changed files grouped as `code/test/docs/config/style/other` with numstat, untracked files, and truncation/timeout markers. Large-repository safety: the fork point uses only merge-base and numstat is path-limited, never a full-history diff.

- **连接器可以先试连再安装**：新增 `mcp.servers.probe`（`probeServer`）。它用与真实连接相同的 initialize 握手，按服务器声明读取 tools / resources / prompts，然后立即释放传输层——不进任何会话、不缓存、不触发工具刷新。失败以 `ok:false` + 原因返回而不是抛异常；非法配置（同时给 `command` 与 `url`）在任何进程启动前就被拒绝。`add` 与 `probe` 共用同一份输入校验，不存在"探测放行、安装拒绝"的分叉。
  - EN: Connectors can be tested before they are installed. The new `mcp.servers.probe` (`probeServer`) performs the same initialize handshake as a real connection, reads the tools / resources / prompts the server declares, then releases the transport immediately — it joins no session, caches nothing and triggers no tool refresh. Failures come back as `ok:false` with the reason instead of throwing, and an invalid config (both `command` and `url`) is rejected before any process starts. `add` and `probe` share one input contract, so a probe can never bless a config the install would refuse.

- **更新通道可切换（正式版 / 测试版）**：设置 → 交互 → Startup & Updates 里的「更新通道」不再是隐藏项，桌面端会真的跟随它。稳定版构建可以切到测试版通道接收 `channel-beta` 的预发布；测试版构建始终留在测试版——它的版本已经高于稳定线，跟稳定通道只会得到「无更新」。桌面 updater 在运行时重设 feed（两条通道都有显式 feed，切回来才对称），把选择落到 `<userData>/update-channel.json` 供下次冷启动的静默检查使用；`updates.check` 与发布说明同样按该通道取清单。
  - EN: The update channel is now switchable (stable / beta). Update Channel under Settings → Interaction → Startup & Updates is no longer hidden, and the desktop app follows it. A stable build can opt into the beta channel and receive `channel-beta` prereleases; a beta build always stays on beta, because its version is already newer than the stable line and following stable would only ever report "no update". The desktop updater repoints its feed at runtime (both channels have an explicit feed, so switching back is symmetric) and persists the choice in `<userData>/update-channel.json` for the next cold-start check; `updates.check` and release notes resolve the same channel.

- **后台完成卡改为用户侧锚定并可整卡折叠**：`async-job-result` 卡片与用户气泡同右缘，卡片头（状态、标签、时长、状态文字）是折叠锚点，输出与提示整体收起；卡片内部仍保留输出预览的展开。思考块流式显示「思考中」，结束后显示「思考过程 · 时长」或「思考完成」；思考块、活动/运行汇总与工具卡片统一使用左侧折叠字形，工具卡片的 chevron 从行尾移到行首。
  - EN: Background completion cards now anchor on the user side and fold as a whole. The `async-job-result` card shares the user bubble's right edge; its header (status, label, duration, status text) is the disclosure anchor while output and hints collapse beneath it, with the output preview's own expansion kept inside. Thinking blocks show "Thinking…" while streaming and "Thinking · duration" or "Thinking done" after settling; thinking blocks, activity/run summaries and tool cards now share one leading fold glyph, with the tool-card chevron moved from the trailing edge to the leading one.

- **运行中的后台任务现在有常驻 HUD，并能打开详情标签页**：会话有运行中 job 时，输入框上方显示卡片（命令、实时时长、取消、详情）与「后台 Bash · N」chip；收起只留 chip。桌面端详情打开右侧「后台任务」surface（运行/全部过滤、实时输出尾部、取消），web / Android / 鸿蒙共用同一组件并在原地展开详情 sheet。读数只来自 `session.jobs` / `session.jobOutput` / `session.jobsCancel`，1s 轮询且仅在页面可见时进行，没有运行中的 job 时不渲染也不轮询。
  - EN: Running background jobs now have a persistent HUD and a detail tab. While a job runs, the composer shows a card (command, live duration, cancel, details) and a "Background Bash · N" chip that collapses to the chip alone. On desktop the detail opens the right-panel Background jobs surface (running/all filter, live output tail, cancel); web, Android and HarmonyOS share the same component and expand an inline detail sheet instead. The readings come only from `session.jobs` / `session.jobOutput` / `session.jobsCancel`, polled once a second while the page is visible; with no running job the component renders nothing and stops polling.

- **目录型钩子脚本真正可执行**：新增 `musepi-hooks-scripts` 行包，把 `roots/pre/<tool>` 与 `roots/post/<tool>` 的脚本扫描成共享命令钩子配置（`pre` → PreToolUse、`post` → PostToolUse，文件名为工具名，`*`/`all` 表示全部），与 Claude Code / Codex 方言共用匹配、退出码、上下文与合并语义。此前的 `.claude/hooks` 目录只被发现和列出，不会执行。
  - EN: Directory-shaped hook scripts now actually run. The `musepi-hooks-scripts` row package scans `roots/pre/<tool>` and `roots/post/<tool>` into the shared command-hook config (`pre` → PreToolUse, `post` → PostToolUse, file stem is the tool name, `*`/`all` means every tool) and shares matching, exit-code, context and merge semantics with the Claude Code and Codex dialects. Previously `.claude/hooks` directories were discovered and listed but never executed.

- **命令钩子补齐阻断契约，并按方言拆成插件**：`before_agent_start` 新增 `cancel/reason`，被拒绝的提示词在发出与落库之前停止，文本交还输入框；`tool_result` 新增 `block/reason`，被拒绝的结果改写为带原因的错误而不是成功输出。共享引擎位于 `extensibility/hooks/command`，方言行包 `musepi-hooks-claude-code` / `musepi-hooks-codex` 各自读取 `configPath`，映射 SessionStart / UserPromptSubmit / PreToolUse / PostToolUse / Stop 五个钩子点。`ask` 暂降级为允许并告警，`SubagentStart/Stop` 标记为 skipped。
  - EN: Command hooks gained their blocking contracts and were split into dialect plugins. `before_agent_start` now accepts `cancel`/`reason`, so a refused prompt stops before it is sent or persisted and the text returns to the composer; `tool_result` accepts `block`/`reason`, turning a rejected result into an error carrying the reason instead of a success. The shared engine lives in `extensibility/hooks/command`, with `musepi-hooks-claude-code` and `musepi-hooks-codex` as dialect row packages that each read a `configPath` and map SessionStart / UserPromptSubmit / PreToolUse / PostToolUse / Stop. `ask` degrades to allow with a warning, and `SubagentStart/Stop` are reported as skipped.

- **预设可以自带 MCP 服务器与文件技能**：新增两个 `presetOnly` 行包。`musepi-mcp-client` 每行声明一台 MCP 服务器（stdio 或 HTTP/SSE），在会话的 `session_start` 里注册到该会话并在会话结束时释放；`musepi-skill-filesystem` 把行配置里的 `roots` 下的 `SKILL.md` / 扁平 `.md` 技能注册进本会话。两者都不做全局环境装载，切换预设即随 generation 回收，对齐 dsh 的 `mcp-client` 行与 `skill-filesystem` 插件模型。
  - EN: Presets can now carry their own MCP servers and file skills. Two `presetOnly` row packages: `musepi-mcp-client` declares one MCP server per row (stdio or HTTP/SSE) and registers it on the session during `session_start`, releasing it when the session ends; `musepi-skill-filesystem` registers the `SKILL.md` bundles and flat `.md` skills under its `roots` row config into the session. Neither loads ambiently and neither has a global config surface — switching presets reclaims both with the preset generation, matching dsh's `mcp-client` row and `skill-filesystem` provider model.

- **新增「进展更新」：agent 工作中可以在对话里发布阶段进展**：默认开启。新建会话会带上 `notify_user` 工具，模型可在多步任务中发布标题 + 正文的阶段更新；更新是独立的卡片，不随回合折叠消失，也不会被「读取了 N 个文件」的工具汇总吞掉。设置 → 外观 → 显示里的「进展更新」可关闭；开关在会话创建时生效，只影响之后新建的会话。
  - EN: Agents can now post progress updates in the conversation, on by default. New sessions carry a `notify_user` tool for headline-plus-body updates during multi-step work; the update is its own card that survives the turn fold and is never swallowed by a tool-run summary. 进展更新 under Appearance → Display turns it off; the switch is read at session creation, so it applies to sessions created after it.

- **空队列不再出现，也不再清不掉**：队列芯片此前按 daemon 的“待处理总数”计数，而这个总数还包括 parked 的顾问卡片与隐藏的下一轮提醒——它们不是行、也不受「清空队列」影响，于是留下一个 1 条却空白的队列。现在计数只来自可操作的行；没有文本也没有图片的排队消息既不显示也不再入队。
  - EN: The queue chip no longer appears with nothing in it, and can always be cleared. It used to show the daemon's pending-work total, which also counted parked advisor cards and hidden next-turn notices — neither is a row nor affected by 清空队列, leaving a one-item chip with an empty panel. The chip now counts only actionable rows, and a queued message with neither text nor images is neither shown nor enqueued.

- **粘贴图片时不再吞掉同时复制的文字**：剪贴板同时带文字和图片时（从 Word、聊天记录或网页复制），图片进入附件芯片的那条分支会 `preventDefault()`，默认的文字插入因此丢失。现在图片照常成为芯片，文字插回光标位置。
  - EN: Pasting an image no longer discards the text copied alongside it. When the clipboard carries both text and an image (from Word, a chat log or a web page), the attachment branch called `preventDefault()` and the browser never inserted the text. The image still becomes a chip and the text is inserted at the caret.

- **引用按钮引用的就是选中的那段文字**：消息行内的引用操作此前总是引用整条消息；现在若用户在该行内选中了一段文字，引用的就是选中的段落，没有选区时才回退到整条消息。
  - EN: The quote action now quotes the passage the user highlighted. Previously it always quoted the whole message; with a non-empty selection inside the row it quotes that selection and only falls back to the whole message when nothing is selected.

- **不再为 1–2px 舍入误差画出多余的滚动轨道**：自绘滚动条的“画轨”判定与“回收”判定改用同一个溢出下限，首见容器绑定前也先过该判定；轨道装饰按轴渲染，只有横向噪声的容器不再在右侧画出整列珠链。项目编辑弹窗的 body 也显式禁止横向滚动。
  - EN: Rounding-only overflow no longer paints a scrollbar. The custom rail's draw and retire paths now share one overflow margin, the first bind checks it before allocating a slot, and decorations render per axis — a horizontally noised container no longer paints a vertical bead column. The project dialog body also stops being a horizontal scroll container.

- **每轮只保留一个 agent 头像，滚动时钉在顶部**：后台任务卡片或工具汇总行夹在两条助手消息之间时，头像不再重新出现。头像归属改为按轮次派生——折叠轮由「活动」头或回复行持有，展开轮由首个渲染 agent 正文的行持有；该行滚出视口后，头像吸附在顶部提示胶囊旁，同为该轮提供归属。工具调用概述行之间的行距与外边距同步收紧。
  - EN: A turn now keeps exactly one agent avatar, pinned to the top while its own row is scrolled away. A background-job card or tool-run summary between two assistant messages no longer redraws it: ownership is derived per turn — the 活动 header or collapsed reply on a folded turn, the first row that renders agent content on an expanded one — and once that row leaves the viewport the avatar docks beside the pinned prompt pill. Spacing between tool-run summary rows is tightened at the same time.

- **聊天设置移除「输出风格」预设**：三套预设只改行距、段落间距与代码块密度，字号始终由「消息字号」决定，保留它等于把同一份排版拆成三个入口。现在固定为一套紧凑节奏，正文、段落、标题与代码块间距直接由 transcript 样式提供。
  - EN: The chat settings no longer offer an output-style preset. The three presets only changed line height, paragraph spacing and code-block density while font sizes always came from the message font size setting, so keeping them split one typography decision across three controls. The transcript now ships one compact rhythm for body, paragraphs, headings and code blocks.

- **会话信息卡连接器空态不再重复 MCP 描述**：空态只保留「连接器」标题与进入 MCP 服务器设置的操作，去掉与之同义的说明行。
  - EN: The session info card's empty connector state no longer repeats an MCP description. It keeps the 连接器 heading and the action that opens the MCP server settings, dropping the line that restated the same thing.

## [0.7.4] - 2026-11-11

- **崩溃或重启后不再丢会话记录**：压缩在日志尾部折叠时会把 journal 清成空文件，重新打开后序号从 1 重来，而重放会丢弃检查点序号及以下的所有记录——整段会话就此消失。现在序号从「文件尾」与「检查点」中较大者继续，且压缩不接受倒退的检查点；断线重连改用真正落盘的尾部判断，补不齐时明确要求整页重载，而不是少推一截。
  - EN: A crash or restart no longer loses session records. Compacting at the journal tail emptied the file, reopening renumbered from 1, and replay discards everything at or below the checkpoint seq — the session simply disappeared. Seqs now continue from the higher of the file tail and the checkpoint, compaction refuses a checkpoint that moves backwards, and catch-up decides from the durable tail so an incomplete range asks for a full resync instead of returning a short one.

- **被打断的工具调用不再被当成没执行**：进程在工具执行中途退出时，恢复后该调用呈现为「结果未知、可能已执行」，模型不会把它当成从未发生而重复同一个写入或命令。只有从未开始的调用（结果落在另一条分支上）才会被移除。
  - EN: A tool call interrupted mid-execution is no longer presented as never having run. Recovery shows it as an unknown outcome that may already have taken effect, so the model does not re-issue the same write or command as if the world were unchanged. Only calls that never started — their result sits on another branch — are still removed.

- **插件热重载失败时不再让宿主插件整体缺席**：挂载中途抛错会先回收这次已经注册的服务与 memory 后端 provider，再回滚旧模块；此前残留的注册会让回滚撞上「already registered」，宿主插件从此缺失且没有重试路径。
  - EN: A failed plugin hot reload no longer leaves the host without its plugins. A mount that throws mid-apply now releases the services and memory backend providers it already registered before the rollback remounts the previous module; the leftover registration used to make that rollback fail with "already registered", leaving the host plugins missing with no retry path.

## [0.7.3] - 2026-11-11

- **桌宠气泡悬停不再跳高、回复框平滑展开**：气泡的快捷动作列改为常驻预留宽度，悬停只改变透明度——此前展开动作行会挤窄正文、把卡片撑高，而窗口尺寸没有跟随，多出的高度被透明窗口硬裁。尺寸测量现在也覆盖"首次活动推送后才出现"的气泡容器。内联回复行改为常驻 DOM + 状态驱动展开（沿用条件区块的 Reveal 规范，带焦点与 reduced-motion 处理），不再整块突现。
  - EN: Pet bubbles no longer jump in height on hover, and the inline reply row expands smoothly. The quick-action column always reserves its width and hover only changes opacity — revealing it used to squeeze the text column, grow the card, and overflow the transparent window whose height never followed. Size measurement now also covers the bubbles container that appears only after the first activity push. The reply row stays mounted and expands from state through the shared Reveal contract (focus and reduced-motion handled), instead of appearing as a sudden block.

- **代码块头部改成语言标签 + 三个图标操作**：语言名人性化（`ts` → TypeScript，未标注 → Plain Text），右上角是显隐行号、自动换行、复制三个图标按钮；悬停或键盘聚焦时浮现，触屏常显。行号按钮按块覆盖外观设置里的「显示行号」，换行只切 `pre-wrap` 不改变几何，复制用 morphicons 在 Copy↔Check 之间形变确认。
  - EN: Code block headers now show a human-readable language label (ts → TypeScript, unlabelled → Plain Text) with line-numbers, wrap and copy icon buttons at the end. They appear on hover or keyboard focus and stay visible on touch; the line-numbers button overrides the global appearance switch for that block, the wrap button flips pre-wrap without changing the block's geometry, and copy morphs Copy↔Check through morphicons.
- **宿主级内置插件迁到 cordis 进程运行时，每个进程只执行一次入口并可原地热重载**：`musepi-hmr` 与 `musepi-memory-sharpshooter` 不再由会话装载路径执行，改由新增的进程级宿主运行时经 Track A 的 `mountPlugin` 挂载一次；`provide` 的服务对象发布到宿主服务表，卸载时 fiber、监听器与 memory backend provider 一并回收。宿主包不再进入 `extension-modules` 能力的会话发现面，扩展中心改由独立的 `host-plugins` 能力列出，因此第二个会话不再触发 “memory backend provider already registered”，`202a5cdb6` 的 `hostScope` 跳过标记已删除。宿主包自己的入口/包目录内依赖/manifest 变化现在按 HMR 批次原地替换（失败回滚旧模块）；包目录外的宿主模块与 `hmr` 提供者本身无法原地替换，变更经 `ctx.hmr.requestRestart` 把重启请求交给宿主。扩展中心同时显示宿主插件的 fiber 状态与效果数，`host-plugins` 作为内部能力不再出现在能力列表 UI。
  - EN: Host-level built-in plugins now run on the cordis process runtime, executing each entry once per process with in-place hot reload. `musepi-hmr` and `musepi-memory-sharpshooter` are mounted by a new process-level host runtime through Track A's `mountPlugin` instead of the session load path; `provide` objects are published to the host service registry, and unload reclaims the fiber, listeners and memory backend provider together. Host packages no longer appear in the `extension-modules` capability that sessions discover from — the extension center lists them through a separate `host-plugins` capability — so a second session no longer raises “memory backend provider already registered”, and the `hostScope` skip marker from `202a5cdb6` is gone. A host package's own entry, package-local dependencies and manifest now reload in place from an HMR batch (the previous module is restored if the replacement fails); host modules outside the package directory and the `hmr` provider itself cannot be replaced in place, so their changes hand a restart request to the host through `ctx.hmr.requestRestart`. The extension center also shows host fiber state and effect counts, and `host-plugins` is an internal capability that no longer appears in the capability list UI.

- **桌宠气泡的快捷按钮不再越出窗口**：卡片收起时动作行是零宽裁剪盒，里面的 24px 按钮仍参与排版，而居中把这 80px 溢出平摊到两侧，审批气泡的按钮因此排到 279–359，越过卡片右边界 332 和 344px 窗口。收起态改为贴 inline end，隐藏按钮落在卡片内；展开时第二轨正好等于行宽，按钮位置与 24px 宽度契约不变。
  - EN: Pet bubble quick actions no longer spill outside the window. Collapsed, the row is a zero-width clip box while its 24px buttons still lay themselves out, and centring that overflow split it on both sides, so approval bubbles placed their buttons at 279–359 — past the card's 332 edge and the 344px window. The collapsed row now flushes to the inline end, keeping the hidden buttons inside the card; when revealed, the second track equals the row width, so button positions and the 24px width contract are unchanged.

- **会话启动即带上宿主提示词区块，插件注册随卸载回收**：`createAgentSession` 在 `session_start` 之后重建一次基础提示词，内存指令、自动学习与 MCP 路由等宿主区块不再等到下一次提示词变更才出现；这次启动重建不写已应用工具签名，后续工具激活仍会各自重建，fork 的 prompt-cache 亲和性也保留。`agent-preset/selected` 现在连同会话上下文一起派发给 `pi.on` 监听器。动态插件运行时 `dispose()` 先逐个卸载插件再销毁分组，内存后端 provider 这类进程级注册不再泄漏到同一进程的下一个运行时（重启插件不再报 already registered）。
  - EN: A session's prompt now carries the host sections from startup, and plugin registrations are reclaimed on unload. `createAgentSession` rebuilds the base prompt once after `session_start`, so memory instructions, auto-learn guidance and MCP routes no longer wait for the next prompt mutation; that startup rebuild leaves the applied-tool signature unset, so later tool activations still rebuild on their own, and a fork keeps its inherited prompt-cache affinity. `agent-preset/selected` is dispatched to `pi.on` listeners together with the session context. The dynamic plugin runtime's `dispose()` unloads each plugin before tearing down the group, so process-wide registrations such as memory backend providers no longer leak into the next runtime in the same process (a reload no longer fails with "already registered").

- **鸿蒙端会话切换器改为原生系统半模态**：标题按钮在鸿蒙壳里不再打开 Web 玻璃卡片，而是拉起 ArkUI `bindSheet`（系统材质 + `Search` + `List`）。行内文案由页面算好随 JSON 传入，点选只回传意图与 id；会话列表变化时页面用 `setSessions` 刷新。滚动、搜索、行高、点击反馈和材质因此全部来自系统实现，而数据与导航仍归页面。这条边界（系统 chrome 用原生、内容流留 Web、页面拥有状态、原生只拥有呈现与手势）同样适用于 Android 与网页端。
  - EN: The HarmonyOS session switcher is now a native system sheet. The header title opens an ArkUI `bindSheet` with the system material, a native `Search` field and a native `List` instead of the web glass card. The page sends pre-formatted rows as JSON and receives only an intent plus the chosen id; `setSessions` refreshes the rows when the list changes. Scrolling, search, row metrics, press feedback and material therefore all come from the system, while data and navigation stay with the page. That boundary — native for system chrome, web for the content stream, page owns state, native owns presentation and gestures — applies to Android and the browser as well.

- **鸿蒙壳首屏不再空白，半模态用上原生沉浸光感**：ArkWeb 的异步渲染（surface 独立送显）叠加 Web 组件的 `expandSafeArea` 时整页不送显——DOM 已渲染、CDP 能截到页面，窗口里只剩背景色；切到原生底部页签后异步面还会再丢一次。壳改为固定同步渲染并去掉 Web 上的 `expandSafeArea`，系统条区域由主题色窗口背景承担。连接用的原生半模态改用 `SheetOptions.systemMaterial`（API 26+，`THICK` + 透明背板），低版本回落实色卡片；表单输入框支持软键盘「完成」直接提交，不再要求先收键盘才能点到按钮。debug 构建开放 WebView 调试通道（release 不开）。
  - EN: The HarmonyOS shell no longer opens to a blank screen. An async-rendered ArkWeb surface (independently presented) combined with `expandSafeArea` on the `Web` component presents nothing — the DOM is live and CDP screenshots it while the window shows only its background — and the surface drops again after the native bottom tabs switch. The shell now pins `SYNC_RENDER` and drops `expandSafeArea` on the `Web`, leaving the system strips to the theme-coloured window background. The connect sheet takes the native immersive material (`SheetOptions.systemMaterial`, API 26+, `THICK` over a transparent backing, opaque fallback below that), and its fields submit from the keyboard's Done action so the action button never hides behind the IME. Debug builds expose the WebView to DevTools; release builds do not.

- **键盘弹起时输入框不再与键盘之间留一条死区**：两个原生壳此前都为底部页签预留整条 `--sh-tabbar-space`，但键盘弹起时页签在 IME 后面，这段预留变成输入卡片与键盘之间的空白。键盘 inset 现在由一个共享写入点 `setKeyboardInset()` 同时切换 `--mp-keyboard-inset` 和 `sh-ime-open`，该状态把 `.sh-app`／`.sh-connect`／`.sh-composer` 的底距收敛到键盘本身（safe-bottom 随之失效，那部分屏幕已被键盘占据）。消息操作按钮补齐 44px 最小宽度，会话列表里缺失时间戳的会话不再显示「NaN 天前」。
  - EN: The composer no longer leaves a dead strip between itself and the keyboard. Both native shells reserved `--sh-tabbar-space` for the bottom tabs, which sit behind the IME when the keyboard is up, so the reserve turned into a gap. A shared `setKeyboardInset()` now writes the inset and toggles `sh-ime-open`, which collapses the bottom padding of `.sh-app`, `.sh-connect` and `.sh-composer` to the keyboard itself (the gesture inset goes with it — that screen area belongs to the keyboard). Message action buttons gained the 44px minimum width, and a session without a timestamp no longer renders "NaN 天前".

- **会话列表可把任意会话打开到右侧面板**：右键「在侧边打开」把目标会话作为 `session` 标签页放进右侧面板，与主聊天并排。同一会话只保留一个标签，当前主会话不重复打开；侧边会话拥有自己的实时 transcript、输入框、审批卡和提问卡，可继续操作，不是只读预览。打开导致面板收起时会自动展开，标签内可「在主区域打开」。顾问 transcript 沿用只读说明并显示「顾问会话」标题；普通会话的输入区复用主 Composer，样式与主聊天一致。窄视图下统计条改为可换行、思考预览收口不再撑出横向滚动，输入区按钮在窄容器里收紧；侧边会话使用与主聊天相同的上下渐进模糊边缘。
  - EN: Any session can now open beside the active chat from the session list's "Open to the Side" action. The right panel keeps one `session` tab per target, never duplicates the active main chat, and expands when it was collapsed. The side session keeps its own live transcript, composer, approval cards and ask cards, so it remains steerable instead of becoming a read-only preview; "Open in main area" moves the same session to the center. Advisor transcripts keep the read-only note and the "Advisor session" title, while an ordinary session reuses the main Composer so its input matches the center chat. On narrow widths the stats row wraps, the thinking preview no longer creates horizontal scroll, and the composer toolbar tightens; the side transcript uses the same progressive top/bottom edge blur as the main chat.

- **新功能日志只显示当前语言，列表边缘柔和淡出**：`- EN:` 子行不再出现在中文界面的「新功能」完整日志里；英文界面会把 EN 正文提升到同一加粗标题下（没有 EN 的旧条目保留中文）。亮点卡与完整日志共用同一份本地化投影，日志体改用共享羽化容器，上下边缘不再硬切。
  - EN: The what's-new full log now shows only the active language: a Chinese UI drops every indented `- EN:` child, while an English UI promotes the EN body under the same bold title (older entries without an EN child keep their Chinese line). Highlight cards and the full log share one localized projection, and the log body renders through the shared feather container so its top/bottom edges fade instead of cutting.

- **会话头部改成沉浸式浮层、能力中心搜索与行高亮更自然**：Chat|地图 切换、消息树与会话信息按钮不再占据会话上方的一行，而是绝对定位浮在记录之上——行本身不拦截指针，按钮照常可点，下方消息仍可选中；记录用滚动内边距给它们留出初始位置，滚动后内容从浮层下方穿过。顶部/底部渐进模糊的遮罩移到带子整体（加在模糊图层上会让 Chromium 跳过采样、只剩一块不透明暗色），顶部带加高到 56px、tint 调轻。能力中心工具行去掉不透明底色，改为透明吸顶 + 背景模糊，列表滚过时在搜索框背后虚化；插件列表的悬停改成内缩的圆角高亮，不再是一条通栏矩形。
  - EN: The Chat|Map switch, message tree and session-info buttons no longer own a flow row above the conversation — they are absolutely positioned over the transcript, with the row itself hit-transparent (the controls stay clickable and messages underneath remain selectable). The transcript reserves their footprint with scroll-content padding, so later rows pass under the floating controls. The transcript's top/end progressive blur moved the ramp mask onto the band (masking the blur layers made Chromium skip the sampling and paint an opaque slab); the top band is 56px with a lighter tint. The capability center's tool row drops its opaque fill for a transparent sticky bar with a backdrop blur, so the list blurs behind the search field, and plugin rows use an inset rounded hover instead of a full-width rectangle.

- **滚动边缘不再出现割裂的模糊矩形**：设置页、设置导航、右栏与轨迹等通用滚动区恢复柔和的羽化——遮罩在整个滚动口宽度上淡出内容，和侧边空白之间保持连续；会话列表与能力中心这类有外框的场景改用「外置带子 + 整体遮罩」的渐进模糊，遮罩加在带子而不是模糊图层上（后者会让 Chromium 跳过背景采样、只剩表面 tint），也不再使用会被内边距顶下去的 sticky 带。
  - EN: Scroll edges no longer show a detached blur rectangle. General scrollers — settings content, settings nav, the right pane and the trajectory list — return to the soft mask feather, which fades the content across the full scrollport and stays continuous against the blank sidebar. Framed surfaces (session list, capability center) use an out-of-flow band with the ramp mask on the band itself rather than on the blur layers: masking a `backdrop-filter` element makes Chromium skip the backdrop sampling and paint only the tint, and a sticky band is pushed down by the scroller's own padding.

- **启动：daemon 并行预热、界面交叉淡出**：冷启动的 daemon 生成改到 Electron `app.ready` 时并行进行——`start()` 会接管已存在的监听并合并进行中的启动，不会重启外部 daemon；主界面在连接成功时立即挂载，splash 以 300ms 交叉淡出覆盖其上，不再等 950ms 停留后硬切；强制停留降到 650ms（覆盖 620ms 的 orb 入场）。
  - EN: The daemon now warms in parallel with window creation on cold starts: Electron main calls the deduplicated `start()` at `app.ready`, which adopts a live listener and joins a spawn already in flight without ever restarting a foreign daemon, so the renderer's later `daemon-start` reuses it. The shell mounts as soon as the daemon answers, with the splash cross-fading over it for 300ms instead of the old 950ms hold followed by a hard cut; the minimum splash time is now 650ms, covering the 620ms orb intro.

- **折叠侧栏的悬停预览可点击提交、文件标签页不再多出滚动条**：悬停现在只做预览，点击才提交——在浮出的二级左栏里点击任意一行（任务、能力导航或会话）会先切到该面板所属的目的地并展开侧栏，再让被点的行生效；此前这些页面只在目的地打开时挂载，行点击发出的导航动作没有订阅者、被直接丢弃，所以看起来「点了没反应」。任务中心与能力中心的导航动作会保留最后一次未被消费的动作，供页面挂载时补投。右侧「文件」标签页不再把自带滚动的 `FilePane` 包进羽化滚动容器：`height:100%` 的子节点在带内边距的 `overflow-y:auto` 中溢出，在文件列表自己的滚动条旁又画了一条。
  - EN: Hover now only previews; a click commits. Clicking any row in the floating second-tier pane (task, capability navigation or session) first switches to that panel's destination and reveals the sidebar, then lets the clicked row act. Those pages only mount while their destination is open, so the row's nav action previously reached no subscriber and was dropped — the click appeared dead. Task-center and capability-center actions now retain the last unconsumed action for the page to replay on mount. The right panel's Files tab no longer wraps its self-scrolling `FilePane` in the feather-scroll container: the `height:100%` child overflowed the padded `overflow-y:auto` box and painted a second scrollbar beside the file list's own.

- **移动端会话页按场景重排：顶栏分组、底部输入区与鸿蒙原生栏连续**：展开态阔折叠此前把宽屏网页的顶栏原样搬过来——八个平铺图标，加上模型、思考档位、上下文用量、参与者头像挤在标题旁，标题被裁成「r... ~__ Claude Haiku 4.5 medium 27%」。现在所有移动壳（含 939vp 展开态）都走分组面板菜单，按「会话上下文 / 扩展 / 偏好」用细分隔线分区，顶栏只留标题、连接状态和高频动作；桌面远控网页保留原来的宽屏工具条。鸿蒙侧原来在整个页面上预留 96px 底栏空白，又叠加了一次 `--safe-bottom`，而原生悬浮栏的 26vp 底边距已经避让了手势区，两者重复计数导致输入框卡片与原生栏之间约 47vp 裸背景、输入区像悬在屏幕中间；现在由输入框自己承担整条底部 dock（实色背景延伸到屏幕边、细顶边分区），原生沉浸光感栏浮在其上，避让值只算一次。矮横屏（≤540px 高）里零高度的 sticky 引用胶囊会盖住正文，该形态下不再吸附，引用本身仍留在消息里。
  - EN: Mobile session shells now get a scenario-specific layout instead of the wide desktop web header. An unfolded foldable used to show eight flat icon buttons plus model, thinking level, context gauge and participant avatars crammed beside the title, clipping it to "r... ~__ Claude Haiku 4.5 medium 27%". Every mobile shell — including the 939vp unfolded state — now uses a grouped panel menu split by intent (session context / extensions / preferences) with hairline separators, leaving only the title, connection dot and high-frequency actions in the header; the remote desktop browser keeps its wide toolbar. On HarmonyOS a flat 96px reserve on the whole page plus a second `--safe-bottom` double-counted the gesture inset already handled by the native bar's 26vp bottom margin, leaving ~47vp of bare background between the input card and the bar so the composer looked suspended mid-screen. The composer now owns the whole bottom strip as one solid dock down to the screen edge with a hairline top border, the native immersive bar floats on it, and the reserve is applied once. In short landscape (≤540px height) the zero-height sticky quote reference no longer pins over the message body; the quote itself stays in the transcript.

- **折叠侧栏的悬停预览在任务中心 / 能力中心下也能操作**：左侧栏折叠后，把鼠标移到任务中心或能力中心的 rail 按钮上能弹出二级左栏，但鼠标移过去时面板会立刻消失——这两页的页级界面盖在 keeper 上方，`pointerenter` 收不到，预览被 `pointerleave` 的 140ms 计时器清掉了。现在预览打开期间按指针位置判定：指针仍在 rail + 面板这一列内就保持（并继续 `holdPeek`），移出右侧才关闭。
  - EN: Hover-peek on the collapsed sidebar is now usable for 任务中心 and 能力中心 too. The panel appeared when hovering those rail buttons but vanished as soon as the pointer moved toward it: page-level chrome on those destinations sits above the keeper, so its `pointerenter` never fired and the `pointerleave` timer cleared the peek. While a peek is open, the pointer's position now decides — anywhere in the rail + panel column keeps it, moving out to the right closes it.

- **会话列表的子会话连线、浮层避让输入框与产物空态文案**：子会话的树连线去掉末尾的 L 形折角、改由竖线在行底 12px 淡出，连线上那颗带光晕的樱花粉圆点收敛为 4px 中性小点（悬停变亮、当前行用强调色），不再和行内的项目色块/角标抢视觉。`useFloatingMenu` 现在会把跨到输入框列上的浮层高度压在输入框上沿之上，右侧面板里打开的长菜单不再被输入框卡片压住最后几行。产物面板的空态与入口提示改为用户语言（「设计模式产出的产物会出现在这里」），不再把 `artifact.manifest.json` 这个实现细节写进创造模式的界面。
  - EN: Sub-session threads in the session list drop the hard L foot (the vertical line now fades over its last 12px) and the glowing sakura dot becomes a 4px neutral pip that brightens on hover and takes the accent on the active row, so the guide no longer competes with the row's project colour and badges. `useFloatingMenu` now stops a menu that spans the composer's columns at the composer's top edge, so long menus opened from the right panel are no longer covered by the composer card. The artifacts empty state and entry hint now speak in user terms ("Artifacts created in design mode appear here") instead of naming the `artifact.manifest.json` implementation detail inside creation mode.

- **插件市场：列表图标与详情一致、第三方插件可以正常安装**：市场目录里的插件条目现在会回填插件自身的图标（`.codex-plugin/plugin.json` 的 logo，与详情弹窗同源），不再只有打开详情才显示图标。Windows 上点击安装会稳定失败（`EPERM: rename ... .staging-*`）：daemon 的 HMR watcher 监视整个 `plugins/` 目录，市场缓存的 staging 目录一出现就被 chokidar 加上句柄，而 Windows 无法重命名带句柄的目录。现在 watcher 忽略 `plugins/cache`，缓存落盘的重命名也不再一次性放弃（短暂 EPERM/EBUSY 会重试）。
  - EN: Marketplace cards now show the plugin's own icon (`.codex-plugin/plugin.json` logo — the same file the detail dialog uses) instead of a generic glyph that only turned into artwork inside 详情. Installing a third-party plugin on Windows failed every time with `EPERM: rename … .staging-*`: the daemon's HMR watcher watches the whole `plugins/` tree, chokidar attached a handle to each new cache staging directory, and Windows cannot rename a directory with an open handle. The watcher now ignores `plugins/cache`, and the cache rename retries instead of failing on the first transient `EPERM`/`EBUSY`.

- **滚动边缘羽化改为 progressive blur，并抽成共享原语**：能力中心、侧栏会话列表的上下边缘不再用 mask 渐变把内容“淡掉”，改为三段叠加的 backdrop 模糊带（6 / 14 / 22px，强度朝边缘递增，带低透明度表面 tint）。新原语 `ScrollEdgeBlur` 作为滚动容器里的零高 sticky 子节点渲染，不需要包装容器、不动布局，显隐直接读容器自身的 `data-top-scroll` / `data-bottom-scroll`；覆盖能力中心、侧栏会话列表、设置页导航/正文、以及通用 `FadeScroll` 容器（右栏 tab 体、git/diff/pr 面板、导入向导等）。横向窄条保留 mask 羽化。
  - EN: Scroll edges now dissolve with progressive blur instead of a mask gradient (capability center and the sidebar session list first). The new `ScrollEdgeBlur` primitive renders as a zero-height sticky child of the scroller — no wrapper, no layout change — driven by the scroller's own `data-top-scroll` / `data-bottom-scroll`, and covers the capability center, the sidebar session list, settings nav/content and every generic `FadeScroll` container. Horizontal strips keep mask feathering.

- **悬浮菜单不再因为滚动而消失**：模型选择器、思考档位、`GuiSelect` 等共用 `useFloatingMenu` 的浮层，原先在光标悬停时滚轮会直接关闭菜单（“浮层自己滚不动就关掉、把滚轮转给聊天区”）。现在浮层能滚就在内部滚动；滚到边界或内容本身不满一屏时，把手势转交给锚点所在的滚动容器（聊天区兜底），菜单保持打开并继续跟随锚点重新定位。
  - EN: Floating menus no longer disappear on scroll. The model/thinking selectors, `GuiSelect` and every other `useFloatingMenu` popup used to close as soon as the wheel moved while the pointer rested over them. A popup now scrolls itself when it has room and otherwise forwards the gesture to the anchor's scroll container (falling back to the transcript), staying open and re-positioning with its anchor.

- **滚动进度轨：动效降级、视口漂移、可替换席位与 ui-theme 主题 bundle**：横向/纵向浮动轨现在同时尊重 `prefers-reduced-motion` 与应用的 `gui-motion-off`（橡皮糖不再挤压/回弹，吃豆人停止咀嚼）；面板滑动/高度形变等祖先动画会触发受影响轨重测，文档级滚动也会重测，轨不再停在动画前的坐标；换皮肤或卸载时清除拖拽快照，避免轨被永久钉住；导入皮肤的 `url()`/`image-set()`/`expression()` 颜色会被拒绝；轨池上限从 6 提升到 24 的安全阀并加入悬停发现（只在空槽上绑定，不抢占正在滚动的轨）；RTL 横向原点、键盘意图收敛到最近滚动祖先、gummy/snake/横向胶囊的透明命中区一并补齐。滚动条本身迁入渲染端 Cordis 主题 bundle `packages/desktop-app/src/plugins/ui-theme/`：`rail.tsx`（组件）、`rail.css`、`skins.ts`（内置目录）与 `settings.tsx`（皮肤/导入/常显分区）归同一所有者，注册 `app.overlay` + `settings.appearance.item`，设置回到内置外观页（dsh 同构）；插件也可选择 `settings.item.<id>` 或两处都注册并用 `slot` 分支。宿主保留内置 `<FloatingScrollbar/>` 回退。daemon 侧主题插件仍可经新增的 `registerScrollbarSkin` 贡献皮肤，经 `extensions.list.scrollbarSkins` 下发并按 contributed → imported → built-in 合并。
  - EN: The floating scroll rail now honors both `prefers-reduced-motion` and `gui-motion-off` (the gummy capsule no longer squash-springs and the Pac-man stops chewing), re-measures itself after ancestor transitions/animations and document scrolling instead of keeping pre-motion coordinates, clears a stale drag snapshot when the skin base is replaced or the overlay unmounts, and rejects imported skin colours that can fetch or execute (`url()`, `image-set()`, `expression()`). The rail pool is now a 24-slot safety valve with hover discovery (a free slot only; hover never evicts the rail being scrolled), RTL horizontal origin, keyboard intent scoped to the nearest overflowing ancestor, and transparent-border grab targets. The scrollbar itself moved into the renderer-side Cordis theme bundle `packages/desktop-app/src/plugins/ui-theme/` — `rail.tsx` (component), `rail.css`, `skins.ts` (built-in catalog) and `settings.tsx` (skin/import/always-visible section) now share one owner and register `app.overlay` + `settings.appearance.item`, putting the settings back in the built-in Appearance page (dsh parity); a plugin may also choose `settings.item.<id>` or both and branch on the injected `slot`. The host keeps `<FloatingScrollbar/>` as fallback. Daemon theme plugins can still contribute skins through the new `registerScrollbarSkin` verb, shipped as `extensions.list.scrollbarSkins` and merged contributed → imported → built-in by id.

- **左侧图标栏不再被浮动轨绑定，页面也不再被自身溢出顶起**：图标栏根节点与内部滚动容器重新标记 `data-gui-scrollbar="off"`（设计稿的既有契约此前未落到 DOM），悬浮/滚动不再在左侧生成吃豆人/胶囊轨；图标栏改为裁剪自身溢出，去掉向页面泄漏的 26px 滚动高度——此前聚焦底部设置按钮会把整个界面向上滚 26px，顶部「返回工作区」被裁掉、底部出现空隙。
  - EN: The left icon rail is no longer bound by the floating scroll rail, and its own overflow no longer lifts the page. The rail root and its inner scroller carry `data-gui-scrollbar="off"` again (the design contract was missing from the DOM), so hovering or scrolling the icon column cannot create a Pac-man/capsule rail there; the rail also clips its own overflow, removing the 26px scroll height that leaked into the page. Focusing the bottom settings button used to scroll the whole UI up 26px, cutting off 返回工作区 and leaving a gap at the bottom.

- **会话信息卡的子代理进度分区插件化**：`chat.session-info.agents` 由 Cordis 插件渲染，宿主只透传只读投影（百分比/阻塞/耗时/成本），插件停用或编译失败时回退内置分区；运行中进度封顶 85%，失败/中止保留实际工作量，单阶段 swarm 也绘制分段条。
  - EN: The session info card's subagent section is now a Cordis contribution at `chat.session-info.agents`: the host passes a read-only projection (percent, blocked, elapsed, cost) and falls back to the built-in section when the plugin is disabled or fails to compile. Running progress is capped at 85%, failed/aborted members keep the work they actually did, and a single-phase swarm still draws the segment bar.

- **欢迎页光带改为页面入场触发**：输入框边框光带在欢迎页显示后跑 3 秒即停，聚焦输入框不再重新触发；专注模式仍完全不显示。
  - EN: The welcome composer's border beam now runs once for three seconds when the welcome page mounts instead of restarting on every input focus; focus mode still hides it.

- **目标续跑在守护进程/桌面会话中生效**：`goal.continuationModes` 新增 `daemon`。daemon 现在直接由会话事件驱动续跑，不再依赖 TUI 编辑器的 800ms 空闲窗口；`continuationPending` 会阻止空闲回收在决策中途释放会话，会话切换或重启也不会插入一轮续跑。
  - EN: Goal auto-continuation now runs in daemon/desktop sessions. `goal.continuationModes` gains `daemon`; the daemon drives continuation from session events instead of the TUI editor's 800ms idle window, `continuationPending` keeps idle reclamation from disposing a session mid-decision, and a session switch or restart cannot interleave a continuation turn.

- **插件安装/升级/启停对已打开会话即时生效**：安装、卸载、市场升级与包启停不再只影响下一个会话，daemon 会把该包贡献的入口路径交给每个活跃会话，按已装载状态执行 load / reload / unload；流式会话把操作挂到回合结束后的空闲边界并报 `deferred`，不再中途替换工具面。结果经 `plugins.apply.state` 广播，安装弹窗在能力摘要下显示已生效、回合后生效或失败的会话数；同一入口的连续请求折叠为最后一次，单条失败不影响其它入口与会话。
  - EN: Plugin install, upgrade and enablement now reach sessions that are already open. Install, uninstall, marketplace upgrade and package toggles hand the package's entry paths to every live session, which loads, reloads or unloads each entry according to what it has mounted; a streaming session parks the operations until its next idle boundary and reports `deferred`, so a turn never has its tool surface swapped mid-flight. The outcome is broadcast as `plugins.apply.state`, and the install dialog shows live, after-this-turn, or failed session counts under the capability summary. Repeated requests for one entry collapse to the newest operation, and a single failed entry never blocks the others.

- **单条扩展模块启停与 bundle 组合即时生效**：`extensions.setEnabled` 对 `plugin-module` / `extension-module` 条目同样投递到已打开会话（技能、工具、MCP 行不冒充扩展入口）；bundle 或组合行启停后 daemon 立即重跑宿主级 reconcile——新增行挂载、消失行卸载、源码变了的行重载——不再等下一次运行时读取。
  - EN: Enabling or disabling a single extension module now reaches open sessions too (module entries only; skill/tool/MCP rows are not extension entries). A bundle or composed-row toggle immediately reruns the host-level reconcile — new rows mount, absent rows unload, edited entries reload — instead of waiting for the next runtime read.

- **每轮用量可点开明细**：助手回合末尾的 token 行现在是按钮，浮层显示本轮总量、提供方/模型、缓存命中率、未缓存输入、缓存读取/写入与输出；首轮没有缓存时省略缓存行。wire 的 `AssistantMessage` 增加可选 `provider`，历史条目只显示模型。
  - EN: The per-turn token line is now a button. Its popover shows total tokens, provider/model, cache-hit rate, uncached input, cache read/write, and output; a first turn omits the cache rows. `AssistantMessage` gained an optional `provider`, and historical entries show the model alone.

- **插件页面分工：设置看概览与管理，能力中心只做管理**：设置里的插件页是超集——顶部四张统计卡（会话 / 全局 / 实验性 / 已安装）兼任筛选，预设选择单独一行；下方管理列表是多卡片网格（内置插件配置 / 实验性插件 / 已安装），统一承载开关、配置与详情。能力中心保留同一套管理分组的紧凑列表。「添加插件」菜单新增「让 Agent 创建插件」：把欢迎页预设切到 Creator，不创建会话也不发消息。`enabledInPresets` 继续只是「启用于」事实。
  - EN: The plugin pages now follow dsh's division. Settings is the superset: four stat cards (Session, Global, Experimental, Installed) summarize the inventory and double as scope filters, with the preset selector naming the session plane; the management list below is a multi-card grid (Built-in plugin settings, Experimental plugins, Installed) that owns toggles, configuration, and details. The capability center keeps the same management groups as a compact list. The 添加插件 menu adds a third path — let the Agent create a plugin — which arms the welcome composer with the Creator preset and sends nothing. `enabledInPresets` remains only the "enabled in" fact.

- **退出确认改为现场询问 daemon**：Electron 的 `before-quit` 不再读托盘的 ≤5s 缓存，而是现场调用新增的 `system.quitInspection`——正在生成/压缩/跑工具的会话、排队消息、存活的异步 job、已武装的定时提醒都会计入，并按「活动任务 / 定时任务 / 两者都有」给出对应文案。询问超时按有活动任务处理，没有 daemon 则没有可中断的工作；确认后才执行协作式 `system.shutdown` 与既有的 SIGTERM→SIGKILL 升级。
  - EN: Quit confirmation now inspects the daemon at quit time, matching dsh's `quit-inspection`. Electron's `before-quit` no longer reads the tray's ≤5s cache; it calls the new `system.quitInspection`, which counts generating/compacting/tool-running sessions, queued messages, live background jobs, and armed scheduled reminders, and picks copy for active work, scheduled reminders, or both. A timed-out inspection is treated as active work, while no daemon means nothing to interrupt; only after confirmation does it run the cooperative `system.shutdown` and the existing SIGTERM→SIGKILL escalation.

- **能力中心的搜索、筛选与操作统一为液态玻璃控件**：技能（发现/已安装）、插件、插件市场原先各自一套搜索框与按钮样式（深色输入框、实心金色按钮、不同圆角与高度并存）。现在三者统一为 34px 高、`radius-lg`、凹陷玻璃填充 + hairline + 内 rim 的控件；搜索框统一为「图标 + input」包装，`GuiSelect` 与操作按钮同高同材质，主操作使用强调色玻璃而非实心色块；插件市场分段控件也并入同一语言。三个工具行改为嵌在内容列里的圆角悬浮玻璃卡，复用共享 `.gui-liquid-glass` 表面（半透明填充 + 边缘折射 + hairline/内 rim），吸顶时自然浮在列表上；搜索焦点改回主题强调色；列表上下补上与会话记录同款的三层 progressive blur 边缘带（顶部在内容滚入后出现，底部在滚到底时隐藏）。侧栏“插件”徽标改为该面板可管理条目总数（官方 manage 层 + 插件包 + 未归属模块），不再用市场安装包数量冒充 0。
  - EN: Capability-center search, filters and actions now share one liquid-glass control language. Skills (discover/installed), plugins and marketplace previously had three search fields and three button styles (dark inputs, a solid gold button, mismatched radii and heights). All three now use 34px controls with `radius-lg`, a recessed glass fill, hairline and inset rim; every search field is the same icon+input wrapper, `GuiSelect` and action buttons share the height and material, and the primary action is accent-tinted glass instead of a solid colour block. The marketplace segments join the same language. Each toolbar is now a rounded floating glass card inset in the content column, reusing the shared `.gui-liquid-glass` surface (translucent fill, edge refraction, hairline/inset rim) that sticks over the list; search focus uses the theme accent, and the list gains the transcript's three-layer progressive-blur edge bands (top appears once content scrolls under the row, end hides at the bottom). The sidebar plugin badge now counts every manageable entry in the pane (official manage lane + packages + user-level modules) instead of showing the marketplace package count as 0.

- **凭据库关闭后立即释放数据库文件**：`SqliteAuthCredentialStore.close()` 漏掉 `#setSecretStmt.finalize()`；只要还有未 finalize 的语句，Bun 的 SQLite 就会继续持有 `auth.db` 与 WAL 句柄，Windows 上删除暂存或 agent 目录时会以 `EBUSY` 失败并耗完重试窗口。现在 `close()` 回收全部语句。
  - EN: The credential database now releases its files on close. `SqliteAuthCredentialStore.close()` missed `#setSecretStmt.finalize()`; while any statement stays unfinalized, Bun's SQLite keeps `auth.db` and its WAL handles mapped, so deleting a staged or agent directory on Windows failed with `EBUSY` until the retry window expired. `close()` now reclaims every prepared statement.

- **空闲维护收成一个 bundled 插件，recap 与压缩共用一次回合结算**：`compaction.idleEnabled` 与 `recap.enabled` 不再由 TUI 的 `event-controller` 和 daemon 的 `session-host` 各持一套计时器，改由 `musepi-idle-maintenance` 在 `agent_end` 统一武装，收到新回合、输入、自动压缩、会话切换/分叉/树导航或关闭时一起取消；触发前复查空闲、草稿、`isSettled` 与阈值。recap 通过 `ctx.runEphemeralTurn` 生成，带 goal/todo 锚点，先 `ctx.recordRecap` 落库再由宿主 `ctx.showRecap` 展示；daemon 现在会初始化扩展 actions，因此同一插件也接管 GUI 的 recap 与空闲压缩。扩展运行时新增 `ctx.runEphemeralTurn` / `ctx.getGoalModeState` / `ctx.getTodoPhases` / `ctx.recordRecap` / `ctx.showRecap` / `ctx.isSettled`，继续沿用 `ctx.settings` 与 `ctx.hasDraftInput`。
  - EN: Idle maintenance is one bundled plugin, and recap shares a single settlement with compaction. The timers behind `compaction.idleEnabled` and `recap.enabled` no longer live separately in the TUI `event-controller` and the daemon `session-host`; `musepi-idle-maintenance` arms them together on `agent_end` and disarms them on a new turn, input, host maintenance, session switch/branch/tree navigation, or shutdown, re-checking idleness, draft input, `isSettled`, and the threshold before firing. Recaps are generated through `ctx.runEphemeralTurn` with goal/todo anchors, persisted by `ctx.recordRecap`, and formatted by the host through `ctx.showRecap`; the daemon now initializes extension actions, so the same plugin also drives GUI recaps and idle compaction. The extension runtime gained `ctx.runEphemeralTurn`, `ctx.getGoalModeState`, `ctx.getTodoPhases`, `ctx.recordRecap`, `ctx.showRecap`, and `ctx.isSettled`, alongside the existing `ctx.settings` and `ctx.hasDraftInput`.

- **空态页的快捷提示词与实时提醒不再抖动/误出滚动条**：提示词展开收起改为一次性挂载全部额外 chip（保留逐个交错入场），外层用 `HeightMorph` 只补间一次高度，不再每 90ms 重设运行中的高度过渡；收起先淡出 150ms 再卸载并补间一次。实时提醒列表改为测量真实行高与 padding（ResizeObserver），不再用行数常量估算，完整可见的列表不会因 1–2px 误差出现滚动条；超过 200px 上限后才滚动。
  - EN: Empty-state suggestion chips and reminders no longer jitter or show a spurious scrollbar. Expanding or collapsing the chips mounts every extra chip in one commit (keeping the per-chip stagger) and runs a single height morph through `HeightMorph`; collapse fades the chips for 150ms, then unmounts them and morphs once. The reminders list measures rendered row heights plus padding with a ResizeObserver instead of estimating from a row constant, so a fully visible list no longer overflows by 1–2px and paints a scrollbar; it scrolls only past the 200px cap.

- **内置模型目录刷新**：重新生成各供应商的绑定模型清单（供应商内条目 4499 → 5448，新增 949 条），同步 stencil.so / OpenRouter / Kilo / Umans 的最新模型、上下文与价格。适配修正：Moonshot Kimi K3 固定官方价格、零缓存写入与 131,072 输出上限；NVIDIA NIM 的 `qwen/*` 保留 reasoning 标记以继续走 `chat_template_kwargs`；Umans 切换到 K3/GLM 5.3/DeepSeek V4.1 等新条目，MiniMax-M3 与 Command Code 的二进制 1M 窗口跟随实际限额。
  - EN: Refreshed the bundled provider model catalog (4,499 → 5,448 provider-scoped entries, 949 added) from the current stencil.so / OpenRouter / Kilo / Umans metadata, including models, context windows, and pricing. Adaptation fixes: Moonshot Kimi K3 is pinned to its official pricing, zero cache-write rate, and 131,072 output ceiling; NVIDIA NIM `qwen/*` rows keep their reasoning flag so the host-specific `chat_template_kwargs` path stays armed; Umans moves to the K3 / GLM 5.3 / DeepSeek V4.1 rows, and MiniMax-M3 and Command Code follow their actual binary 1M limits. Provider seed policies now match upstream's compiled rules: credential-scoped and static-seed-complete providers are never resurrected from the previous snapshot, and authored seeds declare `always`/`fallback` policies instead of relying on ad-hoc conditions.

- **Anthropic 原生服务端压缩**：第一方 Claude 模型在 `compat.supportsServerCompaction` 为 true 时，压缩走 `compact-2026-09-04` on-demand 请求：只发送待摘要的前缀，把返回的带签名 `compaction` 块存入 `preserveData.anthropicCompaction`，后续回合按块重放而不是重复发送摘要文本；没有签名块或请求失败时按配置的方法顺序回退。
  - EN: First-party Claude models with `compat.supportsServerCompaction` now compact through one `compact-2026-09-04` on-demand request containing only the prefix to summarize. The returned signed `compaction` block is stored in `preserveData.anthropicCompaction` and replayed on later turns instead of resending the summary text; a response without a signed block, or a failed request, falls back to the configured method order.

- **Bedrock 原生压缩与上下文笔记插件化**：Bedrock 上的 Anthropic 模型现在通过 `InvokeModelWithResponseStream` 侧请求执行同一条 on-demand 压缩链，普通回合仍走 Converse。`context_notes` / `new_context` 以及笔记本注入从核心迁到 bundled Cordis 插件（`musepi-context-notes`），插件清单可独立启停；扩展运行时新增 `ctx.ensureSessionOnDisk` / `ctx.appendCustomEntry` / `ctx.flushSession` 作为会话日志写入缝。
  - EN: Anthropic models on Bedrock now run the same on-demand compaction chain through an `InvokeModelWithResponseStream` side request while ordinary turns stay on Converse. `context_notes` / `new_context` and the notebook injection moved out of the core into the bundled Cordis plugin `musepi-context-notes`, so a profile can enable or drop the component independently; the extension runtime gained `ctx.ensureSessionOnDisk` / `ctx.appendCustomEntry` / `ctx.flushSession` as the session-journal write seam.

- **侧栏项目行补齐悬停操作，并修复“打开文件夹”在 Windows 无效**：项目行悬停时在右侧显示编辑（隐式项目为“转为托管”）、更多、在该项目新建会话三个动作，覆盖在计数上，长项目名的截断宽度不变；显隐只跟随指针悬停与 `:focus-visible`，鼠标点击后不会常驻。同一契约也应用到会话标签与设置页建议行：`:focus-within` 的常驻问题一并修复，操作条显示时状态点与 label 留白同帧让位，透明状态下的建议行操作不再可点击。右键菜单的“打开文件夹”此前调用 preload 中并不存在的 `revealPath`，所有平台都无反应；现在改用 `shell.openPath`，文案按系统显示为“在资源管理器中打开 / 在 Finder 中打开 / 在文件管理器中打开”，失败时给出提示。
  - EN: Project rows gain hover actions, and "Open folder" now works on Windows. Hovering a project row shows edit (or promote for an implicit row), more, and new session rooted at that project; the strip overlays the count so a long name keeps the same truncation budget; it follows pointer hover and `:focus-visible` only, so a mouse click cannot leave it stuck open. The same contract now covers session tabs and settings suggestion rows: their `:focus-within` stickiness is gone, status dots and label padding yield in the same state set, and transparent suggestion actions are no longer clickable. The context menu's open-folder item called `revealPath`, which does not exist in the preload, so it was a no-op on every platform; it now uses `shell.openPath`, labels itself by the shell (Explorer/Finder/file manager) and reports a failure.

- **多根工作区在上下文与会话信息卡里成为一等公民**：附加目录的项目级技能现在与主目录一起发现（同名以主目录为准），系统提示的 `<workspace-roots>` 明确说明这些是项目目录而非只读参考资料，并说明其下的 AGENTS.md 已注入。另一个窗口新增的项目目录会立即补进同根的活动会话并重建提示词，无需手动重载。会话信息卡用同一种可折叠行渲染主目录与每个附加目录（分支、文件数、±、展开后的逐文件列表），主目录只多一个徽标；标签与可打开表面全部列出、卡片自身滚动，不再出现“查看更多”，连接器改为可折叠且折叠时保留头部操作。
  - EN: Multi-root workspaces are first-class in context and in the session info card. Project skills are now discovered for additional directories together with the primary root (the primary wins a name collision); the `<workspace-roots>` prompt block states that these are project directories rather than read-only references and that their AGENTS.md files are injected. A project directory added in another window is merged into open sessions of the same root and their prompt is rebuilt without a manual reload. The card renders the primary and every added directory with the same collapsible row (branch, file count, ±, per-file list on expand), with only a badge distinguishing the primary; tabs and openable surfaces are listed in full while the card scrolls, so no "Show more" remains, and connectors are a disclosure that keeps its header actions reachable while folded.

- **后台投机压缩不再换入失效摘要**：上下文越过阈值后继续大幅增长时，armed 摘要若换入后仍高于恢复带、或相对当前分支没有真正变小，现在会被丢弃并由阻塞压缩重新摘要，避免"换入即再次越线"。native remote 投机遇到不可重试错误后按会话/模型记住，后续边界与阻塞压缩都不再重发同一个失败请求；handoff 跳过 post-turn 维护的回合若以 overflow/length 结束，会先等待进行中的投机结果再恢复维护。
  - EN: Background speculative compaction no longer swaps in a stale summary. When the context keeps growing past the threshold, an armed summary that would still land above the recovery band, or would not actually shrink the current branch, is now discarded and the blocking pass summarizes a fresher cut, so a swap cannot immediately re-trip the threshold. A non-retriable native remote speculation failure is remembered per session/model, so neither later boundaries nor the blocking pass re-send the same failing request, and a turn that skipped post-turn maintenance for handoff generation and then ended with overflow/length now waits for the in-flight speculation before maintenance resumes.

- **按模型压缩阈值与笔记式上下文窗口**：`compaction.modelThresholds` 可按 `provider/model-id`（或 `provider/…*` 前缀）给单个模型设置压缩策略：令牌数是策略缩放的基准，`f90000` 是固定触发点，`80%` 替换阈值百分比；`compaction.modelThresholdsEnabled` 可整体关闭。`compaction.experimentalContextManagement` 开启实验性的笔记式上下文窗口：接近阈值时先提醒模型用 `context_notes` 更新持久笔记本，模型调用 `new_context` 后以固定边界滚动到新窗口，旧原文仍可通过 `history://current/full` 读取；rollover 同样经过 `session_before_compact`，扩展可以取消或替换该边界。投影与 tokenizer 现在也计入会进入模型上下文的 `custom_message`；wire 会丢弃图片的模型不再被当成 snapcompact 可用。
  - EN: Per-model compaction limits and notes-backed context windows. `compaction.modelThresholds` sets a compaction policy for one `provider/model-id` (or a `provider/…*` prefix): a token count is the base the policy scales, `f90000` is a fixed trigger, and `80%` replaces the threshold percentage; `compaction.modelThresholdsEnabled` disables the overrides wholesale. `compaction.experimentalContextManagement` enables experimental notes-backed context windows: near the threshold the model is reminded to refresh its persistent notebook with `context_notes`, and after it calls `new_context` the session rolls to a fresh window at a fixed boundary while the raw transcript stays readable through `history://current/full`; the rollover also runs `session_before_compact`, so an extension can cancel or replace the boundary. Compaction projections and the tokenizer now count `custom_message` rows that reach model context, and a model whose wire drops images no longer counts as snapcompact-capable.

- **Preset 选择补齐完整注册与选择链路**：新增 `agentPresets.list` / `agentPresets.read` / `agentPresets.select`；`list` 合并文件 preset、profile preset 与扩展贡献的 mode（宿主级注册表：装载即登记，daemon 启动时按 cwd 与工作区注册表的全部根目录预加载并复用会话插件缓存，无需活跃会话；同 id 文件优先，每项带 `source`），`read` 对后两者返回序列化文档；解析也走宿主注册表兜底（当前会话未装载该扩展时同样可解析）；选择成功后向会话的 Cordis 总线派发 `agent-preset/selected`（`{ sessionId, agentPreset }`，清除为 `null`），并把选择写入会话头（字段沿用 `modeId`，resume 已读它）。`AgentSession.getAgentPreset()` / `selectAgentPreset()` 是 dsh 命名的访问器，SDK 热切换现在也会持久化会话头（此前只有 daemon 写）。
  - EN: Preset selection now has dsh's shape. `agentPresets.list`, `agentPresets.read`, and `agentPresets.select` are available; `list` merges file presets, profile presets, and extension-contributed modes (a host-level registry: a mount records them, and daemon startup preloads every known workspace root through the shared session-plugin cache, so no live session is needed; file wins an id collision, each row carries `source`), and `read` serializes the latter two; resolution falls back to the host registry too, so a listed preset stays selectable even when the current session did not load its extension; a successful selection dispatches `agent-preset/selected` on the session's Cordis bus (`{ sessionId, agentPreset }`, `null` on clear) and writes the choice into the session header (stored as `modeId`, which resume already reads). `AgentSession.getAgentPreset()` / `selectAgentPreset()` are the dsh-named accessors, and the SDK hot switch now persists the header too (previously only the daemon wrote it).

- **插件实例可以跨会话共享**：同一 `(cwd, 插件入口集)` 的会话现在复用一套插件实例与 runtime；会话级监听、prompt 贡献与 effect 由会话 fiber 承担，派发按会话过滤，所以共享不会串台。`ctx.promptHost` 只在 `session_start` 之后可用（`apply` 里是 `undefined`），核心提示词插件改为在会话开始时注册；服务与内存后端 provider 归插件实例（装载时启动、卸载时停止），某个会话结束不再停掉它们。`pi.state` 与 `pi.events` 是实例级，需要会话状态的插件自行按会话 key。
  - EN: Plugin instances can now be shared across sessions. Sessions with the same `(cwd, plugin entry set)` reuse one instance set and runtime; session listeners, prompt contributions, and effects belong to the session fiber, and dispatch is filtered per session, so sharing cannot leak. `ctx.promptHost` is only available from `session_start` onward (`undefined` in `apply`), and the core prompt plugins now register there; services and memory-backend providers belong to the instance (started at load, stopped at unload), so one session ending no longer stops them. `pi.state` and `pi.events` stay instance-level, and a plugin that needs session state keys it by session itself.

- **HarmonyOS 返回键补齐（实现完成，尚未编译验证）**：鸿蒙壳此前没有 `onBackPress`，返回键既不会先收键盘、也不会关闭已打开的 sheet/面板/抽屉，直接交回系统。现在 `Index.onBackPress()` 与 Android 同序：先收键盘（`document.activeElement.blur()`，不引入 IMEKit 依赖），再走页面层栈（`window.__harmonyBack()` → `dispatchBack()`），都没有才交回系统。`onBackPress` 必须同步作答而 `runJavaScript` 是异步的，所以页面通过新增的 `harmonyNative.setBackDepth` 在每次层栈变化时同步深度。顺带修掉 `EntryAbility` 里 `BusinessError` 未导入导致的编译错误，以及 `copy-web-assets.js` 仍指向旧包名 `guest-client`、`rawfile` 永远拷不进去的问题；新增 `scripts/build-hap.mjs`（`devecocli` 无 IDE 出包）。**已编译并签名通过**：Command Line Tools 26.0.0.865 + SDK API 26 + hvigor 6.26.9，`bun run build:hap` 产出 `entry-default-unsigned.hap`（ArkTS 零 error 零 warn），`devecocli signature generate --type debug` 后产出 `entry-default-signed.hap`；签名材料在 `~/.ohos/config/`，配置机器绑定故未入库。工程结构升到 `modelVersion: 6.0.0`、补 `entry/hvigorfile.ts`、`nodeOptions.maxOldSpaceSize: 8192` 解决打包 OOM，`copy-web-assets` 改为增量拷贝+差异清理（原来的整目录删除会半途失败并留下空 rawfile）。**真机未验证**：无 USB 设备，模拟器要求固件虚拟化（本机未开启）。调用 `devecocli` 需清掉 `HTTP(S)_PROXY`，否则登录/云接口报 `Invalid proxy authorization`。
  - EN: HarmonyOS back key completed (implemented, not yet compiled). The HarmonyOS shell had no `onBackPress` at all, so back neither dismissed the keyboard nor closed an open sheet/panel/drawer — it fell straight through to the system. `Index.onBackPress()` now follows the Android order: dismiss the IME first (via `document.activeElement.blur()`, no IMEKit dependency), then the page's layer stack (`window.__harmonyBack()` → `dispatchBack()`), and only then hand the press back. Because `onBackPress` must answer synchronously while `runJavaScript` is async, the page mirrors its layer depth through the new `harmonyNative.setBackDepth` on every change. Also fixed a missing `BusinessError` import that broke `EntryAbility` compilation and `copy-web-assets.js`, which still pointed at the old `guest-client` package so `rawfile` was never populated; added `scripts/build-hap.mjs` for a headless `devecocli` build. Compiled and signed: Command Line Tools 26.0.0.865 + SDK API 26 + hvigor 6.26.9; `bun run build:hap` produces `entry-default-unsigned.hap` with zero ArkTS errors or warnings, and `devecocli signature generate --type debug` yields `entry-default-signed.hap`. Signing materials live in `~/.ohos/config/` and the config is machine-bound, so it is not committed. The project moved to `modelVersion: 6.0.0`, gained the missing `entry/hvigorfile.ts`, sets `nodeOptions.maxOldSpaceSize: 8192` to stop the packer from running out of heap, and `copy-web-assets` now copies incrementally and prunes only stale files (the old whole-directory delete could abort halfway and leave rawfile empty). On-device checks remain unverified: no USB device, and the emulator needs firmware virtualization this machine has disabled. Calling `devecocli` requires clearing `HTTP(S)_PROXY`, otherwise login and cloud calls fail with `Invalid proxy authorization`.

- **移动端界面与生命周期专项修复（11 项）**：单会话主机（`sessions === null`）此前完全没有设置入口——设置页、断开连接、管理电脑都藏在会话 Sheet 的 footer 里，而窄屏又隐藏了主题/主机/退出按钮；`settings` 现在进入面板菜单（wide 档同排）。会话水合期不再冒充"全新会话"：加载历史时显示骨架屏并抑制空态问候与建议 chips。collab 线没有心跳，Wi-Fi 切蜂窝 / NAT 超时 / Doze 冻结不会触发 `close`，UI 会一直显示 live 且发送按钮可能永久卡住；新增 15s 探测（5s 超时 + 一次重试，两次失败重建连接，回前台立即探测）、host 侧 `ping` RPC、`rpc` 的 `opts.timeoutMs`，草稿首条消息与附件分片上传也有了上限。草稿改为按「连接::会话」持久化，切会话不再把半句话带到别的会话、进程被杀也不丢。后台通知以订阅时的时间为基线，不再重推旧的 assistant 回复。返回键先收键盘（草稿页不再直接退出应用）。会话行滑动补 `touch-action: pan-y` 并按松手速度吸附；sheet 补键盘 inset，banner/toast 补安全区；扫码加了成功闩锁（一次扫码不再建多个连接）与后台释放摄像头；manifest 补 `VIBRATE`（此前全部触感反馈静默失效）；浏览器/PWA 移除重复的键盘补偿；15px 字号按物理密度归一（只乘文字）；`BootSplash` 尊重 reduced-motion。核对记录见 `docs/mobile-design.md` §14。
  - EN: Mobile UI and lifecycle pass, 11 fixes. A single-session host (`sessions === null`) had no way to reach settings at all: the settings page, disconnect, and host manager lived only in the session sheet's footer, while the compact header hid the theme/host/leave buttons; `settings` now sits in the panel menu (and the wide toolbar). Session hydration no longer impersonates a brand-new session — a skeleton covers the load and the empty-state greeting and suggestion chips stay out of the way. The collab wire has no heartbeat, so a Wi-Fi→cellular handoff, NAT expiry, or Doze freeze never fires `close`: the header kept claiming live and the composer could hang on "sending" forever. Added a 15 s liveness probe (5 s timeout, one 1.2 s retry, rebuild after two misses, immediate probe on foreground), a host-side `ping` RPC, `opts.timeoutMs` on `rpc`, and bounded deadlines on the draft-session create and chunked attachment uploads. Drafts are now keyed and persisted per connection+session, so a half-typed prompt no longer follows the user into the next session and survives a process kill. Background notifications baseline against the transcript at subscribe time instead of re-announcing the last old reply. Back now dismisses the keyboard first (the draft screen no longer exits the app). Session rows gained `touch-action: pan-y` and velocity-based settle; sheets gained the IME inset and the banner/toasts gained safe areas; the QR scanner latches on the first decode (one scan no longer opens several connections) and releases the camera when backgrounded; the manifest declares `VIBRATE` (every haptic was silently dropped); the browser/PWA path lost its duplicate keyboard compensation; the 15px base is normalised to physical density (text only); `BootSplash` honours reduced motion. Audit record: `docs/mobile-design.md` §14.

- **`ctx.effect` 的归属跟随调用点**：在 `apply` 里注册的清理归扩展自己的 fiber，卸载/重载时执行；在 `session_start` 等会话处理器里注册的归会话 fiber，会话结束即执行。插件实例因此可以跨会话复用而不丢 apply 期资源，会话资源也不会被下一个会话继承；没有 fiber 的旧式装载继续使用实例账本，卸载与会话停止都会执行。
  - EN: `ctx.effect` now belongs to the scope where it was registered. A cleanup registered in `apply` lives with the extension's own fiber and runs on unload or reload; one registered inside a session handler (`session_start`) lives with the session fiber and runs when the session ends. A plugin instance can therefore be reused across sessions without losing resources opened at apply time, and session resources never carry into the next session. Legacy loads without a fiber keep the per-instance ledger and still run on unload and session shutdown.

- **会话作用域归 runner，派发按会话过滤**：`ctx.session` 的 Cordis fiber 从 runtime 移到 runner，当前作用域通过 `AsyncLocalStorage` 传给正在执行的处理器；派发以 carrier 作为 subject，Cordis `Context.filter` 只放行「无标签（实例/宿主级）」与「本会话」的监听，所以同一个事件总线可以同时服务多个会话而不串台，为插件实例跨会话共享铺路。会话关闭后 runner 保留会话标识，迟到派发仍被过滤到该会话，不会广播给同一总线上的其他会话。
  - EN: The session scope now belongs to the runner, and dispatch is filtered per session. `ctx.session`'s Cordis fiber moved from the runtime to the runner, and the active scope reaches handlers through `AsyncLocalStorage`; dispatch passes a carrier as the event subject so `Context.filter` admits only untagged (instance/host-scope) listeners and the dispatching session's own. One event bus can therefore serve several sessions without cross-talk, clearing the way for shared plugin instances. A closed session keeps its identity on the runner, so a late dispatch is still filtered to it instead of broadcasting to every listener on the bus.

- **内置浏览器补齐安全视觉输入、元素子树读取与面板激活**：`click_if_interactive` 只在请求点本身可交互时点击，附近候选明确标注「未执行点击」且不会被自动选择；`visual_type` 支持坐标输入与替换，`press` 可先聚焦坐标。`read` 新增 `scope:"element"`，按同一元素快照的 `snapshotId`/`ref` 只读取子树并保留分页；`activate_panel` 仅显示内置浏览器面板，不创建或切换标签。Agent 提示词、结构化结果、用户卡片与双语文档同步覆盖这些契约。
  - EN: The built-in browser now supports safe visual input, element-subtree reads, and panel activation. `click_if_interactive` clicks only an interactive element at the requested point; nearby candidates are explicitly marked “No click performed” and are never selected automatically. `visual_type` enters or replaces text at visual coordinates, and `press` can focus a point first. `read` accepts `scope:"element"` with the same snapshot's `snapshotId`/`ref`, excluding siblings while preserving pagination. `activate_panel` reveals the built-in panel without creating or switching tabs. Agent guidance, structured results, user cards, and bilingual docs cover the same contracts.

- **mini 窗只保留聊天，右侧面板最大化贴齐场景卡片**：`?mini=1` 迷你窗不再渲染右侧 rail 与面板壳（它们属于工作区 chrome），欢迎页/会话在 520px 宽的窗口里拿回被 rail 占掉的 44px。右侧面板最大化改用「当前可见场景卡片」做锚点：有会话时仍是会话列，欢迎页则是欢迎卡；欢迎页/会话切换时会重测并忽略正在退场的旧场景。此前在欢迎页最大化会沿用上一个会话的旧矩形，面板比成品区窄、还高出顶部一截。
  - EN: The mini window is chat-only and the maximized panel now tracks the visible scene card. `?mini=1` no longer renders the right rail or the panel shell (workspace chrome), so the 520px window gives that 44px back to the welcome/chat surface. Maximize anchors to the visible scene card — the session column with a session open, the welcome card on the welcome scene — and re-measures across the welcome↔session swap while ignoring the outgoing scene; before this, maximizing on the welcome scene kept the previous session's rectangle, leaving the panel narrower than the surface and rising above its top edge.

- **插件实例现在是一个 Cordis fiber**：扩展装载时在自己的 fiber 里执行 `apply`（`eventBus.inject`），`pi.on` 登记的监听归该 fiber 的 effect 账本所有，重载、卸载、回滚统一 `fiber.dispose()`；监听列表只保留精确计数与没有 fiber 的装载路径，不再承担所有权。`ctx.session` 仍是它下面更窄的一层 fiber，会话结束只回收会话级监听。工厂抛错会让 fiber 拒绝，装载错误仍是 `Failed to load extension: …`。
  - EN: A plugin instance is now its own Cordis fiber. The factory runs inside `eventBus.inject`, every `pi.on` listener belongs to that fiber's effect ledger, and reload, unload, and rollback are one `fiber.dispose()`; the listener list keeps exact counts and the loaders that run without a fiber but no longer owns cleanup. `ctx.session` remains a narrower fiber underneath, so a session end reclaims only session-scoped listeners. A factory throw rejects the fiber and keeps the `Failed to load extension: …` error contract.

- **设置可全局搜索并直达具体行，偏离默认值的行可一键恢复**：设置搜索从「只过滤左侧页面」升级为索引全部 schema 设置（393 条；daemon `settings.schema` 新增 `all: true`，一次返回全部 12 个 tab）：输入关键词会在导航上方列出跨页匹配项（标签、描述、键名，双语），点击打开所属页并把目标行居中、闪一次高亮；交互 tab 被拆到工具/语音/外观页的分组也按实际渲染页路由。存储值与 schema 默认值不一致的行在标签行显示重置箭头（与 TUI 的 `changed` 对齐），点击写回默认；数组/对象按内容比较并拷贝默认值，凭据行与无默认值行不显示。
  - EN: Settings are now globally searchable down to the row, and a row that differs from its default can be reset in one click. The settings search indexes every schema row (393; the daemon's `settings.schema` gained `all: true`, returning all 12 tabs in one round trip) instead of only filtering the page nav: matches are listed above the nav pages (label, description, key; both locales), and clicking one opens the owning page, centres `[data-setting-key]` and pulses it once. Interaction-tab groups re-sliced onto the tools/voice/appearance pages route to the page that actually renders them. A row whose stored value differs from its schema default exposes a reset arrow in its label line (TUI `changed` parity); arrays/objects compare by content and reset to a copy, and credential or defaultless rows never show it.

- **新增会话级注册面 `ctx.session`**：插件可以在 `session_start` 里用 `ctx.session.on(...)` 注册只属于本次会话的监听——作用域在事件派发前打开、会话收尾时关闭，监听不会带到下一个会话；`session_start` 重放（插件重载后的 `refreshSessionStart`）先关旧作用域再开新的，只重注册、不叠加。`ctx.on` 仍是实例级（活到插件卸载），与 dsh 的 `ctx.on` / `agent.ctx` 同一分工；会话外 `ctx.session` 为 `undefined`。监听 disposer 改为幂等，「会话关闭 + 插件卸载」双重释放不会把计数减成负数。
  - EN: Plugins can now register listeners for one session with `ctx.session.on(...)` inside `session_start`. The scope opens before the event dispatch and closes at session teardown, so a listener never reaches the next session; a replayed `session_start` (the `refreshSessionStart` after a plugin reload) closes the old scope first, so it re-registers instead of stacking. `ctx.on` stays instance-scoped until unload, matching dsh's `ctx.on` / `agent.ctx` split, and `ctx.session` is `undefined` outside a session. Listener disposers are now idempotent, so closing the session and unloading the plugin cannot under-count the listener ledger.

- **事件派发收成一张穷尽表，插件卸载不再漏清监听**：runner 里按事件名硬编码的派发分支合并为 `EVENT_DISPATCH`——键就是事件联合类型，新增事件不声明模式即编译失败；迁移时遗留、从未被读取的 `CORDIS_LIFECYCLE_EVENTS` 删除。插件的 effect、事件监听 disposer 与 state 现在由 `disposePluginPrimitives` 一个入口清空，四条此前只清 effect 的卸载路径（bundled 宿主 reset、动态组件卸载、插件管理卸载、preset 卸载）不再把监听留在 runtime 的事件上下文里，重载也不会叠成双份；会话停止仍只清 effect，监听保留到真正卸载。
  - EN: Event dispatch is one exhaustive table and unload no longer forgets listeners. The runner's per-event hardcoded branches collapse into `EVENT_DISPATCH`, keyed by the event union so a new event cannot ship without a declared mode; the migration leftover `CORDIS_LIFECYCLE_EVENTS`, never read, is gone. Effects, listener disposers, and state now drain through one entry point, `disposePluginPrimitives`, so the four unload paths that used to clear effects only (bundled host reset, dynamic component unload, plugin-management uninstall, preset teardown) no longer leave listeners on the runtime's event context and a reload cannot stack a second copy; session stop still drains effects only and keeps listeners until a real unload.

- **有序设置与快捷提示词改用统一的拖拽排序，快捷提示词页重做**：`ui.ordered` 设置（压缩方式顺序、web 搜索顺序等）的行改用 dnd-kit 拖拽——与右侧面板标签同一手势：仅把手起拖、行抬起、落点实时让位，键盘聚焦把手后空格抓取、方向键移动、空格放下；旧的 ↑↓ 按钮与其 `moveOrderedOption` 辅助一并移除。快捷提示词页换掉自研 pointer 钩子，同样用 dnd-kit 排序，并把行重做为「拖拽把手 + 名称 + 内置标记 + 指令预览 + 悬停出现的编辑/删除」；编辑改为名称输入 + 指令 textarea 的独立卡片，底部是虚线「添加提示词」，标题行只保留「恢复默认」，页内不再重复区块标题。
  - EN: Ordered settings and starter prompts now share one drag gesture, and the starter-prompts page was rebuilt. Rows of `ui.ordered` settings (compaction method order, web search order, …) use the same dnd-kit gesture as the right-panel tabs: handle-only activator, lifted row, live drop slots, and Space + ↑/↓ on the handle for keyboard; the old ↑↓ buttons and their `moveOrderedOption` helper are gone. The starter-prompts page drops its bespoke pointer hook for the same dnd-kit sorting and rebuilds each row as drag handle + label + built-in tag + prompt preview + hover-revealed edit/remove; editing is a card with a name input and a prompt textarea, the list ends with a dashed Add prompt row, the header keeps only Reset to defaults, and the duplicated section title is removed.

- **事件系统只剩一份实现：`Extension.handlers` 删除**：动态沙箱的撤销改为弹出 Cordis 监听 disposer，`CordisDynamicPluginRuntime.emit` 改走共享 runtime 的事件总线；runner 的所有手写兜底循环、`hasHandlers` 的 map 分支与 `Extension.handlers` 字段一并删除，诊断计数改用监听账本。会话停止只清 effect，不误删监听（runner 可复用）；重载/卸载才回收监听。
  - EN: The event system now has one implementation and `Extension.handlers` is gone. The dynamic sandbox undoes registrations by popping the Cordis listener disposer, `CordisDynamicPluginRuntime.emit` dispatches through the shared runtime's event bus, and every hand-written fallback loop, the `hasHandlers` map branch, and the `handlers` field are removed; diagnostics count the listener ledger instead. Session stop drains effects without touching listeners (the runner may be reused); reload/unload reclaims them.

- **角色模型卡片工具栏与外观控制行对齐，窄列时角色列表真正变成单列**：角色卡片工具栏此前没有 `display: flex` 规则，下拉与图标按钮按行内元素排布，宽度不够就换行、末尾按钮掉到下一行；现在工具栏是单行 flex，思考级别下拉可收缩，图标按钮统一 26px 且不再被 `.gui-btn` 的 32px 最小高度撑开。角色网格的容器查询从网格自身移到 `.gui-model-rail-body`（元素无法查询自己的尺寸，原来的 `@container` 永远不生效），窄列时网格真正塌成单列；该列改为仅纵向滚动，角色区底部的横向滚动条随之消失。外观 → 滚动条样式的「导入皮肤」按钮去掉 24px 小号与 8px 上边距，与 34px 下拉同高同字号。
  - EN: Role card toolbars and the appearance control row now align, and a narrow role column really becomes one column. The role card toolbar had no `display: flex` rule, so the thinking select and icon buttons flowed like inline text, wrapped when space ran out, and dropped the last button onto its own line; it is now a single flex row, the select shrinks, and icon buttons share a 26px box that is no longer stretched by `.gui-btn`'s 32px minimum. The role grid's container query moved from the grid itself to `.gui-model-rail-body` (an element cannot query its own size, so the old `@container` never matched) — a narrow column now truly collapses to one column, and that column scrolls vertically only, removing the horizontal scrollbar under the role grid. In Appearance → Scrollbar style, the import button lost its 24px small variant and 8px top margin and now matches the 34px select's height and font size.

- **会话管理成为唯一的会话读取面，「历史会话」页删除**：设置 → 会话现在支持点会话名就地展开消息（默认只看搜索命中，可切全部消息，长消息可展开全文），搜索框同时检索会话名与会话内容，命中的会话带计数留在列表中；筛选改为即时生效，不再需要点「查询」。表格改为固定列宽布局，操作列不再被裁掉；状态列显示会话真实结局（已完成/已中断/已中止/出错）。「数据与统计 → 历史会话」入口及其两栏浏览器、相关文案与样式一并移除。
  - EN: Sessions in settings is now the only place sessions are read. Clicking a session name expands its messages in place (search hits first, full transcript on demand, long messages expandable), the search box covers both session text and message content, and sessions matched by message stay listed with a hit count. Filters apply immediately instead of behind an Apply button, the table uses fixed column widths so the action cell is no longer clipped, and the status column reports the transcript verdict (complete/interrupted/aborted/error). The 数据与统计 → 历史会话 entry, its two-pane browser, and their copy and styles are gone.
