- **新增 `session.lastPrompts` 批量路由**：会话表格的「最后一条 prompt」列不再对每行调用一次 `session.search` 扫转录尾部（长会话尾部常全是 assistant/tool 行，该列因此常年为空），改为每页一次请求，用一条 `JOIN (MAX(seq))` 查询取回各会话最后一条用户消息。
  - EN: The session table's last-prompt column reads one batched `session.lastPrompts` request per page instead of one `session.search` per row. Scanning each transcript tail left the column empty for long sessions whose tail is all assistant/tool rows; a single `JOIN (MAX(seq))` query now returns each session's newest user message.

- **强调色从「跟随主题」切走时，过渡图标现在真的会变颜色**：跟随主题会把整套 `--accent` 落成内联 token，内联声明的优先级高于 tokens.css 里的 `[data-accent]` 块，所以切换前的预测只翻 `data-accent` 就读回了当前色——遮罩图标的终点色等于起点色，动画全程不变色（反方向与其他预设都正常）。现在探测前先摘掉内联 `--accent*`，读完立刻复原，起点色仍是主题自带的强调色、终点色是目标预设色。
  - EN: Leaving the follow-theme accent now recolors the flip icon. Follow-theme materializes the whole `--accent` family as inline tokens, and inline declarations outrank the `[data-accent]` blocks in tokens.css, so the pre-swap probe that only flipped `data-accent` read the current color back: the veil icon's target equaled its source and the animation never changed color (the reverse direction and every explicit preset were fine). The probe now drops the inline `--accent*` tokens first and restores them immediately, so the source stays the active theme's accent and the target is the preset's own color.

- **连接器市场支持远端 MCP 一键接入**：新增连接器目录，可读取本地索引、WorkBuddy 兼容包目录或远端索引；设置 → 智能体 → 连接器 提供搜索、连接/断开与行内授权等待，Token 连接器在行内填写并加密保存；启用的连接器以 `connector:<id>` 进入现有 MCP 发现链，OAuth 直接复用 `mcp.oauth.start/status/cancel`，无需第二套凭据存储。新增只读 `connector_catalog` 工具，模型可先查目录再推荐；目录行不会返回请求头或凭据。
  - EN: The connector market now onboards remote MCP servers. A catalog reads a local index, WorkBuddy-compatible package directories, or a remote index; Settings → Agent → Connectors provides search, connect/disconnect, an inline authorization wait, and an encrypted inline Token form; enabled connectors join the existing MCP discovery chain as `connector:<id>`, and OAuth reuses `mcp.oauth.start/status/cancel` without a second credential store. A read-only `connector_catalog` tool lets the model inspect real entries before recommending one, and catalog rows never return headers or credentials.

- **MCP OAuth 凭据加密落盘**：`mcp_oauth:` / `mcp_oauth_` 行在 `agent.db` 中改为密文列 + 明文哈希标记，compare-and-swap 刷新语义不变；密钥位于数据库旁的 `mcp-oauth.key`，可用 `MUSEPI_MCP_OAUTH_KEY` 覆盖；打开时自动迁移旧明文行，解密失败保留密文并视为不可用。
  - EN: MCP OAuth credentials are encrypted at rest. `mcp_oauth:` / `mcp_oauth_` rows now keep ciphertext in a dedicated column with a plaintext hash marker, preserving compare-and-swap refreshes; the key lives beside the database as `mcp-oauth.key` and can be overridden by `MUSEPI_MCP_OAUTH_KEY`; legacy plaintext rows migrate on open, and a decrypt failure keeps the ciphertext and treats the row as unusable.

- **连接器目录支持本地 stdio 进程**：`mcp.json`/索引里的 `command` 条目现在进入目录；连接时先返回确切命令、参数与环境变量名，用户确认后才写入命令指纹并挂载，命令变化或断开都会使批准失效。目录不安装 npm 包，`npx`/`uvx` 或已安装二进制直接可用。
  - EN: The connector catalog now accepts local stdio processes. `command` entries from `mcp.json` or the index enter the catalog; connecting first returns the exact command, args, and env keys, and only a confirmed approval writes the command fingerprint and mounts it. A changed command or a disconnect invalidates the approval. The catalog does not install npm packages, so `npx`/`uvx` or an installed binary must already be available.

- **连接器目录支持托管 CLI 包**：`cli.json` 的安装/版本/认证/状态命令进入目录；安装落在 `~/.musepi/agent/cli-connectors` 的隔离 prefix，连接前展示安装与认证命令并等待批准，认证进程输出 URL/设备码后由设置页轮询 `status`，成功后启用；断开时执行 `unAuth`。Windows 上裸命令名按 `PATH`/`PATHEXT` 解析，npm 安装出的 `.cmd` 垫片经 `cmd.exe` 运行，取消授权会一并结束其子进程。
  - EN: The connector catalog now supports managed CLI packages. `cli.json` install/version/auth/status commands enter the catalog; installs land in an isolated prefix under `~/.musepi/agent/cli-connectors`, connecting shows the install and auth commands for approval, the settings page polls `status` after the auth process prints a URL/device code, and the connector enables on success; disconnecting runs `unAuth`. On Windows a bare command name resolves through `PATH`/`PATHEXT`, `.cmd` shims installed by npm run through `cmd.exe`, and cancelling authorization ends their child processes too.

- **桌面 MCP OAuth 支持深链回调**：设置页登录改为请求 `musepi://oauth/callback`；Electron 注册协议并在渲染器就绪前排队，渲染器经 `mcp.oauth.callback` 交给 daemon；状态与 PKCE 在 flow 内校验，回环回调仍作为 Web/非桌面路径保留。
  - EN: Desktop MCP OAuth now uses a deep-link callback. Settings login requests `musepi://oauth/callback`; Electron registers the scheme, queues callbacks until the renderer is ready, and the renderer hands them to the daemon through `mcp.oauth.callback`; state and PKCE are validated inside the flow, and loopback remains for the web/non-desktop path.

- **审批生命周期事件补入 Cordis 单次通道**：`tool_approval_requested` / `tool_approval_resolved` 是最后两个仍在旧派发的事件，现与其它通知事件共用 `serial`；插件事件的注册与派发至此没有遗留分支。
  - EN: The approval lifecycle events are on the Cordis one-shot path too. `tool_approval_requested` / `tool_approval_resolved` were the last two events still on the legacy dispatcher; they now use `serial` like the other notifications, leaving no plugin event on the old path.

- **剩余专用事件全部迁到 Cordis，插件事件派发至此统一**：`before_agent_start`（消息与 systemPrompt 累积）、`before_provider_request`（payload 逐次改写）、`after_provider_response`、`cache_warming_decision`（最后一个 action 生效）都进入同一事件上下文；`credential_disabled` / `mcp_notification` 走单次通知通道。新增三例真实 runner 回归（提示词链、payload 链、action 覆盖）。
  - EN: The remaining dedicated events now dispatch through Cordis, so every plugin event uses one event context: `before_agent_start` (accumulated messages and systemPrompt), `before_provider_request` (payload rewrites chained), `after_provider_response`, and `cache_warming_decision` (last action wins); `credential_disabled` / `mcp_notification` use the one-shot notification path. Three real-runner tests cover the prompt chain, payload chain, and action override.

- **`user_bash` / `user_python` / `resources_discover` 迁到 Cordis serial**：前两者首个非空结果即中断（旧的 bail 语义），`resources_discover` 按扩展路径累积三类资源；同时补上 `session.compacting` 的结果记忆分支（上一批只做了登记）。新增 user_bash 首结果与资源归属两例回归。
  - EN: `user_bash` / `user_python` / `resources_discover` now dispatch through Cordis serial. The first non-empty user-event result bails (the old semantics), and resource discovery accumulates all three path kinds with their owning extension; the missing `session.compacting` result-memory branch is wired as well. New tests cover first-result-wins and resource attribution.

- **`session.compacting` / `session_stop` 迁到 Cordis serial**：compacting 保留最后一个有效结果；stop 在“继续 + 带上下文”时立即中断并返回该结果，`event.signal` 经执行器合成到 handler 预算（含超时与错误上报）。续跑判定抽成 `sessionStopContinues`，两条路径共用。
  - EN: `session.compacting` and `session_stop` now dispatch through Cordis serial. Compacting keeps the last valid result; stop bails immediately when it asks to continue with context, with `event.signal` composed into the handler budget by the executor (timeout and error reporting included). The continuation predicate is shared through `sessionStopContinues`.

- **单次通知事件批量迁到 Cordis serial**：`session_switch/branch/tree/compact`、`goal_updated`、`agent_start/end`、`auto_compaction_*`、`auto_retry_*`、`retry_fallback_*`、`ttsr_triggered`、`todo_reminder` 以 `serial` 保持注册顺序，零监听时短路。新增顺序回归。
  - EN: One-shot notification events now dispatch through Cordis `serial` — `session_switch/branch/tree/compact`, `goal_updated`, `agent_start/end`, `auto_compaction_*`, `auto_retry_*`, `retry_fallback_*`, `ttsr_triggered`, and `todo_reminder` — preserving registration order and short-circuiting with no listeners. A new ordering test pins it.

- **流式/通知事件改走 Cordis parallel，`ctx.on` 支持 `prepend`**：`message_start/update/end`、`tool_execution_*`、`turn_start/end` 并发派发，零监听时仍短路；`ctx.on(event, handler, { prepend })` 对已迁事件是 Cordis 全局顺序，对 legacy 事件只在同一扩展内提前。新增两类顺序回归。
  - EN: Streaming and notification events now dispatch through Cordis `parallel` — `message_start/update/end`, `tool_execution_*`, `turn_start/end` — and still short-circuit with no listeners. `ctx.on(event, handler, { prepend })` gives Cordis global ordering for migrated events and within-extension ordering for legacy ones. Two ordering tests were added.

- **`context` / `input` 迁到 Cordis serial**：两者与 `tool_result` 同一模式——监听器返回 patch，派发侧在同一个状态上累积，后者看到前者的改写；`input` 的 `handled` 仍然立即中断。新增“后一个监听能看到前一个的改写”的回归。
  - EN: `context` and `input` now dispatch through Cordis serial with the same accumulated-state pattern as `tool_result`: listeners return a patch, the dispatcher applies it to one state, and later listeners see earlier rewrites; `input` still stops immediately on `handled`. New tests pin the cross-listener visibility.

- **`tool_result` 改写迁到 Cordis serial**：监听器只返回自己的 patch，派发侧把 patch 依次应用到同一个累积事件上，后者能看到前者的修改（last-write-wins，与旧循环一致）；单个 patch 的应用抽成 `applyToolResultPatch`，两条派发路径共用。
  - EN: `tool_result` rewrites now dispatch through Cordis serial. Each listener returns only its patch; the dispatcher applies it to one accumulated event in order, so later listeners see earlier changes (last-write-wins, as before). Patch application is shared by both paths through `applyToolResultPatch`.

- **`tool_call` 门禁迁到 Cordis serial，保持 fail-closed**：`tool_call` 监听进入同一事件上下文，以 `serial` 顺序派发；超时或抛错仍返回 `block: true`（原因文本不变），首个 block 立即中断，非 block 结果保留最后一个有效值。超时、信号中断、工具注册屏障与错误上报继续由 runner 的执行器处理，语义未变。
  - EN: The `tool_call` gate now dispatches through Cordis `serial` and stays fail-closed. A timeout or throw still returns `block: true` with the same reason text, the first block stops the chain, and non-block results keep the last valid value. Timeout, signal abort, tool-registration barriers, and error reporting still come from the runner's executor, so behavior is unchanged.

- **`session_before_*` 也迁到 Cordis serial，并保留取消语义**：`session_before_switch/branch/compact/tree` 现在与 start/shutdown 同一事件上下文，以 `serial` 派发；只有 `cancel: true` 中断链，其余结果按“最后一个有效值”记住后继续，与旧循环一致（新增取消/继续两例回归）。
  - EN: `session_before_*` events now use Cordis `serial` with the old cancel contract. `session_before_switch/branch/compact/tree` share the lifecycle event context; only `cancel: true` stops the chain, while any other result is remembered (last one wins) and dispatch continues, matching the previous loop. New tests pin both the continue and cancel paths.

- **能力中心改为两级左栏，与首页 / 任务中心共用一套规范**：技能 / 插件 / 插件市场三个切换按钮从 `WorkspacePageHeader.tabs` 页内 tab 行搬到 rail 右侧的二级左栏（参照 ChatGPT「自定义」），成为 `.gui-side-keeper` 里第三个常驻面板 `CapabilitySidePanel`：整宽主操作（添加能力 → 市场）、紧凑搜索、扁平「浏览」分组下三条整宽行（图标 + 标签 + 已安装/插件计数）、一种强调色选中态、一行无匹配提示。页头只留标题 / 描述 / 返回，内容列只渲染一个 pane，技能的「发现 / 我安装的」二级分段仍在内容列。新增 `lib/capability-nav.ts` 单向桥（页面发布 tab 与两份计数，面板回传 select / add，卸载清理）并登记为宿主共享模块，编译版页面与面板不会各带一份副本。`computeSettingsShellState` / `resolveSidebarPanel` / `AppRail.panelId` / `data-panel` 决策表扩到 `capability`，折叠、拖拽、hover-peek 与等高圆角卡片全部沿用任务中心那套几何。面板是 Cordis 贡献 `sidebar.panel.capability`（`plugins/capability-panel/`），插件缺失或编译失败回退同一内置组件。
  - EN: The capability center now uses a two-tier left column like 首页 / 任务中心. The Skills / Plugins / Marketplace switches moved out of `WorkspacePageHeader.tabs` into `CapabilitySidePanel`, a third mounted pane in `.gui-side-keeper` (ChatGPT's 自定义 panel is the reference): one full-width primary action, a compact search row, one flat 浏览 section of three full-width rows with icons + labels + installed/plugin counts, a single accent selection and a one-line no-match hint. The page header keeps only title / description / back, the content column renders one pane, and the 技能 discover/installed sub-segment stays there. A new `lib/capability-nav.ts` bridge (page publishes the tab + two counts, panel sends select / add, clears on unmount) is registered as a shared host module so the compiled page and panel bind one store. `computeSettingsShellState`, `resolveSidebarPanel`, `AppRail.panelId` and the `data-panel` decision table now cover `capability`; folding, resizing, hover-peek and the equal-height rounded card reuse the task-center geometry. The panel is the Cordis contribution `sidebar.panel.capability` (`plugins/capability-panel/`) with the same built-in component as fallback.

- **`session_start` / `session_shutdown` 改走 Cordis 分发**：两者注册进 runtime 的 Cordis 事件上下文，分别以 `serial` 与 `parallel` 派发；超时与错误仍走原有 error 通道（`handler timed out after Nms` 文本不变），工具注册屏障计入同一预算。热重载会先跑完旧实例的 effect（含生命周期监听）再挂新实例，一次 emit 不会跑两遍。
  - EN: `session_start` / `session_shutdown` now dispatch through the runtime's Cordis event context — `serial` for start and `parallel` for shutdown — while timeouts and errors keep the existing error channel and `handler timed out after Nms` text, and tool-registration barriers count against the same budget. Hot reload drains the old instance's effects (lifecycle listeners included) before the replacement is live, so one emit never runs both.

- **欢迎页也有右侧面板与 rail，会话级表面按可用性禁用**：右侧面板与 44px rail 从聊天场景提升到工作区行，欢迎页与会话共用同一份右列，不再随场景切换卸载；欢迎页打开面板即显示「从这里开始」导航页。文件 / 改动 / 项目知识 / 产物 / 浏览器按所选项目目录可用，上下文 / 轨迹 / 智能体在无会话时保留入口但禁用，并提示先创建会话。会话前的 tab 集以项目为键，创建会话后切换到会话自己的布局。
  - EN: The welcome screen now keeps the right panel and rail. Both were hoisted from the chat scene onto the shared workspace row, so the welcome and chat scenes reuse one right column instead of unmounting it on every swap, and opening the panel on the welcome screen shows the Start here page. Files, changes, project knowledge, artifacts and the browser work against the selected project directory, while context, trajectory and agents stay listed but disabled until a session exists. The pre-session tab set is keyed by project; creating a session switches to that session's own layout.

- **`ctx.hmr` 对插件补齐事务与模块查询，扩展作者技能改讲 Track A**：插件拿到的 `ctx.hmr` 现在与宿主同面——`runExclusive(tx)`、`getLinked(file)`、`getOuterStack(file)`，`on` 也接受 `prepend`。`musepi-extension-dev` 技能与 `docs/skills/authoring-extensions.md` 改为具名导出 + `ctx.*` 入口，并纠正“子模块改动需 touch 入口”的旧说法、补上 `list` 配置与 `musepi.scope: "host"`。
  - EN: Plugins now see the full `ctx.hmr` surface — `runExclusive(tx)`, `getLinked(file)`, `getOuterStack(file)`, and `prepend` on `on`. The `musepi-extension-dev` skill and `docs/skills/authoring-extensions.md` now teach the named Cordis entry and `ctx.*` namespaces, fix the outdated "touch the entry for submodule edits" rule, and cover `list` config fields and `musepi.scope: "host"`.

- **设置控件按语义渲染：预设数值变阶梯滑块，自由数值变滚动数字，数组不再逗号拆分**：设置页不再把所有配置都摊成一个输入框。带 `ui.options` 的数值设置（温度、阈值、列数等 39 项）渲染为刻度阶梯滑块，滚轮、拖动与方向键只能落在声明的预设档位上，当前档位标签常驻，存储值若不在预设中会单独成档而不是被吸附；预设里以 `default` 命名的档位（压缩阈值 / Token 限制）映射到该设置的数字哨兵值 `-1` 并常驻档位，调到别的档后仍能回到默认；无预设的数值设置渲染为滚动数字，支持滚轮/方向键步进、按住拖动改值、点击或直接键入，遵循新增的 `ui.min/max/step`，其余按数值量级推导步长（4 位端口步长 1，6 位超时步长 100）。自由数组不再用逗号拼成一个文本框：字符串数组逐行增删，对象数组（`bash.patterns`、`prompt.sections`，由新增的 `ui.item: "record"` 声明）改用 JSON 列表并在非法 JSON 时内联报错、不提交，含逗号的正则与提示词文本不再被拆坏。所有数组编辑器统一独占整行。
  - EN: Settings controls now follow each setting's semantics instead of rendering every row as one text field. Numbers that declare a preset ladder (temperature, thresholds, output columns — 39 settings) render as a detent slider: wheel, drag and arrow keys land only on declared presets, the active preset's label stays visible, and a stored value outside the ladder keeps its own stop instead of snapping. A preset spelled `default` (compaction thresholds) maps to the setting's numeric sentinel `-1` and stays on the ladder, so the default remains reachable after leaving it. Free numbers render as a rolling counter with wheel/arrow stepping, drag-to-scrub, and click-to-type; they honour the new `ui.min/max/step` hints and otherwise step by one per digit beyond four (a 4-digit port by 1, a 6-digit timeout by 100). Free-form arrays are no longer joined into one comma-separated field: string arrays are edited row by row, and object arrays (`bash.patterns`, `prompt.sections`, declared with the new `ui.item: "record"`) use a JSON list that reports invalid JSON inline without committing — so regexes and prompt text containing commas stay intact. Every array editor now owns a full-width line.

- **配置字段新增 `list` 类型，`root` / `ignored` 改用列表**：清单可声明 `type: "list"` 与 `string[]` 默认值；wire 侧把数组或逗号/换行分隔的字符串统一钳制成 `string[]`，GUI 配置表单以逗号输入编辑。`musepi-hmr` 的 `root`（相对 `base`）与 `ignored` 因此支持多个值，留空仍沿用原生扩展/插件根。
  - EN: Config fields gained a `list` type, and `root` / `ignored` now use it. A manifest may declare `type: "list"` with a `string[]` default; the wire layer coerces arrays or comma/newline separated strings to `string[]`, and the GUI config form edits one comma-separated field. `musepi-hmr`'s `root` (relative to `base`) and `ignored` therefore accept multiple values, while an empty `root` keeps the native extension/plugin roots.

- **会话边缘改为渐进模糊，顶部功能区与羽化不再有色阶；收起终端坞不再留细线**：会话记录上下羽化由不透明色块改为三层递进 `backdrop-filter`(2/6/14px,越靠边越强)+ 同派生表面的低透明度 tint,内容渐进溶解而不是被色块遮住;「对话/地图/信息」头部与开关改用同一套玻璃 token,head 上内边距 8px → 6px,`@supports not` 保留不透明回退。收起态终端坞因 `gui-glass.css` L1 四边 1px border 在 `height:0` 下残留一条线,现由最后一张表的高特异性规则清零四边 border / 背景 / 阴影,展开态玻璃不变。
  - EN: Transcript edge fades are now progressive blur instead of an opaque color slab — three stacked backdrop layers (2/6/14px, masked to shrinking ramps) plus a low-alpha tint of the same derived card surface, so rows dissolve gradually instead of being hidden. The chat head and its 对话/地图/info controls switched to the shared glass tokens (head padding 8px → 6px), removing the color seam; `@supports not` keeps an opaque fallback. The collapsed terminal dock left a 1px hairline at the window bottom because the L1 shorthand re-armed all four borders on a `height: 0` shell; a last-sheet higher-specificity rule now zeroes every border, the fill and the shadow while the open state keeps the full glass.

- **`ctx.hmr` 的事件改走真实 Cordis 事件服务**：`hmr/change` / `hmr/reload` 不再由自建监听表分发，而是注册进 Cordis 的事件 registry，支持 `prepend` 顺序；单监听失败仍被隔离，不会中断同批其他监听。这是事件总线吸收的第一阶段，会话/工具事件的分发模式迁移另列计划。
  - EN: `ctx.hmr` events now go through a real Cordis event service. `hmr/change` / `hmr/reload` are registered in the Cordis registry instead of a private listener table and support `prepend` ordering, while one failing listener stays isolated. This is the first stage of the event-bus absorption; session and tool event dispatch modes are a separate, staged migration.

- **HMR 事件名与配置字段补齐**：`ctx.hmr` 现在发布 `hmr/change` 与 `hmr/reload` 两个事件（`onChange` 保留为别名）；`musepi-hmr` 的配置补齐 `debounce`、`base`、`root`、`ignored`，`root` 留空时仍用原生的扩展/插件根。另确认模块级重载本就成立：Track B 按整张源码图的 mtime 判定并重载属主入口（`legacy-pi-submodule-reload` 覆盖），Track A 走 `ProfileModuleGraph.plan` 的同一套身份比较，缓存击穿由加载标签对整图生效。
  - EN: HMR event names and configuration now match dsh. `ctx.hmr` publishes `hmr/change` and `hmr/reload` (`onChange` remains an alias), and `musepi-hmr` gained `debounce`, `base`, `root`, and `ignored`; an empty `root` keeps the native extension/plugin roots. Module-level reload was already in place: Track B decides from the whole source graph's mtimes and reloads the owning entry (covered by `legacy-pi-submodule-reload`), while Track A uses the same identities through `ProfileModuleGraph.plan`, with the load tag busting the entire graph.

- **补齐 HMR 与插件生命周期的四个缺口**：`ctx.hmr` 新增 `runExclusive`（安装/配置写入与重载批次串行）、`getLinked` / `getOuterStack`（来自 loader 模块图的归属与 importer 链）；`musepi-hmr` 的 `debounceMs` 改由自己的 `musepi.config` 提供，`apply(ctx, config)` 现在真的收到声明字段；`ctx.effect` 放宽到所有插件并由统一的 Extension 账本回收，bundled 宿主装载器 reset、会话 runner 收尾与 cordis fiber 三条路径一致，watcher 不再泄漏。
  - EN: Four HMR and plugin-lifecycle gaps are closed. `ctx.hmr` gained `runExclusive` (installs and config writes serialize against reload batches), plus `getLinked` / `getOuterStack` (entry ownership and importer chains from the loader's module graph). `musepi-hmr` now reads `debounceMs` from its own `musepi.config`, and `apply(ctx, config)` actually receives the declared fields. `ctx.effect` is available to every plugin and drained by one Extension-keyed ledger, so the bundled host loader's reset, the session runner's teardown, and the cordis fiber all reclaim the same way — the watcher no longer leaks.

- **HMR 变成真正的 host 插件，`on()` 返回值可注销监听**：热重载不再是 daemon 私有实现——`musepi-hmr`（`musepi.scope: "host"`）通过 `provide: ["hmr"]` 发布服务，宿主侧经新的宿主服务注册表读回，插件照旧用 `ctx.hmr.onChange`。新增 `host-services` 注册表承载 host 插件发布的对象；`ctx.on` / `pi.on` 现在返回 disposer（Cordis 语义），插件可只撤销单个监听而无需整体卸载。
  - EN: HMR is now a real host plugin, and `on()` returns a disposer. Hot reload is no longer daemon-private: `musepi-hmr` (`musepi.scope: "host"`) publishes the service through `provide: ["hmr"]`, the host reads it back from a new host service registry, and plugins keep using `ctx.hmr.onChange`. The registry also carries any object a host plugin publishes; `ctx.on` / `pi.on` now return a disposer (the Cordis contract), so a plugin can remove one listener without unloading.

- **远程连接新增首次使用免责声明**：首次打开移动端远程控制或实例管理时，先阅读并明确同意远程访问风险，拒绝、关闭或 Escape 都不会进入功能。声明区分完整控制与只读链接、会话内容加密与中继元数据、第三方 Bot 策略及实例 token 的本地存储风险；同意后记住当前声明版本，之后可从实例菜单和两个对话框随时查看，不重复拦截。
  - EN: Remote connections now include a first-use disclaimer. Opening mobile remote control or instance management first requires an explicit acceptance; declining, closing, or pressing Escape does not enter the feature. The text distinguishes full-control from view-only links, content encryption from relay metadata, third-party Bot policies, and localStorage risks for instance tokens. The accepted version is remembered, while the disclaimer remains available from the instance menu and both dialogs without repeated prompts.

- **热重载成为 `ctx.hmr` 服务，daemon 与终端共用一份 watcher**：进程级单一 watcher 按稳定窗口把变更合成一批（`trigger: watch | manual`），插件用 `ctx.hmr.onChange` 订阅、`reload()` 手动触发；宿主各管各的反应——daemon 失效扩展缓存并广播 `extensions.changed`，TUI/ACP/SDK 失效发现缓存。原先写在 daemon 里的 chokidar 监听与监视根解析一并迁入该服务。
  - EN: Hot reload is now the `ctx.hmr` service, and the daemon and terminal share one watcher. A single process-wide watcher batches changes per settle window (`trigger: watch | manual`); plugins subscribe with `ctx.hmr.onChange` and can force a pass with `reload()`. Each host owns its reaction — the daemon drops extension caches and broadcasts `extensions.changed`, while TUI/ACP/SDK drop discovery caches. The daemon's private chokidar watcher and watch-root resolution moved into the service.

- **配色主题自带强调色，一屏只保留一套调色板**：每个配色预设现在提供自己的 `--theme-hue`,外观设置新增「跟随主题」并作为新装默认,品牌 / 单色 / 海洋 / 翡翠 / 自定义色降级为显式覆盖。全局液态玻璃 L1 填充、描边、hover 态与会话记录上下羽化带全部改为从当前主题的 `--bg-*` / `--fg` 派生,会话信息卡并入 L1——修复「品牌黄 + 午夜蓝」时左边栏 / ChatView / 右侧 rail 是默认暖色卡片、会话信息卡与羽化却是蓝色的混搭。定时任务与日历控件里残留的前 rebrand 蓝色改为强调色派生。默认深/浅主题的表面阶梯与 hairline 对比度同步调整。
  - EN: Color themes now ship their own accent and one screen keeps one palette. Every preset supplies a `--theme-hue`; a new "Follow theme" accent (the fresh-install default) resolves the whole `--accent` family from it, while brand / mono / ocean / jade / custom remain explicit overrides. The liquid-glass L1 fills, hairlines, hover states and the transcript fade bands are now derived from the active theme's `--bg-*` / `--fg`, and the session-info card joins L1 — fixing brand-gold + midnight-blue showing default warm cards next to blue info cards and blue fades. Pre-rebrand blue literals in cron/calendar controls became accent mixes, and the default light/dark surface ladder and hairline contrast were tuned.

- **TUI、ACP 与 SDK 也装载宿主级插件**：除 daemon 在开监听前装载外，`createAgentSession` 也会在首个会话前调用同一个 `ensureHostHalfPluginsLoaded`，每个进程一次。宿主级插件注册的后端 provider 因此在终端、ACP、SDK 与桌面端都一致可见，TUI 不再依赖内置回退；宿主装载器重置时会先执行注册 disposer，再允许重新装载。
  - EN: The TUI, ACP, and SDK now mount host-scope plugins too. Besides the daemon mounting them before it listens, `createAgentSession` calls the same `ensureHostHalfPluginsLoaded` before the first session, once per process. Backend providers registered by host-scope packages are therefore visible on the terminal, ACP, SDK, and desktop alike, and the TUI no longer depends on the built-in fallback. Resetting the host loader runs the registration disposers before allowing another mount.

- **项目弹窗统一管理名称与源文件夹，项目目录自动进入新会话**：项目入口统一为“添加项目”，侧栏项目右键的“重命名项目 / 管理工作目录”合并为“编辑项目…”，两者共用同一个弹窗：创建时先打开弹窗、主目录在弹窗内选择，名称、主目录、附加源文件夹一次编辑；“+ 添加目录”走系统目录选择器，重复目录就地提示，缺失目录标红并阻止保存，编辑模式下可暂存主目录切换并在保存时先 `setPrimary` 再 `update`。原“打开文件夹”文案与“新建空白项目”入口（含 `fs.mkdir` 创建流程）已移除，侧栏、欢迎页、顶部菜单与命令面板都走同一创建流程。`session.create` 在调用方未显式传目录时按 cwd 读取项目注册表的 `additionalDirs` 并继承（缺失目录跳过），显式目录仍叠加；会话右键新增“添加并记住到项目…”，把目录同时写入当前会话与项目默认；远程镜像会话不写入本地项目。设置页的 `workspace.additionalDirectories` 仍作为全局默认。
  - EN: A single project dialog now owns the name and source folders, and project directories flow into new sessions. The entry is now Add Project everywhere; the sidebar's separate rename/manage entries became Edit project…, sharing one modal with Add Project: the dialog opens first and the primary folder is picked inside it, then name, primary root and additional source folders are edited together; the old Open Folder label and the New Blank Project entry (including its `fs.mkdir` flow) are gone, so the sidebar, welcome composer, header menu and command palette all use the same create flow; Add directory opens the native picker, duplicates are reported inline, missing directories block saving, and an edit-mode primary swap is staged then committed with `setPrimary` before `update`. `session.create` inherits the project registry's `additionalDirs` for its cwd when the caller passes none (missing directories are skipped) and still merges explicit roots; the session menu adds Add and remember in project…, which writes both the live session and the project defaults; remote mirror sessions stay out of the local project registry. The `workspace.additionalDirectories` setting remains the global default.

- **多根工作区的改动面板与会话目录状态**：右侧“改动”新增根目录选择器，可在会话的主目录与附加目录之间切换，并显示文件数；列表/树形视图由 Git 设置统一驱动、双向实时同步，树形按完整路径递归展开，status/diff/stage/commit 都跟随当前根。`git.status` 的 `numstat` 现在返回按 staged/working 拆分的 `fileStats`，每个文件行显示自己的 `+n −m`（二进制文件跳过）。会话信息卡新增“项目目录”区，逐目录显示分支、文件数、±，非 Git 目录明确标注，点击直接切到对应根的改动面板；多目录状态不再定时轮询：daemon 按连接引用计数监听 `.git`（HEAD/index/refs）并推送 `git.statusChanged`，客户端在推送、挂载、窗口重新聚焦/可见、一轮工作结束和 Git mutation 失效时刷新，15 秒 TTL 缓存与单飞请求兜底，最多四路并发。新增 `git-prefs`、`git-status-cache` 与 `git-root` 三个共享模块。
  - EN: The right-pane changes view now has a workspace-root selector across the session's primary and additional directories, with a file count. The flat/tree preference is owned by the Git settings and stays in sync both ways; the tree recurses over full paths, and status/diff/stage/commit follow the active root. `git.status` with `numstat` now returns `fileStats` split by staged/working, so each file row shows its own `+n −m` (binary files skipped). The session info card gains a Project directories section with each source's branch, file count, ±, or an explicit Not-a-Git-directory state; clicking a row focuses that root. Additional-root status is event-driven, not timer-driven: the daemon refcounts per-connection roots, watches `.git` (HEAD/index/refs) and pushes `git.statusChanged`; clients refresh on that push, mount, window focus/visibility, a finished turn and local Git mutation invalidation, with a 15s TTL cache, single-flight requests and four-way concurrency. `git-prefs`, `git-status-cache` and `git-root` are the shared modules.

- **跨根 @ 补全、全局目录编辑器与附加目录安全护栏**：`@` 文件补全合并主目录与会话/项目附加目录，附加目录条目以绝对路径插入并标注根名，避免相对路径落到主目录；欢迎页也会读取项目已记住的目录。设置页的全局默认目录从逗号输入框改为目录列表选择器。附加目录拒绝 home 与文件系统根（realpath + Windows 大小写不敏感比较），`session.create` 会跳过历史遗留的不安全项目目录而不是阻塞创建；项目主目录缺失会在编辑弹窗标红，隐式项目转为托管后直接打开编辑弹窗。
  - EN: `@` completion now merges the primary root with the session/project additional directories; entries from additional roots insert as absolute paths and carry a root badge so a relative path cannot resolve against the primary cwd, and the welcome composer reads the project's remembered roots too. The global default directories setting is a directory list picker instead of a comma input. Additional roots reject the home directory and filesystem roots (realpath, case-insensitive on Windows); `session.create` skips a stale unsafe project directory instead of blocking creation, a missing primary root is flagged in the project dialog, and promoting an implicit project opens the editor directly.

- **会话行补齐 Emoji、重新加载与双击重命名**：会话右键菜单新增“设置 Emoji…”（本地持久化，显示在标题前，可清空）和“重新加载”。`session.reload` 对齐 Kimi 的 `/reload`：空闲会话重读全局/项目配置（同时清 capability 与文件内容缓存）、重新发现并注入 AGENTS.md、重载已加载扩展、重读会话文件、重新发现技能并重跑 `session_start`；忙碌时拒绝，已冷却的历史会话会先激活再重载，`session_before_switch` 取消时明确失败。重载会先把项目注册表新增的附加目录补进会话（只增不删，避免误删会话级目录）。重读后对比 AGENTS.md、技能目录与目录集的快照，只对真实变化发一条隐藏提醒，无变化不发；扩展重载失败时提示为警告而不是成功。双击会话标题进入内联编辑，Enter/失焦保存、Esc 取消；侧栏、项目、分组列表共用同一个行组件。
  - EN: Session rows gain emoji, reload and double-click rename. The context menu adds Set emoji… (persisted locally, shown before the title, clearable) and Reload. `session.reload` now matches Kimi's `/reload`: an idle session re-reads global/project config (clearing both the capability and file-content caches), rediscovers and injects AGENTS.md, reloads loaded extensions, re-reads the session file, rediscovers skills and re-runs `session_start`; a busy session is rejected, a cold history session is activated first, and a cancelled `session_before_switch` fails explicitly. Reload first merges directories newly added to the project registry into the session (add-only, so session-scoped directories are never dropped). It then diffs snapshots of AGENTS.md, the skill catalog and the directory set and sends one hidden reminder only for real changes, staying silent otherwise; extension reload failures surface as a warning instead of a success. Double-clicking the title opens inline editing with Enter/blur to save and Esc to cancel; the sidebar, project and group lists share the same row component.

- **宿主级插件在 daemon 启动时装载**：清单新增 `musepi.scope: "host"`，这类内置包在开监听前、首个会话之前挂载一次，会话装载跳过它们；`discoverBundledPluginPaths` 按 `session` / `host` / `all` 三种视图发现。首个消费者是 `memory-sharpshooter`：它把 Sharpshooter 引擎注册为记忆后端 provider，核心仍保留内置回退，TUI 行为不变。
  - EN: Host-scope plugins now mount when the daemon starts. A manifest can declare `musepi.scope: "host"`; such packages load once before listening and before the first session, and session loading skips them. `discoverBundledPluginPaths` exposes `session` / `host` / `all` views. The first consumer is `memory-sharpshooter`, which registers the Sharpshooter engine as a memory backend provider; the core keeps the built-in fallback, so terminal behavior is unchanged.

- **深浅主题的卡片边框与菜单 hover 更清晰，并统一液态玻璃规范**：新增全局三层玻璃阶梯——卡片、浮层、交互态各有独立的填充、描边、镜面 rim 与阴影；深色用白色 hairline、浅色用中性暗 hairline，两种主题下圆角聊天卡与侧栏卡的边界都能看清。悬浮菜单原先的 hover 背景与卡片底色相同，现在改为明显提亮/压暗并带内描边，选中态与键盘焦点环同步补齐；菜单弹层外框也不再是深色下看不见的黑边。卡片获得顶部高光、底部暗回声与对角 sheen，减少动效和高对比度偏好都保留。
  - EN: Card edges and menu hover states are clearer in both themes, backed by one global liquid-glass spec. A three-layer ladder gives cards, floating layers and interaction states their own fill, hairline, specular rim and shadow: dark uses white hairlines, light uses neutral dark ones, so the rounded ChatView and sidebar cards have visible borders in both schemes. The context-menu hover used the card's own surface color; it now lifts with a clearly brighter/darker fill plus an inset hairline, with matching active and keyboard-focus states. The popup outline is no longer an invisible black edge on dark. Cards gain a top highlight, bottom echo and diagonal sheen; reduced-motion and high-contrast preferences stay respected.

- **记忆后端由注册表驱动，插件可以新增或替换**：`MemoryBackendProvider` 增加 `label` / `description`；`memory.backend` 的枚举值合并已注册 provider，设置页把 provider 的显示名与说明追加为选项，TUI 与 GUI 都能直接选择插件贡献的后端。插件卸载后选项消失，仍指向该 id 的配置回退到 `off`；替换内置 id 依旧生效。未知后端的设置状态卡显示 id，不再误标为 Off。
  - EN: Memory backends are now registry-driven, and a plugin can add or replace one. `MemoryBackendProvider` gained `label` / `description`; `memory.backend` merges registered provider ids into its enum, the settings picker appends each provider's own label and description, and both TUI and GUI can select a contributed backend. Unloading the plugin removes the option and a config still pointing at the id falls back to `off`; replacing a built-in id keeps working. The status card shows a contributed backend's id instead of labelling it Off.

- **第一方插件入口有一致性测试守卫，并修复两个不合规入口**：`design-guidance` 在迁移时丢掉了具名的 `apply` 导出，插件加载直接失败、设计简报整体消失；现已改回标准入口，`product-skills` 也补上 `name` / `inject`。coding-agent 与 desktop 各新增一处一致性测试，校验每个插件包都有 `name` / `inject` / `apply`、没有 default 导出、不使用平铺 `pi.register*`，且清单里的入口文件真实存在。
  - EN: A conformance test now guards first-party plugin entries, and two non-conforming entries are fixed. `design-guidance` lost its named `apply` export during the migration, so the plugin failed to load and the design briefs disappeared; it is back on the standard entry, and `product-skills` gained `name` / `inject`. New conformance tests in coding-agent and desktop assert that every plugin package exports `name` / `inject` / `apply`, has no default export, never uses a flat `pi.register*` alias, and keeps manifest entries pointing at real files.

- **`session_start` 告诉插件它在为哪个 agent 工作**：事件新增可选 `{ agentId, source }`，`source` 区分主 agent 与 `task` 子 agent。每个 session-bound agent 本来就各有一份 runner，`apply` 即该作用域的 setup、`session_shutdown` 即回收；插件不必再从 `agentId` 猜测身份。
  - EN: `session_start` now tells a plugin which agent it serves. The event carries optional `{ agentId, source }`, where `source` distinguishes the primary agent from a `task` subagent. Each session-bound agent already gets its own runner, so `apply` is that scope's setup and `session_shutdown` is its teardown; plugins no longer have to infer the role from `agentId`.

- **侧栏恢复折叠动效，改成等高圆角卡片，并补上「开始使用」引导**：二级左栏现在与聊天区使用同一张卡片语言（相同外距、圆角、阴影与高度），折叠按 220ms 弹簧曲线收放、悬浮面板按 140ms 淡入淡出，不再瞬间闪现或消失。首页面板底部新增「开始使用」引导卡：可折叠、可隐藏、可重置，进度条随访问过的入口推进，四行分别打开设置、供应商、会话导入与任务中心；折叠与进度在重启后保留。
  - EN: The sidebar has its fold animation back, now matches the chat card geometry, and gains a Getting started guide. Both columns use one card language (same gutter, radius, shadow and height); folding runs on a 220ms spring curve and the floating panel fades/slides over 140ms instead of snapping. A Getting started card sits at the bottom of the Home sidebar: collapsible, hideable and resettable, with a progress bar that advances as its four rows open settings, providers, session import and the task center. Collapse state and progress survive a restart.

- **浏览器工具同时给 Agent 和用户完整结构化信息**：新增 `status`、`read`、`screenshot` 动作，保留既有 `open`/`run`/`close` 与标签所有权。Agent 可读取真实受控/可见标签、导航状态、按范围和游标分页的页面文字及截图元数据；静态提示词同步指导状态发现、文本续读、元素观察和视觉截图。用户侧新增对应专用卡片，原始 JSON 折叠保留。
  - EN: Browser tools now provide complete structured information to both the Agent and the user. New `status`, `read`, and `screenshot` actions preserve existing `open`/`run`/`close` behavior and tab ownership. The Agent can read real controlled/visible tabs, navigation state, scope- and cursor-bounded page text, and screenshot metadata; the static prompt now guides state discovery, text continuation, element observation, and visual capture separately. Dedicated user cards present the same results, with raw JSON retained behind a disclosure.

- **整轮浏览器来源卡正确区分已读与经过**：来源卡现在把结构化 `read` 和旧 `observe` 计为已读，把 `open`/`goto` 计为经过；重复 URL 去重并累计打开次数，截图、点击和状态查询不再被误算成经过页面。
  - EN: The turn-level browser Sources card now distinguishes read pages from pages passed through. Structured `read` and legacy `observe` count as reads, while `open`/`goto` counts visits. Repeated URLs are deduplicated with their open counts preserved, and screenshots, clicks, and status queries no longer invent visited pages.

- **浏览器元素与视觉快照可直接驱动操作**：新增 `elements`/`visual` 快照及 ref、坐标动作，支持点击、填写、输入、按键、悬停、滚动、拖拽、选项、勾选和滚动到元素。快照在标签内有效 60 秒，提示词同步说明续读与重拍规则；用户侧展示元素、视觉和成功/失败回执卡，过期引用不会盲点。
  - EN: Browser element and visual snapshots now drive explicit actions. New `elements`/`visual` snapshots support ref and coordinate actions for click, fill, type, press, hover, scroll, drag, select_option, set_checked, and scroll_into_view. Snapshots are tab-scoped for 60 seconds, and the prompt explains continuation and recapture rules. Dedicated cards show elements, visual metadata, and success/failure receipts; stale refs never click blindly.

- **浏览器对话框可读取并由 Agent 应答，标签控制不再混淆释放与关闭**：新增 `dialog`/`dialog_respond` 与 pending/handled 结构化记录，支持 alert、confirm、prompt 及自动策略处理方记录；弹窗打开时当前操作及时返回，不再被请求拦截清理或标题读取卡住。新增 `activate`/`switch`/`release`，分别选择控制目标、显示真实标签、释放控制且保留页面。提示词指导 Agent 检查后应答并重新读取页面，用户侧同步展示对话框与控制卡片。
  - EN: Browser dialogs can now be inspected and answered by the Agent. New `dialog`/`dialog_respond` actions and pending/handled records cover alert, confirm, prompt, and auto-policy attribution. An open dialog returns the current operation promptly instead of hanging in request-interception cleanup or title reads. New `activate`/`switch`/`release` actions select a control target, reveal its real tab, and detach without closing the page. The prompt teaches inspection, response, and page re-reading, while dedicated cards expose dialog and control outcomes to the user.

- **浏览器支持原图裁剪、连续路径拖拽与设备配置**：`visual_crop` 从视觉快照的原始图像裁出区域，返回来源快照、捕获时间和真实 PNG；`drag_path` 支持 2–500 个点、按钮、总时长和修饰键，可绘制曲线或签名；`device_profiles`/`set_device` 提供手机、平板、桌面及 responsive 视口，区分移动身份、触摸与显示比例，禁用后清除模拟。提示词和用户卡片同步展示这些结果。
  - EN: The browser now supports original-image crops, continuous drag paths, and device configuration. `visual_crop` reads a region from the original visual capture and returns its source snapshot, capture time, and real PNG bytes. `drag_path` supports 2–500 points, button, total duration, and modifiers for curved strokes or signatures. `device_profiles`/`set_device` cover phone, tablet, desktop, and responsive viewports while distinguishing mobile identity, touch, and display scale; disabling clears emulation. The prompt and user cards expose the same results.

- **内置浏览器历史与下载记录可跨会话读取**：Electron 主进程按 Profile 持久化导航和下载记录，历史最多 200 条、下载最多 100 条，最新优先。`history`/`downloads` 工具与卡片展示真实 URL、访问时间、下载状态、字节进度和可选保存路径；非受管后端会明确报错，保存路径不代表文件仍存在。
  - EN: Built-in browser history and downloads are now retained across conversations. The Electron main process persists navigation and download records per profile, newest first, capped at 200 history entries and 100 downloads. The `history`/`downloads` actions and cards expose real URLs, visit times, download status, byte progress, and optional save paths; unsupported backends fail explicitly, and a recorded path does not guarantee the file still exists.

- **浏览器导航与条件等待具备真实生命周期和证据**：新增 `navigate`/`search`/`back`/`forward`/`reload`/`stop`/`wait_load`，以及支持 element/text/URL 的 `wait_for`。搜索使用配置的搜索引擎，加载状态已满足时立即返回；条件等待支持 stableForMs，匹配和超时都返回 lastObserved，超时同时标为错误。用户侧同步展示导航与等待结果卡。
  - EN: Browser navigation and condition waiting now expose real lifecycle behavior and evidence. New `navigate`/`search`/`back`/`forward`/`reload`/`stop`/`wait_load` actions are joined by `wait_for` for element, text, and URL conditions. Search uses the configured engine, satisfied load states return immediately, stableForMs is honored, and both match and timeout return lastObserved; timeout is also an error result. Navigation and wait cards expose the same outcomes to users.

- **记忆工作区浏览跟随后端，而不是固定读本地目录**：`MemoryBackend` 新增可选 `workspaceDir(agentDir, cwd)`；`memory.workspace*` 先解析活动后端，未实现该能力时返回 `supported: false` 并拒绝读取/定位，只有返回目录的后端才拥有设置页文件浏览。`memory.*` 继续作为核心契约，替换 provider 即整体切换。
  - EN: The memory workspace browser now follows the backend instead of always reading a local directory. `MemoryBackend` gained an optional `workspaceDir(agentDir, cwd)`; `memory.workspace*` resolves the active backend first, answers `supported: false` and refuses reads or reveals when the capability is absent, and only a backend that returns a directory owns the settings file browser. `memory.*` stays a core contract, so replacing the provider switches the whole surface.

- **内置命令名可由第一方插件接管**：`/memory` 这类保留名以前对扩展一律跳过；现在随宿主分发的插件（`bundled-plugins/`、`desktop-plugins/` 与仓库内插件包）注册同名命令即接管，内建实现退化为插件缺席时的回退，命令补全与分发优先走插件；第三方插件仍保留冲突告警。
  - EN: First-party plugins can now own a built-in command name. Reserved names such as `/memory` used to be skipped for every extension; a plugin shipped with the host (`bundled-plugins/`, `desktop-plugins/`, and in-repo plugin packages) now takes the name over, the built-in becomes the fallback for when the plugin is absent, and completion and dispatch prefer the plugin. Installed third-party plugins still get the collision warning.

- **记忆设置页由 Cordis 插件提供，并补上后端替换 seam**：设置 → 记忆现在是一个第一方 Cordis 页面，后端配置优先展示、工作区记忆仅在本地下显示、状态卡使用本地化后端说明与状态标签，维护操作重新分组；插件缺失或编译失败时仍回退内置页面。新增 `MemoryBackendProvider`，插件可替换任一记忆后端，卸载时自动恢复内置实现；扩展已注册同名记忆工具时，核心不再重复创建。
  - EN: The memory settings page is now provided by a first-party Cordis plugin, and a backend replacement seam is available. Settings → Memory puts backend configuration first, shows the workspace browser only for the local backend, renders localized backend hints and status chips, and regroups maintenance actions; the built-in page remains the fallback when the plugin is missing or fails to compile. A new `MemoryBackendProvider` lets a plugin replace any memory backend and restores the built-in implementation on unload; when an extension registers a memory tool with the same name, the core no longer creates a duplicate.

- **应用内通知收敛为一套共享契约**：桌面右下角与 guest/mobile 底部条带现在读写同一个 store；daemon 的 `notice`、自动重试与自动压缩会出现在通知栈里，不再被桌面端丢弃。两条以上通知折叠成细条，悬停或键盘聚焦展开；用户主动关闭会播 180ms 退场，关闭钮带随剩余时长走完的倒计时环（悬停冻结），并支持先取消再关闭的次要按钮。
  - EN: In-app notifications now follow one shared contract. The desktop corner and the guest/mobile bottom strip read and write the same store; daemon `notice`, auto-retry, and auto-compaction events now appear in the stack instead of being dropped by the desktop. Two or more toasts collapse into slivers and expand on hover or keyboard focus; user dismissals play a 180 ms exit, the close affordance carries a countdown ring that freezes on hover, and an optional cancel button runs before dismissal.

- **结算卡解析 JSON，回合导航的悬浮与跳转也不再打架**：`async-job-result` 的输出若是 JSON 对象，现在按字段渲染（`notes` 这类字符串值里的 `\n` 变成真实换行，不再是转义字面量），非 JSON 仍按等宽正文；展开/收起改用与 IRC 卡相同的 collapse morph。TurnRail 方面修复三处交互：悬浮面板的指针移动不再冒泡到刻度条（之前会把高亮改到别的轮次并误触边缘翻页）；指针重新进入 rail 会取消隐藏计时（面板与刻度条之间的 6px 空隙另有透明桥接）；点击刻度或面板行跳转后立即收起列表，指针离开 rail 前不再自动弹开，非主键点击不跳转。
  - EN: Settlement cards now parse JSON, and the turn rail's hover/jump interactions no longer fight each other. When an `async-job-result` output is a JSON object it renders as fields (string values such as `notes` keep real line breaks instead of literal `\n`); anything else stays monospace text, and expansion uses the same collapse morph as the IRC card. The TurnRail fixes three interaction defects: pointer moves inside the hover panel no longer bubble to the tick strip (which used to move the highlight to another turn and trigger the edge carousel), re-entering the rail cancels a pending hide (a transparent element also bridges the 6px gap to the panel), and a jump from a tick or a panel row dismisses the list until the pointer leaves the rail — non-primary clicks never jump.

- **二级左栏有了统一规范，悬停预览只认对应的目的地**：rail 与侧栏之间的面板现在共用一套布局语言（主操作、搜索/过滤一行、扁平分组、整宽 hover 行、统一空态），不再套卡片和行分隔线。收起侧栏后，只有鼠标悬停到「拥有二级面板」的 rail 按钮上才浮出对应面板：首页浮出会话列表、任务中心浮出任务列表，rail 空白处与其它目的地不再误弹首页侧栏。任务面板同时改由 Cordis 插件提供，插件未加载或编译失败时自动回退内置面板，不影响任务中心使用。
  - EN: The second-tier sidebar now follows one layout spec, and hover peek only responds to the matching destination. The panel shares a single surface language (one primary action, one compact search/filter row, flat groups, full-width hover rows, one empty state) instead of nested cards and row borders. With the sidebar folded, only a rail button that owns a panel floats that panel — Home shows the session list and Scheduled shows the task list; the empty rail band and other destinations no longer pop up the Home sidebar. The task panel is now contributed by a Cordis plugin, with the built-in panel as the fallback when the plugin is missing or fails to compile.

- **任务中心有了独立的左侧任务面板**：任务列表从页面内部移到左 rail 右边的侧栏槽，空任务时面板依然可见，可直接搜索、按状态筛选、新建任务并选中查看详情；面板可随侧栏一起收起，设置打开时由设置导航临时接管槽位。页面与面板共用一份定时任务数据，切换不会重复请求，也不会丢失选中状态。
  - EN: The task center now has its own left panel. The task list has moved out of the page and into the sidebar slot beside the left rail; it stays visible even with no tasks and supports search, status filtering, creating tasks and selecting one for its detail view. The panel folds with the sidebar, and Settings temporarily takes over the slot. Page and panel share one cron data snapshot, so switching views issues no duplicate requests and keeps the selection.

- **内置浏览器支持真实的解压扩展管理**：受管浏览器扩展菜单现在列出当前分区实际安装的扩展，可显式选择解压目录、确认 manifest 权限后安装，并按 id 卸载；源目录不会被删除。扩展目录在重启后重新加载，失败会显示真实错误，外部 Browser Relay 仍只在「外部浏览器集成」设置中管理。
  - EN: The managed browser now supports real unpacked-extension management. Its Extensions menu lists only extensions actually loaded in the managed partition, lets users explicitly choose an unpacked directory, confirm manifest permissions before installation, and uninstall by id without deleting source files. Recorded directories reload on restart and failures remain visible. External Browser Relay remains exclusively under External browser integrations.

- **IRC 卡展开有了过渡，widget 卡也不再留空白**：IRC 正文原先用 `max-height` 瞬切，现在复用共享 collapse morph（220ms `--spring`）——展开时先摘下 clamp，收起动画结束后再挂回，高度平滑增减。截图里的空白来自 transcript 行的 `content-visibility: auto`：含 widget iframe 的行被 Chromium 跳过布局时按 60px 内在高度计高（实测 686px 的卡只量到 72px），虚拟列表据此排版，导致相邻行重叠、尾部出现空白；现在行高只由虚拟列表管理。widget 页面的高度探针也按 openchamber 对齐：root 只在内容溢出视口时采用，否则取 body 内容范围（拉伸的 `body{height:100%}` 也能缩回）；贴底滚动容器会撑高 frame 而不是内部滚动；只有宿主封顶 frame 时页面才拥有自己的滚动条与滚轮。
  - EN: IRC cards now animate their expansion, and widget cards no longer leave blank space. The IRC body used to toggle `max-height` instantly; it now uses the shared collapse morph (220ms `--spring`), dropping the clamp before expanding and re-applying it once the collapse settles. The blank area came from `content-visibility: auto` on transcript rows: when Chromium skipped the layout of a row containing a widget iframe, the row was sized from its 60px intrinsic height (a 686px card measured 72px), so the virtualizer laid the transcript out against a collapsed row — neighbours overlapped and the tail went blank. Row height is now owned by the virtualizer alone. Widget faces use a content-height probe aligned with openchamber: the root document is used only when it overflows the viewport, otherwise the body's content extent (so a stretched `body{height:100%}` shrinks); a scroller pinned to the frame's bottom edge grows the frame instead of scrolling internally; and the page owns its scrollbar and the wheel only when the host caps the frame.

- **共享模式滑块在缩窗后不再越过分栏**：分段控件的最小宽度此前会把拉伸后的分段宽度再次计入，导致窗口变窄时旧宽度无法回落。现在只按标签、padding、图标和间距计算内容所需宽度，远程控制弹窗及其它使用同一控件的界面都能随父列正常缩放，长标签仍不会被裁掉。
  - EN: The sharing-mode thumb no longer crosses column boundaries after resizing. The segmented control previously fed stretched segment widths back into its minimum, preventing it from shrinking with the window. It now measures only the label, padding, icon, and gap, so the remote-control dialog and every other use of the shared control can shrink with their parent without clipping long labels.

- **内置浏览器不再误展示外部扩展**：Browser Relay 与共享 Chromium 扩展属于外部浏览器集成，现已从受管浏览器工具栏移除，只在设置 → 外部浏览器集成中管理；无效的扩展按钮显隐设置一并删除。
  - EN: The managed browser no longer shows external extensions. Browser Relay and shared-Chromium extensions belong to external browser integrations, so they have been removed from the managed toolbar and are managed only under Settings → External browser integrations. The ineffective extensions-toolbar preference has been removed too.

- **窄浏览器面板保留可用的地址栏**：面板不超过 480px 时，标注、开发者工具和新建标签改从「更多操作」进入，不再让重复的按钮把地址输入压到十几像素；这些能力仍可使用。
  - EN: Narrow browser panels keep a usable address bar. At 480px or less, annotation, developer tools, and new-tab move into More actions instead of duplicate toolbar buttons squeezing the address field to a few pixels; every action remains available.

- **GUI 崩溃后重启不再卡在「正在启动本地 daemon」**：被强杀（或崩溃）的 GUI 留下的 daemon 现在按完整终止阶梯回收，而不是只发一个 SIGTERM——Windows 上那只结束根进程，子进程仍持有继承来的监听 socket，端口不释放，新 daemon 随即 EADDRINUSE，界面停在启动阶段。回收成功后才丢弃注册表记录；回收失败则保留记录供下次重试。被正常终止的 daemon 也同步注销自己的记录，重启不会再留下指向死进程的条目。
  - EN: A GUI that crashed or was force-killed no longer strands the next start on "starting local daemon". The orphaned daemon is now reclaimed through the full termination ladder instead of a bare SIGTERM, which on Windows ends only the root: a child keeps the inherited listening socket, the port never frees, the replacement exits with EADDRINUSE, and the window hangs in startup. The registry record is dropped only after the reap actually succeeds, and kept for a retry when it does not. Daemons stopped normally now unregister themselves too, so restarts no longer leave records pointing at dead processes.

## [0.7.2] - 2026-11-10

- **展开态旋转后右侧代理栏不再压住消息**：宽屏会自动展开子代理栏，窗口旋转或折叠变窄时该栏变为带遮罩的浮层，但 `railOpen` 没有随之收回，灰色遮罩盖住整个消息区，正文还在浮层边缘被硬裁断。现在只收回「自动展开」的那一次（用户手动打开的不动），回到宽屏再自动展开。真机连续两次 90° 旋转复测：无遮罩、消息完整换行、WebSocket 不断、Ability 保持前台。
  - EN: The agents rail no longer covers the transcript after rotating an unfolded screen. Wide layouts auto-reveal it, but when rotation or folding narrows the window the rail becomes a backdrop overlay while `railOpen` stayed true, hiding the transcript behind a grey scrim and clipping text at the overlay edge. Only the auto-revealed instance is retracted (a rail the user opened explicitly stays open), and returning to a wide layout reveals it again. Verified on the device across two consecutive 90-degree rotations: no scrim, text wraps cleanly, the WebSocket stays open, and the ability stays FOREGROUND.

- **鸿蒙端可以连上局域网主机了**：为了绕开 `resource://rawfile/` 的跨域限制，页面跑在私有 `https://musepi.local`；这使所有 `ws://` 协作链接被 ArkWeb 判为混合内容直接拦截（"An insecure WebSocket connection may not be initiated from a page loaded over HTTPS"），点「连接」没有任何反应。现在给 Web 组件设 `mixedMode(MixedMode.All)`：页面内容仍只来自本地 rawfile，放行的只是用户自己电脑上的明文 WebSocket，等价于 Android 的 `MIXED_CONTENT_ALWAYS_ALLOW`。模拟器实测 host 侧收到 `guest joined`、会话页正常渲染，旋转 90° 后窗口仍在前台且连接不断。另外 ArkWeb 不对滚动内容合成 `backdrop-filter`，半透明顶栏会透出被裁断的消息气泡；鸿蒙壳改用实心顶栏，与系统导航栏一致。
  - EN: The HarmonyOS build can now reach LAN hosts. The page is served from a private https origin to work around CORS on `resource://rawfile/`, which made every `ws://` collab link a mixed-content request ArkWeb blocked outright ("An insecure WebSocket connection may not be initiated from a page loaded over HTTPS") — tapping Connect did nothing. The Web component now uses `mixedMode(MixedMode.All)`: the page still comes only from the local rawfile bundle, and the only insecure traffic allowed is the plaintext WebSocket to the user's own computer, the equivalent of Android's `MIXED_CONTENT_ALWAYS_ALLOW`. Verified on the emulator: the host logged `guest joined`, the session page rendered, and after a 90-degree rotation the window stayed in the foreground with the connection intact. ArkWeb also does not composite `backdrop-filter` over scrolling content, so a translucent header leaked clipped message bubbles; the HarmonyOS shell now uses a solid header that matches the system navigation bars.

- **鸿蒙端支持旋转与实况窗**：旋转改用官方推荐的单一策略 `auto_rotation_unspecified`——不再按设备类型写死方向，窗口跟随传感器并尊重系统旋转锁，布局交给已有的宽/高断点（展开态 939×664 与横屏 664×939 都走同一套 `lg` 规则），旋转时不重建 WebView、不丢会话状态；横屏矮窗（672×459）单独压缩垂直节奏，三张方法卡也放得下。新增实况窗（Live View）适配：会话运行中在状态栏胶囊/通知中心显示「会话标题 · 运行中」，结束即撤下。该能力有两道闸门——AGC 必须开通实况窗权益，且 `event` 需为审核通过的场景值——任一道未开时全部调用静默降级，行为与未接入完全一致。沉浸光感材质同时补了 API 版本守卫：安装下限仍是 API 12，低于 26 的设备直接跳过 `ImmersiveMaterial`，避免旧机在底栏处崩溃。
  - EN: Rotation and Live View on HarmonyOS. Rotation now uses the single official strategy `auto_rotation_unspecified` instead of per-device orientation rules: the window follows the sensor and the user's rotation lock, and layout is left to the existing width/height breakpoints (unfolded 939×664 and landscape 664×939 share the same `lg` rules), without recreating the WebView or dropping session state, and the short landscape window (672×459) compresses its vertical rhythm so even three method cards fit. Live View shows 会话标题 · 运行中 in the status-bar capsule and notification centre while a turn runs, and clears when it ends. It is gated twice — the Live View entitlement must be opened in AGC and `event` must be an approved scenario — and every call degrades silently when either gate is closed, leaving behaviour identical to not having it. The immersive material also gained an API-level guard: the install floor stays API 12, so devices below 26 skip `ImmersiveMaterial` instead of risking a crash at the bar.

- **鸿蒙端底部页签改用系统沉浸光感材质**：移动端此前没有底部导航，设置只能从会话里进；现在「连接 / 会话 / 设置」常驻底栏，鸿蒙壳用原生 `BottomTabBarStyle` + `barFloatingStyle` 承载 `ImmersiveMaterial`，按压弹性形变与光随指动都交给系统，材质按「精美 / 均衡 / 轻柔 / 极薄」四档递进可调，并写入 `ohos.arkui.UIMaterial.state` 与 `targetSdkVersion 26`（安装下限仍是 API 12，旧设备回落到普通底栏）。图标与文字读同一个 `activeTab`，切换不会再出现「文字在原页签、图标在新页签」；扫码等全屏浮层会临时隐藏底栏。远程网页按 `isMobileShell()` 判定不渲染底栏，桌面浏览器保持原布局。
  - EN: The HarmonyOS build's bottom tab bar now uses the system immersive material. Mobile had no bottom navigation and Settings was only reachable from inside a session; 连接 / 会话 / 设置 are now persistent, and the HarmonyOS shell renders them with the native `BottomTabBarStyle` plus `barFloatingStyle` carrying an `ImmersiveMaterial`, so press deformation and the light that follows the finger come from the system. Four material thicknesses (rich / balanced / soft / ultra-thin) are selectable and persisted, enabled through `ohos.arkui.UIMaterial.state` and `targetSdkVersion 26` (the install floor stays API 12, where the bar falls back to a plain one). Icon and label read the same `activeTab`, so a tab can no longer show its label on one destination and its icon on another, and full-screen overlays such as the QR scanner hide the bar while open. The remote web build skips the bar entirely via `isMobileShell()`, keeping the desktop layout unchanged.
- **连接页配对改原生半模态，并修掉折叠态滚动与双指缩放**：点「使用配对码 / 远程访问链接」原先在卡片内联展开，整页高度瞬间重排；鸿蒙壳改为原生 `bindSheet`（底部升起、系统标题槽、品牌金主按钮、`stateStyles` 按压反馈），提交后经 `runJavaScript` 回传 `__musepiPair` / `__musepiConnectLink`，桌面网页与 Android 保留内联表单。同时修掉三处真问题：`@media (max-width:640px)` 把连接卡片改成 `max-height:none; overflow:visible` 导致折叠态「内容全显仍可滚动」；viewport 缺 `user-scalable=no` 且 ArkWeb 未关手势，双指可缩放整个界面；品牌区是「纯色方块 + 方块外 π 字符」两段拼接，已换成 S5 球脸单图并同步 favicon、`entry` 与 `AppScope` 启动图标。展开态连接卡片居中加宽到 860vp、方法卡三列排布。
  - EN: The pairing flow is now a native sheet, and the folded-state scroll plus pinch zoom are fixed. Tapping 使用配对码 / 远程访问链接 used to expand inline inside the card and re-flow the whole page; the HarmonyOS shell now uses a native `bindSheet` (rises from the bottom, system title slot, brand-gold primary action, `stateStyles` press feedback) and pushes the values back through `__musepiPair` / `__musepiConnectLink` over `runJavaScript`, while the desktop web and Android builds keep the inline form. Three real defects went with it: `@media (max-width:640px)` turned the connect card into `max-height:none; overflow:visible`, so the folded layout could still scroll with all content visible; the viewport lacked `user-scalable=no` and ArkWeb never disabled its pinch gesture, so two fingers zoomed the whole UI; and the brand lockup was a solid square plus a π glyph outside it, now a single S5 orb mark with favicon, `entry` and `AppScope` launcher icons aligned. The unfolded connect card is centred at 860vp with the methods in three columns.
- **增强启动页让开屏不再是纯色板**：`module.json5` 增加 `startWindow` 指向 `resources/base/profile/start_window.json`（API 19+ 增强启动页），品牌图标按系统 128/192/256vp 缩放居中、背景色跟随应用深浅色（`startWindowColorModeType: FOLLOW_APPLICATION`），简易启动页保留为低版本回落。设置页同时改成鸿蒙列表语言：分组标题在卡片外、行高 56vp、0.5px 分隔线、控件右对齐。
  - EN: The launch plate is no longer a bare colour field. `module.json5` now points `startWindow` at `resources/base/profile/start_window.json` (the API 19+ enhanced start window), so the brand icon is drawn at a system-scaled 128/192/256vp and the plate follows the app's light or dark preference (`startWindowColorModeType: FOLLOW_APPLICATION`), with the simple start window kept as the pre-19 fallback. Settings also adopts the HarmonyOS list language: group titles outside the card, 56vp rows, hairline separators, controls right-aligned.

- **浏览器扩展、Chrome 导入与真实 Chrome 中继有了统一入口**：受管浏览器工具栏新增可隐藏的扩展按钮，弹层内可直接查看 Chrome 导入、MusePi Browser Relay 和共享 Chromium 扩展；设置 → 浏览器拆成常规 / 扩展 / 数据三视图，并提供完整网址显示开关。导入流程现在有等待、成功与失败反馈，明确只把默认 Chrome Profile 的 Cookie 和 LocalStorage 复制到共享自动化 Chromium，不宣称支持密码、受管分区导入或持续同步；标签、Agent 控制和真实 Chrome Relay 仍沿用原有实现。
  - EN: Browser extensions, Chrome import, and the real-Chrome relay now have a unified entry point. An optional Extensions button in the managed-browser toolbar opens Chrome import, MusePi Browser Relay, and shared-Chromium extensions, while Settings → Browser is split into General, Extensions, and Data with a full-URL preference. Import now reports progress, success, and failure, and clearly copies only cookies and localStorage from the default Chrome profile into the shared automation Chromium; it does not claim password import, managed-partition import, or synchronization. Tabs, Agent controls, and the real-Chrome Relay keep their existing implementations.

- **子会话有了确定的标题**：spawn 时把 `tasks[].name`（如 Alice / Bob）写成 `titleSource: auto` 的会话标题，注册表 `displayName` 也用同一个 label——此前子会话的 title 槽为空，左侧会话树显示「未命名会话」，右侧子代理面板则显示所有实例共享的 agent 类型名 `task`。没有传 `name` 时标题回退到自动生成的 agent id；更早的 transcript 在列举时回退文件名（`Alice`/`Bob`），advisor 仍显示为顾问而不是普通子会话。
  - EN: Sub-agent sessions now have deterministic titles. At spawn, `tasks[].name` (Alice, Bob, …) is written as the session title (`titleSource: auto`), and the registry `displayName` uses the same label. Previously the title slot stayed empty — the session tree showed "Untitled session" and the agents panel showed the shared agent type name (`task`) for every instance. Spawns without a `name` fall back to the generated agent id, older transcripts fall back to their filename (`Alice`/`Bob`) when listed, and advisor transcripts keep their advisor marker instead of becoming ordinary sub-sessions.

- **后台任务结算不再露出一段模型 XML**：daemon 投递或重启回放的 `async-job-result` 以前落进通用自定义消息，界面直接显示 `async-job-result` chip 和 `<task-result …>`、`<meta>`、`<output>` 原文。现在它是一张结算卡：状态字形（✓ / ✕ / ⊘）+ agent 名 + `[task]` + 时长 + 状态 + 行数/体积，输出以等宽预览最多 8 行、可展开，尾部 idle / `history://` 提示弱化为一行。界面只读 `details` 与信封内的 `<output>`，模型标记不再出现；终端侧同一类型也改成状态行 + 限量预览，两端共用 `pi-wire` 里的同一份解析。
  - EN: Settled background jobs no longer expose a model-facing XML envelope. The daemon's `async-job-result` (normal delivery and restart replay) used to fall through to the generic custom row, printing the `async-job-result` chip and the raw `<task-result …>`, `<meta>` and `<output>` markup. It is now a settlement card: status glyph (✓ / ✕ / ⊘), agent name, `[task]`, duration, status and line/size meta, with the output as a monospace preview capped at eight lines and expandable, and the trailing idle / `history://` hint reduced to one faint line. The row reads only `details` and the envelope's inner `<output>`; the markup never renders. The terminal row now uses the same view from `pi-wire` for its status line and bounded preview.

- **远程控制弹窗把连接与恢复操作放到主路径**：共享范围、安全说明和开始按钮现在组成一条清晰流程；公网隧道缺少 Cloudflared 时会提供安装指南、重试和可展开的技术详情，切换共享方式后旧错误不再残留。Bot 渠道列表去掉重复的「关闭」文字，补齐加载失败重试、连接中状态和渠道专属操作名称，凭据输入保留可见标签；插件清单默认收起，窄窗口下扫码与渠道区域改为单列。
  - EN: The remote-control dialog now puts connection and recovery actions on the main path. Sharing scope, safety guidance, and the start button form one clear flow; when a public tunnel needs Cloudflared, the dialog offers an installation guide, retry, and expandable technical details, and changing scope clears stale errors. Bot-channel rows drop redundant Off copy, add list-failure retry, pending states, and channel-specific accessible action names, while credential fields keep visible labels. The plugin inventory is collapsed by default, and narrow windows stack scanning and channels in one column.

- **自动朗读的四处边界补齐**：逐项复核语音链路后修了四个会让人「听着不对」或「白花钱」的地方。① 打开历史会话或切换会话时不再把屏幕上已有的旧回复读一遍——只有本次打开后开始生成的回复才自动朗读，正在生成中的回复仍能正常收尾。② 流式朗读遇到尚未闭合的代码围栏时，先读围栏之前的句子，把代码块攒到闭合再处理，不会在代码写到一半时把半截片段送去合成或改写。③ 开启增强语音速写后，增量朗读原本每完成一句就调一次小模型，长回复会连发几十次；现在单条回复最多改写 12 句，其余走机械清理。④ 合成后端失败后不再为队列里剩余的每句继续发请求。另外修正条件渲染：语音页传入的插件状态此前只用于分节折叠，单行仍回退到内置条件表，插件停用时那一行并没有真正按条件隐藏，现在两处共用一个求值器。
  - EN: Four edge cases in auto-read are closed after a line-by-line review. (1) Opening a history session or switching sessions no longer reads the reply already on screen — only replies that START generating after the session is opened are auto-read, and an in-flight reply still flushes normally. (2) When a streaming reply is inside an unclosed code fence, text before the fence is spoken immediately and the code block is held until the fence closes, so a half-written block is never synthesized or rewritten. (3) With Enhanced Speech Rewriting on, delta read-aloud used to call the small model once per completed sentence — dozens of calls on a long reply; one reply now rewrites at most 12 sentences and the rest use mechanical cleanup. (4) After a synthesis failure no further requests are queued for the remaining sentences. Also fixed condition rendering: the plugin state passed by the voice page only drove the group collapse while each row fell back to the built-in condition table, so a row was not actually hidden when its package was disabled; both now share one evaluator.

- **实例菜单、快捷键和远程控制不再随面板一起消失**：这三个入口原先只在会话侧栏底部，改成两级左栏后，面板一收起或切到看板/任务中心就没有了。现在它们固定在左 rail 底部：实例按钮收成状态点（菜单内容不变——本地/远程切换、版本与延迟、host 增删改、重连、断开、浏览器桥诊断），旁边是快捷键与手机远程控制，设置仍在最下。侧栏底部那一份由 `showFooter` 关掉，不保留两套重复入口；其它宿主仍可默认显示。
  - EN: The instance menu, keyboard shortcuts and remote control no longer disappear with the panel. Those three entries lived only in the session sidebar footer, so folding the panel or switching to a page destination hid them entirely. They now sit in the left rail's footer: the instance button collapses to a status dot while keeping its full menu (local/remote switching, version and latency, host add/remove/switch, reconnect, disconnect, browser-bridge diagnostics), next to shortcuts and phone remote control, with Settings at the bottom. The sidebar footer is switched off via `showFooter` so there is no duplicated entry; other hosts keep the default.

- **代理间 IRC 消息有了自己的消息行**：此前 `irc:*` 只渲染成一个发送者小标签加原始 Markdown，relay 的正文又自带 `[IRC Alice → Bob]` 表头，于是一条消息出现两次发言人；autoreply 还会把发送者显示成字面量 `irc`。现在路由由 `details` 组合（`← Alice` / `Alice → Bob` / `我 → Bob`），`relay`、`incoming`、`autoreply` 分级着色，`auto` / `reply` 作为标签；同一对 agent 的连续消息只显示一次表头，正文超过 3 行截断并可展开。导出的 Markdown 也不再丢弃 IRC 记录，按 `## IRC · Alice → Bob` 输出。
  - EN: Inter-agent IRC messages now have their own transcript row. `irc:*` used to render as a sender chip plus raw Markdown, while relay bodies already carried an `[IRC Alice → Bob]` header — so every message named its sender twice — and autoreply showed the literal `irc` as the sender. The route is now composed from `details` (`← Alice`, `Alice → Bob`, `Me → Bob`), `relay` / `incoming` / `autoreply` are weighted separately, and `auto` / `reply` become tags. Consecutive messages between the same pair share one header, and bodies over three lines clamp with an explicit expansion. Markdown export no longer drops IRC traffic and writes `## IRC · Alice → Bob`.

- **记忆写入更可靠，并新增 Sharpshooter 项目决策记忆**：Hindsight 的工具 `retain`/`learn` 现在先写入持久 outbox，服务端拒绝、网络失败或进程退出都不会静默丢记忆，失败会指数退避重试，被拒或过期事件进入隔离区；Mnemopi 启动时会把即将被 TTL 清理的旧 working memory 提前提升为 episodic，嵌入模型变更改为先完整嵌入新模型再原子替换，失败保留旧向量；首轮 recall 写入会话文件，恢复/切换/fork 不再重查并保持 prompt cache，recall 后发生变化的记忆用一次性隐藏消息报告；`retain`/`learn` 在支持的后端上可选择 `scope: "global"`；所有记忆写入共用一套密钥脱敏。新增 `sharpshooter` 后端，把架构/产品/风格决策抽取到三个项目文件并后台合并。本地嵌入模型改为每台机器一个常驻服务，空闲 15 分钟退出，启动失败自动回退到每进程 worker。
  - EN: Memory writes are now more reliable, and Sharpshooter project-decision memory is available. Hindsight tool `retain`/`learn` writes first enter a durable outbox, so a refused request, network failure, or process exit no longer drops memories silently; failures retry with exponential backoff, and refused or expired events move to quarantine. Mnemopi promotes old working-memory rows before a TTL trim, and embedding-model changes embed the whole corpus under the new model before replacing stored vectors atomically, keeping the old ones on failure. First-turn recall is stored in the session file, so resume, switch, and fork reuse it without re-querying or changing the prompt cache, and memories changed since that recall are reported in a one-time hidden message. `retain`/`learn` accept `scope: "global"` on supporting backends, and every memory write shares one credential-redaction pass. A new `sharpshooter` backend extracts architecture, product, and style decisions into three project files and consolidates them in the background. Local embedding models now run in one machine-wide server that exits after 15 idle minutes and falls back to a per-process worker when it cannot start.

- **语音设置能试听边生成边朗读，配置依赖也讲清楚了**：设置 → 语音底部模拟会话的助手消息原先只有一个「整段朗读」按钮，delta 级流式朗读在设置里没法体验；现在多一个「流式试听」，按时间片把固定样本喂给与自动朗读同一个增量控制器，第一句写完就出声，再点即停。配置项之间原先有三处没讲明白：`tts.inputMode` 在「增强语音速写」开启时被改写结果整个盖掉、`sttPluginEnabled` / `ttsPluginEnabled` 这两个条件名在 GUI 的条件表里不存在（只能靠页面按前缀硬隐藏，未知条件一律静默可见）、朗读范围只管自动朗读而手动按钮始终读该条消息。现在改写之后仍应用内容模式，语音页把插件状态作为条件求值器传给 schema 渲染器，并在配置列表下方补一段「朗读设置如何配合」说明：顺带写明「文字转语音提供商」只影响 tts 工具生成音频文件，不决定桌面朗读用哪个本地模型或音色。
  - EN: Voice settings can now preview streaming read-aloud, and the config dependencies are spelled out. The simulated conversation at the bottom of Settings → Voice used to offer only a whole-reply read-aloud button, so delta-level speech could not be tried from settings; a new "Streaming preview" button feeds the fixed sample to the same incremental controller auto-read uses, speaking the first sentence as soon as it is complete, and stops on a second click. Three relationships were previously unclear: `tts.inputMode` was silently discarded when Enhanced Speech Rewriting returned a rewrite; `sttPluginEnabled` / `ttsPluginEnabled` had no evaluator in the GUI condition table (the page hid rows by prefix, and unknown conditions silently stayed visible); and read-aloud scope only governs auto-read while the manual button always speaks that one message. The content mode now applies after rewriting, the voice page passes plugin state as condition evaluators to the schema renderer, and a "How the read-aloud settings fit together" note under the list also clarifies that Text-to-Speech Provider only affects audio files produced by the tts tool — it does not choose the local model or voice used by desktop read-aloud.

- **页面卡片和会话卡片现在一模一样大**：两级左栏改变聊天列宽度后，主页的圆角容器与任务中心、看板、能力中心这些页面的容器不再对齐——会话卡是「窗口 gutter 8px + 卡片 gutter 8px」两层，圆角和阴影读 `--radius-2xl` / `--gui-card-shadow`；页面卡却还是早年的 Tailwind `m-2` 单层 8px、`rounded-2xl` 和写死的 `0 4px 24px rgba(0,0,0,.25)` 重阴影，于是页面看着更大、更贴边、投影更重。现在两类卡片都走同一套 token：`.gui-chat-surface` 承担窗口 gutter，内部卡片（`.gui-float-card` / `.gui-page-card`）承担卡片 gutter，圆角、阴影、表面色全部同一来源。用 headless Chrome 读计算样式逐项比对，边距、宽高、圆角、阴影、背景全部一致（diff 为空）。
  - EN: Page cards and the chat card are now exactly the same size. The two-tier left rail changed the chat column width and exposed an old split: the chat card uses two 8px gutter layers (window + card) with `--radius-2xl` and `--gui-card-shadow`, while board, scheduled, PR and capability pages still used a bare Tailwind card — a single `m-2`, `rounded-2xl` and a hard-coded heavier `0 4px 24px rgba(0,0,0,.25)` shadow — so they looked wider, closer to the edge and more heavily shadowed. Both now use the same tokens: `.gui-chat-surface` owns the window gutter, the inner card (`.gui-float-card` / `.gui-page-card`) owns the card gutter, and radius, shadow and surface colour come from one source. A headless-Chrome computed-style comparison reports identical insets, width, height, radius, shadow and background (empty diff).

- **桌面朗读追上了生成速度**：自动朗读此前要等整条回复写完，才开始按句合成播放；现在每次快照更新都把当前完整回复喂给增量控制器，只挑出新完成的句子合成，第一句出声时模型还在写下一段，与终端 vocalizer 的 delta 级朗读对齐。未写完的尾句留在缓冲区，回合结束时补读；换回合或换会话会停掉旧音频并重建控制器，手动朗读按钮与自动朗读互斥、不会叠着播。改写、音色、语速、朗读范围全部沿用上一轮的同一组 `tts.*` 设置，无需另开开关；合成失败只报一次错误，不会为剩余每句刷通知。
  - EN: Desktop read-aloud now keeps pace with generation. Auto-read used to wait for the whole reply before synthesizing it sentence by sentence; each snapshot update now feeds the current full text to an incremental controller that synthesizes only newly completed sentences, so the first sentence plays while the model is still writing the next one — matching the terminal vocalizer's delta-level speech. An unfinished tail stays buffered and is flushed at turn end; switching turns or sessions stops the old audio and rebuilds the controller, and the manual read-aloud button is mutually exclusive with auto-read so they never overlap. Rewriting, voice, rate and scope all keep using the same `tts.*` group from the previous change, with no new switch; a synthesis failure reports once instead of one notification per remaining sentence.

- **小组件卡片在各展示面都完整可用**：收起行的括号里现在显示小组件自己的标题（没有标题才显示类型），不再重复「组件 · html」并把右括号顶到行尾；独立展示卡与全屏也补上了标注入口，此前只有展开的工具卡才有。自定义 HTML 页不再被卡片内边距包住，而是整幅铺满，跨列阅读时不再显得又窄又局促。取消标注后按钮不再失效——之前取消会让卡片的标注状态卡住，之后点它没有任何反应。
  - EN: Widget cards are now complete on every surface. The collapsed row's parenthetical shows the widget's own title (falling back to its type) instead of repeating "Widget · html" with the closing paren stranded at the far edge, and the standalone card and fullscreen view now carry the annotation entry that only the expanded tool card had. Custom HTML pages are no longer inset by the card's body padding — they span the full card, so they stop reading as a narrow strip in a wide timeline. Cancelling an annotation no longer leaves the button dead: the card used to stay stuck in its annotating state, so later clicks did nothing.

- **可视化卡片的标注入口与高度更稳**：自定义 HTML 页的标注按钮移到页面上方独立一行，不再压住页面自己的右上角控件；键盘聚焦与触屏下都能看到，不再只在鼠标悬停时出现。页面高度按内容记住，滚动离开再回来不会从 320px 下限重新长高。
  - EN: The visualization card's annotation entry and height are steadier. On a custom HTML page the button now sits in its own row above the page instead of floating over the page's own top-right controls, and it is reachable by keyboard focus and on touch screens rather than only on mouse hover. The measured height is remembered per page, so scrolling a card away and back no longer restarts it at the 320px floor and grows again.

- **可视化页面的观感更稳**：自定义 HTML 页首帧不再闪白（页面绘制前保持透明，按记住的高度占位），正文按内容高度显示（下限 48px、上限 20000px，不再固定 320px 留白），并继承对话正文的字号——agent 用 rem/em 写的页面会跟着界面字号缩放。
  - EN: HTML pages are steadier to look at. The first frame no longer flashes white (it stays transparent until the page paints, at its remembered height), the page takes its content height (48px floor, 20000px cap instead of a fixed 320px block), and it inherits the chat body's font size — agent markup in rem/em now scales with the interface size setting.

- **页面交互不再抢走应用快捷键**：页面获得焦点时，它自己没有处理的 ⌘/Ctrl 组合键会回放到宿主，⌘K 之类的应用快捷键照常生效（复制、全选、撤销仍归页面）；页面滚到底或顶时，滚轮/滑动交还给对话，不再卡在页面上。
  - EN: A focused page no longer swallows the app's shortcuts. Cmd/Ctrl chords the page did not handle are replayed to the host, so ⌘K and friends keep working (copy, select-all and undo stay the page's own), and a wheel or swipe at the page's top or bottom hands scrolling back to the chat instead of dying on the frame.

- **等待选择的页面有状态提示**：`widget` 的 page 动作新增 `awaitAnswer`；这类页面下方会显示「等待你的选择」，用户从页面里提交后变为「已回复」，不再看起来像点击无响应。
  - EN: Pages that ask the user to choose now say so. The `widget` page action takes `awaitAnswer`, and such a page shows "Waiting for your choice" beneath it, switching to "Answered" once the user submits from the page instead of looking unresponsive.

- **可视化卡片全屏开合是一镜到底**：点全屏时从卡片原位放大到全屏面板，关闭（✕ 或 Esc）时缩回卡片位置，中途不会先闪一个固定尺寸的遮罩；360ms 共享元素 FLIP，跟随减少动效开关。飞行期间内容层加模糊、暂停命中测试，只让一个合成层做缩放，页面与 iframe 不在每个中间尺寸重排重绘，落位时立刻恢复清晰。
  - EN: Opening a widget fullscreen now grows the panel out of the card's own rect and closing (✕ or Esc) shrinks it back into place, instead of flashing a fixed-size overlay first: a 360ms shared-element FLIP that follows the reduced-motion setting. While the panel flies its content layer is blurred and stops taking hits, so the compositor scales a single raster instead of re-laying out the page and its frame at every intermediate size, and the view settles sharp the moment the flight lands.

- **三处面板按钮各归其位，折叠按钮补齐悬停状态**：标题栏侧栏按钮保留 panel-close/open，右侧栏折叠按钮改用镜像的 panel 图标（原来是箭头，和左栏读起来不是一套），会话信息卡原来借用了同一对图标，现在换成 info glyph。图标在展开/收起之间用 morphicons 形变，悬停时朝面板将要移动的方向轻推（左栏展开态向左、收起态向右，右栏与之镜像），按下再略微缩小；减少动效时只保留颜色变化。信息卡里被折叠的标签与面板列表的「查看更多（N）」也改回整行文字按钮，不再挤在 20px 的图标位里折成两行。
  - EN: The three panel toggles now each read for themselves: the title bar keeps panel-close/open, the right rail uses the mirrored panel glyph instead of a chevron, and the session info card — which had borrowed the same pair — shows an info glyph. All toggles morph their glyph between expand and collapse, nudge toward the action's direction for the current state on hover, and scale slightly when pressed; reduced motion keeps only the color change. The folded "More (N)" rows in the info card are full-width text buttons again instead of wrapping inside a 20px icon slot.

- **可视化卡片撑满列宽，标注真的能用了**：小组件卡片此前按标题文字的宽度收缩，iframe 只有默认 300px，看起来是宽列里的一条窄条；现在卡片与页面撑满消息列。标注入口并进卡片头部操作组，与复制、菜单、全屏同一行（铅笔↔关闭用 morphicons 形变）；看板与 HTML 导出没有卡片头，才回退到页面上方那一行。标注此前点了没反应，是两个独立原因叠加：渲染进程 CSP 不允许 `eval`，沙箱 iframe 继承该策略，注入脚本被静默拦下；改成注入内联 `<script>` 后又撞上第二个——overlay 源字符串自带结尾分号，被 `( … );` 包装后成了语法错误，沙箱只能回「overlay did not start」。现在按表达式注入并去掉结尾分号，标注可正常进入；构建侧也加了守卫测试，防止源字符串再带回分号。
  - EN: The visualization card now spans the message column instead of shrinking to its title text with a 300px iframe, and the annotation entry actually works. It also sits in the card's own action cluster — the same row as copy, menu and fullscreen — with a morphing pencil/close icon; only a host without a card head (board canvas, HTML export) falls back to the row above the page. Two separate faults had stacked up: the renderer's CSP forbids `eval` and the sandboxed iframe inherits that policy, so the injected overlay was dropped silently; switching to an inline `<script>` then hit the second — the overlay source carries its own trailing semicolon, which made `( … );` a parse error the frame reported as "overlay did not start". It is now injected as an expression with the terminator trimmed, and a guard test keeps the source from growing one back.

- **小组件不再出现两份**：开启「独立展示」后，可视化卡片现在直接**替代**工具调用行——位置不变，保留标题、菜单、全屏与标注，不再出现「折叠行 + 下方独立卡」的重复；回合折叠时这张卡仍被保留在上方。关闭该开关则回到可折叠的工具卡片，展开偏好只在那时生效。
  - EN: Widgets no longer appear twice. With the standalone display on, the visualization card now replaces the tool-call row in place — same position, keeping the title, menu, fullscreen and annotation, with no folded row above a second card; a folded round still keeps that card visible. Turning the setting off returns the collapsible tool card, and the expanded preference only applies then.
- **非图片附件有了自己的文件卡和预览**：用户消息里的 `[Attachment] blob://…` 引用行现在渲染成一排文件卡（扩展名、文件名、大小），正文里不再夹着原始引用文本；点开按 8 MiB 分页读回预览，二进制内容明确提示无法预览并可另存。手机与浏览器访客也能在输入框里选择文件发送：8 MiB 以内单次上传，更大的走 3 MiB 分片，上传失败直接显示在芯片上并阻止发送。
  - EN: Non-image attachments now have their own file cards and preview. `[Attachment] blob://…` references in a user message render as a row of file cards (extension, name, size) instead of raw reference text in the body; opening one pages the content back in 8 MiB slices, and binary files say they cannot be previewed while offering a save action. The phone and browser guest composer can attach files too: up to 8 MiB in one upload, larger files in 3 MiB chunks, with failures shown on the chip and the send blocked.

- **语音增强改写不再是终端专属**：「增强语音速写」原先只在终端里生效——`SpeechEnhancer` 需要会话的 `ModelRegistry`、凭据与 `@tiny` 角色，只有 TUI 的 `EventController` 会构造它并塞进 vocalizer；桌面朗读走 `tts.synthesize`，拿到的只是正则清理过的文本。现在 daemon 新增 `tts.rewrite` RPC，用当前会话的同一套依赖构造 `SpeechEnhancer`，桌面按 fence-aware 的段落块逐块改写（并发上限 2、每块 10 秒上限、最多 20 块），模型判定「不可朗读」的块跳过，无模型/超时/失败时逐块回退机械清理，整段都不可朗读则保持静默。两个界面因此共用同一个改写实现，设置项不再标「仅 TUI 生效」。顺带把名不副实的「摘要」改成「精简（前两句）」——它一直是截断前两句再限长 600 字，从不调用模型。
  - EN: Enhanced speech rewriting is no longer terminal-only. The switch used to do nothing on the desktop: `SpeechEnhancer` needs the session's `ModelRegistry`, credentials and `@tiny` role, and only the TUI's `EventController` constructed it and handed it to the vocalizer; desktop read-aloud went through `tts.synthesize` with regex-cleaned text. A new `tts.rewrite` RPC builds the same `SpeechEnhancer` from the live session, and the desktop rewrites fence-aware markdown blocks (two concurrent completions, 10s per block, 20 blocks max). Blocks the model judges unspeakable are skipped; a missing model, timeout or failure falls back to mechanical cleanup per block, and a wholly unspeakable reply stays silent. Both shells now share one rewrite implementation and the setting no longer carries a terminal-only badge. The misleading "Summary" option is renamed "Condensed (first two sentences)" — it always truncated to two sentences and 600 characters and never called a model.

- **顶栏控件回到原位，收起侧栏不再空一大块，无面板的视图不再有折叠按钮**：之前为给左 rail 让位，把窗口左上角的控件组从 16px 挪到 68px，动了原本的顶栏布局；现在改由 rail 自己用 48px 空带规避顶栏，控件恢复原位。侧栏收起时顶栏的 carve spacer 仍按整段控件宽度撑开，多算了 rail 的 52px，会话标题与标签右边多出一大截空白，现已减去。看板 / 问题与 PR / 能力中心这些自己占满画面的视图不再渲染折叠按钮（spacer 仍按「收起」的几何保留，否则会话标题会压到顶栏控件底下）——点折叠把顶部会话标签挤到左边的现象消失。
  - EN: The titlebar controls are back where they were, collapsing no longer leaves a wide gap, and views without a left panel no longer show a fold button. The window's top-left cluster had been moved from 16px to 68px to make room for the left rail; the rail now avoids the titlebar with its own empty 48px band instead. The collapsed header spacer still reserved the cluster's full width, counting the rail's 52px twice and pushing session titles and tabs to the right; it now subtracts the rail. Board, Issues & PRs and the Capability Center no longer render the fold control (the spacer stays at its collapsed value because their geometry is identical, otherwise the session title would slide under the titlebar cluster), so folding can no longer shove the session tabs into the corner.

- **收起后悬停 rail 会滑出浮层面板，任务中心也有了自己的左面板**：面板收起时不再只剩一条图标栏——鼠标进入 rail，同一个已挂载的会话列表以浮层滑出（从顶栏下方开始、圆角 + 投影、x:-8 入场），移开 140ms 收起，点顶栏折叠按钮重新固定；浮层是 fixed 定位，不占布局宽度，也不新建第二份列表。任务中心的 260px 列从「工作区分组列表」升级成完整面板：标题 + 计数、新建任务、搜索、状态过滤（全部 / 已启用 / 已暂停 / 最近失败），过滤与右侧详情共用同一份任务数据，过滤无结果有明确提示。
  - EN: Hovering the rail now slides out a floating panel, and Scheduled Tasks has a left panel of its own. With the docked panel closed, entering the rail floats the SAME mounted session list — starting below the titlebar, rounded and elevated, sliding in from x:-8 — and leaving dismisses it after 140ms; the titlebar toggle docks it again. The overlay is fixed, takes no layout width and creates no second list. The Scheduled Tasks 260px column grew from a bare workspace grouping into a real panel: title with count, new task, search, and a status filter (All / Enabled / Paused / Failed recently) that narrows the same task data the detail pane reads, with an explicit empty-filter state.

- **桌宠分成矢量与雪碧图两种模式**：设置与首次启动先选形态——矢量球体（31 态表情、手势、配饰）或雪碧图宠物（8×9 网格或 atlas 命名帧）。网格只显示当前形态的形象，导入入口也按形态区分；两种形态各自记住上次的选择，来回切换不会丢，切换后旧形象里没选过的会自动落到该形态的默认值。此前只有 id、没有形态的旧选择按形象本身归位，无需重选。
  - EN: The companion now has two explicit modes, chosen before picking a pet in Settings and first-run: the vector sphere (31 states, gestures, accessories) or a spritesheet pet (8×9 grid or atlas-named frames). The grid only lists pets of the chosen mode and the import actions split accordingly. Each mode remembers its own last pick, so switching back and forth never loses a selection, and a mode you never picked falls back to that mode's default. Selections stored before modes existed resolve by the pet's own shape, with nothing to re-pick.
