- **语音设置从两组并成一组**：设置 → 语音此前同时摆着 `tts.*`（桌面按句流式朗读）与 `speech.*`（终端按增量朗读）两套开关、两个音色、两种范围，改一个另一个不动，看不出哪个生效。现在两个界面读同一组 `tts.*`：`tts.autoRead` 是总开关、`tts.readMode` 管朗读范围（桌面「全部」会带上思考内容）、`tts.localVoice` 管音色；只有需要小模型改写口语的 `tts.enhanced` 仍由终端执行，已标注「仅 TUI 生效」。`speech.*` 保留为隐藏的旧键，`Settings.get()` 在新键未写入时回退读它，旧 config.yml 不用迁移。桌面与终端的播放器仍各按自己的运行环境播音频，但开关、范围、音色与文本处理不再各存一份。
  - EN: Voice settings are one group instead of two. Settings → Voice used to expose `tts.*` (desktop sentence-streamed readback) and `speech.*` (terminal delta-streamed vocalization) side by side — two switches, two voice pickers and two scopes, with no hint which one applied. Both shells now read the same `tts.*` keys: `tts.autoRead` is the switch, `tts.readMode` sets the scope (the desktop's "all" also speaks thinking blocks) and `tts.localVoice` picks the voice; only `tts.enhanced`, which needs a rewrite model, stays terminal-only and is badged as such. `speech.*` survives as hidden legacy keys and `Settings.get()` falls back to them when the new key was never written, so older config.yml files need no migration. Each shell still plays audio through its own runtime, but the switch, scope, voice and text preparation are no longer stored twice.

- **设置里的枚举下拉不再显示 raw / sanitize 这类裸 id**：daemon 对没声明 `ui.options` 的枚举会把值原样当标签合成给界面，翻译表查不到 `sanitize`、`one-at-a-time` 这类 id，于是下拉从触发按钮到展开列表都是英文标识。现已为全部 17 个可见枚举补齐选项声明，并给 30 余个标签补中英词条；`q4`、`fp16`、`xhigh` 等量化精度与推理档位是有意保留原样的技术标识。新增契约测试：每个可见枚举都必须声明选项、标签不得等于存储值、选项集合必须与取值一一对应。
  - EN: Enum dropdowns no longer print bare ids like raw / sanitize. For enums without `ui.options`, the daemon synthesized the value as the label and the translation table had no entry for ids such as `sanitize` or `one-at-a-time`, so both the trigger and the open list showed raw identifiers. All 17 visible enums now declare their options, with 30+ new zh/en labels; quantisation and effort tokens (`q4`, `fp16`, `xhigh`) deliberately stay verbatim because they are technical identifiers. A new contract test requires every visible enum to declare options, forbids labels equal to stored values and checks the option set against the declared values.

- **「仅 TUI 生效」不再误伤桌面设置**：`startup.changelogMode`（启动新功能面板）、`startup.checkUpdate`（更新检查）、`task.showResolvedModelBadge`（任务卡模型标记）都由 daemon 或工具渲染层读取，桌面同样受影响，却一直被折叠进终端专属区块。这三项移回主列表；其余终端外观、状态栏、终端通知等 36 项保持原标记。
  - EN: Terminal-only badges no longer hide desktop-effective settings. `startup.changelogMode` (startup What's New panel), `startup.checkUpdate` (update check) and `task.showResolvedModelBadge` (job card model badge) are read by the daemon or tool renderers and affect the desktop too, yet were folded into the terminal-only block. Those three are back in the main list; the other 36 terminal appearance, status-line and notification settings keep their badge.

- **可视化工具现在会被主动用上**：「什么时候该出卡片或页面」原先只写在 `widget` 的工具描述里，模型常常略过——问「各包代码行数分布」得到的是一份列表，要再要求一次才画图。现在同一段规则由 `visuals/when.ts` 单一来源同时注入工具描述和系统提示的「Visual answers」段，且仅当本次会话真的挂载了 `widget` 才注入（工具关掉或子代理工具集不含它时不会出现「调用不存在的工具」的误导）。规则自带边界：两三个数字、短列表、代码或明确要纯文字时不用，每个回答最多一页，卡片里已有的内容不再复述——避免把每句话都变成卡片。
  - EN: Visualizations now get used without being asked for. The "when to render a card or page" rule lived only in the `widget` tool description, which agents routinely skipped: asked for a breakdown of lines of code per package, an agent answered with a list and drew the chart only when asked twice. One rule in `visuals/when.ts` now feeds both the tool description and a "Visual answers" section of the system prompt, injected only when the `widget` tool is actually mounted so a session without it is never told to call it. The rule carries its own limits — no widget for a few numbers, short lists, code or an explicit plain-text request, at most one page per answer, and never restate what the card already shows — so it does not turn every answer into a card.

- **固定到桌面的卡片有投影了，不再和背后的看板卡糊成一块**：固定窗口里的卡片恰好占满整个窗口（`100vw/100vh`），它的 `box-shadow` 画在窗口表面之外，整圈被裁掉——固定卡因此从来没有过投影。悬停在看板原卡上时两块表面直接相接，透明圆角缺口里透出背后卡片的浅色填充，读作「方角 + 有色底」。现在卡面在窗口内留出纯阴影边距（上 16 / 左右 16 / 下 32px），投影得以渲染，卡片与背后内容有了明确边界；卡面同时改由 tone 契约供给（与来源看板卡同一 ramp），此前 `gui-settings.css` 的字面 `--color-surface-raised` 按 import 顺序压过了 tone 的取值。
  - EN: The pinned desktop card finally casts a shadow and no longer fuses with the board card behind it. The card inside the pin window filled the viewport exactly (`100vw/100vh`), so its `box-shadow` painted outside the window surface and was clipped in full — pinned cards never had a shadow. Hovering over the board's own card, the two surfaces touched directly and the lighter fill of the card behind showed through the transparent corner notches, reading as square corners over a coloured backdrop. The card now keeps pure shadow margins inside the window (16px top/sides, 32px bottom) so the shadow renders and the card reads as a separate floating object; the surface itself is supplied by the tone contract (the same ramp as the board card it was pinned from) — previously a literal `--color-surface-raised` in `gui-settings.css` outranked the tone by import order.

- **语音页「朗读内容」不再显示 raw / sanitize / summarize**：设置 → 语音的这一项没有声明 `ui.options`，daemon 只能把枚举值原样当选项标签合成给界面，翻译表里查不到 `raw` 这类 id，于是下拉从触发按钮到展开列表全是裸英文。现在补齐三项声明，标签复用内置 TTS 插件配置面板同一套英文源句（Raw text / Sanitized / Summarized），中英文都已有译文；同一页其余枚举都有声明，不受影响。
  - EN: Read content under Voice settings no longer shows raw / sanitize / summarize. The setting declared no `ui.options`, so the daemon synthesized the enum ids as option labels and the translation table had no entry for them — the trigger and the open list both painted bare ids. The three choices are now declared, reusing the same English source labels as the built-in TTS plugin's config panel (Raw text / Sanitized / Summarized), which already have zh-CN and en-US translations. Every other enum on that page already declared its options.

- **「侧信道模型」不再像两个设置项**：设置 → 模型与供应商 → 模型行为里，分节标题叫「侧信道模型」，紧跟着的行标签又写一遍「侧信道模型」，读起来像两套并列配置，实际只有一行下拉。分节标题保留，行内改为只留选择说明，可访问名称仍挂在这个下拉上。
  - EN: The side-channel model section no longer reads as two settings. Under Models & Providers → Model behavior, the section heading said "Side-channel model" and the row label immediately below repeated it, looking like two parallel configurations while there is only one dropdown. The heading stays; the row now carries just the selection hint, and the select keeps its accessible name.

- **桌宠导入支持显式状态图集、一次性动作和包内气泡配色**：保留既有金色球体、31 态会话表情、Petdex 预设与 SVG 路径，新增 `format:"atlas"` 包格式；ZIP 与目录导入可读取命名动作、独立左右移动行、每行动画帧数／fps 和循环方式。空闲时会播放包内随机动作，双击可触发自定义动作，播放完自动回到实时会话状态；一次性动作不会覆盖悬停、拖拽、睡眠或工作状态。包还可声明气泡背景、文字和边框色，仅在六个十六进制颜色字段完整且文字对比度达到 4.5:1 时采用，低对比度配置继续使用应用默认玻璃气泡。
  - EN: Companion imports now support explicit-state atlases, one-shot actions and package bubble palettes. The existing gold sphere, 31 session states, Petdex presets and SVG path remain intact; the new `format:"atlas"` contract lets ZIP and directory imports declare named actions, separate left/right movement rows, per-row frame counts/fps and looping. Package idle actions play while calm, double-click triggers custom actions, and completion returns to the live session state; one-shots never override hover, drag, sleep or work. A package may also declare bubble background, foreground and border colors, applied only when all three six-digit hex values are present and text contrast reaches 4.5:1; low-contrast configurations keep the application's default glass bubbles.

- **内置看板卡片的深浅主题不再错位**：待办、指标、画览、专注的底色是写死的浅色渐变，文字色却由卡片 `tone` 决定，深色主题下就成了白底配亮字——待办里勾掉的条目几乎看不见，番茄钟的 25:00 只剩一层灰影；浅色主题下则是深底配深字的反向错位。卡面现在交回 `tone`：外壳画 `--gui-tone-step`、文字读 `--gui-tone-label-*`，同一张卡片在 default/dark/light/blue 与深浅主题之间都自洽，纸面质感仍由 `light` tone 提供。文字对比度（WCAG，深色主题）由 1.17 / 1.09 / 1.09 回到 11.73 / 4.54 / 9.55。番茄钟内部还引用着只有转录卡片才定义的 `--tv-*` 变量，越界时回退成固定浅色墨，现改为读卡片 token；凹槽与悬停也改按卡片墨色推导，浅色卡片在深色主题下的内嵌格不再比卡面更亮。
  - EN: Built-in board cards no longer invert in the other theme. Todo, metric, gallery and pomodoro painted a hard-coded light gradient while their text colour came from the card's `tone`, so in dark theme they were light text on a near-white face — a checked-off todo nearly vanished and the pomodoro clock read as a grey ghost — and in light theme the same cards went the other way, dark text on a dark face. The surface now belongs to the tone again: the shell paints `--gui-tone-step` and the labels read `--gui-tone-label-*`, so one card holds up across default/dark/light/blue and both schemes, with the paper feel still supplied by the `light` tone. Text contrast (WCAG, dark theme) goes from 1.17 / 1.09 / 1.09 to 11.73 / 4.54 / 9.55. The pomodoro also referenced `--tv-*` variables that only exist inside a transcript card and fell back to fixed light ink; it reads the card tokens now. Recesses and hover states derive from the card's own ink too, so a light card's inset fields are no longer brighter than the card itself in dark theme.

- **左栏改成两级，图标不再压在顶栏上**：会话、看板、定时任务、能力中心原本全挤在一条可收起的侧栏里——收起之后没有任何入口，只能靠顶部开关把整条栏叫回来。现在左侧常驻一条 52px 图标 rail，第一格是首页，后面是任务中心、看板、问题与拉取请求、能力中心，底部是设置；顶部单独留 48px 空拖拽带，图标不再和窗口标题栏叠在一起；窗口原有的折叠按钮右移到 rail 之后（无框窗口从 16px 改 68px），rail 里不再多出一个重复的折叠按钮。目的地各管各的左栏：首页就是原来的会话列表，设置是设置导航，看板/任务中心这类页面自己的地盘由页面占满、会话列表折起但不卸载，点 rail 首页随时回来。原先在会话列表里重复一遍的任务中心/看板/能力中心菜单行已删除，新建与搜索仍留在首页面板；定时任务刚跑完的提示从侧栏高亮改到 rail 上任务中心的圆点。
  - EN: The left column is now two-tier and the icons no longer sit on the titlebar. Sessions, board, scheduled tasks and the capability center all lived inside one collapsible sidebar, so collapsing it left no entry point at all. A persistent 52px icon rail now starts with Home, followed by Scheduled, Board, Issues & PRs and the Capability Center, with Settings at the bottom; a separate 48px draggable band at the top is left empty so destinations never overlap the window header; the window's existing fold control now sits after the rail (16px → 68px on frameless windows) instead of colliding with a duplicate button in the rail. Each destination owns its left column: Home shows the session list, Settings shows the settings nav, and page destinations fold the session list away (still mounted) so their own surfaces get the width — the rail's Home brings it back. The duplicated board/task/capability menu rows are gone from the session list; new task and search stay in the Home panel, and the "scheduled task just finished" glow now shows as a dot on the rail's Scheduled destination.

- **卡片操作不再全藏在「⋯」里**：看一张图想复制代码，要先点开「⋯」再选一次，而这条菜单里还混着下载、下载为图片、复制为图片和查看代码。复制代码现在是卡片头部的一等按钮，复制后图标变成对勾；其余动作仍在「⋯」里。整条操作区改为悬停显现（键盘焦点同样触发），触屏等无悬停指针的设备保持常显——一屏卡片不再像一排工具栏。
  - EN: Card actions are no longer buried behind "⋯". Copying a widget's source took opening the menu and picking an item, next to download, save-image, copy-image and view-code. Copy code is now a first-class button in the card header that flips to a check mark, with the rest still in the menu. The whole action row fades in on hover (keyboard focus included) and stays visible on pointers without hover, so a column of cards no longer reads as a toolbar grid.

- **外观里的两个下拉不再显示 i18n key**：设置 → 外观 → 时间格式 / 一周起始日 的选中项一直渲染成 `general.auto`。组件把 i18n key 当成选项值写进了 state，而下拉的选项值是 `auto` / `monday`，匹配不到就回退成原始值；同一个值又被 `resolveHour12` 和 `weekStartIndex` 当作 id 读取，这两项设置因此从来没真正生效。现在 state 只存选项 id，读取统一走 `readTimeFormat` / `readWeekStart`，早期残留的 `general.*` 值会归一化成 id，无法识别的值回退默认而不透传到界面。
  - EN: The two dropdowns under Appearance no longer render i18n keys. Time format and Week starts on displayed `general.auto` because the component stored the i18n key as the option value while the options were keyed by `auto` / `monday`; an unmatched value falls back to the raw string. The same value was read as an option id by `resolveHour12` and `weekStartIndex`, so neither setting ever took effect. State now holds option ids only, reads go through `readTimeFormat` / `readWeekStart`, leftover `general.*` values are normalized to ids, and unrecognized values fall back to the default instead of reaching the UI.

- **技能市场的技能详情与「我安装的」用同一套抽屉**：卡片点开原本弹一个居中的小窗，和已安装技能的右侧详情抽屉是两种观感；窗里还是一张 72 行的文件清单平铺，底部留一块与内容无关的空白。现在预览直接复用已安装详情的外壳：右下 480px 玻璃抽屉、40px 字形 + 名称 + 来源/作者、金色「安装」主按钮、tab 分栏，正文按 tab 收起。顺带修掉一个定位 bug——能力中心面板内有磨砂层，会把固定定位的包含块换成面板自身，弹层因此锚在内容区而不是窗口右下，遮罩也盖不住侧栏；预览改为挂到 body，Escape 与焦点走共享弹窗栈。skills.sh 的 SKILL.md 现在按 Markdown 渲染，不再是等宽纯文本，文件的字节数右对齐到行尾。
  - EN: Skill details in the skill market now use the same drawer as installed skills. A card used to open a small centered window — a second visual language next to the installed-skill drawer — showing a flat 72-row file list over a block of dead space. The preview now reuses the installed drawer's shell: a 480px glass drawer in the bottom-right corner, 40px glyph with name and source/author, the accent Install button, and tabbed content. A positioning bug is fixed with it: a frosted layer inside the capability center re-parents the fixed overlay, so the popup anchored to the content pane instead of the window corner and its scrim never covered the sidebar; the preview now mounts on the body and takes Escape and focus from the shared modal stack. skills.sh SKILL.md renders as Markdown instead of monospace plain text, and file sizes right-align on each row.

- **两个技能列表的卡片不再一边挤一边松**：已安装卡片的「内置 · 随客户端分发 · 不可卸载」脚注贴着下边框，看起来像被裁掉；市场卡片的实心「＋」绝对定位在右上角，长技能名末尾几个字被它压住。根因是两个列表各写一份尺寸：已安装侧四行 10.5px、市场侧三行 11.5px 且列宽不同。现在共用一套卡片几何（220px 最小列宽、固定两行描述、脚注钉底），已安装卡片收敛成「顶行 / 描述 / 状态标签 + 级别·来源·版本」三行，重复的「不可卸载」说明删掉（详情抽屉的风险块已写），认证徽章下移到脚注并改用与「启用」同一种描边胶囊，安装动作改成与「⋯」同构的 22px 幽灵按钮；hover 抬升幅度、分段标题字号也统一。顶部「技能 / 插件 / 插件市场」那行原本叠了三层缩进共 72px，比下面的列表右移约 50px，现在与正文共用同一条 20px 栏线。六张实测卡片一律 238×126、脚注离下边框 15px、无溢出。
  - EN: Skill cards no longer pack one list tight and leave the other loose. The installed card's "built-in · cannot uninstall" footer sat against the bottom border and looked clipped, while the marketplace's solid "+" was absolutely positioned over the top-right corner and covered the last characters of long skill names. Each list had its own geometry — four rows of 10.5px text on one side, three rows of 11.5px and a different column width on the other. Both now share one rule (220px minimum column, fixed two-line description, footer pinned to the bottom). Installed cards reduce to title / description / status pill plus level, source and version; the duplicated "cannot uninstall" note is gone because the detail drawer's risk block already states it. The verified badge moves into the footer as the same outlined pill as "Enabled", and the add action becomes a 22px ghost button matching the installed card's menu. Hover lift and section-heading type are aligned too. The top Skills / Plugins / Plugin store row stacked three indents for 72px, sitting about 50px right of the lists below; it now shares the body's 20px gutter. All six measured cards are 238x126 with a 15px gap under the footer and no overflow.

- **用户头像来源不再是三个挤扁的方块**：设置 → Git 的这一行原本把三个选项塞进 38px 见方的图标按钮，「自动（GitHub → 像素）」在里面竖排折行，三个选项读成一团。现在复用同页「更改视图」的分段控件：等宽分段、单一滑块、方向键与 Home/End 可选；标签压成「自动 / 像素脸 / 首字母」，回退顺序写进行描述。同一行的两处真 bug 一并修掉：GitHub 头像地址写进本地存储时没有广播变更，连接或禁用账号后已经显示的聊天气泡仍挂着旧头像；聊天气泡读到的头像地址在挂载后不再刷新，且图片加载失败会一直停在兜底脸，换账号或切回自动也不重试。
  - EN: The user avatar source is no longer three squashed squares. That Settings → Git row packed three options into 38px icon buttons, so "Auto (GitHub → pixel)" wrapped vertically and the row read as one blob. It now uses the same segmented control as the Changes view row above it — equal-width segments, one sliding thumb, arrow keys plus Home/End — with the labels shortened to Auto / Pixel face / Initial and the fallback order moved into the row description. Two real bugs in the same feature are fixed too: the GitHub avatar URL was written to storage without announcing the change, so already-rendered chat bubbles kept the old picture after connecting or disabling an account, and the bubble's avatar URL stopped refreshing after mount while a failed image latched the fallback face until restart.

- **顾问笔记不再被当成用户发言**：顾问的 `<advisory>` 块以 `custom` 消息发出，核心层转成 `developer`，而凡是没有会话中段 `developer`/`system` 槽位的 provider（`openai.com`/Azure 之外的 OpenAI 兼容宿主、不支持 mid-conversation-system 的 Anthropic）都会把它降级成普通 `user` 消息。系统提示词此前从不解释这个标签，主代理于是把整块读成用户输入——实机回出「你提醒得对」。`<conventions>` 现在显式声明 `<advisory>` 来自独立顾问模型，既不是用户也不是权威指令。
  - EN: Advisor notes are no longer mistaken for the user's own words. A note travels as a `custom` message that the core converts to `developer`, and every provider without a mid-conversation `developer`/`system` slot (OpenAI-compatible hosts outside `openai.com`/Azure, Anthropic below mid-conversation-system support) collapses that to a plain `user` message. The system prompt never explained the tag, so the primary read the whole block as user input and answered "you're right to remind me". The `<conventions>` block now states that `<advisory>` comes from a separate advisor model — neither the user nor an authoritative instruction.

- **顾问消息不再「闪过一次就消失」**：顾问笔记插进会话后，下一条助手回复落地时会把卡片挤出活跃路径，转录里只剩一瞬间；会话地图同时又把它读成上一条回复的「重答」分支，画布比轮数标签多一格。根因是 live 父链打标把新消息挂到最近的**消息**祖先上，卡片与回复因此成了同父兄弟。现在投影层还原诚实链（卡片 → 回复），卡片留在路径上，顾问轮回到主线——地图与轮数标签重新同口径。
  - EN: Advisor notes no longer flash once and vanish. Once the next assistant reply landed it pushed the card off the active path, so the transcript showed it for a moment only; the session map simultaneously read it as a re-answer branch of the previous reply and drew one node more than the round count claimed. The live parent-chain stamp attaches a new message to the nearest **message** ancestor, which made the card and the reply siblings of one parent. The projection layer now restores the honest chain (card → reply), so the card stays on the path and the advisor round returns to the main line: map and round label agree again.

- **「运行中 / 全部」滑块不再截字**：等宽分段控件按各标签宽度之和定宽再平分，最长的那颗被静默裁掉（「运行中」只显示一半）。组件现在量出最宽段并写成 `--seg-min`，CSS 用它设下界，中英文标签都不再截字，滑块仍与段同宽。
  - EN: The Running / All slider no longer clips its labels. Equal-width segments sized by the SUM of their labels and then split it evenly, silently cutting the longest one. The control now measures the widest segment and publishes it as `--seg-min`, and CSS uses it as a floor: CJK and English labels both stay whole while the thumb keeps matching one segment exactly.

- **任务面板的「全部」名副其实**：选项写着「全部」却只列 8 条已结束任务，第二个分节还叫「最近」，看起来像历史记录丢了。现在取 50 条已结束任务并把分节改名为「已结束」，「全部」= 运行中 + 已结束。
  - EN: The jobs panel's All filter now means all. It listed only 8 finished jobs while the second section was called Recent, which read as lost history; the pane now asks for 50 and names the section Finished, so All is running plus finished.

- **两个会话不再抢同一个 `Main`**：每个顶层会话过去都注册裸 `Main`，后来的会话顶替前一个，它退出时又把 id 一并注销——先启动的会话仍然活着，子 agent 却收到 `Unknown agent Main`，`history://` 也随该会话的产物目录一起失效。现在第一个会话持有 `Main`，并发的会话依次注册 `Main-2`、`Main-3`，各自只注销自己的 id；子 agent 写 `Main` 时按自己的父链解析，永远落到本会话的主代理上；对等名单也不再列出别人会话的主代理。给已释放 agent 发消息时，错误会指明它的转录仍可在 `history://<id>` 读取。
  - EN: Two conversations no longer fight over one `Main`. Every top-level session used to register the bare id, so a newer conversation replaced the earlier one and released the id on teardown: the first session stayed alive while its subagents got `Unknown agent Main`, and `history://` lost that session's artifact directory with it. The first session now holds `Main`, concurrent ones register `Main-2`, `Main-3`, and each releases only its own id; a subagent writing `Main` is resolved through its own lineage, so it always reaches its own conversation's main agent, and peer rosters stop listing another conversation's main. Messaging a released agent now names its readable transcript at `history://<id>`.

- **后台任务结果不再投进别的会话**:异步结果的投递槽按 agent id 注册,两个会话都用 `Main` 时后注册的会覆盖前一个 —— A 会话的后台任务结果会出现在 B 的转录里。主 agent id 现在按会话唯一,投递槽天然分开。
  - EN: Background job results no longer land in another conversation. Delivery sinks are keyed by agent id, so two sessions sharing `Main` let the newer sink replace the older one and session A's job result surfaced in B's transcript. Main ids are unique per session now, so the slots stay separate.

- **启动等待不再被误报为卡死**：查找、启动、校验、连接各阶段独立计时，阶段一推进就重置窗口；正常的多秒等待只显示当前阶段和「仍在继续」的信息提示，只有单阶段真正停住或整次启动超时才提示 daemon 可能卡住，并给出「导出诊断」与「重启应用」。阶段与提示文案改为同一套规范的交叉淡入淡出，读写全局动效 token，关闭动效时直接切换。
  - EN: Normal startup waits no longer read as a hang. Each boot phase (probe, spawn, version gate, connect) has its own budget and resets the clock when the phase advances; a slow but progressing start shows only the phase and an informational "still working" note, and the daemon-stuck diagnosis with recovery actions appears only when a phase stalls or the whole boot times out. Phase and notice copy now cross-fade through one shared status-text primitive on the global motion tokens, swapping instantly when motion is off.

- **拖动标签不再整条上下漂移**：标签条只跟随横向指针位移，往下拖去分栏时标签留在自己那一行；右侧 rail 只沿列表方向移动，横向拖动不再让条目斜着跑。碰撞检测仍读真实指针位置，所以拖到右半区新建分栏、分栏间跨列都没受影响。
  - EN: Dragging a tab no longer slides the strip vertically: a tab follows the pointer on X only and stays on its own row when you drag downward to split, and the right rail moves on Y only so its items no longer drift sideways. Collision detection still reads the real pointer position, so drag-to-split and cross-column moves are unchanged.

- **标签与 rail 恢复拖拽，分栏与跨列拖动一并修好**：React 19 移除了 `react-dom` 的 `unstable_batchedUpdates`，而 dnd-kit 6.3.1 在拖拽开始与结束时仍调用它并抛错，异常打断了整条拖拽派发——按下有效、却没有一次拖拽真正生效。补丁落在 `patches/`，把该调用换成本地透传；标签排序、rail 排序、拖到右半区新建分栏、分栏间跨列移动因此全部恢复。标签条同时不再自建 drag 上下文：此前的手势被截留在条内，面板的分栏落点因此永远不会激活。
  - EN: Tabs and the rail drag again, together with drag-to-split and cross-column moves. React 19 removed `react-dom`'s `unstable_batchedUpdates`, which dnd-kit 6.3.1 still calls when a drag starts and ends; the throw aborted the whole drag dispatch, so a press armed but no gesture ever took effect. A patch under `patches/` routes the call through a local pass-through, restoring tab reorder, rail reorder, drag-to-split onto the body's right half, and moves across split columns. The strip also stops nesting its own drag context — that trapped the gesture inside the strip, so the panel's split drop zone never armed.

- **开发实例重启不再留下旧窗口**：`relaunch-gui.mjs` 此前把仓库路径写死成上游目录名，改名或复制出来的检出匹配不到任何 Electron 进程，于是每次重启都在旧构建上再叠一个新窗口，改动看起来没生效。现在按当前检出的根目录匹配，旧实例（含其 daemon）先退出再启动。
  - EN: Restarting a dev instance no longer leaves the previous window behind. `relaunch-gui.mjs` hard-coded the upstream directory name in its process filter, so a renamed or copied checkout matched no Electron process and every restart stacked a new window on the stale build, making changes look ignored. The filter now anchors on this checkout's root and quits the old instance (daemon included) before starting.

- **消息树移到会话卡头部，记录点击改为跳转**：消息树按钮从覆盖首条消息的浮动位置移进聊天卡顶部、信息卡按钮左侧；点击记录只滚动并高亮该消息，不再切换会话 leaf。画布双击与面包屑仍走节点切换。
  - EN: The message-tree button moved from its floating spot over the first message into the chat header, left of the session-info toggle. Clicking a row now jumps to that message instead of switching the session leaf; canvas double-click and breadcrumbs still switch nodes.

- **面板标签改用指针传感器，浏览器入口不再重复**：面板标签与 rail 统一使用指针传感器，替换旧版鼠标传感器；浏览器由内置 surface 承载，配套插件不再额外注册一个 rail 入口。
  - EN: Panel tabs now use the same pointer sensor as the rail instead of the legacy mouse sensor, and the browser pane is launched by its built-in surface: the companion plugin no longer registers a second rail entry.

- **空态聚焦光带改为入场强调**：欢迎页输入框的聚焦光带只扫 3 秒后停止，不再在打字时常驻重绘；专注模式不显示光带。
  - EN: The welcome composer's focus beam is now an entrance accent: it sweeps for three seconds and stops instead of repainting while you type, and focus mode never shows it.

- **设计模式与产物画廊更易浏览**：创作类型 chip 各带一个图形，chip 排可以扫视区分；起点模板按当前类型筛选，选图片/音视频时不再出现原型模板；产物画廊在存在多种产物类型时提供类型筛选（含数量），单一类型时不加多余控件。
  - EN: Creation type chips carry one glyph each so the rail is scannable; the start-from shelf follows the active shape instead of offering prototypes for media, and the design-output gallery offers kind filters with counts when a scan holds more than one kind, with no extra chrome for a single-kind list.

- **会话信息卡在各颜色主题下都清晰可读**：卡片内的次级文字改为从卡片主文字色派生两档，不再直接取主题的 `--fg-faint`（部分深色预设低到 oklch 0.42，叠在提亮的玻璃上几乎看不见）；短标签在窄列里改为单行省略，不再把中文逐字折成竖排；图标按钮与折叠标题补上原生 button 背景/边框重置，深色预设下不再出现浅色实心方块。
  - EN: The session info card stays readable across colour presets. Secondary text is derived from the card's own primary text instead of the theme's `--fg-faint` — as low as oklch 0.42 in some dark presets and nearly invisible on the lifted glass. Short labels now ellipsise on one line instead of stacking CJK glyphs vertically, and icon/disclosure buttons reset their native buttonface fill so they stop painting as pale solid blocks.

- **右侧面板标签拖拽的落点与手势调整**：分栏落点不再在 `onDragStart` 的同一帧切换 `disabled` 与 `display`，碰撞检测在传感器没有指针坐标时回退到被拖标签中心并改用稳定引用，手势护盾改为常驻、只翻转命中状态；标签条改为桌面按下鼠标即激活拖拽、触摸长按激活，并把拖拽监听器放到属性末尾。
  - EN: Adjusted the right-panel tab drag pipeline. The split drop zone no longer toggles `disabled` and `display` in the `onDragStart` render, collision detection falls back to the dragged chip's centre when the sensor reports no pointer and keeps a stable callback, the gesture shield stays mounted and only flips hit-testing, and tab drags now arm on mousedown (long-press on touch) with activator listeners bound last.

- **拖文件到会话即可添加附件**：从系统拖文件到窗口任意位置时，会话卡上浮出「松开鼠标添加附件」的虚线锚点，指针在卡片（或欢迎页卡片）内才显示；松手把文件交给当前 composer，图片进入既有的缩放/附件流程，其他文件成文件 chip。窗口里无人认领的文件拖拽由该锚点接管：插件安装落点、弹窗和两个输入框各自处理自己的拖拽，锚点不抢落点；在卡片外松手不会产生附件、也不会让窗口导航到文件；Esc 或移出卡片即撤销。欢迎页输入框也补上了自己的 dragover 处理。
  - EN: Dropping OS files onto the session now attaches them. A dashed "Drop to attach files" anchor appears over the chat card while files hang over the window, and only while the pointer is inside that card (or the welcome card). Releasing stages the files on the mounted composer — images through the existing resize/attachment pipeline, other files as file chips. The anchor claims only unclaimed file drags for the window: the plugin-install dropzone, open dialogs and both composer textareas keep their own drop handling; a release outside the card stages nothing and never navigates the window to the file; Escape or leaving the card withdraws it. The welcome composer now prevents dragover on its own too.

- **附件改为内容寻址，不再写进项目目录**：输入框里的非图片附件在发送时经新增的 `attachment.put` 存入全局 blob 存储，提示词引用 `[Attachment] blob://<hash> (文件名)`，模型用 `read` 直接打开；同一份文件只存一次，跨会话复用，也不再污染工作区。`@文件` 提及展开为 `[Attachment:文件名]`，地址由上传行给出，避免指向不存在的路径。新增 `attachment.get`/`attachment.stat` 供分页取回与存在性探测，单次上限 8 MiB。
  - EN: Attachments are now content-addressed instead of being written into the project. A non-image attachment in the composer goes through the new `attachment.put` into the global blob store and the prompt references `[Attachment] blob://<hash> (filename)`, which the model opens with `read`; identical bytes are stored once, reused across sessions, and the workspace is left untouched. An `@file` mention expands to `[Attachment:filename]` and takes its address from the upload line, so it can never name a path that does not exist. `attachment.get` and `attachment.stat` cover paged retrieval and existence checks, each capped at 8 MiB per call.

- **产物引用可跨会话定位**：`artifact://` 新增 `artifact://<会话ID>/<序号>` 形态，按会话目录（含子代理一层）解析，序号在其它会话或设备上不再指错文件；旧的 `artifact://<序号>` 继续按当前会话优先解析。同时新增 `blob://<哈希>` 读取入口，二进制内容会被明确拒绝而不是解码成乱码。
  - EN: Artifact references can now name their session. The new `artifact://<sessionId>/<seq>` form resolves within that session's directory (plus one level of subagent output), so a bare counter can no longer point at a different file on another session or device; the legacy `artifact://<seq>` keeps resolving to the calling session first. A `blob://<hash>` read entry point was added alongside it, and binary blobs are refused explicitly instead of being decoded into mojibake.

- **大附件可以分片上传**：新增 `attachment.begin` / `chunk` / `commit` / `abort`，每片 3 MiB（base64 后 4 MiB），不再受单条请求 16 MiB 的限制，适合远程与移动端；分片可乱序重试，提交时校验总大小与空洞，未提交的上传 10 分钟后自动清理、同时最多 4 个。
  - EN: Large attachments can now upload in pieces. `attachment.begin` / `chunk` / `commit` / `abort` send 3 MiB chunks (4 MiB as base64), so the 16 MiB per-request limit no longer caps the file size — the path intended for remote and mobile clients. Chunks may arrive out of order and be retried, commit verifies the declared size and rejects holes, and an uncommitted upload is dropped after 10 minutes with at most four open at once.

- **附件在加入输入框时就开始上传**：选择或拖入文件后上传立即在后台进行，发送时只等剩余部分，不再让大文件把发送按钮卡住；上传失败会在发送时自动重试（先按路径，再内联）。
  - EN: Attachments start uploading as soon as they join the composer. Picking or dropping a file begins the upload in the background, so send only waits for whatever is left instead of stalling on a large file; a failed upload is retried at send time (by path first, then inline).

- **图片可以直接引用内容地址**：`session.send` 与引导目标现在接受 `blob:sha256:` 形式的图片，运行时在交给模型前解析成真实字节；引用已失效时明确报错，而不是把空图发给模型。
  - EN: Images can now be referenced by content address. `session.send` and the guided-goal kickoff accept a `blob:sha256:` image, which the runtime resolves to real bytes before the model sees it; a reference whose blob is gone fails the send instead of handing the model an empty image.

- **大文本附件可以按行翻页**：`blob://<哈希>` 现在支持与 `artifact://` 相同的行选择器（`:N`、`:N-M`、`:raw:N-M`），超过 8 MiB 的文本附件不再被整篇拒绝——读取按需切片，不会把整份内容读进内存。
  - EN: Large text attachments can now be paged by line. `blob://<hash>` accepts the same selectors as `artifact://` (`:N`, `:N-M`, `:raw:N-M`), so a text attachment above 8 MiB is sliced on demand instead of being refused as a whole, and the full content is never loaded into memory.

- **本机附件不再受上传大小限制**：桌面端选择或拖入的文件改为把路径交给运行时自己读盘，字节不再经过单条请求的 16 MiB 上限（内联 base64 实际上限约 12 MiB）；剪贴板与没有路径的片段仍走内联上传，路径读不到时也会自动回退。两种方式写入同一内容寻址存储，引用形态不变。
  - EN: Attachments picked or dropped on the same machine now hand their path to the runtime, which reads the file itself, so the bytes never enter a JSON-RPC frame and the 16 MiB per-request cap (about 12 MiB of inline base64) no longer applies. Clipboard blobs and paths the daemon cannot read still upload inline, with an automatic fallback. Both paths write the same content-addressed store and produce the same reference.

- **右侧面板分栏可拖动调整宽度**：两列之间新增分隔线（7px 命中带 + 1px 细线），拖动按指针位置重算列 0 的占比，每列至少保留 160px；面板窄到放不下两列最小值时下限退化为各占一半，并保留 15% 的结构下限。双击回到均分，聚焦后 ←/→ 每次 2%（Shift 10%）。占比随该会话的面板布局持久化，旧布局按均分恢复；拖动期间沿用全屏手势护盾，指针越过浏览器页面也不会中断。
  - EN: The right panel's split columns can now be resized by dragging the divider between them (7px hit band, 1px hairline). A drag maps the pointer to column 0's share and keeps at least 160px on each side; when the panel is too narrow for two such columns the floor degrades to an even split, on top of a 15% structural floor. Double-click recentres, and a focused divider steps 2% per arrow key (10% with Shift). The share persists with that session's panel layout and older layouts restore to an even split. The drag reuses the full-screen gesture shield, so crossing the browser page never stalls it.

- **右侧面板标签带支持稳定拖拽排序与分栏落点**：落点判定改为按指针位置给出唯一结论（`lib/panel-tab-dnd.ts`），不会再在标签与整列区域之间来回跳；在标签自己所在列的空隙松手会排到该列最近的其它标签，拖到右半区仍表示新建一列，标签带外侧 48px 内松手也能落在最近标签上。单栏与分栏的标签行改用同一个头部组件，行高、内边距与分隔线一致，开关分栏不再改变标签行高度；新列滑入、分栏目标淡入与被拖动标签的抬升一并补齐，并受动效开关与系统减少动态效果约束。
  - EN: Right-panel tab strips now reorder and split reliably. Drop resolution returns one verdict per pointer position (`lib/panel-tab-dnd.ts`) instead of flipping between a chip and the column-sized zone: releasing in a gap inside the dragged tab's own column reorders to that column's nearest other chip, releasing over the right half still means "new column", and a release up to 48px outside the strip lands on the nearest chip. The single and split layouts share one header component, so row height, padding, and rule match and toggling split no longer changes the strip's height. The new column slides in, the split target fades in, and the dragged chip reads as lifted, all disabled by the motion setting and reduced-motion.

- **会话信息卡分区可由插件扩展**：任何 `chat.session-info.<id>` 贡献现在自动渲染为信息卡的一个分区，`chat.session-info.plugins` 仍保留内置回退；插件新增自己的分区不再需要改宿主。
  - EN: Any `chat.session-info.<id>` contribution now renders as its own session-info section, while `chat.session-info.plugins` keeps its built-in fallback; a plugin can add a section without a host change.

- **上下文维护更安全**：`/shake` 与信息卡「移除工具输出」在恢复快照写入失败时不再静默抹除内容；历史改写失败会回滚内存条目，避免内存与磁盘不一致。TUI 执行 `/shake` 后清空终端回滚区；GUI 在移除后重新读取对话，不再继续显示被替换前的工具输出，并可从状态行打开被移除内容的快照。信息卡新增「重新加载对话」，在本轮回复进行中禁用维护动作，并在没有可移除内容时明确提示。
  - EN: Context maintenance is safer. `/shake` and the info-card "Drop tool output" action now fail closed when the recovery snapshot cannot be written instead of eliding content silently, and a failed history rewrite rolls the in-memory entries back so memory cannot drift from disk. The TUI clears the scrollback after `/shake`, and the GUI re-reads the transcript so it stops showing the pre-shake tool output, with a status-line link to the removed content's snapshot. The info card gains Reload conversation, disables maintenance while a response streams, and says so when there is nothing left to remove.

- **运行中的前台子代理可转后台**：任务卡片的运行中子代理行新增「转后台」；点击后本轮不再等待，该子代理继续跑完，结果经新增 `agents.detach` 接管为后台 `task` 作业后照常投递到对话。并发额度随运行一起转交（不会提前释放），宿主不支持该能力时按钮不显示。
  - EN: A running foreground subagent can move to the background from its task-card row. The turn stops waiting while the agent keeps running, and `agents.detach` adopts it into a `task` background job whose result arrives through the usual delivery channel. The spawn concurrency slot moves with the run instead of being released early, and hosts without the capability hide the action.

- **插件市场预置 Superpowers 与 Modern Web Guidance**：两个已审查插件无需添加 marketplace 即可见，由用户自行安装、启停和更新，默认不自动下载或启用；自定义目录继续保留。市场与安装后的卡片均显示上游品牌图标，加载失败回退到原有矢量图标。详情页展示完整介绍、能力与技能，支持真实启停；搜索、分类、视图和打开的详情在重载后恢复，详情源码由 daemon 缓存持久化。安装只在缓存中适配，不修改上游源目录，升级保留禁用状态。Superpowers 复用现有 Pi 入口；Modern Web Guidance 注册同名技能和 `modern_web_guidance` 工具，支持 search/retrieve/list，执行版本固定为已安装包版本，取消、60 秒超时或超过 256 KiB 输出上限都会终止子进程。
  - EN: The plugin marketplace now lists the reviewed Superpowers and Modern Web Guidance plugins without adding a marketplace. Users choose when to install, enable, disable, or update them; nothing downloads or activates automatically, and custom marketplaces remain intact. Marketplace and installed cards show upstream branding, with the existing vector icon as the image-failure fallback. Details now include the full introduction, capabilities and skills with real enablement; search, category, segment and the open detail survive reloads, and detail sources persist in the daemon cache. Installation adapts only the cache without changing upstream sources, and upgrades preserve disabled state. Superpowers reuses its existing Pi entry. Modern Web Guidance registers its skill and a `modern_web_guidance` tool with search/retrieve/list, pins execution to the installed package version, and kills the child process on cancellation, its 60s deadline, or the 256 KiB output cap.

- **实验性子 Agent 配置可自动检测**：Codex、Claude Code、Kimi Code、CodeBuddy 与 OpenCode 插件详情打开时自动检测 CLI，路径失焦或手动重检也会显示真实版本与解析路径；找不到或执行失败会说明原因。环境变量 JSON 在失焦时校验，修正时立即清除旧错误。检测只验证可执行文件，不虚假保证原生模型或凭据有效。
  - EN: Experimental subagent settings now check themselves. The Codex, Claude Code, Kimi Code, CodeBuddy, and OpenCode plugin details automatically probe the CLI on open and on path blur or manual recheck, showing its real version and resolved path, with explicit errors when it is missing or fails. Environment JSON is validated on blur and stale errors clear as the user corrects it. A successful probe verifies only the executable and does not falsely claim that a native model or credential is valid.

- **子代理会话作为右侧面板标签页打开**：agents 花名册行与子会话行的面板按钮不再只能停靠面板内抽屉，而是在右侧面板打开该子代理的独立标签页（同一 agent 只保留一个标签页），复用抽屉的实时 transcript、统计、错误提示与输入框；会话已结束或重启后快照丢失时显示空态，并给出在主对话中打开该子会话的入口。
  - EN: Subagent sessions now open as right-panel tabs. An agents-roster row, or a sub-session row's panel action, opens that agent's own tab instead of only the in-pane drawer, reusing the drawer's live transcript, stats, error dock, and composer. One tab per agent; a tab whose agent is gone shows an empty state with an action to open the child session in the main chat.

- **Shell 精简统计与原始输出预览**：设置 → Shell 展示按字节聚合的节省量、加权压缩率和过滤器明细，并开放自定义 TOML、命令白名单/排除、捕获上限和旧版过滤器设置。Bash 卡片显示单条压缩率，可在所属会话中打开只读原始输出弹窗；预览限制 8 MiB，统计不保存命令、路径或输出。
  - EN: Settings → Shell now shows measured byte savings, weighted reduction, and per-filter totals, plus controls for custom TOML, command allow/exclude lists, capture limits, and legacy filters. Bash cards show per-command reduction and open a read-only raw-output preview in the owning session; previews are capped at 8 MiB, and the savings journal retains no commands, paths, or output.

- **对齐 KimiCode 2.5.1 的四处修复**：窄行无法停靠时信息卡不再自动浮出遮挡 transcript，只在点开关后出现；本地图片路径含空格或中文时（渲染器会把它们转成 `%20`/`%E4…`）按解码后的真实路径读取；隐式项目在没有会话再指向它时清除；删除项目的会话从会话列表隐藏但仍可按 id 恢复，重新登记同一目录只恢复删除之后新建的会话（对应 KimiCode 的已删工作区口径）。
  - EN: Four KimiCode 2.5.1 fixes are absorbed. A row too narrow to dock no longer auto-floats the session info card over the transcript — it waits for the toggle. Local image paths with spaces or non-ASCII characters (the renderer encodes them as `%20`/`%E4…`) are decoded before the file is read. Implicit projects are pruned once no session points at their cwd. Sessions of a removed project leave the session list but stay resumable by id, and registering the same folder again restores only sessions created after the removal, matching KimiCode's deleted-workspace contract.

- **视频成为一等内容块**：wire 与 `packages/ai` 的用户消息、工具结果都能携带 `video` 块（`url` 优先、`data` 兜底），`session.send` 接受 `videos` 参数；Gemini 在模型声明 `video` 输入时走 `fileData`/`inlineData`，其余供应商用 `[video omitted]` 占位而不静默丢弃；transcript 的用户消息与通用工具卡就地播放。`agnes_video_gen` 的结果卡与异步完成行也复用同一个播放器。输入框里选择/拖入/粘贴的视频只要桌面桥能读到路径，就以 `file://` 视频块随消息发出（粘贴的无路径片段仍走工作区文件通道），欢迎页首条消息与「引导目标」同规则；所选模型声明视频输入时，本地片段在会话边界读成内联字节（≤20MB），更大的片段在请求构建时经供应商 Files API 上传并附 `providerFile` 引用（Gemini）。TUI 对视频显示 `[video: <mime>]` 标记。内联视频字节在持久化时外置到会话 blob 存储、加载时还原，与图片同一机制。
  - EN: Video is now a first-class content block: user messages and tool results in the wire and `packages/ai` accept a `video` block (`url` first, `data` as the inline fallback), and `session.send` takes a `videos` parameter. Gemini sends `fileData`/`inlineData` when the model declares video input; every other provider degrades the block to a `[video omitted]` marker instead of dropping it silently. The transcript plays user-message videos and generic tool-result videos inline. A video picked, dropped or pasted into the composer becomes a native `file://` video part when the desktop bridge can read its path (a pasted clip without a path stays on the workspace-file channel); the welcome composer and the guided-goal kickoff use the same rule. When the selected model declares video input, a local clip up to 20 MiB is read into inline bytes at the session boundary, and a larger one is uploaded through the provider's Files API when the request is built (Gemini), with a `providerFile` reference attached to the request copy. The TUI shows `[video: <mime>]` for video blocks. Inline video bytes are externalized to the session blob store on persist and resolved on load, the same way images are. The `agnes_video_gen` result card and its background-job completion row reuse the same player.

- **子代理继承父会话审批**：新增 `task.approvalMode`（默认 `inherit`）。`inherit` 时子代理沿用父会话的 `tools.approvalMode`，审批/提问弹窗转发到父会话 UI；父会话没有 UI 或显式设 `yolo` 时回退自动批准，避免无界面时卡死。子代理等待审批期间信息卡显示“等待审批”，授权或拒绝后自动清除。
  - EN: Subagents now inherit the parent session's approval surface through the new `task.approvalMode` setting (default `inherit`). With `inherit`, the child reuses the parent's `tools.approvalMode` and forwards approval/ask dialogs to the parent UI; without a parent UI, or with `yolo`, it auto-approves as before so headless runs never hang. While a child waits on a dialog, its info-card row shows Awaiting approval and clears when the dialog settles.

- **复制会话快捷键**：`Mod+Alt+C` 复制全部对话为 Markdown，`Mod+Alt+Shift+C` 只复制最终总结；输入框、文本域和可编辑区域保持原生复制。
  - EN: `Mod+Alt+C` copies the whole conversation as Markdown and `Mod+Alt+Shift+C` copies only the final summary; inputs, textareas, and editable regions keep native copy.

- **信息卡显示当前分支 PR**：当前分支关联 PR 时，改动统计下方显示 `PR #编号` 与开放/草稿/已合并/已关闭状态，点击在系统浏览器打开；没有 PR 或非 git 目录时不渲染。
  - EN: The session info card now shows the current branch's PR below the change totals — number plus open/draft/merged/closed — and opens it in the system browser on click. It stays absent without a PR or outside a repository.

- **信息卡子代理行显示成本与耗时**：运行中/已结束的子代理行直接读取 `AgentProgress` 的 `cost`、`durationMs` 与状态，结束行显示实际耗时；不再需要打开子代理详情才能看到花费。阻塞状态仍无独立字段，未伪造。
  - EN: Subagent rows in the session info card now show the cost and duration carried by `AgentProgress`; finished rows use the measured duration instead of the local first-seen timer, so spend is visible without opening the detail panel. There is still no explicit blocked field, so none is invented.

- **会话卡顶部工具条与信息卡推拉**：对话/地图切换移到会话卡左上角，信息卡开关留在右上角并改用 morphicons 形变图标；两者改为同一行布局，不再浮在消息上遮挡首行与悬浮引用按钮。会话信息卡停靠列按右面板同一弹簧折叠/展开，transcript 随之推拉，浮动座关闭时先淡出再卸载。
  - EN: The Chat|Canvas switch moves to the chat card's top-left and the session-info toggle stays top-right with a morphing icon; both now sit in one layout row instead of floating over the transcript and covering the first row and its hover quote action. The docked info column folds and unfolds on the right panel's spring so the transcript pushes and pulls with it, and the floating card fades out before it unmounts.

- **MCP 设置支持浏览器 OAuth 登录**:设置 → MCP 服务器列出已配置连接器，OAuth 类通过 `mcp.oauth.start/status/cancel` 在系统浏览器完成授权，凭据写入 auth storage 后自动重连并刷新工具；不提供 token 输入框，Token/stdio 凭据仍由 mcp.json 或子进程管理。
  - EN: MCP settings now support browser OAuth. The configured connector list shows transport, URL, and auth kind; OAuth entries authorize through `mcp.oauth.start/status/cancel` in the system browser, the credential lands in auth storage, and the connection reconnects with refreshed tools. No token input is offered; token and stdio credentials stay in mcp.json or the child process.

- **粘性当轮提问**:消息区向上滚动后,当前轮次的用户提问以单行玻璃胶囊钉在顶部,点击回到该提问并释放底部跟随;提问行可见、滚到顶部、空文本或顾问开启的轮次不显示。
  - EN: The current turn's user prompt stays pinned at the top of the transcript as a one-line glass pill after scrolling up; clicking it returns to that prompt and releases follow. It hides when the prompt row is visible, at the top, empty, or when an advisor opened the turn.

- **修复 9 处圆角渲染为直角**：`--radius-full` 被 `.gui-chip`、`.sh-pull-indicator`、`.sh-attach-remove`、`.ss-search`、`.ss-search-clear`、`.ss-grabber`、`.ss-item-dot`、`.gui-pr-review-outdated` 引用但从未定义，`var()` 失效让 `border-radius` 回退到初始值 `0`。现在于 tokens.css 定义为 9999px（胶囊与圆点共用，按盒子一半收敛），半径守门脚本同时校验每个引用都有定义，未定义时 CI 直接失败。
  - EN: Nine corners rendered square because `--radius-full` was referenced by `.gui-chip`, `.sh-pull-indicator`, `.sh-attach-remove`, `.ss-search`, `.ss-search-clear`, `.ss-grabber`, `.ss-item-dot`, and `.gui-pr-review-outdated` but never defined, leaving `border-radius` at its initial `0`. The token is now defined as 9999px in tokens.css (one value for capsules and round dots, clamped to half the box), and the radius gate also fails CI on any reference without a definition.
- **连接器管理回到 MCP 设置，撤销独立集成页**：不设「集成」概念；MCP 连接器在设置 → MCP 服务器管理，插件在扩展与插件市场安装。安装/移除连接器仍会 reconcile live manager，并把刷新后的 MCP 工具集推给运行中会话。
  - EN: Connector management moves back into Settings → MCP servers, with no separate Integrations concept; plugins install from Extensions & plugins. Adding or removing a connector still reconciles the live manager and refreshes running sessions' MCP tool set.


- **引用卡改为非模态，网页右键「标注」直达元素卡**：引用/标注卡打开时聊天与浏览器页面仍可滚动；网页右键菜单里的「标注」直接按指针位置打开结构化元素卡，不再要求二次框选；关闭「引用发送截图」后不再先捕获再丢弃。
  - EN: Quote and annotation cards are non-modal, so the transcript and browser page keep scrolling while they are open. The page context menu's Annotate entry opens the structured element card at the pointer without a second selection, and turning off Include screenshot no longer captures an image that would be discarded.

- **伙伴显隐与启动页改为粒子过渡**：显示伙伴时粒子光球在球体位置汇聚，球体从 0.72 弹入并淡入（420ms，弹簧缓动）；隐藏时球体先碎成粒子散开淡出，主进程再等 280ms 才收起窗口，期间再次显示会取消这次收起。启动页开机时运行同一个粒子光球，就绪后用 300ms 交叉淡入静态品牌球体。开启「减少动态效果」时粒子与缩放全部关闭，显隐保持瞬时；Petdex 精灵图不受影响。
  - EN: Showing the companion is now a transition: the particle orb converges at the sphere's box while the brand sphere springs in from 0.72 scale and fades up (420ms, spring easing). Hiding dissolves the sphere into scattering particles first and only then hides the window, 280ms later — a show inside that window cancels the pending hide. The startup splash runs the same particle orb while booting and cross-fades to the static brand sphere over 300ms once the shell is ready. With reduced motion the particles and the scale are dropped and showing/hiding stays instant; Petdex sheets are unaffected.

- **默认伙伴改为内置球体品牌形象**：`DEFAULT_PET_ID` 指向内置矢量球体 `musepi-note-bot`——球壳加上眼动追踪、眨眼与情绪表情，耳机配饰，以及按压/拖拽弹簧。设置与首次启动的伙伴列表以它开头，其后保留全部 Petdex 精灵图预设；显式保存的选择优先，未知 id 回落到球体。粒子光球 `ThinkingOrb` 不再是伙伴形象，继续用于智能体头像与顶栏活动指示器。
  - EN: The default companion is now the builtin vector sphere, `musepi-note-bot` — the shell with its eye-tracking, blink and mood faces, the headphones accessory, and the press/drag spring. Settings and first-run list it first, keep every Petdex spritesheet preset selectable, honour an explicitly stored pick, and fall back to the sphere for an unknown id. The particle orb (`ThinkingOrb`) is no longer a companion; it remains the agent avatar and the top-bar activity indicator.

- **营销预览改为真实渲染器 + 夹具 daemon**：站点交互预览直接运行桌面渲染器，`build:web-demo` 从 `dist/` 组装 `web/app/`，并用 daemon 自己的插槽编译器产出 `app.chat`、`panel.tab.files`、`panel.shell` 组件；`web/demo-src/` 用页面内 JSON-RPC mock 替换 `window.WebSocket`，会话、文件与 git 应答照真实 daemon 的形状录制。socket 目标固定为死端口 `ws://127.0.0.1:9/demo`，夹具失效时只会停在连接页，不会连到本机 8300 的 daemon。`verify-web-demo.mjs` 用无头 Edge 断言外壳、欢迎输入框、会话 transcript、零个 8300 请求与零个真实 WebSocket 连接。
  - EN: The site's interactive preview now runs the real desktop renderer against a fixture daemon. `build:web-demo` assembles `web/app/` from `dist/` and compiles the `app.chat`, `panel.tab.files`, and `panel.shell` slot components with the daemon's own compiler; `web/demo-src/` swaps `window.WebSocket` for an in-page JSON-RPC mock whose session, file, and git answers follow recorded daemon shapes. The socket target stays the dead port `ws://127.0.0.1:9/demo`, so a broken fixture set can only reach the connect screen and never a daemon on 8300. `verify-web-demo.mjs` drives headless Edge and asserts the shell, welcome composer, session transcript, zero :8300 requests, and zero real WebSocket connections.



- **HTML 卡片标注使用结构化面板，表格加宽可持久化**：会话内 HTML 卡片的标注与浏览器页面共用同一张结构化元素卡；表格加宽状态写入本地偏好，重新挂载或切换会话后仍保持，恢复默认即清除。
  - EN: HTML cards in the session now open the same structured annotation card as browser pages, and table width preferences persist across remounts and session switches, clearing when the table returns to its default width.

- **标注面板显示结构化元素信息**：选中网页元素时显示标签/类名、定位与几何、语义与状态、data-* 属性、页面与捕获信息，并可切换「引用发送截图」；区域与手绘只显示几何与评论。关闭开关后不再附加截图。
  - EN: Element annotations now show structured details — tag and classes, position and geometry, semantics and state, data-* attributes, and page/capture info — plus an Include screenshot toggle. Regions and strokes show geometry and comment only, and turning the toggle off omits the screenshot attachment.

- **插件状态条移入会话信息卡**：运行中插件状态条与逐项运行/禁用明细显示在信息卡的插件分区，能力中心不再重复；插件状态仍是全局（profile 级），与 DSH 的插件管理一致。
  - EN: The running-plugin status strip and its per-plugin state details now live in the session info card's plugin section, with no duplicate in the capability center. Plugin state remains global (profile-level), matching DSH's plugin manager.

- **AI 重命名即时反馈**：成功后会话标题立即更新，不再等待整棵树刷新；请求期间被重命名的会话行显示行内 spinner、状态条同步提示并禁用菜单，空会话、标题模型失败、离线、超时与旧 daemon 分别给出可操作提示。
  - EN: AI rename now updates the session title immediately instead of waiting for a tree refresh; while the request runs, the target row shows an inline spinner, a status strip names the session, and the menu item is disabled. Empty sessions, title-model failures, offline, timeout, and old-daemon cases each explain their own fix.

- **旧浮动状态卡移除，信息集中到会话信息卡**：Git 改动、连接器、运行中插件、子代理与待办全部由同一张会话信息卡承载；子代理显示进度与已用时，待办显示当前任务与完成数。运行中插件状态条移到能力中心，欢迎页暂存的连接器选择在会话创建后立即写回。
  - EN: The old floating status card is gone; Git totals, connectors, running plugins, subagents, and todo progress all live in the session info card. Subagents show progress and elapsed time, todos show the current task and count, the running-plugin status strip moves to the capability center, and connector selections stashed on the welcome screen flush as soon as the session exists.

- **本地文件预览 scheme 改为 musepi-file**：受管浏览器用 `musepi-file://` 提供 HTML 等本地预览；旧 `omp-file://` 不再接受，已持久化的面板/浏览器标签在读取时自动迁移到新 scheme。Windows 盘符路径仍正确落到 `file:///D:/...`。
  - EN: The managed browser's local preview scheme is now `musepi-file://`. Legacy `omp-file://` is no longer accepted; persisted panel and browser tabs migrate to the new scheme when loaded. Windows drive paths still resolve to `file:///D:/...`.

- **表格可加宽，会话可复制为 Markdown**：表格工具栏新增「加宽表格 / 恢复默认宽度」；会话菜单新增「复制全部对话为 Markdown」与「仅复制最终总结」，无内容时禁用。
  - EN: Tables gain Widen / Restore width in their toolbar, and the session menu adds Copy all conversation as Markdown and Copy final summary, disabled when there is nothing to copy.

- **引用带锚点并在原文高亮，选区保留 Markdown**：引用会记录文本位置并在源消息中高亮，点击引用卡跳转并闪烁；代码块、表格、强调与链接从渲染 DOM 还原为 Markdown，长选区不再截断。
  - EN: Quotes now record an anchor, highlight the source text, and jump-and-flash from the quote card. Selections preserve code fences, tables, emphasis, and links as Markdown, and long selections are no longer truncated.

- **会话信息卡分区可插件扩展**：运行中插件分区改由 `chat.session-info.plugins` Cordis 插件贡献，插件停用或编译失败时回退内置列表。
  - EN: Session info sections are plugin-extensible. The running-plugins section is contributed by the `chat.session-info.plugins` Cordis plugin, with the built-in list as the fallback when it is disabled or fails to compile.

- **会话信息卡收拢连接器、插件与改动入口**：停靠卡改为内容自适应高度，深色下不再是一整列空卡片；连接器勾选与运行中插件列表直接放进卡内，输入框与状态卡不再保留第二套入口；改动数字可打开 Git，可打开表面超过 6 项折叠为「查看更多」。
  - EN: The session info card now owns connectors, plugins, and change entry points. The dock shrinks to its content instead of painting an empty column in dark mode, connector selection and running plugins live inside the card, the composer and status cards keep no duplicate entries, change totals open Git, and more than six openable surfaces fold behind More.

- **引用工具栏可带评论添加引用**：选中文本后点「评论」，在浮层里输入评论并添加；评论随引用卡进入输入框，可继续编辑，发送时进入引用正文。文件、PDF 与 Office 选区同样支持。
  - EN: The selection toolbar can add a quoted selection with a comment. Choosing Comment opens an inline field; the comment travels with the quote card into the composer, stays editable, and becomes part of the quoted body on send. File, PDF, and Office selections support it too.

- **HTML 文件可在侧边面板浏览器中完整打开**：HTML 预览头部新增侧边面板浏览器按钮，通过受管浏览器的本地预览通道渲染页面，样式、脚本与相对资源都能加载，页面内相对链接继续导航；Windows 盘符路径的本地预览 URL 也已修正。
  - EN: HTML files can open fully in the side panel browser. The HTML preview header adds a side-panel action that renders through the managed browser's local preview channel with styles, scripts, and relative assets, and relative links keep navigating inside the page. Local preview URLs for Windows drive paths are fixed as well.

- **会话信息卡改为停靠/悬浮双座位，不再占用右侧面板标签**：右侧面板收起时，信息卡停靠成会话右侧的独立列，不覆盖聊天内容；面板展开时默认收起，点头部信息按钮后以圆角玻璃浮卡悬浮在会话上。浮卡层级低于状态卡、菜单与 tooltip，状态卡与连接器信息始终可见。
  - EN: The session info card now has two seats instead of a right-panel tab. With the panel collapsed it docks as its own column at the session's right edge without covering the transcript; with the panel open it stays collapsed until the header button shows it as a rounded-glass card floating over the session. The card layers below status cards, menus, and tooltips, so connector info stays visible.

- **右侧面板最大化/还原恢复可靠**：最大化收敛为唯一控制器，点击与状态、图标、`aria-pressed` 在同一次提交内同步；动效进行中再次点击会从当前矩形反向，还原回到记住的停靠宽度，切换标签、折叠再展开后面板几何仍然正确。
  - EN: Panel maximize/restore is reliable again. One controller owns the transition, committing the state, icon, and `aria-pressed` together with the geometry. A click during the morph reverses from the current rectangle, restore returns to the remembered docked width, and geometry stays correct across tab switches and collapse/expand.

- **历史会话管理改为 Kimi 式页面并做成 Cordis 插件**：设置中的会话页由 `settings.tab.sessions` 插件接管（插件不可用时回退内置页），支持项目/状态/更新时间筛选、搜索、可排序分页表格、最后一条 prompt、批量归档/恢复/删除与打开会话；会话默认值和保留策略保留在同一页。
  - EN: Session history management is now a Kimi-style page delivered by the `settings.tab.sessions` Cordis plugin, falling back to the built-in page when unavailable. It adds project/status/time filters, search, a sortable paged table with the last prompt, batch archive/restore/delete, and open-session; session defaults and retention stay on the same page.

- **项目名称在设置等位置统一显示文件夹名**：设置、归档、定时任务、记忆与用量视图不再显示完整路径或磁盘工作区键；重名目录自动加父级区分，完整路径只保留在悬停标题中，标题栏回退也按文件夹名处理。
  - EN: Project names now read as folder names across settings, archives, scheduled tasks, memory, and usage views instead of full paths or on-disk workspace keys. Duplicate folder names widen with parent segments, full paths remain in hover titles, and the header fallback uses the folder name too.

- **浏览器网页内右键菜单，浮层打开时点击网页即关闭**：页面右键提供复制、粘贴、标注、检查元素、复制选中文字的链接，不可用项禁用；右键菜单、rail 悬浮提示等浮层与网页宿主统一层级，菜单打开时在页面上覆盖透明关闭层，点击网页直接收起而不必先点面板空白处。
  - EN: The browser page now has its own context menu with copy, paste, annotate, inspect element, and copy-link-of-selection, disabling what does not apply. Menus and rail tooltips share one overlay stack above the page host, and while a menu is open a transparent dismissal layer covers the page so clicking the page closes it without first clicking panel chrome.

- **每轮末尾显示来源卡与改动文件卡**：来源卡汇总本轮浏览，按读过/经过分组，显示重复访问次数、完整 URL，并提供右侧打开、系统浏览器打开、复制链接；文件卡显示「N 个文件已修改」、本轮 +/−、前五行与「还有 N 个文件」，每行可打开文件，无法逐项统计时给出说明。无数据时两卡都不渲染。
  - EN: Each turn now ends with a sources card and a changed-files card. Sources group read and passed pages with repeat counts, full URLs, and open-in-panel, open-in-system-browser, and copy-link actions; the files card shows the changed count, the turn's +/−, the first five rows with "N more files", per-file open, and an explanation when per-file stats are unavailable. Both stay absent without data.

- **工具调用标题改为「已使用 X（关键参数）」**：内置工具头使用本地化名称与关键参数，执行中显示「正在使用 …」；工具选择调用也有独立卡片；未知工具保持原 wire 名且不追加空括号。
  - EN: Tool heads now read "Used X (key argument)" with localized names and key arguments, switching to "Using …" while running. Tool-selection calls get their own card, and unknown tools keep their wire name with no empty parentheses.

- **新增右侧会话信息面板（Cordis 插件）**：右侧 rail 出现「会话信息」入口，默认不打开标签（收起），内容含会话 ID 与复制、消息数、审批模式、工作目录、`provider/id` 模型、上下文占用条与 token 汇总；加载、空、错误三态齐全，切换会话不串数据。
  - EN: A Session info panel ships as a Cordis plugin. The right rail shows its entry, the tab stays closed by default, and the panel lists the session id with copy, message count, approval mode, working directory, the `provider/id` model, a context-usage bar, and token totals, with loading, empty, and error states that never leak across session switches.

- **项目与多工作目录持久化，取代前端 localStorage 项目表**：项目注册表落在 daemon 配置目录的 `projects.json`，支持新建、重命名、删除、设为主目录；项目菜单可添加/移除附加工作目录，重复目录就地提示；文件面板可在多个根目录间切换，隐式项目可一键转为托管项目。
  - EN: Projects and extra working directories are now persisted in the daemon's `projects.json` instead of the renderer's localStorage. Create, rename, delete, and set-primary are available, the project menu adds and removes extra directories with duplicate feedback, the file pane switches between roots, and implicit projects promote to managed in one step.

- **文件树可展开工作区内的软链接目录**：指向目录的软链显示链接徽标并可展开子项，展开受工作区边界与循环保护约束；工作区外软链仍是叶子，越界读取继续被拒绝。
  - EN: The file tree expands symlinked directories inside the workspace. Directory links show a badge and expand with boundary and cycle protection, while links pointing outside the workspace stay leaves and out-of-root reads remain rejected.

- **运行中分叉改为快照分叉，不再打断源会话**：流式输出或工具执行中从历史轮次分叉时，复制边界收敛到当前轮次的起点，源会话继续运行；同一源会话的并发快照分叉返回可重试错误，界面提示重试。branch、重试等操作仍保留原来的停止语义。
  - EN: Forking while the session runs now snapshots history instead of interrupting the source. When streaming or tools are active, the copy boundary collapses to the current turn's start and the source keeps running; concurrent snapshot forks on the same source return a retryable error and the GUI asks to retry. Branch and retry keep their existing stop semantics.

- **子智能体浏览器标签归属细化到 agent，浮层显示操作者**：同名标签不再被不同智能体互相复用，运行期标记当前操作者；子智能体结束只回收自己的标签，有父级时移交父级并保持打开，用户自己的连接/转发页面永不被智能体关闭。
  - EN: Browser tab ownership now follows the agent. Same-named tabs are no longer reused across agents, runs mark the active operator, and a finishing subagent releases only its own tabs — handing them to the parent when one exists — while the user's connected or relay pages are never closed by an agent.

- **插件市场支持更新徽标、升级与自定义源**：卡片显示已装版本与「v旧 → v新」，可单项或全部升级；市场分「已装 / 目录 / 自定义源」，源可添加、刷新、移除并显示逐源失败；安装、卸载、升级前统一确认来源与版本，安装时校验目录版本未漂移。
  - EN: The plugin marketplace shows installed versions and `vold → vnew` update badges with single and bulk upgrade. It splits into installed, catalog, and custom sources; sources can be added, refreshed, and removed with per-source failures shown; install, uninstall, and upgrade share one confirmation with source and version, and installs reject a catalog version that drifted after review.

- **启动页显示加载阶段，并可直接导出诊断或重启**：加载页显示当前阶段，超过 8 秒提示启动较慢；导出诊断在 daemon 未连通时也能工作，收集日志尾部、系统信息、daemon 探测与渲染层错误并返回保存路径；重启应用会先拆除自启动的 daemon，避免孤儿进程。连接失败页提供同样的两个入口。
  - EN: The startup page now shows the current phase and, after eight seconds, a slow-start note. Export diagnostics works before the daemon is reachable, collecting log tails, system info, daemon probes, and renderer errors and returning the saved path. Restarting tears down the daemon this app launched first, avoiding orphans, and the connection-failure page offers the same two actions.

- **插件入口按层级拆分，图标改用清单标记与透明卡框**：能力中心只列可启停或可配置的官方插件与已安装/自建插件；设置 → 扩展与插件保留完整库存（含只读提示词/渲染包与预设专用 persona），行内显示 id、只读/预设专用、配置与组件数等技术身份。插件图标改用清单声明的 emoji、字形或 URI，卡框改为透明底 + 0.5px 细边，图片按 contain 缩放且解码失败回退字形。
  - EN: Plugin entrances now split by layer, and plugin marks come from the manifest. The capability center lists only toggleable or configurable official plugins plus installed ones, while Settings → Extensions & plugins keeps the full inventory — read-only prompt/render packs and preset-only personas included — with ids, read-only/preset-only tags, and config/component counts per row. Plugin marks use the manifest's emoji, glyph, or URI; the card frame is transparent with a 0.5px hairline, images fit with contain, and a failed decode falls back to the glyph.

- **右侧面板按会话隔离，修复 rail/拖拽/设备尺寸与标注动效**：面板标签以会话为存储键，浏览器标签带 owner 会话，A/B 会话各自恢复分栏、标签顺序和网页；rail tooltip 提升到页面宿主之上，rail 不再挂全局皮肤滚动条，贪吃蛇与橡皮糖滚动条收窄；拖拽面板标签时加手势护盾，浏览器打开时仍可排序并拖出分栏；设备尺寸预设扩展为 iPhone/Pixel/Galaxy/折叠屏/iPad/桌面并新增宽高拖动滑块；标注元素悬停框改为 110ms 形变。
  - EN: Right-panel state is now session-scoped, including browser tabs and split layout. Rail tooltips sit above the page host, the rail opts out of the global skin scrollbar, and snake/gummy bars are slimmer. A tab-drag shield keeps reorder and drag-to-split working over the browser guest. Device presets now cover modern phones, foldables, iPads, and desktops, with width/height sliders beside numeric entry, and the annotation element box morphs over 110ms.

- **Markdown 可视化编辑直接渲染 README 原始 HTML**：文件面板的 `.md` 编辑器不再把 `<p align="center">`、`<img>` 和徽章链接显示成源码，而是按白名单净化后渲染（居中块、图片、可点击链接），相对图片仍按当前文件目录经桌面桥读取；脚本、事件属性和不安全 URL 被丢弃，源码在保存时原样写回。
  - EN: The file pane's visual Markdown editor now renders README raw HTML instead of printing it. Centered blocks, images, and badge links display through a sanitized node view, relative images still resolve against the file's directory through the desktop bridge, scripts/event attributes/unsafe URLs are dropped, and saving writes the original HTML back verbatim.

- **侧栏分组/项目在窄面板下变为纯图标，实例弹窗改用滑块 tab**：分组/项目胶囊按标签实际宽度布局，滑块跟随当前 pill；放不下文字时自动 morph 成图标 pill（保留 tooltip 与可访问名称），不再出现「分组项…」挤压。实例与远程工作区弹窗的 tab 换成共享 `Segmented` 滑块，实例页改为列表 + 添加实例双栏，输入框带标签，删除入口 hover/focus 出现，并去掉固定高度留下的大片空白。侧栏的搜索、全部展开/收起、归档三个动作改为状态图标 morph（search↔close、expand↔contract、archive↔unarchive），归档视图补上常显选中态；实例弹窗的「测试并添加」在提交中从 + morph 成加载圈。
  - EN: The sidebar group/project capsule now sizes pills to their labels with a measured thumb and morphs to icon-only pills (tooltips and accessible names preserved) when the pane cannot fit the text, so labels no longer clip. The instances & remote workspaces dialog uses the shared sliding Segmented control for its tabs, and the instances page is a list + add-instance two-column layout with labelled fields, a hover/focus remove action, and no fixed-height void. The sidebar's search, expand/collapse-all, and archive actions now morph with state (search↔close, expand↔contract, archive↔unarchive), the archive view keeps a persistent on-state, and the dialog's Test and add button morphs from + to a spinner while connecting.

- **会话树引导线在最后一个子会话处收口**：最后一个子会话的分支线改为 ⌊ 形，不再延伸进下一个根会话；某一层祖先没有后续兄弟时也不再画那条竖线，层级归属更清晰。
  - EN: **Session-tree guides close at the last child**: the last child's branch now ends in an elbow instead of running into the next root session, and an ancestor's vertical line disappears once that branch has ended, so nesting stays unambiguous.

- **状态图标统一走 morphicons**：剩余的状态切换图标从 oc-icons 的 `StateIcon` 改为 lucide stroke + `MorphIcon`（搜索/展开/归档、播放/暂停、眼睛、加载/完成、折叠箭头、模式选择等），并把 `morphicons` 依赖升到 `^1.7.1`；收藏星标/默认目标改为单字形 + CSS fill 过渡（morphicons 只插值 stroke，不能动画 fill）。
  - EN: **State icons now use morphicons**: the remaining state-swap icons move from oc-icons `StateIcon` to lucide stroke `MorphIcon` (search/expand/archive, play/pause, eye, loading/done, fold arrows, mode selection), and the `morphicons` dependency is raised to `^1.7.1`; the favorite star and default target now use one glyph with a CSS fill transition (morphicons interpolates strokes only, not fill).

- **更新日志按界面语言显示**：更新 Toast、更新弹窗和设置里的「检查更新」说明不再中英混排；manifest 只有一份双语字符串，渲染时按当前语言套用与「新功能」相同的 `- EN:` 选择规则。
  - EN: **Update notes follow the UI language**: the update toast, update dialog, and Settings → Check for updates no longer mix Chinese and English. The manifest ships one bilingual string, and rendering picks a language with the same `- EN:` rule as the What's New panel.

- **浏览器设备栏改为 KimiCode 式紧凑单行，标签菜单提供状态化操作**：尺寸预设、模拟宽高、页面背景与显示比例改为带当前值文本的下拉控件，背景选中项不再依赖悬停；隐藏设备栏时整条工具栏消失并停止设备模拟，显示时恢复原设置。浏览器标签右键菜单新增右侧新建、刷新、重命名、重命名后的标题保持、静音/取消静音与关闭右侧，空白页和 URL 状态决定各项是否可用。
  - EN: The browser device bar is now a compact KimiCode-style row: size preset, emulated dimensions, page background, and display scale are labelled dropdowns, so the selected background no longer depends on hover. Hiding the bar removes it entirely and stops device emulation; showing it restores the saved settings. Browser tab menus add new-tab-right, reload, rename with runtime title persistence, mute/unmute, and close-right, with availability derived from blank and URL state.

- **右侧面板共享宽度，选中会话自动揭示**：切换右侧标签不再跳变宽度，整个面板只记住一个宽度并把旧分表面值自动迁移；选中或新建会话时只展开其项目与父会话链，其他手动折叠保持原状。
  - EN: The right panel now shares one remembered width across tabs, migrating the first legacy per-surface value, and selecting or creating a session reveals only its project and ancestor-session chain while unrelated folds stay collapsed.

- **浏览器标签统一投影到右侧面板**：每个受管浏览器标签对应一个右栏标签，新增、切换、复制、关闭、分栏与 Agent 揭示双向同步，内部标签条移除；重启恢复由受管宿主重建投影，不再恢复失效的旧面板项。所有内置标签显示图标，浏览器使用 favicon 回退并在后台 Agent 活动时显示 badge；最大化/还原图标、tooltip 和可访问名称保持同步，动态标题不会被旧 tooltip 覆盖。
  - EN: Managed browser tabs now project one-to-one into the right panel. New, select, duplicate, close, split-column, and Agent-reveal operations reconcile in both directions, and the internal tab strip is gone. Restart restoration rebuilds projections from the managed host instead of restoring stale panel entries. Built-in tabs show icons, browser tabs use favicon fallback and expose background Agent activity, and maximize/restore icons, tooltips, and accessible names stay synchronized without stale tooltip text overwriting dynamic titles.

- **浏览器菜单与空白页动作模型对齐**：页内查找、打印、缩放、设备工具栏、保持焦点、截图、复制网址、系统打开与硬刷新统一只在非空白页面启用；下载、历史、清除浏览数据和浏览器设置保持可用。设备栏支持菜单控制显隐，空白页上的设备预设、尺寸、比例和背景控件禁用且不能通过键盘激活；打印、截图、下载与清数据失败会显示反馈；截图由主进程按下载策略保存 PNG，复制网址使用原生剪贴板。折叠时几何轮询不再覆盖隐藏状态，地址栏聚焦后的文本选择不会因事件对象释放而报错。
  - EN: Browser menu and blank-page actions now share one availability model. Find, print, zoom, device toolbar, page focus, screenshot, copy URL, system open, and hard refresh require a nonblank page, while downloads, history, data clearing, and browser settings remain available. The device toolbar supports menu-controlled visibility, and its presets, dimensions, scale, and background controls reject pointer and keyboard activation on blank pages. Print, screenshot, download, and cleanup failures surface feedback. Screenshots are saved as PNG by the main process under the download policy, and URL copying uses the native clipboard. Folded geometry polling no longer overrides the hidden report, and address-bar selection no longer reads a released event object.

- **浏览器工具调用变成可回看的操作时间线**：卡片头直接显示浏览页面数、点击/截图次数、失败数与总耗时，并提供「查看」跳到 Agent 浏览器标签；步骤按页面分组，带动作图标、目标/坐标、单步耗时和展开详情，缩略图可点击跳转。操作事件补充 `pageUrl`，导航后的步骤归到新页面；页面标题与元素数量继续保留。
  - EN: Browser tool calls now read as a reviewable operation timeline. The card header shows page count, clicks, screenshots, failures and total duration, plus a View button that jumps to the agent's browser tab. Steps group by page with action icons, target/coordinates, per-step duration, expandable details, and clickable thumbnails. Operation events carry pageUrl so steps after a navigation belong to the new page; page titles and element counts remain.

- **插件入口按粒度拆分，桌宠气泡状态跟随会话轮询**：能力中心的插件列表只显示内置的真实插件与用户安装/自建插件，完整的内置插件单元留在 设置 → 扩展与插件；设置里的清单保留整块表面，能力中心改用无外框紧凑行。桌宠气泡卡片在 `session.list` 轮询把会话从「工作中」更新后立即重推，不再停留在旧的「正在工作」。
  - EN: Plugin entrances now differ by scope, and pet cards follow the session poll. The capability center lists only built-in real plugins plus user-installed or self-built ones, while the full built-in unit inventory stays in Settings → Extensions & plugins; the settings list keeps its boxed surface and the capability center uses frameless compact rows. Pet bubble cards re-push as soon as the session.list poll flips a session out of working, so they no longer keep a stale "working" state.

- **会话列表与输入区细节对齐**：子会话的引导线与折叠按钮同列，子会话标题与父会话左对齐；顾问会话没有标题时显示「顾问会话」而不是未命名占位。窄视图下「思考」标签不再逐字竖排；运行中插件数移到输入框上方、与连接器同一行，并统一称为插件。设置里的插件列表改成管理清单（单列、分隔行、状态与开关），与能力中心的市场卡片（图标磁贴、分类、安装动作）形成明确区分。
  - EN: Session-list and composer details now line up. Child-session thread lines sit in the disclosure gutter so child titles share the parent's left edge, and an untitled advisor transcript shows "Advisor session" instead of the untitled placeholder. The thinking label no longer stacks vertically in narrow views; the running-plugin count moves above the input onto the connector row and uses plugin terminology. The settings plugin list is now a management list (single column, divider rows, status and toggle), clearly distinct from the marketplace's discovery cards (icon tiles, categories, install actions).

- **浏览器设置补齐 Agent 开关、下载策略、缩放与 UA**：设置 → 浏览器新增「允许 Agent 操控内置浏览器」开关、默认搜索引擎、每次下载前询问保存位置、Agent 下载位置与大小上限、默认缩放、自定义 User Agent。Agent 下载直接写入指定目录并在超过上限时取消并删除；用户下载按开关询问；缩放与 UA 应用到已打开和新建标签页，地址栏非 URL 输入走所选搜索引擎。
  - EN: Browser settings now cover agent control, downloads, zoom, and UA. Settings → Browser adds an Allow Agent Control switch, default search engine, ask-before-download, agent download folder and size cap, default zoom, and a custom User Agent. Agent downloads save straight to the chosen folder and are cancelled and deleted past the cap; user downloads ask when the switch is on; zoom and UA apply to open and new tabs, and non-URL address-bar input searches through the selected engine.
- **浏览器操作记录显示坐标、页面标题与缩略图**：托管浏览器里每次点击/填写/导航等操作在转录中显示目标元素坐标、当时页面标题，以及一张操作后的页面缩略图（每次运行最多 24 张），长流程不用回看截图也能定位动作落点。
  - EN: Browser operation rows now show coordinates, page title, and a thumbnail. In the managed browser, each click, fill, navigation, and similar step in the transcript shows the target's coordinates, the page title at that moment, and a small post-action page thumbnail (up to 24 per run), so a long flow stays readable without opening screenshots.
- **浏览器上传文件改为应用内确认**：托管浏览器里点击 `<input type="file">` 不再弹出系统对话框，而是显示「网站请求上传文件」卡片（站点、接受类型、取消 / 选择文件…），选定后才把文件交给页面；Agent 调用 `tab.uploadFile` 同样先经过这张卡片并列出文件名，取消或 60 秒超时不会向网站传送任何文件。上传动作写入浏览器操作明细（host、accept 与文件名），非托管后端保持原有行为。
  - EN: Uploads in the managed browser now ask in-app. Clicking an input type=file no longer opens a native dialog; the pane shows a "Website wants to upload files" card with the site, accepted types, and Cancel / Choose files…, and files reach the page only after you pick them. tab.uploadFile goes through the same card with the file names listed, and a cancel or 60-second timeout sends nothing to the site. Uploads appear in the browser operation details (host, accept, and file names), and other browser backends keep their existing picker behavior.

- **引用工具栏跟随选区末端并换成液态玻璃**：长文本选中时工具栏出现在松开鼠标的位置（键盘选择回退到选区最后一行），不再固定在整个选区顶部；面板改用共享的玻璃光泽、描边与液态弹簧入场。
  - EN: The quote toolbar now follows the end of the selection and uses the shared liquid glass. After a long selection it appears at the release point (keyboard selections fall back to the last line) instead of the top of the whole selection, and the chip uses the shared sheen, rim, and liquid-spring entrance.

- **修复压缩方式顺序设置项排版**：设置 → 上下文的「压缩方式顺序」不再把标签与说明挤成一字一行；有序设置改为纵向优先级列表（序号、名称、上移/下移/移除），未启用的方式通过「+」胶囊追加，数组胶囊控件统一换到标签下方整行。
  - EN: The Compaction Method Order row no longer squeezes its label and description into one character per line. Ordered settings now render as a numbered priority list (rank, name, move up/down, remove), unlisted methods are appended through "+" chips, and array chip controls take a full-width line below the label.

- **会话信息卡去掉重复入口**：连接器的设置入口收进连接器分区标题行（未配置连接器时仍保留引导入口），不再在列表下另起一行「MCP 服务器」；插件只保留分区自己的运行数 + 展开列表，移除上方重复的「N 个插件运行中」状态条及其浮层。
  - EN: The session info card no longer repeats its entries. The connector settings link moves into the connector section header (the empty state keeps its setup entry) instead of a second "MCP servers" row under the list. Plugins keep only the section's running count plus its expandable roster; the duplicate "N plugins running" strip and its popup are gone.

- **右面板收敛：context 改为上下文用量，任务并入 agents**：context 标签按会话内助手消息的 usage 汇总输入/输出/缓存读写四个 token 分桶、总用量、缓存命中率与会话花费，保留上下文窗口占用条与消息数/时长，移除与标题栏重复的 cwd、模型、模式和工作区路径、会话维护动作；任务面板不再单独占一个标签，改为 agents 视图底部可折叠的「任务」区块（折叠时不轮询）；复制工作区路径、抖动上下文、重置 provider 流与清空会话上下文移入会话信息卡标题行的「…」菜单，清空仍先确认。
  - EN: The right panel is consolidated. The context tab now summarizes the session's assistant-message usage — input, output, cache read, cache write, total, cache hit rate, and spend — next to the context-window bar and the message/time stats, while the cwd/model/mode rows that repeated the title bar and the copy-path and session-maintenance actions are gone. Jobs no longer take a rail surface of their own: they live in a collapsible Jobs block at the bottom of the agents view, which mounts its poller only while open. Copy workspace path, shake context, reset provider stream, and clear session context move into a "…" menu in the session info card header, and clearing still asks first.

- **桌宠气泡已读同步实时提醒**：点击完成或失败卡片的 ✓ 后，对应会话在实时提醒、侧栏和桌宠角标中同步变为已读；× 和清除全部仍只关闭气泡，不改变已读状态。
  - EN: Pet bubble acknowledgements now stay in sync. Clicking ✓ on a completed or failed card marks that session read across real-time reminders, the sidebar, and the pet badge; × and Clear all still only dismiss bubbles without changing read state.

## [0.7.1] - 2026-11-04

### Added
- **移动端会话搜索与下拉刷新**：SessionsSheet 新增按标题或 cwd 的即时搜索（大小写不敏感，Escape 先清空再关闭），打开时当前会话自动滚入可视区；transcript 顶部下拉可重连/重取快照，桌面与鼠标环境不启用，Rejoin 按钮仍是显式等价入口。
  - EN: Mobile sessions now have search and pull-to-refresh. The SessionsSheet filters by title or cwd as you type (case-insensitive; Escape clears the filter before closing) and scrolls the current session into view when opened. Pulling down from the top of the transcript reconnects and re-fetches the snapshot; desktop and pointer devices are unaffected, and the Rejoin button remains the explicit equivalent.
- **插件可以贡献自己的卡片类型**：新增 `registerWidget` 能力缝——插件声明类型、字段、默认值、色调与渲染器模块，daemon 侧用同一份元数据校验 `widget` payload、生成 agent schema 与看板校验，GUI 编译模块后注册进共享 widget 注册表。内置 `metric` 与 `clock` 已迁为试点，同名类型可覆盖内置渲染器；桌面聊天与 guest/移动端看板共用同一份插件加载器（`extensions.list.widgets` → blob import），插件卡片不再只在桌面可见；渲染器加载失败的卡片显示类型占位，不再留下空位。
  - EN: Plugins can now contribute their own card types through the new registerWidget seam. A plugin declares the type, fields, defaults, tone, and a renderer module; the daemon uses the same metadata for widget payload validation, the agent schema, and board validation, while the GUI compiles the module into the shared widget registry. The built-in metric and clock cards are the first migrated types, and a plugin type may override a built-in renderer. The desktop chat and the guest/mobile board share one plugin loader (extensions.list.widgets to blob import), so plugin cards are no longer desktop-only; a card whose renderer fails to load shows a labelled placeholder instead of a blank slot.
- **示例看板换成可直接使用的数据**：每日财经与工作台两张内建看板改为真实卡片内容（A股盯盘、汇率、K线、市场温度、今日待办、专注、劳务费个税等），移除纯演示卡与假任务记录；工作台不再叫「一块活的看板」。
  - EN: The built-in example boards now ship usable data instead of defaults. Daily finance and Workbench carry real card content (A-share watchlist, FX, K-line, market temperature, today's tasks, focus, labor-fee tax), and the demo-only cards and fake task history are gone. The second board is no longer named "A living board".
- **内联页面获得统一成品类与图表色板**：`widget` 的 `page` 面现在注入一套 `.musepi-*` 成品类——表格（宽了横向滚动）、数字对齐、状态胶囊、进度条、编号步骤、表单字段/输入/开关/分段控件，以及 `--chart-1` 到 `--chart-5` 图表系列色；主按钮改用主题的 `--accent-fg` 前景色，修掉 accent 背景上继承文字色可能不可读的问题，字号改用 rem 并跟随界面字号。提示词与 `widget-design` 技能同步列出这些类和版式规则。
  - EN: Inline pages now get one shared set of ready classes and a chart palette. The widget tool's page face injects the .musepi-* kit — tables that scroll sideways, aligned numbers, status badges, bars, numbered steps, and form fields, inputs, switches, and segmented controls — plus --chart-1 through --chart-5 series colors. The primary button now uses the theme's --accent-fg, fixing an unreadable inherited text color on the accent fill, and text sizes in rem so it follows the interface size. The prompt and the widget-design skill name the same classes and layout rules.
- **提示词注册表公开函数文本、严格变量与来源生命周期**：新增 `@musepi/pi-coding-agent/prompts/registry` 与 `prompts/orders`；区块文本可为每次组装求值的函数（返回 `null`/`undefined` 即省略），变量经 `registerVariable` 显式 opt-in 并严格解析（缺失即抛错），同名后注册遮蔽、按 source 整体卸载，最多一个 `complete` 区块替换整段提示词。旧入口 `composer.add` / `registerPrompt` 的字符串逐字保留、不参与严格插值，同名直接替换且不恢复旧层；`complete` 冲突在 disposer 与 `removeBySource` 中原子拒绝并保留 revision。插件 load/reload 按 source 立即重挂区块并刷新活动会话的基础提示词；`setDesignSystemId` 等仅提示词路径同样立即重建，均无需重启会话。核心模板、日期、cwd、skills/rules、工具清单与 persona/预设仍留在 `buildSystemPrompt`，尚未迁入注册表。
  - EN: Prompt sections now expose function text, strict variables, and source-scoped lifecycle. New @musepi/pi-coding-agent/prompts/registry and prompts/orders entry points let section text run per assembly and return null/undefined to omit it, variables opt in through registerVariable with strict resolution that throws when missing, registry registrations shadow earlier ones, whole sources unload together, and at most one complete section replaces the prompt. Legacy composer.add / registerPrompt strings stay byte-for-byte literal and never interpolate; a same-name add replaces the old layer outright, and complete-section conflicts are rejected atomically by disposers and removeBySource without bumping the revision. Plugin load/reload re-syncs sections by source and refreshes a live session's base prompt immediately; prompt-only paths such as setDesignSystemId also rebuild directly, with no session restart. Core templates, date, cwd, skills/rules, the tool inventory, and persona/preset data remain in buildSystemPrompt and are not migrated yet.
- **插件包可直接打包、分享并安装**：`musepi plugin pack [path] [-o dir] [--json]` 会校验清单和入口并生成 `.tgz`，接收方可通过插件页或 `musepi plugin install <绝对路径>` 安装。纯 Cordis 与 legacy 入口都使用生产挂载链校验，校验后释放资源；该命令只打包，不执行发布。
  - EN: Plugin packages can now be packed, shared, and installed directly. `musepi plugin pack [path] [-o dir] [--json]` validates the manifest and entries before producing a .tgz file, which recipients can install through the Plugins view or `musepi plugin install <absolute-path>`. Pure Cordis and legacy entries both validate through the production mount chain and release validation resources afterward; packing never publishes the package.
- **每会话提示词宿主接入会话装配**：每个会话（主代理与子代理）现在创建一个 `PromptHost`，插件工厂经 `pi.promptHost` 注册 section/context/variable/tool 贡献，并按 scope 继承、lane 优先级（默认 `core` < `extension` < `preset` < `session`）、owner 整组释放与 `cacheScope` 缓存粒度放置；装配传入活动工具集与 wire 名映射,host 区块按 baseSlots 锚定合并进基础提示词（`systemPrompt` 函数路径即子代理路径同样到达）;预设切换的启用/禁用方向、HMR 热重载与 preset 卸载按 owner 释放/重放贡献,不残留旧区块;会话 dispose 时关闭宿主,不再残留注册;空宿主时输出与之前逐字节一致。`CreateAgentSessionResult` 以可选字段暴露 `promptHost`。
  - EN: A prompt host is now wired into every session assembly. Each session (main agent and subagents) creates a PromptHost whose contributions plugins register through pi.promptHost — sections, contexts, variables, and tools placed by scope inheritance, lane priority (default core < extension < preset < session), owner-scoped bulk release, and cacheScope cache granularity; assembly passes the active tool set and wire-name mapping and merges host sections into the base prompt anchored by baseSlots — the systemPrompt-function path subagents take included. Preset enable/disable switches, hot reload, and preset teardown release or replay contributions per owner with no stale sections; session dispose closes the host so no registration lingers, and an empty host keeps the prompt byte-identical. CreateAgentSessionResult now exposes promptHost as an optional field.
- **核心提示词指引在所有会话中稳定挂载**：委派、文件工具、浏览器、电脑操作、LSP 与宿主集成六个第一方只读提示词包在每条会话加载路径（预加载复用、子代理、工具受限会话与完整发现）自动挂载到该会话的提示词宿主；路径按解析结果去重，核心包恰好挂载一次且按会话隔离、从不全局。关闭插件发现或使用预加载时，这些模型可见指引不再丢失。
  - EN: Core prompt guidance now loads reliably in every session. The six first-party readonly prompt packages — delegation, file tools, browser, computer, LSP, and host integrations — mount on each session's prompt host in every load path (preloaded reuse, subagents, tool-restricted sessions, and full discovery). Paths are deduped by resolved path so each core package mounts exactly once, per session and never globally. Disabling plugin discovery or preloading no longer drops this model-visible guidance.
- **项目上下文迁入会话提示词宿主**：AGENTS context 文件、工作区树与嵌套活动仓库改为会话提示词宿主上的 snapshot 区块，发现状态变化时替换对应区块而不是叠加副本；custom system prompt 布局仍在自己的模板中渲染 context 文件。承载发现内容的区块以 `interpolate:false` 注册——只跳过最终变量替换、花括号按字面保留，默认严格解析不变。
  - EN: Project context now lives on the session prompt host. AGENTS context files, the workspace tree, and the nested active repo render as snapshot sections that replace their section on discovery changes instead of stacking copies; the custom system-prompt layout keeps rendering context files in its own template. Sections carrying discovered content register with interpolate:false, which only skips the final variable substitution and keeps braces literal — strict resolution remains the default.
- **插件易变 context 接入每轮投递**：`pi.promptHost.registerContext` 现在真正接线——SDK 每轮用与装配相同的活动工具集和上下文调用 `collectContext`，`AgentSession` 把每条投递转为隐藏的 `role:"custom"`、`customType:"prompt-context:<name>"` 消息，走常规 provider 与持久化管线：模型可见、随会话保存、时间线不显示。`required` context 每轮投递；其余在与历史中最近一条同类型消息内容相同时跳过，而这份历史是活动、resume 或 branch 之后的最新副本，续接会话不会叠加重复。context 与区块一样支持 `interpolate:false`，`xd://` 设备文档中的花括号因此按字面保留；会话历史是唯一去重依据，压缩或装配中止不会把未再出现的旧副本提前记作已投递。
