  - EN: Plugin volatile contexts are now delivered every turn. pi.promptHost.registerContext is fully wired: the SDK calls collectContext each turn with the same active tool set and context it passed to assemble, and AgentSession converts every delivered context into a hidden role:"custom" message typed prompt-context:<name> that rides the normal provider and persistence pipeline — model-visible, persisted with the session, never shown in the transcript. A required context is delivered every turn; any other is skipped while the latest same-type message in history — live, resumed, or branched — already carries identical content, so resumed sessions never stack repeats. Contexts accept interpolate:false like sections, which keeps the braces inside xd:// device docs literal. Session history is the sole dedupe authority, so compaction or an aborted assembly cannot mark an older copy as delivered after it has left history.
- **导入会话改成两步式面板**：来源智能体与候选会话分两步呈现，选择来源后才扫描；会话按来源与工作区分组，可搜索、按工作区全选，已导入项就地标记并禁用，底部固定显示进度、失败与导入结果。引导弹窗内嵌同一组件，不再重复标题与说明。
  - EN: Import sessions is now a two-step sheet. Agent sources and candidate sessions are separate steps, and scanning starts only after sources are picked; results group by source and workspace, with search, per-workspace select-all, in-place imported markers that disable already-copied rows, and a pinned footer for progress, failures, and results. The onboarding sheet embeds the same component without repeating its title and description.
- **文件树与文件打开方式统一为桌面工作区的版式**：文件夹与文件共用 28px 行高、相同的展开箭头与图标槽位，文字列因此对齐；图标按代码、配置、数据、图片、表格、文档、音视频与压缩包区分。打开方式改为胶囊控件，显示记住的应用的真实图标，菜单可切换、设为默认或重新扫描。
  - EN: The file tree and open-with control now follow the desktop workspace layout. Folders and files share a 28px row height with the same disclosure and glyph slots, so text columns line up, and glyphs distinguish code, config, data, images, sheets, documents, audio/video, and archives. Open-with is a capsule that shows the remembered app's real icon, with a menu to switch, set the default, or rescan.
- **Markdown 文件预览渲染 README 的图片与链接**：预览会把 README 常用的原始 HTML（图片、链接、`<br>`、`<kbd>`、居中包装）转换为 Markdown 再渲染，相对图片按文件所在目录读取并内联，网页链接交给系统浏览器，工作区内路径在文件面板打开；页头不再重复「外部打开」按钮。
  - EN: Markdown file previews now render README images and links. The preview converts the raw HTML READMEs commonly use (images, links, br, kbd, centering wrappers) to Markdown before rendering, resolves relative images against the file's own directory and inlines them, sends web links to the system browser, and opens workspace paths in the file pane; the redundant external-open button is gone from the header.
- **浏览器设备工具栏可折叠并改用共享控件**：预设、尺寸、显示比例与页面背景改用分段控件、选择器与色板，折叠后只保留一行提示条，选择按窗口记住；键盘方向键可在背景选项间移动。
  - EN: The browser device toolbar is collapsible and uses shared controls. Presets, size, display scale, and page background now use the segmented control, select, and swatches, collapsing to a single reveal row whose state is remembered per window; arrow keys move between background options.
- **插件市场的卡片图标与空态不再使用表情符号**：卡片按分类与关键词映射到矢量图标，安装量、已安装等元信息改用图标；空目录与筛选无结果分别给出说明和「清除筛选」操作，加载失败时不再误显示空态。
  - EN: Marketplace cards and empty states no longer use emoji. Cards map categories and keywords to vector icons, with icons for installs and the installed badge; an empty catalog and a filtered-out result get distinct explanations plus a Clear filters action, and a fetch error no longer shows the empty state.

### Changed
- **运行时附录收编进 `host:runtime-context` 插件区块**：记忆指令、auto-learn 指南、MCP 挂载路由、MCP 服务器说明与用户 append 不再由会话拼接进基础 append 提示词（project 页脚与 customSystemPrompt 模板也不再承载这些内容），统一由 host-integrations 插件在 order 250 的 `host:runtime-context` 区块渲染——该区块 `interpolate:false`，服务器/用户文本中的花括号按字面保留；MCP 服务器说明改为按名称排序并保留 4000 字符截断。customSystemPrompt 布局中原位渲染用户 append，宿主侧省略同名副本避免双写；项目上下文两个区块同样改用 `interpolate:false`,删除字面花括号变量守卫。最终提示词每块内容仍只出现一次。
  - EN: Runtime appendices now render solely through the host:runtime-context plugin section. Memory instructions, auto-learn guidance, mounted MCP routes, MCP server instructions, and the user append no longer compose the session's base append prompt, and the project footer and customSystemPrompt template stop carrying them; the host-integrations plugin renders them all in its order-250 section with interpolate:false, so braces in server/user text stay literal, while MCP server instructions arrive sorted by name with the 4000-character truncation preserved. A customSystemPrompt layout keeps rendering the user append in its original position and the host omits its copy to avoid a double write; the two project-context sections also switch to interpolate:false, dropping the literal-brace variable guard. Every block still appears exactly once in the final prompt.
- **语音、面板最大化与建议状态也共享宿主实例**：`voice`、`panel-maximize`、`suggestions` 纳入冻结宿主模块表，播放/取消、最大化状态和贡献者订阅不再在宿主与插件之间分叉。
  - EN: Voice, panel maximize, and suggestion state now also resolve to shared host instances. voice, panel-maximize, and suggestions join the frozen host module table, so playback/cancellation, maximize state, and contributor subscriptions no longer diverge across host and plugins.
- **`board` 工具收编为内置 Cordis 插件 `musepi-board`**：工具、提示词与看板存储（`store.ts`）归插件包，`board.enabled` 经工具定义的 `enabled` 闸门继续生效；`board.list` / `board.save` RPC 与 `widget.schema` 仍由 daemon 暴露并读同一份存储与类型表，行为不变。
  - EN: The board tool now ships as the built-in musepi-board Cordis plugin. The plugin package owns the tool, its prompt, and the board store, while board.enabled keeps working through the tool definition's enabled gate. The board.list and board.save RPCs and widget.schema stay on the daemon and read the same store and type table, so behaviour is unchanged.
- **`widget` 收编为内置 Cordis 插件 `musepi-visuals`**：工具注册、类型表与提示词由插件包持有，内置会话工具表不再直接构造它；`widget.enabled` 与预设/用户的 `tools.disabled` 通过新增的 `ToolDefinition.enabled` 闸门继续生效，注册型工具也会遵守同一份禁用清单。看板校验、`widget.schema` RPC 与 GUI 渲染器读同一张类型表，行为不变。
  - EN: The widget tool now ships as the built-in musepi-visuals Cordis plugin. The plugin package owns its registration, type table, and prompt, and the built-in session tool slate no longer constructs it directly. The widget.enabled switch and preset or user tools.disabled lists keep working through a new ToolDefinition.enabled gate, and registered tools now honour the same denylist. Board validation, the widget.schema RPC, and the GUI renderer read the same type table, so behaviour is unchanged.
- **Monaco 运行时与 LSP 状态纳入共享宿主模块表**：编译插件对 `monaco-runtime` 与 `monaco-lsp` 的匹配 import 改为读取宿主发布的实例，不再各自复制 `monacoPromise`、全局 `MonacoEnvironment`、worker 工厂以及 LSP documents、provider 注册、peek model 与编辑器 opener；插件编辑器与宿主共用同一 Monaco 实例和同一套语言服务状态。
  - EN: Monaco runtime and LSP state now share the host instance. Compiled plugins resolve monaco-runtime and monaco-lsp imports to the instances published on window.MusePiHostModules instead of bundling a second monacoPromise, MonacoEnvironment, worker factory, document map, provider registrations, peek models, or editor opener, so plugin editors share the host's Monaco instance and language-service state.
- **slot-host 也共享宿主实例，React context 必须登记**：编译插件对 `slot-host` 的匹配 import 改为读取宿主发布的实例，slot 注册表与挂载函数不再复制进产物；`slot-host` 在模块加载时自注册，避免与宿主模块表形成循环依赖。新增静态守卫：`src/lib` 下创建 React context 的模块必须登记到冻结宿主模块表，已知状态模块也必须同时具备契约路径与发布实例。
  - EN: slot-host now shares the host instance, and React context owners must register. Compiled plugins resolve slot-host imports to the instance exposed on window.MusePiHostModules instead of bundling the slot registry and mount helpers, and slot-host registers itself as the module loads so the table gains no import cycle. A new static guard requires every module under src/lib that creates a React context to be listed in the frozen host module table, and pins the known state-bearing modules to both a contract path and a published instance.
- **共享状态与语言引用进一步对齐**：client-core 包内以 `../i18n/index.js` 等相对形式引用 i18n 时也归到同一个宿主 locale 服务，非 client-core importer 不受影响；`update-ux` 与 `tooltips` 新增为共享宿主模块，更新提示和气泡状态不再分裂。
  - EN: Package-relative i18n imports inside client-core now also resolve to the host locale service, leaving other importers untouched. update-ux and tooltips join the shared host module table, so update notices and tooltip state stay single across host and plugins.
- **插件与宿主共享 client-core 与语言状态**：`@musepi/client-core` 及 `@musepi/client-core/src/i18n` 也纳入冻结宿主模块表，插件不再复制 UI 原语与 locale 注册表，语言切换对宿主和插件同时生效。
  - EN: Compiled plugins now share the host's client-core namespace and locale state. @musepi/client-core and @musepi/client-core/src/i18n join the frozen host module table, so UI primitives and the locale registry are no longer duplicated, and language switches apply to host and plugins alike.
- **内置浏览器支持显式接管用户标签页**：`app.target` 可按 URL/标题子串命中已打开的用户标签页并直接在原地操作，不新建 agent 标签、不抢焦点，未命中时明确报错；无参数仍使用专用 agent 标签页。
  - EN: The built-in browser now supports explicit adoption of a user's open tab. app.target selects a tab by URL/title substring and operates on it in place, without creating an agent tab or stealing focus, and a miss is an explicit error. Without it, the dedicated agent tab is used.
- **编译插件共享宿主模块实例**：`prompt-dialog`、`managed-browser-host`、`action-toast`、`file-workspace`、`floating-menu` 的匹配 import 不再打包进编译产物，而是读取 `window.MusePiHostModules` 暴露的宿主实例；宿主未发布该表时导入即报错，不再静默退回副本。确认弹窗、toast、文件草稿和浮层互斥量因此在宿主与插件间保持同一份状态。
  - EN: Compiled plugins now share host module instances. Imports of prompt-dialog, managed-browser-host, action-toast, file-workspace, and floating-menu resolve to the instances exposed on window.MusePiHostModules instead of being bundled, and a host that does not publish the table fails at import rather than silently falling back to a copy. Confirmation dialogs, toasts, file drafts, and the floating-menu mutex therefore keep one state across the host and every plugin.
- **插件管理界面与语音设备检测统一**：普通包和模块使用相同的横向卡片，补齐中英文名称、说明与结构化「实验性」标签；详情页移除无关组件说明。语音详情和设置共用设备选择、热插拔刷新与实时音量检测，关闭界面即停止采集。
  - EN: Plugin management and voice-device monitoring now use consistent interfaces. Packages and modules share horizontal cards with complete Chinese and English names, descriptions, and structured Experimental badges, while irrelevant component prose is removed from details. Voice details and settings share device selection, hot-plug refresh, and live level monitoring, and closing the owning surface stops capture.
- **Cordis 热重载支持组合级传播与事务恢复**：共享源码变更会沿 importer 传播到所有受影响组合行，同一入口的多个行保留独立配置。重载先预导入再替换，失败时恢复旧实例；外部宿主模块仍要求重启。
  - EN: Cordis hot reload now propagates across compositions and restores transactions on failure. Shared source changes follow importer edges to every affected composition row, and rows sharing one entry retain independent configuration. Reload imports replacements before swapping fibers and restores old instances on failure; external host modules still require a restart.
- **顾问由会话级 Cordis 插件持有，Creator 快捷创建不再污染消息**：顾问的订阅、停止和记录器关闭纳入同一 fiber 生命周期，修正回合不会再次回喂顾问。快捷创建只发送原始需求，保存和校验指令移入 Creator 预设提示词。
  - EN: A session-scoped Cordis plugin now owns advisor subscriptions, shutdown, and recorder closure, and correction turns are not fed back for another advisor review. Creator quick-create sends only the original requirement, with save and validation instructions moved into the Creator preset prompt.
- **消息队列支持暂停、双端管理与跨重启恢复**：桌面和手机均可暂停或恢复投递、取回、删除、立即发送和清空队列，桌面另支持编辑与同组拖拽排序。消息、附件、顺序和暂停状态会随会话保存，重开应用后仍可继续处理；历史会话正确显示待发送数量，空对话中的首条排队消息也能在恢复后正常发出。
  - EN: Message queues now support pause, dual-client management, and restoration across restarts. Desktop and mobile clients can pause or resume delivery, restore, delete, send immediately, and clear queued messages; desktop also supports editing and same-group drag ordering. Messages, attachments, order, and pause state persist with the session for later handling after reopening the app. History shows the correct pending count, and the first queued message in an empty transcript sends correctly after resumption.
- **实例连接与版本状态统一管理**：桌面实例管理集中处理本地／远程 daemon 和 SSH 工作区，新增连接通过探测后才保存，侧栏不再单独显示「断开」。设置、新功能弹窗和实例管理统一区分桌面壳与 daemon 版本，版本不一致时提示重启应用，不再误报为应用更新。
  - EN: Instance connections and version status are now managed consistently. Desktop instance management covers local and remote daemons plus SSH workspaces, saves new connections only after a successful probe, and removes the separate sidebar Disconnect control. Settings, announcements, and instance UI distinguish shell and daemon versions, prompting for an application restart on mismatch instead of incorrectly reporting an application update.
- **会话页支持插件化管理和自定义问候**：欢迎页、消息流、输入区及关联面板由 `chat-view` Cordis 插件统一提供；宿主继续管理会话与工作区状态，停用插件不会留下第二套会话页。设置「常规」可自定义欢迎问候语并实时预览，留空恢复分时段问候。
  - EN: The chat surface is now plugin-managed and supports custom greetings. The chat-view Cordis plugin provides the welcome page, transcript, composer, and related panels while the host retains session and workspace state; disabling it leaves no second chat implementation. Settings → General offers a custom welcome greeting with immediate preview, and blank input restores the time-aware greeting.
- **桌面客户端浏览器默认使用内置面板**：桌面应用连接时，browser 工具默认把页面开进右栏内置浏览器，用户与 agent 共享同一浏览器实例与登录态；agent 使用可见的专用标签页，用户可随时观看、交互或接管。无头 Chromium 只在 CLI、无桌面宿主或用户显式关闭 `browser.gui` 时兜底，工具摘要、提示词、设置文案与文档不再把它写成默认行为。
  - EN: The desktop client now uses its built-in browser by default. When the desktop app is attached, the browser tool opens pages in the right-side panel, where the user and the agent share one browser instance and login state; the agent gets its own visible tab that the user can watch, interact with, or take over. A headless Chromium is only the fallback in the CLI, without a desktop host, or when `browser.gui` is explicitly off, and the tool summary, prompt, setting copy, and docs no longer describe it as the default.

### Fixed
- **动态加载插件的重启用与重载失败可恢复**：宿主级动态插件重载先预导入全部替代模块再动手，任何挂载失败都从保留的旧实例整批恢复，回滚不再重读磁盘；组件禁用后重新启用按重载语义重新求值入口，拾取禁用期间的修改，失败只隔离为该组件的 `failed` 记录；装载失败经效果账本反向回滚登记，不再留下半挂载 fiber 或重复注册。
  - EN: Dynamically loaded plugins now recover from failed re-enable and reload. Host-level dynamic reload imports every replacement module before touching active fibers and restores the whole batch from the retained old instances on any apply failure without re-reading disk; re-enabling a disabled component re-evaluates its entry like a reload, picking up edits made while it was disabled, and a failed re-mount is isolated as that component's failed record; mount failures roll registrations back through the effect ledger, leaving no half-mounted fiber or duplicate registration.
- **会话插件的提示词区块在重启用与重载失败后恢复**：动态首次加载插件的 PromptHost 注册现在会写入会话台账，禁用后重新启用可以重放，热重载失败也会恢复旧区块，不再永久丢失。
  - EN: Session plugin prompt sections now recover from re-enable and failed reload. PromptHost registrations made by a plugin first loaded at runtime are recorded in the session ledger, so disabling and re-enabling replays them and a failed hot reload restores the previous sections instead of losing them permanently.
- **记忆学习、清空与会话刷新保持一致**：通过实际 `learn` 工具保存的新经验会立即失效当前会话快照并刷新提示词；清空记忆同时删除按工作区根缓存的基线，daemon 对同一工作区的活动会话逐一失效并刷新，不再出现新经验不生效或旧经验在清空后复活。
  - EN: Memory learning, clearing, and session refresh now stay consistent. Lessons saved through the real learn tool invalidate the current session snapshot and refresh the prompt immediately; clearing memory also drops the workspace-root baseline, while the daemon invalidates and refreshes every live session in that workspace, preventing new lessons from staying invisible or cleared lessons from resurrecting.
- **压缩重试的技能消息保持排队**：重试后排空的技能调用不再被空闲会话立即自动续跑消费，`queueOnly` 会按契约保留在 follow-up 队列中且不启动新回合，消息与图片仍保留且不会提前写入会话历史。
  - EN: Skill invocations queued for compaction retry now stay queued. The queueOnly path no longer lets an idle session immediately consume the follow-up through automatic continuation; it starts no turn, preserves message images, and does not append the skill to session history prematurely.
- **`xd://` 设备文档不再破坏提示词装配**：挂载设备目录的文档含 `{{...}}` 序列时提示词装配不再抛错——设备目录区块以 `interpolate:false` 注册，花括号按字面保留，不触发严格变量解析。
  - EN: Mounted xd:// device docs no longer break prompt assembly. A device catalog whose documentation contains {{...}} sequences no longer throws during assembly — the section registers with interpolate:false, keeping braces literal instead of triggering strict variable resolution.
- **complete 提示词冲突改为 fail-closed**：host complete 区块与预设 `promptComplete` 或 composer complete 区块同时存在时显式报错；预设组合挂载失败且没有贡献任何提示词区块时同样报错，不再静默回退到默认提示词——那会把用户刚切走的工作 persona 悄悄换回来。
  - EN: Complete-prompt conflicts now fail closed. A host complete section competing with a preset promptComplete or composer complete section is an explicit error, and a preset whose packages failed to mount and contributed no prompt section also errors instead of silently falling back to the default prompt — which would quietly restore the work persona the user just switched away from.
- **会话导入改为幂等复制并补上安全提示**：同一来源会话再次导入会跳过并复用已导入副本（`imported-sessions.json` 台账；副本被删则允许重新导入），来源文件保持只读不变；选择界面新增搜索、已导入标记、逐条进度与失败列表，并明确「只读复制，不修改/删除来源」。
  - EN: Session import is now idempotent and states its safety contract. Re-importing the same foreign session is skipped and reuses the existing copy via an imported-sessions ledger (a deleted copy may be imported again), while source files stay read-only. The picker adds search, imported markers, per-item progress and a failure list, and states that import is a read-only copy that never modifies or deletes the source.
- **置顶小窗支持插件卡片**：`pin.html` 随 pin 记录携带编译后的渲染模块与元数据，启动后先注册再渲染；重启恢复的钉子也能显示插件卡片，i18n 与 React 共用宿主实例。
  - EN: Pinned widget windows now support plugin cards. pin.html carries the compiled renderer and metadata in the pin record, registers them before rendering, and restored pins keep working after a restart; i18n and React come from the host instance.
- **设置里关闭展示工具立即生效**：会话新增 `session.toolGates`，写设置后按 `widget.enabled` / `board.enabled` / `tools.disabled` 移除已挂载的注册型工具，无需重启；重新开启仍需新会话（该工具从未在本会话创建）。
  - EN: Turning a presentation tool off in settings now takes effect immediately. The new session.toolGates route removes mounted registered tools denied by widget.enabled, board.enabled, or tools.disabled without a restart; re-enabling still needs a new session because the tool was never created in this one.
- **看板首次保存前会先加载插件类型**：`board.save` / `widget.schema` 在读取进程索引前先执行一次会话无关的插件装配，GUI 启动后立刻保存插件卡片不再被误判为未知类型。
  - EN: A board save now loads plugin types first. board.save and widget.schema run a session-less plugin assembly before reading the process index, so a plugin card saved immediately after GUI launch is no longer rejected as an unknown type.
- **插件卡片类型进入运行时校验表**：`widget` 与 `board` 在调用时合并会话里已加载插件声明的类型，`board.save` / `widget.schema` 读取 daemon 进程索引，第三方 `registerWidget` 类型不再被判为未知；lookup 失败仍回退内建表。
  - EN: Plugin card types now reach runtime validation. The widget and board tools merge types declared by loaded plugins when called, while board.save and widget.schema read the daemon process index, so a third-party registerWidget type is no longer rejected as unknown; a miss still falls back to the built-in table.
- **注册型工具的会话闸门不再被迟到注册绕过**：`tools.disabled` 或工具自己的 `enabled` 判定为否的工具，在插件注册监听器或构造竞态补录时也会再次过滤；新增走真实会话装配的回归用例，防止「初始快照过滤、随后又被加回」。
  - EN: The session gate for registered tools is no longer bypassed by late registration. A tool denied by tools.disabled or its own enabled predicate is filtered again when a plugin registration listener or the construction-race catch-up runs. A new regression test goes through real session assembly so the initial snapshot cannot be undone afterwards.
- **模式上下文不再重复注入，日期与星期始终一致**：plan/goal/vibe 在流式进入时已通过 steer 持久化的上下文，下一轮提示词只在内容变化时重新投递，相同内容不再产生第二份；日期与星期改为从同一时刻派生，跨午夜也不会错配。日期、cwd、skills/rules、工具清单与 persona/预设仍由宿主 `buildSystemPrompt` 装配；项目上下文、记忆与 MCP 说明已改经会话提示词宿主与 `host:runtime-context` 区块渲染。
  - EN: Mode context is no longer injected twice, and date/weekday always agree. Plan/goal/vibe context already persisted by a streaming steer is re-sent only when its content changes, so identical content no longer produces a second copy; date and weekday derive from one instant, so a midnight boundary cannot split them. Date, cwd, skills/rules, the tool inventory, and persona/preset remain assembled by the host's buildSystemPrompt, while project context, memory, and MCP instructions now render through the session prompt host and its host:runtime-context section.
- **停止按钮恢复可用并立即中断**：Cordis 编译的 `chat-view` 插件不再自带一份确认上下文，停止按钮点击直接发送中断，不再被静默的确认默认值吞掉；与 Esc 的即时中断语义一致。树操作等仍需确认的入口继续使用宿主弹窗。
  - EN: The stop button now interrupts immediately. Compiled chat-view plugins reuse the host's prompt/confirm hooks instead of a bundled copy whose silent default swallowed the click, matching the existing Escape behavior. Confirmed actions such as tree operations still use the host dialog.
- **关闭动态效果时欢迎语立即完整显示**：`gui-motion-off` 下品牌字与问候语不再逐字计时显现，符合设置中「关闭动态效果」的承诺，低动效环境也不会残留定时更新。
  - EN: Welcome branding and greetings now appear in full immediately when motion is off. The stepped reveal no longer starts under gui-motion-off, matching the setting's promise and leaving no pending timer updates in low-motion environments.
- **开发客户端启动 daemon 时正确解析 Bun**：开发环境会从 `BUN_EXECUTABLE` 或 PATH 解析 Bun 的绝对路径，避免 Windows 首轮启动因子进程找不到裸 `bun` 命令而失败；启动错误同时包含退出码和子进程输出，便于定位连接失败。
  - EN: Development clients now resolve Bun correctly when starting the daemon. The launcher resolves an absolute Bun path from BUN_EXECUTABLE or PATH, preventing the first Windows launch from failing because the bare bun command cannot be spawned. Startup errors also include the exit code and child-process output to make connection failures diagnosable.
- **OpenCode 推理请求按会话血缘正确路由**：OpenCode 与 OpenCode Go 的 Completions、Responses、Anthropic Messages 和 Google Generative AI 统一发送会话亲和键，fork、task/Vibe 子代理和 advisor 与父会话共享路由，不再让不同对话默认共用安装实例 ID。独立会话 ID 仍用于凭据和遥测；关闭提示缓存不会改变路由，显式请求头保持优先。
  - EN: OpenCode inference now routes by conversation affinity. Chat Completions, Responses, Anthropic Messages, and Google Generative AI for OpenCode and OpenCode Go share the parent affinity across forks, task/Vibe subagents, and advisors instead of defaulting unrelated conversations to one install ID. Independent session IDs remain available for credentials and telemetry, disabling prompt caching does not change routing, and explicit headers retain precedence.
- **右栏与浏览器拆分为独立 Cordis 插件并恢复真实交互**：`panel.shell` 独立管理右栏，`browser.host` 与 `panel.tab.browser` 分离常驻网页宿主和浏览器页面，插件目录统一为 `panel`、`browser-ui` 并消除与后端 `browser` 的身份冲突。最大化由应用宿主直接提交面板几何，并先读取停靠位置、清除旧内联宽度再播放扩展动画；快捷入口可正常打开网页，HTML 预览支持全宽切换、相对 CSS 内联和 TSX 构建提示，预览控件不再误显示「试听」。
  - EN: The right panel and managed browser are now independent Cordis plugins with working interactions. panel.shell owns the right panel, while browser.host and panel.tab.browser separate the persistent guest host from the browser pane; plugin directories are normalized to panel and browser-ui to avoid the backend browser identity conflict. Maximize is committed directly by the application host, and its expansion animation now measures the docked rectangle and clears the stale inline width before changing state. Quick links open real pages, and HTML preview supports full-width switching, inlined relative CSS, and TypeScript/JSX build notices without the incorrect audition label.
- **会话切换、地图、轨迹与输入区布局恢复正常**：从顾问记录返回主会话会重新落在最新回合，不再复用旧 Transcript 状态。地图视口和缩放控件在横纵布局都避让输入区；轨迹显示模型与思考级别的前后参数；插件与消息队列 chip 在输入框上方对齐。面板最大化按钮增加指针事件隔离，Markdown 页边距与工具栏同步收紧。
  - EN: Session switching, map geometry, trajectory details, and composer layout are corrected. Returning from an advisor record now lands on the latest turn without reusing stale Transcript state. Map viewports and zoom controls avoid the composer in both orientations; trajectory events show previous and new model or thinking-level parameters; plugin and queue chips align above the input. Panel maximize controls isolate pointer events, while Markdown margins and toolbar sizing are tightened.
- **内置浏览器与 HTML 预览恢复完整显示和关闭生命周期**：浏览器等待渲染宿主就绪后创建 guest，按分区存储路径校验身份，并正确发送 target detach 事件，原生 `page.close()` 不再卡死。HTML 预览使用全高 iframe，并按当前文件解析相对资源。
  - EN: The managed browser and HTML previews now render and close correctly. Browser creation waits for renderer readiness, partition identity uses storage paths, and target detach events are emitted so native page.close no longer hangs. HTML previews use full-height iframes and resolve relative resources against the current file.
- **桌宠气泡操作与堆叠跟随对齐**：悬停或聚焦显示的快捷按钮改为横向排列并按实际数量展开，移除卡片左侧的强调色条纹。堆叠与展开形变期间逐帧同步气泡窗口尺寸，气泡底边与桌宠 sprite 始终保持 8px 间距，操作按钮展开不再引起窗口宽度抖动。
  - EN: Desktop pet bubble actions now appear in a horizontal row sized to the actual button count on hover or focus, and cards no longer show a left accent stripe. Bubble window size now updates on every frame of stack expansion and collapse, keeping its bottom edge 8px above the pet sprite and preventing width jitter when actions appear.
- **停止操作不再多发模型请求或留下顾问运行**：工具回合后的外部中断会直接结束主循环，已完成工具结果仍保留；用户停止同时暂停在途顾问并等待双方收敛。`/btw` 的内部队列清理也不会再改写原会话或误触分支一致性检查。
  - EN: Stop no longer issues an extra model request or leaves advisors running. An external interrupt after a tool round ends the main loop immediately while preserving completed tool results; user stop also pauses active advisor work and waits for both loops to settle. Internal queue cleanup for /btw no longer rewrites the original session or trips the branch consistency check.
- **自动恢复缺少会话插件的旧 daemon**：桌面启动时会检查 `chat-view` 插件清单；同版本旧 daemon 明确缺少该条目时自动重启，避免欢迎页和会话页同时空白。Windows 下增加 `Get-NetTCPConnection` 监听进程识别回退，重启不再因找不到 PID 而静默复用旧进程。探测超时或权限错误不会触发重启。
  - EN: Desktop startup now checks the chat-view extension manifest and restarts a same-version stale daemon when the entry is explicitly missing, preventing blank welcome and transcript views. On Windows, a Get-NetTCPConnection fallback resolves the listener PID when netstat cannot, so restart no longer silently reuses the stale process. Probe timeouts and permission errors do not trigger a restart.
- **右侧面板最大化后可正确还原分栏**：还原时先停用旧宽度过渡，再测量分栏目标，避免把最大化宽度保留下来；浏览器标签的损坏图标也会正确隐藏。
  - EN: Restoring a maximized right panel now returns to the docked split layout. The previous width transition is disabled before measuring the restore target, so the maximized width cannot persist. Broken browser-tab favicons are also hidden correctly.
- **Windows 安装器遵循所选语言并处理运行实例**：简体中文、繁体中文和英文界面不再统一显示为简体中文；安装、升级或卸载前会提示并关闭仍在运行的 MusePi，用户取消时保留现有安装。
  - EN: The Windows installer now follows the selected language and handles running instances. Simplified Chinese, Traditional Chinese, and English no longer collapse to Simplified Chinese. Install, upgrade, and uninstall prompt to close a running MusePi, while cancellation preserves the existing installation.
- **Markdown 所见即所得选中不再跳动，引用工具栏稳定出现**：首个选区出现时前导区块不再因临时 widget 消失而丢掉上边距，滚动条占位与滚动留白也固定下来；选择工具栏改为按事件解析容器并在文档级监听，面板首次挂载时没有预览容器也能绑定，键盘选择、滚动后选择与重新聚焦都能显示，折叠选区即消失。
  - EN: Selecting text in visual Markdown no longer shifts the document, and the quote toolbar appears reliably. The leading block keeps its top margin whether or not the transient ProseMirror widget is present, and the scrollbar gutter and scroll padding are fixed. The selection toolbar now resolves its container per event and binds at the document level, so it works even when the pane has no preview container on first mount, appears for keyboard selections, selections after scrolling, and after refocus, and dismisses when the selection collapses.
- **插件迟到的工具注册重新遵守处理超时**：注册监听器现在把当前处理器的取消信号传给激活队列，超时的处理器不再让挂起的工具激活退回到 30 秒兜底并阻塞后续注册。
  - EN: Late tool registrations from plugins now honour the handler timeout again. The registration listener passes the current handler's cancellation signal into activation, so a timed-out handler no longer leaves a stalled activation on the 30-second fallback blocking later registrations.
- **看板首页卡片恢复可聚焦且不再嵌套按钮**：卡片改为可聚焦的容器，回车或空格打开看板；静态预览不再把卡片内的复选按钮嵌进外层按钮，删除入口成为独立按钮，焦点恢复保持正常。
  - EN: Board home cards are focusable again and no longer nest buttons. The card is a focusable container opened with Enter or Space; its static preview no longer puts checkbox buttons inside an outer button, the delete control is its own button, and focus restoration still works.

## [0.7.0] - 2026-11-01

### Added
- **任务工具可调用五种外部 CLI 子智能体**：新增 Codex、Claude Code、Kimi Code、CodeBuddy 和 OpenCode 实验性 Cordis 插件，可分别配置可执行文件、原生模型与产品权限参数。插件只负责产品命令和协议适配，统一复用现有任务进度、后台作业、取消、超时、结果卡片和完整产物；加载或启停插件不会启动外部进程，也不会默认添加自动绕过权限或沙箱的参数。
  - EN: The task tool can now call five external CLI subagents. New experimental Cordis plugins for Codex, Claude Code, Kimi Code, CodeBuddy, and OpenCode provide executable, native-model, and product-permission settings. Each plugin owns only its product command and protocol adapter, while existing task progress, background jobs, cancellation, timeouts, result cards, and full artifacts remain shared. Loading or toggling a plugin starts no external process, and no automatic permission or sandbox bypass argument is enabled by default.
- **可以直接复用 Kimi Code 的技能、命令与 MCP 配置**：MusePi 会读取用户和项目 `.kimi-code` 配置，以及 Kimi Code 已启用插件中的 Skills、Commands 和 MCP；保留官方插件命名与单服务器启停覆盖。当前不接入 Kimi 的 hooks、agents、systemPrompt 或 sessionStart，Pi、OMP、Codex 和 Claude 的既有适配保持独立。
  - EN: Kimi Code skills, commands, and MCP configurations can now be reused directly. MusePi reads user and project .kimi-code configuration plus Skills, Commands, and MCP from enabled Kimi Code plugins, preserving official plugin naming and per-server enablement overrides. Kimi hooks, agents, systemPrompt, and sessionStart are not imported yet; Pi, OMP, Codex, and Claude retain their independent adapters.
- **Cordis 组合与生命周期统一**：插件页按同一卡片管理普通包和 profile bundle，不再单设组合页；preset 行在会话中挂载真实 Cordis fiber，支持按服务隔离的 realm，并在切换或销毁时清理工具与 effect。能力中心改为 `app.capability` 插件页面，Android skill 与工具、UI 同属一个插件生命周期。
  - EN: Cordis composition and lifecycles are unified. The Plugins page manages ordinary packages and profile bundles with one card model and no separate Composition tab. Preset rows mount real Cordis fibers in each session with service-isolated realms and clean up tools and effects on switches and disposal. The capability center is now an app.capability plugin page, and the Android skill shares one plugin lifecycle with its tools and UI.
- **Android 模拟器面板与移动调试插件**：Agent 截图后可按需提供右侧设备标签，查看实时画面、点按或滑动、发送导航键与文本、安装工作区 APK、启动 Activity，并读取 UI 树和 logcat；配套 `android_emulator` 工具与 skill 支持完整调试流程。模拟器和压缩摘要等会话页面不会占用常驻 rail 或新建标签菜单。ADB 与 Emulator 路径可在插件配置中指定。
  - EN: The Android Emulator panel and mobile debugging plugin let Agent screenshots open an on-demand device tab with live frames, taps, swipes, navigation keys and text, workspace APK installation, activity launching, UI trees and logcat. The android_emulator tool and plugin-owned skill support the full debugging workflow. Session-provided pages such as emulators and compaction summaries no longer occupy the permanent rail or new-tab menu. ADB and Emulator paths are configurable in the plugin settings.

### Changed
- **开发与发布运行时统一升级至 Bun 1.4.4**：CLI、桌面构建、GitHub Actions 和发布流程均从根 `package.json` 读取同一 Bun 版本，依赖安装、类型定义和原生包要求同步更新，纳入新版运行时的性能与安全修复。
  - EN: Development and release now use Bun 1.4.4 consistently. The CLI, desktop builds, GitHub Actions, and release workflows read the same Bun version from the root package.json, while dependency installation, type definitions, and native package requirements move together to include the runtime's performance and security fixes.
- **语音设置统一归入独立分类**：语音输入、朗读、语音供应商和 Live Voice 不再散落在供应商与交互页面；桌面和 TUI 使用同一分类，停用语音包只隐藏依赖控件，不会清空配置。文件发现的 hooks 也已确认通过真实 ExtensionRunner 执行，TUI 继续共享插件发现与启停状态。
  - EN: Voice settings now have one dedicated category. Speech input, read-aloud, speech providers, and Live Voice no longer appear across Providers and Interaction; desktop and TUI share the same category, and disabling a speech package hides only dependent controls without clearing configuration. Discovered hooks are also verified to execute through the real ExtensionRunner, while the TUI continues to share plugin discovery and enablement state.
- **Word、Excel 与 PowerPoint 支持安全编辑和精确引用**：可直接编辑结构安全的 Word 段落、Excel 单元格和 PowerPoint 文本形状，并沿用未保存标记、关闭确认、顶部保存与 Ctrl/Cmd+S。公式、复杂或超大工作簿保持只读，PowerPoint 明确采用文本版式预览。选中内容可复用现有引用工具栏，历史引用会恢复到对应段落、工作表与单元格范围或幻灯片形状。
  - EN: Word, Excel, and PowerPoint now support safe editing and precise references. Structurally safe Word paragraphs, Excel cells, and PowerPoint text shapes are directly editable with the existing dirty state, close confirmation, header save action, and Ctrl/Cmd+S. Formulas and complex or oversized workbooks remain read-only, and PowerPoint is explicitly a text-layout preview. Selections reuse the existing reference toolbar, and historical quotes restore their paragraph, sheet and cell range, or slide shape.
- **内置技能与预设纳入统一插件生命周期**：六个产品技能改为随 `musepi-product-skills` 插件包发现和启停，完整保留技能资源；设计与创作预设显式组合该插件，旧预设升级不会覆盖用户修改的插件行。安装包直接携带插件内技能，不再维护另一份 `bundled_skills` 副本。
  - EN: Built-in skills and presets now share the plugin lifecycle. Six product skills are discovered and toggled with the musepi-product-skills package while retaining their complete resources; design and creator presets explicitly compose that package, and preset upgrades do not overwrite user-edited plugin rows. Installers carry package-owned skills directly instead of maintaining a second bundled_skills copy.
- **第一方能力支持真实的插件与组件启停**：语音引擎、终端后端、浏览器后端、文件工具族、Computer Use 与 LSP 已迁移为真实 Cordis 包和独立 entry 组件。关闭包会卸载对应 fiber 并反注册能力，关闭单个组件不影响同包其他组件；能力中心读取实际运行状态。第一方与外部 MusePi 包统一使用包名和 `<package>/<component>` 组件键，不再保留第一方旧 RPC ID、设置镜像或旧禁用列表；功能领域设置仍独立控制对应行为。
  - EN: First-party capabilities now have real plugin and component lifecycles. Speech engines, terminal and browser backends, file tool families, Computer Use, and LSP are Cordis packages with independent entry components. Disabling a package unloads its fiber and unregisters its capabilities, while disabling one component leaves its siblings running; the capability center reads actual runtime state. First-party and external MusePi packages share package names and <package>/<component> keys, with no legacy first-party RPC aliases, settings mirrors, or legacy deny-lists; domain settings still control their respective features independently.
- **官网新增可交互的 0.7.0 能力浏览**：集中展示文件工作区、Office 安全编辑与精确引用、插件生命周期和五种外部子智能体，并注明实际使用边界。中英文同步，支持方向键切换、移动端两列入口和减少动态效果设置。
  - EN: The website now has an interactive 0.7.0 capability explorer. It covers the file workspace, safe Office editing and precise references, plugin lifecycles, and five external subagents with their actual usage boundaries. Chinese and English stay in sync, with arrow-key navigation, a two-column mobile index, and reduced-motion support.
- **文件工作区迁移为可启停的 Cordis 插件**：文件树、Monaco、Markdown、PDF、文档预览与引用界面由 `file-workspace` 插件统一提供，并沿用现有文件标签布局。宿主继续唯一管理标签、未保存草稿、定位请求和编辑历史；停用插件会移除文件入口，确认关闭后不会恢复已丢弃草稿。
  - EN: The file workspace is now an enableable Cordis plugin. The file-workspace plugin provides the tree, Monaco, Markdown, PDF, document previews, and reference UI while preserving existing file-tab layouts. The host remains the sole owner of tabs, unsaved drafts, reveal requests, and editor history; disabling the plugin removes file entries, and confirmed close never restores discarded drafts.
- **能力中心统一官方分组与入口生命周期**：插件管理列表按「已安装」「官方」分组，官方插件显示完整中英文名称与说明，不再因源码目录被误标为已安装或重复显示 preset、错误分组。能力中心停用后，侧栏、标签菜单、命令面板和外部跳转入口同步消失；已打开页面会返回聊天，重新启用后恢复。
  - EN: The capability center now has consistent official grouping and entry lifecycles. Plugin management lists Installed before Official, shows complete Chinese and English names and descriptions for official plugins, and no longer misclassifies them by source directory or repeats preset and error groups. Disabling the capability center removes its sidebar, tab-menu, command-palette, and external-navigation entries; an open page returns to chat and re-enabling restores access.
- **PR 评审可直接提交、定位代码并引用给 Agent**：开放且非草稿的 PR 可批准或要求修改，普通评论支持 Ctrl/Cmd+Enter；失败保留草稿，要求修改必须填写正文。详情页显示审查结论、讨论线程和可折叠 diff；当前右侧行可在固定文件标签中打开并定位到 Monaco 行号，评审与代码上下文可一键加入输入框。过期或删除侧评论、缺失行号及越界路径不会打开本地文件；PR 列表显示评论数，并在窗口重新聚焦时刷新陈旧列表。
  - EN: PR reviews can now be submitted, opened in code, and quoted to Agent. Open, non-draft PRs support approval and change requests, and Ctrl/Cmd+Enter submits general comments. Failed submissions preserve drafts, and change requests require a body. The detail view shows review conclusions, discussion threads, and collapsible diff hunks; a current RIGHT-side line opens a pinned file tab and reveals the exact Monaco line, while the review and code context can be added to the composer. Outdated or deleted-side comments, missing line numbers, and escaping paths cannot open local files. PR rows show comment counts, and stale lists refresh when the window regains focus.
- **应用内通知支持分级、详情与来源管理**：通知新增 warning 级别、独立标题和可展开详情；相同来源与去重键会替换旧通知，最多保留三条。来源恢复后可按来源关闭对应通知，错误、警告和状态反馈统一使用液态玻璃浮层。
  - EN: In-app notifications now support levels, details, and source management. Notifications add a warning level, a separate title, and expandable details; matching source and dedupe keys replace older entries, with at most three retained. A recovering source can dismiss its own notifications, while errors, warnings, and status feedback share the liquid-glass overlay.
- **手机、折叠屏与远程网页共用响应式外壳**：手机收起态输入框只保留附件、文字和语音／发送，聚焦或操作展开完整模型与编辑控制；短横屏自动使用紧凑布局，子代理栏仅在宽屏常驻。完整控制区切换不再卸载编辑器，键盘展开时可正常打开模型 Sheet，compact／medium 出现子代理也不会自动遮挡消息。
  - EN: Phones, foldables, tablets, and remote web now share a responsive shell. The resting phone composer keeps attachment, text, and voice/send controls, while focus or related actions reveal the full model and editing controls. Short landscape uses compact layout, and the agents rail is persistent only on wide screens. Control changes no longer unmount the editor, model sheets open correctly with the keyboard visible, and new subagents no longer automatically cover compact or medium transcripts.
- **移动端远程工作区视觉与导航重构**：连接页优先展示已保存电脑与扫码主按钮；多会话手机连接后直接进入带简洁空态和输入框的新会话草稿，会话列表收进底部 Sheet，并固定提供新会话、电脑管理和独立设置入口。顶栏只保留核心动作，外观、语音和断开连接集中进设置页；桌面网页继续保留工作区目录。
  - EN: The mobile remote workspace has revised visuals and navigation. The connect screen prioritizes saved computers and QR scanning. Multi-session phones now open a new-session draft with a quiet empty state and composer; the session sheet provides fixed New session, Manage computers, and Settings actions. The header retains only core actions, while appearance, voice, and disconnect controls move to a dedicated settings page. Desktop web keeps its workspace directory.
- **移动端统一主机管理与恢复**：已保存主机支持添加、切换、重命名和删除，元数据与房间凭证分离存储；启动、回前台和网络恢复按明确优先级处理，避免慢探测覆盖新连接或重复创建 socket。桌面分享界面及 RPC 新增「远程访问」命名，旧链接和 `collab.*` 调用继续兼容。
  - EN: Mobile now has unified host management and restoration. Saved hosts support adding, switching, renaming, and removal, with metadata stored separately from room credentials. Startup, foreground, and network recovery follow explicit precedence so slow probes cannot replace newer connections or create duplicate sockets. The desktop share UI and RPCs now use remote-access naming, while legacy links and `collab.*` calls remain compatible.
- **可写移动端 Guest 支持工具审批**：远程审批接入真实 daemon ApprovalBridge，由主机权威结算并同步关闭各设备请求；切换会话、结束会话和返回工作区会清理过期审批，只读链接保持只读。
  - EN: Writable mobile guests can now answer tool approvals. Remote requests use the real daemon ApprovalBridge, with host-authoritative resolution and completion propagated to every device. Switching or ending sessions and returning to the workspace clear stale approvals, while read-only links remain read-only.
- **球体桌宠拖拽改为连续矢量表演**：球体不再套用 Petdex 的行走帧、左右镜像或额外弹跳；拖动速度会形成连续倾斜与拉伸，释放后带阻尼落地，并与会话动作、交互动效和眼动自然组合。
  - EN: The orb desktop pet now uses continuous vector gestures while dragging. It no longer inherits Petdex walk rows, horizontal mirroring, or an extra bounce; pointer velocity produces smooth tilt and stretch, release settles with a damped landing, and these gestures compose with session motion, interaction reactions, and gaze.
- **桌宠会话气泡统一三态与打开行为**：气泡统一为 20px 圆角；桌宠旁唯一控件按展开、堆叠、隐藏、堆叠循环，隐藏时显示未读数量。点击堆叠卡片正文展开列表，点击展开卡片正文直接打开对应会话且保持展开；回复、审批和关闭等按钮不会误触正文操作。
  - EN: Desktop pet conversation bubbles now have consistent three-state and opening behavior. Cards use a 20px radius, and the single control beside the pet cycles through expanded, stacked, hidden, and stacked, showing the unread count while hidden. Clicking a stacked card body expands the list; clicking an expanded card body opens its conversation without collapsing the list. Reply, approval, dismiss, and other action buttons do not trigger the card body.
- **桌宠通知按新活动置顶并保留关闭边界**：新完成、出错或待操作通知只置顶一次，重复轮询不会反复抢顶或重开计数状态；关闭状态按活动版本或请求 id 记忆，新回合可以重新提醒。关闭单卡和清除全部不再自动标记已读或批准请求，未读仍由主窗口管理，审批必须显式决定。
  - EN: Desktop pet notifications now prioritize new activities once and preserve dismissal boundaries. New completions, errors, and actionable requests move to the front only once; repeated polling cannot steal priority or reopen count mode. Dismissal is remembered per activity revision or request id, allowing a later turn to notify again. Dismissing a card or clearing all no longer marks conversations read or approves requests; the main window owns unread state, and approvals require an explicit decision.
- **桌宠气泡升级为液态玻璃堆叠**：堆叠时显示优先卡片、近层与远层背卡及剩余条数，展开后保留完整快捷操作；卡片、清除胶囊、回复框和控件共享 MusePi 的玻璃材质与弹性动效，气泡底边与 sprite 保持 8px 间距，透明阴影区域不会被裁切。减少动态效果时直接显示完整文字并跳过尺寸形变。
  - EN: Desktop pet bubbles now use liquid-glass stacking. The stacked state shows the priority card, near and far card backs, and the remaining count; expansion retains the full quick actions. Cards, the clear-all capsule, reply field, and controls share MusePi's glass material and spring motion. The bubble bottom stays 8px above the sprite, and transparent shadow clearance is preserved. Reduced motion shows complete text immediately and skips size morphs.
- **桌宠消息统一主会话范围与动画坐标回报**：气泡、托盘菜单和欢迎页只显示主会话，顾问与子代理会话不再混入；辅助会话的审批仍然保留。sprite 动画期间持续按帧上报交互区域和气泡锚点，避免桌宠移动后点击区域或气泡位置滞留。
  - EN: Desktop pet messaging now uses a consistent main-session scope and animated coordinate reporting. Bubbles, the tray menu, and the welcome view show only main sessions, excluding advisor and subagent sessions while retaining approvals from auxiliary sessions. During sprite animation, the pet reports its hitbox and bubble anchor every frame so clicks and bubble placement do not retain stale coordinates.
- **引导页个性化改为真实桌宠预览**：可直接选择矢量球体、内置 Petdex 或已导入桌宠，实时查看光泽、配饰和显示位置效果；桌面模式会展示独立桌宠与玻璃气泡，Petdex 不再出现无效的装饰开关。
  - EN: Onboarding personalization now uses live pet previews. Users can select the vector orb, a built-in Petdex preset, or an imported pet and immediately preview gloss, accessories, and display placement. Desktop mode shows the independent pet with a glass bubble, while Petdex options no longer expose ineffective decoration controls.
- **桌宠眼神跟随更自然**：眼球快速跟随指针，头部与耳机以较慢速度跟随；指针静止 3 秒后平滑回中，眨眼间隔加入自然变化，同时保留各状态的凝视与禁止眨眼规则。
  - EN: Desktop pet gaze now feels more natural. The eyes follow the pointer quickly while the head and headphones respond more slowly; the head smoothly recentres after three seconds of pointer inactivity. Blink intervals vary naturally while each state's fixed-gaze and no-blink rules remain intact.
- **Git 更改对比升级为 Monaco Diff**：现有工作区更改面板可直接对照 HEAD、暂存区与工作区版本，支持语法高亮、并排或内联布局、长段折叠和上一处／下一处导航；未跟踪文件按新增内容展示，大二进制或读取失败时保留原有补丁视图。
  - EN: Git change review now uses Monaco Diff in the existing workspace panel. It compares HEAD, index, and working-tree versions with syntax highlighting, side-by-side or inline layouts, collapsed unchanged regions, and previous/next navigation. Untracked files appear as additions, while large, binary, or unreadable content retains the patch view.
- **文件编辑器升级为 Monaco**：文本文件编辑现在支持语法高亮、行号、代码折叠和明暗主题跟随；保留原有保存、未保存标记、离开确认和跨标签缓冲，并维持 CRLF、末尾换行、光标、选区与撤销历史。文件树单击使用可替换的预览标签，双击或跨文件定义跳转打开固定标签。编辑器接入已有 LSP 配置，支持诊断、悬浮提示、补全以及跨文件定义、实现和类型定义跳转；没有可用语言服务器时保留基础编辑。Ctrl+S 直接保存当前模型快照，避免丢失最后输入，保存期间继续输入仍保留未保存标记。
  - EN: The file editor now uses Monaco. Text editing gains syntax highlighting, line numbers, code folding, and light/dark theme tracking while preserving saving, dirty state, leave confirmation, cross-tab buffers, CRLF, final newlines, cursor position, selection, and undo history. A file-tree single click opens a replaceable preview tab, while a double click or cross-file definition navigation opens a pinned tab. The editor uses the existing LSP configuration for diagnostics, hovers, completions, and cross-file definition, implementation, and type-definition navigation; basic editing remains available without a language server. Ctrl+S saves the current model snapshot without dropping the latest input, and edits made during saving remain dirty.
- **Monaco 支持定义与引用速览**：按 Alt+F12（或 Ctrl/Cmd+Shift+F10）可内嵌查看和切换定义，按 Shift+F12 可查看和切换引用，均不离开当前文件；多个结果、未打开文件会自动加载，速览中的代码可直接编辑并用 Ctrl/Cmd+S 保存，Esc 关闭。速览采用统一的液态玻璃样式，原有 F12 跳转及编辑位置历史保持不变。
  - EN: Monaco now supports inline definition and reference peek. Alt+F12 (or Ctrl/Cmd+Shift+F10) shows and switches between definitions, while Shift+F12 shows and switches between references, all without leaving the current file. Multiple results and files that are not already open load automatically; code inside the peek is directly editable and saves with Ctrl/Cmd+S, while Esc closes it. Peek views use the shared liquid-glass style, and existing F12 navigation and editor-location history remain unchanged.
- **Monaco 支持 F2 跨文件符号重命名**：原生重命名输入框会修改所有受影响文件，包括尚未打开的文件；每个文件保留独立草稿、未保存状态和保存操作，保存前不写入磁盘。定义速览、引用速览和重命名快捷键已纳入统一的固定「编辑器」分组，重命名浮层使用液态玻璃样式。
  - EN: Monaco now supports F2 cross-file symbol rename through its native input. Every affected file, including files not already open, retains an independent draft, dirty state, and save action; nothing is written before each file is saved. Definition peek, reference peek, and rename shortcuts now appear in the shortcut registry's fixed Editor group, and the rename overlay uses the shared liquid-glass style.
- **文件定义跳转支持后退与前进**：同文件和跨文件定义跳转都会记录编辑位置；文件头新增后退／前进按钮，也可使用 Alt+←／Alt+→，返回时恢复精确光标位置。后退后发生新跳转会清空前进记录。
  - EN: File definition navigation now supports backward and forward movement. Same-file and cross-file jumps record editor locations; new header buttons and Alt+Left / Alt+Right restore the exact cursor position. A new jump after going backward clears forward history.
- **文件页标签与阅读编辑流程更完整**：文件树打开首个文件后直接进入文件页，文件页可展开带动效的非模态树，并在独立标签中打开其他文件；点击当前文件不会重复打开。文本直接编辑，HTML 支持实时预览与源码视图。Markdown 默认使用单栏所见即所得编辑，顶部工具栏保持单行，溢出命令收进「更多操作」；切换全宽源码时保留实际修改，未修改文档保留原始源码，Front Matter 提供提示和快捷源码入口。路径栏支持横向滚动，路径层级可打开锚定的可搜索目录菜单。代码与 Markdown 选区可引用、询问、复制、新建会话或添加笔记，Markdown 还能把当前未保存内容导出为真正的 Word 文档。
  - EN: File tabs now support a complete reading and editing flow. The first file consumes its tree tab, while the file page's animated non-modal tree opens subsequent files in separate tabs without duplicating the current file. Text is directly editable, and HTML provides live preview plus source-only views. Markdown defaults to single-column WYSIWYG editing with a one-row toolbar and a More actions overflow menu. Full-width source mode preserves real edits, unchanged documents retain their original source, and Front Matter provides a notice with a quick source action. Breadcrumbs scroll horizontally and open anchored, searchable directory menus. Code and Markdown selections support quoting, asking, copying, new sessions, and notes. Markdown exports the current buffer, including unsaved edits, as a real Word document.
- **PDF 阅读器支持按需渲染与结构化引用**：内嵌阅读器提供缩略图、页码跳转、缩放、旋转和中文搜索；单行工具栏将放不下的操作收进「更多操作」。选中 PDF 文字可引用、询问、复制、新建会话或添加笔记；跨页引用保留起止页码、字符索引和每页矩形几何，点击后恢复起始页和原选区。输入框将文件与会话引用分别折叠成计数芯片，完整内容在弹层中查看；加载失败可重试或改用系统默认应用。
  - EN: The PDF reader now renders pages on demand and supports structured references. It provides thumbnails, page navigation, zoom, rotation, and Chinese text search; its one-row toolbar moves actions that do not fit into More actions. Selected PDF text supports quoting, asking, copying, new sessions, and notes. Cross-page quotes retain start and end pages, character indices, and per-page rectangle geometry, then restore the start page and original selection when clicked. The composer collapses file and conversation references into separate counted chips, with complete references available in a popover. Loading failures offer retry and the system default application.
- **引导扩展为多会话与语音输入的可操作设置**：五步引导首次启动默认推荐开启多会话标签页，开关和预览即时联动；语音页可检查麦克风、选择识别模型、按界面语言获得推荐并查看真实下载进度，只有点击下载才获取模型，失败可重试或跳过。完成页默认展开功能速览，说明斜杠命令、上下文引用和语音输入等操作。
  - EN: Onboarding now provides actionable multi-session and voice-input setup across five steps. It recommends session tabs on first launch with a live switch and preview; the voice page checks the microphone, offers language-aware recognition-model recommendations, and shows real download progress. Models download only after an explicit click, and failures allow retry or skip. The completion page expands the feature overview by default and covers slash commands, context references, voice input, and other workflows.
- **声纹录入反馈与液态玻璃风格统一**：准备、录音、处理、单段通过、失败和完成具有不同状态反馈，录音动效由真实音量驱动；失败只重试当前段，完成后保留明确的完成操作，减少动态效果时仍显示静态进度与状态文本。
  - EN: Voiceprint enrollment now matches the liquid-glass design and distinguishes preparation, recording, processing, clip acceptance, failure, and completion. Recording motion follows real volume; failures retry only the current clip, completion retains a clear Done action, and reduced motion preserves static progress and status text.
- **输入框语音状态带更紧凑直观**：移除框内方框和大片空白，以液态玻璃麦克风、明确阶段、真实音量波形和实时文字构成状态反馈；转写时切换为品牌色处理环，Esc 改为紧凑键帽，窄窗口优先保留阶段和识别文字。
  - EN: The in-composer voice status strip is now more compact and legible. A liquid-glass microphone, explicit phase, real level wave, and live text replace the nested box and empty space; transcription switches to an accent processing ring, Esc uses a compact keycap, and narrow windows preserve the phase and recognized text first.

### Fixed
- **实例管理只提示真正的新版本**：左下角实例信息会比较当前 daemon 与最新发布的版本号，不再把旧版本或不可识别的版本误报为可升级。
  - EN: Instance management now only offers actual upgrades. The instance menu compares the current daemon version with the latest release instead of treating older or unrecognizable versions as updates.
- **安装包恢复完整内置插件**：桌面发布包随 daemon 一并携带并校验能力中心、文件工作区、Android 模拟器及其余第一方插件，启动时从安装目录发现，不再退回不完整的旧内置列表。组件入口、普通及传递依赖、终端原生运行时和桥接文件均纳入暂存；两种 PTY 后端及组件启停在安装布局中可用。
  - EN: Installed builds now restore the complete set of first-party plugins. Desktop packages include and validate the capability center, file workspace, Android emulator, and other first-party plugins beside the daemon, then discover them from the installation directory instead of falling back to an incomplete legacy built-in list. Staging includes component entries, direct and transitive JavaScript dependencies, native terminal runtimes, and the PTY bridge; both PTY backends and component switches work from the installed layout.
- **官网与桌面、移动端统一应用图标**：导航、页脚和 favicon 直接使用桌面端品牌主图，不再显示旧版简化球体；Android 安装图标继续由同一主图生成。
  - EN: The website now uses the same app icon as the desktop and mobile apps. Navigation, footer, and favicon use the desktop brand master instead of the older simplified orb; Android launcher assets continue to be generated from the same master.
- **桌面窄屏导航与键盘操作恢复完整**：≤640px 时侧栏以覆盖式抽屉打开，不再压缩聊天列；关闭抽屉不可交互。会话标签、设置折叠按钮和看板空状态补齐键盘及可访问语义，命令面板打开后正确接管焦点，Tab 不会逃出面板，Escape 后焦点返回触发按钮。
  - EN: Narrow desktop navigation and keyboard operation are restored. At 640px and below, the sidebar opens as an overlay drawer without compressing the chat column, and the closed drawer is not interactive. Session tabs, settings collapse controls, and the board empty state now expose keyboard and accessibility semantics. The command palette captures focus when visible, keeps Tab inside, and restores focus to its opener on Escape.
- **移动端正文、触控与页面缩放更易用**：正文提供 13／15／17px 三档字号，常用操作与触屏公式、图表和表格控件满足 44px 触控尺寸；用户可缩放页面，输入框仍避免聚焦时自动放大。移动壳补充启动覆盖层、随主题变化的状态栏和打包字体。
  - EN: Mobile reading, touch controls, and page zoom are more usable. Body text offers 13, 15, and 17px sizes, while common actions and touch controls for formulas, diagrams, and tables meet a 44px target size. Users can zoom the page while focused inputs still avoid automatic magnification. The mobile shell adds a launch overlay, theme-aware status bars, and bundled fonts.
- **官网主要操作与预览恢复可用**：下载与使用入口指向有效目标，FAQ 和下载菜单支持正确的 ARIA、键盘与焦点行为，低对比度文字和远期卡片透明度已调整，并展示两张真实产品预览图。
  - EN: Primary website actions and previews work correctly again. Download and usage links have valid destinations, the FAQ and download menu provide correct ARIA, keyboard, and focus behavior, low-contrast text and future-card transparency have been adjusted, and two real product previews are shown.
- **能力中心标签导航与内置只读约束恢复一致**：技能、插件和市场标签通过宿主传入的嵌套 `page` 导航属性切换，不再读取错误的顶层属性；声明 `readonly` 的内置项不显示无效启停操作，daemon 同步拒绝启停请求。
  - EN: Capability-center tab navigation and built-in read-only constraints are consistent again. Skills, Plugins, and Marketplace tabs use the host's nested page navigation props instead of incorrect top-level props. Built-in entries marked readonly no longer expose ineffective enablement controls, and the daemon rejects their enablement requests as well.
- **悬浮提示不再滞留或遮挡高层界面**：富提示在滚轮移动时跟随锚点，窗口失焦或锚点移除后立即关闭；普通提示与富提示统一液态玻璃材质、间距和换行，并保留既有浮层层级。
  - EN: Tooltips no longer linger or obscure higher-level UI. Rich tooltips follow their anchors during wheel movement and close on window blur or anchor removal. Standard and rich tooltips share the liquid-glass material, spacing, and wrapping while retaining the existing overlay stacking order.
- **远程桌面网页不再误判为手机原生壳**：Capacitor 浏览器桥存在时仍按真实平台检测，桌面网页恢复 Workspace 目录，安全存储继续使用浏览器存储；原生 Android 路径保持独立。
  - EN: Remote desktop web is no longer mistaken for the native phone shell. Platform detection now distinguishes Capacitor's browser bridge, so desktop web retains its Workspace directory and browser secure-storage fallback while Android keeps its native path.
- **PDF 首屏挂载与加载失败恢复更可靠**：视口尺寸在页面门锁外同步上报，并在尺寸就绪后初始化自动缩放，首次打开不再一直空白；引擎和文档加载有明确超时，重试会重建加载状态。损坏或加密文件会显示错误与外部打开入口，关闭阅读器会终止相关 Worker。
  - EN: PDF first-page mounting and load-failure recovery are more reliable. Viewport dimensions are published outside the page gate and automatic zoom initializes once dimensions are ready, so opening a PDF no longer stays blank. Engine and document loading have explicit deadlines, and retry resets the loading state. Corrupt or encrypted files show an error and an external-open action, while closing the reader terminates its Workers.
- **移动端新会话首条消息进入正确会话**：空草稿首次发送会先创建并选择目标会话，等待其快照就绪后再发送，不再误入主机先前打开的会话；创建后的目录刷新不会取消选择或中断首条消息，创建失败或重复点击不会误发，文字与图片草稿会保留。
  - EN: The first message in a mobile draft now reaches the correct session. Sending creates and selects the target session, waits for its ready snapshot, and only then sends the prompt instead of using the host's previously focused session. Directory refreshes after creation no longer clear the selection or interrupt the first prompt. Creation failures and repeated taps cannot send it accidentally, and text and image drafts remain intact.
- **Android 主机保存与手机布局恢复可用**：连接成功后，房间凭证可正常写入系统安全存储并出现在主机管理中，不再因插件初始化相互等待而丢失；连接页品牌不再被挤压或装饰遮挡，会话标题保持单行省略，窄屏仍保留核心导航操作。返回新草稿会清除旧消息；设置页不再显示会话下拉并保留完整电脑名称，会话滑动操作不再透出重叠，点按后也不会留下悬浮提示。
  - EN: Android host saving and phone layouts now work correctly. After connecting, room credentials reach the OS secure store and appear in host management instead of being lost to mutually waiting plugin initialization. The connect-screen brand is no longer squeezed or obscured by decoration, session titles stay on one ellipsized line, and narrow screens retain core navigation actions. Returning to a draft clears old messages; Settings no longer shows the session dropdown and retains full computer names, swipe actions no longer bleed through session rows, and taps no longer leave stuck tooltips.
- **移动端浮层与长链接布局不再裁切**：六项面板菜单完整显示在底部；电脑管理先显示真实加载状态，再以两行布局保留完整触摸操作；长电脑名称和远程链接不再撑宽连接页；扫码主按钮点击后仍保持品牌底色与文字对比度。
  - EN: Mobile sheets and long-link layouts no longer clip. All six panel actions fit in the bottom menu, computer management shows a real loading state before its two-row touch-friendly list, long computer names or remote links cannot widen the connect screen, and the QR primary action retains its accent background and text contrast after tapping.
- **Android 原生插件在重新同步后保持完整**：移动壳明确声明安全存储、应用生命周期、键盘、本地通知、状态栏和角标依赖，`cap sync` 不再从原生工程移除这些插件；混合内容配置保留在正确的 Android 配置段。
  - EN: Android native plugins now remain intact after resynchronization. The mobile shell explicitly declares secure storage, app lifecycle, keyboard, local notification, status bar, and badge dependencies, so `cap sync` no longer removes them from the native project. Mixed-content configuration remains in the correct Android section.
- **文件树标签与首行选区操作恢复正确**：切换到文件树标签不再残留上一个文件；首行选区工具栏在空间不足时移到选区下方，不再遮挡路径或误触新建会话。
  - EN: File-tree tabs and first-line selection actions now behave correctly. Switching to a file-tree tab no longer retains the previous file, and the selection toolbar moves below the selection when space above is insufficient instead of covering breadcrumbs or triggering a new session.
- **文件树不再重复显示目录，大 PDF 使用完整读取上限**：相同路径会先去重，父目录被截断或隐藏时其后代不再错误提升为根节点；浏览子目录仍能显示直接子项。文件读取现在显式请求 32 MiB 查看上限，不再让 8 MiB 默认值提前拒绝可预览的 PDF。
  - EN: The file tree no longer duplicates directories, and large PDFs use the full read allowance. Duplicate paths are removed, descendants of truncated or hidden parents are no longer promoted to roots, and direct children remain visible when browsing a subdirectory. File reads now explicitly request the 32 MiB viewer allowance instead of letting the 8 MiB default reject previewable PDFs early.
- **Windows 文件保存不再静默失败**：Monaco 产生的反斜杠路径和小写盘符会正确转换为工作区相对路径；写入失败时保留未保存状态并显示错误，不再误发保存完成通知。
  - EN: Windows file saves no longer fail silently. Monaco backslash paths and lowercase drive letters now convert to workspace-relative paths correctly; write failures preserve dirty state and show an error instead of emitting a save notification.
- **Windows 跨文件重命名不再丢失未打开文件的草稿**：不同斜杠方向和盘符大小写现在使用同一缓存键，重新打开目标文件会保留重命名结果与未保存状态，确认关闭后也不会恢复已丢弃的草稿。
  - EN: Windows cross-file rename no longer loses drafts in files that were not already open. Slash direction and drive-letter casing now share one cache key, so reopening a target preserves its renamed text and dirty state, while a confirmed close cannot restore the discarded draft.
- **桌宠会话气泡恢复显示**：修正气泡窗口与桌面桥接层的尺寸、可见性和点击区域接口名称，会话卡片、展开列表、回复入口和隐藏计数角标可正常呈现；窗口高度现在包含控件间距与阴影留白，展开列表下方的圆形收起按钮不再被裁切。
  - EN: Desktop pet conversation bubbles are visible again. The bubble window now uses the correct desktop-bridge method names for content size, visibility, and hit testing, so session cards, the expanded list, reply controls, and the hidden-count badge render correctly. Window height now includes control spacing and shadow clearance, so the circular collapse button below the expanded list is no longer clipped.
- **Petdex 状态动画恢复完整图集裁切**：状态帧按完整 8×9 图集计算背景尺寸，不再把整张精灵图压缩进单帧；切回矢量球体时会清除 Petdex 的镜像、拖拽和弹跳残留。
  - EN: Petdex state animations now crop the complete sprite atlas correctly. Background sizing uses the full 8×9 sheet instead of squeezing it into one frame, and switching back to the vector orb clears residual Petdex mirroring, dragging, and squash state.
- **中文朗读模型可以完整下载，失败时显示具体原因**：MeloTTS 会下载全部必需的中文规则文件；下载失败时保留具体文件和网络错误，不再只显示通用失败提示。
  - EN: Chinese speech models now download completely, and failures show their actual cause. MeloTTS downloads every required Chinese rule file, while download errors preserve the affected file and network diagnostic instead of a generic failure message.
- **普通窗口中的外观设置恢复独立成行**：「多会话标签页」不再嵌入上一条设置，窄窗口下的标签和开关保持正常布局。
  - EN: Appearance settings once again use independent rows in regular windows. Session tabs no longer nest inside the preceding setting, keeping labels and the toggle aligned in narrow windows.
- **输入框的语音输入按钮恢复正常**：默认语音识别调用保留正确的实例上下文，点击麦克风不再触发私有方法访问错误。
  - EN: Voice input in composers works again. The default speech-recognition call retains its instance context, so clicking the microphone no longer raises a private-method access error.

## [0.6.4] - 2026-10-30

### Added
- **稳定版与测试版现在使用彼此隔离的更新通道**：测试版采用 `x.y.z-beta.N` 版本号，CLI、桌面客户端和 npm 都只会检测测试版更新；稳定版继续只接收稳定版。发布会阻止旧测试版覆盖新版本，并在上传后核对更新清单。
  - EN: Stable and beta builds now use isolated update channels. Numbered beta releases check only beta updates across the CLI, desktop app, and npm, while stable builds remain on stable releases. Publishing rejects beta rollbacks and verifies the uploaded update manifest.
- **能力中心可以直接管理 profile 的 bundle 组合**：「组合」页列出已安装和缺失的已选 bundle，启停会真实写入 `musepi.profile.bundles`，组合行覆盖仍保存在 profile 的 `cordis.patch.yml`；普通插件和会话级 Agent preset 继续沿用各自的管理入口。
  - EN: The capability center can now manage profile bundle compositions. The Composition tab lists installed bundles and missing selections, writes toggles to `musepi.profile.bundles`, and keeps row overrides in the profile's `cordis.patch.yml`; ordinary plugins and session-scoped Agent presets retain their own management surfaces.
- **语音输入支持实时草稿、机主声纹与可选降噪**：桌面和 TUI 会分段显示临时文本，正式结果随说话停顿逐步进入输入框；可录入本机声纹以减少旁人误录入，灰区只保留草稿且不自动提交，严格模式仅转写已确认语音。录音动效由真实音量驱动，减少动态效果时保留静态进度。
  - EN: Voice input now supports live drafts, owner voiceprints, and optional noise suppression. Desktop and TUI show temporary text in segments and insert finalized results as speech pauses; local voiceprint enrollment reduces accidental transcription of other people, uncertain speech stays a draft without auto-submit, and strict mode transcribes only confirmed speech. Recording motion follows actual volume, with static progress under reduced motion.
- **语音模型可以下载后删除并释放磁盘空间**：STT 与 TTS 缓存行新增确认删除，先停止对应 worker 再清理本地模型，不会在删除后立即自动重新下载。
  - EN: Speech models can now be deleted after download to reclaim disk space. Cached STT and TTS rows offer confirmed deletion, stop the corresponding worker before removing local files, and do not immediately download the deleted model again.

### Changed
- **Computer Use 默认开启**：新会话默认注册桌面控制工具，审批策略和操作系统权限检查仍按原流程执行；可在插件页或会话中显式关闭。
  - EN: Computer Use is now enabled by default. New sessions register the desktop-control tool while approval policies and OS permission checks remain unchanged; it can still be disabled explicitly from the plugin page or within a session.
- **Agent preset 与 profile 使用同一组合声明模型**：bundle 中的 Agent preset 会进入预设列表和真实会话解析，默认值保存在 profile 的 `agent-preset-registry.config.selectedDefault`；首轮后 preset 锁定，已有会话保留原组合，profile 字段统一为 `musepi.profile.bundles`，DSH 字段直接忽略。
  - EN: Agent presets and profiles now share one composition model. Bundle declarations flow into the preset list and real session resolution, defaults persist in the profile's `agent-preset-registry.config.selectedDefault`, presets lock after the first turn, existing sessions keep their composition, and only `musepi.profile.bundles` is supported, with DSH profile fields ignored.

### Fixed
- **Windows 更新不再因过期校验值失败，手动下载也会匹配当前平台**：发布前会按最终安装器重算 SHA-512 和文件大小，避免二次包装后的安装包与旧更新元数据一起发布；更新清单按平台和架构提供安装器地址，Windows 不再跳到 macOS ARM64 下载页。
  - EN: Windows updates no longer fail because of stale checksums, and manual downloads now match the current platform. Publishing recomputes SHA-512 and file size from final installers so wrapped packages cannot ship with old metadata, while the update manifest maps each platform and architecture to the correct installer instead of sending Windows users to macOS ARM64.
- **父插件停用时组件不再显示为仍在运行**：组件选择会保留，但只有父插件和组件自身都允许时才显示绿色可用状态；父插件停用期间组件开关不可操作，重新启用后恢复原选择。读取组合页也不再自动创建空 profile 文件。
  - EN: Components no longer appear active while their parent plugin is disabled. Selections are preserved, but green availability requires both the parent and component to be enabled; controls are disabled while the parent is off and restore on re-enable. Reading the Composition tab no longer creates empty profile files.
- **语音设置页恢复完整配置项**：语音、静音阈值、提交触发、朗读和输入设备等既有设置重新与模型管理一起显示，声纹录入内容也在固定弹窗中垂直居中。
  - EN: The voice settings page once again shows the complete configuration. Existing speech, silence threshold, submit trigger, playback, and input-device settings appear alongside model management, and voice enrollment content is vertically centered in its fixed dialog.

## [0.6.3] - 2026-10-29

### Fixed
- **Windows 安装器不再混显 NSIS 原生页面**：交互安装只显示与客户端同风格的自绘向导，NSIS 改为在后台完成文件安装，不再出现两套安装界面交替打开的情况。
  - EN: The Windows installer no longer mixes in native NSIS pages. Interactive setup shows only the client-styled custom wizard while NSIS installs files in the background, eliminating the alternating setup interfaces.
- **插件兼容性检查不再误拒合法版本区间**：`^0.6.0`、预发布运行时和常见 SemVer 区间会按实际兼容性判定，同时继续拦截空区间和非法配置。
  - EN: Plugin compatibility checks no longer reject valid version ranges. Caret ranges, prerelease runtimes, and common SemVer ranges are evaluated correctly, while empty and malformed requirements remain blocked.
- **Windows 凭据进程和路径显示恢复正常**：未加引号的 `credential_process` 路径不再丢失反斜杠，跨盘符的工具工作目录也会正确缩短为用户主目录形式。
  - EN: Windows credential processes and path display work again. Unquoted credential_process paths retain their backslashes, and tool working directories on another drive shorten correctly relative to the user's home directory.

## [0.6.2] - 2026-10-29

### Changed
- **内置浏览器的接管提示和交互动效更完整了**：Agent 在后台标签操作时也会提示，并可一键切回对应标签；接管期间会阻止重复请求，操作结束后通过淡出和滑动完成退出。
  - EN: Built-in browser takeover now has more complete feedback and motion. Background agent tabs surface an activity notice with a quick way back, takeover requests cannot be duplicated while cancellation is pending, and operations finish with a fade-and-slide exit.

### Fixed
- **Windows 安装器现在会真正打开自定义安装向导**：打包配置已接入 WebView2 安装器，不再错误地回退到 NSIS 默认页面。
  - EN: The Windows installer now actually opens the custom wizard. The packaging configuration loads the WebView2 installer instead of silently falling back to the stock NSIS pages.
- **Windows 安装器不再依赖系统临时目录是否可用**：安装包会先创建当前用户私有的临时目录，再启动原有自绘向导，避免“Can’t initialize plug-ins directory”；静默更新参数和 OTA 校验数据保持有效。
  - EN: The Windows installer no longer depends on a usable system temporary directory. A native bootstrap creates a private per-user temporary directory before launching the same custom wizard, preventing "Can't initialize plug-ins directory" while preserving silent-update arguments and OTA checksums.
- **供应商登录和 API Key 导入恢复正常**：首次打开供应商设置不再关闭共享凭据数据库；纯 API Key 供应商只显示一个导入动作，导入失败也会在弹窗中明确显示。
  - EN: Provider login and API-key import work again. Opening provider settings no longer closes the shared credential database, API-key-only providers show one import action, and import failures remain visible in the dialog.
- **原生构建不再输出无效依赖警告**：移除了未使用的工作区主页字段和重复/错位的 serde 依赖声明，测试所需依赖仍然保留。
  - EN: Native builds no longer emit unused-dependency warnings. The unused workspace homepage and duplicate or misplaced serde declarations were removed while retaining the dependencies required by tests.
- **Deadline 到期不会再吞掉排队中的后续消息**：停止边界现在会在轮询前后各检查一次 Deadline；轮询期间到期时，hook 能收到取消信号，消息仍保留在队列中，已经到期的回合则不再发起新的出队。
  - EN: Follow-up messages now stay queued across deadline boundaries. The stop path checks the deadline before and after polling: hooks receive cancellation when it expires mid-poll, while an already-expired run starts no new dequeue.

## [0.6.1] - 2026-10-27

### Added
- **压缩摘要可以在右侧标签中打开了**：自动压缩后，会话中的横幅会显示压缩前后 token；点击「查看摘要」即可阅读完整内容，关闭或分栏后仍按原条目恢复。摘要面板由可替换的内置插件提供，右侧扩展组件也能获得各自标签的 target 与统一 token 格式。
  - EN: Compaction summaries now open in right-panel tabs. The transcript banner shows before/after token counts, and "View summary" opens the full text, restored against the same entry after closing or splitting tabs. The panel is provided by a replaceable bundled plugin, and right-panel extension components receive their own tab target and shared token formatting.
- **内置浏览器的 Agent 操作现在看得见，也能随时接管**：页面会显示操作边框、当前动作和已控制时长；点击「我来接管」停止本轮自动化后，当前页面和登录状态仍然保留，可以马上手动继续。设备工具条同时支持尺寸、显示比例和外框背景调整。
  - EN: Built-in browser automation is now visible and easy to take over. The page shows an operation ring, current action, and elapsed control time; "Take over" stops the current turn while preserving the page and login state for immediate manual use. The device toolbar also supports dimensions, display scale, and frame background.
- **浏览器工具调用现在展示具体步骤和重试结果**：查看页面、点击、填写、滚动和截图等操作会实时列在卡片中，刷新历史后仍然保留；连续调用自动汇总页面与操作次数，已成功的重试不再被误报为最终失败，展开仍可查看完整过程。
  - EN: Browser tool calls now show their actual steps and retry outcomes. Page inspection, clicks, fills, scrolling, and screenshots appear live in the card and persist through history reloads. Consecutive calls summarize pages and actions, recovered retries no longer count as final failures, and the complete sequence remains expandable.

- **Issue 和 PR 可以直接阅读详情并发表评论了**：列表旁新增独立详情栏，自动预览当前选中项；正文与评论支持 Markdown 渲染，可查看元信息、标签、检查状态和评论，并直接在客户端发表评论。PR 的合并、关闭、加标签和审查者操作保留在详情栏中。
  - EN: Issues and pull requests now have a real reading and commenting workspace. A dedicated detail pane previews the selected item beside the list, renders bodies and comments as Markdown, shows metadata, labels, checks, and discussions, and accepts new comments without leaving the app. Existing PR merge, close, label, and reviewer actions remain available in the detail pane.
- **Git 设置可以管理多个账号、用令牌直接接入了**：设置 → Git 新增「已连接的账号」列表——每个主机一个活跃凭据（带「活跃」徽标），支持移除；「使用访问令牌接入」可以直接填个人访问令牌（PAT），主机填 github.com 或企业版/自托管域名（如 ghe.example.com），校验通过才保存。以前只有 GitHub 设备授权一条路，且只能存一个账号。
  - EN: Git settings now manage multiple accounts and accept direct token sign-in. Settings → Git gains a "Connected accounts" list — one active credential per host (with an "Active" badge), each removable — and a "Connect with an access token" form that takes a personal access token (PAT) with a host of github.com or an enterprise/self-hosted domain (e.g. ghe.example.com), saved only after validation. Previously the only path was the GitHub device flow, storing a single account.
- **关闭窗口的行为可以自己选了**：设置 → 常规新增「关闭窗口时最小化到托盘」开关（Windows / Linux）。默认关闭——点关闭按钮就是退出客户端，后台的本地守护进程随退出一起停止，不再出现「窗口关了、进程还在后台跑」的误会；开启后关闭只把窗口藏进托盘，客户端继续在后台运行，随时从托盘唤回。
  - EN: Closing the window is now a choice. Settings → General gains a "Minimize to tray when closing the window" toggle (Windows / Linux). It defaults to off — the close button quits the app and the local daemon stops with it, so a closed window no longer leaves processes running behind your back. Turn it on to hide the window to the tray instead and keep the app alive in the background, one tray click away.
- **智能体现在能读笔记全文了**：`knowledge` 工具的 `show` 以前只支持计划，智能体问「那条笔记写了什么」只会报一条错误，只能去磁盘上翻文件；现在 `show` + `kind: "note"` 直接返回笔记完整正文（`list` 的摘要仍然只有 80 字预览，用 `show` 看全文）。
  - EN: The agent can now read a note in full. The knowledge tool's `show` used to accept plans only — asking "what does that note say" produced an error and the agent had to go digging through files on disk; `show` with `kind: "note"` now returns the note's complete body (`list` still gives an 80-character preview; use `show` for the full text).

### Changed
- **冷启动更快，启动桌宠也会跟随主题色**：加载指示在品牌动画结束后仍持续活动；首屏不再等待后台 SDK 预热或慢设置读取，daemon 主动重启不再被误判为掉线而重复启动，能力探测也不会再发起远程请求。启动页仅保留品牌与进度，只有真实连接失败才进入恢复页。
  - EN: Cold starts are faster, and the startup pet now follows the theme. Loading feedback keeps moving after the brand intro; the shell no longer waits for background SDK prewarming or slow settings discovery, intentional daemon restarts no longer trigger duplicate recovery boots, and capability probes avoid remote requests. Only a real connection failure opens the recovery view.
- **输入框增加跟随鼠标的光晕，空态提示不再轮播**：空态与会话输入框共享细腻的指针光晕，移动时不触发输入区重渲染，减少动态效果时自动关闭；placeholder 和补充提示保持静止，降低视觉干扰。
  - EN: Composers gain a subtle pointer-follow glow while empty-state hints stay still. The effect does not rerender the input subtree and turns off under reduced motion; placeholders and supplementary tips no longer rotate, reducing visual distraction.
- **悬浮提示现在稳定跟随触发元素，键盘和触屏也能一致使用**：提示按锚点定位并在窗口边缘自动翻转或内移，滚动和缩放时不再突然消失或错位；悬停与键盘焦点使用相同延迟，触屏支持长按，Escape 可直接关闭，减少动态效果时不再有多余位移。
  - EN: Tooltips now stay anchored and work consistently with keyboards and touch. They flip or shift at viewport edges, follow the trigger through scrolling and resizing, use the same delay for hover and focus, support touch long-press, and close with Escape. Reduced motion no longer leaves positional transitions.
- **Issue/PR 工具区和右侧标签更整齐流畅了**：搜索与 PR/问题子页签归入同一工具栏，筛选器不再多层挤压换行；右侧零标签页改为垂直居中的自适应卡片网格。右侧与内置浏览器标签新增统一的打开/关闭动效，减少动态效果时立即完成，浏览器页面销毁顺序不受影响。
  - EN: Issue/PR tools and panel tabs are cleaner and smoother. Search and the PR/issues subtabs now share one toolbar, while filters keep their own structured row. The empty right panel is now a vertically centered adaptive card grid. Surface and built-in browser tabs share open/close motion that snaps under reduced motion without changing WebView disposal order.
- **提示、通知、抽屉和弹窗统一了玻璃质感与可读性**：六类浮层共享一致的边缘、光泽和阴影；关闭动效或减少动态效果时，入场与出场都会立即完成。降低透明度、系统不支持背景模糊以及 Windows 无模糊路径会使用清晰的不透明底色。嵌套弹窗只响应最上层的键盘操作，危险删除确认也不会再被 Enter 误触发。
  - EN: Tooltips, toasts, drawers, and dialogs now share consistent glass edges, sheen, and shadows across six overlay types. Entrance and exit motion both stop when animation is disabled or reduced motion is requested. Reduced transparency, missing backdrop-filter support, and Windows without blur use readable opaque surfaces. Only the top nested dialog handles keyboard input, and destructive confirmations can no longer be triggered accidentally with Enter.
- **Issue 和 PR 面板在窄窗口也能读详情了**：宽屏保留双列列表与详情，低于 960px 时详情独占宽度，列表改为当前项按钮弹出的玻璃面板；同时移除重复区块标签，列表和详情统一使用液态玻璃背景、边缘和阴影。
  - EN: The issue and PR workspace now stays readable in narrow windows. Wide layouts keep list and detail side by side; below 960px the detail takes the full width and the list opens in a glass popup from the current-item button. Duplicate section labels are gone, and both surfaces share the liquid-glass background, borders, and shadows.
- **任务中心、看板、Issue 与 PR、能力中心统一了页面排版**：四个一级页面现在共享一致的标题、返回入口、说明、操作区与标签页结构；任务日历支持按日期展开全部任务，日历和状态看板都能直接进入任务详情。
  - EN: Task center, boards, issues & pull requests, and capability center now share one page-header layout for titles, back navigation, descriptions, actions, and tabs. The task calendar exposes every task for a selected day, and both calendar and status-board items open the corresponding task details.

### Fixed
- **本地 daemon 启动不再轻易超时或重复拉起**：慢编译不再被 10 秒时限误杀，并发启动共享同一过程，已有 daemon 会直接接管；能力探测使用合法参数，避免把参数校验失败误判为旧版本并反复重启。
  - EN: Local daemon startup no longer times out or spawns duplicates unnecessarily. Slow compilation is allowed to finish, concurrent starts share one process, and an existing daemon is adopted instead of replaced. Capability probes now use valid parameters, preventing validation errors from being mistaken for an outdated daemon and causing restart loops.
- **专注输入框和看板合集更整齐流畅**：专注模式的桌宠不再被顶部裁切，提示文本不会因盒模型提前换行；合集卡片按宽高完整适配并保持同心圆角，深色示例卡统一液态玻璃层次。合集预览改用静态示例，不再同时启动行情请求、刷新定时器和音频动画；真实看板仍保留实时能力，快捷输入框始终吸住底部。
  - EN: Focus mode and board collections are cleaner and smoother. The composer pet is no longer clipped, placeholders no longer wrap early, and collection cards fit both axes with concentric radii and a unified dark liquid-glass treatment. Collection previews now use static examples instead of starting market requests, refresh timers, and audio animation together; real boards keep live behavior, and the quick-create input stays pinned to the bottom.
- **长会话和地图不再打开就拉取全部历史**：对话、地图和右侧轨迹共享最新有限窗口；只有主动加载更早内容或定位旧消息时才按页读取，轮次索引从最新 200 轮开始，前插内容按条目身份去重，不再反复累积或卡住首屏。
  - EN: Long sessions and the map no longer fetch the entire history on open. Chat, map, and trajectory share a bounded newest window; older pages load only on demand or when locating an old message. The turn index starts with the latest 200 turns and prepended entries are deduplicated by identity, preventing repeated accumulation and stalled first paint.
- **会话地图和轨迹在历史加载后保持清晰与一致**：advisor 链节不再制造幻影分支，嵌套分支连接真实父轮；加载更早历史不会把展开/折叠或选中状态转移到其他轮次，也不会重置地图缩放与平移。轮卡、轨迹事件和 Overview 轮段均可用键盘操作。
  - EN: The session map and trajectory stay coherent while history loads. Advisor links no longer create phantom branches, nested branches connect to their real parent turn, and older-history prepends preserve expansion, collapse, selection, zoom, and pan state. Turn cards, trajectory events, and Overview segments are keyboard-operable.
- **历史会话和 advisor 记录不再被陈旧缓存误报为未回答**：恢复时会核对磁盘 transcript 是否已有更新的完成记录；轮次索引也可从最新有限窗口开始，按需向前分页，旧客户端的无参请求保持兼容。
  - EN: Historical sessions and advisor records are no longer reported as unanswered because of a stale cache. Recovery now checks whether the on-disk transcript contains newer completed records. Turn indexes can also start with a bounded newest window and page backward on demand, while parameter-free requests from older clients remain compatible.
- **长会话分页更稳定，advisor 只读记录不再误报无应答**：历史页面按当前会话隔离加载，重复触发共享同一请求，切换或刷新后旧页面不会覆盖新内容；失败会保留现有消息并明确提示，advisor 记录也不再进入普通会话的无应答检查。
  - EN: Long-session paging is more reliable, and read-only advisor records no longer report a missing reply. History loads are isolated to the current session, repeated triggers share one request, and stale pages cannot overwrite a newer snapshot. Failures preserve visible messages with an explicit notice, while advisor records skip ordinary unanswered-prompt checks.
- **首次进入会话就会正确测量输入区高度**：从欢迎页打开会话时，消息尾部、渐隐带和跳转按钮现在能立即获得真实输入区高度，不再一直使用默认间距而遮住最新内容。
  - EN: The composer is measured correctly on the first session entry. Opening a session from the welcome scene now publishes its real height to the transcript tail, fade band, and jump control immediately instead of retaining fallback spacing that could cover the newest content.
- **CLI 先选定状态目录再加载环境变量**：启动时不再过早读取默认 `.env`，`--home` 或 `MUSEPI_HOME` 选定目录中的环境配置会在命令模块加载时正确生效。
  - EN: The CLI now selects its state home before loading environment variables. Startup no longer reads the default `.env` too early; environment configuration from the directory selected by `--home` or `MUSEPI_HOME` takes effect correctly when command modules load.
- **并发归档不会再绕过同会话的构建上限**：文件收集阶段也会占用构建槽位，快速重复发起归档时最多同时运行两个任务，不再因请求重叠而全部启动。
  - EN: Concurrent archives no longer bypass the per-session build limit. File collection now reserves a build slot, so rapid overlapping requests start at most two builds instead of all running at once.
- **跳到最新按钮和输入框桌宠不再难用**：跳到最新按钮始终位于输入框上方安全区，输入框增高、附件/引用卡或统计行变化时仍保持间距，不再遮住消息底部；输入框旁的品牌球体桌宠也已放大，窄窗口下眼睛、轮廓和点击区域依然清晰协调。
  - EN: The jump-to-latest control and composer pet are easier to use. The control stays in the safe area above the composer with consistent spacing as the input, attachment or quote cards, and stats row change height, without covering the message tail. The brand orb is larger too, with clear eyes and outline and a coordinated click target across window widths.
- **文件、代码、网页和会话引用统一成结构化卡片了**：引用不再把大段裸文本塞进输入框；来源、选区、评论、预览和移除都在卡片里处理，发送后仍保留专用展示。文件预览现在可以直接引用选中的代码及行号，队列和持久化不会丢来源；连续编辑批注也不会再卸载输入框或丢失焦点。
  - EN: File, code, browser, and chat references now share structured cards. Sources, selections, comments, previews, and removal stay out of the raw textarea and remain visible after sending. File previews can quote selected code with line numbers, while queues and persistence preserve the source. Continuous annotation editing no longer remounts the input or drops focus.
- **折叠子会话不再把父会话一起藏掉**：父行始终保持可见，只有子行执行高度动画；「全部展开/收起」现在能跨所有列表实例工作，展开时恢复原来的子会话。
  - EN: Collapsing sub-sessions no longer hides the parent. Parent rows remain visible while only child rows animate, and "expand/collapse all" now works across every list instance and restores the original children on expansion.
- **角色和回退模型可以分别选择 Auto 与思考等级了**：主角色和每个回退项都有独立的 inherit/auto/off/模型支持等级；`provider/id:auto` 不再被误当模型名或被 clamp 清除，修改一个回退项不会覆盖其他项。
  - EN: Roles and fallback models now have independent Auto and thinking-level selectors. `provider/id:auto` is no longer parsed as a model id or cleared by clamping, and editing one fallback entry does not overwrite the others.
- **禁用魔术关键词后提示和高亮会一起消失**：关键词的提示文本、检测和渐变高亮现在只由插件注册表驱动，不再被静态路径重复执行；卸载或清空注册后，第一方关键词也不会残留。
  - EN: Disabling magic keywords now removes both notices and highlights. Prompt generation, detection, and gradients are driven only by the plugin registry, with no static duplicate path or first-party residue after unload.
- **会话切换浮层完整显示了**：多会话主机的标题按钮打开的工作区 sheet 不再被顶栏裁到屏幕外，移动和桌面视口都能查看会话列表、关闭浮层并恢复焦点。
