  - EN: The session switcher now opens fully in view. The workspace sheet opened from a multi-session host title is no longer clipped above the screen by the header, so mobile and desktop users can see the session list, close the sheet, and restore focus.
- **PR 详情不再因审查者字段报错**：GitHub CLI 的 `reviewRequests` 现在会正确转换为界面需要的审查者列表，不再出现 `Unknown JSON field: "reviewers"`，PR 描述、评论、标签和操作区可以正常加载。
  - EN: PR details no longer fail on the reviewer field. GitHub CLI's `reviewRequests` is normalized into the reviewers list used by the GUI, eliminating `Unknown JSON field: "reviewers"` so descriptions, comments, labels, and actions load normally.
- **窄窗口的设置搜索不再被侧栏裁切**：折叠成图标栏后，搜索改为独立浮层，输入和结果完整可见；搜索不会过滤掉背后的导航图标，选择结果或按 Escape 会正常关闭并恢复焦点。
  - EN: Settings search is no longer clipped by the collapsed sidebar. Its input and results now appear in an independent popup, searching leaves the icon rail intact, and choosing a result or pressing Escape closes it and restores focus correctly.
- **开放的 Issue 不再显示成已关闭**：GitHub 返回的大写 `OPEN` 现在会被正确识别，列表图标和关闭/重新打开操作与真实状态一致；任务中心的定时任务列表也不再因持续自动刷新而重复重渲染。
  - EN: Open issues no longer appear closed. GitHub's uppercase `OPEN` state is recognized correctly, keeping list icons and close/reopen actions consistent with reality. The scheduled-task page also no longer re-renders continuously from an unstable refresh callback.
- **新功能弹窗的折叠栏目有动效了**：「本次还有 N 项修复与改进」和历史版本折叠区以前展开、收起都是瞬间跳变，现在按全局规范的 240ms 高度伸缩动效过渡；完整日志仍在第一次展开时才渲染，开机不会被它拖慢。
  - EN: Collapsible sections in the what's-new panel now animate. The "N more fixes" and older-release folds used to pop open and shut instantly; they now expand and collapse with the standard 240ms height motion, and the full log still renders only on first expand so startup doesn't pay for it.
- **聊天里「添加到笔记」后右栏立刻有显示**：从选中文本添加笔记后，项目知识面板以前要切走再切回来才刷出这条笔记；现在添加成功后面板立即刷新。
  - EN: "Add to notes" from selected text now shows up immediately. The project-knowledge panel used to need a tab switch away and back before the new note appeared; it refreshes the moment the add succeeds.
- **「⋯ 还有 N 行」能点开了**：设置成「运行中回合只保留最近 N 行」时，被折起来的活动以前只是一行干巴巴的字，想看前面的过程没办法；现在点这一行就把整个回合的活动全部展开，下个回合开始时再恢复折叠。
  - EN: The "⋯ N more lines" row is now clickable. With "running turn keeps only the latest N rows" on, the folded work used to be a bare line with no way back; clicking it now reveals the turn's full activity, and folding resumes when the next turn starts.
- **界面彻底卡死时客户端不再反复重启页面**：渲染进程一旦崩溃，客户端以前会立刻重新加载，页面要是反复崩溃就会每秒拉起一个新进程空转；现在一分钟内连崩三次后停止重载，直接走正常退出流程（退出前仍会提示正在运行的会话、并停掉本地守护进程）。
  - EN: A renderer that keeps crashing no longer gets reloaded forever. The client used to reload instantly after every crash, so one pathological page spun up a fresh process every second; after three crashes within a minute it now stops reloading and takes the normal quit path instead — running sessions still get the warning prompt, and the local daemon is torn down with the app.
- **Windows 版又能正常收到更新了**：最近一次版本发布的 Windows 安装包和更新清单漏传了，旧客户端点「检查更新」会一直失败；修复后新版本照常附带安装包与更新清单。
  - EN: Windows builds receive updates again. The most recent release shipped without its Windows installer and update manifest, so older clients failed every "check for updates"; new releases now include the installer and manifest again.
- **检查更新遇到「 release 还没带上更新清单」会说清楚了**：发布暂时缺了当前平台的更新元数据时，设置页以前显示一句笼统的「检查更新失败」，让人误会是自己网络出问题；现在明确提示「未找到当前平台的更新发布信息」，真正的网络故障仍然单独归类。
  - EN: A "check for updates" against a release missing this platform's update metadata now says exactly that instead of a generic failure that reads like the user's network is at fault; genuine network errors keep their own classification.
- **Windows 安装器又能正常完成了**：打包脚本调用系统 tar 解压组件时会把盘符误判成远程主机，导致安装器构建在 Windows 上失败。
  - EN: The Windows installer builds again. The packaging script passed the nupkg to tar in a way Windows read as a remote-host path, so installer builds failed on Windows.
- **检查更新失败时不再甩出一段英文报错原文**：设置里的「检查更新」遇到网络或发布资产问题以前直接把原始错误贴在页面上，现在显示一句本地化的「检查更新失败」，原始错误收进下方可展开的小字里。
  - EN: A failed update check no longer dumps a raw English error string. The settings page now shows a localized "update check failed" line and tucks the original error into a small expandable detail below.
- **卸掉的插件不再赖在设计体系列表里**：卸载或禁用插件后，欢迎页的预览栏在几秒内仍显示该插件注册的设计体系；现在失效缓存会一并清掉，卸载后立即消失。
  - EN: Uninstalled plugins no longer linger in the design-systems list. After uninstall or disable, the welcome-page preview rail kept showing the plugin's registered design systems for a few seconds; the invalidation now clears that cache too, so they vanish immediately.
- **用 `#` 路径别名（package imports）的老插件又能加载了**：这类插件的子模块此前绕过源码改写直接交给运行时解析，引旧包名时会报「找不到包」；现在子模块照常走改写管线，别名与旧包名映射都生效。
  - EN: Legacy plugins that use `#` package-import aliases load again. Their submodules used to bypass the source rewrite and fail with "package not found" when importing legacy package names; submodules now go through the rewrite pipeline, so aliases and legacy-name remaps both apply.
- **一个回合只会出现一个智能体头像了**：回合展开时，工具汇总行和「还有 N 行」截断行以前会各自再带一个头像，一屏叠出好几个；现在头像只钉在回合的第一个内容行上，过程行不再重复。
  - EN: A turn now shows a single agent avatar. When expanded, the tool-summary rows and the "N more lines" trim row each used to carry another avatar, stacking several on one screen; the avatar now anchors to the turn's first content row only.
- **token 用量只在回合最终回复下面显示一次**：以前每个工具行的末尾都挂着一条「↑ ↓ 耗时」统计，刷一长串；现在整回合的用量聚合到最终回复一条上。
  - EN: Token usage now renders once, under the turn's final reply. It used to dangle off every tool row as a long chain of "↑ ↓ duration" stats; the turn's usage now aggregates into a single line on the final reply.
- **思考行不再假装还在运行**：思考结束后，「思考」标签以前仍播放高亮扫过的动画，看起来像一直在想；现在结束即恢复静态，收起状态下还会在旁边预览思考内容的第一行，不用点开也知道这步在想什么。
  - EN: Finished thinking rows no longer pretend to be running. The "Thinking" label used to keep playing its shimmer animation after the block settled, reading as permanently active; it now rests once done, and while collapsed the row previews the reasoning's first line next to the label — you can tell what was pondered without expanding it.
- **命令行工具正确计入「命令」统计**：自动执行的 bash 工具以前被算进「其他操作」，工具汇总显示「执行了 N 次其他操作」；现在归到「命令」段。
  - EN: Shell commands now count as commands. Auto-approved bash tool calls used to be tallied as "other operations", so the run summary read "ran N other operations"; they now land in the commands segment.
- **插件加载和热更新不再被类型文件拖慢**：运行时依赖图会跳过 `import type` 和 `export type`，不再递归读取 SDK 的整条类型导入链；实际运行模块及其子模块热更新保持原有行为。
  - EN: Plugin loading and hot reload no longer get bogged down by type files. The runtime dependency graph skips `import type` and `export type` instead of recursively reading the SDK's type-import chain, while runtime modules retain their existing submodule HMR behavior.
- **Windows 上中断 Python 执行不再丢失内核状态**：无控制台的桌面或服务进程也能通过控制通道请求 `KeyboardInterrupt`，并行 `agent()` 会及时取消且保留之前定义的变量；只有中断长时间无响应时才关闭并重建内核。
  - EN: Interrupting Python execution on Windows no longer discards kernel state. Desktop and service processes without a console can request `KeyboardInterrupt` through the control channel, cancelling parallel `agent()` calls promptly while preserving previously defined variables. The kernel is terminated and recreated only when interruption stays unresponsive.
- **Cursor 的文件搜索在 Windows 使用正确的 glob 路径**：`pi_grep` 与 `pi_find` 拼接目录和匹配模式时保留工具协议需要的 `/` 分隔符，不再生成无法匹配的混合路径。
  - EN: Cursor file searches now use correct glob paths on Windows. `pi_grep` and `pi_find` join directories and patterns with the `/` separator required by the tool protocol instead of producing unusable mixed-separator paths.
- **MCP 资源读取不再静默空返回**：`musepi read` 会等待已发现的服务器完成连接，再读取原生 URI 或 `mcp://` 资源；异步 CLI 命令在输出或错误写完前不会提前以成功状态退出。
  - EN: MCP resource reads no longer return empty success. `musepi read` waits for discovered servers to connect before reading native or `mcp://` URIs, and asynchronous CLI commands stay alive until their output or error has been written.
- **Windows 后台服务在 broker 替换后继续运行**：detached daemon 不再随启动它的 broker 退出；启动失败会附带子进程的实际错误，便于直接定位权限、路径或运行时问题。
  - EN: Windows background daemons now survive broker replacement. Detached processes no longer exit with their original broker, and startup failures include the child process error to make permission, path, and runtime issues directly diagnosable.
- **Windows 的文件过滤与远程数据库读取更可靠**：带行号的 grep 在原生路径使用不同分隔符时仍按范围过滤；远程 SQLite 内容不再因临时文件清理竞争被误降级为二进制通知，设置存储也能确定性关闭。
  - EN: File filtering and remote database reads are more reliable on Windows. Line-ranged grep now respects ranges across native path separators, remote SQLite content is no longer downgraded to a binary notice by a temporary-file cleanup race, and settings storage closes deterministically.

## [0.6.0] - 2026-10-22

- **安装插件的弹窗不再样式错乱**：弹窗里的搜索输入框以前被撑成一颗巨大的椭圆、「安装」按钮是一块没经过样式的裸绿色块。现在输入框恢复正常的胶囊尺寸，安装按钮用回标准的主按钮样式。
  - EN: The install-plugin dialog no longer renders broken. The search input inside used to be stretched into a giant ellipse and the Install button was a bare, unstyled green block; the input is back to a normal capsule size and the button uses the standard primary style.

### Added
- **连接器进了工作状态面板，欢迎页也能选了**：会话右上角的工作状态卡展开后新增「连接器」一节（在 Git 与智能体之间），列出当前项目的全部 MCP 服务——每个一行，勾选即用、显示传输方式和连接状态，不用回到输入框上方那颗胶囊。欢迎页（还没开会话）的输入框上方现在也有连接器胶囊：这里做的勾选会先记住，会话一建立就自动套用。
  - EN: Connectors move into the work-status panel, and the welcome page can pick them too. Expanding the work-status card at a session's top-right now shows a Connectors section (between Git and Agents) listing every MCP service in the project — one row each, tick to use, with transport and connection status, no trip back to the capsule above the composer. The welcome-page composer (no session yet) gains a connectors capsule as well: choices made there are remembered and applied automatically once the session is created.
- **给页面画标注，和智能体说「就这里」**：内置浏览器和对话里的 HTML 卡片共用同一套标注工具——选元素、框区域、随手画，再加带序号的注释；浏览器地址栏新增标注、开发者工具、外部打开三个按钮（缩放收进菜单），卡片右上角也有一个标注钮。完成的截图连同注释文本自动附进输入框，发送后智能体看到的是带圈注的画面。
  - EN: Annotate a page and tell the agent "right here". The built-in browser and HTML cards in the conversation share one annotation tool — pick an element, box a region, draw freely, and drop numbered comment pins. The browser bar gains Annotate, DevTools and Open-in-browser buttons (zoom moves into the menu), and cards carry an annotate button in their top-right corner. The finished screenshot plus your comment text lands in the composer automatically; the agent receives the circled image.
- **侧栏新增「问题与拉取请求」入口，打开就是一整页 PR/问题中心**：点侧栏菜单里的新项，主视图整块切到中心页（聊天留在底下，返回原样恢复）——当前分支的 PR 卡片（检查状态、可合并性、评审结论、浏览器打开/复制链接），分支没有 PR 时同一位置变成创建表单（标题自动从分支名生成、选目标分支、可勾选创建为草稿、一键推送并创建）；下面还列着仓库所有打开的拉取请求。旁边的「问题」页签按打开/已关闭/全部过滤，标签带原色徽章、带评论数。右侧面板的 Git 页回归「工作区改动 + 提交历史」两页签，不再塞 PR。全部走本机 gh 命令行，gh 没装或没登录时给出明确提示而不是空白面板。
  - EN: The sidebar gains an "Issues & PRs" entry that opens a full-page PR/issue center in the main view (the chat stays mounted underneath and returns exactly as you left it): a card for the PR on the current branch (check status, mergeability, review decision, open-in-browser, copy link), turning into a create form when the branch has none (title prefilled from the branch name, base-branch pick, "create as draft" tick, one-click push-and-create); below it sit all of the repo's open pull requests. A companion Issues tab filters by open/closed/all with labels in their own colours and comment counts. The right git panel goes back to just workspace changes + commit history — no PR tab there anymore. Everything runs through the local gh CLI; a missing or unauthenticated gh gets a clear message instead of a blank pane.
- **右栏 Git 面板长出第三条页签「储藏」，还配了一排同步按钮**：储藏页签可以写说明把当前改动（含未跟踪文件）存起来、列出全部储藏、逐个「恢复并移除」或「删除」（删除需点两次确认）；页签栏右端常驻 获取/拉取/推送 三个按钮和 ↑↓ 未同步计数——拉取永远走变基、不产生合并提交，失败的原因直接显示在旁边。PR 看板的列表也新增了 打开/已关闭/已合并/全部 状态过滤，详情里的标签和审查者点 × 就能移除。
  - EN: The right git panel grows a third tab, Stashes, plus a sync button row. The stash tab stashes the current changes (untracked included) with an optional message, lists every stash, and pops or drops each — drops confirm with a second click. The sub-tab row permanently hosts fetch / pull / push buttons with the ↑↓ unpushed counter: pull always rebases and never creates a merge commit, and failures show their reason right beside. The PR board list gains an open/closed/merged/all state filter, and labels and reviewers in the detail can be removed with the × on their chip.
- **设置 Git 页的 GitHub 账号卡说真话了**：卡片以前一律显示「已通过 gh CLI 认证」，即使账号其实是走设备授权连的；现在会区分 通过 GitHub 设备授权连接 / 已通过 gh CLI 认证，设备授权连接的还显示连接日期。
  - EN: The GitHub account card in settings Git tab now tells the truth. It used to always say "Authenticated via gh CLI" even when the account was actually connected through the device flow; it now distinguishes "Connected via GitHub device authorization" from "Authenticated via gh CLI", and the device-flow connection also shows its date.
- **问题与拉取请求看板能展开详情、直接处理了**：点列表里任意一条，下方展开完整详情——正文、评论、每个检查项各自的通过状态；打开的 PR 可以直接合并（先选创建合并提交/压缩合并/变基合并，再点一次确认）、关闭或重新打开，还能顺手补标签和审查者。问题页签新增按标签、按作者过滤，点问题同样展开正文与评论，能直接关闭或重开，不必跳浏览器。
  - EN: The Issues & PRs board now expands details and takes action. Clicking any row unfolds the full detail below — body, comments, and each check's own pass state; an open PR can be merged right there (pick create-merge-commit / squash / rebase, then confirm with a second click), closed or reopened, and you can add labels and reviewers inline. The Issues tab gains filter-by-label and filter-by-author, and issues expand to body and comments too, with direct close/reopen — no browser detour.
- **子会话行左侧长出了樱花树枝**：侧栏会话树的子会话行现在用一条竖线挂在自己的父行下面，最深的那根线端带一朵小樱花，谁是谁的孩子一眼就能看出来；悬停或选中时线和花微微发亮。每级缩进宽度不变，标题对齐和以往完全一致。
  - EN: Child session rows now grow a sakura branch: each row hangs off its parent on a vertical guide line ending in a small blossom, so parentage reads at a glance; the line and flower glow faintly on hover or selection. Per-level indent width is unchanged, so title alignment is exactly as before.
- **窗口顶栏可以开多会话标签页了**：设置→外观→聊天里打开「多会话标签页」，最近打开的会话会以标签排在顶栏——点标签即切换，拖动能排序，悬停出现关闭钮、中键也能关。关标签不会删会话；关掉当前标签会自动切到旁边那个，旁边没有了就开一个全新的草稿会话。工作中的会话标签转着小圆圈，有未读消息的带小圆点。标签列表只存在本机，最多记住 10 个；快捷键 ⌘Tab / ⌘⇧Tab 左右切换（可在设置→快捷键里改）。
  - EN: The window header can now show session tabs. Enable "Session tabs" in Settings → Appearance → Chat: recently opened sessions line up as tabs in the header — click to switch, drag to reorder, close via the hover button or a middle click. Closing a tab never deletes the session; closing the active one activates its neighbour, and with no neighbour left it opens a fresh draft session. A working session spins a small circle on its tab, an unread one shows a dot. The tab list lives only on this machine and remembers up to 10; ⌘Tab / ⌘⇧Tab cycle left and right (rebindable in Settings → Shortcuts).
- **Windows 安装器换上了品牌化的安装向导**：安装过程不再是一屏灰白的默认界面，而是带着 MusePi 配色的分步向导——欢迎、选择安装位置、进度、完成，每一步都是应用自己的视觉。界面由 WebView2 承载、资源名带内容哈希，卸载界面同样换了品牌外观。
  - EN: The Windows installer now runs a branded wizard. Setup is no longer a plain default screen but a MusePi-styled step-by-step flow — welcome, install location, progress, finish — every step in the app's own visual language. The UI rides on WebView2 with content-hashed assets, and the uninstaller gets matching branding.
- **长任务的画面不再一路往上滚，你可以选择只保留最近几行**：一个回合跑上几分钟时，每个工具调用都占一行，正在做的事很快被顶出视野。设置→外观→显示里新增「运行中回合显示多少」：默认「全部显示」和现在一样；选「最近 7 行」就只留最近 7 行，更早的折成「⋯ 还有 N 行」。选项是写清楚的档位而不是一个数字，不需要猜 0 是什么意思。只作用于正在运行的那个回合：跑完的回合照旧折进活动摘要行，两者互不干扰。
  - EN: A long task no longer scrolls its own work off the screen, and you can choose how much of it stays visible. A turn that runs for minutes puts one row on screen per tool call, so what is happening right now gets pushed out of view. Settings → Appearance → Display gains "Running Turn Detail": the default "Show all" matches today; pick "Latest 7 rows" and only those stay, with the earlier ones folded into a "⋯ N more lines" row. The choices are named levels rather than a bare number, so nothing has to be guessed. It applies only to the turn that is running: a finished turn still folds into its activity summary, and the two do not interfere.
- **设置里的效果预览现在画的是真实内容的样子**：以前示例只有一句纯文本，看不出表格、代码块、列表这些实际排出来什么样。现在示例本身就是一段带表格、代码、列表和强调的回复，折叠和展开两种状态也并排画出来。顺带把正文的标题层级、引用块和列表间距调好了：以前标题只比正文大 1–3 像素又没有字重，层级几乎看不出来。
  - EN: The effects preview in settings now shows what real content looks like. The sample used to be one plain sentence, so there was no way to see how a table, a code block or a list actually renders. The sample is now a reply carrying a table, code, a list and emphasis, with both fold states drawn side by side. Heading levels, quotes and list spacing in the body text were tuned at the same time: headings were only 1–3px larger than body text with no weight change, so the hierarchy was nearly invisible.
- **答案主要是数据或结构时，智能体会直接摊开一页给你看，而不是罗列文字**：多个项目的规模对比、占比拆分、时间趋势、方案横向比较、组件连接关系、界面改动的前后对照——这些它现在画成内联页面，你还能在页面上直接选一项，选择会作为你的回复回到对话。工具描述里写明了什么时候该用页面、什么时候一行文字更好（两个数字画一页反而不如一句话），以及每轮最多一页。卡片仍然优先：有对应结构类型时用它，更省也更规整。
  - EN: When the answer is mostly data or structure, the agent now draws a page instead of listing prose: sizes or counts compared across items, a share of a whole, a trend over time, options compared on several criteria, how components connect, or a proposed interface change shown before and after. You can pick an option right on the page, and that choice comes back as your reply. The tool description states when a page helps and when a line of text is better (a page for two numbers is worse than a sentence), and caps it at one page per answer. Cards still come first: when a structured type fits, it is cheaper and more regular.
- **展开过的折叠，下次打开还在**：长对话里你手动展开的回合折叠，以前换个会话再回来就全部收回默认状态，滚动位置停在折叠处更是找不到内容。现在展开/收起按会话记住，重开会话原样恢复。
  - EN: Folds you expanded stay expanded. Round folds you opened by hand used to snap back to the default whenever you left and returned to a session — and a restored reading position inside an open fold landed on a closed one. Expansion is now remembered per session and restored on return.
- **智能体现在能读写你的「项目知识」了**：右侧面板里的笔记、待办、计划，此前只有你能操作——智能体完全够不着。现在它有了一个 `knowledge` 工具，能列出、新增、修改、删除这三类内容，和你看到的是同一份数据：它记下的笔记你立刻能在面板里读到，你勾掉的待办它下一轮就知道。顺带修好了一个隐秘的割裂：面板里的待办以前存在浏览器本地，定时任务或后台子会话根本读不到，现在和笔记、计划一样存在后端。已有的本地待办会在首次打开时自动迁移过来，不会丢。设置→工具里也有独立开关，不想要可以关掉。
  - EN: The agent can now read and write your project knowledge. The notes, todos and plans in the right panel used to be yours alone — the agent could not reach any of them. A `knowledge` tool now lists, adds, edits and deletes all three against the same data you see: a note it writes shows up in the panel immediately, and a todo you tick is known to its next turn. This also closes a hidden split: panel todos were stored in the browser, so scheduled tasks and background subagent sessions could not read them at all — they now live on the backend beside notes and plans. Existing local todos migrate automatically on first open and nothing is lost. Settings → Tools carries its own switch if you would rather turn it off.
- **智能体写的计划可以直接在界面上打开，也不会覆盖你正在改的内容**：它现在能把一份计划"摊开"给你看——对话里点一下，右侧面板就跳到那份计划并打开编辑器，不用自己去找。同时，改动长期文档（笔记/计划）时可以带上"我上次读到的是这段"，如果这段已经被你在面板里改过，改动会停下来提示重新读取，而不是默默把你的修改盖掉。知识按项目分开，它也能按项目 id 或路径指定要操作哪个项目。
  - EN: Plans the agent writes can now be opened right in the interface, and they no longer overwrite what you are editing. It can lay a plan out for you: one click in the chat jumps the right panel to that plan and opens the editor, instead of leaving you to find it. When it edits a long-lived document (a note or a plan) it can carry "this is the text I last read", so if you changed it in the panel meanwhile, the edit stops and asks it to re-read rather than silently discarding your change. Knowledge is kept per project, and it can name which project to work on by id or by path.
- **组件卡片和桌面看板现在可以关掉了**：这两个都是「往你眼前画东西」的工具——一个在对话里画实时卡片，一个往桌面看板上写卡片。它们此前没有开关，永远可用；不想要智能体自作主张画卡的人没法关。现在设置→工具里多了一组「展示工具」，两个开关各自独立，关掉后模型完全看不到该工具（既调不了、也不占上下文）。默认都开着，行为和以前一致。
  - EN: Widgets and the desktop board can now be turned off. Both are "draw something in front of you" tools — one renders a live card in the transcript, the other writes cards onto the desktop board — and neither had a switch: they were always available, so anyone who did not want the agent drawing cards had no way to say so. Settings → Tools gains a Presentation group with two independent switches; turning one off removes the tool from the model's list entirely (it cannot be called and costs no context). Both default to on, so existing behaviour is unchanged.
- **HTML 卡片可以直接让你选，选择自动回到对话**：模型给出的可交互 HTML 面现在能带选择按钮——点一下，你选的那项就作为一条用户消息发回给智能体，下一轮它就按这个选择继续。宿主直接注好选项卡和按钮的样式、以及深浅两套配色变量，模型不用自己写组件也不用自己挑颜色。一个卡片只能提交一次：提交后控件自动变灰，重复点击不会再发出第二条消息。需要在同一轮内就拿到答案的场景，仍然用提问工具。
  - EN: HTML cards can now collect your choice and carry it back into the conversation. An interactive HTML surface from the model can carry choice buttons — click one and your selection is sent to the agent as a message, so the next turn continues from what you picked. The host injects the option-card and button styles along with the colour values for both themes, so the model writes neither the component nor the palette. A card answers once: its controls grey out afterwards and a second click sends nothing. Questions that must be answered within the same turn still go through the ask tool.
- **插件池模型进入模型列表**：扩展注册的 provider 只要上次发现产出了模型即视为可用——此前 `getAvailable` 只认宿主 AuthStorage 里的凭据，账号池/导入凭据的渠道被发现后又被过滤掉，输入框模型列表永远看不到它们。池子清空后发现归零，对称关闭。
  - EN: Pool-backed extension models now reach the model list. A runtime (extension-registered) provider whose last discovery yielded models counts as available — previously `getAvailable` only honored host AuthStorage credentials, so discovered pool/import channels were filtered out of every picker. An emptied discovery closes the gate symmetrically.
- **扩展可以给自己的 provider 起名字、自己管凭据了**：`registerProvider({ label, resolveApiKey })` 两个新字段。`label` 让模型选择器的供应商分组显示「CodeBuddy」而不是注册 id；`resolveApiKey` 让账号池扩展在宿主 AuthStorage 之前提供凭据——此前池式 provider 的模型能被发现、能进列表，但每一轮都因「找不到 API key」而失败（宿主的存储一个 provider 只能存一条凭据，装不下账号池）。返回空即按「缺少凭据」报错，池子用尽时提示可操作。
  - EN: Extensions can now name their providers and own their credentials. `registerProvider({ label, resolveApiKey })` adds two fields: `label` makes the model picker's provider section show "CodeBuddy" instead of the registration id, and `resolveApiKey` lets a pool-backed extension supply credentials ahead of the host AuthStorage — previously such a provider's models were discovered and listed, then every turn failed with "no API key found", because the host's store holds one credential per provider and cannot represent a pool. Returning nothing reports a missing credential, so a drained pool reads as an actionable auth problem.
- **HTML 卡片选完之后知道自己选了什么**：卡片提交后不再只是变灰——它现在能读到「你选了哪一项」，从而把那一条标成已选、显示「已选择：A 档」，而不是点完就哑了。答案在卡片重建（比如深浅色切换）之后仍然保留。
  - EN: HTML cards can now tell which choice they sent. After a card submits it no longer just greys out — it can read back the answer, mark the chosen option and show "Selected: A", instead of going silent the moment you click. The answer survives the card rebuilding itself, such as on a light/dark switch.

- **问题与拉取请求中心可以搜索了**：列表顶部新增搜索框——按标题关键字过滤，输入 `#编号` 精确命中某一条，把一条 PR 或问题的链接粘贴进来还会给出直达的「打开链接」行。
  - EN: The issues and pull-requests center gains search. A search box at the top of the list filters by title keyword, an exact `#number` jumps straight to one entry, and pasting a PR or issue link offers a direct "open link" row.
- **设置 Git 页显示系统身份**：身份列表顶部新增一行只读的系统身份——直接读出本机 git 配置里的用户名和邮箱，还没写自定义身份时由它顶替默认，取自仓库本地配置时也会注明。
  - EN: The settings Git page now shows the system identity. A read-only row at the top of the identity list reads the user name and email from your machine's git config, standing in as the default until you write a custom identity, with a note when it comes from the repo's local config.

### Fixed
- **退出客户端后后台服务不再残留旧版本**：此前反复重开客户端时，上一个界面实例留下的本地服务会一直被沿用——它不属于当前实例，退出时不会被关闭，界面改动的代码也往往要等重启电脑后才生效。现在启动时会识别这种无人认领的服务：没有在运行的任务就直接换新（同时归当前实例管理，退出即关闭）；有任务在跑则只移交归属、不打断任务，退出时一样会正确关闭。开发重开流程也改为先优雅退出（正常走服务拆卸）再兜底强杀，不再每次重开都制造残留。
  - EN: The local service no longer lingers as a stale version after the client exits. When the client was relaunched repeatedly, the service left behind by the previous window instance was reused indefinitely — it belonged to no living instance, was never shut down on exit, and code changes often only took effect after a full machine reboot. Now the boot sequence detects such unowned services: when no task is running it is replaced with a fresh one (and put under the current instance's ownership, so it closes on exit); when a task is running, only ownership transfers — the task is not interrupted — and the service still shuts down correctly on exit. The dev relaunch flow also quits gracefully first (running the normal service teardown) and only force-kills as a fallback, instead of manufacturing a leftover service on every relaunch.
- **「问题与拉取请求」不再永远转圈了**：打开这个页面时如果没有选工作区、当前会话也没有工作区，页面会一直停在「加载中…」——那里其实没有任何在途请求，只是缺一个可查仓库的目录。现在看板有了自己的项目记忆：记住上次看的工作区（没了就回退到当前工作区、再回退到最近列表的第一个），页头常驻一个工作区切换器——切换只影响看板自己，不动应用其余部分；一个已知工作区都没有时，给一行说明而不是转圈。
  - EN: The Issues & PRs page no longer spins forever. When it opened with no workspace picked and the current session had none either, it sat at "Loading…" indefinitely — there was no request in flight, just no directory to query. The board now keeps its own project memory: it remembers the workspace it last showed (falling back to the current workspace, then the first known one), and the header hosts a workspace picker — switching moves only the board, never the rest of the app. With no known workspace at all it shows a one-line hint instead of spinning.
- **窄窗口下设置的搜索能正常用了**：窗口窄到设置左栏收成一列图标时，搜索框以前被挤成一条残缺的输入条，点了也没反应。现在收成一颗居中的放大镜按钮，点一下浮出完整的搜索面板——输入即过滤、点结果直达对应分区、点外面或按 Esc 收起。
  - EN: The settings search works in narrow windows again. When the window is narrow enough that the settings nav folds to an icon rail, the search field used to be crushed into a clipped sliver that did not respond; it is now a centered magnifier button that opens a full floating panel — type to filter, click a result to jump, click outside or press Escape to close.
- **挂靠输入框的桌宠不再被裁掉半边**：除了默认的小球，其他桌宠（精灵图）在输入框上方只显示左半边，右耳右脸被一条直边切掉。现在整只完整显示，多余的部分向左探进输入框上方区域，不再裁剪。
  - EN: Desk pets perched on the composer are no longer cropped to their left half. Besides the default orb, sprite pets showed only their left side above the input — the right ear and cheek cut off by a straight edge. The full sprite now renders, overflowing to the left over the input area instead of being clipped.
- **侧栏「全部展开/收起」按钮对子会话也生效了，收起展开还带上了动画**：以前点这个按钮只会折叠项目文件夹，父会话下面那一层层子会话纹丝不动——想全部收起只能挨个父行手动点。现在子会话树和项目文件夹同收同展：全展开时按下全部收起，有任何一个收起则全部展开。每个父行 chevron 的收起/展开也不再是行瞬间消失，而是整棵子树的行高一起平滑收缩/长开，和其余界面的伸缩动效同一节奏。
  - EN: The sidebar "expand/collapse all" button now covers sub-sessions too, and folding plays the shared animation. It used to fold only the project folders, leaving every parent's sub-session tree untouched — collapsing everything meant clicking each parent row by hand. Sub-session trees and project folders now move together: press when everything is expanded to collapse all, press when anything is collapsed to expand all. A per-parent chevron fold also no longer pops rows away instantly — the whole subtree's rows shrink and grow together in the same motion rhythm as the rest of the interface.

### Changed
- **窗口顶栏做了减法，终端和实例信息各归其位**：顶栏右上角只留工作状态与运行控制——「切换面板」「看板」两个按钮移除了（面板开关仍在右栏底部的折叠钮上，看板入口在侧栏菜单里），「终端」也挪进会话右侧的图标栏、和面板折叠钮做邻居（dsh 同款位置），开发服务器按钮照旧在顶栏。原来挤在顶栏右侧的实例信息（本地/远程守护进程、版本、延迟、浏览器桥诊断）整块搬到左侧面板底栏：状态点 + 「本地」胶囊，点开还是那套切换、添加实例、重连、退出的菜单，侧栏底部现在是「实例在左、设置在右」的两段布局。
  - EN: The window header was slimmed down and the terminal and instance info found their own homes. The header's top-right now keeps only work-status and run controls — the "toggle panel" and "board" buttons are gone (the panel fold button still sits at the bottom of the right rail, the board entry lives in the sidebar menu), and "terminal" moved into the session's right icon rail next to the panel-fold button (the dsh position); the dev-server button stays in the header. The instance info that used to crowd the header's right side (local/remote daemon, version, latency, browser-bridge diagnostics) moved wholesale to the sidebar footer: a status dot + "Local" capsule opening the same switch/add/reconnect/quit menu, so the sidebar footer now reads instance on the left, settings on the right.
- **多会话标签页更干净了**：激活的标签不再显示头像和标题下方的小字行——一条标签就是一行字，工作状态照旧用转圈和小圆点表达。省下的横向空间让标签能容下更长的标题。
  - EN: Session tabs got cleaner. The active tab no longer shows an avatar or the small second line under the title — a tab is now one line of text, with work state still carried by the spinner and dot. The saved horizontal room lets tabs hold longer titles.
- **会话标签页更像浏览器标签了**：每个标签有了固定宽度，长标题统一省略号收住，整排不再忽宽忽窄；鼠标悬停标签时浮出「⋯」菜单（关闭标签页、关闭其他标签页、归档）和单独的关闭钮，状态点同时退开让位；还没发出第一条消息的会话，标题显示为斜体——草稿会话一眼可辨。关闭其他标签页只留当前这个；归档把会话收进归档区，动作可撤销。
  - EN: Session tabs behave more like browser tabs. Each tab has a fixed width with long titles ellipsized, so the row no longer breathes wide and narrow; hovering a tab reveals a "⋯" menu (close tab, close other tabs, archive) plus a dedicated close button, while the status dot steps aside. A session that has not sent its first message shows its title in italics, so draft sessions are identifiable at a glance. Close-other keeps only the current tab; archive files the session away with an undo.
- **侧边通道模型可以跟着角色走了**：设置→模型→行为里的「侧边通道模型」（自动标题、空闲总结、快速提问这些旁路功能用的模型）过去只能选一个具体模型，选完就把角色模型的分配晾在一边——改角色模型它也不跟着变。现在选择器优先给角色项：「跟随 TINY 角色」「跟随 DEFAULT 角色」……选中即存 `@tiny` 这类角色别名，用的时候按角色分配实时解析；留空照旧跟随会话模型，旧的已存具体模型仍然有效。
  - EN: The side-channel model can now follow a role. The "side-channel model" picker in Settings → Model → Behavior (the model behind auto titles, idle recaps and quick side questions) used to force one concrete model, leaving role assignments out in the cold — changing the role's model never moved it. The selector now offers roles first: "Follow the TINY role", "Follow the DEFAULT role", … — selecting stores a role alias like `@tiny` resolved through the role assignment at use time; empty still follows the session model, and previously stored concrete models keep working.
- **输入框和工作状态卡的玻璃有了「液态」边缘**：磨砂玻璃只会把背后的画面糊掉，真正的液态玻璃在边缘还会让光线弯一下——现在输入框卡片和工作状态卡展开时，背后的内容会在边缘产生细微的折射弯折，外加一圈淡淡的内高光，看起来像一块有厚度的玻璃而不是一层磨砂膜。文字和控件保持清晰，只有背景被弯折；不支持这一效果的系统上自动退回原来的磨砂样子，不会有任何缺失感。
  - EN: The composer and work-status glass now has a liquid edge. Frosted glass only blurs what sits behind it; real liquid glass also bends light at the rim — the backdrop behind the composer card and the expanded work-status card now refracts subtly at the edges, with a faint inner highlight, reading as a piece of glass with thickness rather than a matte film. Text and controls stay sharp; only the backdrop bends, and systems without the effect fall back to the previous frosted look with nothing missing.
- **左侧会话多起来之后，列表和搜索不再跟着一顿一顿**：以前每 5 秒一次的状态同步会把每一条会话记录重新造一遍，哪怕只有一台后台会话的计数变了；搜索打字、点开菜单这些小事也会把全部会话重新排一遍序。现在没变的会话保持原样、整列没变化时界面一次重绘都不做，排序结果也只在会话列表真正变动时才重算——会话越多，差距越明显。
  - EN: With a long session history, the sidebar list and search no longer stutter. The 5-second status sync used to rebuild every session record even when only one background session's counter moved, and small things like typing a search or opening a menu re-sorted the entire list. Unchanged sessions now keep their identity, a fully unchanged sync triggers zero repaints, and the sort is recomputed only when the list actually changes — the more sessions you have, the bigger the difference.
- **设置项按各自的归属重新归位，不再出现「主题设置中间夹着字号」这种错位**：外观页里强调色原本排在滚动条设置之后，离它该在的主题块很远，现在紧跟主题模式与浅/深主题；聊天页里「输出风格」和「流式输出动效」原本混在「消息渲染」里，但它们管的是文字怎么画、不是画面显示什么，现在独立成「呈现风格」一组。只调整归属，上下顺序基本没动，找东西的地方还是原来的地方。
  - EN: Settings now sit with the group they belong to, instead of a theme control stranded among layout options. The accent colour used to come after the scrollbar settings, far from the theme block it belongs to; it now follows the theme mode and the light/dark themes. On the chat page, output style and the streaming effect were mixed into message rendering, but they govern how text is DRAWN rather than what is displayed, so they now form their own Presentation section. Only the grouping changed — the overall order is essentially as it was, so things are still where you last found them.
- **设置里重复的预览合并成一处，多余的信息状态条去掉了**：外观页和聊天页各画了一块示例内容（标题、段落、代码、列表），其实看的是同一件事——现在只在外观页保留一块，它会跟着你在聊天页选的输出风格和流式动效走。另外移除了「信息状态条」选项：它画的那行模型/上下文/消耗信息，状态卡片里已经用更完整的方式显示了，留着只是两处说同一件事。
  - EN: The duplicated previews in settings are now one, and the redundant info bar is gone. The appearance page and the chat page each drew a sample block (heading, paragraph, code, list) showing the same thing — only the appearance page keeps it now, and it follows the output style and streaming effect you pick on the chat page. The "info status bar" option is removed too: the model/context/usage line it drew is already shown more fully by the status cards, so keeping it meant saying the same thing twice.
- **看板卡片的配色统一了，加一个新配色只要改一行**：卡片此前把整套背景、边框、四级文字色都写死在"深色/蓝色/浅色"三个块里，而且同一份代码在看板卡片、集合预览、编辑器预览台各抄了一遍——改一次配色要改三个地方。现在这套配色变成一条"离底色多远"的色阶：卡片只声明自己在第几级，配色跟着你的主题色走，文字色从卡片自己的背景推导出来。深色、浅色这类需要反色的卡片仍然单独处理。新增一个配色是加一行声明，不是加一段复制的主题。
  - EN: Card colours are defined once. A card used to hard-code its background, border and four text colours, and the same block was copied into the board card, the collection preview and the editor preview stage — so a palette fix meant editing three places. The palette is now one ramp of "how far off the app surface this card sits": a card only declares which step it takes, the tint follows your accent colour, and text colours are derived from the card's own surface. The tones that must invert (dark, light) still say so. A new tone is one declaration, not a copied theme.
- **智能体画的页面里，颜色在浅色卡片上也读得清**：页面跑在一个拿不到主题的隔离沙箱里，宿主把配色变量送进去。以前是原样转发——某个蓝色在应用背景上清楚，落到一张浅色卡片上就几乎看不见了，而页面自己无从检查（它看不到主题）。现在宿主在送之前先按卡片实际的底色量一次对比度，不够就把颜色往黑或白推一点，推到刚好可读为止，同时尽量保留原来的色相，不会把品牌色换成纯黑白。
  - EN: Colours inside an agent-drawn page now stay legible on a tinted card. The page runs in an isolated sandbox with no access to the theme, so the host ships the colour variables in. It used to forward them as-is — a blue that reads fine on the app background all but vanishes on a light card, and the page had no way to check, since it cannot see the theme. The host now measures each colour against the surface it actually lands on and nudges it toward black or white just far enough to clear the bar, keeping as much of the author's hue as possible instead of flattening it to pure black or white.
- **更长的 HTML 页面不再被截断，收起的活动折叠也不再让正文跳动**：内联 HTML 面的长度上限从 64KB 提到 200KB——长一点的仪表盘、对照表、界面示意以前会被判为过大而显示成空白，现在能完整渲染；这个上限在工具侧和渲染侧是同一个值，不会再出现"工具收下了、界面却不画"。另外，收起一个位于视口上方的活动折叠时，它下面的内容会立刻固定住：以前折叠动画跑完，你正在读的回复会跟着往上跳一段。
  - EN: Longer HTML pages render instead of being truncated, and closing an activity fold no longer moves the text under you. The inline HTML ceiling rises from 64KB to 200KB, so a longer dashboard, comparison table or interface sketch no longer degrades to a blank box; the limit is now one shared value on both the tool and the render side, so it can no longer be accepted by one and refused by the other. Closing a fold that sits above the viewport also pins what follows it — the animation used to drag the reply you were reading upward.
- **慢一点的请求不再误报失败，等待时也能看到它在走**：单个请求的等待上限从 15 秒提到 45 秒——建会话要装载整套扩展、模型回合要在供应商侧准备一会，这两件事本来就会超过 15 秒，过去那时会弹出红色的「请求失败」，而重试一次立刻成功。探活另有一条独立的链路负责（心跳每 20 秒一次、45 秒全静默才判定连接已断），所以放宽这个上限不会把「卡死」拖成「一直等」。等待超过 2.5 秒后，界面上会跟着显示「已等待 N 秒」，不再是一块不动的骨架。
  - EN: A slow request no longer reports a false failure, and a long wait shows that it is moving. The per-request ceiling rose from 15s to 45s — creating a session loads the whole extension set and a model turn spends time in provider prep, both of which legitimately outrun 15s and used to surface as a red "request failed" banner that a retry immediately fixed. Liveness is a separate mechanism (a 20s heartbeat, force-close only after 45s of total silence), so raising this ceiling does not turn a hang into an endless wait. Past 2.5 seconds the wait now displays "Waiting Ns" instead of a frozen skeleton.
- **流式输出时界面明显更轻**：此前智能体每吐一个词，整个界面（对话、顶栏、状态球）就重绘一次；现在按约 10 次每秒合批刷新，且顶栏只在「运行中 / 输出中 / 有审批」这几个状态真的变化时才重绘，不再跟着每个词走。首字出现的速度不变——第一批内容仍是立刻上屏。空闲时的状态栏也不再每 3 秒无谓重绘一次。
  - EN: The interface is noticeably lighter while a turn streams. Previously every token the agent emitted repainted the whole shell — transcript, header and status orb; refreshes are now batched at about 10 per second, and the header repaints only when "working / replying / has an approval" actually changes instead of following every token. First-token latency is unchanged: the first content still lands immediately. The status bar also stops repainting needlessly every 3 seconds while idle.
- **插件的设置面板不再空等**：插件自己的 daemon 侧 RPC 过去每次都要重新装载一遍入口，装好的插件调一次要等数秒——面板还没画出第一行就超时了，看着像「插件加载不出自己的数据」。现在同一个插件只加载一次，装卸/启停/配置改动仍然立刻生效；面板本身也改成先画出全部渠道的骨架、账号数据随后并行到位，重开页面时沿用上次的内容，不再从零转圈。
  - EN: A plugin's own settings panel no longer waits on an empty screen. Each session-less plugin RPC used to re-load the plugin's entry module — several seconds per call on a real plugin — so a panel firing its four reads timed out before the first row rendered, which read as "the plugin can't load its own data". The entry now loads once per plugin while install, enable/disable and config changes still take effect immediately; the panel itself draws the full channel rail first and loads account data in parallel, and reuses the last view on reopen instead of spinning from scratch.
- **新建会话不再偶发超时**：创建会话会为新会话装载全部扩展，会话多的时候会超过默认 15 秒的 RPC 等待——那时报的是「请求超时」，而重试一次就能成功。现在给这个操作单独放宽了等待时间。
  - EN: Creating a session no longer times out intermittently. Creating one instantiates the whole extension set for the new session, which with many sessions exceeds the default 15-second RPC budget — it reported "request timeout" while a retry succeeded immediately. The operation now has its own longer budget.

- **切换表面不再丢编辑器未存文本**：右面板只挂载当前 tab 的 body，跨表面切换会卸载文件面板——此前未存草稿缓存在实例级 ref 里，随卸载一起死，回来变成磁盘旧字节。现在缓存提为模块级（绝对路径键；保存/改回已存即删），卸载 stash、重开恢复、dirty 守卫协议不动；终端/浏览器/chat 列此前已是 keep-alive，无需动。文件树与 trajectory 滚动位置明确不保留（重挂重置）。
  - EN: Switching surfaces no longer drops unsaved editor text. The right panel mounts only the active tab's body, so leaving for another surface unmounts the file pane — unsaved drafts used to live in an instance-scoped ref that died with it and came back as stale disk bytes. The cache is now module-scoped (absolute-path keys; dropped on save or edit-back-to-saved) with the stash/restore/dirty-guard protocol unchanged; terminal, browser, and the chat column were already keep-alive. File-tree and trajectory scroll positions are explicitly not preserved (reset on remount).
- **扩展能注册自己的内部 URL 协议了**：`ctx.registerUrlScheme({scheme, immutable, resolve, write?, complete?})` 向进程内 URL 路由贡献新的 `scheme://` 命名空间——读/写/补全直呈 `ProtocolHandler` 形状；已占用的 built-in 或他方槽位拒绝抢占，同源重注册替换；`unregisterUrlScheme` 摘除，会话结束仅清理仍属自己的槽。
  - EN: Extensions can register their own internal URL schemes. `ctx.registerUrlScheme({scheme, immutable, resolve, write?, complete?})` contributes a new `scheme://` namespace to the in-process URL router with the `ProtocolHandler` shape directly; occupied built-in or foreign slots refuse to be hijacked while re-registering our own replaces it, and session shutdown only removes slots that are still ours.
- **扩展能注册自己的子代理类型了**：`ctx.registerSubagent({name, description, systemPrompt, ...})` 向本会话的 task 工具提供新代理类型——解析优先于文件发现（含 bundled），`disabledAgents`、模型覆盖、深度守卫自动适用；`unregisterSubagent` 摘除；runner-less 命令上下文明确拒绝（无归属会话可解析）。
  - EN: Extensions can register their own subagent types. `ctx.registerSubagent({name, description, systemPrompt, ...})` contributes an agent type to the session's task tool — resolution consults registered types before filesystem discovery, and `disabledAgents`, model overrides, and depth guards apply automatically; `unregisterSubagent` removes one, and runner-less command contexts refuse explicitly.
- **扩展能注册自己的 MCP 服务了**：`ctx.registerMcpServer(name, config)` 把服务接到当前会话——配置走 manager 自身校验，工具经正常刷新到达并沿用会话既有审批；同名同配置重复注册是无操作，异配置冲突失败而非抢占；`unregisterMcpServer` 摘除，会话结束自动断开仍持有的全部。runner-less 命令上下文因无 manager 而报不可用（校验本身可用）。
  - EN: Extensions can register their own MCP servers. `ctx.registerMcpServer(name, config)` attaches a server to the current session — configs pass the manager's own validation, tools arrive through the normal refresh under the session's existing approval, re-registering an identical config is a no-op while a conflicting live name fails instead of hijacking, and whatever is still owned disconnects on session shutdown.
- **扩展能给自己定提醒了**：扩展可通过 `ctx.schedule` 创建一次或周期提醒（`afterSeconds`/`at`/`everySeconds`），到点以跟随轮的方式回到本会话；提醒随会话共存亡——会话结束即止，跨重启的日历级计划仍走守护进程定时任务。
  - EN: Extensions can set their own reminders. `ctx.schedule` creates one-shot (`afterSeconds`/`at`) or recurring (`everySeconds`) reminders that return to the owning session as a follow-up turn; reminders live and die with the session, while durable calendar-grade plans stay with the daemon cron tasks.
- **插件安装不再静默执行依赖脚本**：此前插件工程没有 `trustedDependencies`，bun 会静默执行所有依赖的安装脚本——拖进来的包可在安装时跑任意代码，装完也没有任何提示。现在默认拒绝（空 allowlist），被拦下的脚本在安装结果里按名点出，带着这份名单重装一次即按名单放行并持久记录；安装失败回滚不会撤销已给的批准。
  - EN: Plugin installs no longer execute dependency scripts silently. The plugins project previously had no `trustedDependencies`, so bun ran every dependency's install scripts without a word — a dragged-in package could run arbitrary code at install time with nothing reported afterwards. The default is now deny (empty allowlist): blocked scripts are named in the install result, and reinstalling with that name list approves and persists them; a failed install's rollback never revokes a granted approval.
- **会话行右键菜单补齐删除、导出与 AI 重命名**：此前行菜单只能置顶/归档/手工改名/复制——删除藏在归档视图与顶栏，导出只对当前会话，标题只能手改。现在行内直接删（同顶栏确认）、导出任意会话为 Markdown、让模型按首条消息重起标题（空会话或模型不可用会明说原因而不是没反应）。
  - EN: Session rows gained delete, export, and AI rename in their right-click menu. Previously the row menu could only pin/archive/rename/copy — deletion lived in the archive view and the top bar, export covered just the active session, and titles were manual-only. Rows now delete in place (same confirm as the top bar), export any session to Markdown, and ask the model to title from the first message (empty sessions or an unavailable model report the reason instead of staying silent).
- **安装版启动不再每次清空资源缓存**：清空缓存原本只为了开发时 dist 重建后不给旧文件，但它却在每一次启动都执行——包装版的资源名本身带内容哈希，清空只会让首帧重新下载一遍全部资源。现在只在开发模式下执行。
  - EN: Packaged launches no longer wipe the resource cache on every start. Clearing it only existed so a rebuilt dist would not serve stale files in dev, but it ran on every launch — and since packaged assets carry content-hashed names, clearing only forced the first frame to re-download everything. It is now dev-only.
- **安装包现在真正切分代码块，首屏不再整包一起下**：渲染器有二十处动态加载（图表、截图库、线协定义等），但打包未开分割，它们被内联进主包——主窗口首屏要下载 11.6 MB 的单个脚本，五个窗口各打一份几乎相同的完整副本（合计 39 MB），而其中还装着只有打开 PDF 预览或终端面板才用的库。现在共享块真正被切分：主窗口首屏减少 25%，宠物、气泡、托盘菜单三个窗口各减少 39%。
  - EN: The shipped bundle now really splits code. The renderer had twenty dynamic imports (mermaid, html-to-image, the wire types), but the build did not split, so they were inlined into the main bundle: the main window's first frame pulled a single 11.6 MB script, and each of the five windows shipped its own near-complete copy (39 MB total), libraries included that only load when a PDF preview or the terminal panel opens. Shared chunks are now split for real: the main window's first load is 25% smaller and the pet, bubble and tray-menu windows are each 39% smaller.
- **应用内的结果提示统一为右下角浮窗**：保存成功、移动工作区、归档会话这类操作结果以前有的挤进顶部错误条、有的几乎没有反馈，报错则是一整条横幅横在页面最上方。现在它们收敛成右下角浮起的小卡片——成功、失败、信息三种形态各带对应图标，卡片上的动作按钮点了直接执行，几秒后自行消失；真正的守护进程连接错误仍保留顶部横幅。
  - EN: In-app result notices are unified as toasts in the bottom-right corner. Results like a successful save, a moved worktree or an archived session used to squeeze into the top error bar or get no feedback at all, while errors were a full banner across the very top. They now converge into small cards floating at the bottom-right — success, failure and info forms with matching icons, action buttons that run on click, auto-dismiss after a few seconds; genuine daemon-connection errors keep the top banner.
- **能力中心的工具行不再跟着列表滚走**：插件、技能两个页签顶部的工具行（搜索、添加、整理、筛选）以前随内容一起滚动，滚下去就找不到。现在它们吸在页头，列表在下方独立滚动。
  - EN: The capability center's tool rows no longer scroll away with the list. The header rows (search, add, organize, filters) in both the plugins and skills tabs used to scroll off with the content; they now stick to the page header while the list scrolls underneath.
- **问题与拉取请求看板刷新时不再闪空**：切换或自动刷新时，面板以前会先把内容清空、转圈等数据回来。现在刷新沿用上一次的内容，只有第一次打开还没有任何数据时才显示骨架行。
  - EN: The issues and pull-requests board no longer blanks out on refresh. Switching or auto-refreshing used to clear the pane and spin until data arrived; a refresh now keeps the previous content, and skeleton rows appear only on the very first load with nothing to show yet.

### Fixed
- **PR 列表行上的检查状态圆点以前全是灰色**：小圆点区分成功/失败/进行中三种样式类，但样式表里缺了对应的规则，所以不管检查跑得怎么样都只显示一颗无色圆点。现在三种状态各自带上绿/红/呼吸灰的颜色，和卡片里的汇总行一致。
  - EN: The check-state dots on PR list rows used to be all grey. The dot carried one of three state classes (success / failure / pending) but the stylesheet had no rules for them, so every check rendered as a colourless dot no matter how it ran. The three states now get their green / red / pulsing-grey colours, matching the summary line in the card.
- **「图片 URL 后端」这类多选设置不再碎成一地单字格子**：候选多的列表型设置（图片发布后端有 50 个候选）以前把每个候选渲染成一颗芯片，芯片又被拉成等宽，长名字全被裁得只剩一个字，根本没法点。现在已选项显示为芯片（可排序的带前移/后移钮），未选的后端收进一个「添加」下拉，一眼就是一条清爽的优先级链。
  - EN: Multi-select settings like "Image URL Backends" no longer shatter into single-character chips. A long candidate list (the image backends ship ~50 choices) used to render every candidate as a chip stretched to an equal share of the row, clipping each label down to its first character. Selected items now show as chips (with move-earlier/later buttons for ordered lists) and the unselected choices collapse into one "Add" dropdown — a clean priority chain at a glance.
- **设置导航折成图标栏时，搜索框不再只剩一个残缺的输入框**：窄窗口下设置导航自动收成一列图标，搜索框以前被挤得只剩放大镜和一像素宽的输入缺口；现在它是一颗居中的放大镜按钮，点一下在下方弹出正常宽度的输入面板，输入体验不受影响。
  - EN: The settings nav search box no longer collapses into a broken sliver when the nav folds to an icon rail. In a narrow window the nav auto-collapses to a column of icons, and the search box used to be squeezed into a magnifier plus a one-pixel input gap; it is now a centered magnifier button that drops a full-width input panel below on click, so searching works the same.
- **设置界面里那条来路不明的竖线消失了**：打开设置后主会话侧栏其实还挂在原地（只是藏起来），它的宽度拖拽柄以前照常渲染，在设置页面上留一条贯穿的细线。现在设置占据侧栏期间拖拽柄一并隐藏，返回会话才恢复。
  - EN: The mysterious vertical line in the settings view is gone. Opening settings kept the main session sidebar mounted (just hidden), and its width drag handle still rendered — leaving a full-height hairline across the settings page. The handle now hides while settings occupy the sidebar and returns when you go back.
- **「扩展与插件」页底部不再长出插件的整页面板**：装了自带管理面板的插件后，扩展清单底部以前会再渲染一遍它的完整设置页（账号池、余额列表一大块），卡片标题还显示插件入口文件名「index.ts」。现在整页面板只出现在它该在的地方——插件详情弹窗的「插件设置」区和设置导航自己的页面项；扩展清单底部只留每行的紧凑配置卡，标题显示插件名。
  - EN: The Extensions & Plugins page no longer sprouts a plugin's full page panel at the bottom. With a plugin that ships its own management panel, the extension inventory used to render the whole page again (account pool, balance list and all) under the list, with the entry file name "index.ts" as the card title. Full pages now live only where they belong — the plugin detail dialog's "Plugin settings" section and the settings nav page entry; the inventory footer keeps only per-row compact config cards, titled by the plugin's name.
- **PR 与问题中心不再把「查不到」伪装成「没有」**：gh 没装、没登录或 daemon 离线时，以前 PR 页会静默显示成「当前分支没有 PR」并给出创建表单，问题页则可能一直转圈。现在这类底层失败会直接显示失败提示和重试入口，不再误导你去创建一个其实已存在的 PR。
  - EN: The PR & Issues center no longer disguises "couldn't fetch" as "nothing there". When gh was missing, unauthenticated or the daemon unreachable, the PR pane used to silently render as "no PR on this branch" and offer the create form, while the issues pane could spin forever. Such low-level failures now surface as an explicit error with retry, instead of luring you into creating a PR that already exists.
- **扩展注册的模型发现失败后可以强制重试了**：宿主侧新增 `models.refreshRuntime` 接口，清掉按供应商缓存的模型目录缓存并立刻重跑在线发现——此前发现一旦失败（或跑在凭据落地之前），缓存期内模型列表会一直空着，插件面板也只能干等。账号池类插件的设置页可以用它给「重新发现模型」按钮兜底。
  - EN: Failed model discovery for extension-registered providers can now be forced to retry. The host gains a `models.refreshRuntime` endpoint that busts the per-provider catalog cache and re-runs online discovery immediately — previously a failed discovery (or one that ran before credentials landed) left the model list empty for the whole cache TTL, with nothing a plugin panel could do but wait. Pool-style plugins can back their "rediscover models" button with it.
- **滚到最底部时，最新消息稳稳停在输入框上方**：以前把对话钉到底部时，最后几条消息会滑进悬浮输入框的玻璃底下，拖窗口、新开会话、切换布局后尤其明显，切走再切回才恢复。根因是输入框预留间距的那段空白挂在滚动容器外面，从没算进滚动高度，滚动锚点再怎么修也够不到它；现在这段空白在滚动内容里，钉底时最后一条消息与输入框之间始终留着正常间距。回复流式输出时最新一行的下缘也不再被底部的渐隐吃掉——静止时消息是以渐变方式溶进输入框玻璃后面的，轮到最新一行则完全清晰。
  - EN: Pinning a conversation to the bottom now keeps the newest message comfortably above the composer. The last rows used to slide under the composer's glass when pinned — most visibly after a window resize, a new session or a layout switch, and only a leave-and-return cleared it. The root cause was the reserved blank band living OUTSIDE the scroller: it never counted toward the scroll height, so no scroll-anchor fix could reach it. The band is now part of the scroll content, and the latest message keeps a steady gap above the composer whenever you are at the bottom. The bottom edge of the newest line is also no longer eaten by the fade while a reply streams — rows dissolve into the glass gradient at rest, but the newest line stays fully legible.
- **空态输入框的提示语轮播只在输入框聚焦时转动**：以前欢迎页的提示语（可输入 @ 引用文件之类的用法提示）不管有没有焦点都在后台轮播，切专注模式时和形态动画叠在一起造成明显卡顿。现在失焦即停、聚焦从头开始，专注模式切换不再被轮播拖慢。
  - EN: The welcome-composer tip carousel now only advances while the composer is focused. The tips (usage hints like "@ to reference a file") used to keep cycling in the background regardless of focus, and entering focus mode stacked the carousel against the morph animation into a visible stutter. It now stops on blur and restarts from the first tip on focus, so focus-mode switching is no longer slowed by the carousel.
- **专注模式的提示语不再错行折断**：形态动画期间提示文字会短暂换行错位，现在动画期间隐藏、落定后显示，并加了对齐约束，提示永远是一行。
  - EN: The focus-mode tip no longer wraps onto a wrong line. The tip text used to break mid-animation; it is now hidden while the morph plays and shown once settled, with an alignment constraint, so the tip stays on one line.
- **输入框上方的连接器胶囊不再占满整行，用量胶囊也贴回底部**：连接器胶囊以前在停靠栏里被拉成整行宽，一行只有一个胶囊却横贯输入框；现在按内容自适应宽度、靠左排。输入框下方「性能与用量」胶囊与窗口底部的间距也收紧了一档，不再悬空。
  - EN: The connectors capsule above the composer no longer spans the full row, and the usage capsule sits flush again. The capsule used to be stretched to the full dock width — one capsule bar across the whole composer; it now sizes to its content and aligns left. The gap between the "Performance & usage" capsule under the composer and the window bottom was also tightened by a notch instead of floating.
- **插件详情弹窗不再把设置整页塞进小窗**：装了带设置页的插件后，从插件列表点进去，弹窗里会把那个插件的完整设置页再渲染一遍——长长一页挤在弹窗里，和它本来就在的设置导航项完全重复。现在弹窗只显示插件注册的紧凑组件（概览卡一类），整页设置留在设置导航里。
  - EN: The plugin detail dialog no longer squeezes a whole settings page into the small window. With a plugin that registers a settings page, opening it from the plugin list rendered that full page again inside the dialog — a long scrollable page duplicated next to the settings nav entry it already lives in. The dialog now shows only the compact components a plugin registers (overview cards and the like); full pages stay in the settings nav.
- **工作状态卡展开时不再盖住右侧的最新消息**：这张卡锚在会话视图右上角，展开成面板后高度大增，原先会把右边几行消息压在玻璃底下。现在展开时会话列表会在头部自动让出与面板等高的空间，收起回胶囊后空间随即归还，平时不占用任何额外高度。
  - EN: The expanded work-status card no longer covers the newest messages on the right. The card anchors to the session view's top-right corner, and expanding it into a panel used to press the right-hand message rows under the glass. Expanding now reserves headroom equal to the panel height at the top of the transcript, returned the moment it collapses back to the capsule — no extra height is spent in the steady state.
### Added
- **插件之间可以互相依赖、互相提供服务了**：一个插件可以声明 `provide` 发布自己的能力，另一个插件用 `inject` 声明需要它——装载时就会检查，缺依赖的插件直接拒绝装载并说明缺谁（而不是装到一半、首次调用才炸）。运行期通过 `pi.service(name)` 取到对方发布的对象；提供者卸载时服务一并消失，消费者不会拿到一个已经死掉的引用。
  - EN: Plugins can now depend on each other and provide services to each other. A plugin declares `provide` to publish what it offers and another declares `inject` to say what it needs — checked at load time, so a plugin with a missing dependency is refused outright and told which one (instead of half-mounting and failing on first call). At runtime `pi.service(name)` returns the object the provider published, and when a provider unloads its service goes with it so a consumer never holds a dead reference.
- **插件可以声明式地声明自己的入口了**：插件入口除原来的默认导出外，现在还认 `name` / `inject` / `provide` / `Config` / `apply(ctx, config)` 这套形状——`name` 让诊断信息不再是一串会随机器变化的文件路径，`Config` 声明的配置会经过校验后作为第二参传进 `apply`，不必再自己绕一圈读配置存储。原来的默认导出写法继续可用。
  - EN: Plugin entries can now be declared. Besides the default export, an entry may take the `name` / `inject` / `provide` / `Config` / `apply(ctx, config)` shape — `name` makes diagnostics read as a name rather than a path that changes per machine, a declared `Config` is validated and handed to `apply` as its second argument instead of a round-trip through the config store, and the previous default-export form keeps working.
- **插件有了两个自己的原语：effect 和 state**：`pi.effect(cleanup)` 给任意副作用登记回收——插件自己开的定时器、监听、连接以前没人管，重载几次就留下几个孤儿；现在卸载、会话结束、登记回滚时都会调用它，其中一个抛错也不影响其余。`pi.state` 是插件私有的作用域存储，按插件实例隔离、随卸载清空——替代了模块级变量那种"同一份代码被加载几遍就分成几份"的写法。
  - EN: Plugins gained two primitives of their own: `effect` and `state`. `pi.effect(cleanup)` registers a reclamation for any side effect — timers, listeners and connections a plugin opens itself used to have no owner, so a few reloads left a few orphans behind; it now runs on unload, session shutdown and registration rollback, with one throwing cleanup unable to stop the rest. `pi.state` is per-plugin-instance storage that is cleared on unload, replacing the module-level variable that silently split into one copy per load of the same code.
### Fixed
- **回合进行中，最后一条消息不再被输入框压住，弹出的提问卡也不会盖住末尾内容**：此前会话一进入工作状态，输入框与最后一行之间的预留距离就按「开场高度」锁死——状态条或排队消息把输入框撑高后，最后一行仍停在原位置，整场回合都压在玻璃底下；提问卡浮在输入框上方时同理，它的高度根本不在布局测量里，弹出即遮文。现在工作态的预留改为跟随输入框当前高度（保持一圈固定间隙），提问卡弹出期间则按其高度额外加一段预留，关闭即恢复。
  - EN: While a turn runs, the last message no longer sits under the composer, and an ask card no longer covers the tail either. The reserved gap used to lock to the opening height the moment the session started working, so once the working strip or a queued message grew the composer, the final line stayed put — under the glass for the whole turn; the ask card, floating above the composer, was never part of the layout measurement at all and hid text the moment it appeared. The working reservation now follows the composer's current height (keeping one fixed gap), and while an ask card is up its height is added to the reservation, restoring on close.
- **「性能与用量」读数回到了输入框正下方居中，点胶囊不再报错，最新消息也不会再被输入区盖住**：统计行此前被挪到了输入框上方，还退化成两枚点不动的死数字，点胶囊更是直接弹错。现在两枚胶囊（活动：轮次/步数 · 解码速度；用量：tokens · 缓存命中率）回到输入框正下方居中，点开各有明细弹窗——模型/工具耗时、平均首字、token 拆分。点不开的根因是插件拿到的界面库少了弹窗依赖的那一个入口，已补上。同根因的还有长会话里最后一条消息被输入区压住：连接器读数这类信息是输入框画出来之后才异步到位的，界面却按「还没到」时的最小高度预留了空间，现在开场预留会等这些行落定再锁高度。
  - EN: The performance-and-usage readouts are back below the input, centered, clicking a pill no longer errors, and the latest message no longer hides under the composer. The row had drifted above the input and degraded to two dead numbers that ignored clicks — clicking even threw an error. The two pills (activity: turns/steps · decode speed; usage: tokens · cache hit rate) now sit centered below the input again, each opening its detail popover — model/tool time, average time-to-first-token, token breakdown. The click failure came from the UI library handed to plugins missing the one entry its popover needs; that entry is supplied now. The last-message cover-up shared the root cause: readouts like the connector land only after the composer has painted, yet the layout reserved space by the too-small minimum from before they arrived — the opening reservation now waits for those rows to settle before latching.
- **连接器菜单有了自己设计的样式，空间不够时胶囊收成 已选/总数**：弹窗的行换成自绘勾选框（玻璃材质下的 accent 勾选，不再是原生蓝方块），头部显示面板名和服务器数量、操作链接右对齐，列表过高时在内部滚动；输入框一行挤不下时，胶囊从「连接器 3/11」收成「3/11」，默认态仍保留「连接器」字样。
  - EN: The connector menu got a design of its own, and the pill narrows to chosen/total when space runs short. Popup rows use a custom-drawn checkbox (an accent tick in the glass language instead of the native blue square), the header shows the panel name and server count with action links right-aligned, and a tall list scrolls internally; when the input row cannot fit it, the pill shrinks from "连接器 3/11" to "3/11", keeping the word 连接器 only in the default state.
- **悬浮输入框回到会话栏里面，不再压住右侧面板**：悬浮输入框的定位参照物选错了层级，锚到了整个窗口——它因此画出了会话栏，盖住右侧面板，输入区一行的连接器胶囊也随之看不见。现在它锚在会话栏内，连接器和用量读数都回到输入框旁边。
  - EN: The floating composer no longer spills over the right panel. Its anchor resolved to the whole window instead of the conversation column, so it painted outside the conversation, covered the right panel, and the connector capsule in the input row vanished with it. It now anchors inside the column, and the connector and usage readouts sit back beside the input.
- **「性能与用量」恢复成两枚可点击的胶囊，点开出明细弹窗**：统计行搬进插件时退化成两枚只读数字，点击毫无反应。现在活动胶囊（轮次/步数 · 解码速度）与用量胶囊（tokens · 缓存命中率）都恢复可点击——点开液态玻璃弹窗列出模型耗时、工具耗时、平均首字与账单桶拆分等明细，Escape 或点外部关闭，两个弹窗同排最多开一个。命中率格式也顺调：能用整数表达且未到 100 就用整数，贴近 100 时才带出最少小数位。
  - EN: The performance-and-usage stats are clickable pills again, each opening a detail popover. When the row moved into a plugin it degraded to two read-only numbers that ignored clicks. The activity pill (turns/steps, decode speed) and the usage pill (tokens, cache hit rate) are clickable once more — a liquid-glass popover lists model time, tool time, average time-to-first-token and the per-bucket token breakdown; Escape or an outside click closes it, and at most one popover opens per row. The hit-rate format was adjusted along the way: an integer is used whenever it expresses the value and stays below 100, with the minimum decimals appearing only close to 100.
- **设置项的英文文案不会再漏翻而没人发现**：设置清单里的每一条标签和描述都要在界面词表里有中文，之前没有任何检查盯着这件事——新增一个设置项忘了翻译、或者大小写和词表对不上，都会静默显示英文。现在有了门禁，新漏翻译的会直接挡在提交前；同时把已知的存量记成了基线，只会减不会增。顺带修好一处选项说明的提示框（鼠标悬停时那段小字）一直显示英文的问题。
  - EN: A settings label can no longer go untranslated without anyone noticing. Every label and description in the settings schema needs a Chinese entry, and nothing was checking that — a newly added setting with no translation, or a key whose capitalisation drifted from the dictionary, silently rendered in English. A gate now blocks a new untranslated string at commit time, with the existing backlog recorded as a baseline that can only shrink. Also fixed an options tooltip (the small text shown on hover) that had always rendered in English.
- **设置页里几个开关的描述一直是英文，现在都跟界面语言走了**：「显示」分组下有七行说明没翻译（重整历史滚动区、渲染 Mermaid 图表、流式渲染等），连同新加的「运行中回合显示多少」和它的档位选项。原因是这些文案来自设置清单，而桌面端读的是界面词表——之前只把中文加到了终端那份词表里，桌面端自然读不到。现在两份都齐了。
  - EN: A few settings descriptions stayed in English regardless of the interface language. Seven rows under "Display" had no translation (resize scrollback, render Mermaid diagrams, streaming rendering and others), along with the new running-turn setting and its level names. The text comes from the settings schema, and the desktop app reads the interface dictionary — the Chinese had only been added to the terminal's dictionary, so the desktop could not see it. Both dictionaries carry the entries now.
- **写入文件的过程中不再显示「无效的 content」，看着像工具出错了**：工具调用是先到参数、后到内容，写文件的正文分块传过来。这期间界面把「还没到」当成了「参数不对」，于是正在进行的写入被画成一条红色报错。现在流式过程中只显示一行「正在写入内容…」，真的写完了参数还是不合法才会报错。改动编辑类工具同理。
  - EN: A write in progress no longer shows "invalid content" as if the tool had failed. A tool call receives its arguments first and its content in chunks, so for most of a write the renderer saw a path and no content. That was reported as a malformed argument, which painted an in-progress write as a red error. Mid-stream it now says "Writing content…" and only a settled call with genuinely bad arguments reports a fault. The edit tools follow the same rule.
- **恢复被误删的「输出风格」和「流式输出动效」两个选择项**：上一轮合并预览时删多了一块，这两组选择器一起没了，只剩预览。现在都回来了，位置和原来一致。
  - EN: The output-style and streaming-effect pickers are back. Consolidating the previews last round deleted one block too many and took both pickers with it, leaving only the sample. They are restored in their original positions.
- **展开后的内容有时会被切掉一截，不再随回合结束而消失**：折叠/展开的动效会在元素上钉一个具体高度再让它自己过渡。如果这个过程被中途打断又重新开始（严格模式的二次执行、挂起边界揭开、长对话里行被虚拟化回收又放回），代码会误以为"状态没变"而跳过设置，把当时测到的高度当成终值——展开状态下那是一个像素值，元素从此不再随新内容长高，于是这一轮后面流出来的内容全被裁掉；折叠状态下则会留下一片该有内容的空白。现在这种情况下直接落到正确终态（展开为自适应高度、折叠为目标高度），动效本身不受影响。
  - EN: Expanded content is no longer cut off for the rest of the turn. The collapse/expand animation pins a concrete pixel height and lets the transition carry it to its target. When that run is torn down mid-flight and starts again (StrictMode's double invoke, a suspense boundary revealing, a row recycled by the transcript virtualizer), the code could conclude "the state did not change" and skip setup, treating the height it happened to measure as final — on an expanded body that is a pixel value, so the element stops growing and everything the turn streams afterwards is clipped; on a collapsed one it leaves blank space where content used to be. A replayed run now settles onto the correct target instead (auto when expanded, the closed height when not), and real transitions still animate.
- **插件之间不再"各记一份状态"，扩展改完还是立刻生效**：同一个插件以前会被装成好几份——会话一份、设置面板调用它自己接口时又一份，于是插件在自己面板上读不到它刚在会话里记下的东西（表现为"设置页一直加载中""渠道信息看不到"）。现在插件在进程里只有一份，面板和会话看到的是同一份状态；而热更新走单独的重载通路，改完文件仍然立即生效，不受影响。顺带把"随发行版一起装的插件"那条特殊装载路径去掉了——它本来就是同一类插件，不该走两套语义。
  - EN: A plugin no longer keeps one state per copy, and edits still take effect immediately. The same plugin used to be instantiated several times — once for the session and again when its own settings panel called its RPCs — so the panel could not see what the plugin had just recorded in the session (it showed up as "settings page stuck loading" or "channel info never appears"). There is now one copy per process, so the panel and the session share one state, while hot reload goes through its own reload path so edited files still take effect at once. The separate loading path for plugins that ship with the distribution is gone too — they are the same kind of plugin and should not follow two different semantics.
- **扩展状态浮窗不再被输入框上边缘切掉，选中文字的浮动工具条也回到光标正上方**：这两处是同一类问题的两个面——浮层被画在了会裁剪它的容器里。扩展状态列表原本从输入框向上展开，而输入框外框自己裁剪内容，扩展一多列表就被切掉大半；选中文字的工具条则因为所在容器声明了尺寸包含，视口坐标被误当成容器坐标，于是整条偏了位置。现在两者都和全局其他浮层走同一条通路（渲染在窗口顶层、按空间自动向上/向下、不再受任何容器裁剪或坐标系影响）。
  - EN: The extension-status popup is no longer cut off by the input box's top edge, and the selection toolbar lands above the cursor again. Both were the same class of defect in two forms: a floating layer drawn inside a container that clips it. The extension list opened upward from the composer while the composer's own frame clips its content, so a few running extensions were sliced off; the selection toolbar sat inside a container that declares size containment, so its viewport coordinates were resolved against that container and the whole bar landed offset. Both now use the shared floating-layer path (window-level, flipping on space, unaffected by ancestor clipping or coordinate systems).
- **退出应用不会再误杀别人启动的守护进程**：以前启动时只要端口被占就直接杀掉监听者——如果你同时开着安装版、终端里也跑着一个，新版一连接就可能把那个杀掉。现在只有「自己启动过、且确认原宿主已经退出」的守护进程才会被回收，别处启动的一律不动；真被占用时如实报端口冲突，而不是悄悄替换掉。
  - EN: Quitting no longer risks killing a daemon this app did not start. Launching used to SIGTERM whatever listened on the port, so with the installed app open and a terminal daemon also running, a second instance could silently take the other one down. Only daemons this app spawned whose original host has provably exited are reclaimed now; anything launched elsewhere is left alone, and a genuine port conflict is reported instead of being replaced.
- **关闭守护进程时终于会先好好收尾，而不是一刀切**：此前靠发送系统信号让它退出——在 Windows 上这个信号根本不可捕获，等于每次都硬杀，正在跑的那一轮对话随之丢失。现在会先通过守护进程自己的通道请求它「有序关闭」（释放会话、终端与扩展），等它收拾完再退出；收不掉才逐级升级为强制结束。同时，结束时会连同它派生出的子进程一起清理，不再出现「进程没了但端口还被占着」的状态——那种情况下界面会一直卡在启动画面，只能重启机器。
  - EN: Stopping the daemon now winds down cleanly instead of cutting it off. It used to rely on a system signal, which on Windows cannot be caught at all — so every teardown was a hard kill and the running turn was lost with it. The app now first asks the daemon to shut down in order over its own channel (releasing sessions, terminals and extensions), waits for it to finish, and only escalates through force if that fails. Teardown also reclaims the child processes it spawned, so the "process is gone but the port is still held" state — which left the window stuck on the splash until the machine was rebooted — no longer happens.
- **右侧栏「更多工具」按钮点开真的会弹出菜单了，提示也跟着界面语言走**：此前这个菜单画在工具栏内部一个 44px 宽的卡片里，而那张卡片自己是裁剪的——菜单被整块裁掉，点下去什么都看不到，折叠起来的工具和「显示已隐藏的表面」全都够不着。现在它和全局其他菜单走同一条浮层通路（渲染在窗口顶层、贴着工具栏向左展开、过高时内部滚动），不再被任何容器裁到；按钮与展开/收起面板的提示文字也从写死的英文换成了词表，中文界面下显示「更多工具」「收起右侧面板」。
  - EN: The right rail's "more tools" button now actually opens its menu, and its hints follow the interface language. The menu used to be drawn inside the rail — a 44px card that clips its own overflow — so the menu was clipped away entirely and clicking did nothing, leaving the folded tools and "show hidden surfaces" unreachable. It now goes through the same floating-layer path as every other menu (rendered at window level, flying out to the left of the rail, scrolling internally when tall) and cannot be clipped by any container; the button and the panel collapse/expand hints also moved off hard-coded English into the dictionaries, so a Chinese interface reads 更多工具 / 收起右侧面板.
- **Windows 上的悬浮菜单终于处处磨砂一致**：此前工作区把系统亚克力材质垫在不透明窗口后面，而 Chromium 采不到那一层——同一张菜单盖在聊天卡片上是磨砂的，盖在侧栏和顶栏上就直接透出后面的字。现在改成 openchamber 的做法：窗口不垫材质，由页面自己画不透明底，每个浮层下面都是页内内容，磨砂处处一致。代价是桌面不再透过工作区显示，磨砂玻璃透明度滑块改为控制底色深浅。
  - EN: Floating menus frost uniformly on Windows now. The workspace used to sit on a system acrylic material behind an opaque window, and Chromium cannot sample that layer — so the same menu was frosted over the chat card but showed the text behind it over the sidebar and header. The page now paints its own opaque backdrop the way openchamber does, so every floating surface has in-window content to sample and the frost is uniform. The trade: the desktop no longer shows through the workspace, and the frosted-glass transparency slider now controls tint depth.

- **顾问卡不再把会话地图撕成假分支**：恢复历史会话时，顾问卡等非消息节点被跳过重挂父链，顾问后的回复被挂到了顾问前的消息之下——地图把顾问与下一条用户消息算成同一父轮的两个分支（"3-1/3-2"两条泳道）。现在父链原样保留，线性历史就是一条线，消息树挂载时自己走链经过非消息节点。
  - EN: Advisor cards no longer tear the session map into phantom branches. When recovering a history session, non-message nodes were skipped while relinking parents, so the reply after an advisor card was re-hung under the pre-advisor message — and the map counted the advisor plus the next user message as two branches of one parent turn ("3-1/3-2" in two lanes). Parent chains are now preserved verbatim, so linear history renders as one lane, and the message tree walks up through non-message nodes itself.
- **发送失败不再留下永远卡住的气泡**：daemon 从未收到这条消息时，不会再有任何事件来认领乐观回显——消息在发送失败的当场即被撤下，不留幽灵气泡（用户已发出下一条时不误伤）。
  - EN: Failed sends no longer leave a permanently stuck bubble. When the daemon never received the message, no event will ever arrive to claim the optimistic echo — so it is withdrawn at the failure site instead of haunting the transcript (a newer message the user already sent is never harmed).
- **菜单磨砂加了不透明回退面**：浮动菜单与会话悬浮卡的玻璃面多了一层实色底——渲染变量解析失败时退化成实心面而不是全透明文字直接叠在内容上；正常渲染像素不变。
  - EN: Floating menus gained an opaque fallback surface. Context menus and session hover cards now sit on a solid base under the glass wash — if the render variables ever fail to resolve they degrade to a solid surface instead of fully transparent text over content; healthy rendering is pixel-identical.
- **切换会话后聊天记录不再停在旧进度**：历史会话恢复时，如果运行日记缺失了会话文件里已经有的记录（异步写入丢失、跨进程重写、崩溃），恢复现在回退到完整记录而不是展示残片；残片此前还会让后续到达的实时消息被当成重复丢弃，且没有任何自愈路径。
  - EN: Switching sessions no longer leaves the transcript stuck on stale progress. When recovering a history session whose journal missed records the transcript file already holds (async-write loss, cross-instance rewrite, crash), recovery now falls back to the complete transcript instead of serving the fragment; the fragment used to also make later live messages look like duplicates to be dropped, with no self-healing path.
- **一次失败的会话日记写入不再冻结之后的所有记录**：此前一次写入失败会让整条写链永久停摆（之后的所有记录静默丢失，还伴随未处理的拒绝），现在单条失败只记一条错误日志，链条保持可用。
  - EN: One failed session-journal write no longer freezes every later record. A single write failure used to wedge the whole append chain (all later records silently lost, plus unhandled rejections); now a failed write logs one error line and the chain stays alive.
- **闲时产生的提醒卡片会实时显示**：此前非打断型提醒（如顾问的一般性建议）在空闲时只写文件不广播，横幅要等下次重载才出现；现在它们到达即广播（仍只落盘一次，不会重复）。
  - EN: Notice cards produced while idle now appear live. Non-interrupting notes (such as an advisor's plain suggestions) used to be persisted without any broadcast when the agent was idle, so no banner showed until the next reload; they are now broadcast on arrival while still persisted exactly once.
- **吃豆人滚动条不再是一条粗灰杠，吃豆人也画对了**：已吃段此前是和豆列等宽的纯色块，进度一多就盖住整条轨道变成粗灰杠，现在收成 3px 的居中细轨；轨道线也居中到豆列正下方，不再和豆子错成两条；吃豆人此前是黄盘上贴深色嘴，12px 下糊成一团，现在是真正的镂空嘴形（透明嘴 + 顶眼，动画三帧不再和静帧叠画）。
  - EN: The pacman scrollbar is no longer a thick grey bar, and the glyph is a real pac-man. The eaten run used to be a full-width flat tint that covered the whole rail at high progress; it is now a 3px centred trail, the thread is centred under the bead column, and the glyph is a true cutout mouth (transparent opening, centred eye) with the chomp frames no longer double-painted over the rest body.
- **插件详情弹窗补上整体启停开关**：内置插件单元此前点开详情只有状态、组件与配置，没有整体开关——要启停得回列表找行，而用户插件包那边一向有。现在内置与用户插件同一套实现：详情头部一个总闸（与列表行、插件包开关同一个 RPC 语义），只读展示项照旧不渲染死开关。
  - EN: The plugin detail dialog gains a whole-plugin enable switch. Built-in plugin units used to open into status, components and config only — enabling or disabling one meant going back to the list to find its row, while installed packages had always had a switch in their detail view. Built-in and user plugins now share one implementation: one master switch in the dialog header, with the same RPC semantics as the list row and the package toggle, and read-only display items still render no dead switch.
- **组件列表不再随插件启停消失**：插件停用后，详情里的「包含的组件」段曾整个不见了——用户无从知道停用期间关掉了什么，重新启用后那段也空着。现在组件的声明面与插件是否在役无关：停用只卸载运行体，组件行照旧列出，各自读真实的未挂载状态。
  - EN: The component list no longer vanishes with the plugin's toggle. Disabling a plugin used to make the whole "included components" section disappear from its detail view — there was no way to see what was switched off, and after re-enabling the section stayed empty. A component's declared surface is now independent of whether its plugin is in service: a disable unloads the runtime only, the component rows stay listed, and each reads its real not-mounted state.
- **扩展文件变动不再让守护进程的会话重载崩溃**：监视器防抖回调假设宿主一定能枚举会话；宿主面不全（或正在拆卸）时这次重掷异常直接中断整批后续刷新。现在缺该能力时跳过重载——没有会话可重载，本来就不该抛。
  - EN: An extension file change no longer crashes the daemon's session reload. The watcher's debounced callback assumed the host could always enumerate sessions; when that surface was absent (or mid-teardown) the throw aborted the whole follow-up refresh. Reload is now skipped when the capability is missing — no sessions to reload means nothing to throw about.

### Removed
- **模型胶囊旁的积分余额读数移除了**：这个读数只在一部分登录渠道上有值，其余渠道永远空着，占位却让胶囊行显得拥挤。与其留着半份功能，不如先撤下。
  - EN: The credit balance readout beside the model capsule is removed. It had a value on only some sign-in channels, stayed blank on the rest, and the reserved space crowded the capsule row. Rather than leaving a half-working feature, it is withdrawn for now.

## [0.5.6] - 2026-10-13

### Fixed

- **插件包开关现在同时启停它的全部组件**：一个插件包此前有两套互不知情的开关——包卡片写的是「包已停用」，它挂进设置页的面板与组件行写的却是另一处存储，两边可以各说各话：包显示已停用而面板还在役，点它的设置 tab 直接报加载错误，重新启用也只恢复一半。现在包开关是唯一权威，停用即卸载整个插件运行体，它注册的槽位组件、命令、供应商、RPC 全部随生命周期回收；启用则重新挂载、全部恢复；重启后两处存储保持一致，不再复活一半。
  - EN: A plugin package toggle now enables or disables everything it registered together. A package previously had two switches that wrote two stores and never reconciled: the package card recorded "package off" while its settings-page panel and component rows wrote elsewhere, so the two could disagree — the package read as disabled while its panel stayed in service, opening its settings tab raised a load error, and re-enabling restored only half. The package toggle is now the single authority: a disable unloads the plugin's whole runtime so every registration (slot components, commands, providers, RPC) is reclaimed with its lifetime, an enable re-mounts it and everything returns, and both stores agree across a restart instead of reviving half.
- **扩展中心的插件卡片也能直接启停**：插件模块卡片此前只显示图标、名称与描述，整行只做「打开详情」，没有启停开关——能力清单 tab 的同类行都带开关，这个面成了管不了的裂缝。现在与官方组同一口径：非只读项在卡片头部渲染开关，停用后整卡状态同步（只读展示项仍不渲染死开关）。
  - EN: Plugin module cards in the extension center can now be toggled directly. They used to render only the icon, name and description — clicking opened details, but there was no enable switch, while the equivalent rows in the capability list had one, so this surface silently could not manage anything. Cards now carry the same switch as the official group: non-readonly items get one in the card header and the whole card follows the state (readonly display items still render no dead switch).
- **首次配置引导的供应商名字贴着图标左对齐**：引导页供应商行用了两端对齐布局，而中间的供应商名没有占据剩余空间——文字被两侧间隙平分，读起来是居中的。现在名字独占行内剩余宽度，与品牌图标紧贴左对齐。
  - EN: Provider names in the first-run onboarding list are flush-left with their icon. The onboarding row used a space-between layout while the name in the middle took no remaining space, so the free width split into the gaps on both of its sides and the text read centered. The name now owns the row's remaining width and sits against its brand icon.
- **启动等待期间也能拖动窗口**：冷启动时守护进程要预热数秒，界面停在全屏的启动动画上——此前这块区域不参与窗口拖拽，想把它挪到一边只能等加载完。现在启动画面的空白处可直接拖动窗口，重试按钮照常点击不受影响。
  - EN: The window can be dragged while startup is in flight. On a cold start the daemon prewarms for a few seconds and the interface sits on the full-screen splash — that area previously did not participate in window dragging, so moving it aside meant waiting for the load to finish. The splash now drags the window directly, and the retry button still clicks normally.

## [0.5.5] - 2026-10-13

### Added

- **退出 MusePi 前会提醒还在运行的会话**：此前点退出会直接关闭——正在运行的智能体任务被静默中断，用户往往事后才发现。现在有会话在运行时退出会先弹确认（提示有几个会话正在运行、退出会中断它们），选择留在应用则什么都不会发生；没有会话在跑时退出依旧无声。
  - EN: Quitting MusePi now warns about sessions that are still running. Quit used to close immediately — running agent tasks were silently interrupted and users only found out afterwards. With sessions active, quitting first asks for confirmation (how many are running, and that quitting interrupts them); choosing to stay aborts the quit entirely. With nothing running, quitting stays silent.
- **插件可以贡献自己的魔术关键词**：新增 `pi.registerMagicKeyword`，扩展能注册一个输入框里的触发词（提交时向模型追加一条隐藏提示），与内置三个关键词同规则：小写散文边界匹配（不会在 `deepdive.ts`、行内代码或 `deepdiving` 这类更长的词里误触发），开关走 `magicKeywords.<词>` 设置键，没人配置时默认停用。仓库内附完整可运行样板 `examples/plugin-magic-keyword`（关键词 + 自带设置面板 + 后台方法一套）。
  - EN: Plugins can contribute their own magic keyword. `pi.registerMagicKeyword` lets an extension register a composer trigger word that appends a hidden notice to the model on submit, under the same contract as the three built-in keywords: lowercase prose-delimited matching (so `deepdive.ts`, inline code, and longer words like `deepdiving` never trigger it), gated by a `magicKeywords.<word>` setting that defaults to off when nobody configured it. A complete runnable sample ships in the repo at `examples/plugin-magic-keyword` (keyword plus its own settings panel and daemon method).
- **插件设置表单有了防覆盖与「恢复默认」能力**：插件自带设置界面过去只有一个裸的读写接口，写多了会互相覆盖、也分不清某个值是用户改的还是继承清单默认值。现在提供带版本栅栏的设置表单——基于旧值发起的写入会被拒绝（界面能明确知道「这次编辑输了」而不是静默覆盖别人的改动），并区分清单默认值与用户层：键的存在即代表用户改过，因此可以逐项恢复默认。
  - EN: Plugin settings forms gained lost-update protection and a per-field reset. A plugin's own settings UI previously had only a bare read/write proxy, so concurrent edits clobbered each other and there was no way to tell a user-set value from an inherited manifest default. Forms now carry a revision fence — a write issued from a stale read is refused, so an editor can say "this edit lost" instead of silently overwriting a newer one — and separate the manifest base from the user layer: a key's presence marks a user override, so any field can be reset to inherit.
- **插件组合里的条件行现在真的按条件生效**：补丁里 `!!js` 标记的表达式（例如只在某个系统启用某一行）过去一律不求值、永久显示为「未知」，组合模型对这类行等于失效。现在按参考实现的做法求值（`with (ctx)`），条件行会算出真实的启停；表达式算不出来时该行如实标为「未知」并给出原因，不会因为求值出错就当成启用。profile 的覆盖依旧优先于 bundle 的条件。
  - EN: Conditional rows in a plugin composition now actually take effect. Expressions tagged `!!js` in a patch (enabling a row only on a given platform, say) were previously never evaluated and stayed permanently "unknown", which made the composition model inert for exactly those rows. They are now evaluated the way the reference loader does (`with (ctx)`), so a conditional row resolves to a real enablement; when an expression cannot be computed the row is reported as unknown with the reason, and is never treated as enabled just because evaluation went wrong. A profile's override still outranks a bundle's condition.
- **插件组合升级后即可发现与使用**：升级到带组合模型的版本时，若当前 profile 还没有组合清单，会在它旁边写一份可直接编辑的骨架（bundle 列表 + profile 自有 patch 的注释示例），只补缺失文件、绝不覆盖你已有的声明或 patch；组合视图因此不再是「空页面 + 不知道要填什么字段」。此外组合出的扩展入口会随行的启停重新装载，勾上即生效。
  - EN: Plugin compositions are discoverable and usable right after upgrading. When an upgraded installation's profile declares no composition, a directly editable skeleton is written beside it (the bundle list plus a commented example of the profile's own patch), creating only missing files and never overwriting a declaration or patch you already have — so the composition view is no longer an empty page asking for a field nobody would know to write. Composed extension entries are also re-loaded when a row is toggled, so enabling a row takes effect immediately.
- **插件可以整包分发、逐条启停（DSH bundle/profile 组合模型）**：新增 profile/bundle 组合面——profile 清单声明自己叠了哪些 bundle，bundle 的 `cordis.patch.yml` 插入若干行，profile 自己的 patch 可按行 id 覆盖某一行（配置整体替换，因此也能删掉 bundle 设的键）。行的启停写进 profile 自己的 patch 文件，与 DSH 同款写法；重新组合后回报组合的真实状态，而不是回报请求。「整包安装却什么都没贡献」的失败模式现在可见：缺失的插件、条件启停（`!!js` 表达式本宿主不求值，明确标为未知而不是猜一个启停）、缺失的 bundle 都如实报出。
  - EN: Plugins can now ship as bundles and be toggled row by row (DSH's profile/bundle composition model). A profile manifest declares the bundles it stacks, a bundle's cordis.patch.yml inserts rows, and the profile's own patch can override one row by id (the config is replaced wholesale, so a profile can also remove a key the bundle set). Toggling a row writes into the profile's own patch file, the same write DSH performs, and the answer reported is the recomposed state rather than the request. The failure mode of "installed a bundle and it contributes nothing" is now visible: a missing plugin, a conditional enablement (a !!js expression this host does not evaluate, reported as unknown rather than guessed either way) and a bundle that is not installed are all reported as they are.

- **插件面板不再加载失败，官方插件也都有图标了**：插件自带的设置面板原先一律报「模块加载失败」——界面在组件还在下载时就回收了它的地址；现在加载完成后才释放。同时每个插件项都画出了自己的标识（插件声明的图标、内置单元的类别图标，或按包名生成的彩色字母标），状态改为标识角上的小圆点，不再和图标挤在一起。
  - EN: Plugin panels no longer fail to load, and official plugins have icons too.
