A plugin's own settings panel always reported "module failed to load" — the UI released its address while the download was still in flight; it now waits for the load to finish. Every plugin also draws its own mark (a declared icon, a built-in unit's kind glyph, or a colored letter tile derived from the package name), and status moved to a corner badge instead of crowding the icon.
- **插件详情弹窗补齐**：点击已安装插件打开详情弹窗——大图标、版本、状态与启用开关、头部卸载按钮；插件自带的组件收进弹窗「包含的组件」逐项开关（不再与插件包重复占列表行），插件的设置界面（如账号池管理）也挂载在弹窗内。
  - EN: The plugin detail dialog completes the picture. Clicking an installed plugin opens a dialog with the large icon, version, status, enable toggle and an uninstall button in the header; the plugin's own modules collapse into per-item included-component toggles (no longer duplicated as separate list rows), and its settings surface (an account pool, for example) mounts inside the dialog.
- **插件列表支持一键卸载**：已安装插件卡片和列表行新增卸载按钮，确认后由守护进程移除插件目录并即时刷新列表；卸载失败时错误横幅提示且列表保持不变。
  - EN: One-click uninstall in the plugin list. Installed plugin cards and rows gain an uninstall button; after a confirmation the daemon removes the plugin directory and refreshes the list immediately, and a failed uninstall shows an error banner while leaving the list untouched.
- **插件图标可自定义**：插件可在清单里声明 `icon`（emoji、字符或图片 URL），列表立即采用；未声明时按包名生成确定的彩色字母标，同包同名同色。
  - EN: Customizable plugin icons. A plugin can declare `icon` in its manifest (emoji, glyph, or an image URL) and the list adopts it; without one, a deterministic colored letter mark is derived from the package name — same package, same mark.

### Fixed

- **启动等待期间也能拖动窗口**：冷启动时守护进程要预热数秒，界面停在全屏的启动动画上——此前这块区域不参与窗口拖拽，想把它挪到一边只能等加载完。现在启动画面的空白处可直接拖动窗口，重试按钮照常点击不受影响。
  - EN: The window can be dragged while startup is in flight. On a cold start the daemon prewarms for a few seconds and the interface sits on the full-screen splash — that area previously did not participate in window dragging, so moving it aside meant waiting for the load to finish. The splash now drags the window directly, and the retry button still clicks normally.
- **会话启动恢复到正常速度**：随产品分发的内置插件包（魔术关键词三件套）此前走了为第三方插件设计的源码图装载管线——那条管线会遍历插件整个 import 图逐文件读盘分析，而内置包引用宿主自身模块，图里有几千个文件，导致每次会话装配多花约两分钟。内置包与宿主同仓库同版本、整体随版本更新，不需要逐文件快照，现在直接走原生模块加载（毫秒级）。
  - EN: Session startup is back to normal speed. The bundled plugin package that ships with the product (the magic-keyword trio) went through the source-graph loading pipeline designed for third-party plugins — a file-by-file walk of the package's entire import graph, which for a package importing host modules means thousands of files read and parsed per load, adding roughly two minutes to every session assembly. Bundled packages share the host's repo and version and are replaced as a whole on upgrade, so they never needed the per-file snapshot; they now load through a plain native module import in milliseconds.
- **插件贡献的模型在扩展重载后即时出现在模型选择器**：扩展注册的供应商模型此前只在会话创建时进模型目录——扩展热重载后目录已更新，但会话内的模型选择器只认 models.changed 事件，永远停在旧列表，插件新增的模型要重进会话才可见。现在扩展重载广播同时扇出 models.changed，选择器同帧刷新。
  - EN: Models contributed by a plugin now appear in the model selector right after an extension reload. Extension-registered provider models only entered the catalog at session creation — after a hot reload the catalog updated, but the in-session selector listens only for models.changed and kept the stale list, so a plugin's new models required re-entering the session. The extension-reload broadcast now fans out models.changed too, and the selector refreshes in the same frame.
- **第三方插件面板真正挂载得出来（渲染端 CSP 修复）**：插件自带的面板此前一律报「组件加载失败 — TypeError: Failed to fetch dynamically imported module: blob:…」——页面安全策略只允许脚本来自本页与内联，而扩展组件恰恰是以 blob 模块动态导入的；第一个用 `settings.extensions` 槽位的第三方插件（如 musepi-auth-hub 的账号池面板）首当其冲。现在 `script-src` 放行 blob:（仍然拒绝 eval），并用契约测试钉住这条安全边界。
  - EN: Third-party plugin panels now actually mount (renderer CSP fix). A plugin's own panel always failed with "component failed to load — TypeError: Failed to fetch dynamically imported module: blob:…" — the page's security policy only allowed page and inline scripts, while extension components are precisely blob-imported modules; the first third-party plugin using the settings.extensions slot (musepi-auth-hub's account pool, for example) hit it head-on. script-src now admits blob: (eval stays refused), with a contract test pinning that boundary.
- **插件启用开关反映真实装载结果**：拨动开关此前只代表"已写入启用意图"——守护进程写完锁文件就返回成功，从不回读插件是否真的装载；装载失败的插件在界面上照样显示"已启用"，什么都没跑起来。现在启用后守护进程重新装载该插件的扩展条目并回报真实结果：装载失败时开关不再翻转、错误横幅给出原因。
  - EN: The plugin enable toggle now reflects the real load outcome. Toggling previously only meant "the enable intent was written" — the daemon returned success right after persisting the lockfile, never re-reading whether the plugin actually loads; a plugin whose load fails showed "enabled" in the UI while nothing registered. The daemon now re-loads the plugin's extension entries after enabling and reports the real outcome: on load failure the toggle does not flip and an error banner gives the reason.
- **第三方插件包与内置插件单元在列表里形态一致**：插件包（如 musepi-auth-hub）此前渲染成只有包名、没有归属组件也进不去设置的裸行。现在插件包与内置单元共用同一套条目布局（图标 + 名称 + 来源/版本标签 + 描述 + 右侧开关，整行可点开详情），且包与其扩展模块的归属判定改用路径相对计算——不再依赖两侧拼写出同一个分隔符，也不会把只共享前缀的同级包误判成子模块。
  - EN: Third-party plugin packages and built-in plugin units now share one row layout. A package (musepi-auth-hub, for example) previously rendered as a bare row carrying only its name, with no attributed components and no way into its settings. Packages and built-in units now use the same row (icon + name + source/version tag + description + trailing toggle, the whole row opening details), and package-to-module attribution compares paths relatively instead of assuming both sides spell the separator the same way — a sibling that merely shares a prefix is no longer mistaken for a child.

- **供应商列表折叠展开不再卡死**：快速来回点「显示全部 / 折叠」时，上一次的展开动画还没结束新的就开始，网格会冻在半展开的高度（卡片被裁掉一截）。现在每次展开/收起都先清掉上一次动画残留的锁定，被打断的动画立即恢复正常布局。
  - EN: The provider list no longer freezes mid-collapse. Clicking 显示全部/折叠 in quick succession started a new expansion before the old one settled, leaving the grid stuck at a half-open height with cards clipped. Every toggle now clears the previous animation's leftover lock first, and an interrupted animation restores natural layout immediately.
- **专注模式切换不再先跳后长**：会话里切专注模式时，输入框原先先整块跳到更高的底边再开始长高——顺序是反的；同时高度动画被两套过渡同时驱动，帧数明显不够。现在动画从你切换前的真实位置出发，上下两条边一起沿曲线走到目标位置（正是"按上下边距比例放大"的观感），且整个窗口期只由一条曲线驱动；输入框聚焦时光束会在形变期间定格，不再叠加逐帧重绘。
  - EN: Focus-mode toggle no longer jumps before it grows. The composer used to leap to its final position first and only then grow — the order was backwards — while two competing height transitions fought per frame and dropped the frame rate. The animation now starts from the composer's true pre-toggle position and carries both edges along one curve to the target (the proportional "grows along the margins" read), a single curve drives the whole window, and a focused beam freezes for the morph instead of repainting per frame on top of it.
- **空态输入框飞向会话态不再卡顿**：输入框边缘的彩色旋转光束会在欢迎页与会话页之间的位移动画里逐帧重绘，叠在位移本身的缩放+模糊动画上就是卡顿感的来源。现在位移进行时光束定格、落地后恢复，空态发送消息的那一刻光束也立即熄灭，不再陪跑到场景淡出。
  - EN: The empty-state composer no longer stutters as it flies into the session layout. The composer's rotating color beam repaints every frame, and stacked on the flight's own scale+blur animation that repaint was the stutter. The beam now freezes for the flight and resumes on landing, and sending from the empty state turns the beam off at the send itself instead of letting it run until the scene fades out.
- **登录弹窗取消后按钮不再卡死五分钟**：关闭登录弹窗（点遮罩或 Esc）只取消了界面，登录中的标记还挂在供应商上——期间该供应商的登录按钮一直是禁用态，要等 5 分钟 RPC 超时才恢复（「点了添加凭证后再点不弹」）。现在取消时同步清除标记，并通知 daemon 终止登录流程。
  - EN: Cancelling the login dialog no longer bricks the buttons for five minutes. Closing the dialog (backdrop or Esc) only dismissed the UI — the in-flight marker stayed on the provider, keeping its login buttons disabled until the 5-minute RPC timeout. Cancel now clears the marker immediately and asks the daemon to abort the flow.
- **首次配置引导的供应商列表合成一列**：订阅登录、已配置与 API key 导入此前是三段分组——「API 密钥导入」小标题把同一件事割成两个区域，行为还不一致。现在是一个列表：每行一个供应商，行尾动作只有一种（已配置 → 凭证菜单，OAuth 供应商 → 登录，纯 API 供应商 → 导入），排序、搜索、图标一致。
  - EN: The onboarding provider list is one list now. Subscription logins, configured providers, and API-key imports were three separated groups — an "API key import" subheading split one concern into two areas with inconsistent behavior. One list remains: one row per provider with a single trailing action (configured → credential menu, OAuth provider → sign in, key-only provider → import), with shared ordering, search, and icons.
- **供应商标识图标铺到设置与引导各处**：供应商卡片、角色模型侧栏、引导供应商行此前都是纯文字，逐一读名才能分辨。现在与模型选择器同一套 Lobe 品牌图标，未知供应商回落到通用 AI 图标。
  - EN: Provider brand icons now appear across settings and onboarding. The provider cards, the role-model sidebar, and the onboarding provider rows were text-only, forcing name-by-name reading. They now share the same Lobe brand icons as the model picker, with a generic AI fallback for unknown providers.
- **角色卡回退链可以编辑和拖拽排序了**：此前每条回退只能删除重加，顺序错了只能重建整条链。现在点击条目原地换模型，拖动条目直接重排，把手悬停浮现。
  - EN: Role-card fallback chains can be edited and drag-reordered. Entries were delete-and-retype only — a wrong order meant rebuilding the whole chain. Clicking an entry swaps the model in place, dragging reorders directly, and the grip appears on hover.
- **角色卡工具行按钮高度统一**：思考档位下拉与旁边的按钮此前高低不齐，同一行读起来像两种组件。现在该行所有控件统一 26px 高度。
  - EN: The role-card toolbar buttons share one height. The thinking-level select rendered visibly shorter than the buttons beside it, reading as two different components in one row; all controls in the row are now 26px.
- **自定义供应商表单两处对齐**：引导表单此前缺「添加模型」手动行（模型列表看着像只读），两处的 Base URL 占位提示也不一致。现在两处共用同一套字段与协议感知的占位提示，表单滚动条换成应用样式（原生绿色滚动条与暗色玻璃面冲突）。
  - EN: The custom-provider forms now match between onboarding and settings. The onboarding form lacked the manual "add model" row (its model list read as read-only), and the two forms showed different base-URL placeholder hints. Both now share the same fields and protocol-aware hints, and the form scrollbars use the app styling (the native green scrollbar clashed with the dark glass).
- **删除定时任务时选「同时删除会话」现在真的删得掉**：此前删除后任务中心里的会话仍在——关闭会话时日志文件句柄的释放是不等待的，Windows 上紧接着的删除会因句柄未释放而失败，留下的文件让下一次扫描把会话复活。现在删除前先等句柄释放完毕，删除动作对暂时性的占用失败自动重试，不再静默留下半删的文件。
  - EN: Choosing "delete its sessions" when deleting a scheduled task now actually deletes them. The journal handle released on session teardown was never awaited, so the immediately following delete failed on Windows with the handle still open, and the leftover file resurrected the session on the next scan. The delete now waits for the release first and retries transient lock failures, so no half-deleted file is left behind silently.
- **供应商卡片不再把同一个账号显示成两张**：同一供应商的两种登录方式（如 ChatGPT 的浏览器回调与设备码登录）凭证存在同一处，此前按内部 id 分列成两张卡，看着像登录了两次，且每张卡的「添加另一个凭证」各自只能走到其中一种流程。现在折成一张卡，另一种登录方式收进凭证菜单，凭证、退出登录共用一份。
  - EN: A provider account no longer renders as two cards. A provider's two sign-in flows (ChatGPT's browser callback and device-code login, say) share one credential store, but the cards were split by internal id — reading as logged in twice, with each card's "add another credential" reaching only one of the two flows. They now fold into one card, the other flow living in the credential menu, sharing credentials and logout.
- **「添加另一个凭证」在订阅类供应商上不再跳去 API key 输入框**：有订阅登录流程的供应商（同时恰好自带模型目录）此前被误判成纯 API key 供应商，点下去打开的是粘 key 的输入框。现在按卡片实际持有的凭证种类走：订阅账号开新的登录流程，纯 API key 供应商才开 key 输入框。
  - EN: "Add another credential" no longer jumps to the API-key box on subscription providers. A provider with a subscription login flow — which also happened to ship a bundled model catalog — was misread as API-key-only, so the button opened the key input. It now follows the kind of credential the card actually holds: a subscription account starts another login, and only a pure API-key provider opens the key input.
- **登录与导入 API key 改为居中弹窗**：这两个流程此前渲染在设置页内部——先是在页面顶部，离触发它的供应商卡片隔着一整屏；后来嵌进卡片里，三张卡片并列时又宽到放不下，链接和按钮被截掉。现在以弹窗打开：宽度充裕、永远居中、Esc 可关，从哪张卡片发起都一样。
  - EN: Login and API-key import now open in a centered dialog. Both flows used to render inside the settings page — first at the top, a full screen away from the provider card that triggered them, then embedded in the card, where two neighbouring cards left too little width and clipped the link and buttons. They now open as a dialog: roomy, always centered, closable with Esc, identical from wherever in the grid they start.
- **定时任务存储跟随数据根目录**：任务列表此前固定写在用户主目录下，数据根被重定位（开发隔离、自定义数据目录）后守护进程读的是另一个位置——表现为删掉的任务一重启又回来。现在存储位置随激活的数据根解析，开发环境不再读写真实任务列表，测试也不再向生产目录落任务。
  - EN: The scheduled-task store follows the active data root. The task list was hardcoded under the home directory, so a relocated data root (dev isolation, a custom data directory) made the daemon read a different location — deleted tasks reappearing after restart. The store now resolves against the active root: the dev environment no longer touches the real task list, and tests no longer seed tasks into production storage.

### Added

- **插件面板不再加载失败，官方插件也都有图标了**：插件自带的设置面板原先一律报「模块加载失败」——界面在组件还在下载时就回收了它的地址；现在加载完成后才释放。同时每个插件项都画出了自己的标识（插件声明的图标、内置单元的类别图标，或按包名生成的彩色字母标），状态改为标识角上的小圆点，不再和图标挤在一起。
  - EN: Plugin panels no longer fail to load, and official plugins have icons too. A plugin's own settings panel always reported "module failed to load" — the UI released its address while the download was still in flight; it now waits for the load to finish. Every plugin also draws its own mark (a declared icon, a built-in unit's kind glyph, or a colored letter tile derived from the package name), and status moved to a corner badge instead of crowding the icon.
- **插件详情弹窗补齐**：点击已安装插件打开详情弹窗——大图标、版本、状态与启用开关、头部卸载按钮；插件自带的组件收进弹窗「包含的组件」逐项开关（不再与插件包重复占列表行），插件的设置界面（如账号池管理）也挂载在弹窗内。
  - EN: The plugin detail dialog completes the picture. Clicking an installed plugin opens a dialog with the large icon, version, status, enable toggle and an uninstall button in the header; the plugin's own modules collapse into per-item included-component toggles (no longer duplicated as separate list rows), and its settings surface (an account pool, for example) mounts inside the dialog.
- **插件列表支持一键卸载**：已安装插件卡片和列表行新增卸载按钮，确认后由守护进程移除插件目录并即时刷新列表；卸载失败时错误横幅提示且列表保持不变。
  - EN: One-click uninstall in the plugin list. Installed plugin cards and rows gain an uninstall button; after a confirmation the daemon removes the plugin directory and refreshes the list immediately, and a failed uninstall shows an error banner while leaving the list untouched.
- **插件图标可自定义**：插件可在清单里声明 `icon`（emoji、字符或图片 URL），列表立即采用；未声明时按包名生成确定的彩色字母标，同包同名同色。
  - EN: Customizable plugin icons. A plugin can declare `icon` in its manifest (emoji, glyph, or an image URL) and the list adopts it; without one, a deterministic colored letter mark is derived from the package name — same package, same mark.
- **插件页现在可以直接填包名、git 地址、压缩包或本机路径来安装插件**：以前只有市场能装已收录的包，自己做的、还没发布的插件没法进。新的入口在插件页工具栏「安装插件」——填入内容后立即开始，边装边显示输出，可以中途取消，失败后原样重试；**也可以把压缩包或插件文件夹直接拖进去**，自制的插件不用先发布给别人。取消或失败都会把插件目录还原回安装前的样子，不会留下装了一半的依赖。装完会告诉你这个插件能不能在当前环境跑：依赖对得上就标为可用，缺东西或者有界面找不到落点时，把缺的是什么一并列出来，不用等到加载失败才发现。
  - EN: The plugins page can now install from a package name, git address, archive, or local path. Only the marketplace could install before, so a plugin you built yourself — or one that was never published — had no way in. The new entry sits in the plugins toolbar: fill in the spec and it starts, shows output as it runs, can be cancelled mid-flight, and retries unchanged after a failure; **or drop an archive or plugin folder straight onto it**, so a hand-built plugin needs publishing to nobody first. A cancellation or a failure restores the plugin directory to its pre-install state, so no half-installed dependency is left behind. Once it finishes you are told whether the plugin can actually run here: matching dependencies mark it usable, and a missing runtime or a component with no mount point on this host is listed by name, instead of surfacing later as a load failure.

## [0.5.4] - 2026-10-06

### Added

- **插件面板不再加载失败，官方插件也都有图标了**：插件自带的设置面板原先一律报「模块加载失败」——界面在组件还在下载时就回收了它的地址；现在加载完成后才释放。同时每个插件项都画出了自己的标识（插件声明的图标、内置单元的类别图标，或按包名生成的彩色字母标），状态改为标识角上的小圆点，不再和图标挤在一起。
  - EN: Plugin panels no longer fail to load, and official plugins have icons too. A plugin's own settings panel always reported "module failed to load" — the UI released its address while the download was still in flight; it now waits for the load to finish. Every plugin also draws its own mark (a declared icon, a built-in unit's kind glyph, or a colored letter tile derived from the package name), and status moved to a corner badge instead of crowding the icon.
- **插件详情弹窗补齐**：点击已安装插件打开详情弹窗——大图标、版本、状态与启用开关、头部卸载按钮；插件自带的组件收进弹窗「包含的组件」逐项开关（不再与插件包重复占列表行），插件的设置界面（如账号池管理）也挂载在弹窗内。
  - EN: The plugin detail dialog completes the picture. Clicking an installed plugin opens a dialog with the large icon, version, status, enable toggle and an uninstall button in the header; the plugin's own modules collapse into per-item included-component toggles (no longer duplicated as separate list rows), and its settings surface (an account pool, for example) mounts inside the dialog.
- **插件列表支持一键卸载**：已安装插件卡片和列表行新增卸载按钮，确认后由守护进程移除插件目录并即时刷新列表；卸载失败时错误横幅提示且列表保持不变。
  - EN: One-click uninstall in the plugin list. Installed plugin cards and rows gain an uninstall button; after a confirmation the daemon removes the plugin directory and refreshes the list immediately, and a failed uninstall shows an error banner while leaving the list untouched.
- **插件图标可自定义**：插件可在清单里声明 `icon`（emoji、字符或图片 URL），列表立即采用；未声明时按包名生成确定的彩色字母标，同包同名同色。
  - EN: Customizable plugin icons. A plugin can declare `icon` in its manifest (emoji, glyph, or an image URL) and the list adopts it; without one, a deterministic colored letter mark is derived from the package name — same package, same mark.
- **外观新增「始终显示滚动条」开关**：打开后已经出现的滚动条不再自动淡出，会一直亮着；横向也能拖动了，此前只有纵向有把手。
  - EN: Appearance gains an "Always show scrollbars" switch — a scrollbar that has appeared stays lit instead of fading out; they are also draggable horizontally, which only the vertical axis supported before.
- **左右并排的两个可滚动区域现在各有一条滚动条**：此前只有最后滚动的那一个区域有轨道，另一个区域的滚动状态完全看不到。滚动条槽位按容器分配，左右两栏、上下分栏互不抢占了。
  - EN: Two independently scrolling areas side by side each get their own scrollbar. Previously only the most recently scrolled area had a rail, so the other one's position was invisible. Rail slots are now keyed per container, so side-by-side and stacked panes no longer steal each other's indicator.
- **内容自己滚动（比如新消息到达时自动跟随到底）不再凭空冒出滚动条**：只有你真的滚了（滚轮、触摸、PageDown 这类按键、拖动把手）滚动条才出现，此前程序化的滚动也会点亮它。
  - EN: Content scrolling on its own — an incoming message auto-following to the bottom, say — no longer summons a scrollbar out of nowhere. The rail now appears only when you actually scrolled (wheel, touch, scrolling keys like PageDown, or dragging the thumb); previously programmatic scrolling lit it up too.
- **发出去没回应的消息，现在会告诉你**：一条消息发出去，会话已经空闲，既没有回复也没有报错 —— 转录里只能表现为「到这儿就断了」，看上去像还在想。这个状态现在会在输入框上方亮一张横幅。这个判断本身是要核实的：实时流可能只是漏掉了这一轮的事件，而且也没有别的东西会重读会话。所以横幅出现之前会按 0 / 10 / 30 秒的节奏重读：读到了回复就不出现，读不到才出现；重读本身失败也算「读不到」，因为一次读失败不该把一次真实的静默藏起来。重试次数从那张不再显示的卡片并到了正文里，所以「试了几次」不会因为卡片退场而丢失。
  - EN: A message that got no answer now says so. A prompt that was sent, on a session that has since gone idle, with neither a reply nor an error shows in the transcript only as an abrupt stop — indistinguishable from still-thinking. It now raises a banner above the composer. The verdict is checked rather than guessed: a live stream can simply drop a turn's events, and nothing else re-reads the session, so the transcript is re-read at 0, 10 and 30 seconds first. A reply found by a read means no banner; only a read that finds nothing raises one, and a read that fails counts as finding nothing — a failed check must not hide a genuine silence. The retry attempt count moves off the card that no longer renders and onto the body, so "how many attempts" survives the card's retirement.

### Changed
- **磨砂玻璃预设去掉了，玻璃固定成一套配方**：这个预设原本在「真透视」和「磨砂强度」之间做交易，而窗口不再借用系统材质之后已经没有真透视可交易，留着它只是让用户在几种「碰巧有多清晰」的玻璃里挑一个。文字和底色的对比度不该是选项——现在由自适应机制固定推导，深浅两套主题各一个值。「窗口透明度」开关保留。Windows 上那份为迁就亚克力材质而调低的密度一并撤掉，各平台玻璃浓度重新一致。
  - EN: The frosted-glass preset is gone and the glass runs on one recipe. The preset used to trade true see-through against frost strength, and with the window no longer borrowing a system material there is no see-through left to trade — keeping it only let you pick between several accidental levels of legibility. Text contrast is not a preference, so it is derived now: the adaptive mechanism computes one transparency per scheme off a fixed value. The window-transparency toggle stays. Windows also loses the lower density that existed to accommodate the acrylic material, so the glass reads the same on every platform.

- **扩展供应商的模型目录可以声明自己的刷新节奏**：registerProvider 新增 discoveryTtlMs——此前动态模型目录一律按宿主默认 24 小时缓存，一个通过同一端点下发每模型积分倍率的供应商，倍率变了选择器要滞后一整天才更新。Auth Hub 的全部渠道现在声明 5 分钟（对齐 jet-hub 的目录节奏），倍率与模型清单跟上真实变化；其余供应商行为不变。
  - EN: Extension providers can now declare their own model-catalog refresh cadence. registerProvider gains discoveryTtlMs — dynamic catalogs all used the host's 24-hour default, so a provider serving per-model credit multipliers through the same endpoint kept showing a changed rate for a whole day. Every Auth Hub channel now declares 5 minutes (jet-hub's catalog cadence), and rates plus model lists track reality; other providers behave exactly as before.
- **在 GUI 里输入 /autoresearch 加目标即可开启自主研究**：此前 GUI 里这条命令只会打开状态面板，实验模式本身开不了——和终端里“命令加目标即开启”的用法不一致。现在带参数的调用真正执行：开启、关闭、清空都与终端同语义；不带参数才打开状态面板。会话右上角的扩展弹层也改为向上展开，不再被下方内容盖住。
  - EN: Typing /autoresearch with a goal in the GUI now starts autonomous research. The GUI command previously only opened the status panel — the mode itself could not be turned on, unlike the terminal where command-plus-argument arms it. Argument calls now execute for real: start, stop, and clear all match the terminal semantics; only the bare command opens the panel. The extensions popup in the session header also opens upward now, so it is no longer covered by the content below.
- **三个魔术关键词改为以插件包形态随产品分发**：ultrathink / orchestrate / workflowz 现在是 `bundled-plugins/` 下的一个真实插件包（首个从静态内置注册表迁出的单元），经与第三方插件完全相同的发现与装载路径运行——一个包三行声明，复用既有的提示词渲染与渐变高亮实现。行为与设置键不变（`magicKeywords.*` 默认值不动）；顺带修掉一个语义缺口：插件注册的关键词现在能把触发词与标识分开声明（内置的 `workflow` 触发词是 `workflowz`，这类场景此前无法表达）。
  - EN: The three magic keywords now ship as a plugin package. ultrathink / orchestrate / workflowz moved out of the static built-in registry into `bundled-plugins/` — the first first-party unit in plugin form — and run through exactly the same discovery and loading path as a third-party plugin: one package, three rows, reusing the existing notice rendering and gradient highlight implementations. Behavior and settings keys are unchanged (`magicKeywords.*` defaults untouched); a semantic gap closed along the way: a plugin-registered keyword can now declare its trigger word separately from its identity (the built-in `workflow` triggers on `workflowz`, which the old shape could not express).
- **魔术关键词的会话消费切换到注册表**：会话现在从关键词注册表读取全部触发词（内置三件套经 bundled 插件包装载、扩展经 registerMagicKeyword 注册），内置静态分支删除——单一消费路径。顺带修掉两个切换中暴露的真缺陷：其一，带渲染钩子的关键词被拒绝时（如 orchestrate 在没有 task 工具的会话里）会错误地回落到静态文本发出提示，现在钩子的决定被尊重；其二，关键词的启停读取此前对内置键与扩展键用了两种语义，现在统一（schema 内的键取类型化默认值，schema 外的键未写入即停用）。
  - EN: The session now reads magic keywords from the registry. All trigger words — the first-party trio loaded through the bundled plugin package and every extension-registered one — come from one registry, and the compiled-in branches are gone: a single consumption path. Two real defects the switch surfaced are fixed along the way: a keyword with a render hook that DECLINED (orchestrate in a session without the task tool, for example) wrongly fell back to its static text and fired anyway — a hook's decision is now respected; and keyword enablement was read with two different semantics for built-in versus extension keys, now unified (in-schema keys take their typed defaults, out-of-schema keys stay off until written).
- **随产品分发的插件包在插件列表归入官方组**：内置插件包（魔术关键词）此前因为没有静态注册表条目可打「内置」标，被归进了「已安装」组——一个随发行版自带的单元显示成用户装的东西。现在 daemon 对 bundled 目录下来的模块行统一打内置标，GUI/TUI 的分组共用这一判定。
  - EN: Bundled plugin packages now group under Official in the plugin list. The built-in package (magic keywords) previously landed in Installed because it had no static registry entry to carry the built-in badge — a unit that ships with the distribution presented as something the user installed. The daemon now marks module rows resolved from the bundled directory, and the GUI/TUI grouping reads that single judgment.

- **界面里的失败不再是一整块红色告警**：一次失败的请求此前是一张带边框的卡片，正文用红色等宽字体整段倒出来，长报文还会换行铺满、把折叠开关挤到折线以下。现在这一轮的原因**就是那一轮的正文** —— 助手列里的普通文字，正常前景色，不带卡片也不带边框；严重程度由一个小标记和标签承担，供应商原文按需折叠（超过 1000 字才折，预览 400 字 / 6 行，展开后在自己的框里滚动）。运行时就某一轮给出的失败不再挤进消息流，而是停靠在输入框上方，用的是输入框那一层一直在用的液态玻璃，只把色调换成红色。**下一次发送就是它的关闭动作** —— 不需要 × 按钮：失败是当前状态，新一轮自然会顶掉它；往上翻它也不跟着走。同一次失败仍然只出现一次。
  - EN: A failure in the interface is no longer a block of red alarm. A failed request used to be a bordered card that printed the provider's body in red monospace, and a long body wrapped to fill the width and pushed the disclosure below the fold. The round's reason is now the round's own text — ordinary message typography in the message column, normal foreground colour, no card and no border. Severity is carried by a small mark and a label, and the provider's own text folds on demand: past 1000 characters it collapses to a 400-character, 6-line preview, and expands into a box that scrolls on its own. A failure the runtime reports for the round no longer crowds the message flow at all — it docks above the composer in the liquid glass the composer's own layer already uses, with the tint switched to the error hue. The next send is the dismissal: there is no × button, because a failure is current state and a new round supersedes it, and scrolling up does not take it along. One failure still surfaces once.
- **回合之间不再有「模型 · 时间 · 已完成 · 耗时」那一行**：它和每条回复下面本来就有的计时行说的是同一件事，但两者的模型来源不同 —— 计时行直接读消息自己的模型，而那一行走的是另一份投影，切换模型之后它还挂着上一个模型的名字，看起来像是上一轮用的。现在这条行被移除，回合之间的间距改由回合起始行自己承担。每条回复下面的「用时 · 模型 · 思考档位」保持不变，它本来就是实时的。
  - EN: The per-round "model · time · done · duration" line is gone. It said what the timer line under each reply already says, but the two read the model differently: the timer line takes it from the message itself, while that line went through a separate projection and kept naming the previous model after a switch, as though the round before had used it. The line is removed and the space between rounds now belongs to the row that starts the turn. The "elapsed · model · thinking level" line under each reply is unchanged, and it was always current.
- **`/usage` 现在是全屏面板**：命令打开一个占满备用屏幕的用量面板，每个账户一栏、每条额度一行，显示供应商、账户、剩余比例、预付费余额与重置时间。**回车**在摘要与完整报告之间切换，**滚轮**或 **PageUp/PageDown** 滚动，**Esc** 关闭。完整报告就是此前内联打印的那份内容，两处不会再出现不一致。
  - EN: `/usage` is now a fullscreen panel. The command opens a usage dashboard on the alternate screen — one block per account, one row per quota, showing the provider, the account, what share is left, any prepaid balance and when the window resets. **Enter** toggles between the summary and the full report, the **wheel** or **PageUp/PageDown** scrolls, and **Esc** closes. The full report is the very text `/usage` used to print inline, so the two surfaces cannot drift apart.
- **用量面板里被截断的内容可以悬停看全**：额度名、账户与重置时间在窄窗口下会截断，而终端本身没有悬停，此前只能拉宽窗口。现在把鼠标移到那一格，底部会显示完整文本；宽度够、不曾截断的格子不触发。
  - EN: Truncated text in the usage panel can be read in full by hovering it. Quota names, accounts and reset times were cut off in a narrow window, and a terminal has no hover of its own, so widening the window was the only way to see them. Moving the pointer over such a cell now shows the full text along the bottom; a cell that fits never raises a hint.

### Fixed

- **Windows 上桌面通知不再找不到会话总线**：`XDG_RUNTIME_DIR` 已设置但 `DBUS_SESSION_BUS_ADDRESS` 未导出时，程序按 Windows 的路径分隔符去拼 socket 路径，找不到就判定没有桌面会话。现在按 POSIX 分隔符拼接，与实际路径一致。
  - EN: Desktop notifications on Windows no longer miss the session bus. With `XDG_RUNTIME_DIR` set but `DBUS_SESSION_BUS_ADDRESS` unexported, the socket path was joined with Windows separators, never matched, and the session was reported as headless. The path is now joined POSIX-style, as the real one is.
- **Windows 与 WSL 上输出积压的上限重新生效**：输出被拒后，程序按「写了多少次」判断何时停止输出，而 Windows 终端会把大块绘制拆成多段写出，于是次数被放大、真正的积压量被低估。现在按字节判断，跨平台一致。
  - EN: The output backlog cap counts bytes again. When a write is refused the program decided when to stop by counting write calls, but a Windows terminal splits a large paint into several writes, which inflated the count and understated the real backlog. The accounting is now by bytes, consistently across platforms.

- **预付费余额现在在每一处用量视图里都能看到**：只有余额、没有额度的供应商，此前只在命令行报告里显示；终端用量面板在那个位置只有一排暗点，客户端卡片上则完全没有这行。现在三处都会给出该余额，账户级的共享池只计一次。
  - EN: A prepaid balance now appears in every usage view. A provider that reports only a remaining balance and no allowance showed it in the command-line report alone — the terminal panel drew dim dots in that cell and the client card had no line for it. All three now report the balance, counting an account-wide pool once.

- **客户端用量卡片不再把「按量计费」的额度显示成 0%**：只有绝对数额、没有百分比的额度（Z.ai 的 token 与 credit 额度、按美元计的额外用量），在桌面转录、移动端与鸿蒙端此前显示成「0% used · 0% left」加一条空进度条。现在直接显示供应商报出的数额，进度条按剩余比例画。
  - EN: The usage card no longer shows an amount-metered quota as 0%. Meters that report an absolute amount and no percentage — Z.ai token and credit quotas, dollar-denominated extra usage — read as "0% used · 0% left" with an empty bar in the desktop transcript, mobile and HarmonyOS clients. The card now shows the amount the provider reported and draws the bar from the remaining share.

- **`/usage` 现在会显示 Z.ai GLM Coding Plan 的 credit 额度**：这类额度此前被整条丢掉——只带 credit 窗口的账户连用量报告都拿不到，在界面上等同于「未上报」。现在按 credit 计量显示，进度用服务端的精确比值，而不是取整后的百分比。
  - EN: `/usage` now shows Z.ai GLM Coding Plan credit windows. They were dropped entirely — an account whose only meter is a credit window produced no usage report at all and read as unreported. Credits render as their own unit, and the bar uses the exact consumed ratio instead of the server's rounded percentage.

- **走代理的供应商现在按配置的地址校验凭据**：`/usage` 与后台用量服务此前只从已发现的模型里推地址；对只在发现后才出现的供应商，这一刻拿不到地址，于是把代理作用域的密钥发给了供应商的官方主机。现在配置与运行时的供应商地址优先于模型推导。
  - EN: A proxied provider is now probed at its configured address. `/usage` and the background usage service derived the address only from discovered models, so a provider that appears solely through discovery had none yet and sent a proxy-scoped key to the vendor's canonical host. Configured and runtime provider addresses now take precedence over model-derived ones.
- **`/usage` 会显示预付费余额**：只有余额、没有额度的供应商此前只在每个凭据下逐条列出余额，汇总行里什么都不显示——同一账户挂多个密钥时也看不出那是一个共享池。现在汇总给出池级总额，共享池只计一次。
  - EN: `/usage` now reports a prepaid balance. A provider that reports only a remaining balance and no allowance listed it per credential and showed nothing in the summary, so several keys on one account never read as a single shared pool. The summary now totals the pool, counting a shared pool once.

- **工作目录被删除后，会话不再崩溃**：长任务里临时目录、已移除的 worktree、整个项目被删掉，都会让会话的工作目录在运行中消失；此前这一刻会在准备子 shell 环境时抛错并中断会话。现在环境的默认工作目录取会话启动时记录的那一个，删除之后仍能继续跑。
  - EN: A session no longer crashes when its working directory is deleted. In a long-running task a temp directory, a removed worktree, or a whole project can disappear underneath the session; that used to throw while preparing the child-shell environment and interrupt the session. The environment now defaults to the directory recorded at session start, so work continues after the deletion.
- **`/debug` 的内存报告不再因为快照太大而失败**：此前堆快照按字符串生成，堆大的时候会超过字符串长度上限直接报错；快照为空时又会静默落下一个 0 字节文件。现在快照按二进制生成，并在为空时明确报错。
  - EN: A `/debug` memory report no longer fails because the snapshot is too large. Heap snapshots were generated as a JavaScript string, which exceeded the string length limit on a large heap; an empty snapshot silently produced a zero-byte file instead. Snapshots are now generated as binary data, and an empty one raises an explicit error.
- **MCP 服务器重连后不再逐次累积内存**：每次重连都会重新包装该服务器暴露的工具，而上一代的包装仍被引用着，于是重连次数越多、占用越大。现在工具包装共享同一个上下文工厂，并且在新一代替换成功之后释放上一代的引用。
  - EN: Reconnecting an MCP server no longer accumulates memory on every reconnect. Each reconnect re-wrapped the tools the server exposes while the previous generation stayed reachable, so memory grew with the number of reconnects. Tool wrappers now share one context factory, and the previous generation's references are released once the new one has replaced it.

- **内容折叠回去后，滚动条有时会留在屏幕上**：内容收进可视高度（切换标签页、折叠一段工具输出、消息被合并）时轨道不会立刻收回，要等窗口尺寸变化才会纠正 —— 那时候它会停在半途，或者干脆指向一个已经不需要指示的地方。现在内容一旦不再溢出，轨道就会收回。
  - EN: After content collapses back into view, the scrollbar could stay on screen. When content folded into the visible height — switching a tab, folding a tool output, messages merging — the rail did not retract immediately and only corrected itself on a window resize, at which point it could sit mid-content or point at something it no longer indicated. The rail now retracts as soon as there is nothing left to scroll.
- **滚动条可能压在弹出的对话框底下**：轨道的层级此前按容器算一次就记住，之后弹窗打开、面板最大化都不再看 —— 于是那条本来该跟着内容走的轨道沉到了遮罩之下。现在每次重新测量都重新读层级。
  - EN: The scrollbar could end up beneath a dialog that opened over it. The rail's stacking level was resolved once per container and then remembered, so a toast opening or a panel maximizing never updated it and the rail sank under the scrim. The level is now re-resolved on every measurement.
- **滚动条层级会随容器切换越攒越多**：每滚过一个容器就把它的尺寸监听器挂上且从不摘下，长会话里滚过的每个列表都被钉在内存里。现改为按槽位复用，回收时一并解除。
  - EN: The scrollbar accumulated observers as you moved between containers: each one scrolled was watched and never unwatched, so every list you had scrolled stayed pinned in memory over a long session. Observation is now per slot and released when a slot is recycled.
- **标签条上那条拖不动的纵向滚动条没有了**：横向滚动的标签条（轨迹 / 文件 / 上下文，右栏面板 tab）为了横向滚动开了 overflow-x，纵向就被裁掉；但被裁掉的溢出仍然算进 scrollHeight，所以只要尺寸差 2px 就会凭空画出一条纵向轨，正好压在标签的关闭按钮上。现在只有真正能滚的那条轴才画把手 —— 被裁掉的溢出不算溢出。
  - EN: The phantom vertical scrollbar on tab strips is gone. A horizontally scrolling tab strip (trajectory / files / context, right-panel tabs) opens overflow-x for the sideways scroll, which clips the vertical one — and the clipped overflow still counts toward scrollHeight, so a 2px difference was enough to grow a vertical thumb, sitting exactly on top of a tab's close button. Only an axis that can actually scroll now grows a handle: clipped overflow is not overflow.
- **滚动时每帧都在读布局**：轨道此前在每个滚动帧里重新读一次容器的样式、位置和尺寸，再把这些结果写回 `left` / `top` / `height`，所以一次滚动会触发多次强制重排。现在这些只在容器尺寸真的可能变化时读（首次出现、窗口变化、内容增删、鼠标移入、换皮肤），滚动帧只读滚动距离、只写位移。
  - EN: Every scroll frame read layout. The rail re-read the container's styles, position and size on each frame and wrote the results back as `left` / `top` / `height`, so one scroll forced several reflows. Those reads now happen only when the container's box can actually have changed (first sight, window resize, content churn, pointer entry, skin switch); a scroll frame reads the scroll offset and writes a transform.
- **窄窗口下设置侧栏的宽度跟上了折叠**：窗口变窄时左侧选项会缩成图标栏，但外框仍停在主侧栏那个可拖动的宽度上 —— 于是图标浮在一条空带里，看起来像是设置页也有一个能随手拖宽的侧栏。现在外框和图标栏一起收成 64px；两者写在同一条规则里，正是因为它们此前分散在两个文件，才会一个跟着折叠、另一个不跟。
  - EN: The settings sidebar now follows the collapse on narrow windows. The left-hand options collapse to an icon rail, but the frame around them kept the main sidebar's user-dragged width, so the icons floated in an empty band and the settings page looked like it had a sidebar of its own to drag wider. The frame and the rail now collapse together to 64px, declared in one rule because keeping them in two files is how one of them came to follow the collapse and the other did not.
- **界面里的报错不再伪装成模型说的话**：一次失败的请求此前会占着助手行的头像与行内缩进，把供应商返回的原始报文用红色等宽字体整段倒出来 —— 读起来就像模型自己说了这句，但它其实是**关于这次请求的事实**。「重试失败」那张卡也走同一条路径，于是同一次失败能在两副面孔下各出现一次。现在两处都走同一个错误呈现组件：按形状而非按文案切分，摘要只留用户能操作的那句，其余折叠进「显示详情」。判定靠形状而不是供应商措辞，是因为改一次供应商文案就会静默改变分类结果。
  - EN: A failed request no longer masquerades as something the model said. It used to take the reply's avatar and gutter and print the provider's raw body in red monospace, which reads as the model having said it — but it is a fact about the request. The retry-failure card took the same path, so one failure could appear twice in two guises. Both now render through one error component, splitting by shape rather than by phrasing: the summary keeps the line the user can act on and the rest folds behind "Show details". Shape rather than wording, because renaming a provider's phrasing would silently reclassify the failure.
- **「余额不足」不再显示两遍**：请求报文与供应商捕获的响应合并时，去重比对的是**带后缀**的那份（形如 `… (type=1113)`），所以当供应商自己的消息就是捕获正文时永远判不出来，正文被追加一次 —— 一个 429 读起来就成了「余额不足… 余额不足… (type=1113)」。现在正文与元数据分开携带、各补各的缺：正文只出现一次，供应商错误码仍然可见。
  - EN: "Insufficient balance" no longer prints twice. When the request message was merged with the provider's captured response, deduplication compared the _decorated_ form — the one carrying a `(type=…)` suffix — so a provider whose own message was the captured body never matched and the body got appended. A 429 read as "insufficient balance … insufficient balance (type=1113)". The merge now carries body and metadata apart and fills in whichever is missing: the body appears once, and the provider error code stays visible.
- **终端里的报错按终端宽度折行，不再按固定列截断**：TUI 打印失败回合的两个位置（编辑器上方的钉住横幅、会话内联的报错块）此前都先丢掉空行、把每行按固定列截断再渲染。这对供应商报错恰好是反的：400/429 的报文通常是一整行 JSON 或长文本，而**解释失败原因的那句在尾部** —— 截断恰好切掉用户最需要的那部分。而且截断发生在折行之前，窄终端下等于被切两次。改为按渲染时的真实宽度折行、折行后再限高，所以任何宽度下块高都可预期。折行本身还需要第二遍处理：`Bun.wrapAnsi` 只在空白处断行，无空白的整块 token（JSON 信封、base64、长 URL —— 供应商报错大多是这类）会原样超宽返回，现在改为按可见宽度切片，并把该行的转义序列在每片两侧重新输出，颜色既不会跨折行渗出也不会中途消失。
  - EN: Terminal errors wrap to the terminal instead of being cut at a fixed column. Both TUI surfaces that print a failed turn dropped blank lines and truncated every line before rendering, which is backwards for provider errors: a 400 or 429 body arrives as one long line whose tail carries the explanation, so the cut removed exactly the part a user needs — and on a narrow terminal the text was cut twice, once at the column limit and again by the renderer's wrap. Wrapping now happens against the real render width and the clamp follows the wrap, so the block's height is predictable at any width. The wrap needed a second pass: Bun.wrapAnsi breaks at whitespace only, so a token with none in it — a JSON envelope, a base64 blob, a long URL, which is most of what a provider error body turns out to be — came back as one over-wide row. Those rows are now sliced by visible width with the row's escape sequences re-emitted around each piece, so colour neither bleeds past the fold nor stops mid-row.
- **重试耗尽时不再把重试器的内部账目塞给用户**：超过延迟上限而中止时，用户看到的是「Provider requested 1800000ms wait, exceeds retry.maxDelayMs (300000ms). Original error: …」—— 可操作的原因排在最后，压在我们自己的一句配置说明后面。本地续跑失败那条路径取的是相反的赢家：钩子坏了是用户该修的，供应商错误已经钉在屏幕上了，所以以本地原因为主，供应商原文进日志。
  - EN: Retry bookkeeping no longer reaches the user at the point retry gives up. A rate-limit abort over the delay cap showed "Provider requested 1800000ms wait, exceeds retry.maxDelayMs (300000ms). Original error: …", putting the actionable reason last, behind a sentence about our own configuration. The local-continuation path picks the opposite winner: a broken hook is the user's to fix and the provider error is already pinned on screen, so that one leads with the local cause and logs the provider text.

## [0.5.3] - 2026-10-03

### Added

- **插件面板不再加载失败，官方插件也都有图标了**：插件自带的设置面板原先一律报「模块加载失败」——界面在组件还在下载时就回收了它的地址；现在加载完成后才释放。同时每个插件项都画出了自己的标识（插件声明的图标、内置单元的类别图标，或按包名生成的彩色字母标），状态改为标识角上的小圆点，不再和图标挤在一起。
  - EN: Plugin panels no longer fail to load, and official plugins have icons too. A plugin's own settings panel always reported "module failed to load" — the UI released its address while the download was still in flight; it now waits for the load to finish. Every plugin also draws its own mark (a declared icon, a built-in unit's kind glyph, or a colored letter tile derived from the package name), and status moved to a corner badge instead of crowding the icon.
- **插件详情弹窗补齐**：点击已安装插件打开详情弹窗——大图标、版本、状态与启用开关、头部卸载按钮；插件自带的组件收进弹窗「包含的组件」逐项开关（不再与插件包重复占列表行），插件的设置界面（如账号池管理）也挂载在弹窗内。
  - EN: The plugin detail dialog completes the picture. Clicking an installed plugin opens a dialog with the large icon, version, status, enable toggle and an uninstall button in the header; the plugin's own modules collapse into per-item included-component toggles (no longer duplicated as separate list rows), and its settings surface (an account pool, for example) mounts inside the dialog.
- **插件列表支持一键卸载**：已安装插件卡片和列表行新增卸载按钮，确认后由守护进程移除插件目录并即时刷新列表；卸载失败时错误横幅提示且列表保持不变。
  - EN: One-click uninstall in the plugin list. Installed plugin cards and rows gain an uninstall button; after a confirmation the daemon removes the plugin directory and refreshes the list immediately, and a failed uninstall shows an error banner while leaving the list untouched.
- **插件图标可自定义**：插件可在清单里声明 `icon`（emoji、字符或图片 URL），列表立即采用；未声明时按包名生成确定的彩色字母标，同包同名同色。
  - EN: Customizable plugin icons. A plugin can declare `icon` in its manifest (emoji, glyph, or an image URL) and the list adopts it; without one, a deterministic colored letter mark is derived from the package name — same package, same mark.
- **侧栏底部新增快捷键按钮 + 快捷键总览弹窗**：侧栏底部状态行多一个 ⌘ 图标按钮，弹窗按分组双栏列出全部快捷键，标题下一行说明，末尾两条提示。**弹窗的每一行都从 `lib/shortcut-registry.ts` 推导，因此弹窗**不可能**与按键处理器的实际行为不一致：重复维护的表会自信地列出一个用户早已改掉的键位。由此白得两个性质：`bindingLabel` 是平台感知的（⌘→Ctrl、↩→Enter、⎋→Esc）且读的是**生效绑定**；注册表在改键时派发 `SHORTCUTS_CHANGED_EVENT`，所以弹窗开着也能实时刷新（7 例契约测试钉住，含"注册表每一条都必须出现"和"改键后立即跟随"）。
  - EN: The sidebar footer gained a shortcuts button and a keyboard-shortcuts reference: a ⌘ icon button in the footer status row opens a two-column, grouped list of every shortcut, with a lede under the title and two tips at the tail. It is never a hand-maintained list. a hand-written list would inline 167 literals (its layout decisions live in that array); every row here is derived from `lib/shortcut-registry.ts`, so the dialog cannot disagree with what the keydown handlers actually do — a duplicated table ends up confidently listing a binding the user rebound months ago. Two properties fall out for free: `bindingLabel` is platform-aware (⌘→Ctrl, ↩→Enter, ⎋→Esc) and reads the EFFECTIVE binding, and the registry fires `SHORTCUTS_CHANGED_EVENT` on a rebind so a dialog left open updates in place (7 contract cases, including "every registry entry appears" and "follows a rebind immediately").
- **快捷键弹窗的两处取舍**：① **每行不加图标** —— 快捷键注册表没有图标字段，为一个装饰性字段污染唯一真相源不值得；② **分组到栏位的分配手工维护** —— 贪心填充能保持两栏高度均衡，但会把阅读顺序打乱（读者会先看到"会话"再看到"布局"），而分组的意义就是阅读顺序；代价是新增分组时要在这里选栏位，已写在注释里。
  - EN: Two deliberate choices in the shortcuts dialog: ① **no per-row icon** — the shortcut registry has no icon field, and adding a decorative one to the single source of truth is not worth it; ② **section-to-column assignment stays hand-written** — greedy packing keeps the columns balanced but scrambles reading order (a reader would meet 会话 next to 布局), and reading order is the point of grouping; the cost is that a new group means choosing its column here, which the comment now says.
- 顺带修掉 `shortcut-registry` 之外新暴露的一处：`.gui-dialog` 基类是 600×420 的设置箱，装不下五个分组的双栏（分组标题会被滚掉），所以新增 `gui-dialog--shortcuts`（880px 宽、内部滚动、窄窗口单栏），沿用 `--settings` 这个尺寸修饰词的先例；`--ui-font-mono` 是我一开始编的键名，改用仓库既有的 `var(--font-mono, …)` 写法。
  - EN: Also fixed what the work exposed: the `.gui-dialog` base is a 600×420 settings box, which cannot hold five grouped sections in two columns without scrolling the headings away, so `gui-dialog--shortcuts` was added (880px, internal scroll, single column on narrow windows) following the existing `--settings` size-modifier precedent; and `--ui-font-mono` was a name I invented, replaced with the repo's existing `var(--font-mono, …)` form.
- **回合导轨新增"加载更多提示"按钮**：此前导轨在指针进入顶边时**已经**会翻页，但那个手势**完全没有任何视觉提示** —— 屏幕上没有任何东西说明还有更早的回合；唯一的翻页信号是转录顶部那条 `aria-hidden` 的加载条，出现在滚动的另一端。现在刻度带上方多一个 20px 圆形按钮，仅在 daemon 报告还有更早历史时出现，在途时 `disabled` 并换成 spinner，**按钮自己管 pending 状态**而不是依赖那条 aria-hidden 的加载条。几何沿用上游（20px 圆、`margin-bottom: 6px`、`inset-inline-end: 2px` 与刻度列对齐，左右两侧通用）；刻度保持 `pointer-events: none`，按钮可点，点击不冒泡到导轨的 y→刻度映射。
  - EN: The turn rail gained a "load more prompts" button. The rail already paged older history when the pointer entered its top edge, but that gesture had no visual affordance at all — nothing on screen said older turns existed, and the only paging signal was the transcript's `aria-hidden` loading bar at the far end of the scroll. The track now carries a 20px circular button above it, shown only when the daemon reports older history, disabled with a spinner while a page is in flight — the button owns its own pending state instead of leaning on that aria-hidden bar. The geometry follows upstream (20px circle, `margin-bottom: 6px`, `inset-inline-end: 2px` to sit on the tick column, valid for both rail sides); ticks stay `pointer-events: none`, the button is clickable, and its click does not bubble into the rail's y→tick mapping.
- **历史翻页卡死不再静默**：`session.history` 有三条失败路径会置 `hasMore = false` —— daemon 判定游标失效（压缩掉了锚点）、空页却仍声称有剩余、页面没有任何推进。三条原本都只有 `console.warn`；而 `markHistoryStale` 的注释写着 "and surface it"，却**从来没有 reader**，所以用户唯一的信号就是**加载按钮凭空消失**。三条路径现在统一置 `historyBlocked`，转录顶部显示一行"无法加载更早的对话：会话历史已被压缩或替换。"，`reloadFromSnapshot` 会重新武装翻页（终态属于撞上它的那一次视图，不属于会话）。
  - EN: A stalled history backfill is no longer silent. Three failures set `hasMore = false` — the daemon refuses the cursor as stale (compaction dropped the anchor), an empty page arrives while the daemon still reports rows remaining, and a page advances nothing. All three only logged to `console.warn`; `markHistoryStale`'s own comment said "and surface it" while nothing ever read it, so the user's only signal was the load-more control silently going away. All three now set `historyBlocked`, the transcript shows an "older history is unavailable" row at the top, and `reloadFromSnapshot` re-arms paging — the terminal state belongs to the view that hit it, not to the session.
- 顺带拆掉一个命名陷阱：`TurnRail` 里 `hasMoreAbove` 原本**有两个不同含义** —— 局部变量指"刻度窗口上方还有刻度"（轮播翻页），而 `turnsData.hasMoreAbove` 指"daemon 还有更早历史"。新按钮正好坐在这两者旁边，因此把局部量改名为 `moreTicksAbove` / `moreTicksBelow`，并加测试钉住"刻度窗口变长不会凭空生出加载按钮"。
  - EN: Removed a naming trap along the way: `hasMoreAbove` meant two different things inside `TurnRail` — the local flag is "more ticks above the window" (carousel paging), while `turnsData.hasMoreAbove` is "the daemon still has older history". The new button sits right between the two, so the locals are now `moreTicksAbove` / `moreTicksBelow`, with a test pinning that a long tick window never conjures the pager.
- **空闲会话提示缓存预热**：在缓存条目即将过期前用「1 token 输出预算」重发上一次请求，避免长时间空闲后整段前缀缓存被重写。成本感知调度——在声明的条目寿命 90% 处触发（至少保留 10 秒余量），且仅当「预计省下的缓存未命中费用 − 刷新成本 ≥ $0.05」时才真正发送；streaming / idle 两段策略分别用 100% / 15% 的续写概率，并受 60 分钟 / 30 分钟固定安全窗口约束。**只对 catalog 里显式标注了 `promptCache` 寿命的模型生效**（目前仅直连 Anthropic：5m / 1h，与官方文档一致），Bedrock / 网关 / openai-compat 代理一律不标注——它们的重放行为与过期时间未经验证，宁可不预热也不编造。自定义模型可在 `models.yml` 里用 `promptCache` 自行开启。新增 `providers.cacheWarming` 开关（off / streaming / idle，默认 idle），并新增 `cache_warming_decision` 扩展事件可覆盖单次决策。扩展可读取 `session.cacheWarmingStatus` 查看当前预热状态。原先 Anthropic 专属的固定 keep-alive 刷新回路（ai 层 stream 调度机制）已随之移除。
  - EN: Idle prompt-cache warming: the last request is re-sent with a one-token output budget shortly before its cache entry expires, so a long idle gap no longer forces a full-prefix cache re-write. Cost-aware scheduling — the refresh fires at 90% of the declared entry lifetime (keeping at least a ten-second margin), and only when the expected avoided cache-miss cost minus the refresh cost clears $0.05; the streaming/idle phases use 100%/15% continuation probability and are bounded by fixed 60-minute / 30-minute safety windows. It only arms for models whose catalog entry explicitly declares a `promptCache` lifetime (currently direct Anthropic only: 5m / 1h, matching the vendor docs); Bedrock, gateways, and openai-compat proxies stay unannotated because their replay behavior and expiry are unvalidated — better not to warm than to guess. Custom models can opt in via `promptCache` in `models.yml`. New `providers.cacheWarming` setting (off / streaming / idle, default idle) plus a `cache_warming_decision` extension event to override an individual decision; extensions can read `session.cacheWarmingStatus` for the current state. The previous Anthropic-only fixed keep-alive refresh loop (and its stream-level scheduling machinery in the ai package) is removed.

### Changed

- **历史翻页按轮次对齐：最老的一轮不再是个碎片**：此前一次翻页是 `maxMessages: 500` 平铺前置，批次边界是任意的 —— 于是**最顶上那一轮往往是残缺的**，导轨最顶刻度代表一轮而它的前半截还没加载，点进去会落到某一轮的中间，没有任何报错只是屏幕内容不对。客户端每页 100 条，**批次最老那条必须是轮起点**，不满足则最多再补 2 页，整批原子提交（补读失败则保留原有窗口和游标）。daemon 游标始终权威 —— 对齐只决定"多读几页"，绝不臆造或改写锚点；游标重复出现即停止而不是重放同一页；空页却仍声称有剩余也停止。`requestJump` 的翻页循环自动继承该行为，⌘F 命中因此也落在完整轮次上。
  - EN: History backfill is now turn-aligned, so the oldest turn is no longer a fragment. A backfill used to be a flat `maxMessages: 500` prepend, which makes the batch boundary arbitrary — so the oldest loaded turn was often incomplete, the rail's top tick stood for a turn whose earlier half was not loaded, and clicking it scrolled into the middle of a turn with no error, just the wrong content on screen. The client now uses 100 messages per page, the batch's oldest entry must be a turn start, up to 2 extra reads when it is not, and the whole batch commits atomically (a failed follow-up read leaves the previous window and cursor intact). The daemon cursor stays authoritative throughout — alignment only decides how many extra pages to read and never invents or rewrites an anchor; a repeated cursor stops the walk instead of replaying a page, and so does an empty page that still claims history remains. `requestJump`'s paging loop inherits this, so a ⌘F hit also lands on a whole turn.
- **翻页每批从 500 降到 100–300 条**：页大小从 500 降到 100–300 是为换取批次边界正确。单次点击/自动加载拿到的消息数变少（原来一次 500，现在 100 起、最多 300），滚到很深的历史需要更多次加载；换来的是导轨最顶刻度永远指向一个完整的轮次。若更看重单次载荷，可把 `HISTORY_PAGE_MESSAGES` 调大 —— 对齐逻辑与页大小相互独立。
  - EN: Each backfill batch drops from 500 to 100–300 messages. The smaller page size trades how many messages one click loads for a correct batch boundary. One click (or auto-load) now yields fewer messages than before, so reaching deep history takes more loads; in exchange the rail's top tick always points at a whole turn. If single-load payload matters more, raise `HISTORY_PAGE_MESSAGES` — the alignment walk and the page size are independent.
- **以顾问笔记开头的会话，翻页对齐不再错位**：轮边界判定用 `isTurnStartEntry`（user 消息**或** `display: true` 的顾问笔记），而不是"是否 user 消息"。这是本仓库其它各处（导轨、轨迹地图、daemon 轮次索引）统一的轮边界；agent 主动发起的轮**本来就没有 user 消息**，只认 user 消息会让"以顾问笔记开头"的那批会话仍然错位。
  - EN: History backfill no longer misaligns sessions that open with an advisor note: the turn boundary is `isTurnStartEntry` (a user message OR a displayed advisor note) rather than "is a user message". That is this repo's turn boundary everywhere else — rail, trajectory map, the daemon's turn index — and an agent-initiated round has no user prompt by construction, so a user-message-only test still misaligns exactly those sessions.
- **会话地图的事件卡从"一行字"升级为信息 + 操作卡**：展开轮后的事件卡重构为主行 + 副行结构——主行 = kind 药丸（用户/助手/工具/顾问/系统，与图标同色系）+ 摘要（工具行显示工具名）+ 元信息（usage/耗时/ttft/时刻）；工具行副行显示参数摘要（mono、单行截断）。交互上单击卡片直接跳回对话定位该事件（取代原先只高亮无用的选中态），悬停浮现「切换到此分支 / 重答」操作按钮（user 行双操作、其余行仅切换，锚分派与右键菜单同一套 branchAt 语义）；事件卡高度按形态区分（单行 28px / 工具双行 44px），布局增量与渲染同源。轨迹检视器时间线模式的轮头同步补上同款悬停「重答 / 切换」双操作（重答锚轮首 user 事件、切换锚轮末事件）。
  - EN: Session map event cards upgraded from "one line of text" to info + action cards: each card is now a main row + detail row — the main row carries a kind pill (User/Assistant/Tool/Advisor/System, same palette as the icons) + summary (tool rows show the tool name) + metadata (usage/duration/TTFT/timestamp); tool rows get a detail row with the argument summary (mono, single-line ellipsis). Interactions: clicking a card jumps straight back to the conversation and locates that event (replacing the useless highlight-only selection state), and hovering reveals "switch to this branch / re-answer" action buttons (user rows get both, other rows only switch, with the same branchAt anchor dispatch as the context menu). Card heights now follow the shape (28px single line / 44px tool detail) and the layout increment is derived from the same source as the rendering. The trajectory viewer's timeline mode turn headers gained the same hover "re-answer / switch" dual action (re-answer anchors the turn's first user event, switch anchors the turn-end event).

- **⌘F 在当前会话内查找消息**：查找栏浮在 transcript 顶边，带命中计数与上下跳转（Enter / Shift+Enter），点预览行直接定位。查找范围限定当前会话——会话是全文可搜的，不必先滚到那一段。跳转复用既有的行定位通道，会自动翻历史分页、展开压缩折叠并高亮命中行。
  - EN: ⌘F finds messages within the open conversation. The find bar floats over the transcript's top edge with a match counter and next/previous navigation (Enter / Shift+Enter); clicking the preview row jumps straight there. The search is scoped to the current session, so it sees the whole conversation rather than only the part you have scrolled to. Jumping reuses the existing row-reveal path, which pages history in, opens the compaction fold and flashes the hit.
- **归档会话后可撤销**：侧栏行菜单与顶栏归档按钮都会弹出一条底部居中的撤销 toast。归档本身只是本地列表改动，所以撤销是同步且零成本的 —— 不需要确认对话框，也不会出现"归档完找不回来"。
  - EN: Archiving a session can now be undone. Both the sidebar row menu and the header archive action raise a bottom-center undo toast. The archive is a local list edit, so the undo is synchronous and free — no confirmation dialog, and no "archived and now it's gone".
- **命令面板增量**：新增「重新加载界面」命令；粘贴完整会话 ID 可精确命中该会话（模糊搜索对长 hex ID 无效，此前只能得到「无结果」）；面板会记住上一次的搜索词，⌘K 切换不再清空敲了一半的内容。
  - EN: Command palette additions: a Reload UI command; pasting a full session id now resolves to exactly that session (fuzzy search cannot match a long hex id, which previously always returned "no results"); and the palette remembers its last query, so toggling ⌘K no longer wipes a half-typed search.

- **会话地图重做分叉形态与轮内颗粒度（按实机手绘示意图）**：① 分叉改在父轮结束之后的主干线上形成独立交汇圆点（git 树式）——此前分支贝塞尔从父轮卡片右缘中部拉出，视觉上兄弟轮被画成父轮的子节点；现在交汇点落在父轮下缘间距中点的干线上（横向模式为转置几何），同一父轮的兄弟分支共享一个圆点，贝塞尔先从圆点沿主干走一段再进入分支卡。② 每轮展开后框内事件从 24px 裸行升级为独立事件卡（微玻璃面 + kind 色点 + 描边，28px + 4px 间距），轮次徽章改为框上的 accent 药丸角标（分支/叶子/顾问轮各有配色）。布局常量与 CSS 同步（turnExpandedExtra 按事件卡 32px 步进 + 泳道留白 8 封顶 TURN_LANE_MAX_H + 8）。3 例新契约测试（交汇点落在父轮之后干线间距中点 / 兄弟分支共享一个交汇点 / 横向模式转置几何）。
  - EN: The session map's fork shape and per-turn granularity were reworked (per the on-device hand-drawn sketch): ① forks now form an independent junction dot on the trunk right after the parent turn ends (git-tree style) — previously the branch bezier started from the middle of the parent card's right edge, visually drawing sibling turns as children of the parent; the junction now sits on the trunk at the mid-gap below the parent card (transposed geometry in horizontal mode), siblings branching from the same parent share one dot, and the bezier runs along the trunk from the dot before entering the branch card. ② events inside an expanded turn upgrade from 24px bare rows to standalone event cards (micro glass surface + kind color dot + border, 28px + 4px gap), and the turn badge becomes an accent pill corner mark on the frame (branch/leaf/advisor turns each get their own tint). Layout constants stay in sync with CSS (turnExpandedExtra steps 32px per event card + 8px lane padding, capped at TURN_LANE_MAX_H + 8). 3 new contract tests (junction sits at the trunk mid-gap after the parent / siblings share one junction / transposed geometry in horizontal mode).

### Fixed

- **发版不再重新解析依赖图**（`release.ts`）：改版本号本来只需要让 `Cargo.lock` 记下新的成员版本，用的却是 `cargo generate-lockfile` —— 它会把**每一个**依赖重新解析到最新兼容版本。实测确认：换成 `cargo update --workspace` 后输出 `Locking 0 packages`，第三方依赖一个都不动；原命令即使在钉住 `pdf-inspector` 之后仍会顺手换掉 5 个无关 crate。今后任何依赖的升级都是一次单独的、可评审的改动。
  - EN: A release no longer re-resolves the dependency graph (`release.ts`). Bumping versions only needs `Cargo.lock` to record the new member versions, but the script ran `cargo generate-lockfile`, which re-resolves **every** dependency to the newest compatible version. Measured: `cargo update --workspace` reports `Locking 0 packages` and moves no third-party pin, while the old command still swapped 5 unrelated crates even with `pdf-inspector` pinned. Moving a dependency is now a separate, reviewable change.
- **原生依赖不再随发布漂移**（`pdf-inspector` 钉到 `=1.25.2`）：`release.ts` 重新生成 lockfile 时跑的是**无约束**的 `cargo update`，所以 caret range 意味着一次 patch 版本发布可以在没人决定的情况下改变原生行为 —— 0.5.3 就这样把 `pdf-inspector` 从 `1.20.0` 带到了 `1.25.2`。新版**扩大并改严**了"这页需要 OCR"的判定（新增扫描件 / 矢量文字 / 不可见文字层 / 无文本 / 乱码五类，`lib.rs:4747-4791` 还会对被判定的页跑 `is_cid_garbage()` 并**丢弃**其中的文本项）。评估后**接受**这次行为变化：它堵的是"把乱码当正文喂给用户"的洞，退回 1.20.0 等于重新打开它。代价是这份我们专门留作回归夹具的畸形 PDF 现在会走"请用浏览器工具渲染"而不是直接出正文 —— 这类输入下的降级是诚实的，且新版还多给了机器可读的 `ocr_reasons_by_page`（尚未接入）。本条把版本改成精确钉住，之后再升级必须是一次显式决策。
  - EN: Native dependencies no longer drift with a release (`pdf-inspector` pinned to `=1.25.2`). `release.ts` regenerates the lockfile with an **unconstrained** `cargo update`, so a caret range let a patch release change native behaviour with nobody deciding it — which is how 0.5.3 pulled `pdf-inspector` from `1.20.0` to `1.25.2`. The new version broadens and tightens "this page needs OCR" (five new reasons — scanned, vector text, invisible text layer, no text, garbled; `lib.rs:4747-4791` additionally runs `is_cid_garbage()` on flagged pages and **discards** their text items). The behaviour change is accepted on evaluation: it closes the hole where garbled text reaches the user as body copy, and reverting to 1.20.0 reopens it. The cost is that the deliberately malformed PDF we keep as a regression fixture now degrades to "render it with the browser tool" instead of emitting text — an honest outcome for that input — and the new version exposes machine-readable `ocr_reasons_by_page`, not yet surfaced. This pins the version exactly; future upgrades are now a deliberate act.
- **装好的插件终于会热重载**：扩展 HMR 的监视根是一份手写清单，只有 `<agentDir>/extensions` 和 `<cwd>/.musepi/extensions` 两项 —— 而插件真正被加载的根是另外两个：`getPluginsDir()`（市场安装 / `plugin link` 的插件住在这里）和**锚点之上**的项目插件根。于是**用户唯一能安装的那种插件，恰好是唯一完全没有热重载的那种**：改插件入口文件不触发任何刷新，只能等会话重开；手写扩展一切正常，所以本地开发永远撞不到这个缺口。项目根那一项还踩了第二个坑 —— 旧代码硬编码 `cwd/.musepi/extensions`，而 loader / 安装 / 卸载 / 列表 / discovery 共用的判定是 `resolveActiveProjectRegistryPath` 向上找 `.omp/` 或 `.git/`，两边迟早漂。根清单抽到 `daemon/extension-watch-roots.ts`，**复用那一个解析器**而不是再写一遍向上查找（3 例契约测试；回退验证确认删掉插件根即失败，而两个既有扩展根仍然通过）。
  - EN: Installed plugins now hot-reload. The extension HMR watch roots were a hand-written list of two entries — `<agentDir>/extensions` and `<cwd>/.musepi/extensions` — while the roots plugins are actually loaded from are two others: `getPluginsDir()` (where marketplace installs and `plugin link` land) and the project plugin root **above** the project anchor. So the only kind of plugin a user can install was the only kind with no hot reload at all: editing a plugin entry file triggered nothing and the change appeared only after reopening the session, while hand-written extensions reloaded fine — which is why local development never hits this gap. The project root carried a second mismatch: the old code hardcoded `cwd/.musepi/extensions`, but the loader, install, uninstall, list, and discovery all share `resolveActiveProjectRegistryPath`, which walks up for `.omp/` or `.git/`; the two lists were bound to drift. The root list now lives in `daemon/extension-watch-roots.ts` and reuses that one resolver instead of writing a second walk-up (3 contract cases; reverting the fix to the old two-root list fails the plugin-root case while both pre-existing extension roots still pass).
- **第一个真实的第一方插件包 + 内置目录**（`musepi-git`）：扩展中心此前**在结构上就是空的** —— 商店面板遍历 `marketplaces.json`，而那个文件只有用户会写，仓库里没有任何东西播种它，于是第三方插件无从发现。现在有了：内置目录 `packages/marketplace.json`（用真实 catalog 解析器校验）、插件包 `packages/musepi-git`（注册 `/git`，报告分支 / HEAD 形态 / 工作区计数）、以及 README 双语对。全部 git 访问走宿主中央 helper（`repo.root` / `head.resolve` / `status.summary`），**不报 ahead/behind** —— 中央 helper 没有对应读取器，用 `rev-list` 按两个方向各拼一次就是"第二套实现"，补它该改 `utils/git.ts` 而不是改插件。插件**刻意多文件**（入口只做装配，命令 / 读取 / 格式化各自独立），好让上一条修的子模块热重载有一个真实消费方，而不只是 fixture。7 例契约测试钉住目录与包不会各自漂移（改名 / 挪文件时静默失效，运行时只有"插件装不上"或"扩展根本不加载"）。
  - EN: The first real first-party plugin package, plus a bundled catalog (`musepi-git`). The extension center was structurally empty: the store panel iterates `marketplaces.json`, which only the user ever writes — nothing in the repo seeds it, so no third-party plugin was discoverable. There is now a bundled catalog at `packages/marketplace.json` (validated with the real catalog parser), the plugin package `packages/musepi-git` (registers `/git`, reporting branch, HEAD shape and working-tree counts), and the README pair the repo requires. All git access goes through the central host helper (`repo.root` / `head.resolve` / `status.summary`), and ahead/behind is deliberately absent: the helper has no reader for it, and hand-assembling `rev-list` per direction is the second-implementation problem — that belongs in `utils/git.ts`, not in a plugin. The package is deliberately multi-file (the entry only wires modules together; command, state reading and formatting are separate) so the submodule hot reload fixed above has a real consumer instead of only fixtures. Seven contract cases pin the catalog and the package against silent drift (a rename or a moved file fails quietly; at runtime the only symptom is "the plugin will not install" or "the extension never loads").
- **扩展改子模块不用再 touch 入口**：多文件扩展改一个非入口文件，以前**什么都不会发生** —— 文档里写着"改子模块需 touch 入口"，看起来像 Bun 模块图的固有语义，其实是我们自己的两个缺陷叠在一起。其一，装载时会对入口的整张源码图做走查（含静态 `import`、CJS `require`、动态 `import()`），但 daemon 的变更判定只比**入口文件**的 mtime，所以图内其它文件的改动**根本不会被判定为需要重载**；其二，图内 specifier 走 `toGraphImportSpecifier`，而它在 win32 上返回 `file://` —— Bun **忽略 `file://` 上的 query**，于是整图静默命中进程缓存。入口之所以能重载，是因为 `loadLegacyPiModule` 早在 win32 上就拒绝 `file://`、改发裸路径；图内模块从没跟上这个待遇，这个不对称就是"入口能刷、子模块不能"的来源。现在：装载时快照逐文件 mtime，判定按整图比对（`extensionEntriesNeedingReload`，图内任一文件变了就重载其入口，删除也算），图内 specifier 一律发裸路径 + `?mtime=`。仓库里原本就有 4 个**红着的**契约测试钉这件事（扩展本地 bare 依赖的子模块、已安装插件的子模块、被相对 CJS 子模块 require 的提升依赖、惰性 import 的 CJS 重导出）——它们在 HEAD 上就是失败的，现在 4 个全绿；另加 3 例新测试覆盖"只改子模块即被判定"、"重载后不再重复判定"（防止重载成环）、"子模块被删"。
  - EN: Editing an extension submodule no longer requires touching the entry. For a multi-file extension, changing anything other than the entry used to do nothing at all — the docs said "touch the entry when you edit a submodule", which read as an inherent property of the Bun module graph but was two of our own defects stacked. First, loading already walks the entire source graph of the entry (static `import`, CJS `require`, and dynamic `import()`), but the change detection in the daemon compared only the mtime of the **entry file**, so edits anywhere else in the graph never even registered as needing a reload. Second, graph specifiers go through `toGraphImportSpecifier`, which returned `file://` on win32 — and Bun **ignores query strings on `file://` URLs**, so the whole graph silently hit the process cache. The entry reloaded on Windows only because `loadLegacyPiModule` already refuses `file://` there and emits a bare path; graph modules never got that treatment, and that asymmetry is exactly why the entry refreshed and its children did not. Loading now snapshots per-file mtimes and detection compares the whole graph (`extensionEntriesNeedingReload`: any file in the graph reloads its entry, and a deletion counts); graph specifiers now always carry a bare path plus `?mtime=`. The repo already had **four failing** contract tests pinning this — children of an extension-local bare dependency, children of the extension-local bare dependency of an installed plugin, a hoisted dependency required by a relative CJS child, and a lazily imported CJS re-export. They fail at HEAD and all four are green now — plus three new cases covering "a submodule-only edit is detected", "no repeat detection after a reload" (so a reload loop cannot form), and "a deleted submodule is detected".
- **Markdown 导出不再丢掉顾问卡片**：会话导出原先只挑 `type === "message"`，于是 `custom_message`（顾问笔记、hook 通知、collab / skill 提示）整族从导出的文件里**静默消失** —— 没有占位、没有报错，用户转发出去的 transcript 就不再解释发生过什么。第二个更隐蔽的问题：顾问的 `content` 是**给模型看的** `<advisory severity="…" guidance="…">` XML，从错误字段取值会把 XML 而不是笔记写进文件。选择规则连同"绝不导出 XML"这条约束一起抽到 `lib/session-markdown.ts`（10 例契约测试，含隐藏笔记不导出、多条笔记以 `; ` 合并不丢、`display: false` 不外泄），文件名生成也在同一处。XML 解包与 daemon 的轮次摘要共用 `@musepi/pi-wire` 新增的 `advisorNoteText`，那份拷贝随之删除。
  - EN: The Markdown export no longer loses advisor cards. It used to select only `type === "message"`, so the whole `custom_message` family (advisor notes, hook notices, collab/skill prompts) vanished from the exported file — no placeholder, no error — and a transcript the user re-shares no longer explained what happened. The second failure is quieter: an advisor entry's `content` is the model-facing `<advisory severity="…" guidance="…">` XML, so reading the wrong field writes XML into the file instead of the note. The selection rule, together with the never-export-the-XML constraint, moved to `lib/session-markdown.ts` (10 contract cases, covering hidden notes staying out, multiple notes joining with `; ` without loss, and `display: false` never escaping); the filename derivation lives there too. The XML unwrap is now shared with the daemon's turn summary via the new `advisorNoteText` in `@musepi/pi-wire`, and that copy is gone.
- **子代理视图里的顾问卡片不再消失**：`rpc-subagents.ts` 用 `entry.type === "message"` 过滤 `messages`，而同一响应的 `entries` 字段是完整的（类型上能看出来），于是任何从更顺手的 `messages` 建 transcript 的嵌入方都会静默丢掉顾问卡片。改用共享的 `extractRenderableEntries`，`messages` 里现在带 `role: "custom"`，与实时内存 transcript 一致。这条路径可达：`advisor.enabled` 的子代理（agent frontmatter 驱动）确实会把顾问卡片写进**自己的** JSONL。
  - EN: Advisor notes no longer vanish from the subagent view. `rpc-subagents.ts` filtered `messages` with `entry.type === "message"` while the sibling `entries` field in the same response is complete (the types make that visible), so any embedder building a transcript view from the more ergonomic `messages` lost advisor cards silently. It now uses the shared `extractRenderableEntries`, so `messages` carries `role: "custom"` — matching what the live in-memory transcript already holds. The path is reachable: a subagent with `advisor.enabled` (driven by agent frontmatter) does write advisor cards into its OWN jsonl.
- **HTML 分享导出与 `/tree` 共用同一份"默认隐藏条目"清单**：`export/html/template.js` 带着一份自己的字面量副本，**已经少了三个类型**（`service_tier_change`、`title_change`、`reset_boundary`），于是这三类记账行会漏进导出的 default / no-tools 视图。模板是被 `index.ts` 以原始文本内联进 HTML 的浏览器 JS，无法 import TS 模块，所以由 `index.ts` 把 `HIDDEN_BY_DEFAULT_ENTRY_TYPES` 序列化进一个全局、模板读取它；字面量副本随之删除。全局缺失时降级为"什么都不隐藏" —— 漏出几行记账行只是观感问题，藏掉对话内容才是。全程断言在**拼装后的模板**上比对注入值，而不是比对某份源文件文本。
  - EN: The HTML share export and `/tree` now share one hidden-by-default entry list. `export/html/template.js` carried its own literal copy, which had already lost three types (`service_tier_change`, `title_change`, `reset_boundary`), leaking those bookkeeping rows into the export's default and no-tools views. The template is inlined browser JS and cannot import the TS module, so `index.ts` serialises `HIDDEN_BY_DEFAULT_ENTRY_TYPES` into a global that the template reads, and the literal copy is deleted. A missing global degrades to "nothing is hidden" — leaking a few bookkeeping rows is cosmetic, hiding conversation content is not. The assertion compares the injected value on the ASSEMBLED template rather than grepping a source file.
- **代理中心全屏 transcript 不再吞掉顾问卡片**：查看器用 `entry.type === "message"` 挑可渲染条目，把 `custom_message` 整族静默丢弃 —— 没有占位、没有报错。而 `advisor: true` 的子代理会话**自己的 jsonl 里就带顾问卡片**，于是查看器恰好丢掉了标识"这是在看什么"的那几行；实时转录（`chat-transcript-builder` 走 `role: "custom"` → `#appendCustomMessage`）渲染同样的条目，两个表面因此对同一份文件给出不同内容。同时它是**不对称**的：同一函数显式处理了 `model_change`，只漏了 `custom_message`，说明作者在逐类型枚举时漏了这一族。现新增 `session/transcript-records.ts` 作为唯一的记录族规则：`custom_message` 转成渲染器吃的 `role: "custom"` 消息（payload 与 `#persistMessageEnd` 落盘时写入的五个字段一一对应），"默认隐藏"的条目类型清单也从 `tree-selector` 的函数内常量提为共享常量（此前 `export/html/template.js` 另有一份副本，已少 4 个类型）。可见性仍由渲染器决定（`#appendCustomMessage` 开头就 `if (!message.display) return`），选择层不重复这条规则。
  - EN: The agent hub's fullscreen transcript no longer swallows advisor cards. The viewer selected renderable entries with `entry.type === "message"`, which silently dropped the whole `custom_message` family — no placeholder, no error. A subagent session run with `advisor: true` keeps its advisor cards in its OWN jsonl, so the viewer dropped exactly the rows identifying what it was showing; the live transcript renders the same entries (chat-transcript-builder routes `role: "custom"` to #appendCustomMessage), so two surfaces disagreed about one file. The gate was also asymmetric: the same function handled `model_change` explicitly and simply missed `custom_message`, i.e. the author was enumerating types and missed this family. `session/transcript-records.ts` is now the single record-family rule: `custom_message` is lifted into the `role: "custom"` message the renderer consumes (payload matching the five fields #persistMessageEnd writes), and the hidden-by-default entry types moved out of tree-selector's function-local const into a shared one — the HTML export kept a second copy that was already missing four types. Visibility stays the renderer's call (#appendCustomMessage opens with `if (!message.display) return`), so the selection layer does not duplicate that rule.
- **落在废弃分支上的顾问笔记不再被画进主干线**：地图用 `turn.events[0].branch` 判定整轮是否分支，而只有 message 事件带这个标记（`branch: isBranch`）—— 顾问事件的 push 里从没有这个字段，于是 `branchAt` 之后旧链上的顾问笔记会被当作主线轮渲染，与它实际所在的分支不符。现把分支判定提成 `isBranchEntry` 闭包，message 与顾问事件共用同一口径（活跃路径优先，其次 first-child 启发式），顾问事件补 `branch`。反向也钉住了：顾问在活跃路径上时不得被标记，否则每条顾问笔记都会被塞进独立车道。
  - EN: Advisor notes on a discarded branch are no longer drawn on the main line. The map decides whether a whole turn is a branch from `turn.events[0].branch`, but only message events ever carried that flag — the advisor event's push had no such field, so after `branchAt` an advisor note on the old chain rendered as a main-line turn, contradicting the branch it actually sits in. The branch test is now a single `isBranchEntry` closure shared by message and advisor events (active path first, then the first-child heuristic), and advisor events carry `branch`. The negative direction is pinned too: a note that IS on the active path must not be flagged, or every advisor note would get its own lane.
- **顾问轮摘要不再向界面泄漏 `<advisory>` XML；daemon 轮次判定改用权威实现**：`session.turns` 的 `summary` 被导航条悬停面板（`TurnRail`）原样渲染，而顾问条目的 `content` 是**给模型看的** `<advisory severity=… guidance=…>` XML，人读文本在 `details.notes[].note` —— 旧的文本提取对顾问条目回退到 `content`，于是悬停预览里直接显示 XML 标签。地图那条链路早就有同样的解包和注释（"永不外露"），daemon 这条一直漏修；旧测试的夹具把 `content` 写成纯文本，所以从没暴露。现按 `details.notes[].note` 解包（多条以 "; " 连接、空条丢弃），**没有 notes 时留空而不是回退到 XML**，并把测试夹具换成真实条目形状。另：`daemonIsTurnStart` 原是 `isTurnStartStart` 判定的手抄副本，仅靠一条注释约束（注释本身还写错了源头），而轮次边界同时决定导航条、地图和条目 id 跳转，漂移等于全局少算多算轮次 —— 现改为直接委托 `@musepi/pi-wire` 的 `isTurnStartEntry`，并把该判定本身改成全函数（journal 行是未校验的 JSON，缺 `message` 的 `type:"message"` 行必须跳过而不是抛异常，否则一行坏数据会带倒 daemon）。
  - EN: Advisor turn summaries no longer leak `<advisory>` XML into the UI, and the daemon's turn-start test now uses the canonical implementation. `session.turns` ships a `summary` that the turn rail's hover panel renders verbatim, but an advisor entry's `content` is the MODEL-FACING `<advisory severity=… guidance=…>` XML — the reader-facing text lives in `details.notes[].note`. The old extractor fell back to `content` for advisor entries, so the hover preview showed raw XML tags. The map path already unwrapped notes and documented the leak ("never surfaced"); this path was missed, and the old test fixture wrote `content` as plain text, so nothing caught it. Notes are now unwrapped from `details.notes[].note` (joined with "; ", blanks dropped) and a note-less entry yields an empty summary instead of falling back to the XML; the fixtures now use the real entry shape. Separately, `daemonIsTurnStart` was a hand-copied twin of the turn-start rule whose only guard was a comment (and that comment named the wrong source), yet turn boundaries decide the rail, the map and the entry-id jumps at once — so a drift is an app-wide miscount. It now delegates to `isTurnStartEntry` from `@musepi/pi-wire`, and that predicate was made total: journal lines are unvalidated JSON, so a `type: "message"` row with no `message` must be skipped rather than thrown, since one bad line would otherwise take the daemon down.
- **⌘F 命中跳不过去 + 查找栏停靠在会话视图内**：① 跳不过去的原因是时间戳类型不一致 —— daemon 消息表的 `timestamp` 存的是**数字**（epoch 毫秒），而转录条目的 `timestamp` 是 **ISO 字符串**，两者恒不相等，解析返回 null 后翻页循环跑到 `hasMore` 为假便静默丢弃；计数仍显示 1/1，所以看起来正是"搜到了但不跳"。现按 epoch 毫秒归一化比对，并留 ±1 秒窗口 —— daemon 存的是*消息*的时间戳、转录存的是*条目*的时间戳，两者出自不同代码路径，实测一致但不是同一次读数；窗口内多个候选再用命中文本消歧。② 查找栏原先挂在三层外某个 `focusMode ? … : …` 三元表达式里的 Tailwind `relative` 上，偏移父级实际解析到了窗口根，于是整个界面而非会话视图圆角卡片；且 TurnRail 是贴着用户所选边缘的 24px 绝对定位覆盖层，固定 14px 内缩正好压在它上面。现改为挂在 `.gui-chat-body`（会话列自身的盒子），偏移父级写进我们自己的 CSS 而非依赖工具类，并用 `ResizeObserver` **实测**导轨占位后下发内缩量——不再硬编码边距。
  - EN: Fixed ⌘F hits that would not jump, and docked the find bar inside the conversation view. ① The jump never happened because the timestamps differ in type: the daemon's message table stores `timestamp` as a **number** (epoch ms) while transcript entries carry an **ISO string**, so the two never compared equal — the resolver returned null, the paging loop ran until `hasMore` was false, and the jump was dropped without a trace. The counter still read 1/1, which read as "found but won't jump". Comparison is now normalized to epoch ms with a ±1s window: the daemon stamps the _message_ and the transcript stamps the _entry_, two different code paths that agree in practice but are not the same reading; when the window admits more than one candidate the matched text decides. ② The bar had been hanging off a Tailwind `relative` buried in a `focusMode ? … : …` ternary three levels up, so its offset parent resolved to the window root and it docked to the whole UI instead of the conversation card; separately the TurnRail is a 24px absolutely-positioned overlay hugging the edge the user picked, and a fixed 14px inset landed right on it. The bar now hangs off `.gui-chat-body` (the chat column's own box), the offset parent is declared in our own stylesheet instead of a utility class, and a `ResizeObserver` **measures** the rail's footprint to publish the inset — no hardcoded margins.
- **修复会话跳转定位到错误轮次**：两轮会话若起始时间戳落在同一毫秒（顾问笔记与用户提问同刻、秒级精度的历史会话、乐观回显条目），导航条 / 消息树 / 轨迹 / ⌘F 查找都会滚到**更早**的那一轮 —— 这些路径都用时间戳作键，而查找一律取首个匹配。现改为用条目 id 作键；⌘F 这条链路的命中来自 daemon 消息表（不存条目 id），按命中文本消歧。
  - EN: Fixed jumps landing on the wrong turn. When two turns started within the same millisecond (an advisor note and the user's prompt in the same tick, second-precision history, optimistic echo rows), the turn rail, message tree, trajectory and ⌘F find all scrolled to the earlier turn — every one of those paths keyed on the timestamp, and every lookup took the first match. Jumps are now keyed on the entry id; the ⌘F path, whose hits come from the daemon message table (which stores no entry id), disambiguates by the matched text.
- **⌘F 统一为会话内查找，侧栏会话搜索移至 ⌘⇧F**：⌘F 查找栏上线后与侧栏搜索加速器冲突（两个 window 级监听各开各的），按出来的是侧栏搜索而不是会话内查找。现按以下语义分派：⌘F = 当前会话内查找（find-in-chat 注册表绑定），⌘⇧F = 侧栏会话搜索（tooltip 与快捷键同步更新），两者按 shift 修饰键区分、互不冒泡。
  - EN: ⌘F is now uniformly the in-chat find bar, and sidebar session search moved to ⌘⇧F. The find bar's launch conflicted with the sidebar search accelerator (two window-level listeners firing independently), so the sidebar search opened instead of the in-chat find. Dispatch now follows these semantics: ⌘F = find within the current conversation (find-in-chat registry binding), ⌘⇧F = sidebar session search (tooltip and accelerator updated together); the two are distinguished by the shift modifier and no longer collide.

- **删除会话级联清理子代理/顾问会话，直接删子会话不再复活**：子代理 transcript 在深度 3（`<slug>/<父文件基名>/<子id>.jsonl`），既有的深度 2 清理 glob 永远扫不到——删父会话残留其顾问/子会话行（日志与物化行幸存），直接删顾问会话删不干净、下次 session.list 重扫又复活。修法：① `deleteSession` 先经列表扫描收集目标——自身的精确 transcript 路径（任意深度）+ 按父 id 匹配的全部子会话，任一目标在流式中即整体拒绝（不做半吊子级联）；② 每个目标按精确路径删 transcript + 日志 + 暂停 sidecar + 物化行，父目录产物照旧递归清理；③ 父 id 推导收编为单一 `subagentParentIdOf`（列表嵌套与级联收集共用，防两处漂移）。5 例新契约测试（时间戳基名取 id / id 含下划线 / 深度 2 返回 null / 纯 slug 目录返回 null / 自身路径不自分）。
  - EN: Deleting a session now cascades to its subagent/advisor sessions, and deleting a child directly no longer resurrects it. Subagent transcripts live at depth 3 (`<slug>/<parent-file-base>/<subId>.jsonl`), invisible to the existing depth-2 cleanup glob — deleting a parent left its advisor/child rows behind (journals and materialized rows survived), and deleting an advisor directly never removed its transcript, so the next session.list re-scan brought it back. Fixes: ① deleteSession first collects targets via the listing scan — the exact transcript path of the session itself (any depth) plus every child matched by parent id; if ANY target is streaming the whole cascade is refused (no half cascades); ② each target loses its exact-path transcript + journal + pause sidecar + materialized row, with the parent's artifacts dir still recursive-cleaned as before; ③ parent-id derivation is consolidated into a single subagentParentIdOf shared by the list nesting and the cascade collection, so the two cannot drift. 5 new contract tests (timestamp base yields id / id with underscores / depth-2 returns null / plain slug dir returns null / self path not parented to itself).

- **轨迹分支树的悬停分支操作按节点类型分派「重答 / 切换」**：此前树行对所有节点只给一个「在此重答」按钮，但 `session.branchAt` 对 assistant/工具锚的语义是 leaf 直接落到该节点（= 纯切换，不回填、不重新回答）——标着"重答"点在旧分支的助手消息上实际只是把会话叶切过去，与地图修掉的假切换同源。现树行按 entry kind 分派：user 消息行只渲染「在此重答」（定位父级 + 回填草稿），assistant/工具行只渲染「切换到此分支」（leaf 落节点 + 关草稿回填 + 清遗留草稿，与地图右键同一语义）。3 例新契约测试（user 行只有重答 / assistant 行只有切换 / 工具结果行只有切换）。
  - EN: The trajectory branch tree's hover branch action is now dispatched by node type between "re-answer" and "switch". Previously every tree row offered only a "re-answer here" button, but session.branchAt on an assistant/tool anchor lands the leaf directly on that node — a pure switch with no draft backfill and no regeneration — so clicking "re-answer" on an old branch's assistant message merely moved the session leaf, the same fake-switch family the map fix addressed. Rows now dispatch on entry kind: user message rows show only "re-answer here" (position at parent + backfill draft), assistant/tool rows show only "switch to this branch" (leaf lands on the node + draft backfill disabled + leftover drafts cleared, same semantics as the map's context menu). 3 new contract tests (user row shows only re-answer / assistant row shows only switch / tool-result row shows only switch).

- **会话地图「切换到此分支」改用轮末事件为锚，事件卡支持单击选中/双击跳转/独立右键菜单**：此前切换锚误用轮首 user 消息——`session.branchAt` 对 user 消息定位到其父级并重答回填草稿，导致地图上怎么切都停在旧分支状态、输入框还残留旧草稿；现切换锚一律取轮末带 entryId 的 assistant/工具事件（leaf 落到该分支末尾，转录立即显示切到的分支），user 事件锚只保留给「在此重答」。事件卡从"单击即跳转"改为单击选中（kind 图标 + accent 选中态）、双击才跳转对话定位、右键菜单直接以该事件为锚（切换/重答/跳转/分叉不再只作用于整轮卡片）。`switchToNode` 新增 `backfillDraft` 选项，地图切换路径显式关回填并清掉上一轮重答遗留草稿。3 例新契约测试（事件卡 kind 图标渲染 / 选中态唯一 / 切换锚取轮末非 user 事件、重答锚取轮首 user 事件）。
  - EN: The session map's "switch to this branch" now anchors on the turn-end event, and event cards support click-to-select / double-click-to-jump / per-card context menu. The previous switch anchor wrongly used the turn's leading user message — session.branchAt answers a user message by positioning at its parent and refilling the draft, so switching on the map stayed stuck on the old branch state with a stale draft in the composer; the switch anchor is now always the last entryId-bearing assistant/tool event of the turn (the leaf lands at the branch tip and the transcript immediately shows the switched branch), while the user-event anchor is kept only for "re-answer here".
