  - EN: Capability center drops the legacy extensions tab + sidebar hub becomes a hover menu + own provider rebranded to MusePi Plugins. ① The capability center's "Extensions" tab is removed — legacy pi/omp extensions (.pi/.omp extensions, not under .musepi) are managed by the Settings "Extensions & plugins" control center instead, and the capability center no longer duplicates them; the tab union narrows to "Skills | Plugins | Marketplace", ExtensionsRuntimeTab is deleted with no dangling references repo-wide. ② The sidebar capability entry changes from "main button + chevron sub-button" to a hover menu — hovering the row pops three items (Skills / Plugins / Marketplace) jumping to the matching tab, while a direct click lands on the marketplace tab, with a 180ms delayed close tolerating mouse migration across the portal. ③ The own-plugin provider brand renames from "MusePi Extensions" to "MusePi Plugins" (the provider id `musepi-extensions` stays unchanged so existing data keeps working; the creator persona and musepi-help skill doc update in sync; the settings nav item "Extensions" becomes "Extensions & plugins").

- **终端后端可插拔：bun-pty / node-pty 注册为可替换 provider**：新增 `daemon/terminal-registry.ts`——TerminalRegistry（owner-scoped registerBackend，结构化 `DUPLICATE_BACKEND`）：同类型重复注册 = 结构化 `DUPLICATE_BACKEND`（不允许覆盖式静默顶替）；反注册句柄 dispose 只在该注册仍是当前持有者时移除（被顶替的旧注册 dispose 不误删新 backend）；`getBackend` 未注册 = 结构化 `NO_BACKEND`——「终端后端已停用，原因：插件被禁用」归因（回退保护③）的真实数据源。`terminal-provider.ts` 静态 PROVIDERS 表改注册表驱动：`registerBuiltinTerminalBackends`（幂等，builtin bun-pty/node-pty 注册进给定注册表）+ `getTerminalProvider(name, registry)`——显式 provider 的 open 直查注册表，backend 缺席 = NO_BACKEND（manifest 显式声明严格语义，不静默回退）；auto 只在在册 backend 间按 bun-pty → node-pty 回退、跳过缺席者，全缺席 = NO_BACKEND。provider 解析策略（manifest > settings > auto）与 RPC 名、`isTerminalProvider` 取值域（含 "auto"）、TerminalHandle 形状逐点不变。`TerminalService` 增 `opts.registry` 注入点（缺省回落共享注册表）；server.ts 构造独立注册表、注册 builtin 后端，经 `terminal-host-wiring` 插件把 `terminal:backends` / `terminal:deps` 两键 provide 进 cordis 根 Context（terminal-core 插件化时的 inject 落点，strangler-fig 薄适配层），同一实例注入服务。契约测试 9 例（DUPLICATE/NO_BACKEND 结构化、反注册交错安全、builtin 注册幂等、显式缺席不静默回退、auto 在册回退与全缺席）。模型面终端工具核对结论：BUILTIN_TOOLS 无终端工具可挪（bash-interactive 是 bash 工具的 TUI 辅助），tool-terminal 插件（spawn/send/read/list/signal）留待 terminal-core 插件化刀。
  - EN: Terminal backends are now pluggable: bun-pty and node-pty register as swappable providers. New `daemon/terminal-registry.ts` — TerminalRegistry (owner-scoped registerBackend, structured `DUPLICATE_BACKEND`): re-registering the same type is a structured `DUPLICATE_BACKEND` (no silent override); the unregister handle's dispose removes only while that registration is still the current holder (a superseded registration's dispose never deletes the new backend); an unregistered `getBackend` is a structured `NO_BACKEND` — the real data source for the "terminal backend stopped: plugin X disabled" attribution (fallback protection ③). `terminal-provider.ts`'s static PROVIDERS map is now registry-driven: `registerBuiltinTerminalBackends` (idempotent — registers the builtin bun-pty/node-pty into a given registry) + `getTerminalProvider(name, registry)` — an explicit provider's open queries the registry directly, a missing backend is NO_BACKEND (strict manifest semantics, no silent fallback); auto degrades only among registered backends in bun-pty → node-pty order, skipping absent ones, and is NO_BACKEND when all are absent. Provider resolution (manifest > settings > auto), RPC names, `isTerminalProvider`'s value domain (including "auto"), and the TerminalHandle shape are unchanged point-by-point. `TerminalService` gains an `opts.registry` injection point (falling back to the shared registry); server.ts constructs a dedicated registry, registers the builtin backends, provides the `terminal:backends` / `terminal:deps` keys into the cordis root Context via a `terminal-host-wiring` plugin (the inject landing point for the future terminal-core pluginization, strangler-fig thin adapter), and injects the same instance into the service. Nine contract tests (structured DUPLICATE/NO_BACKEND, unregister interleaving safety, idempotent builtin registration, explicit-missing no-silent-fallback, auto fallback among registered and all-absent). Model-facing terminal tool audit: BUILTIN_TOOLS has no terminal tools to move (bash-interactive is a TUI helper of the bash tool); the tool-terminal plugin (spawn/send/read/list/signal) waits for the terminal-core pluginization cut.

- **创造模式自举工具环自动激活（creator 预设 settings 轴 + `tools.extensionMetaTools`）+ 扩展开发技能补 cordis 运行时**：回答「创造模式工具集是否完备」的落地缺口——自举工具本就有 11 个（`extension_load/reload/status/validate/rollback` 生命周期五件套 + `ext_define/run/stop/undefine/inspect` 会话级动态插件，加常激活的 `extensions_list`；实现 `daemon/extension-lifecycle-tools.ts` / `daemon/extension-runtime-tools.ts`），但此前激活唯一入口是 `/extensions` 斜杠命令，creator 预设不自激活，且实证发现 defaultInactive custom 工具初始只挂为 xd:// 设备而非顶层工具。本轮：① 新隐藏设置键 `tools.extensionMetaTools`（settings-schema，默认 false）；② SDK 会话初始工具集组装消费该键——开启时 10 个 meta 工具从会话起即**顶层激活**（并钉住不被 xd:// 分区重新挂载），显式 `toolNames` 会话不扩列；③ creator 内置预设模板 `settings: { "tools.extensionMetaTools": true }` + persona 指向自举闭环（写文件 → 加载 → 自查 → 热修 → 回退），`BUILTIN_TEMPLATE_REVISION` 4→5，LEGACY_TEMPLATES 补 rev4 creator 字面量快照（未动过的旧 creator 预设安全升级链）；④ `musepi-extension-dev` 技能文档新增「自举工具环」「Cordis 插件运行时（组件/子 fiber/兼容性预检 + agentDir `compatibility.json` 精确版本豁免）」两节，并把「改扩展必须重启 daemon」修正为 HMR 热拔插现实（入口文件 ~500ms watcher 广播、GUI ~1s 刷新、reloadExtension 热载；子模块改动需 touch 入口；只有动发现/加载机制本身才重启）。契约测试 4 例（默认 xd:// 设备态、设置开启顶层激活且不进 xd 分区、显式 false 不激活、显式 toolNames 不扩列）。
  - EN: Creator preset auto-activates the extension bootstrap tool ring (creator preset settings axis + `tools.extensionMetaTools`) + the extension-dev skill gains the cordis runtime section. This lands the gap behind "is the creator mode toolset complete": the 11 bootstrap tools already existed (`extension_load/reload/status/validate/rollback` lifecycle quintet + `ext_define/run/stop/undefine/inspect` session-scoped dynamic plugins, plus the always-active `extensions_list`; implemented in `daemon/extension-lifecycle-tools.ts` / `daemon/extension-runtime-tools.ts`), but the only activation entry was the `/extensions` slash command, the creator preset did not self-activate, and empirically a defaultInactive custom tool initially mounts only as an xd:// device, not a top-level tool. This round: ① new hidden settings key `tools.extensionMetaTools` (settings-schema, default false); ② the SDK session-start tool-set assembly consumes it — when on, the 10 meta tools are **top-level active from session start** (pinned so the xd:// partition cannot remount them), and explicit-`toolNames` sessions are not widened; ③ the builtin creator preset template gains `settings: { "tools.extensionMetaTools": true }` + a persona pointing at the bootstrap loop (write file → load → self-check → hotfix → rollback), `BUILTIN_TEMPLATE_REVISION` bumps 4→5 with a rev4 creator literal snapshot in LEGACY_TEMPLATES (untouched old creator presets upgrade safely); ④ the `musepi-extension-dev` skill doc gains "Bootstrap tool ring" and "Cordis plugin runtime (components / child fibers / compatibility preflight + agentDir `compatibility.json` exact-version exemption)" sections, and the "extension changes require a daemon restart" claim is corrected to the HMR hot-plug reality (entry-file changes: ~500ms watcher broadcast, ~1s GUI refresh, reloadExtension hot-load; submodule edits need the entry touched; only changes to the discovery/loading mechanism itself need a restart). Four contract tests (default xd:// device state, setting-on top-level activation without xd partitioning, explicit false stays off, explicit toolNames not widened).

- **插件兼容性预检 + 精确版本豁免**：插件装载前三入口统一预检——宿主 runtime `load`/`loadAll`、会话装配 `loadExtensions`、marketplace `installPlugin`——读 package.json `peerDependencies`，`@musepi/*` peer 对照运行时 VERSION 判定（平台单版本线语义；`workspace:^/~/*` 按运行时版本解析；非法/空区间 = 结构化拒绝——Bun 1.4.2 实测 `Bun.semver.satisfies` 对非法区间与裸 `^` 都返回 true，判定带 order 语法探针 + 数字字面量双重防护；预发布运行时按 base 版本复检，等价 includePrerelease 意图）。拒绝 = 结构化 `DynamicExtensionLoadError`（新 code `incompatible-version`/`incompatible-peer`/`malformed-manifest`）+ failed 记录 + 会话装配侧 errors[] 带诊断且**先于 import**（拒绝路径零副作用）；`extensions.list` 透出 version/compatibility 证据面。豁免登记面 = **agentDir 下用户可编辑的 `compatibility.json`**（命名 profile 时天然按 profile 隔离）：精确 `name@version` → 被豁免运行时版本列表，授予必须 `acceptRisk` 显式知情且只能批当前运行时版本，原子写 0o600，读取 fail-safe（坏文件 = 零豁免不阻塞装配），含被拒记录时拒绝重写保护用户手工内容；新 RPC `extensions.setVersionExemption`（server.ts case + 路由表双向门禁覆盖）授予/撤销后失效清单缓存扇出 extensions.changed；list 对在役记录的 exempted 标注对照当前清单复核，撤销后管理面讲当下真话（降级 incompatible，不热卸载在役 fiber）。GUI 详情弹层新增兼容性归因段——incompatible 红块（未满足 peer 清单 + 运行时版本 + 授予入口，液态玻璃 confirm 知情确认）、exempted 琥珀块（可撤销），豁免键与判定同源用 manifest name@version。builtin 插件不做预检（随宿主同版本发布）。契约测试 25 例（纯判定/登记面 19 + 会话装配预检 2 + 宿主整链 4，含预发布复检、垃圾区间防护、撤销后标注降级、拒绝零副作用探针）。
  - EN: Plugin compatibility preflight + exact-version exemptions. All three load entry points — host runtime `load`/`loadAll`, session assembly `loadExtensions`, marketplace `installPlugin` — read package.json `peerDependencies` and check `@musepi/*` peers against the runtime VERSION (single-version-line platform semantics; `workspace:^/~/*` resolve to the runtime version; invalid/empty ranges are structured refusals — Bun 1.4.2's `Bun.semver.satisfies` returns true for garbage ranges and a bare `^`, so the check layers an order() syntax probe plus a digit-literal guard; prerelease runtimes re-check against their base version, equivalent to includePrerelease intent). A refusal is a structured `DynamicExtensionLoadError` (new codes `incompatible-version`/`incompatible-peer`/`malformed-manifest`) + a failed record +, on the session-assembly side, an errors[] entry with diagnosis **before any import** (refused paths have zero side effects); `extensions.list` surfaces version/compatibility evidence. The exemption registry is a **user-editable `compatibility.json` under agentDir** (naturally per-profile under named profiles): exact `name@version` keys map to exempted runtime-version lists, granting requires explicit `acceptRisk` and only approves the current runtime version, writes are atomic at 0o600, reads are fail-safe (a broken file means zero exemptions and never blocks assembly), and files containing rejected records refuse rewriting to protect hand-edited content; the new `extensions.setVersionExemption` RPC (server.ts case + bidirectional route-coverage gate) invalidates the list cache and fans out extensions.changed after grant/revoke; the list re-checks in-service exempted annotations against the live registry so the management plane tells current truth after a revocation (downgraded to incompatible without hot-unloading the in-service fiber). The GUI detail dialog gains a compatibility attribution block — a red block for incompatible (unmet peer list + runtime version + grant entry behind a liquid-glass informed-confirm) and an amber block for exempted (revocable), with the exemption key sourced from the manifest name@version exactly as the gate computed it. Builtin plugins skip the preflight (shipped at the host version). 25 contract tests (19 pure/store + 2 session-assembly preflight + 4 host end-to-end, covering prerelease re-check, garbage-range guard, post-revoke annotation downgrade, and the refused-zero-side-effect probe).

- **插件清单里的组件可独立启停**：user 插件可在 package.json `musepi.components`（omp/pi 块同读）声明组件 `{id, description?, entry?}`——entry 组件挂为插件 fiber 下的**独立 cordis 子 fiber**（与插件入口同一 importAndBind 管线，ledgerApi owner 名 `<plugin>/<component>`，跨组件命令碰撞 = 组件 FAILED 层隔离）；entry 缺席的组件为只读展示项。`extensions.setComponentEnabled` 新增 user 插件径：校验声明 → 真实启停组件 fiber（停用 = dispose + 效果回收，启用 = 重挂）→ 成功才持久化隐藏键黑名单；运行时尚未 reconcile 时先装载再切换。`extensions.list` 组件面下发声明组件 + enabled + canToggle + 非 ACTIVE 时的 fiberState/effects 真实运行态；GUI 详情弹层组件行显示 fiber 状态行。组件命令宿主可调用（句柄 invoke = 主入口 ∪ 按声明序在役组件）。修一个真实生产 bug：黑名单键 `extensions.disabledComponents` 与 dotless `extensions` 数组键点号嵌套冲突，写一次即宿主扩展装载崩溃——改无点号键 `disabledExtensionComponents`。契约测试 +10（组件生命周期 6 + service 接线 4，含未声明组件拒绝不落盘、首次开关先装载再切换）。
  - EN: Components declared in a plugin manifest can be enabled and disabled on their own. User plugins can declare components in package.json `musepi.components` (also read from omp/pi blocks) as `{id, description?, entry?}` — entry components mount as **independent cordis child fibers** under the plugin fiber (same importAndBind pipeline as the plugin entry, ledgerApi owner named `<plugin>/<component>`, cross-component command collisions surface as component-level FAILED isolation); components without an entry are read-only display items. `extensions.setComponentEnabled` gains the user-plugin path: validate declaration → genuinely start/stop the component fiber (disable = dispose + effect reclamation, enable = remount) → persist the hidden-key denylist only on success; when the runtime hasn't reconciled yet, load first then toggle. `extensions.list` ships declared components + enabled + canToggle + real fiberState/effects when not ACTIVE; the GUI detail dialog shows a fiber-state line per component row. Component commands are host-callable (handle invoke = main entry ∪ in-service components in declaration order). Fixes a real production bug: the denylist key `extensions.disabledComponents` collided with the dotless `extensions` array key via dot-nesting, crashing host extension loading after a single write — renamed to the dotless `disabledExtensionComponents`. Ten new contract tests (6 component lifecycle + 4 service wiring, including undeclared-component rejection leaving no trace, first toggle loads then toggles).

- _*cordis 收编第二刀：user 插件宿主级 fiber 化装载（真实 ExtensionAPI 同源 + register* 效果账本）**：user 动态插件从「探测桩」升级为宿主级真实装载——每个 user 插件 = `musepi-dynamic-extensions` 组 fiber 下独立子 fiber，运行面与生产会话装载**同源_*（loader.ts 新增 `createConcreteExtensionAPI` 唯一构造工厂 + `importAndBindExtension` 同一条 import/bind 管线，bind 捕获 factory 抛错经新 `DynamicExtensionLoadError` 透传结构化 code）；26 个 register*/on 登记动词经 ledgerApi（Proxy 壳）逐条进 `ctx.effect` 效果账本（标签如 `registerCommand:xxx`），unload/reload = fiber.dispose() 按 verb 撤销表反向回收（命令摘除、事件退订、provider 排队注册注销，其他插件无损）；跨插件命令名碰撞守卫落地为 FAILED fiber（不留半挂载）；`loadAll` 对账 reconcile（新路径装载、消失路径卸载）返回与 `loadExtensions` 同形状结果，`extension-service.getExtensionRuntimeLoad` 改走 fiber 运行时（server.ts 注入，动态 import 避免 index 大环），失败 fail-soft 回退直接装载。HMR 沿用 loader 的单调 `?mtime=` 标签口径（spike 证伪裸查询串方案），reload 拾取入口与子模块改写实测通过。契约测试重写 10 例 + 接线测试 3 例（TTL 重入不重复登记、失效 reconcile 效果回收）。
  - EN: Cordis adoption, second cut: user plugins load as host-level fibers with a real ExtensionAPI from the same source + a register* effect ledger. User dynamic plugins upgrade from probe stubs to real host-level loading — each user plugin is an independent child fiber under the `musepi-dynamic-extensions` group, sharing the exact runtime surface with production session loading (loader.ts gains `createConcreteExtensionAPI` as the single construction factory + `importAndBindExtension` on the same import/bind pipeline, with factory throws surfacing a structured code via the new `DynamicExtensionLoadError`); all 26 register*/on verbs pass through a ledgerApi (Proxy shell) into per-entry `ctx.effect` ledger entries (labels like `registerCommand:xxx`), and unload/reload = fiber.dispose() reversing each verb's undo table (commands removed, events unsubscribed, queued provider registrations cancelled, other plugins unaffected); cross-plugin command-name collisions surface as a FAILED fiber (no half-mounted state); `loadAll` reconciles the discovery manifest against in-service records (new paths loaded, vanished paths unloaded) returning the same shape as `loadExtensions`, and `extension-service.getExtensionRuntimeLoad` switches to the fiber runtime (injected from server.ts via dynamic import to avoid the index cycle), with fail-soft fallback to direct loading. HMR reuses the loader's monotonic `?mtime=` tag mechanism (the spike disproved raw query-string busting), with reload picking up entry and submodule edits verified live. Contract suite rewritten to 10 cases + 3 wiring cases (no duplicate registration on TTL re-entry, effect reclamation on invalidation reconcile).

- **侧边栏能力入口合并为 WorkBuddy 式单按钮枢纽**：能力中心与扩展中心两个并列导航项合并为一行——主点击进能力中心，尾部 chevron 展开下拉菜单直达「技能 / 插件 / 扩展 / 市场」四个标签页（复用全局玻璃菜单配方，下拉项走 gui-view-opt 行样式）；`omp-open-capability` 命令接受目标 tab 参数。能力中心内新增「扩展」标签页（`ExtensionsRuntimeTab`）：列出用户/项目级 runtime 扩展模块，复用插件详情弹层与启停开关。新增 4 个双语词条（capability hub open / extensions runtime 等）。
  - EN: Sidebar capability entries merge into a WorkBuddy-style single-button hub: the side-by-side CapabilityCenter and ExtensionsCenter nav items become one row — the main click opens CapabilityCenter, a trailing chevron drops a menu with direct jumps to the "Skills / Plugins / Extensions / Marketplace" tabs (reusing the global glass menu recipe, items rendered as gui-view-opt rows); the `omp-open-capability` command accepts a target tab parameter. CapabilityCenter gains an "Extensions" tab (`ExtensionsRuntimeTab`) listing user/project-level runtime extension modules, reusing the plugin detail dialog and enable/disable toggle. Four new bilingual entries (capability hub open / extensions runtime, etc.).

- **会话列表行内展开子代理名册**：`session.list` 逐行返回 `agentCount`（live 会话取运行中句柄的 sessionFile，历史会话回退列表项路径），AgentRegistry 按产物根目录前缀过滤（排除 advisor，与 `registerPersistedSubagents` 的注册目录约定同源）；新 RPC `session.agents` 返回名册（id/显示名/kind/状态/最近活动）。GUI 侧会话行 agentCount>0 时出现 chevron（图标+数量），展开内联名册（状态点+名称+活动摘要），点击行进所属会话；custom group、分组列表、noFolder 全部分支均已接线，5s 轮询同步名册刷新。路由覆盖门禁同步登记（route-coverage 反向断言已含）。
  - EN: Session rows expand an inline sub-agent roster: `session.list` returns per-row `agentCount` (live sessions read the running handle's sessionFile, historical ones fall back to the list entry's path), with AgentRegistry filtered by artifact-root prefix (advisors excluded, same directory convention as `registerPersistedSubagents`); the new `session.agents` RPC returns the roster (id / display name / kind / status / last activity). On the GUI side, rows with agentCount>0 show a chevron (icon + count), expanding an inline roster (status dot + name + activity summary), and clicking an entry enters the owning session; all branches — custom groups, grouped lists, noFolder — are wired, and the 5s poll syncs roster refreshes. The route-coverage gate registers the route (already covered by the reverse assertion).

- **移除智能体中心页面**：侧边栏「智能体中心」导航项、CommandPalette 入口、app.tsx 的 agentsOpen 视图状态与相关 props 全摘——活跃子代理管理由会话内 SubagentPanel 与右栏承担，名册查看由会话行展开承担；`AgentsCenterPage.tsx` 删除。会话上下文面板的 SubagentPanel 不受影响。
  - EN: Agents center page removed: the sidebar "Agents Center" nav item, CommandPalette entry, app.tsx agentsOpen view state and related props are all gone — active sub-agent management lives in the in-chat SubagentPanel and right rail, roster viewing in session-row expansion; `AgentsCenterPage.tsx` is deleted. The SubagentPanel used by the session context panel is unaffected.

- **插件管理页新增「运行状态 / 启用条件」**：① daemon 启动时把 builtin 注册表全部非 annotate 单元挂为 cordis builtin 插件组（`musepi-builtin-plugins`）下的独立子 fiber——与 user 动态插件同一装配语法、不同信任级（设计稿 §1 第 3 步）；每个 fiber apply 提供 `builtin:<id>` 键（builtin 宿主级 inject 落点），`ctx.provide` 进效果账本，单单元装载失败层隔离（回退保护①）记 FAILED 归因不拖垮组。② `extensions.list` 新增真实 runtime 面：`fiberState`（cordis fiber 状态机）+ `effects`（效果账本计数）+ `operational`（镜像设置现读的业务运行判定——stt.enabled=false 时语音确实没在跑，与 fiber ACTIVE 两层如实分开）；builtinRuntime 宿主依赖注入，缺省 {} fail-soft。③ 启用条件（activation）落地为真实可判定语义：镜像设置键（`stt.enabled` 开启时启用），预设提供的 user 插件走 enabledInPresets——不编造 cordis 表达式字符串。④ 插件详情弹窗 CardFacts 加「运行状态」（液态玻璃质感圆点 + 运行中/已停用 + 非 ACTIVE 时附 fiber 状态名）与「启用条件」两行，仅当有真实数据时渲染；user 插件会话装载、宿主无 fiber，如实不显示。契约测试 +7（builtin 组挂载/检视/provide 解析/dispose 清空/局部 defs + runtime/activation 三态）。
  - EN: Plugin management gained a "runtime status / activation condition" surface. ① At daemon startup every non-annotate builtin registry unit mounts as an independent child fiber under the cordis builtin plugin group (`musepi-builtin-plugins`) — same assembly syntax as user dynamic plugins, different trust level (design doc §1 step 3); each fiber's apply provides the `builtin:<id>` key (the host-level inject landing point), `ctx.provide` lands on the effect ledger, and a single unit's mount failure is layer-isolated (fallback protection ①) into a FAILED attribution without dragging down the group. ② `extensions.list` gains a real runtime plane: `fiberState` (cordis fiber state machine) + `effects` (effect ledger count) + `operational` (a live read of the mirrored setting — with stt.enabled=false voice genuinely isn't running, kept honestly separate from the ACTIVE fiber, closer to our architecture than a raw fiberPhase); builtinRuntime is a host-injected dependency, defaulting to {} fail-soft. ③ Activation conditions land as genuinely decidable semantics: the mirrored settings key ("enabled while stt.enabled is on"), with preset-provided user plugins covered by enabledInPresets — no fabricated cordis expression strings. ④ The plugin detail dialog's CardFacts gains "Runtime status" (liquid-glass dot + Running/Stopped, plus the fiber state name when not ACTIVE) and "Activation condition" rows, rendered only when real data exists; user plugins load per session with no host fiber and honestly show neither. Seven new contract tests (builtin group mount/inspect/provide resolution/dispose clearing/partial defs + three runtime/activation states).

- **语音设置页随插件启停变化；插件详情补齐信息卡与面板内搜索**：① 语音设置页由插件状态驱动——voice:stt / voice:tts 被停用后，对应 schema 行、模型选择卡、测试入口整段隐藏并替换为「插件已停用」说明卡（一键启用回环写 extensions.setEnabled），fail-open：列表不可用时按启用渲染。② `extensions.list` 新增「会话插件」轴——解析每个 Agent 预设，只有显式扩展白名单（extensionsExplicit 三态语义，undefined = 全部启用不构成「按预设提供」）的预设才算提供者：返回顶层预设清单（内置预设名走 daemon i18n 回退检测）+ 每个 extension-module 条目的 enabledInPresets「启用于」标签；modesDir 改为宿主依赖注入（缺省回退 $MUSEPI_MODES_DIR / ~/.musepi/modes，测试可隔离），fail-soft 不阻塞清单主数据。③ 插件详情弹窗（PluginDetailDialog）状态段后新增一组 `<dl>` 信息行——完整名称（code id）/配置状态（含「由 Agent 预设按会话提供」）/启用于（预设标签 join）。④ 插件面板（UnifiedPluginsView）工具栏加搜索框（名称/id/描述大小写不敏感过滤 + 无结果提示），模块行/卡片挂预设提供者标签。契约测试 +2（白名单命中挂标签、未命中/无显式声明不进清单）。
  - EN: The voice settings page now follows plugin state, and plugin details gained fact cards plus in-panel search. ① The voice settings page is plugin-state-driven — with voice:stt / voice:tts disabled, the matching schema rows, model picker cards, and test entries collapse into a "plugin disabled" note card (one-click enable loops through extensions.setEnabled); fail-open: the page renders as enabled when the list is unavailable. ② `extensions.list` gains a "session plugins" axis — each Agent preset is resolved, and only presets with an explicit extension whitelist count as providers (extensionsExplicit tri-state semantics: undefined = everything enabled, not "provided by preset"): the response gains a top-level preset list (builtin preset names resolved via daemon i18n with fallback detection) plus an enabledInPresets "enabled in" tag on each extension-module entry; modesDir becomes a host-injected dependency (falling back to $MUSEPI_MODES_DIR / ~/.musepi/modes, isolatable in tests) and the plane is fail-soft, never blocking the main list. ③ The plugin detail dialog (PluginDetailDialog) gains a `<dl>` fact block after the status section — full name (code id) / configuration status (including "provided per session by an Agent preset") / enabled in (preset labels joined). ④ The plugins panel (UnifiedPluginsView) toolbar gains a search box (case-insensitive filter over name/id/description + empty-state hint), and module rows/cards show preset-provider tags. Two new contract tests (whitelist hit attaches the tag; misses / non-explicit presets stay out of the plane).

- **插件详情弹层中文化 + 语音单元模型选择器卡片化 + 语音引擎组件真实启停 + 能力/扩展中心形态分工**：① 详情弹层（PluginDetailDialog）整体中文化——wire 契约的 config 字段新增 `label`/`optionLabels`（plugin-config.ts fail-soft 解析），ConfigFormRenderer 经 `tLoose` 解析 label/description/optionLabels，builtin 覆盖表导出共享；voice:stt/tts 的模型与音色字段从通用表单剔除，内嵌与设置页**同一 SpeechModelPicker 卡片组件**（同一下载通道/存储，零漂移），设置页导航误写成「音色」修正为「语音」（runtime 注册键走 tLoose）。② 语音插件声明式「包含的组件」：voice:stt 声明 whisper/sensevoice/parakeet 引擎组件、voice:tts 声明 kokoro/melotts-zh 组件，组件结构升级为 `{id, deny: "tool"|"stt-engine"|"tts-engine"}`；新隐藏设置键 `voice.disabledEngines` + `voice/engine-denylist.ts`（sttEngineComponentId/ttsEngineComponentId/readDisabledVoiceEngines/isVoiceEngineDisabled/enabled*Models/resolveEnabled*Model），extension-service 双通道 list/setComponentEnabled，消费点全接通——stt/tts modelStatus 过滤目录+默认回退、modelDownload 拒绝、stt.transcribe、tts.synthesize、vocalizer 会话、stt-controller，**被禁引擎从模型选择（设置页选择器同读此 RPC 自动联动）、下载、转写/合成路径同时消失**。契约测试 14 例（含 voice 通道写 voice.disabledEngines 与 tools 正交、禁用引擎真实改变模型解析与回退链）。③ 能力中心保持管理导向列表（行内组件计数徽标），扩展中心切发现导向卡片网格，设置页插件分区复用同一 ModuleCard——单一权威组件三处共用。
  - EN: Plugin detail dialog localization + voice-unit model picker cards + real voice-engine component toggles + capability/extensions center layout split. ① The detail dialog (PluginDetailDialog) is fully localized — the wire config contract gains `label`/`optionLabels` (fail-soft parsing in plugin-config.ts), ConfigFormRenderer resolves label/description/optionLabels via `tLoose`, and the builtin display-name table is exported for sharing; voice:stt/tts drop the generic form fields for model/voice and embed the **same SpeechModelPicker card component as the settings page** (same download channel/storage, zero drift), and the settings nav mislabeled "voice timbre" is corrected to "Voice" (runtime-registered keys go through tLoose). ② Voice plugins declare "included components" in their manifest: voice:stt declares the whisper/sensevoice/parakeet engine components and voice:tts the kokoro/melotts-zh components, with the component shape upgraded to `{id, deny: "tool"|"stt-engine"|"tts-engine"}`; a new hidden settings key `voice.disabledEngines` plus `voice/engine-denylist.ts` (sttEngineComponentId/ttsEngineComponentId/readDisabledVoiceEngines/isVoiceEngineDisabled/enabled*Models/resolveEnabled*Model), dual-channel list/setComponentEnabled in extension-service, and all consumers wired — stt/tts modelStatus filters catalogs + default fallback, modelDownload rejects, stt.transcribe, tts.synthesize, vocalizer sessions, stt-controller: **a disabled engine disappears from model selection (the settings-page picker reads the same RPC and syncs automatically), download, and transcription/synthesis paths simultaneously**. Fourteen contract tests (including the voice channel writing voice.disabledEngines orthogonally to tools, and disabled engines genuinely changing model resolution and fallback chains). ③ CapabilityCenter keeps its management-oriented list (inline component-count badge), ExtensionsCenter switches to a discovery-oriented card grid, and the settings plugins section reuses the same ModuleCard — one authoritative component shared by all three.

- **插件管理页改版：列表布局、详情玻璃弹层、设置卡片分区**：插件页（扩展中心与能力中心共用）改为分组列表布局——顶栏计数 + 「添加插件」按钮、官方/已安装分组标题、行内版本号与组件计数；点击行不再手风琴展开，而是弹出**液态玻璃详情弹层**（`PluginDetailDialog`，DialogFrame 承载：状态段/触发词/来源 + 配置表单 + 资源卡，弹层本身日后可插件化）；设置页新增「插件」卡片分区（`PluginsSection`，两列卡片网格，点卡片开同款弹层），`settings-nav` 注册 plugins 分区（NAV_IDS 测试同步）。新增 10 个双语词条（ext plugins official/installed、ext add plugin、ext plugin components、ext component denied、ext plugin details、ext plugins settings desc 等），i18n 查重通过。
  - EN: Plugin management was reworked: list layout, a glass detail dialog, and sectioned settings cards. The Plugins tab (shared by ExtensionsCenter and CapabilityCenter) switches to a grouped list layout — header count + "Add plugin" button, Official/Installed group headers, inline version and component counts; clicking a row no longer accordion-expands but opens a **liquid-glass detail dialog** (`PluginDetailDialog`, hosted in DialogFrame: status / triggers / source + config form + resource card; the dialog itself can be plugin-ized later); the settings page gains a "Plugins" card section (`PluginsSection`, two-column card grid, same dialog on card click) with a plugins section registered in `settings-nav` (NAV_IDS test updated). Ten new bilingual entries (ext plugins official/installed, ext add plugin, ext plugin components, ext component denied, ext plugin details, ext plugins settings desc, …), dup-key check green.

- **插件「包含的组件」可真实启停**：builtin 注册表单元可声明 `components`（browser/computer/lsp 各声明自身 tool 组件）；`extensions.list` 挂组件状态（enabled/disabledReason: "tools-denied"）；新增 `extensions.setComponentEnabled` RPC——校验组件已在注册表声明 → 读写隐藏设置键 `tools.disabled`（settings-schema 黑名单数组）→ flush → 失效缓存 + onChanged 扇出；工具准入谓词 `isToolAllowed` 最前消费黑名单，**组件开关对 agent 是真实禁用而非展示层**。契约测试 3 例（状态挂载、开关闭环 + 与包级开关正交、未声明拒绝不留痕）。terminal/voice 不声明组件（不发明语义）。
  - EN: The "included components" listed under a plugin can now actually be turned on and off. Builtin registry units may declare `components` (browser/computer/lsp each declare their own tool component); `extensions.list` attaches component state (enabled / disabledReason: "tools-denied"); the new `extensions.setComponentEnabled` RPC validates the component is registry-declared → reads/writes the hidden settings key `tools.disabled` (a settings-schema denylist array) → flushes → invalidates caches + onChanged fan-out; the tool gate predicate `isToolAllowed` consumes the denylist first, so component toggles genuinely disable tools for the agent, not just the UI. Three contract tests (state attachment, toggle loop + orthogonality to package toggles, undeclared rejection leaves no trace). terminal/voice declare no components (no invented semantics).

- **GUI 硬编码文案 AST 门禁**：新门禁 `scripts/verify-client-ui-i18n.ts` 用 @babel/parser AST 扫描 desktop-app/client-core 源（排除 i18n 域与 vendor），把 JSX 文本、文案承载属性（label/placeholder/aria-label/…）、文案命名变量/属性/返回值里的硬编码中英句子钉在 CI 必经路径（root `check:tools` + `test:scripts` spec 11 例）。ratchet 基线 `verify-client-ui-i18n-baseline.json` 记录 106 文件 412 条存量债，只能减不能增，未登记文件零配额；单字符非 CJK 符号（版本号 "v" 前缀等）不算文案。这是此前两次「i18n 手滑漏翻/重复」事故的结构性对策，与 check-i18n-dup-keys 互补（一个管词典、一个管引用侧）。
  - EN: Hard-coded GUI copy AST gate. The new gate `scripts/verify-client-ui-i18n.ts` AST-scans desktop-app/client-core sources with @babel/parser (excluding the i18n domains and vendor), pinning hard-coded Chinese/English sentences in JSX text, copy-bearing attributes (label/placeholder/aria-label/…), and copy-named variables/properties/returns onto the mandatory CI path (root `check:tools` + 11 spec cases in `test:scripts`). The ratchet baseline `verify-client-ui-i18n-baseline.json` records 412 legacy violations across 106 files — debt can only shrink, unlisted files have zero quota; single non-CJK glyphs (the "v" version prefix, etc.) don't count as copy. This is the structural answer to the two past "slipped i18n translation/duplicate" incidents, complementing check-i18n-dup-keys (one guards the dictionaries, the other the reference side).

- **终端/浏览器/电脑控制/LSP 登记为内置插件单元**：builtin 注册表在语音之后补齐四个子系统——`terminal:terminal`（**只读展示**：无总开关语义，不发明——终端面板的启停由终端面板自身 UI 决定，配置项 terminal.provider 后端选择/terminal.showImages/terminal.showProgress 照常可改）、`browser:browser`（开关镜像 `browser.enabled`）、`computer:computer`（开关镜像 `computer.enabled`，schema 默认 false，新增 `unsetDisabled` 镜像语义：设置从未写入时如实上报 disabled）、`lsp:lsp`（开关镜像 `lsp.enabled`）。四单元全部带声明式配置表单，字段键即设置键，与语音单元同一套 list/setConfig 契约；GUI 侧新增 5 个 kind 标签（语音/终端/浏览器/电脑控制/LSP）双语显示名与描述，插件页行内展开即可开关+配置，**只读条目不渲染失效开关**（此前 readonly 项会显示一个点了没反应的死开关）。
  - EN: Terminal / browser / computer-use / LSP registered as builtin plugin units. The builtin registry gains four subsystems after voice — `terminal:terminal` (**read-only display**: no master-switch semantics, nothing invented — the terminal panel's own UI owns its visibility, while config fields terminal.provider / terminal.showImages / terminal.showProgress stay editable), `browser:browser` (toggle mirrors `browser.enabled`), `computer:computer` (toggle mirrors `computer.enabled`, schema default false, with a new `unsetDisabled` mirror semantic: an untouched setting reports disabled truthfully), and `lsp:lsp` (toggle mirrors `lsp.enabled`). All four carry declarative config forms whose field keys are the settings keys, on the same list/setConfig contract as the voice units; the GUI adds bilingual kind tags (Voice / Terminal / Browser / Computer Use / LSP) plus descriptions, with toggle + config in the Plugins tab inline expansion, and **read-only entries no longer render a dead toggle** (previously a readonly row showed a switch that did nothing when clicked).

- **语音子系统插件化登记**：语音输入/播报首次以插件身份进入插件页与能力清单——`voice:stt`（开关镜像 `stt.enabled`）与 `voice:tts`（开关镜像 `speech.enabled`，vocalizer 每次推送本就现读该键）两个内置单元带 **声明式配置表单**：STT 模型档位/语言提示/静音阈值/提交触发，TTS 本地模型/音色/语速（0.5–2 夹取）/朗读内容模式/自动朗读。关键设计：**字段键即设置键**——`extensions.list` 挂注册表声明的 config + 从 settings 现读的 configValues，`extensions.setConfig` 按声明钳制后经 settings 落盘，插件管理表单与设置页语音分区同一条存储、零漂移；未声明键拒绝、越界夹取、changed 扇出与插件包配置同一契约。GUI 侧新 kind「语音」标签 + 双语显示名/描述（`ext builtin stt/tts [desc]`），插件 tab 行内展开即可开关+配置。契约测试 5 例（list 挂字段+设置值、setConfig 落盘回读闭环、越界夹取、未声明键拒绝不留痕、setEnabled 镜像总开关状态翻转）。
  - EN: Voice subsystem registered as plugins. Speech input and readback enter the Plugins tab and capability list for the first time — two builtin units, `voice:stt` (toggle mirrors `stt.enabled`) and `voice:tts` (toggle mirrors `speech.enabled`, which the vocalizer already re-reads on every push), each carrying a **declarative config form**: STT model tier / language hint / silence threshold / submit trigger; TTS local model / voice / rate (clamped 0.5–2) / read-content mode / auto-read. Key design: **the field key IS the settings key** — `extensions.list` attaches the registry-declared config plus live settings-backed configValues, and `extensions.setConfig` clamps against the declaration and persists via settings, so the plugin management form and the settings page voice section share one storage with zero divergence; undeclared keys are rejected, out-of-range values clamped, and the changed fan-out follows the same contract as plugin-package config. GUI gains the "Voice" kind tag + bilingual display names/descriptions (`ext builtin stt/tts [desc]`), with toggle + config in the Plugins tab inline expansion. Five contract tests (list attaches fields + setting values, setConfig persist-and-reread loop, out-of-range clamping, undeclared-key rejection leaves no trace, setEnabled mirror flips the master switch state).

- **插件详情可行内展开；内置插件文案补齐中英双语**：插件 tab（扩展中心与能力中心共用 `UnifiedPluginsView`）的模块行现在可点击整行头手风琴展开详情——状态（含加载失败制胜相位/遮蔽归因）、触发词、来源与路径，以及清单声明的**配置表单**（乐观写入 + restart 生效时机提示 + fail-soft 丢弃警告）与**资源占用卡**；原 `PluginManifestSections` 组件从 ExtensionsCenter 迁入 UnifiedPluginsView 成为单一权威，能力清单 detail pane 反向引用，杜绝双份漂移；条目状态标签（`pluginStateLabel`）同理收归一处。builtin 注册表 7 个内置插件单元（蜂群任务卡片/桌壳/三个魔术关键词/内置主题包/工具卡片渲染器）的硬编码英文 displayName/description 由 GUI 侧 i18n 覆盖（`ext builtin <name>[ desc]` 键，未命中回退原文，不改 daemon 避免 TUI/GUI 分叉），zh/en 双语词条补齐。术语收口：插件相关文案统一「插件」（扩展模块→插件模块、"已发现的扩展插件列表"→"已发现的插件列表"、空态与设置页描述同步），能力清单保持「能力」。
  - EN: Plugin details expand inline, and builtin plugin copy is now bilingual. Module rows in the Plugins tab (ExtensionsCenter and CapabilityCenter share `UnifiedPluginsView`) now expand an accordion detail on row-header click — status (load-failure winning phase / shadowing attribution), trigger, source and path, plus manifest-declared **config forms** (optimistic writes + restart-timing hints + fail-soft dropped-field warnings) and the **resource footprint card**. The `PluginManifestSections` component moves from ExtensionsCenter into UnifiedPluginsView as the single authority, with the capability-list detail pane importing it back — no more duplicated drift; the entry state label (`pluginStateLabel`) is centralized the same way. The builtin registry's 7 units (swarm task card / desktop shell / three magic keywords / built-in theme pack / tool card renderers) get GUI-side i18n coverage over their hardcoded English displayName/description (`ext builtin <name>[ desc]` keys, falling back to the original text when missing; daemon untouched to avoid TUI/GUI divergence), with zh/en entries added. Terminology: plugin-facing copy unifies on "plugin" (extension module → plugin module, "discovered extension plugins" → "discovered plugins", empty-state and settings descriptions aligned) while the capability list keeps "capability".

- **工具注册表时间线升级：模型可见注入**：地基从「纯 UI 标记」升级为完整的通知语义——agent-loop 在 sync 之后、组请求之前比对 wire 工具名，有 diff 时①立即把 developer 角色通知（`formatToolRegistryNotice` 单一文本权威：`Tool added: X` / `Tool removed: Y` 逐行）注入当前上下文，**模型在携带新工具集的同一请求即知晓变化**（pi-ai 的 developer 角色全 provider 序列化，Kimi/OpenAI 家族落为 developer/system）；②会话树里的 `tool_registry` 条目不再被 session-context 排除，convertToLlm 的 custom 分支把它重建为同文本 developer 消息——live 注入与 resume/压缩重建逐字一致，重建后模型不丢工具集知识。前缀缓存只在追加处向前增长，无扰动。对 DeepSeek 原生 `tool_addition`/`tool_removal` wire 块按路由分化处理（不支持路由剥离 developer 消息只换声明表）在我们 OpenAI 兼容家族上由 developer 角色统一覆盖。契约测试 4 例（live 同请求到达、基线/无变化静默、重建同文本、空 diff 丢弃）。
  - EN: Tool-registry timeline upgrade: model-visible injection. The foundation upgrades from "pure UI marker" to full notification semantics — after sync and before assembling the request, the agent loop diffs wire tool names; on a diff it ① immediately injects a developer-role notice (`formatToolRegistryNotice` as the single text authority: one `Tool added: X` / `Tool removed: Y` line each) into the live context, so **the model learns the change in the very request that carries the new tool set** (pi-ai serializes the developer role across providers; the Kimi/OpenAI family lands it as developer/system), and ② the session tree's `tool_registry` entry is no longer excluded by session-context — convertToLlm's custom branch rebuilds it as the same developer message text, so live injection and resume/compaction rebuilds are byte-identical and the model never loses tool-set knowledge after a rebuild. The provider prefix cache only grows forward at the append point, zero disturbance. the route split for native `tool_addition`/`tool_removal` wire blocks for DeepSeek; strip-developer-and-swap-declarations for unsupported routes) is covered uniformly on our OpenAI-compatible family by the developer role. Four contract tests (live same-request delivery, baseline/no-change silence, rebuild identity, empty-diff drop).

- **工具注册表时间线地基**：agent-loop 现在逐模型请求比对实际携带的 wire 工具名快照——扩展/插件在两步请求之间被启停导致工具集增删时，diff 经新 `onToolRegistryChange` 回调（agent 公开 `addToolRegistryChangeListener`）上报；会话侧落 `custom_message` 条目（`tool_registry`：GUI/TUI 时间线渲染「工具已更新 +N/−M + 涉及工具名」注入行、轨迹视图映射 system 事件）。首个请求只建立基线不触发（初始工具集已由 session_init 记录）。模型可见注入随下一刀落地（见上条）。
  - EN: Tool-registry timeline foundation: the agent loop now snapshots the wire tool names actually carried by each model request — when an extension/plugin is toggled between steps and the tool set changes, the diff is reported via the new `onToolRegistryChange` callback (exposed on Agent as `addToolRegistryChangeListener`); the session journals a `custom_message` entry (`tool_registry`: GUI/TUI timelines render a "tools updated +N/−M + affected tool names" injection row, the trajectory view maps it to a system event). The first request of a run only establishes the baseline (the initial tool set is already recorded at session_init). Model-visible injection lands in the follow-up cut (see above).

- **插件页与能力清单口径统一（builtin 插件单元纳入插件 lane）**：扩展中心「插件」标签此前严格过滤 `kind === "extension-module"`，而 8 项 builtin 注册表条目（task-card 风格/桌壳/魔术关键词×3/主题/工具卡片渲染）的 kind 各不相同，且本机无真实扩展模块，导致能力清单显示 8 项而插件页显示「未加载插件」的矛盾。现在 `isPluginLaneEntry`（UnifiedPluginsView 导出）成为单一权威口径——extension-module 全收 + musepi-extensions provider 且 native 层且非 skill 的 builtin 条目（排除 skill 避免与能力清单整屏重复）；ExtensionsCenter 计数与模块行 kind 标签同口径，空态文案改写为如实说明 builtin 插件单元也汇总于此。能力清单与插件页不再各说各话。
  - EN: Plugin tab unified with the capability list (builtin plugin units join the plugin lane): the extension center's Plugins tab previously filtered strictly on `kind === "extension-module"`, while the 8 builtin registry entries (task-card style / desktop shell / magic keywords ×3 / theme / tool-card renderer) carry assorted kinds — and with no real extension modules installed locally, the capability list showed 8 while the Plugins tab claimed "no plugins loaded". `isPluginLaneEntry` (exported from UnifiedPluginsView) is now the single authority — all extension-module entries plus builtin entries from the musepi-extensions provider at native level excluding skills (skills are excluded to avoid duplicating the whole capability list); ExtensionsCenter's count and module-row kind tags use the same rule, and the empty-state copy now honestly explains that builtin plugin units are tallied here too. The two views no longer contradict each other.

- 插件清单字段正名：`musepi` 成为 package.json 权威清单字段（`musepi.extensions` / `musepi.config` / `musepi.resources`），`omp`/`pi` 保留只读兼容——全部读取点统一为 musepi 优先（extensions 加载器、插件 installer/loader、spike 动态运行时、discovery helpers），回归测试切到 musepi 主 fixture 并补「遗留 omp 字段仍可读」用例锁定 fallback；`docs/extensions-dev.md` §13 双语与 bundled skill 同步。
  - EN: Plugin manifest field canonicalized: `musepi` is now the authoritative package.json manifest field (`musepi.extensions` / `musepi.config` / `musepi.resources`), with `omp`/`pi` kept as read-only legacy fallback — every read site resolves musepi first (extensions loader, plugin installer/loader, spike dynamic runtime, discovery helpers); regression tests switched to musepi fixtures plus a "legacy omp block still readable" case locking the fallback; `docs/extensions-dev.md` §13 (both languages) and the bundled skill updated accordingly.

- **扩展运行时读取清单配置**：扩展 API 新增 `pi.config.get(key)` / `pi.config.getAll()`——运行时消费链路收口。清单声明的 meta 按 API 实例缓存（HMR 重载自然刷新），存储值每次现读 `<agentDir>/extensions/plugin-config.json`（进程级缓存 + 写穿透），restart 语义三档落地：none 现读立即可见、session 加载快照、daemon 重启；与第二刀的表单/setConfig 写入共用同一条 pi-wire 钳制链路（未声明键返回 undefined、坏存储值钳制回退）。`extensions/types.ts` 补 M2-2.4 六字段能力缝声明；`docs/extensions-dev.md`（含 .zh-CN.md 双语 + i18n.yaml 配对）新增 §13「插件清单配置」；bundled skill `musepi-extension-dev` 同步「插件配置」节。新增契约测试 4 例（默认值/未声明键 undefined、坏存储值钳制且 rogue 键过滤、无 config 声明读空表、写入后同进程重读）。
  - EN: Extensions read manifest-declared config at runtime (⑥ cordis plugin-ization, third cut). The extension API gains `pi.config.get(key)` / `pi.config.getAll()` — the runtime-consumption link is wired. Manifest-declared meta is cached per API instance (HMR reload refreshes naturally) while stored values are re-read on each call from `<agentDir>/extensions/plugin-config.json` (process-level cache + write-through), landing the three-tier restart semantics: none = visible immediately on live reads, session = load-time snapshot, daemon = service restart; this shares the same pi-wire clamping path as the second cut's form/setConfig writes (undeclared keys return undefined, corrupt stored values clamp back to defaults). `extensions/types.ts` gains the M2-2.4 six-field capability-seam declaration; `docs/extensions-dev.md` (with .zh-CN.md bilingual pair + i18n.yaml) gains §13 "Plugin manifest config"; the bundled `musepi-extension-dev` skill gains a matching "Plugin config" section. Four new contract tests (defaults / undeclared key undefined, corrupt stored value clamped with rogue keys filtered, no-config-declaration reads an empty table, same-process re-read after write).

- **插件配置写入链路：管理页表单真正落盘**：扩展中心配置表单的每次修改经新 RPC `extensions.setConfig` 落盘——daemon 以扩展 manifest 声明的字段为准（未声明的键直接拒绝，防写垃圾键），值经 `coerceConfigFieldValue` 钳制后写入 `<agentDir>/extensions/plugin-config.json`（进程级缓存 + 写穿透，损坏文件按空存储 fail-soft），失效扩展缓存并扇出 `extensions.changed`；`extensions.list` 同帧下发钳制后的 `configValues`，表单初始值 = 清单默认值 ← 存储值。GUI 乐观更新、失败回滚该键并内联报错，成功按字段 restart 声明提示「立即/新会话/重启服务后生效」（运行时消费随 cordis 试点落地）。取值钳制上收 `@musepi/pi-wire` 成为 daemon/GUI 单一权威，删去 desktop-app 的重复实现。新增契约测试 17 例（wire 取值 7、存储 6、服务级写入闭环 4：声明写入→钳制落盘→list 回读、越界夹取、未声明键拒绝不留痕、changed 广播计数）。
  - EN: Plugin config write path: management-page form values actually persist (⑥ cordis plugin-ization, second cut). Every form change lands via the new `extensions.setConfig` RPC — the daemon validates against the extension manifest's declared fields (undeclared keys are rejected outright to stop garbage writes), coerces values with `coerceConfigFieldValue`, and writes `<agentDir>/extensions/plugin-config.json` (process-level cache + write-through; a corrupt file degrades to empty fail-soft), then invalidates the extension cache and fans out `extensions.changed`; `extensions.list` ships the coerced `configValues` in the same frame, so the form seeds from manifest defaults ← stored values. The GUI updates optimistically, rolls the key back with an inline error on failure, and on success hints the field's restart semantics ("immediately / new sessions / after service restart" — runtime consumption lands with the cordis pilot). Value coercion moves up into `@musepi/pi-wire` as the single authority for daemon and GUI; the duplicated desktop-app copy is removed. Seventeen new contract tests (wire coercion 7, store 6, service-level write loop 4: declared write → clamped persist → list round-trip, out-of-range clamping, undeclared-key rejection leaves no trace, changed-broadcast counting).

- **插件清单支持 config/resources 声明；扩展中心新增插件管理段落**：扩展的 package.json 可在 `omp`/`pi` 字段下声明 `config`（声明式配置字段表）与 `resources`（资源占用卡），daemon 侧逐扩展 fail-soft 校验后经 `extensions.list` 下发，扩展中心详情页据此渲染三段式管理视图——配置表单（boolean→开关行、number→NumberStepper 区间步进、select→≤4 项 Segmented / 更多 GuiSelect、string/path→文本行，restart 声明渲染生效时机提示）、资源卡（磁盘/内存/首次准备/模型下载）、坏字段丢弃警告。校验契约在 `@musepi/pi-wire` 新增 `plugin-config` 模块（daemon 与 GUI 共用，不引入 schemastery）；manifest 是用户可写 JSON，坏字段进结构化 errors 被丢弃、好字段照常通过，绝不拖垮扩展登记（回退保护①）。GUI 取值一律经 `coerceConfigValue` 钳制回退默认值，存储坏值不会透出表单。契约测试 21 例（wire 8 + daemon manifest 6 + GUI 取值 7）。
  - EN: Plugin manifests can declare config and resources, and the extension center gained a plugin-management section sections in the extension center (⑥ cordis plugin-ization). An extension's package.json can declare `config` (declarative field table) and `resources` (resource footprint card) under its `omp`/`pi` block; the daemon validates each extension fail-soft and ships the result via `extensions.list`, and the extension center detail pane renders the three management sections — a config form (boolean → toggle row, number → NumberStepper with range/step, select → Segmented for ≤4 options / GuiSelect beyond, string/path → text row, restart hints showing when a change takes effect), a resources card (disk / memory / first-time setup / model downloads), and a dropped-fields warning. The validation contract lands in a new `@musepi/pi-wire` `plugin-config` module (shared by daemon and GUI, no schemastery); the manifest is user-writable JSON — bad fields go to structured errors and are dropped while good fields pass, never dragging down extension registration (fallback protection ①). GUI reads are always clamped by `coerceConfigValue` back to declared defaults so corrupt stored values never surface. Twenty-one contract tests (wire 8 + daemon manifest 6 + GUI coercion 7).

- **「新功能」公告弹窗的桌宠穿戴用户装饰**：版本更新公告里的庆祝桌宠（PetSprite）此前渲染默认裸装——现在经 `usePetDecor()` 挂上用户当前选的配饰与光泽，与主界面桌宠观感一致。
  - EN: The "what's new" announcement overlay's celebrating pet now wears the user's decor: the PetSprite previously rendered the default bare look and now mounts the active accessory and gloss via `usePetDecor()`, consistent with the main-surface pet.

- **输入框「性能与用量」统计条**：输入框下方新增两颗 pill——时间 pill（轮次/步数 · 总 tokens · 模型用时 · 工具用时 · TTFT · tokens/s）与用量 pill（输入/输出/缓存读写 · 缓存命中率 · 成本），点击各自弹出明细浮层（useFloatingMenu 单例互斥）；ContextRing 不动，统计行经 ComposerFrame 新 `belowRow` 插槽渲染在输入卡外（设计稿 `docs/gui-design.md` §5w）。数据源零新 RPC：token 数字复用 `session.contextUsage` 持久投影（分页/压缩安全），时间数字走可见窗口 fold 纯函数 `deriveWindowStats`（八字段，memo 比较器守流式重渲染）；设置 → 聊天 → 「interface and input」新增 详细/简洁 档位（简洁档只留速度与缓存命中），localStorage + 跨标签页 storage 事件同步。纯函数 15 例契约测试。
  - EN: composer "Performance & usage" stats row: two pills ride below the composer — a time pill (turns/steps · total tokens · model time · tool time · TTFT · tokens/s) and a usage pill (input/output/cache read+write · cache hit · cost), each opening a detail popup (useFloatingMenu singleton mutex). The ContextRing stays put; the row renders outside the input card via ComposerFrame's new `belowRow` slot (design doc `docs/gui-design.md` §5w). Zero new RPCs: token figures reuse the durable `session.contextUsage` projection (paging/compaction-safe), time figures come from the visible-window fold pure function `deriveWindowStats` (eight fields, memo comparator guarding streaming re-renders); Settings → Chat → "interface and input" gains a Detailed/Compact density (compact keeps only speed and cache hit), synced over localStorage + the cross-tab storage event. Fifteen contract tests on the pure functions.

- **预设设置页卡片化 + 「查看配置」继承展开只读视图**：设置 → 智能体 → 预设分为「内置预设 / 自定义预设」两卡片区，默认预设高亮，卡片名称统一走 `resolveModeLabel` 取名链（永不裸 id）；每张卡片新增「查看配置」入口，弹窗只读展示继承展开结果——诊断警告（继承环/超深截断/悬空引用）→ 继承链逐层 → 提示词区块（按 order）→ 扩展白名单（全部启用/仅内置核心/chips）→ settings 覆盖键值表 → 标志位（promptComplete 及声明者/runtimeContext/默认模型档位）。合并语义逐条镜像 daemon `resolveMode`（拓扑序、同名子胜父、后者胜、complete 唯一来源），环/悬空/超深只记标记不抛错（读路径容错）；零新 RPC——链闭包由 modes.list 摘要行一次算出、链上全部层并行 modes.get。编辑/新建/复制/删除能力全部保留。纯函数 19 例契约测试（线性链/菱形/环/自环/悬空/截断/string 快捷展开/complete 缩减）。
  - EN: preset settings page card-ified + a "View config" resolved-inheritance read-only view: Settings → Agent → Presets splits into "Built-in presets" / "Custom presets" card groups with the default visually highlighted, and every card name goes through the `resolveModeLabel` chain (never a bare id); each card gains a "View config" entry whose dialog shows the resolved inheritance — diagnostic warnings (cycle / over-depth truncation / dangling refs) → layer-by-layer chain → prompt sections (by order) → extension whitelist (all/core-only/chips) → settings override key-values → flags (promptComplete + declaring source / runtimeContext / default model role). Merge semantics mirror daemon `resolveMode` line by line (topo order, child-wins-by-name, last-wins, complete = sole source); cycles/dangling/over-deep are recorded, never thrown (read-path tolerance); zero new RPCs — the chain closure is computed from modes.list summary rows and every layer is fetched in parallel via modes.get. All edit/create/duplicate/delete flows are preserved. Nineteen contract tests on the pure functions (linear/diamond/cycle/self-cycle/dangling/truncation/string-shorthand expansion/complete reduction).

- **右侧面板分栏 + 标签页跨列拖拽**：右侧面板可一分为二——头部新增分栏开关，双栏时同屏显示两组「标签条 + 内容体」，单列布局与分栏布局共享同一 `renderPanelBody` 渲染闭包，任何表面在两种形态下观感一致。数据模型升 schema v2：`PanelTab` 增 `column` 字段，扁平 tabs 数组保持「先列 0 后列 1」分组不变式（既有邻接回落/预算驱逐逻辑零改动继续成立）；状态增 `split` + 每列显示 `columnActive`。一个 DndContext 横跨两列：同列拖拽走 reorder，跨列或落空白区走 `movePanelTabToColumn`（被移动标签加入目标列切片末尾并带走全局焦点——浏览器单例守卫按 activeId 选宿主列，焦点不跟会错位）；合并分栏时列 1 全部并入列 0 保序，全局焦点保留为唯一显示。浏览器单例守卫：非全局聚焦列里的 browser 标签显示占位按钮（点击即切焦点）。持久化 v1（前分栏）载荷迁移为全列 0。panel-tabs 契约测试扩至 32 例（分组不变式/跨列 reorder no-op/焦点跟随/v1 迁移/分栏往返）。
  - EN: Right-panel split columns + cross-column tab drag: the right panel can divide in two — a split toggle in the header shows two tab strips + bodies side by side, and both the single-column and split layouts render through one shared `renderPanelBody` closure so every surface looks identical in either shape. The data model moves to schema v2: `PanelTab` gains a `column` field and the flat tabs array keeps the column-0-first grouping invariant (existing neighbour fallback / eviction-budget logic keeps holding unchanged); state gains `split` + per-column display `columnActive`. One DndContext spans both columns: same-column drags route to reorder, cross-column or blank-area drops route to `movePanelTabToColumn` (the moved tab joins the end of the target column's slice and takes global focus — the browser singleton guard keys on activeId to pick its host column, so focus must follow); merging collapses every column-1 tab into column 0 in order, keeping the global focus as the single display. Browser singleton guard: a browser tab in the non-focused column renders a placeholder button (click to focus). v1 (pre-split) payloads migrate to all-column-0. panel-tabs contract tests grow to 32 cases (grouping invariant / cross-column reorder no-op / focus follows / v1 migration / split round-trip).

- **预设菜单选项补描述行**：WelcomeComposer 预设选项从单行升级为「名称 + 描述」两行结构；`resolveModeDescription()` 走 `mode {id} description` 词表链（回显返回 null，无描述退化为单行）；四个内置预设补描述键（工作=执行编码任务/对话=轻讨论问答/设计=界面视觉设计/创作=创意发散），双语同步。
  - EN: Preset menu options gain a description row: WelcomeComposer preset options upgrade from a single row to a name + description two-line structure; `resolveModeDescription()` walks the `mode {id} description` i18n chain (returns null on echo — entries without a description degrade to a single row); the four builtin presets gain description keys (work = execute coding tasks / chat = lightweight Q&A / design = UI & visual design / creator = creative exploration), bilingual in sync.

- **单栏直接拖标签分栏**：分栏不再需要先点「分栏」按钮——单列布局下拖起标签即点亮右半身「拖到此处分栏」落区（PanelSplitZone，虚线高亮 + 双语提示），落入即 `setSplit(true)` 并把标签送进第二列；落区与标签条共享同一 DndContext/碰撞判定（指针包含纪律沿用），拖拽含入判定不再把落区当作独立高亮态互抢。ContextPanel 非分栏分支重构为自带 DndContext（分栏分支的 splitSensors/panelSplitCollision 上提共享），SurfaceTabStrip 无关 import 清理。
  - EN: Drag a tab straight into a split. Splitting no longer requires clicking the split button first — in the single-column layout, lifting a tab lights up a right-half "drop here to split" zone (PanelSplitZone, dashed highlight + bilingual hint); dropping there calls `setSplit(true)` and sends the tab into column 2. The zone shares one DndContext / collision discipline with the strip (the pointer-containment rule carries over), so the containment check no longer treats the zone as a competing highlight. The ContextPanel non-split branch is restructured to own its DndContext (the split branch's splitSensors/panelSplitCollision are hoisted and shared), and the unrelated SurfaceTabStrip import is cleaned up.

- **文件面板树/预览分签 + 隐藏文件开关 + 行内打开与引用**：文件树与文件预览拆成两个独立标签页——树占位签不再被首个真实文件吞掉（upsert 例外：files 占位即树浏览器，真实实例在旁新开一签），预览头「返回」激活树签；面板标签页 body 本就独立挂载，树签↔文件签切换会卸载重挂，未保存编辑缓冲改走模块级 bufferCache 闭环（卸载时按预览路径暂存脏缓冲、重挂 openPreview 自动恢复编辑态并重报脏点到标签条）。工具栏新增「显示隐藏文件」开关（ScanEye/EyeOff 双态，git-prefs 同款持久化 + 跨面事件同步；daemon 扫描本就返回点文件，过滤纯 GUI 侧按文件名 `.` 前缀）。树行悬停动作区新增「在应用中打开」（文件夹开资源管理器、文件开默认应用，行尾悬停浮现）。预览头新增「引用文件到输入框」（Quote 图标，走 musepi-gui-insert-text 共享通道把工作区相对路径以 `@path` 插入输入框草稿）。panel-tabs 契约用例随新契约改写（files 占位保留、其他表面仍替换）。
  - EN: File-pane tree/preview split tabs + hidden-files toggle + row open action & cite. The tree and the file preview become two independent tabs — the tree placeholder is no longer consumed by the first real file (upsert exception: the files placeholder IS the tree browser, a real instance opens next to it) and the preview's back button activates the tree tab. Panel tab bodies mount independently, so a tree↔file tab switch unmounts and remounts the pane; unsaved editor buffers now close the loop through the module-level bufferCache (dirty buffer stashed by preview path at unmount, restored into edit mode by openPreview on remount, dirty dot re-reported to the strip). The toolbar gains a "show hidden files" toggle (ScanEye/EyeOff states, git-prefs-style persistence + cross-surface event sync; the daemon scan already returns dotfiles — filtering is a pure GUI-side `.`-prefix name filter). Tree rows gain a hover "open with app" action (folders open Explorer, files open the default app, revealed at the row's right edge on hover). The preview header gains "cite file in composer" (Quote icon — pushes the workspace-relative path into the composer draft as `@path` via the shared musepi-gui-insert-text channel). panel-tabs contract cases rewritten for the new contract (files placeholder kept, other surfaces still replace).

### Fixed

- **回合冻结用时三套键空间统一为回合起点键**：daemon、GUI store、collab 客人/宿主客户端此前按**三种不同键**写 roundDurations——daemon 按回合起点 ts（最后一条 user 消息或 display 的顾问笔记）、GUI store 与两个客户端按**末条 assistant ts**（其中 collab 两端还拿 `Date.now()` 减 user ts 混时钟）；而 Transcript 三处读侧全用 assistant ts 键 → **daemon 恢复快照里的冻结用时在会话视图永远查不到**（轨迹检视器因读侧做了「起点键优先、assistant 键回退」的归一才幸免）。修复：① pi-wire 新增共享 helper `roundDurationRecord`（锚定规则唯一实现：`isTurnStartEntry` + wire 时钟末条 − 起点，时钟回拨返回 null），daemon 视图与全部三个 GUI 写侧统一调用；② collab 两端的 `Date.now()` 混时钟算法随统一一并消除；③ 读侧新增纯函数 `roundTimerKeyByRow`（每回合末条 assistant 行 → 回合起点键），Transcript 行级/回合头/流式幽灵行三处读侧统一走「起点键 + 旧 assistant 键回退」（旧快照可读性保留）；④ 轨迹检视器读侧本就归一，零改动。契约测试：wire helper 6 例、render-units 4 例（多步回合/顾问锚/纯工具轮）、guest client 3 例（起点键、wire 时钟定值、每轮累积）、store 2 例（实时冻结 + daemon 播种同键可读）；既有断言 assistant 键的测试改写为新契约（失败模式即旧键空间：daemon 值永远不可见）。
  - EN: Round-freeze durations unified onto the turn-start key. The daemon, the GUI store and the collab guest/host clients wrote roundDurations under THREE different keys — the daemon keyed by turn-start ts (last user message or displayed advisor note), the GUI store and both collab clients by the LAST assistant ts (with the collab pair additionally mixing clocks via `Date.now() - userTs`) — while all three Transcript read sites looked up assistant-ts keys, so **frozen totals from a daemon-restored snapshot could never be found in the conversation view** (the trajectory viewer survived only because its read side normalized turn-start-first with an assistant-key fallback). Fix: ① new shared pi-wire helper `roundDurationRecord` (single implementation of the anchor rule: `isTurnStartEntry` + last wire-clock entry minus start, null on clock skew), now called by the daemon view and all three GUI write sides; ② the collab pair's mixed-clock Date.now() math eliminated by the same unification; ③ new pure read-side function `roundTimerKeyByRow` (each turn's LAST assistant row → turn-start key), with all three Transcript read sites (row-level, turn header, ghost stream row) unified to turn-start-primary plus the legacy assistant-key fallback (pre-anchor snapshots stay readable); ④ the trajectory viewer's read side was already normalized — untouched. Contract tests: 6 for the wire helper, 4 for render-units (multi-step round / advisor anchor / tool-only round), 3 for the guest client (turn-start key, deterministic wire-clock value, per-round accumulation), 2 for the store (live freeze + daemon-seeded key served through the same registry); the existing tests asserting assistant keys were rewritten to the new contract (their failure mode IS the old key space: daemon values forever invisible).

- **全局事件与会话 journal 水位门串线**：daemon 全局广播（extensions.changed / modes.changed / STT-TTS 下载进度等）此前以 `kind:"event"` 下发——与会话 journal 记录同 kind，而 GUI store 对该 kind 执行 seq 水位门。全局 seq 是独立计数空间：新会话水位 0 时首个全局事件 seq 恰好为 1 → 水位被推进 → **后续 seq 1 的真实 journal 记录被误判「重放」静默丢弃**；seq 越过水位+1 时进重排缓冲 + 触发 catchup + 10s 空洞计时器 → 下载进度风暴可把整会话反复打回重订阅。修复：① daemon `EventService` 广播原语与 extensions.notification 改发 `kind:"global-event"`（seq 保留——帧识别要求数字 seq——但不再进任何会话水位语义）；② GUI app.tsx 事件路由对 global-event 显式早退（消费方本就只走 `rpc.addEventListener` 按 payload.type 过滤，不受影响）；③ session-store.apply 对 global-event 兜底直弃。契约测试：daemon 侧 kind 分离 + 客户端「全局事件先于首条 journal 记录不丢消息」「40 连发下载进度零 catchup 零 resync」；既有 design-systems 广播契约测试更新为新 kind。
  - EN: Global events were cross-wired into the session journal watermark gate. Daemon global broadcasts (extensions.changed / modes.changed / STT-TTS download progress) went out as `kind:"event"` — the same kind as session journal records, which the GUI store watermark-gates. The global seq is an independent counter: on a fresh session (watermark 0) the first global event's seq is exactly 1 → the watermark advanced → **the real journal record seq 1 was then misjudged as a "replay" and silently dropped**; any seq beyond watermark+1 landed in the reorder buffer, triggered catchup and armed the 10s gap timer → a download-progress storm could repeatedly throw whole sessions back into re-subscribe. Fix: ① the daemon EventService broadcast primitive and extensions.notification now emit `kind:"global-event"` (seq kept — frame detection requires a numeric seq — but it enters no session watermark semantics); ② the GUI app.tsx event router early-returns on global-event (consumers only ever listened via `rpc.addEventListener` filtering on payload.type, unaffected); ③ session-store.apply drops global-event as defense-in-depth. Contract tests: daemon-side kind separation + client-side "a global event before the first journal record doesn't lose the message" and "a 40-burst download-progress storm causes zero catchups and zero resyncs"; the existing design-systems broadcast contract test updated to the new kind.

- **桌面端在被企业安全软件拦截的机器上不再启动失败**：`bun run desktop` 在「dist fresh — skipping build」后直接 `exited with code 3`、零输出。排查链：真实二进制 `--version` 同样 exit 3（故障①）→ `ELECTRON_RUN_AS_NODE=1` 正常（exe 完好）→ `--no-sandbox` 下露出真错误 `ProcessSingleton: Lock file can not be created! Error code: 5`（故障②）→ userData 目录普通写入正常、`SeCreateSymbolicLinkPrivilege` 已从令牌消失（系统日志显示 22:01 HONOR PCManager 重置过策略）。根因：Electron 的单实例锁在 Windows 上是**符号链接**（Chromium 内部实现，无法选择不用），创建需要「创建符号链接」用户权限；管家类软件/策略重置会静默回收它，开发者模式救不了（Chromium 不走特权创建标志）。更阴的是故障②的退出码：绕过沙箱后 Chromium 打出 Lock file 错误，但进程以 **0** 退出（单实例守卫干净退出）——裸看退出码是"成功"，窗口却从未出现。修复（仅 dev 循环，发布版沙箱不动）：`desktop-run.mjs` 改为韧性启动——Electron 10 秒内 exit 3 自动以 `--no-sandbox` 重试一次并说明原因；stderr 改为管道扫描（转发同时滚动匹配），任何快速退出只要命中 ProcessSingleton 字样即打印双根因 + 修复指引并以 1 退出，不再把误导性的 0 转发给调用方；配套新增 `scripts/grant-symlink-privilege.ps1`——右键管理员运行一次把 `SeCreateSymbolicLinkPrivilege` 授回当前用户（secedit 只动 USER_RIGHTS 区域：导出→增补 SID→回配），注销重登生效（重启系统通常同时治好沙箱与权限两项）。
  - EN: The desktop app no longer fails to start on machines where enterprise security software blocks it. `bun run desktop` printed "dist fresh — skipping build" then `exited with code 3` with zero output. Root-caused to TWO independent OS-level failures (concurrent on this machine — the system log shows HONOR PCManager resetting a policy scheme at 22:01): ① Chromium sandbox init killed silently — `electron --version` exits 3 with no output sandboxed, fine under `--no-sandbox`; ② `SeCreateSymbolicLinkPrivilege` has vanished from the user token — Electron's single-instance lock is a SYMLINK on Windows (a Chromium-internal implementation; there is no opting out), and creating it needs the "Create symbolic links" user right; once PC-manager "optimizers" / policy resets revoke it, creation fails with ACCESS DENIED, and Developer Mode does not help (Chromium never passes the unprivileged-create flag). The nastier twist is failure ②'s exit code: with the sandbox bypassed, Chromium logs `ProcessSingleton: Lock file can not be created! Error code 5` but the process exits **0** (the single-instance guard quits cleanly) — a bare exit-code check reads "success" while no window ever appeared. Fix (dev loop only; release builds keep the sandbox): desktop-run.mjs becomes resilient — an Electron exit 3 within 10s auto-retries once with `--no-sandbox` and explains why; stderr is piped through a scanner (forwarded while rolling-matched), and any fast exit whose output hits the ProcessSingleton pattern prints both root causes + remediation and exits 1 instead of forwarding the misleading 0; plus a new `scripts/grant-symlink-privilege.ps1` — run once as administrator to grant `SeCreateSymbolicLinkPrivilege` back to the current user (secedit touches only the USER_RIGHTS area: export → append the SID → re-apply), effective after sign-out/sign-in (a reboot usually cures both the sandbox and the privilege).

- **浏览器标签右键菜单 + theme-color 标签样式修复（用户 2026-09-29：网页标签不能右键关闭、个别网页标签样式显示不正常）**：标签条每个标签支持右键——关闭 / 关闭其他（单标签时禁用）/ 复制标签页 / 复制网址，走共享 ContextMenu（useFloatingMenu 玻璃壳、指针锚点、全局菜单互斥）。网页 theme-color 不再整块实底 + 强制黑白文字（部分站点主题色与界面明暗冲突，实底标签读起来就是"样式坏了"）——改为低透明度晕染（18% alpha 背景 + 32% alpha 内描边环），玻璃标签质感与文字可读性都保住；hexLuminance 对比度启发式随实底方案一并下线。
  - EN: Browser tab context menu + theme-color tab style fix (user 2026-09-29: web tabs can't be right-click closed, some web tabs render with broken styling). Every tab in the strip now has a right-click menu — close / close others (disabled with a single tab) / duplicate tab / copy URL — via the shared ContextMenu (useFloatingMenu glass shell, pointer anchor, global menu mutex). A page's theme-color no longer paints the tab as a full-bleed solid with forced black/white text (some sites' theme colors clash with the UI's light/dark and the solid tab just read as "broken styling") — it becomes a low-alpha tint (18% alpha background + 32% alpha inset ring) so the glass tab keeps both its material and legible text; the hexLuminance contrast heuristic leaves with the solid approach.

- **跨列拖拽标签鬼畜闪烁**：分栏后拖拽标签在列间移动时「over」目标来回跳变、两列标签条逐帧互抢动画（用户 2026-09-29：鬼畜闪烁难以正确切换）。根因是 closestCenter 距离判定：被拖 chip 同时接近「小标签矩形」与「大列落区」时，最近者随指针微移在两者之间振荡。修法按 pointerWithin 纪律——只把指针包含在内的 droppable 作为候选，面积最小者胜出（标签优先于包含它的列区），指针一旦进入另一列该列独占全部候选：每个指针位置一个稳定判定，零振荡。
  - EN: Cross-column tab drag flicker fixed. With the panel split, dragging a tab across columns made the "over" target flip back and forth and both strips' animations fight frame by frame (user 2026-09-29: violent flicker, hard to land a switch). Root cause: the closestCenter distance heuristic — a dragged chip sitting between a small tab rect and the big column zone oscillates between the two as the pointer micro-moves. The fix follows the pointerWithin discipline: only droppables whose rect CONTAINS the pointer are candidates, the smallest area wins (a tab beats the column zone holding it), and once the pointer crosses into the other column that column owns every candidate — one stable verdict per pointer position, zero oscillation.

- **ContextRing 液态玻璃落在圆环本体（按钮玻璃化回退）**：上一轮把 `.gui-context-ring-btn` 并进 footer-pill 玻璃家族方向有误（用户 2026-09-29：指的是圆环本身加粗改液态玻璃质感，不是整体变图标）——本次回退按钮壳的玻璃材质（退出五组家族选择器，按钮恢复透明点击区，仅悬停时圆环轻放大），把玻璃做进圆环：弧 stroke 2.5→3.4 加粗、按状态色走渐变 stroke（浅色端 45% 白混入）+ 同色柔光 drop-shadow、轨道改磨砂半透明发丝——材质在环上，不在按钮盘上。
  - EN: ContextRing liquid glass lands on the ring itself (button glass reverted). Glassing the `.gui-context-ring-btn` into the footer-pill family last round was the wrong direction (user 2026-09-29: the RING itself should be thicker with a liquid-glass feel, not the whole thing turned into an icon) — this reverts the button shell's glass material (leaves all five family selector groups; the button is a transparent hit target again, with a subtle ring-only scale-up on hover) and puts the glass into the ring: arc stroke 2.5→3.4 thicker, tone-colored gradient stroke (light end mixed 45% white) + a soft chromatic drop-shadow, and a frosted translucent track — the material lives on the ring, not on a button disc.

- **白屏事故修复：i18n 跨域重复键 + 新增静态门禁**：「性能与用量」统计条新增词表时，`input tokens` / `output tokens` 两键与 settings 域既有键重复——client-core i18n barrel 在模块加载时对跨域重复键硬抛，整个 renderer bundle 加载失败，主窗与托盘（tray-menu.html 独立 entry、共享同一 bundle）全部白屏；`bun run check` 的类型检查每域独立 satisfies，结构上抓不到跨域重复，门禁全绿却运行时必崩。修复：general 域删除两重复键（stats-pills 经 t() 回落 settings 域既有「输入 token / 输出 token」，语义不变）；新增 `scripts/check-i18n-dup-keys.ts` 静态扫描三个语言 barrel（client-core zh-CN / en-US、TUI zh-CN）的跨域键交集，已挂入 `check:tools`（变异验证：塞回事故键即 exit 1）。
  - EN: White-screen incident fixed: duplicate cross-domain i18n key + a new static gate. Adding the "Performance & usage" stats-row keys put `input tokens` / `output tokens` in general, duplicating existing settings-domain keys — the client-core i18n barrel throws on cross-domain duplicates at module load, so the entire renderer bundle failed and both the main window and the tray (tray-menu.html is a separate entry sharing the same bundle) went white; `bun run check`'s per-domain independent `satisfies` type check structurally cannot see cross-domain duplicates, so every gate was green while the app crashed at runtime. Fix: the two duplicate keys are removed from the general domain (stats-pills' `t()` falls back to the settings-domain "输入 token / 输出 token" — same semantics); a new `scripts/check-i18n-dup-keys.ts` statically scans the three language barrels (client-core zh-CN / en-US, TUI zh-CN) for cross-domain key intersections and is wired into `check:tools` (mutation-verified: re-inserting the incident key exits 1).

- **新功能面板（what's-new）两处渲染缺陷**：①底部按钮行此前直接挂在卡片根、复用无内边距的 `.gui-onboarding-actions`，按钮贴死卡片圆角底边被 `overflow:hidden` 裁切——新增作用域规则给按钮行独立内边距+顶部分隔线，成为标准 dialog footer；②面板头部的球体桌宠此前不传配饰配置，用户给桌面宠物配的耳机/音符在新功能页显示为裸球——改走 `usePetDecor()`（与聊天头像、桌面宠物窗同源），配饰与光泽设置即时同步。
  - EN: Two rendering defects in the what's-new panel. ① The footer button row hung directly off the card root reusing the padding-less `.gui-onboarding-actions`, so the buttons kissed the card's rounded bottom edge and got clipped by `overflow:hidden` — a scoped rule now gives the row its own padding plus a top hairline, making it a standard dialog footer. ② The header orb pet never received the decoration config, so the headphones/note accessory the user picked for the desktop pet rendered as a bare ball here — it now resolves through `usePetDecor()` (same source as the chat avatar and the desktop pet window), keeping accessory and gloss in sync live.

- **内置预设中文名补全 + 预设取名链收口**：zh 词表 `preset {id} name` 四个内置预设（work/chat/design/creator）此前是英文原值（"Work" 等），TUI 与 daemon `modes.list` 中文环境下仍显示英文；GUI 侧 WelcomeComposer 预设 chip 与选项菜单、设置页预设列表此前直接用 daemon 裸 `label`，绕过 `resolveModeLabel` 取名链（GUI i18n `mode {id}` → catalog → 兜底）。修复：zh 词表补翻译（工作模式/对话模式/设计模式/创作模式，与 GUI `mode {id}` 词表逐字一致）；三处显示点（预设 chip、chip 菜单、设置页列表）全部改走 `resolveModeLabel`，与 ContextPanel/SessionHoverCard 同名同链（mode-label.ts 契约注释由「两处」扩为「三处」）。用户自建/扩展预设不受影响（t() 回显 key 时回落 catalog label）。
  - EN: Builtin preset Chinese names completed and the preset naming chain closed. The zh locale left all four builtin `preset {id} name` values in English ("Work" etc.), so the TUI and daemon `modes.list` still showed English under a Chinese locale; on the GUI side, the WelcomeComposer preset chip, its option menu, and the settings preset list rendered daemon raw `label` directly, bypassing the `resolveModeLabel` chain (GUI i18n `mode {id}` → catalog → fallback). Fix: the zh table gains the translations (工作模式/对话模式/设计模式/创作模式, verbatim identical to the GUI `mode {id}` table); all three display points (preset chip, chip menu, settings list) now go through `resolveModeLabel`, same name/same chain as ContextPanel and SessionHoverCard (the mode-label.ts contract comment grows from "two places" to "three"). User-created/extension presets are unaffected (when `t()` echoes the key, the catalog label is used).

- **「性能与用量」统计条中文裸键 + 居中 + 浮窗信息增强**：①浮窗此前在中文环境显示裸词表 key（stats turns steps / model time 等 9 键 zh 从未补过）——zh general 域补齐 10 键、en 补 1 键；②统计条此前靠右对齐，改居中（输入框下方视觉重心稳定）；③浮窗补两行——未缓存输入（input − cacheRead）与本次会话花费（复用 ContextRing 的 formatSpend），并加宽至 210px。
  - EN: "Performance & usage" stats row: raw zh keys + centering + popover info. ① The popover showed raw i18n keys in Chinese (stats turns steps / model time — 9 zh keys were never added) — the zh general domain gains 10 keys, en gains 1. ② The row was right-aligned; it now centers (stable visual weight under the composer). ③ The popover gains two rows — uncached input (input − cacheRead) and this-session spend (reusing ContextRing's formatSpend) — and widens to 210px.

- **ContextRing 按钮液态玻璃化**：`.gui-context-ring-btn` 此前 `background: transparent` 游离于玻璃体系外（浅色主题下尤其突兀）——并入 footer-pill 玻璃家族五组选择器（基材/泽/hover/active 与统计 pill、模型胶囊同款），成员规则删自声明背景。
  - EN: The ContextRing button goes liquid-glass: `.gui-context-ring-btn` used to float outside the glass system with `background: transparent` (especially jarring in the light theme) — it now joins the footer-pill glass family across all five selector groups (base/sheen/hover/active identical to the stats pills and the model capsule), and the member rule drops its self-declared background.

- **`desktop` 启动偶发崩溃（updater 在非 Electron 宿主下把整个启动炸掉）+ 启动日志瘦身**：某次 `bun run desktop` 中 launcher shim 被并发 `bun install` 重写踩中、main.cjs 落在纯 Node 宿主下运行，`require("electron").app` 为 undefined，electron-updater 的 autoUpdater getter 在模块加载期直接抛 `Cannot read properties of undefined (reading 'getVersion')`，整个 GUI 启动失败并滚出 65 行崩溃栈。修复双保险：① updater.cjs 头部加宿主守卫——非 Electron 宿主不再 require electron-updater，导出禁用态 stub（state.status="error" + 一句话诊断），GUI 其余部分照常启动；② main.cjs 头部加 fail-fast 守卫，宿主不对时一行说明立即退出，不再向下游炸出莫名其妙的错误。同时治理启动日志：`desktop` 改为 `scripts/desktop-run.mjs`——dist 新鲜（晚于全部打包输入的 mtime，覆盖本包 src/public/html 与 @musepi 各 workspace 源码）时跳过全量重建，一行说明直接起 Electron（`MUSEPI_FORCE_BUILD=1` 强制重建，旧全量行为保留为 `desktop:rebuild`）；build:fix 的逐文件刷屏改为每阶段一行汇总（`VERBOSE=1` 保留明细）。
  - EN: Occasional `desktop` startup crash (updater killed the whole launch under a non-Electron host) + slimmer startup log. In one `bun run desktop` run the launcher shim was caught mid-rewrite by a concurrent `bun install`, main.cjs landed on a plain-Node host where `require("electron").app` is undefined, and electron-updater's autoUpdater getter threw `Cannot read properties of undefined (reading 'getVersion')` at module-load time — the whole GUI launch died with a 65-line crash dump. Two-layer fix: ① updater.cjs gains a host guard at the top — outside Electron it skips requiring electron-updater entirely and exports a disabled stub (state.status="error" + a one-line diagnosis), so the rest of the GUI still starts; ② main.cjs gains a fail-fast guard that prints a one-line explanation and exits immediately when the host is wrong, instead of failing downstream with a cryptic error. The startup log is governed too: `desktop` now runs `scripts/desktop-run.mjs` — when dist is fresh (newer than every bundler input's mtime, covering this package's src/public/html entries and all @musepi workspace sources) the full rebuild is skipped and Electron starts with a one-line notice (`MUSEPI_FORCE_BUILD=1` forces; the old full behavior survives as `desktop:rebuild`); build:fix's per-file chatter becomes one summary line per phase (VERBOSE=1 keeps the detail).

### Changed

- **分栏顶栏改版 + 标签条「+」surface 菜单**：分栏后顶栏不再多出带「分栏」文字的独立控制行（用户 2026-09-29：标签条被挤换行）——该整行删除，`+` / 合并分栏 / 最大化并入每列自己的标签条行内右侧工具组：标签 chips 盒为唯一收缩部分，工具按钮恒定宽度，标签条永不换行；`+` 从「仅浏览器直开」升级为 surface 选择菜单（点按列出全部 primary 表面，选中的在该列新开标签页，浏览器额外开 about:blank），单列与分栏两种形态同一行为，消灭非浏览器表面上的死按钮。
  - EN: Split-pane header redesign + strip "+" surface menu. Splitting no longer adds a separate control line carrying the "分栏" text (user 2026-09-29: the strip was pushed into wrapping) — that whole row is gone; `+` / merge / maximize move into each column's own tab-strip row as a right-end tool group: the chip box is the only shrinking part, tool buttons keep their width, and the strip never wraps. The `+` upgrades from a browser-only direct action to a surface picker (lists every primary surface; the pick opens a new tab in that column, browser additionally opens about:blank), behaving identically in single-column and split shapes and killing the dead button on non-browser surfaces.

- **右栏 rail 改为纯启动器语义**：rail 图标按钮不再与「当前激活标签页实例」绑定——删 `--active` 强调色与 `aria-pressed`（溢出菜单项、扩展 tab 同删），悬停 tooltip、git 角标、⌘ 序号提示全部保留；点击行为维持 open-or-focus（upsert 去重聚焦），，语义是「rail 打开内容、不做实例切换指示」（用户 2026-09-29：rail 不该像是某个实例的标签）。
  - EN: The right rail becomes a pure launcher. Rail icon buttons are no longer bound to the active tab instance — the `--active` emphasis and `aria-pressed` are removed (overflow items and extension tabs alike) while hover tooltips, the git badge, and the ⌘ number hints stay; clicking keeps open-or-focus (upsert dedup + focus), with the rail opening content and never acting as an instance indicator (user 2026-09-29: the rail must not read as some instance's tab).

- **design 模式提示词升级：可验证判断、从参考倒推资源、完成标准与证据链（修订 4）**：role 区块新增「判断可验证三要素」——每个设计判断写明依据什么证据（内容层级/阅读顺序/品牌基准）、用什么尺度衡量（对比度/间距节奏/信息密度）、什么条件下改变结论，禁写「美观」「高级」这类无法判定的词；workflow 区块新增从用户参考与例子逐条倒推资源（效果对应什么版式/组件/素材，缺口先索取）、以真实渲染通过为产物完成标准、交付前自检清单（简报无未知项/渲染通过/manifest 齐全/风格未越界）；brief 区块新增参考提取职责——截图/链接/描述要逐个说明提取什么（配色/版式/组件/文案语气），不把参考当装饰；artifact 区块补「写完文件不等于完成」的证据链（manifest 真实可解析、entry 真实可渲染、预览面板必须能识别）；boundary 区块补修改既有界面的编辑保护（先读现状、局部修改、不推倒重来、不覆盖用户定制）。内置模板修订 3→4；`LEGACY_TEMPLATES` 全部冻结为字面量快照——rev2 档原用 spread 引用当前模板，模板一变历史档即漂移、真实老用户文件比对不上、升级链断裂（本次一并修复并留注释纪律）；新增 rev3→rev4 升级路径回归测试（含「被用户改过的文件不升级」守护）。
  - EN: The design mode prompt gained verifiable judgements, deriving resources from references, completion criteria, and an evidence chain (revision 4). The role block gains the "verifiable judgment triad" — every design judgment states its evidence (content hierarchy / reading order / brand benchmark), its scale (contrast / spacing rhythm / information density), and the condition under which the conclusion changes; non-judgable words like "beautiful" or "premium" are banned. The workflow block gains deriving resources line-by-line from user references and examples (which layout/component/material each effect maps to, asking for gaps first), real rendering as the completion bar, and a pre-delivery self-check (no unknown brief items / render passes / manifest complete / style in-bounds). The brief block gains reference-extraction duties — state per item what to extract from screenshots/links/descriptions (palette/layout/components/copy tone), never treating references as decoration. The artifact block adds the "files written ≠ done" evidence chain (manifest truly parseable, entry truly renderable, preview panel must recognize it). The boundary block adds edit protection for existing interfaces (read the current state first, local edits, no rewrites, never clobber user customizations). Builtin template revision bumps 3→4; `LEGACY_TEMPLATES` is frozen into literal snapshots — the rev2 entry used to spread the current template, so any template edit silently drifted the history entry, real user files stopped matching, and the upgrade chain broke (fixed here with a comment-stated discipline); a new rev3→rev4 upgrade-path regression test lands (including the "user-edited files never upgrade" guard).

- **输入区状态 chips 并入 footer-pill 家族 + composer 浮层材质收口**：C1——mode/todo/swarm/queue 四种状态 chip 删自声明材质，并入输入框 footer-pill 玻璃家族（五组家族选择器同基材/泽/救援/hover/active），mode-chip 各状态改「颜色承载」（goal/paused/armed 染色洗，armed 脉冲动画保留）；C2——`.gui-mode-toggle-popover`/plan/goal/todo/swarm 五张浮层剥掉自声明 background/backdrop-filter/box-shadow，统一收进 useFloatingMenu 的 `.gui-menu-popup` 壳（壳是唯一浮层配方），删孤立的 todo 入场 keyframes；C3——ContextRing 的 `.gui-context-pop` 同样剥材质（该弹层本就挂 useFloatingMenu）。零新视觉 token，§5s 四层配方唯一权威。
  - EN: composer status chips join the footer-pill family + composer overlay material convergence. C1 — the mode/todo/swarm/queue status chips drop their self-declared material and join the composer footer-pill glass family (all five family selector groups share base/sheen/rescue/hover/active); mode-chip states move to "color-carried" washes (goal/paused/armed tint, armed pulse kept). C2 — the five overlays `.gui-mode-toggle-popover`/plan/goal/todo/swarm shed their self-declared background/backdrop-filter/box-shadow and converge on the useFloatingMenu `.gui-menu-popup` shell (the shell is the single overlay recipe), removing the orphaned todo entrance keyframes. C3 — the ContextRing `.gui-context-pop` sheds its material the same way (that popup was already on useFloatingMenu). Zero new visual tokens; the §5s four-layer recipe stays the sole authority.

## [0.5.1] - 2026-09-29

### Added

- **插件面板不再加载失败，官方插件也都有图标了**：插件自带的设置面板原先一律报「模块加载失败」——界面在组件还在下载时就回收了它的地址；现在加载完成后才释放。同时每个插件项都画出了自己的标识（插件声明的图标、内置单元的类别图标，或按包名生成的彩色字母标），状态改为标识角上的小圆点，不再和图标挤在一起。
  - EN: Plugin panels no longer fail to load, and official plugins have icons too. A plugin's own settings panel always reported "module failed to load" — the UI released its address while the download was still in flight; it now waits for the load to finish. Every plugin also draws its own mark (a declared icon, a built-in unit's kind glyph, or a colored letter tile derived from the package name), and status moved to a corner badge instead of crowding the icon.
- **插件详情弹窗补齐**：点击已安装插件打开详情弹窗——大图标、版本、状态与启用开关、头部卸载按钮；插件自带的组件收进弹窗「包含的组件」逐项开关（不再与插件包重复占列表行），插件的设置界面（如账号池管理）也挂载在弹窗内。
  - EN: The plugin detail dialog completes the picture.
