Clicking an installed plugin opens a dialog with the large icon, version, status, enable toggle and an uninstall button in the header; the plugin's own modules collapse into per-item included-component toggles (no longer duplicated as separate list rows), and its settings surface (an account pool, for example) mounts inside the dialog.
- **插件列表支持一键卸载**：已安装插件卡片和列表行新增卸载按钮，确认后由守护进程移除插件目录并即时刷新列表；卸载失败时错误横幅提示且列表保持不变。
  - EN: One-click uninstall in the plugin list. Installed plugin cards and rows gain an uninstall button; after a confirmation the daemon removes the plugin directory and refreshes the list immediately, and a failed uninstall shows an error banner while leaving the list untouched.
- **插件图标可自定义**：插件可在清单里声明 `icon`（emoji、字符或图片 URL），列表立即采用；未声明时按包名生成确定的彩色字母标，同包同名同色。
  - EN: Customizable plugin icons. A plugin can declare `icon` in its manifest (emoji, glyph, or an image URL) and the list adopts it; without one, a deterministic colored letter mark is derived from the package name — same package, same mark.
- **CredentialService 落地——连接器凭据托管，密钥不出 wire**：新增 L2 服务 key="credentials"，只认领 `credentials.list` / `credentials.set` / `credentials.delete` 三路由；`resolve` 是内部 async 方法，**不在 wire 上**——任何客户端都无法 RPC 拉明文密钥（契约测试深扫 wire 面 + 显式断言 routes 不含 resolve）。凭据种类 `token | basic | env`；wire 面只回 `CredentialMeta{id,label,kind,createdAt,updatedAt}`，UI 只见「已配置」。store 为 `~/.musepi/agent/credentials.json`：懒加载缓存 + tmp+rename 写穿、0o600 权限；损坏文件 fail-soft（logger.warn + 回空仓，set 可重建）；id 生成 `cred_` + base64url(12B)。决策点③落实：新建 L2 服务，散点（mcp/remote 配置里的明文位）留后续迁移，不复用。6 例契约测试（wire 深扫 / resolve 不在 routes / 跨实例持久化 / delete / 校验 / 损坏 fail-soft，store 指向隔离 temp）+ route-coverage 桩；daemon 全量 265 例全绿。
  - EN: CredentialService lands — connector credential custody, keys never cross the wire. A new L2 service keyed "credentials" claims exactly `credentials.list` / `credentials.set` / `credentials.delete`; `resolve` is an internal async method that is NOT on the wire — no client can RPC-pull a plaintext secret (a contract test deep-scans the wire surface and asserts routes exclude resolve). Kinds are `token | basic | env`; the wire only returns `CredentialMeta{id,label,kind,createdAt,updatedAt}`, so the UI sees "configured" and nothing more. The store is `~/.musepi/agent/credentials.json`: lazy-loaded cache + tmp+rename write-through, 0o600 perms; a corrupt file fails soft (logger.warn + empty map, `set` can rebuild); ids are `cred_` + base64url(12B). Decision point ③ honored: a brand-new L2 service; the scattered plaintext spots (mcp/remote configs) stay for later migration, not reused. Six contract tests (wire deep-scan / resolve-not-in-routes / cross-instance persistence / delete / validation / corrupt fail-soft, store pointed at an isolated temp dir) plus a route-coverage stub; all 265 daemon tests green.

- **daemon 宿主 cordis 组合内核落地**：`@deepseek-ai/cordis` 4.0.4 走 npm catalog 依赖，新增 `daemon/host-context.ts`——唯一 cordis 适配点（ADR 边界 2：L2 接口文件保持零 cordis 依赖）。strangler-fig 双跑：DaemonServer 构造时把注册表全部服务同源挂载进 cordis 根 Context（provide + 检视面 + orderly shutdown 通路），路由分发权威仍在 HostServices 注册表；双跑期挂载**不代调** start/stop（P1 代码是生命周期权威，防与 server.ts 既有 schedule.start() 等双跑副作用），`dispose()` 拆 Context 即无损回 P1 形态。opt-in `lifecycle: "cordis"` 已支持 start 即 apply / stop 走 effect 账本反向回收（框架保证语义，首个真正 cordis 化服务 SessionService 落地时按服务逐个切换）。daemon close 路径接 dispose。8 例契约测试用真 cordis Context（ADR 边界 3 不 mock）：同源实例 parity、检视面、external/cordis 两生命周期语义、重复 key 拒载、dispose 幂等、回滚后注册表不受影响。`scripts/check-cordis-boundary.ts` 门禁实跑通过（agent/ai/wire/sdk/catalog 五领域包零 cordis import）。
  - EN: daemon host gains the cordis composition kernel. `@deepseek-ai/cordis` 4.0.4 enters via the npm catalog; the new `daemon/host-context.ts` is the single cordis adaptation point (ADR boundary 2: the L2 service interface file stays cordis-free). Strangler-fig dual-run: at DaemonServer construction every registered service is mounted from the same instances into the cordis root Context (provide + inspection surface + orderly-shutdown path), while route-dispatch authority stays with the HostServices registry; during dual-run the mount deliberately does NOT call start/stop (P1 code owns the service lifecycle, preventing double side effects with existing calls like server.ts's schedule.start()), and `dispose()` tears the Context down with zero damage to the P1 shape. The opt-in `lifecycle: "cordis"` already supports start-at-apply / stop recovered through the framework effect ledger, to be switched per service when the first truly cordis-ified service (SessionService) lands. The daemon close path calls dispose. Eight contract tests run against a real cordis Context (ADR boundary 3 — no mocking): same-instance parity, the inspection surface, both lifecycle semantics, duplicate-key rejection, idempotent dispose, and registry unaffected after rollback. The `scripts/check-cordis-boundary.ts` gate passes (zero cordis imports in the five domain packages agent/ai/wire/sdk/catalog).

### Changed

- **连接器按会话白名单生效于 MCP 工具激活集**：SessionTools 新增 `#mcpServerAllowlist`（三态语义：`undefined` = 未配置、维持存量「连接即启用」；已配置 Set、可为空 = 白名单生效，空集 = 零 MCP 工具；来源 server 未知的 manager 工具在名单生效时 fail-closed 一律不放行）。白名单只约束**激活集**——未命中 server 的工具保留在注册表，后续名单变更无需重连即可重新暴露；非 MCP 工具不受影响。daemon 为权威边界：session-host 新增 `setSessionConnectors`/`readSessionConnectors`/`connectorsSnapshot`（含 transport 与连接状态），选择持久化进会话头 `mcpServers`（显式键获胜含 null 清除，`[]` 是有语义的已配置空态），adopt 会话时回注白名单（入 tool-registry 突变队列，顺序安全）；新增 ConnectorService（`connectors.list` / `connectors.setSelected` 两 RPC，非 live 会话报错）。7 例契约测试（存量回归 / 过滤+注册表保留 / 空集零暴露 / 先设后刷新 / 连接中新 server 不暴露 / null 清除 / 未知来源 fail-closed）+ view-store 持久化往返测试。
  - EN: per-session connector allowlist enforced on the MCP tool activation set. SessionTools gains `#mcpServerAllowlist` (three-state semantics: `undefined` = unconfigured, preserving the legacy "connect-and-enable" behavior; an armed Set, possibly empty = the allowlist governs, with an empty set meaning zero MCP tools; manager tools whose origin server is unknown fail-closed and are never exposed while the allowlist is armed). The allowlist only constrains the **activation set** — tools from unlisted servers stay registered so a later selection change can re-expose them without a reconnect, and non-MCP tools are untouched. The daemon is the authoritative boundary: session-host gains `setSessionConnectors`/`readSessionConnectors`/`connectorsSnapshot` (transport + connection status included), the selection persists into the session header as `mcpServers` (explicit key wins, including null-clears; `[]` is a meaningful configured-empty state), the adoption path re-arms the allowlist (via the tool-registry mutation queue, order-safe), and a new ConnectorService exposes two RPCs (`connectors.list` / `connectors.setSelected`, erroring on non-live sessions). Seven contract tests (legacy regression / filter + registry retention / empty-set zero exposure / arm-then-refresh / late-connecting server stays unexposed / null clears / unknown-origin fail-closed) plus a view-store persistence round-trip test.

- **软件图标焕新为 IP 桌宠形象（品牌稿 S4）**：应用图标从点阵 π 换成 IP 桌宠定帧——金色球体 + 白色头戴耳机，无轨道环（真实 PetSprite 的环 rx104 整环藏在不透明球体 r114.27 之后、静止帧不可见，品牌定帧与实拍对齐；几何逐字同源 SplashOrb），叠加在保留的 23×23 淡点阵卡上（80.5% 卡中卡与 superellipse n=5 圆角不变，并排布局不变；球周 r=300 点阵留白晕）。旧规「配色只用主题色」为品牌 IP 有意打破。产物全套重生成：build/icon.svg（手写 + 头注释参数）→ Chromium 渲染 icon.png/icon-dock.png → Pillow 派生 icon.ico（16–256）与 icon.icns（16–512 @1x/@2x，Windows 端跳过旧版 1024 档，macOS 降采样补齐；release bundle icns 打包时在 mac 重生成并重签的流程不变）。站点 favicon（assets/icon.svg）同步换为同款球脸，favicon 尺寸丢弃耳机（<4px 笔画会糊）。设计文档 §6 双语同步重写（改动流程改为 SVG→Chromium→Pillow 链）。16–48px 小尺寸层级实截可读（球体 + 白耳机可辨）。
  - EN: app icon rebranded to the IP orb mascot (branding spec S4): the dot-matrix π becomes a posed still of the pet — gold sphere + white over-ear wearables, with NO orbit ring (the real PetSprite ring, rx 104, is drawn fully behind the opaque sphere r 114.27 and never visible at rest, so brand stills match the on-screen pet; geometry verbatim from SplashOrb), set on the retained 23×23 faint dot-matrix card (the 80.5% card-in-card and superellipse n=5 corners unchanged — the side-by-side layout is intact; a r=300 dot-free halo surrounds the orb). The old "theme colors only" palette rule is deliberately broken for the brand IP. Full artifact regeneration: build/icon.svg (hand-authored, params in the header comment) → Chromium render to icon.png/icon-dock.png → Pillow-derived icon.ico (16–256) and icon.icns (16–512 @1x/@2x; the Windows-side icns skips the legacy 1024 tier — macOS downscales, and the release-bundle icns is still regenerated + re-signed at packaging on mac). The site favicon (assets/icon.svg) switches to the same orb face, dropping the headphones at favicon size (<4px strokes bake into noise). Design doc §6 rewritten bilingually (workflow is now the SVG→Chromium→Pillow chain). Real 16–48px tier screenshots confirm legibility (sphere + white wearables recognizable).

- **开屏焕新为 IP 桌宠形象 + 新定位口号（品牌稿 S3）**：启动 splash 的点阵 π logo.png 换成 SplashOrb 组件——桌面桌宠（PetSprite）的定帧 SVG（金色球体 + 白色头戴耳机，无轨道环——同 S4 的对齐结论，几何与落地页 .mp-orb 逐字同源），落定动画带辉光绽放 + 有界悬浮呼吸（全部有限次数，延续「开屏动画必须有限」的渲染器防卡死约束，gui-motion-off 全静）；开屏文案从「你的桌面编码智能体」改为「结对工程师，处处与你同行 / Your pair engineer, on every surface.」（与官网新 slogan 对齐）。随之删除无引用的 src/vendor/logo.png 死资产，图标同步契约从三处收敛为两处（build/icon.icns + build/icon-dock.png），gui-design/gui-implementation 文档同步。
  - EN: launch splash rebranded to the IP orb mascot + new positioning tagline (branding spec S3): the dot-matrix π logo.png becomes the SplashOrb component — a posed-still SVG of the desktop pet (PetSprite): gold sphere + white over-ear wearables with NO orbit ring (same alignment conclusion as S4), geometry verbatim-identical to the landing page's .mp-orb. The settle animation blooms a glow, then a bounded float/breathe (all iterations finite, per the "splash animations must be finite" renderer-wedge guard; gui-motion-off fully static). The tagline changes from "你的桌面编码智能体" to "结对工程师，处处与你同行 / Your pair engineer, on every surface." (matching the new site slogan). The now-unreferenced src/vendor/logo.png dead asset is deleted, the icon-sync contract shrinks from three places to two (build/icon.icns + build/icon-dock.png), and gui-design/gui-implementation docs are updated.

### Added

- **插件面板不再加载失败，官方插件也都有图标了**：插件自带的设置面板原先一律报「模块加载失败」——界面在组件还在下载时就回收了它的地址；现在加载完成后才释放。同时每个插件项都画出了自己的标识（插件声明的图标、内置单元的类别图标，或按包名生成的彩色字母标），状态改为标识角上的小圆点，不再和图标挤在一起。

- **插件列表支持一键卸载**：已安装插件卡片和列表行新增卸载按钮，确认后由守护进程移除插件目录并即时刷新列表；卸载失败时错误横幅提示且列表保持不变。
  - EN: One-click uninstall in the plugin list. Installed plugin cards and rows gain an uninstall button; after a confirmation the daemon removes the plugin directory and refreshes the list immediately, and a failed uninstall shows an error banner while leaving the list untouched.
- **插件图标可自定义**：插件可在清单里声明 `icon`（emoji、字符或图片 URL），列表立即采用；未声明时按包名生成确定的彩色字母标，同包同名同色。
  - EN: Customizable plugin icons. A plugin can declare `icon` in its manifest (emoji, glyph, or an image URL) and the list adopts it; without one, a deterministic colored letter mark is derived from the package name — same package, same mark.
- **what's-new 结构化亮点卡**：更新弹窗不再倾倒 CHANGELOG markdown 原文——client-core 新增纯函数解析器 `parseChangelogHighlights`（行级状态机按 `## [x.y.z] - date` 切段，`[Unreleased]` 跳过；每版提取 ≤3 条「亮点」（`**` 开头 bullet，标题 + 中文主行第一句，`- EN:` 子行绝不进入）+ Added/Fixed/Changed/Removed 分类计数，11 例契约测试），daemon 零改动。卡面：桌宠「庆祝」sprite 头部 + 「更新到 v X.Y.Z」大标题；当前版 ≤3 张亮点卡（kind 图标 + 两行 clamp 描述）；「本次还有 N 项修复与改进」折叠行点开才挂完整 Markdown；旧版本折叠列表（版本号 + 日期 + 计数徽章）；主按钮「知道了」（Enter）+ 外链「查看完整日志」。解析为空回退原整段渲染不白屏；reward 庆祝票根分支原样保留；退场沿用 180ms 保持挂载模式；新文案入 `announcement` i18n 窄域（双语镜像）。遗留待实机：长 changelog 滚动锚点、motion-off 下桌宠整体隐藏（引擎无静态帧，按「无中间帧」约束取舍）。
  - EN: what's-new structured highlight cards (branding spec S1, first cut after the 4 decision points were approved): the update popup no longer dumps raw CHANGELOG markdown — client-core gains the pure-function parser `parseChangelogHighlights` (a line-level state machine splitting on `## [x.y.z] - date`, skipping `[Unreleased]`; ≤3 "highlights" per version (`**`-led bullets: title + first sentence of the Chinese line, `- EN:` children never enter) plus Added/Fixed/Changed/Removed counts, with 11 contract tests); zero daemon changes. The card: a celebrating-pet sprite header + "updated to v X.Y.Z" headline; ≤3 highlight cards for the current version (kind icon + two-line-clamped description); a "N more fixes and improvements" fold that mounts the full Markdown only when opened; a collapsible older-version list (version + date + count badge); primary "Got it" (Enter) + external "Full changelog" link. Empty parse falls back to the original full rendering (no blank card); the reward-ticket branch is preserved as-is; exit keeps the 180ms stay-mounted pattern; new copy lives in the `announcement` i18n domain (bilingual mirror). Leftovers for on-device pass: scroll anchoring on long changelogs; motion-off hides the pet entirely (the engine has no static frame — a deliberate trade under the no-intermediate-frames rule).

- **guest/安卓壳液态玻璃收敛批次 B：连接屏↔composer / 移动列表↔会话场景 morph + 浮层 L2 对齐**：① 场景 morph 工具（client-core 新 `lib/scene-morph.ts`，复用 image-morph 纯内核不复制）——出场景在 React state 提交前克隆固定于原 viewport rect 并 WAAPI 收缩（height→0 + translateY，时长走 §3.2 高度阶梯 240-480ms），入场景 `useMorphTarget` 挂 `data-morph-id` 元素做 5 段线性化 translate+scale 飞行（360ms，`--spring-liquid`），motion-off（gui-motion-off / prefers-reduced-motion）两半都短路硬切；② ConnectScreen→会话 morph（pair 码/链接输入框与发送按钮作共享元素，仅 guest 壳）；③ 移动端（`isMobileShell()` 门）工作区列表↔会话双向 morph，共享元素为会话标题（卡片标题↔header 标题），Android 实体返回键语义不变；④ `.ss-card`（ModelThinkingSheet/AttachSheet/SessionsSheet/ServerSwitcher 共享视觉类）对齐 §2 L2 float 配方（glass sheen 顶层背景 + 1px glass-border + 单声明 glass-shadow，rim 内嵌），零新 token。纯函数（heightMorphMs/sceneKeyframes/sessionTitleMorphId）配 9 组分支/边界契约测试；desktop-app 零改动。遗留：未真机走查（克隆冻结屏/低端安卓深克隆开销待量化）；ss-card 透明度变化需并排截图确认。
  - EN: guest/Android shell liquid-glass convergence batch B: connect-screen↔composer / mobile list↔session scene morphs + float L2 alignment (M1.10). ① a scene-morph tool (client-core's new `lib/scene-morph.ts`, reusing the image-morph pure core — no copy): the leaving scene is cloned (including canvas bitmaps) into an overlay pinned at its old viewport rect and WAAPI-shrunk (height→0 + translateY, duration on the §3.2 height ladder 240-480ms) synchronously before the React state flip commits, while `useMorphTarget` flies the entering `data-morph-id` element with 5-segment linearized translate+scale (360ms, `--spring-liquid`); motion-off (gui-motion-off / prefers-reduced-motion) short-circuits both halves to a hard cut. ② ConnectScreen→session morph for all guest shells (pair-code/link inputs and the send button as shared elements). ③ Mobile-only (`isMobileShell()` gate) workspace-list↔session morph in both directions, sharing the session title (card title ↔ header title); the Android hardware-back semantics are unchanged. ④ `.ss-card` (the visual class shared by ModelThinkingSheet/AttachSheet/SessionsSheet/ServerSwitcher) aligns to the §2 L2 float recipe (glass-sheen top-layer background + 1px glass-border + single-declaration glass-shadow, rim embedded) with zero new tokens. Pure functions (heightMorphMs/sceneKeyframes/sessionTitleMorphId) carry 9 branch/boundary contract test groups; desktop-app untouched. Leftovers: no on-device pass yet (cloned freeze-frame / low-end Android deep-clone cost to be quantified); the ss-card transparency change needs a side-by-side screenshot check.

- **输入框「+」菜单扩展：已启用插件 / 当前工作区文件两个子选择器**：AttachMenu 尾部新增第三段「上下文」两项，点击在同锚点打开搜索浮层（复用 use-floating-menu 磨砂面与单例互斥：加载/错误/空/无结果四态、↑↓+Enter 键盘流、Esc/外点关闭且焦点回 composer、悬停与键盘高亮同址、文件列表 200 条截断提示）。插件选择器数据源 `extensions.list` 按启用态过滤，行 = 名称 + 描述 + 「已启用」徽记（与扩展中心同源零新 RPC）；文件选择器数据源 `workspace.tree`（gitignore 口径）拍平为文件列表，选中插入 `@相对路径 `（与 @ 补全选中后同形，走既有 fileMention 发送管线），插件选中插入纯插件名（不抢 `@`/`#` 前缀，避免与文件/会话补全冲突）。欢迎页（无会话工作区）整段隐藏。过滤纯函数（拍平/大小写不敏感过滤/截断）配 13 例契约测试；不新增 RPC、不动 coding-agent。
  - EN: composer「+」menu extension: enabled-plugins / workspace-files sub-pickers. Two "上下文" items append a third segment to the AttachMenu; each opens a search popup at the same anchor (reusing use-floating-menu's frosted surface and singleton mutex: loading/error/empty/no-result states, ↑↓+Enter keyboard flow, Esc/outside-click dismissal with focus returned to the composer, hover and keyboard highlight on the same row, and a 200-row truncation hint for files). The plugin picker sources `extensions.list` filtered to enabled state — rows show name + description + an "enabled" badge (same source as the extensions center, zero new RPCs); the file picker sources `workspace.tree` (gitignore semantics), flattens to a file list, and inserts `@relative/path ` on pick (same shape as the @ completion's final text, riding the existing fileMention send pipeline), while a plugin pick inserts the plain plugin name (no `@`/`#` prefix — avoiding collisions with the file/session completions). The whole segment stays hidden on the welcome page (no session workspace). Filter pure functions (flatten/case-insensitive filter/truncation) carry 13 contract tests; no new RPCs, no coding-agent changes.

### Fixed

- **历史分页零进展死循环 + 游标失效静默回退最新页**：长会话向上翻历史时，`session.history` 的游标是 entry-id `findIndex`——日记点检裁剪/resume 换视图后旧 id 消失，`idx === -1` 被静默当成「从最新开始」，返回的一页与客户端已持有的完全重叠、去重后整页丢弃、游标不动、hasMore 仍为真，于是每次上滚都重复同一个零进展 RPC；重复的 entry id（重试/重发，注释承认实机发生过）还会让 first-match 命中更早副本造成同样的全重叠页。修复：① daemon 找不到锚点显式回 `{ stale: true, entries: [] }`，不再回退最新页；锚点匹配改为 LAST 出现（分页客户端锚在最新物化的那条上）；响应携带显式 `nextBeforeId`，整页重叠时客户端采用它推进游标。② 客户端 store：整页重叠且 `nextBeforeId` 为已持有条目 → 采用新锚点（单调前进）；零进展页/空页但声称还有历史 → fail-soft 告警并收起折叠入口，不再死循环；daemon 判 stale → `markHistoryStale()` 停住分页。③ P0-8：`tail.hasMore=true` 但 `beforeId=null` 时从已加载窗口最旧条目推导游标（此前客户端折算成「无入口」，历史明明存在却上不去了），daemon 侧 id 异常记 warn。新增 daemon RPC 契约测试 5 例 + store 契约测试 8 例（含多页单调前进、重复 id 边界）。
  - EN: history paging zero-progress loop + stale cursor silently falling back to the latest page: scrolling up a long session relied on an entry-id `findIndex` cursor — after journal compaction dropped the anchor or resume swapped the view, `idx === -1` was silently treated as "start from latest", returning a page fully overlapping what the client holds; the dedup dropped it, the cursor never moved, hasMore stayed true, and every scroll re-issued the same zero-progress RPC. Duplicated entry ids (retries/re-emits, acknowledged in comments as seen in production) additionally let a first-match findIndex hit an earlier duplicate and produce the same fully-overlapped page. Fixes: ① the daemon now answers an unresolvable anchor with an explicit `{ stale: true, entries: [] }` instead of the latest page, matches the anchor at its LAST occurrence (the paging client anchors at the most recently materialized one), and returns an explicit `nextBeforeId` the client adopts to advance the cursor on a fully-overlapped page. ② the store: fully-overlapped page + a `nextBeforeId` the view holds → adopt the new anchor (monotone progress); a zero-progress or empty page that claims history remains → fail-soft warn and close the fold instead of looping; a daemon stale verdict → `markHistoryStale()` stops paging. ③ P0-8: `tail.hasMore=true` with `beforeId=null` now derives the cursor from the oldest loaded entry (previously the client folded this into "no entry" — the history existed but couldn't be reached), and the daemon warns when the tail id is abnormal. Five daemon RPC contract tests + eight store contract tests added (multi-page monotone walk, duplicate-id boundary included).

- **模型供应商品牌图标适配补全（实机回归 2026-09-29）**：kimi-code 选 `k3`/`k3-256k` 后输入框胶囊和模型菜单行只剩通用占位图标（k2 正常）——`model-brand-icon` 的 provider 映射没有 `kimi-code` 条目，id 回退又要求 id 含 `kimi`/`moonshot`，而 kimi-code 的裸 SKU（`k3`、`k3-256k`）不带任何家族 token，两层匹配全部落空。provider 映射补齐到覆盖内置 catalog 中所有有 Lobe 品牌的供应商（kimi-code、zai/zhipu-coding-plan、minimax 系、stepplan 系、opencode 系、xiaomi 系、bedrock/azure/cerebras/fireworks/github-copilot/qianfan/zenmux 等 70+ 条目）；id 回退新增裸 K SKU（`k2`/`k3` 前缀）、glm、step、minimax/abab、mimo、qwq/qvq、llama、mistral 家族、doubao、hunyuan/hy3、ernie/wenxin、yi、internlm、sensenova、command/cohere、nemotron、phi、solar 家族模式，models.yml 自定义供应商后的同族模型同样能解析出品牌标。
  - EN: provider brand-icon coverage completed (production regression 2026-09-29): picking kimi-code's `k3`/`k3-256k` left the composer capsule and model menu rows with the generic placeholder (k2 was fine) — the `model-brand-icon` provider map had no `kimi-code` entry, and the id fallback required `kimi`/`moonshot` in the id, which kimi-code's bare SKUs (`k3`, `k3-256k`) lack, so both resolution layers missed. The provider map now covers every bundled-catalog provider that has a Lobe brand (kimi-code, zai/zhipu-coding-plan, the minimax/stepplan/opencode/xiaomi families, bedrock/azure/cerebras/fireworks/github-copilot/qianfan/zenmux, …, 70+ entries), and the id fallback learns the bare K SKUs (`k2`/`k3` prefix), glm, step, minimax/abab, mimo, qwq/qvq, llama, the mistral family, doubao, hunyuan/hy3, ernie/wenxin, yi, internlm, sensenova, command/cohere, nemotron, phi, and solar — same-family models behind models.yml custom providers resolve to their brand too.

- **Kimi K3 系列模型补齐思考档位**：内置目录回退条目此前推导为通用四档梯子（minimal/low/medium/high），与 `/coding/v1` 实时声明的 `low/high/max` 不一致——家族谓词 `isKimiK3ModelId` 只认 `kimi-k3*` 命名空间形式，裸 SKU 漏网（该谓词还兼管 1M 上下文路由，不能直接把 256k SKU 并进去）。新增 `isKimiCodeK3ModelId` 专谓词接入梯子分支与 mandatory-reasoning 分支（上游 `supports_thinking_type: "only"`），`compat/anthropic.ts` 里手补的 `spec.id === "k3"` 散点同步收口到该谓词。行为即刻生效：`buildModel` 对陈旧显式梯子做归一化，无需等待 models.json 重生成（本次实跑 `bun run gen:models` 确认 diff 含 2.4 万行无关上游漂移，按仓库节奏留待下次例行重生成烘焙）。resolver 级回归测试覆盖稀疏推导与陈旧显式梯子归一化两条路径；catalog 全量 622 例全绿。
  - EN: The Kimi K3 family (`k3` / `k3-256k`) now carries its thinking-effort ladder: the bundled fallback entries previously derived the generic four-rung ladder (minimal/low/medium/high), disagreeing with the live `/coding/v1` declaration of `low/high/max` — the family predicate `isKimiK3ModelId` only matches the `kimi-k3*` namespace forms, so the bare SKUs fell through (and that predicate also gates 1M-context routing, so the 256k SKU must not be folded into it). A new dedicated `isKimiCodeK3ModelId` predicate feeds the ladder branch and the mandatory-reasoning branch (upstream `supports_thinking_type: "only"`), and the hand-patched `spec.id === "k3"` scatter in `compat/anthropic.ts` is folded into it. Behavior is fixed immediately — `buildModel` normalizes stale explicit ladders, so no models.json regen is required (a trial `bun run gen:models` produced 24k lines of unrelated upstream drift, left for the next routine regeneration to bake). Resolver-level regression tests cover both the sparse-derivation and stale-explicit-ladder normalization paths; all 622 catalog tests green.

- **欢迎页（空态）输入框思考档位不随模型切换（实机回归 2026-09-29）**：欢迎页选了新模型后，思考等级菜单仍显示旧模型的档位（看起来"固定"）——`WelcomeComposer` 抓取档位梯子用的 `effectiveModelId = presetModelId ?? modelId` 没套 `modelTouched` 优先级，而 `presetModelId` 在配置了 DEFAULT 或打开过任何会话后恒非空，用户的选择永远轮不到；创建会话用的却是 `modelTouched.current ? modelId : effectiveModelId`，于是胶囊上显示的是新模型、档位菜单却是预设模型的梯子。抓取模型 id 改为与 `session.create` 同一优先级（真选优先于 DEFAULT/会话快照），切换模型即重新查询 `models.detail`。
  - EN: welcome (empty-state) composer thinking ladder ignored model switches (production regression 2026-09-29): after picking a new model on the welcome screen, the thinking-level menu still showed the old model's rungs (appearing "fixed") — the ladder fetch keyed on `effectiveModelId = presetModelId ?? modelId` without the `modelTouched` precedence, and since `presetModelId` is non-null whenever a DEFAULT is configured or any session was opened, the user's pick never reached it. Session creation, however, used `modelTouched.current ? modelId : effectiveModelId`, so the capsule displayed the new model while the menu showed the preset model's ladder. The ladder fetch now uses the same precedence as `session.create` (a real pick beats the DEFAULT/session snapshot) and re-queries `models.detail` on every model switch.

- **对话内分叉条乱跳/假分支（实机回归 2026-09-28）**：① 轮中间凭空冒出"此节点有 2 个分支"——app.tsx 的 `childCount` 把 compaction/custom 簿记兄弟也数成孩子（如 toolResult 节点挂着 assistant 消息 + compaction 摘要两条"分支"），分叉条门控改用 Transcript 内部已过滤的 `branchChildren`（只含会话分叉 message），调用方传全量 map 也复活不了假条，条上计数与可切换列表因此永远一致；② 折叠/展开工作过程时分叉条位置"变来变去"——锚点行折进零高度槽后条仍留在原像素位置，现随锚点一起隐藏（折叠段/工具汇总尾行收起时不可见，展开后回原位）。回归测试 2 例（全量 childCount 不复活假条；折叠收起隐藏、展开恢复）。
  - EN: in-conversation branch bar jumping / phantom branches (production regression 2026-09-28): ① a phantom "this node has 2 branches" divider appeared mid-round — app.tsx's `childCount` counts compaction/custom bookkeeping siblings as children (e.g. a toolResult node with an assistant message + a compaction summary as its two "branches"), so the bar gate now uses Transcript's pre-filtered `branchChildren` (session-fork messages only), which no caller-supplied full map can resurrect, and the bar count and its switchable list can no longer diverge; ② the bar's position "kept changing" while folding/unfolding the work section — the anchor row collapsed into a zero-height slot but the bar stayed at its old pixel position, so it now hides together with the anchor (invisible while a fold span / summarized run tail is collapsed, back in place when expanded). Two regression tests (a full childCount cannot resurrect phantom bars; hidden while folded, restored when expanded).

- **能力中心用户级技能卸载被误拒（"only user-level file skills can be deleted"）**：daemon `skills.delete` 的来源守卫把 `provider === "native"` 整组拒掉，但 native provider 同时扫 bundled 技能与用户安装的技能（都在 `~/.musepi/agent/skills`，provider/level 完全相同）——用户装的技能（如 dev-expert）显示「用户级」、卸载按钮可点，点了却被 daemon 拒绝。守卫改为按 `BUNDLED_SKILL_NAMES` 名单拒内置技能，用户安装的 native user 级技能放行；同时删除目标从只删 SKILL.md 升级为整目录删除（仅限直接挂在 user skills 根下的技能目录，与卸载确认文案"删除目录及脚本资源"一致；包内技能仍只删 SKILL.md）。守卫与删除目标拆为纯函数 `skillDeleteBlockReason`/`resolveSkillDeleteTarget`，6 例契约测试。GUI 同步收口：卸载确认弹窗文案从「删除其 SKILL.md 文件」改为「将删除其目录及其中的脚本与资源，且不可撤销」（与抽屉风险块、实际删除行为三方对齐）；能力中心卸载按钮显隐改为完整镜像 daemon 守卫（bundled 名单 + musepi-managed + 扩展虚拟技能排除，修复原先 `source.startsWith("managed")` 永不命中的死判断），bundled 技能级别标签从「用户级」修正为「内置」并显示「随客户端分发 · 不可卸载」脚注、「官方」筛选与计数同步纳入；扩展中心 `isDeletable` 同步放行用户安装的 native user 级技能（bundled 行靠注册表 `builtin` 标注排除）。另修复卸载/安装后列表陈旧：`skills.delete`/`skills.install` 现在同时清 `loadCapability` 的 5s TTL 扫描缓存（`resetCapabilities()`，与 builtin-marketplace 变更后同款）——此前确认卸载后的立即重拉命中删除瞬间刚刷新的扫描缓存，能力中心里「卸载了但卡片还在」直到下次挂载。
  - EN: capability-center uninstall wrongly rejected for user-level skills ("only user-level file skills can be deleted"): the daemon `skills.delete` source guard blanket-rejected `provider === "native"`, but the native provider scans BOTH bundled skills and user-installed ones (same `~/.musepi/agent/skills` dir, identical provider/level) — a user-installed skill (e.g. dev-expert) showed as "user level" with a clickable uninstall button that the daemon then refused. The guard now rejects bundled skills by the `BUNDLED_SKILL_NAMES` list and lets user-installed native user-level skills through; the delete target is also upgraded from removing just SKILL.md to removing the whole skill directory (only for dirs directly under the user skills root, matching the uninstall confirmation copy "deletes the directory including scripts and resources"; package-internal skills still lose only their SKILL.md). Guard and target are pure functions `skillDeleteBlockReason`/`resolveSkillDeleteTarget` with contract tests including a real-scan regression for the cache fix below. GUI mirrors the fix end-to-end: the uninstall confirmation copy changes from "removes its SKILL.md file" to "deletes its directory, including scripts and resources, and cannot be undone" (confirmation dialog, drawer risk block, and actual delete behavior now agree); the capability center's uninstall-button visibility fully mirrors the daemon guard (bundled name list + musepi-managed + extension virtual skills excluded — fixing the old `source.startsWith("managed")` check that could never match), bundled skills now display as "built-in" instead of "user level" with the "ships with the client · cannot uninstall" note, and the "official" filter/counts include them; the extensions center `isDeletable` likewise allows user-installed native user-level skills (bundled rows are excluded via the registry's `builtin` annotation). Also fixed stale lists after uninstall/install: `skills.delete`/`skills.install` now also drop the `loadCapability` 5s TTL scan cache (`resetCapabilities()`, same as post-mutation resets in builtin-marketplace) — previously the immediate refetch after confirming an uninstall hit the scan cache refreshed by the delete itself, so the capability center showed "uninstalled but the card is still there" until the next mount.

## [0.5.0] - 2026-09-28

### Added

- **插件面板不再加载失败，官方插件也都有图标了**：插件自带的设置面板原先一律报「模块加载失败」——界面在组件还在下载时就回收了它的地址；现在加载完成后才释放。同时每个插件项都画出了自己的标识（插件声明的图标、内置单元的类别图标，或按包名生成的彩色字母标），状态改为标识角上的小圆点，不再和图标挤在一起。
  - EN: Plugin panels no longer fail to load, and official plugins have icons too. A plugin's own settings panel always reported "module failed to load" — the UI released its address while the download was still in flight; it now waits for the load to finish. Every plugin also draws its own mark (a declared icon, a built-in unit's kind glyph, or a colored letter tile derived from the package name), and status moved to a corner badge instead of crowding the icon.
- **插件详情弹窗补齐**：点击已安装插件打开详情弹窗——大图标、版本、状态与启用开关、头部卸载按钮；插件自带的组件收进弹窗「包含的组件」逐项开关（不再与插件包重复占列表行），插件的设置界面（如账号池管理）也挂载在弹窗内。
  - EN: The plugin detail dialog completes the picture. Clicking an installed plugin opens a dialog with the large icon, version, status, enable toggle and an uninstall button in the header; the plugin's own modules collapse into per-item included-component toggles (no longer duplicated as separate list rows), and its settings surface (an account pool, for example) mounts inside the dialog.
- **插件列表支持一键卸载**：已安装插件卡片和列表行新增卸载按钮，确认后由守护进程移除插件目录并即时刷新列表；卸载失败时错误横幅提示且列表保持不变。
  - EN: One-click uninstall in the plugin list. Installed plugin cards and rows gain an uninstall button; after a confirmation the daemon removes the plugin directory and refreshes the list immediately, and a failed uninstall shows an error banner while leaving the list untouched.
- **插件图标可自定义**：插件可在清单里声明 `icon`（emoji、字符或图片 URL），列表立即采用；未声明时按包名生成确定的彩色字母标，同包同名同色。
  - EN: Customizable plugin icons. A plugin can declare `icon` in its manifest (emoji, glyph, or an image URL) and the list adopts it; without one, a deterministic colored letter mark is derived from the package name — same package, same mark.
- **素材策略行 + 高级折叠区 + 媒体 provider 内联**：欢迎页 design 模式的 composer 下方、设计体系 rail 之下新增「素材：◉ AI 生图 / ○ 色块占位」单选（对齐 WorkBuddy「素材不足时的处理策略」语义，默认 AI 生图），选中值随发送落 `projectMetadata.assetPolicy: "ai-image" | "placeholder"`（默认也落键，契约明确）；daemon metadata 校验值域放行、非法值（含 null）fail-fast 语义报错。其旁「高级 ▸」折叠区（展开态入创作草稿、切类型 chip 不丢）承载类型特有字段（平台/保真度/画幅/时长/音色，默认值沿用 M3.1 对表）——图片/视频/音频 chip 选中时区内内联媒体 provider 选择卡（数据源 `media.providers` RPC，未配置的 agnes-global 渲染为禁用），选中 provider 落 metadata。prompt 注入新增 asset-policy 区块（order 41，紧随设计体系简报 40 之后，每次 rebuildSystemPrompt 重解析）：ai-image = 产物引用处走 generate_image/agnes_video_gen 工具链；placeholder = 色块加文字标注占位、明确禁止调用两工具；无键/未知值不注入不炸。契约测试：assetPolicy 落 metadata 逐字段、非法值报错、prompt 三态注入、折叠态切 chip 不丢、视频 chip 选 agnes 端到端建会话。
  - EN: asset-policy row + advanced fold + inline media provider. Below the design-system rail under the welcome composer in design mode, a new "素材: ◉ AI 生图 / ○ 色块占位" single-select (WorkBuddy-style "asset strategy when material is scarce" semantics, defaulting to AI image generation) writes `projectMetadata.assetPolicy: "ai-image" | "placeholder"` on send (the default lands the key too — explicit contract); the daemon metadata validator admits the key with value-domain validation, failing fast on illegal values (including null). Beside it, an "高级 ▸" fold (open state in the creation draft, survives chip switches) carries type-specific fields (platform/fidelity/aspect/duration/voice, defaults per the M3.1 table) — with image/video/audio chips selected, a media-provider card is inlined (data source `media.providers` RPC; unconfigured agnes-global renders disabled), the chosen provider landing in metadata. Prompt injection gains an asset-policy section (order 41, right behind the design-system brief at 40, re-resolved on every rebuild): ai-image = route asset references through the generate_image/agnes_video_gen toolchain; placeholder = color-block placeholders with text labels and an explicit ban on both tools; missing/unknown values inject nothing and never throw. Contract tests: assetPolicy-in-metadata field-by-field, illegal-value errors, prompt three-state injection, fold state surviving chip switches, and an end-to-end video-chip-agnes session creation.
- **设计体系「＋市场」卡 + 装包即见的宿主重放通路**：① 预览 rail 末尾追加「＋市场」从属卡（dashed 边框 + 加号，radiogroup 语义拆分保 a11y），点击经既有 host 事件 `omp-open-capability`（扩展 `detail.tab` 载荷）落地能力中心市场 tab（CapabilityCenterPage 新增 `initialTab`，旧调用方行为不变）。② 市场分发收敛为目录约定 + GUI 纯推导：client-core 新增 `hasDesignSystemContent()` 纯函数（市场条目 category/tags 声明 `design-system` 即命中，大小写/连字符同义），MarketplaceCard 渲染「设计体系」徽章，筛选复用既有 tag chip，零 wire 改动。③ 「装包即见」daemon 补齐：设计体系注册表此前只在会话引导时重放（宿主进程无会话则 welcome rail 只见内置五套）——现 DesignSystemsService.listDesignSystems 改为宿主级懒加载重放（经 ExtensionService 新 `getExtensionRuntimeLoad()` 同源加载，按 sourceId 先清再注册幂等重写，对已消失来源整源清除），marketplace.install/remove 补 `invalidateExtensionsCache` + `extensions.changed` 广播（失效序契约测试钉死），GUI 监听广播即重拉、装包返回 rail 即见新体系。契约测试 7 例（GUI）+ 6 例（daemon 宿主重放/幂等/失效序/事件名）。
  - EN: design-system "＋市场" card + install-and-see host replay pipeline. ① a trailing "＋市场" subordinate card on the preview rail (dashed border + plus icon, radiogroup semantics split for a11y) opens the capability center's marketplace tab via the existing `omp-open-capability` host event (new optional `detail.tab` payload; CapabilityCenterPage gains `initialTab`, legacy callers unchanged). ② market distribution converges on a catalog convention + pure GUI inference: client-core gains `hasDesignSystemContent()` (a marketplace entry's category/tags declaring `design-system` — case/hyphen synonymous — marks it), MarketplaceCard renders a "设计体系" badge, and filtering reuses the existing tag chips — zero wire changes. ③ the daemon "install-and-see" gap: the design-system registry previously only replayed at session bootstrap (a host process with no session showed just the five built-ins on the welcome rail) — DesignSystemsService.listDesignSystems now does a host-level lazy replay (via ExtensionService's new `getExtensionRuntimeLoad()`, same-source loading, idempotent clear-then-register per sourceId, whole-source cleanup for vanished sources), and marketplace.install/remove add `invalidateExtensionsCache` + the `extensions.changed` broadcast (invalidation-order pinned by a contract test), so the GUI — already listening — refetches on return and the new system is on the rail immediately. Contract tests: 7 (GUI) + 6 (daemon host replay / idempotency / invalidation order / event name).

### Added

- **插件面板不再加载失败，官方插件也都有图标了**：插件自带的设置面板原先一律报「模块加载失败」——界面在组件还在下载时就回收了它的地址；现在加载完成后才释放。同时每个插件项都画出了自己的标识（插件声明的图标、内置单元的类别图标，或按包名生成的彩色字母标），状态改为标识角上的小圆点，不再和图标挤在一起。

A plugin's own settings panel always reported "module failed to load" — the UI released its address while the download was still in flight; it now waits for the load to finish. Every plugin also draws its own mark (a declared icon, a built-in unit's kind glyph, or a colored letter tile derived from the package name), and status moved to a corner badge instead of crowding the icon.
- **插件详情弹窗补齐**：点击已安装插件打开详情弹窗——大图标、版本、状态与启用开关、头部卸载按钮；插件自带的组件收进弹窗「包含的组件」逐项开关（不再与插件包重复占列表行），插件的设置界面（如账号池管理）也挂载在弹窗内。

Clicking an installed plugin opens a dialog with the large icon, version, status, enable toggle and an uninstall button in the header; the plugin's own modules collapse into per-item included-component toggles (no longer duplicated as separate list rows), and its settings surface (an account pool, for example) mounts inside the dialog.
- **插件列表支持一键卸载**：已安装插件卡片和列表行新增卸载按钮，确认后由守护进程移除插件目录并即时刷新列表；卸载失败时错误横幅提示且列表保持不变。
  - EN: One-click uninstall in the plugin list. Installed plugin cards and rows gain an uninstall button; after a confirmation the daemon removes the plugin directory and refreshes the list immediately, and a failed uninstall shows an error banner while leaving the list untouched.
- **插件图标可自定义**：插件可在清单里声明 `icon`（emoji、字符或图片 URL），列表立即采用；未声明时按包名生成确定的彩色字母标，同包同名同色。
  - EN: Customizable plugin icons. A plugin can declare `icon` in its manifest (emoji, glyph, or an image URL) and the list adopts it; without one, a deterministic colored letter mark is derived from the package name — same package, same mark.
- **中文语音模型双档（中文优化 ASR + 中文 TTS）**：语音识别新增 SenseVoiceSmall 档（阿里 FunAudioLLM 出品、sherpa-onnx 分发 INT8 ≈239MB，中/英/日/韩/粤自动识别，中英混合是其主场；daemon sherpa-onnx 引擎新增 sense_voice 加载分支，repo/revision/字节数在加载时逐字核对）——语音设置里标注「中文优化·中英混合」，与既有 Fast/Balanced/Turbo/Parakeet 四档并存。TTS 新增 MeloTTS-zh 档（sherpa-onnx 官方 vits-melo-tts-zh_en，44.1kHz）——daemon TTS worker 加 sherpa-onnx 引擎分支与 Kokoro 并存，中文文本自动路由到中文模型（批量与流式逐段都路由），Kokoro 英文档不变。两档的下载/缓存/预热走既有管线。提醒：权重首次真实下载/推理在本机尚未实测（HF 直连不通的环境会在 worker 下载/加载处显性报错）。
  - EN: two Chinese speech model tiers (Chinese-optimized ASR + Chinese TTS): speech recognition gains a SenseVoiceSmall tier (Alibaba FunAudioLLM, sherpa-onnx distributed INT8 ≈239MB, auto zh/en/ja/ko/yue, with zh/en mixed speech as its home turf — the daemon sherpa-onnx engine gains a sense_voice loading branch, repo/revision/byte counts verified at load), labeled "中文优化 · 中英混合" in voice settings alongside the existing Fast/Balanced/Turbo/Parakeet tiers. TTS gains a MeloTTS-zh tier (sherpa-onnx official vits-melo-tts-zh_en, 44.1kHz) — the daemon TTS worker gains a sherpa-onnx engine branch coexisting with Kokoro, and Chinese text auto-routes to the Chinese model (both batch and streaming per-segment routing); the Kokoro English tier is unchanged. Both tiers ride the existing download/cache/preheat pipeline. Note: first real download/inference of the weights has not been exercised on this machine (in an HF-unreachable environment the worker fails loudly at download/load).
- **朗读模型选择卡 + 测试语音模拟真实对话**：TTS 模型选择此前只有 TUI 有、GUI 缺失（始终锁在不能读中文的 Kokoro）——语音设置新增「朗读模型」单选项卡，与识别模型卡同构（STT/TTS 两卡共享同一 SpeechModelPicker：radio 行 + 徽章 + 人性化副文案 + 右侧下载/就绪按钮），写 `tts.localModel`；daemon 补 `tts.modelStatus`/`tts.modelDownload` RPC（与 STT 同款去重防重入契约，进度经事件流广播）。测试语音输入/输出改为模拟真实对话：听写结果渲染成一条模拟用户消息（消息流同款样式 + 重新录制），TTS 测试是一条内置中英混合示例 markdown 的模拟助手回复卡（点消息上的朗读按钮播放，体现 sanitize 清洗效果）。契约测试 `voice-settings.test.tsx`。
  - EN: local-TTS model picker + test-voice simulation of a real conversation: TTS model selection existed only in the TUI — the GUI was locked to the Chinese-incapable Kokoro — so voice settings gain a "朗读模型" single option card, structurally identical to the recognition card (the STT/TTS cards share one SpeechModelPicker: radio rows + badges + human scenario copy + download/ready buttons on the right), writing `tts.localModel`; the daemon gains `tts.modelStatus`/`tts.modelDownload` RPCs (same dedup/reentrancy contract as STT, progress broadcast on the event stream). Test voice input/output now simulate a real conversation: the dictation result renders as a mock user message (message-stream styling + re-record), and the TTS test is a mock assistant-reply card with a built-in zh/en mixed markdown sample (a speak button on the message plays it, showing the sanitize effect). Contract tests in `voice-settings.test.tsx`.
- **技能市场安装状态机（断网/取消/脚本拦截三场景各有明确状态与恢复路径）**：`skills.marketplace.install` 从一次性动作升级为状态机驱动——RPC 立即返回 `installId`（旧阻塞式装不完大包就撞 15s RPC 超时），进度与终态经新广播事件 `marketplace.install.state` 推送；新增 `skills.marketplace.status`（状态查询）/ `skills.marketplace.cancel`（取消，半成品 staging 与目标目录全部清理）/ `skills.marketplace.approve`（脚本批准）三个 RPC。断网在下载阶段归类 `failed{kind:network}`、重试从头安装；安装内容含可执行脚本（.sh/.bat/.ps1/exe 类扩展名或 package.json install hooks）时进入 `awaiting-approval` 待批准态，批准后继续复制、拒绝则 `failed{kind:script-declined}` 不落盘。GUI 技能市场卡片显示实时状态、可取消，失败给语义文案与重试按钮。
  - EN: skill-marketplace install state machine (network failure / cancel / script interception each get an explicit state and recovery path). `skills.marketplace.install` upgrades from a one-shot action to a state machine — the RPC returns `installId` immediately (the old blocking shape could not finish a large download inside the 15s RPC timeout) while progress and terminal states ride the new `marketplace.install.state` broadcast; new `skills.marketplace.status` (query), `skills.marketplace.cancel` (cancels and cleans all half-written staging/target files), and `skills.marketplace.approve` (script approval) RPCs. A download-phase failure lands as `failed{kind:network}` with retry-from-scratch; content carrying executable scripts (.sh/.bat/.ps1/exe-like extensions or package.json install hooks) pauses in `awaiting-approval` — approving continues the copy, declining lands as `failed{kind:script-declined}` with nothing written. The GUI skill market shows live state and a cancel affordance on cards, plus semantic failure copy with a retry button.
- **web_search 新增 Bing（免凭据）与博查（Bochaai）两个提供商**：Bing 走 `cn.bing.com` SERP 抓取（`providers/bing.ts`，解开 `ck/a` 跳转链接、`recency` 映射 `filters=ex1:"ez*"`），大陆无代理直连可达，成为免凭据兜底链中第一个国内可用的零配置引擎；博查为大陆原生搜索 API（`providers/bochaai.ts`，`BOCHAAI_API_KEY`/`BOCHA_API_KEY` 或存储凭据，`recency` 映射 `freshness`，`after:`/`before:` 映射绝对日期区间）。两者同时进入设置/向导的 provider 列表，Bing 亦加入 Public Web 并行扇出。
  - EN: web_search gains Bing (credential-free) and Bochaai (博查) providers: Bing scrapes the `cn.bing.com` SERP (`providers/bing.ts`, unwraps `ck/a` redirect links, maps `recency` to `filters=ex1:"ez*"`), which is directly reachable from mainland China without a proxy — the first zero-config keyless engine that works there; Bochaai is a China-hosted search API (`providers/bochaai.ts`, `BOCHAAI_API_KEY`/`BOCHA_API_KEY` or a stored credential, `recency` → `freshness`, `after:`/`before:` → absolute date range). Both appear in the settings/setup provider lists, and Bing joins the Public Web fan-out.
- **web_search 连通性失败记忆与可操作报错**：provider 在任何 HTTP 交换之前失败（DNS/TCP/TLS/socket 级，如大陆直连海外引擎）会被进程内记录并在 auto 链中跳过 5 分钟（显式选择的 provider 不受影响，成功一次即清除），同进程后续搜索不再为已死引擎逐个白等硬超时；当所有 provider 都是连接级失败时，错误信息追加三条出路指引（设 `HTTPS_PROXY`/`HTTP_PROXY`、`providers.webSearchOrder` 前置 kimi/zai/bochaai/searxng、`providers.webSearchExclude` 排除不可达引擎）。
  - EN: web_search connectivity-failure memory and actionable errors: a provider that fails before any HTTP exchange (DNS/TCP/TLS/socket level — e.g. overseas engines on a direct mainland connection) is recorded in-process and skipped by the auto chain for 5 minutes (explicitly selected providers are unaffected; a single success clears the record), so later searches in the same process stop paying a hard-timeout wait per dead engine; when every provider fails at the connection level, the error message appends three remedies (set `HTTPS_PROXY`/`HTTP_PROXY`, prefer kimi/zai/bochaai/searxng via `providers.webSearchOrder`, or drop unreachable engines via `providers.webSearchExclude`).
- **web_search 客户端体验：结构化错误卡、实时进度、提供商显示名、有序提供商排序**：① 搜索失败不再原样倾倒英文 `Error:` 长文——GUI 渲染为本地化错误卡（标题 + 详情），桌面端附「打开搜索设置」按钮直达设置→提供商（新 host 能力 `ToolRenderHost.openSettings`）；② 自动链逐个尝试 provider 时流式播报进度（TUI「Trying X」面板列出已失败者，GUI 卡片实时流行同步），不再只有沉默的 spinner；③ 工具卡提供商显示显示名（`Mojeek`）而非配置 id（`mojeek`）——daemon 在 `SearchRenderDetails` 新增 `providerLabel` wire 字段；④ 设置中的有序数组（`ui.ordered`，如 `providers.webSearchOrder`）在 GUI 可排序——已选 chip 按优先级排列并附 ←/→ 移动按钮（TUI ordered-multiselect parity）；TUI 错误面板长消息按宽度折行，不再撑爆单行。
  - EN: web_search client experience — structured error card, live progress, provider display names, and ordered provider sorting: ① failures no longer dump the raw English `Error:` text — the GUI renders a localized error card (title + detail) with an "Open search settings" button on desktop jumping straight to Settings → Providers (new `ToolRenderHost.openSettings` host capability); ② the auto chain streams progress as each provider is tried (TUI "Trying X" panel listing the already-failed, GUI live stream line under the card) instead of a silent spinner; ③ tool cards show the provider display label (`Mojeek`) instead of the raw config id (`mojeek`) — the daemon fills a new `providerLabel` wire field on `SearchRenderDetails`; ④ ordered array settings (`ui.ordered`, e.g. `providers.webSearchOrder`) are reorderable in the GUI — selected chips lead in priority order with ←/→ move buttons (TUI ordered-multiselect parity); the TUI error panel wraps long messages to width instead of one overflowing line.

- **插件/扩展启停覆盖层契约测试**：enable/disable 全路径收敛断言为纯增量覆盖层——`extensions.setEnabled`（disabledExtensions / 镜像设置键 / mcp.json denylist）与 `plugins.setEnabled`（musepi-plugins.lock.json）toggle 前后插件内容物递归 sha256 不变，覆盖层 diff 恰好只含目标 id 的增/删（无冗余快照、不触碰其他条目）；新套件 `test/daemon/extension-toggle-overlay.test.ts` 7 个契约用例。
  - EN: contract tests pin the enable/disable paths to pure-overlay semantics: toggling via `extensions.setEnabled` (disabledExtensions / mirrored setting keys / the mcp.json denylist) and `plugins.setEnabled` (musepi-plugins.lock.json) leaves plugin content byte-identical (recursive sha256) across the toggle, and the overlay diff contains exactly the toggled id's add/remove — no redundant snapshots, no collateral edits; new suite `test/daemon/extension-toggle-overlay.test.ts` with 7 contract cases.

- **技能可独立停用，不再只能卸载**：能力中心的技能启停从 GUI 端 `settings.get`/`settings.set` 读改写改为 daemon 服务端一次完成——`skills.ignoredSkills` 是无 UI 元数据的 glob 列表（`settings.set` 的只读白名单拒写，点「停用」直接报错），专用 RPC 同时消除卡片/抽屉/批量三路并发互相覆盖的窗口；追加/移除精确名，用户手写的 glob 模式原样保留。契约测试 `test/daemon/skills-set-ignored.test.ts`。
  - EN: Skills can now be disabled on their own instead of only being uninstalled: capability-center enable/disable moves from GUI-side `settings.get`/`settings.set` read-modify-write to a single daemon-side write — `skills.ignoredSkills` is a glob list without UI metadata (the settings.set read-only allowlist refused it, so "Disable" errored out), and the dedicated RPC also removes the race where card, drawer, and bulk toggles could overwrite each other; exact names are added/removed while hand-written glob patterns stay untouched. Contract tests in `test/daemon/skills-set-ignored.test.ts`.

- **欢迎页创作面板（六 tab 创建流程 + 会话模板）**：欢迎页 mode chip 选中「设计」即展开全屏创作面板（L2 覆盖层，Escape/收起回到欢迎页）——原型 / 实况产物(Beta) / 演示稿 / 模板 / 媒体 / 其他六个 tab，公共底栏统一收项目名与工作目录。每个 tab 的字段按契约编译为 `projectMetadata` 随 `session.create` 一次落盘：实况产物落档为 prototype + intent 标记并强制高保真，媒体分支收 provider/画幅/时长/音色/提示词模板；daemon 侧在会话引导前完成 ≤16KiB 与版本校验（fail-fast 语义报错），落盘双写——会话头为权威（view-store 快照头 + 查询列双保留，流式持久化不再冲掉创作配置），`<cwd>/.musepi/project.json` 镜像尽力而为（写失败不阻断创建）。模板 tab 接入新会话模板机制（`~/.musepi/creation-templates/*.json` 一模板一文件，坏文件跳过不炸列表）：创建成功的 toast 上可一键「保存为模板」，各 tab 的模板 rail（Blank 恒首位）整体回填表单，模板删除走确认弹窗。新 RPC：`creation.templates.list/save/delete`、`creation.metadata.get`（sessionId 权威头或 cwd 镜像二选一读回）。动效全量取档既有玻璃/弹簧规范（240ms 入场 / 140ms 退场、tab 下划线滑动、内容交叉淡入），`gui-motion-off`/`prefers-reduced-motion` 全部归零。
  - EN: welcome-page creation panel (six-tab creation flow + session templates): picking the "Design" mode chip on the welcome page expands a full-screen creation panel (L2 overlay, Escape/collapse returns to the welcome page) with six tabs — Prototype / Live Artifact (Beta) / Deck / Template / Media / Other — over a shared footer collecting project name and workspace. Each tab's fields compile into `projectMetadata` persisted atomically with `session.create`: Live Artifact lands as prototype + an intent marker with fidelity forced to high, and the media branch captures provider/aspect/duration/voice/prompt template; the daemon validates the ≤16KiB cap and version before session bootstrap (fail-fast semantic errors), then double-writes — the session header is authoritative (preserved across both the view-store snapshot header and the query column, so streaming persists no longer clobber the creation config) while the `<cwd>/.musepi/project.json` mirror is best-effort (a failed mirror write never blocks creation). The Template tab wires up the new session-template store (`~/.musepi/creation-templates/*.json`, one file per template, corrupt files skipped without emptying the list): the success toast offers one-click "Save as template", each tab's template rail (Blank always first) rehydrates the whole form, and template deletion goes through a confirm dialog. New RPCs: `creation.templates.list/save/delete` and `creation.metadata.get` (sessionId authoritative header or cwd mirror, either one reads back). All motion reuses the existing glass/spring spec (240ms enter / 140ms exit, sliding tab underline, cross-fading content) and fully zeroes out under `gui-motion-off`/`prefers-reduced-motion`.
- **design 模式内联创作排**：欢迎页 mode chip 选中「设计」后，类型 chip 排（原型/实况产物/演示稿/图片/视频/音频/模板/其他，单选、默认原型，选中即把 kind/intent 写进创作草稿）直接出现在空态输入框上方一行——没有弹层、没有第二个输入框，发送就走欢迎页 composer 本体（附件/语音/提及/模型选择同源，placeholder 随选中类型变化），创建会话并把首轮消息与 `projectMetadata` 一次落盘（metadata 编译管线不动，chip + 默认值路径产出的字段与 M3.2 表单逐字段一致，回归复用 `creation.test.ts`）；`template` chip 在 chip 排下方内联展开会话模板 rail（点行即建）；「项目名」「工作目录」字段退役（标题由首轮消息生成，cwd 复用当前工作目录）；chip 排横向溢出带左右边缘羽化。布局收口：chip 排落在工作区 chip 排上方一行（顺序：问候语 → 类型 chip → 工作区 chip → 输入框），进出场走 Reveal 条件区块动效（高度 240ms + 淡出 160ms + 内层 6px 位移，收起期组件保持挂载、草稿不丢，`gui-motion-off`/`prefers-reduced-motion` 归零），欢迎页输入框容器加宽至 720px（底栏按钮不再折行）。契约测试 `creation-mode-page.test.tsx`（chip→metadata 逐字段一致性、发送、template rail、Escape 收合、草稿回填、字段退役、无弹层残留）。
  - EN: design mode gains an inline creation row: picking the "Design" mode chip on the welcome page shows a row of type chips (Prototype / Live Artifact / Deck / Image / Video / Audio / Template / Other, single-select, Prototype by default; selecting one writes kind/intent into the creation draft) directly above the empty-state composer — no overlay, no second input box. Sending rides the welcome composer itself (attachments / voice / mentions / model picker all shared, placeholder follows the selected type) and creates the session with the first message plus `projectMetadata` persisted in one shot (the metadata pipeline is untouched; the chip + defaults path is field-for-field identical to the M3.2 form — `creation.test.ts` stays the regression net). The `template` chip expands the session-template rail inline below the chip row (click a row to create); the "Project name" / "Workspace" fields retire (titles come from the first message, cwd reuses the current workspace); the chip row fades at its left/right edges when overflowing. Layout wrap-up: the chip row sits one line above the workspace chip row (order: greeting → type chips → workspace chips → composer), enter/exit rides the Reveal conditional-block motion (height 240ms + 160ms fade + 6px inner translate; the component stays mounted while collapsing so drafts survive, fully zeroed under `gui-motion-off`/`prefers-reduced-motion`), and the welcome composer container widens to 720px so the footer buttons no longer wrap. Contract tests in `creation-mode-page.test.tsx` (chip→metadata parity, send, template rail, Escape collapse, draft refill, field retirement, no overlay residue).

- **设计体系改为可注册能力，扩展可声明新的设计体系**：设计风格从 GUI 硬编码表升级为 daemon 侧注册表——内置五套（minimal/glass/editorial/neubrutalism/darkneon，id 稳定、swatches/tokens 取自既有 gui token 阶梯）+ 扩展经 `pi.registerDesignSystem()` 注册（与 media provider 同一 loader 生命周期：pending→会话初始化重放、扩展 reload/unload 按 sourceId 整源清除、撞内置/撞已注册即抛与 image-providers 同款防撞报错）；新 RPC `design.systems.list`（session-less，builtin+extension 合并视图带 source 徽章）是 GUI 预览 rail 的数据源；命中体系的 prompt 简报随 composer 区块通道注入（source=design-system，order 40 位于 mode 预设区块之后，每次 rebuildSystemPrompt 重解析——扩展新注册的设计体系下一次 prompt 重建即生效；未知/空 id 不注入不炸）。契约测试 `design-systems-registry.test.ts` + `design-system-prompt-injection.test.ts`。
  - EN: Design systems became a registrable capability, so an extension can declare new ones: design styles upgrade from a GUI-hardcoded table to a daemon-side registry — five built-ins (minimal/glass/editorial/neubrutalism/darkneon, stable ids, swatches/tokens taken from the existing gui token ladder) plus extension registrations via `pi.registerDesignSystem()` (same loader lifecycle as media providers: pending → replayed at session init, whole-source cleanup by sourceId on extension reload/unload, built-in/duplicate collisions throw with the image-providers-style error contract); the new session-less `design.systems.list` RPC (builtin + extension merged view with source badges) feeds the GUI preview rail; the matched system's prompt brief rides the composer section channel (source=design-system, order 40 right after the mode preset sections, re-resolved on every rebuildSystemPrompt — an extension-registered system takes effect on the next prompt rebuild; unknown/empty ids inject nothing and never throw). Contract tests in `design-systems-registry.test.ts` + `design-system-prompt-injection.test.ts`.
- **会话内设计体系选中态写通道 `session.setDesignSystem`**：GUI 设计体系选择器经新 RPC 在会话内写 `projectMetadata.designSystemId` 并即时生效——`designSystemId` 非 null 时必须命中注册表（内置五套 + 扩展），未知 id fail-fast 报 `Unknown design system: <id>`（GUI banner 原样显示，不落地悬空选中）；live 会话写共享 metadata 状态并立即重建 base prompt（design-system 简报区块随下一次模型调用出现/消失）；历史（非 live）会话走读-合并-写持久化快照头（`persistHeaderPatch` 整体替换 projectMetadata，先读旧值合并保创建面其它键），两侧均 best-effort 镜像 `<cwd>/.musepi/project.json`（失败不阻断）；重激活（历史会话恢复 live）时把快照头的 projectMetadata 回灌进 `createAgentSession`，重开会话选中态不丢。契约测试 `design-system-set.test.ts`。
  - EN: in-session design-system selection write channel `session.setDesignSystem`: the GUI design-system picker writes `projectMetadata.designSystemId` via the new RPC and it takes effect immediately — a non-null `designSystemId` must hit the registry (five built-ins + extensions) or the RPC fails fast with `Unknown design system: <id>` (shown verbatim in the GUI banner, never persisting a dangling selection); live sessions write the shared metadata state and rebuild the base system prompt right away (the design-system brief section appears/disappears on the next model call); history (non-live) sessions go through read-merge-write against the persisted snapshot header (persistHeaderPatch replaces projectMetadata wholesale, so the old value is read and merged first to keep the other creation-metadata keys), and both paths best-effort mirror `<cwd>/.musepi/project.json` (a failed mirror never blocks); reactivation (resuming a history session to live) re-feeds the snapshot header's projectMetadata into createAgentSession so the selection survives a re-open. Contract tests in `design-system-set.test.ts`.
- **设计体系预览 rail + 风格选择器 hover 预览卡**：数据源从硬编码表切换为新 RPC `design.systems.list`（内置五套 id 保留 i18n 文案、扩展注册体系直出注册表 label，RPC 不可用时静默回退内置静态镜像）；欢迎页 design 模式下输入框下方新增预览 rail——120×72 卡片（色卡条前 4 色拼接 + 体系名 + 内置/扩展徽章）横滑排布、边缘羽化、选中 accent 描边 + 勾选角标、60ms stagger 入场；风格选择胶囊的下拉菜单升级为 opendesign 式 hover 预览——悬停风格行浮出预览卡（色卡条 + tokens 微型 mock + 描述，portal 弹出、空间不足自动换边）；选中态与预览 rail 同源：欢迎页选中随 `projectMetadata.designSystemId` 一次落盘创建，会话内选中走 `session.setDesignSystem` RPC 即时生效（取代旧的「design style brief update」写句子路径），两处 composer（会话内 / 欢迎页）共用同一选择胶囊。契约测试 `design-systems-gui.test.tsx`（10 例）。
  - EN: design-system preview rail + style-picker hover preview card: the data source switches from a hardcoded table to the new `design.systems.list` RPC (the five built-in ids keep their i18n copy, extension-registered systems render the registry label verbatim, and an unreachable RPC silently falls back to the built-in static mirror); under the welcome composer in design mode a preview rail appears — 120×72 cards (a strip of the first 4 swatches + system name + builtin/extension badge) in a horizontally-scrolling row with edge fading, an accent outline + check corner badge on the selected one, and 60ms staggered entrances; the style-picker capsule's dropdown upgrades to an opendesign-style hover preview — hovering a style row floats a preview card (swatch strip + a tokens mini mock + description, portal'd, flipping sides when space is short); the selection state is single-sourced with the rail: on the welcome page the pick persists via `projectMetadata.designSystemId` at creation, and in-session picks ride the `session.setDesignSystem` RPC for instant effect (replacing the legacy "design style brief update" sentence-injection path), with both composers (in-session / welcome) sharing the same selection capsule. Contract tests in `design-systems-gui.test.tsx` (10 cases).
- **桌面端 agent 默认接管右栏内置浏览器（不再偷偷起无头浏览器）**：桌面应用启动后，本地 CDP 桥在 9230-9239 里绑定到一个实际端口，并把这个真值经新 RPC `browser.managedBridge` 推给 daemon；用户**没显式配置过** `browser.gui` 时，agent 的 browser 工具默认驱动的就是右栏内置浏览器——页面在面板里实时可见、共享面板登录态、agent 标签页与你的标签互不干扰。显式设过 `browser.gui: false` 的用户永远以设置为准（启动不会覆盖），设置页读到的也是实际生效值而不是 schema 默认。桥地址是**连接级运行时事实**：GUI 断开即撤销，从不写进设置，避免"上次开着桌面应用"被固化成死端口。browser 与 computer 两个工具的提示词按通道条件注入托管浏览器说明（CLI/TUI 场景不受污染），computer 明确告知内嵌页面不在 OS 窗口列表里、要看就用 browser 工具；设置 → 浏览器区分「托管浏览器桥（实际端口 + agent 标签页状态）」与「共享无头浏览器」两条通道状态，新增 `browser.guiUrl` 编辑框与通道优先级说明；打开浏览器面板即弹接入引导（不必等 agent 先跑一次浏览器）。
  - EN: the desktop app's built-in browser is now what the agent drives by default (no more invisible headless Chromium). On startup the app's local CDP bridge binds the first free port in 9230-9239 and pushes that ACTUAL port to the daemon over the new `browser.managedBridge` RPC; with `browser.gui` left unconfigured, the agent's browser tool drives the right-pane browser — pages stay visible in the panel, login state is shared, and the agent's own tab never disturbs yours. A user who explicitly set `browser.gui: false` always wins (startup never overrides it), and the settings page reports the effective value rather than the schema default. The bridge url is connection-scoped runtime state: it is withdrawn the moment the GUI disconnects and is never persisted, so "the desktop app was running once" cannot fossilize into a dead port. The browser and computer tool prompts inject the managed-browser guidance conditionally per channel (CLI/TUI runs are untouched), and computer now says outright that embedded pages are absent from the OS window list — use the browser tool for them. Settings → Browser separates the two channels ("managed browser bridge" with its real port and agent-tab state, and "shared headless browser"), adds an editable `browser.guiUrl` plus a channel-priority note, and the onboarding hint now appears as soon as you open the browser panel instead of waiting for the agent's first browser call.

### Added

- **插件面板不再加载失败，官方插件也都有图标了**：插件自带的设置面板原先一律报「模块加载失败」——界面在组件还在下载时就回收了它的地址；现在加载完成后才释放。同时每个插件项都画出了自己的标识（插件声明的图标、内置单元的类别图标，或按包名生成的彩色字母标），状态改为标识角上的小圆点，不再和图标挤在一起。
  - EN: Plugin panels no longer fail to load, and official plugins have icons too. A plugin's own settings panel always reported "module failed to load" — the UI released its address while the download was still in flight; it now waits for the load to finish. Every plugin also draws its own mark (a declared icon, a built-in unit's kind glyph, or a colored letter tile derived from the package name), and status moved to a corner badge instead of crowding the icon.
- **插件详情弹窗补齐**：点击已安装插件打开详情弹窗——大图标、版本、状态与启用开关、头部卸载按钮；插件自带的组件收进弹窗「包含的组件」逐项开关（不再与插件包重复占列表行），插件的设置界面（如账号池管理）也挂载在弹窗内。
  - EN: The plugin detail dialog completes the picture. Clicking an installed plugin opens a dialog with the large icon, version, status, enable toggle and an uninstall button in the header; the plugin's own modules collapse into per-item included-component toggles (no longer duplicated as separate list rows), and its settings surface (an account pool, for example) mounts inside the dialog.
- **插件列表支持一键卸载**：已安装插件卡片和列表行新增卸载按钮，确认后由守护进程移除插件目录并即时刷新列表；卸载失败时错误横幅提示且列表保持不变。
  - EN: One-click uninstall in the plugin list. Installed plugin cards and rows gain an uninstall button; after a confirmation the daemon removes the plugin directory and refreshes the list immediately, and a failed uninstall shows an error banner while leaving the list untouched.
- **插件图标可自定义**：插件可在清单里声明 `icon`（emoji、字符或图片 URL），列表立即采用；未声明时按包名生成确定的彩色字母标，同包同名同色。
  - EN: Customizable plugin icons. A plugin can declare `icon` in its manifest (emoji, glyph, or an image URL) and the list adopts it; without one, a deterministic colored letter mark is derived from the package name — same package, same mark.
- **图片预览一镜到底 morph + 附件提及 badge**：composer 附件图片 chip 点击预览时，图片从缩略图位置/尺寸连续变形到全屏舞台（FLIP + `--spring-liquid` 360ms，背板独立 160ms 淡入），关闭时反向飞回 chip 原位后才卸载——全程无整屏淡入淡出（M1.10 §3.3）；chip 在预览期间被删除/滚动则 rect 失效自动降级为 150ms 淡入淡出；`gui-motion-off`/`prefers-reduced-motion` 无中间帧；gallery ←→ 导航不走 morph。图片与文件 chip 带常显序号角标（图片 1/文件 1 同类独立编号），hover 新增「提及」按钮（@ 图标）：点击在输入框光标处插入序号 token `@图片N`/`@文件N`（textarea 单一输入面不动、draft 持久化天然存活；`@` 后连续非空白字符即候选，`@src/foo` 路径形态不算），highlight overlay 将其画成干净的 accent pill（水平 padding 负 margin 偿还，零字形推进，无 rim 噪音），pill 可 hover 出预览卡（缩略图/文件名+大小）、点击跳转光标；序号 token 按「同类第 N 个」解析——粘贴的多张截图同叫 image.png 也不会全部指向第一张（重名附件入列即自动 `-2`/`-3` 去重）；附件删除后 token 退化为 `@文件名` 形态回归普通文本（不 paint、发送不展开、文本原样保留）；发送时 token 展开——序号图片 → `[图片 N]`（第 N 张 wire 图片）、文件 → 现有 `[Attachment] 路径` 机制、旧 `@文件名` 形态照旧兼容。morph 几何抽为纯函数 `computeMorph`（client-core）并有契约测试；token 解析/展开/重编号同样纯函数全测。
  - EN: lightbox 一镜到底 morph for composer image previews + attachment mention badges. Clicking an image chip now flies the preview from the thumbnail's rect into the full-screen stage (FLIP + `--spring-liquid` 360ms, backdrop fading in independently over 160ms) and flies it back on close before unmount — no full-screen cross-fade anywhere (M1.10 §3.3); a chip deleted or scrolled away mid-preview degrades to the legacy 150ms fade, `gui-motion-off`/`prefers-reduced-motion` plays zero intermediate frames, and gallery ←→ navigation never morphs. Image and file chips carry an always-on numbered corner badge (images and files numbered independently) and gain a hover "mention" button (@ icon): clicking inserts an ordinal token `@图片N` / `@文件N` at the caret (the textarea stays the single input surface, draft persistence survives for free; an `@` plus a whitespace-free run is a candidate, path shapes like `@src/foo` never parse), the highlight overlay paints it as a clean accent pill (horizontal padding repaid by a negative margin — zero glyph advance, no rim noise), the pill shows a hover preview card (thumbnail / name + size) and click jumps the caret there; ordinal tokens resolve by "Nth of its kind", so multiple pasted screenshots all named image.png can no longer all point at the first one (duplicate chip names are `-2`/`-3`-suffixed on intake); once the attachment is removed the token degrades to its `@filename` form and reverts to plain text (unpainted, unexpanded on send, kept verbatim); on send tokens expand — ordinal images → `[图片 N]` (the Nth wire image), files → the existing `[Attachment] path` mechanism, and legacy `@filename` tokens keep working. The morph geometry is a pure `computeMorph` function (client-core) under contract tests; the token parse/expand/renumber are pure functions too, fully tested.

- **应用内更新 UX 分层（待重启确认卡 + 失败决策卡）**：更新状态机补显式相位——`available`（有新版）与 `verifying`（下载 ≥100% 到安装就绪之间）在 updater-state 事件流可见；`state.error` 结构化为 `{kind, message, technicalDetails?}`，kind 为 `{check|download|install} × {network 与否}` 语义枚举，界面按 kind 显示语义文案、原始报错折进「技术详情」。新增紧凑模态层（与既有 toast 分层共存）：下载完成 ≥10 分钟未重启且 toast 已关时弹「待重启确认卡」；下载/安装连续失败 ≥2 次弹「失败决策卡」（重试 / 前往下载页 / 暂不）；下载就绪时窗口未聚焦任务栏闪烁一次；「立即重启」的 15 秒安装宽限期内两处 UI 均切为不可点的「正在安装更新，安装完成后将自动重启」。自动检查轮询改为失败 ×2 退避（上限 6 小时）+ ±20% 抖动。
  - EN: in-app update UX layering (reboot-confirm card + failure-decision card). The update state machine gains explicit phases — `available` (newer version detected) and `verifying` (between download ≥100% and install-ready) are now visible on the updater-state stream; `state.error` is structured as `{kind, message, technicalDetails?}` with kind as a stable `{check|download|install} × {network-or-not}` enum — the UI maps the kind to semantic copy and folds the raw error into a 技术详情 (technical details) section. A new compact modal layer coexists with the existing toast (never replacing it): a reboot-confirm card pops when a downloaded update sits uninstalled for ≥10 minutes with the toast closed, and a failure-decision card (retry / open the download page / later) pops after ≥2 consecutive download or install failures; the taskbar flashes once when a download lands while the window is unfocused, and during quitAndInstall's 15-second grace window both surfaces switch to the non-clickable "installing the update, the app will restart automatically" copy. The background check schedule now doubles its delay on failures (capped at 6 hours) with ±20% jitter.

- **消息自动折叠与工具调用汇总两个设置开关**：设置 → 外观 → 聊天设置新增两条。「消息自动折叠」（默认开）= 回合结束时该轮工作段自动收为折叠态仅展示总结，流式中永不收；由原「活动默认展开」开关替换而来，存储键反义映射、旧设置无缝迁移。「工具调用汇总」（默认开）= 回合内连续的工具调用聚合为一行摘要（如"读取了 3 个文件 · 运行了 2 个命令"，按工具类别聚合计数），点击摘要行展开完整过程、再点收回；流式中进行中的段保持逐条（spinner 即进度），关闭则现状逐条渲染；摘要行样式复用 活动 折叠头的 token。
  - EN: two new toggles in Settings → Appearance → Chat. "Auto-fold messages" (default on) folds a turn's working process into the collapsed summary when the turn completes — never while streaming; it replaces the old "Activities expanded by default" toggle with an inverted mapping over the same storage key, so existing settings migrate seamlessly. "Tool call summary" (default on) aggregates consecutive tool calls within a turn into one-line abstracts (e.g. "Read 3 files · Ran 2 commands", counts pooled per tool category); click a summary line to reveal the full process, click again to re-fold; the in-flight stretch stays per-tool while streaming (the spinner is the progress), and turning the toggle off renders tool cards one by one as before. Summary lines reuse the activity fold header's token language.

- **全局滚动容器边缘羽化**：羽化机制收敛为 client-core 单一共享实现（`scroll-shadow.ts` 纯函数 `computeScrollEdgeState` + `useScrollShadow` hook，桌面端原实现变为 re-export）——只在该方向真实可滚动时才显示对应边缘的羽化。覆盖面补齐：消息内 markdown 表格横向滚动条（`.tr-table-scroll`）/代码块 pre/KaTeX/mermaid 容器新增左右羽化（与 Kimi 桌面端表格同效果）；tab 条、git diff 视图、composer 附件排、欢迎页设计 chip 排横向羽化；市场网格/agents 面板等纵向滚动列表补上此前缺失的上下羽化；顺带修复 `.gui-transcript` 此前接错目标的死羽化接线。契约测试 `scroll-shadow.test.ts` 覆盖纯函数阈值分支。
  - EN: edge fading for scroll containers across every direction: the fade mechanism converges on a single shared client-core implementation (`scroll-shadow.ts` — pure `computeScrollEdgeState` plus the `useScrollShadow` hook; the desktop copy becomes a re-export) and only fades an edge when that axis can actually scroll. Coverage completed: in-message markdown table scrollers (`.tr-table-scroll`), code-block pres, KaTeX and mermaid containers gain left/right fading (matching the desktop table treatment); the tab strip, git diff view, composer attachment row, and welcome design chip row get horizontal fading; vertical lists that were missing it (marketplace grid, agents panel, etc.) get top/bottom fading; and the `.gui-transcript` fade wiring that pointed at a dead target is fixed. Contract tests in `scroll-shadow.test.ts` cover the pure-function threshold branches.

- **项目自定义操作**：菜单里的「添加新操作」此前是死按钮（点击只跳设置页）——现弹出编辑对话框，按当前工作目录维护自定义操作（名称 + 命令 + 图标，18 个 sprite 图标可选、上限 12 条，localStorage 按 cwd 分键持久化，损坏存储/缺字段条目读取时静默净化）；菜单在执行区列出已存操作，点击即自动打开终端坞并把命令贴入活动 tab 执行（与 dev server 同一 `musepi-gui-terminal-cmd` 通道），对话框内可改可删。契约测试 `project-actions.test.ts` 9 例。
  - EN: project custom actions: "Add new action" was a dead button (it only jumped to settings) — it now opens an editor dialog for maintaining per-workspace custom actions (name + command + icon, 18 sprite icons to pick from, capped at 12, persisted in localStorage keyed by cwd, with corrupt storage and field-deficient entries silently sanitized on read); the menu lists saved actions in its execution section, and clicking one opens the terminal dock and types the command into the active tab (the same `musepi-gui-terminal-cmd` channel as the dev server), with edit/delete in the dialog. Contract tests in `project-actions.test.ts` (9 cases).

- **composer 附件 chip 拖拽排序**：图片/文件附件 chip 可直接拖拽换位——chip 顺序即发送顺序（wire images 通道与文件上传句柄都按新顺序投影），@提及编号（@图片N/@文件N）随渲染自动重排，正文里已插入的序号 token 同步重写编号（retargetMentionTokens，始终指向同一附件——Kimi refreshAttachmentLabels parity）。dnd-kit distance-8 指针阈值保持点击预览/@提及/删除按钮各自语义，拖拽后的误触 click 被行容器捕获相位守卫吃掉（拖完不会弹 lightbox）；拖动项抬升（z-index/阴影/透明度），兄弟位移走 `--spring-snappy` 200ms，`gui-motion-off`/`prefers-reduced-motion` 归零。欢迎页与会话内 composer 同时生效；两处发送投影抽为共享纯函数（顺带消除 welcome 判空 dataUrl、session 不判的漂移）。契约测试 `use-attachments.test.ts` 4 例。
  - EN: drag-to-reorder for composer attachment chips: image/file chips drag to swap positions — chip order IS the send order (both the wire images channel and the file upload handles project in the new order), the @mention numbering (@图片N/@文件N) re-derives on render, and ordinal tokens already typed into the text are renumbered in lockstep (retargetMentionTokens — they keep pointing at the same attachment). dnd-kit's distance-8 pointer constraint keeps the preview click, the @ mention button and the remove button each fully click-y, and the click that follows a real drag is eaten by a capture-phase guard on the row (a drag never pops the lightbox); the dragged chip lifts (z-index/shadow/opacity) while siblings shift over a 200ms `--spring-snappy` transition, all zeroed under `gui-motion-off`/`prefers-reduced-motion`. Both the welcome composer and the in-session composer gain it; the two send projections are extracted into shared pure functions (also eliminating the drift where the welcome path guarded empty dataUrls and the session path didn't). Contract tests in `use-attachments.test.ts` (4 cases).

### Changed

- **MeloTTS 朗读改分句流式播放 + 朗读模型卡音色子选**：① 此前 `speak()` 把净化后的整段文本塞进一次 `tts.synthesize`，CPU 推理的全部合成时间计入「点击到出声」——现改为按句切分（`splitSpeakableSentences`）：第 0 句立即发起合成、首个 PCM 到达即建共享 AudioContext 出声，后续句在上句播放期间预合成（流水线重叠），长回复的首音延迟不再随长度线性放大。② 朗读模型卡选中某行时行内展开该行音色 radio 子列表（写 `tts.localVoice`，daemon `resolveSherpaSpeakerId` 映射到 sid）——kokoro 行是真实多音色可切，melotts-zh 经 k2-fsa 官方文档与 HF 双源核实为单 speaker（zh 女声 sid 0），如实显示其唯一音色、未编造 id；daemon `tts.modelStatus` 每模型新增 `voices: {id,label}[]`（可选 wire 字段，向后兼容旧 daemon）。契约测试 `voice-speak.test.ts` 13 例 + `voice-settings.test.tsx` +3 例。
  - EN: MeloTTS playback becomes sentence-streamed + the TTS model card gains voice sub-selection: ① `speak()` previously stuffed the whole sanitized text into one `tts.synthesize` call, charging the entire CPU-inference time to click-to-speech — it now splits into sentences (`splitSpeakableSentences`): sentence 0 starts synthesizing immediately, a shared AudioContext starts playing on the first PCM chunk, and later sentences pre-synthesize during playback (pipelined), so first-audio latency no longer grows linearly with reply length. ② selecting a row in the TTS model card expands that row's voice radio sublist (writes `tts.localVoice`; the daemon's `resolveSherpaSpeakerId` maps it to a sid) — the kokoro row is genuinely multi-voice, while melotts-zh was verified against both the k2-fsa official docs and HF to be single-speaker (zh female, sid 0), so it shows its one real voice instead of fabricated ids; daemon `tts.modelStatus` now carries `voices: {id,label}[]` per model (optional wire field, backward-compatible with older daemons). Contract tests: `voice-speak.test.ts` (13 cases) + `voice-settings.test.tsx` (+3).

### Fixed

- **MeloTTS 朗读一顿一顿 + Markdown 念不好 + 合成失败无声无提示**：① daemon `SpeakableStream` 的切句边界只认 ASCII 句末（要求 `.!?` 后有空白）——中文「。！？」后没有空白，整段中文只能等逗号切或 280 字符兜底截断，合成器在半句中间被切开（「一顿一顿」的根因）；现边界正则补全角句读（闭括号跟随句末，ASCII 行为不变），GUI 段间只留 40ms click guard、句末停顿交还模型的标点韵律，speed 维持 1.0（调速能力留为注册表数据位）。② `sanitizeForSpeech` 重写：代码块（含未闭合 fence）念本地化占位「代码块」、标题/引用/列表记号剥除（有序列表 `1. ` → `1, ` 防小数误读）、表格行用「，」连接、链接读文字/图片读 alt/裸 URL 读域名/行内代码去反引号/`snake_case` 中间下划线不再被误删/summarize 模式取首两句——附带修掉裸 URL 正则吞掉 URL 后全角句号的真实 bug（句末标点直接影响 MeloTTS 断句韵律）。③ 错误透出全链路：worker 合成异常经 `tts-client` 新 `onError` 回调 → daemon `tts.synthesize` 失败返回 `{audio:null, error:<worker 原文>}` → GUI 按形态映射（超时/未下载/原样透出如模型文件 404），朗读按钮进入红色错误态（警告图标 + title 原因），纯标记内容在发起 RPC 前即拦截「这段内容没有可朗读的文字」。daemon 56 例 + 桌面端 32 例全绿。
  - EN: MeloTTS read-aloud stutter + poor markdown handling + silent synthesis failures: ① the daemon's `SpeakableStream` sentence boundary only recognized ASCII terminators (requiring whitespace after `.!?`) — with no whitespace after Chinese 「。！？」, a whole Chinese reply waited for comma splits or the 280-char fallback and the synthesizer was cut mid-sentence (the root cause of the "stuttering"); the boundary regex now covers full-width terminators (closing brackets ride the terminator, ASCII behavior unchanged), the GUI leaves only a 40ms click guard between segments and hands end-of-sentence pauses back to the model's punctuation prosody, and speed stays 1.0 (rate control kept as a registry data point). ② `sanitizeForSpeech` rewritten: code blocks (including unclosed fences) read a localized "code block" placeholder, heading/quote/list markers are stripped (ordered `1. ` → `1, ` so it isn't misread as a decimal), table rows join cells with 「，」, links read their label / images their alt / bare URLs their host / inline code loses its backticks / mid-word underscores in `snake_case` are no longer eaten / summarize mode takes the first two sentences — plus a real bug where the bare-URL regex swallowed the full-width period after a URL (end punctuation directly drives MeloTTS phrasing). ③ errors surface end-to-end: worker synthesis exceptions ride the new `tts-client` `onError` callback → a failing daemon `tts.synthesize` returns `{audio:null, error:<worker text>}` → the GUI maps the shape (timeout / not-downloaded / verbatim e.g. a model-file 404), the speak button enters a red error state (warning icon + title with the reason), and marker-only content is intercepted with "nothing speakable here" before any RPC fires. daemon 56 cases + desktop 32 cases all green.
- **上翻经过顾问长消息时列表跳一下（滚动与测量同帧）**：tanstack virtual-core 3.17.11 的 `measureElement` 在 `isScrolling` 时跳过新挂载行的首次测量、推迟到下一帧 ResizeObserver 批次——滚动经过长 advisor 卡时发生两帧分离：mount 提交帧新行以真实高度（约 1500px）插入 DOM 而 spacer 只按 40px estimate 释放，位移被画出一帧，下一帧 RO 批次补偿门才把差值写回 scrollTop 弹回（复现实测漂移恰为 −1460px，且所有 RO 帧漂移恒为 0，证明补偿门本身精确、问题只在位移与补偿不同帧）。修复：Transcript 用自定义 `measureVirtualRow` ref 替换 stock `measureElement`——保留原调用（RO 观察附加），并在 `itemSizeCache` 无记录（首次测量）时于 mount commit 内同步 `resizeItem`，位移与补偿同帧抵消；cache hit 自动跳过，不干扰流式/折叠动画的既有门控。变异验证：禁用同步首测后复现用例精确回归 fail（Received 1460），blacket 禁补偿门 fail（Received 1944）。契约测试新增 `transcript-upscroll-jitter.test.tsx`（恒等式断言：每拍 mount/RO 两帧的内容锚点漂移 ≤ 1px），transcript 套件 154 例全绿。
  - EN: a one-row jump when scrolling up past a long advisor message (measurement and scroll landing in different frames): tanstack virtual-core 3.17.11's `measureElement` skips the first measurement of a newly mounted row while `isScrolling`, deferring it to the next frame's ResizeObserver batch — scrolling past a tall advisor card split into two frames: on the mount-commit frame the new row enters the DOM at its real height (~1500px) while the spacer only releases the 40px estimate, painting the displacement for one frame, and on the next RO frame the compensation gate writes the delta back into scrollTop (repro showed drift exactly −1460px with every RO-frame drift identically 0, proving the gate itself is precise and only the frame split is wrong). Fix: Transcript swaps the stock `virtualizer.measureElement` for a custom `measureVirtualRow` ref — the original call is kept (RO observation attaches), and when `itemSizeCache` has no record (first measurement) it synchronously `resizeItem`s inside the mount commit, so displacement and compensation cancel in the same frame; cache hits skip automatically and never disturb the existing streaming/fold gating. Mutation-verified: disabling the sync first measurement makes the repro fail with Received 1460, a blanket gate disable fails with Received 1944. New contract test `transcript-upscroll-jitter.test.tsx` (identity assertion: per-tick mount/RO content-anchor drift ≤ 1px); the transcript suite is 154/154 green.
- **能力中心卸载确认弹窗被详情抽屉压住 + 卸载后卡片残留**：① 层级——统一确认体系 `lib/prompt-dialog.tsx` 把弹窗 portal 进 `#root`，而技能详情抽屉 portal 到 `document.body`：两者同处 z-3000 模态带、同 z 时 DOM 序定胜负，`#root` 永远排在后挂载的抽屉之前，于是确认弹窗被抽屉盖住不可见、点击还被抽屉全屏背板吞掉（表象即「点了卸载没反应」）；portal 目标改为 `document.body`（DialogFrame 先例，同带 tie 由 DOM 序解决），confirm 变体补挂规范要求的紧凑样式类 `gui-dialog--confirm`（此前一直错挂 `--prompt`），focus 选择器同步。② 卸载后——`removeSkill` 成功后此前只把名字移出选择集、没有重拉列表，已删卡片残留在网格里；现补 `onChanged()` 触发 `skills.list` 刷新。契约测试 `capability-uninstall.test.tsx` 4 例（层级/DOM 序/类名契约、取消负向契约、确认触发 `skills.delete` + 列表刷新）。
  - EN: the capability-center uninstall confirm dialog was pinned under the detail drawer + uninstalled cards lingered: ① stacking — the unified confirm system (`lib/prompt-dialog.tsx`) portal'd dialogs into `#root` while the skill detail drawer portal'd to `document.body`: both sit in the z-3000 modal band and equal z-index falls back to DOM order, where `#root` always precedes the later-mounted drawer, so the confirm dialog was invisible under the drawer and its clicks were swallowed by the drawer's fullscreen backdrop (presenting as "uninstall does nothing"); the portal target moves to `document.body` (the DialogFrame precedent — ties resolve by DOM order), the confirm variant gains the spec-required compact class `gui-dialog--confirm` (it had been wrongly wearing `--prompt`), and focus selectors follow. ② after uninstall — a successful `removeSkill` only removed the name from the selection set without refetching, leaving the deleted card in the grid; it now fires `onChanged()` to refresh `skills.list`. Contract tests in `capability-uninstall.test.tsx` (4 cases: stacking/DOM-order/class-name contract, cancel negative contract, confirm triggers `skills.delete` + list refresh).

- **终端坞切换会话即销毁实例 + 浅色主题下终端漆黑**：① 切到从未开过终端的会话/空态时，坞虽收起但 TerminalPanel 被卸载、daemon pty 全杀——切回来是全新 PowerShell（重跑 profile）、scrollback 全丢。现面板首次开坞后常驻挂载：收起只是高度归零（跟随会话的可见性不变），切往返回到同一实例、输出历史完整。② xterm 调色板背景是 `rgba(0,0,0,0)` 透明设计（融入坞面），但缺 `allowTransparency`（构造器-only 选项）时 alpha 被忽略、画布渲染成不透明黑——深色主题下隐形、浅色主题下整块漆黑（"样式没了"）；主题也改为构造器传入，重生的实例不再闪/卡在默认黑配色。
  - EN: terminal dock destroyed its instances on session switch + the terminal was pitch black in light theme: ① switching to a session that never opened the dock (or the empty state) folded the dock but also UNMOUNTED TerminalPanel, killing every daemon pty — switching back spawned a fresh PowerShell (profile reload) with all scrollback gone. The panel now stays mounted after the first dock open: folding is just height 0 (per-session visibility unchanged), so a switch-and-return lands on the same instance with its output intact. ② the xterm palette background is `rgba(0,0,0,0)` by design (blends into the dock surface), but without `allowTransparency` (a constructor-only option) the alpha is ignored and the canvas paints opaque black — invisible in dark mode, glaring in light mode ("the styling is gone"); the theme is now constructor-provided too, so a respawned instance never flashes or sticks on the default black palette.

- **MeloTTS 中文档下载后播放无声**：① 主因——TTS worker 调 sherpa `generateAsync({text})`，native addon 严格校验请求对象，缺 `sid`/`speed` 直接抛错，异常走到错误通道后客户端只打 debug 日志返回 null，daemon RPC 变成 `{audio:null}`，GUI 不播也不报错（表象即「无声」）；现请求补 `sid`（voice→sid 映射）与 `speed`，worker 错误提升为 warn 并带 modelKey+错误文本（错误不静默）。② 次因——melo 注册清单漏了官方 manifest 的 `dict/`（jieba 分词词典）与 4 个 rule FST（数字/日期/多音字），「无需 dictDir」的注释是错的；现下载/缓存校验/合成配置三处共用一个完整清单（新增 `sherpaTtsRequiredFiles()`）。本地用已缓存模型真实复现并验证修复（缺参必抛、补齐后正常出声 93432 samples @44100；端到端 worker 子进程 10 万+ samples）。契约测试 15 例。
