- **画板文字标签：六个字只显示三个、以及拖不动角手柄**：两个独立的真 bug。① 盒宽按「字符数 × 字号 × 0.62」估算，那是拉丁文的平均字宽——汉字步进约 1.0em，要宽约 1.6 倍，于是六个 24px 汉字被「估算」成 89px 而实际占 ~144px，盒子被夹到最小宽度、中文标签只露出前三个字。现在宽度取自**真实画布度量**（`measureTextWidth`，与 Konva 自己量文字用的是同一种操作），中文、英文、混排一律按实际绘制宽度算，一行放不下就换行、盒子向下增高。② 上一轮从手柄的**渲染**条件里删掉了「排除文字」，却漏了 `onPointerDown` 里同一个闸门——手柄画出来了，但按下时命中没有登记缩放状态，拖动成为空操作。两处现已同时移除，文字与其他图形走完全相同的缩放/拖动/擦除路径。另外：用户亲手拖出的宽度会被**保留**（文字在里面换行，不再弹回去贴合内容）、文字按字断行（中文没有空格，只按词切分会让整句停在一行）、编辑浮层的字体与画布统一（此前提交瞬间文字会重排），并且在无画布环境下度量会降级而不是抛错。
  - EN: Sketch board text labels: six characters showing only three, and corner handles that would not drag — two independent real bugs. (1) Box width was estimated as `chars × fontSize × 0.62`, a Latin average; a CJK glyph advances ~1.0em, about 1.6× wider, so six 24px 汉字 "measured" 89px while occupying ~144px — the box clamped to its minimum and the label showed only its first three characters. Width now comes from a **real canvas metric** (`measureTextWidth`, the same operation Konva itself measures text with), so CJK, Latin and mixed strings are sized by what actually gets painted: anything too long wraps and the box grows downward. (2) The previous pass removed the text exclusion from the handles' **render** condition but missed the identical gate in `onPointerDown` — the handles drew, but the press never registered scale state, so dragging was a no-op. Both are gone now, and text resizes/drags/erases through exactly the path shapes use. Also: a width the user dragged is **kept** (text wraps inside it instead of the box springing back to fit its content), text wraps per glyph (CJK has no spaces, so a word-only splitter left a whole Chinese sentence on one line), the editing overlay shares the canvas font (committing used to visibly reflow the text), and measurement degrades instead of throwing where no canvas exists.

- **画板文字标签随文本自动换行增高，且与其他图形一样可缩放**：旧实现里文字只存一个锚点，宽度每次渲染都用「字符数 × 字号 × 0.62」重算，于是一整句话永远是单行、直接冲出画板、高度永不增长；同时缩放手柄层显式排除文字，导致它是画板上唯一拖不动也缩放不了的对象。现在文字与其他对象**共用同一套对象模型**：标签持有真实矩形（`[x,y,w,h]`），输入时按词换行、高度跟着行数长，宽度回缩到最宽一行（上限是用户拖出的盒宽）；选中后出现与矩形/图片相同的四角手柄，缩放、拖动、擦除全走同一条代码路径；单击＝选中并拖动，**双击**才进入编辑（过去单击即打开插入符，正是它让文字拖不动）。编辑浮层自身也随输入换行增高。存量画板里只有两个数字的旧标签打开时自动升级成盒子，不会错位或丢失。
  - EN: Sketch board text labels now wrap, grow and resize like every other shape. The old label stored a bare anchor and re-derived its width from `charCount × fontSize × 0.62` on every render, so a whole sentence stayed a single line, ran off the board and never grew taller; the scale-handle layer also excluded text outright, making it the one object on the board you could neither drag nor resize. Text now shares the **same object model** as everything else: a label owns a real rectangle (`[x, y, w, h]`), wraps by word as you type, grows taller with its line count and retracts in width to the widest line (capped at the box you dragged), and once selected it shows the same four corner handles as a rect or an image — resize, drag and erase all run down one code path. A single click selects and drags, and **double-click** enters editing (the old single-click caret was exactly what made labels immovable). The editing overlay itself wraps and grows with the text too. Existing boards whose labels hold only two numbers are upgraded to a box on open, so nothing shifts or goes missing.

- **画板：点击画板图重新打开的是原画（A0），不再是粘贴一张导出图（A1）**：此前完成只导出一张 PNG，点回 chip 时又把那张 PNG 当底图挂进画板——每一笔、每个图形和文字全部融成一张位图，再编辑只能在图上涂，任何一处旧细节都改不回来。现在完成同时交出画板的**笔画列表**（`lib/sketch-scene.ts` 的 scene），composer 把它挂在 chip 上：点击 chip 恢复的是对象本身，每一笔依旧可选、可拖、可缩放、可擦除，改哪一处就改哪一处；再次完成是像素与 scene **一起**替换，来回编辑多少次都不退化。恢复出来的内容是画板的开场状态（不进撤销栈、不算未保存改动），笔画 id 从恢复集之后续号以免命中错对象；换窗口尺寸后重开会等比缩放回去（压力值不动，线宽随同一系数）。草稿里也尽量带上 scene（超容量或含导入图片时只丢 scene、保住附件），缺失时回落成旧的整图打开，不会退化成打不开。
  - EN: Sketch board: clicking a board image reopens the original drawing (A0) instead of pasting the exported copy (A1). Finishing used to emit only a PNG, and clicking the chip mounted that PNG back as the base layer — every pen stroke, shape and label fused into one bitmap, so a re-edit could only paint over the picture and no earlier detail was recoverable. 完成 now also hands over the board's **stroke list** (a `scene`, `lib/sketch-scene.ts`) which the composer stores on the chip: clicking it restores the objects themselves, so each stroke is again selectable, movable, scalable and erasable — you fix exactly the part you meant to. Finishing again swaps pixels **and** scene, so the round trip survives any number of re-edits. Restored content is the board's opening state (not an undo step, not a dirty change), stroke ids continue past the restored set so the eraser and select tool never hit the wrong object, and reopening in a resized window rescales proportionally (pressure untouched, thickness follows the same factor). Drafts carry the scene when it fits (oversized payloads or scenes holding an imported picture drop only the scene and keep the attachment), and a missing scene falls back to the old flat-image reopen rather than refusing to open.

## [0.4.34] - 2026-09-20

### Added

- **插件面板不再加载失败，官方插件也都有图标了**：插件自带的设置面板原先一律报「模块加载失败」——界面在组件还在下载时就回收了它的地址；现在加载完成后才释放。同时每个插件项都画出了自己的标识（插件声明的图标、内置单元的类别图标，或按包名生成的彩色字母标），状态改为标识角上的小圆点，不再和图标挤在一起。
  - EN: Plugin panels no longer fail to load, and official plugins have icons too. A plugin's own settings panel always reported "module failed to load" — the UI released its address while the download was still in flight; it now waits for the load to finish. Every plugin also draws its own mark (a declared icon, a built-in unit's kind glyph, or a colored letter tile derived from the package name), and status moved to a corner badge instead of crowding the icon.
- **插件详情弹窗补齐**：点击已安装插件打开详情弹窗——大图标、版本、状态与启用开关、头部卸载按钮；插件自带的组件收进弹窗「包含的组件」逐项开关（不再与插件包重复占列表行），插件的设置界面（如账号池管理）也挂载在弹窗内。
  - EN: The plugin detail dialog completes the picture. Clicking an installed plugin opens a dialog with the large icon, version, status, enable toggle and an uninstall button in the header; the plugin's own modules collapse into per-item included-component toggles (no longer duplicated as separate list rows), and its settings surface (an account pool, for example) mounts inside the dialog.
- **插件列表支持一键卸载**：已安装插件卡片和列表行新增卸载按钮，确认后由守护进程移除插件目录并即时刷新列表；卸载失败时错误横幅提示且列表保持不变。
  - EN: One-click uninstall in the plugin list. Installed plugin cards and rows gain an uninstall button; after a confirmation the daemon removes the plugin directory and refreshes the list immediately, and a failed uninstall shows an error banner while leaving the list untouched.
- **插件图标可自定义**：插件可在清单里声明 `icon`（emoji、字符或图片 URL），列表立即采用；未声明时按包名生成确定的彩色字母标，同包同名同色。
  - EN: Customizable plugin icons. A plugin can declare `icon` in its manifest (emoji, glyph, or an image URL) and the list adopts it; without one, a deterministic colored letter mark is derived from the package name — same package, same mark.
- 桌宠新增**交互反应轴**（戳弄 / 被抓包 / 入睡前）：除会话状态那条轴之外，新增第二条轴专管**用户主动触发的反应**——`startled`（戳一下的惊愕：瞪眼、视线收回正中、刻意不眨眼）、`delighted`（900ms 内补一下的开心：弯眼 + 弹跳）、`curious`（察觉时的偏头，视线明显偏出去）、`dozing`（入睡前的半睁眼，全系统最慢最深的呼吸）、`peek`（"被抓包"的假装没看你）。反应**叠在状态之上**而非常规替换：戳弄绝不写进会话状态、按自己的时钟释放（composer 与桌面桌宠同步），并且期间状态那层的材质（error 的红眼、hover 的增亮辉光）不会被重置——所以球在工作时被戳，仍会一边转圈一边惊一下。身体运动只在戳弄类反应里带过冲入场（全系统唯一允许"闹"的运动），约一秒内自解；同时补齐两个闲时桥段：进入 60s 睡眠闩锁前 40s 开始打瞌睡，以及每 12–26s 一次的偏头/"被抓包"。
  - EN: The pet gains an **interaction reaction axis** (poke / caught-looking / pre-sleep): beyond the session-state axis, a second axis now owns **user-initiated reactions** — `startled` (the gape on a poke: eyes fly wide, gaze snaps to centre, deliberately no blink), `delighted` (a follow-up within 900ms: pleased squint plus a bounce), `curious` (the head-tilt of noticing, gaze parked well off-centre), `dozing` (half-lidded pre-sleep, the slowest and deepest breath in the system), and `peek` (the "caught looking" pretence). Reactions LAYER on top of the mood rather than replacing it: a poke is never written to session state, releases on its own clock (identical on the composer pet and the desktop pet), and the mood's materials (error's red eye, hover's brightened glow) survive underneath — poke the orb while it works and it still startles _and_ keeps spinning. The only loud body motion in the system lives on the poke reactions (an overshoot entrance that resolves in about a second); two new idle beats came with it — drowse starting 40s before the 60s sleep latch, and a peek/curious beat every 12–26s.

### Fixed

- **语音输入第二轮：中文出简体、转录全程有反馈、麦克风按钮回归动效**：中文识别输出繁体的根因是内置 whisper-small q8 的中文语料偏台湾侧——两条引擎都没有 decoder prompt 可偏置文字方向，现在解码后统一过 OpenCC 繁→简（新模块 `zh-hans.ts`，懒加载约 1 MB 词典，仅在语言钉住 zh 时生效，加载失败降级原文；TW/HK 用 auto 可保留繁体）。「转录期间毫无反馈、文字凭空出现」的根因是 composer 用两个布尔标志维护听写状态，点停止时双双清零而转录请求才刚开始——整个等待期渲染成空闲；重构为单一相位状态机（`use-dictation.ts`：录音→转写→完成），转写中再点一次是真取消（旧版只清 UI、在途转录结果照样落进输入框），Esc 任意相位取消、卸载自动清理。反馈形态按裁定重做：30px 麦克风胶囊只承载动效（录音脉冲/转写 spinner），秒数、「转写中…」与 Esc 提示移入输入卡内的状态条——13 根纯 CSS 波形（共享 scaleY 关键帧、负延时错相）+ 真实音量只调透明度，整个转录等待期都有活的反馈；会话输入框与欢迎页共用同一状态机。
  - EN: Voice input round two: Simplified zh output, live feedback through the whole transcription, and the mic button back to motion-only. Traditional-Chinese output traces to the bundled whisper-small q8's TW-leaning zh corpus — neither engine exposes a decoder prompt to bias script direction, so decode now funnels through OpenCC t→cn (new `zh-hans.ts`, ~1 MB dictionary lazy-loaded, applied only when the language is pinned to zh, degrading to the original on load failure; TW/HK users on auto keep Traditional). "No feedback while transcribing, text appears from nowhere" traced to the composer's two-boolean dictation flags, both cleared on stop while the transcribe RPC was only starting — the whole wait rendered as idle; replaced with a single-phase state machine (`use-dictation.ts`: recording → transcribing → done), where toggling during transcription truly cancels (the old UI just cleared its flags and let the in-flight result land anyway), Esc cancels from any phase, and unmount cleans up. Feedback rebuilt per direction: the 30px mic capsule carries motion only (recording pulse / transcribing spinner); seconds, "transcribing…" and the Esc hint moved into an in-input status strip — a 13-bar pure-CSS waveform (shared scaleY keyframes, negative-delay stagger) with the real mic level only modulating opacity, so the entire wait has live feedback; the session composer and the welcome composer share the same state machine.

- **微信 Bot 扫码登录的二维码终于显示出来了**：iLink 的 `get_bot_qrcode` 返回的 `qrcode_img_content` 实测是**二维码要编码的内容 URL**（`liteapp.weixin.qq.com` 扫码目标），不是图片——界面此前把这个 URL 原样塞进 `<img src>`，必然 404，这就是「二维码不显示」的直接原因；现在用内置 QR 编码器把该 URL 本地栅格化为 SVG（真图片 URL 与 base64 PNG 两种旧形状仍直通）。配套把轮询协议补齐：`get_qrcode_status` 带上规范的 `iLink-App-ClientVersion` 头；二维码过期自动换新码（GUI 两秒状态轮询直接浮出新码）；扫码后采纳服务端回传的区域 API base（`baseurl`，后续收发走会话自己的 IDC）；登录时生成协议要求的 `X-WECHAT-UIN`（随机 uint32 → base64）；`getupdates` 命中 `errcode -14`（会话过期）自动回落扫码重登，而不是停在「已连接」却永远收不到消息；长轮询补 45s 超时防挂死；开始轮询时按协议发 `notifystart`。
  - EN: The WeChat bot login QR finally renders: iLink's `get_bot_qrcode` returns `qrcode_img_content` as the URL the QR must ENCODE (the `liteapp.weixin.qq.com` scan target), not an image — the UI was stuffing that URL straight into `<img src>`, which 404s; that was the "QR never shows" bug. The URL is now rasterized locally with the built-in QR encoder (crisp SVG; real image URLs and base64 PNGs still pass through). The polling protocol is completed alongside: `get_qrcode_status` carries the spec `iLink-App-ClientVersion` header, an expired QR automatically re-issues (the GUI's 2s status poll floats the fresh code in), a confirmed scan adopts the server's regional API base (`baseurl` — later sends/polls ride the session's own IDC), login generates the protocol's `X-WECHAT-UIN` (random uint32 → base64), a `getupdates` `errcode -14` (session expired) falls back to QR re-login instead of sitting "connected" while silently receiving nothing, the long poll gets a 45s timeout so a hung connection can't wedge the loop, and the poller announces itself via `notifystart` per the spec.

- **模型选择菜单的点击/滚动响应**：列表此前把鼠标高亮挂在每行的 `mousemove` 上——光标每扫过一行就 setState 重渲染整个数百行的菜单（加上每次渲染重算过滤/分区排序与逐行 `flatRows.indexOf` 的 O(n²)），点开菜单就掉帧。现在鼠标高亮交还给 CSS `:hover`（`mousemove` 不再触碰 React 状态），↑↓ 导航独占高亮期间由列表上的 `data-kbd-nav` 抑制行 hover（滚动驱动的 mousemove 也抢不走高亮——防劫持语义保留），首次真实移动让位回 CSS（一次渲染）；过滤/分区/扁平化全部收进单个 `useMemo`，行级 `kbdIndex` 换 O(1) Map 查找，行组件 `memo` 化——键盘导航只重渲染新旧两行。
  - EN: The model picker menu's click/scroll responsiveness: the list hung its pointer highlight on per-row `mousemove` — every row crossing re-rendered the whole multi-hundred-row menu via setState (on top of re-running the filter + section sort and the per-row `flatRows.indexOf` O(n²) each render), so opening the menu stuttered. The pointer highlight now belongs to CSS `:hover` (mousemove never touches React state); while ↑↓ owns the highlight, a `data-kbd-nav` flag on the list suppresses row hover (scroll-fueled mousemove still can't steal the highlight — the anti-hijack semantics survive), and the first real pointer move yields it back with a single render. Filtering/sectioning/flattening live in one `useMemo`, the row's `kbdIndex` is an O(1) Map lookup, and rows are `memo`'d — keyboard navigation re-renders just the two rows whose highlight moved.

- **弹窗与浮动菜单并入液态玻璃家族**：全应用唯一的弹层配方点 `.gui-menu-popup`（指令候选、模型菜单、右键菜单等一切浮动菜单都从这里拿表面）此前还是磨砂毛玻璃——现在与 composer 胶囊同族：`--glass-bg` 玻璃底垫在 scrim 之下（窗口透明开关的内联 100% 仍能把整面压回不透明）、规格棱边（亮顶/暗底）替代旧的两条均匀内亮线、对角水光 sheen 以 `z-index:-1` 钉在背景之上内容之下（popup 的 fixed 定位自带 stacking context，菜单子结构不可控，不需要 `> *` 救援）。`.gui-dialog` 与画板浮层 `.gui-sketch-board` 同步对齐：border 环换成非对称 inset 棱边、blur/saturate 换家族 token（跟随透明度滑杆，默认值不变）。
  - EN: Dialogs and floating menus join the liquid-glass family: the app's single popup recipe point `.gui-menu-popup` (every floating menu — command candidates, the model picker, context menus — takes its surface here) was still plain frosted glass; it now shares the composer pills' material — a `--glass-bg` pane layered UNDER the scrim (the window-transparency toggle's inline 100% still mutes the whole surface opaque), the asymmetric specular rim (bright top / dim bottom) replacing the old two uniform inset lines, and the diagonal sheen pinned via `z-index:-1` above the pane and below every child (the popup's fixed positioning provides the stacking context, so no `> *` rescue is needed with uncontrolled menu children). `.gui-dialog` and the sketch board overlay align the same way: the border ring becomes the asymmetric inset rim, and blur/saturate move to the family tokens (slider-following, identical at default).

- **触发词/命令高亮升级为胶囊徽标，shell 前缀 `!` 纳入高亮**：输入框里的活语法 token（行首 `/` 命令、行首 `!`/`!!` shell 前缀、独立 magic 词）从细金色文字改为**accent 胶囊徽标**，特殊字段与普通文本一眼可分；`!`/`!!`（TUI 同款 shell 前缀）此前完全没有样式，现已按行首规则纳入（胶囊盖住 `!` 与命令头，`! cmd` 同样覆盖）。胶囊**度量中性**：横向 padding 由等量负 margin 偿还，镜像层与 textarea 的字符 advance 仍逐像素一致，光标不偏。语义上刻意保守：`@` 与 `#` 不加胶囊——发送链路里没有任何展开逻辑，胶囊会许诺不存在的行为；一行里第二个及之后的 `/token` 也不加——daemon 只把第一个 token 当命令名、其余是参数（`/security /guided-goal` 执行的是 security，后者是参数），胶囊只给真正会执行的 token。解析器新增 first-wins 去重契约（`!!workflowz` 是 shell 命令而非思考触发词），补 9 例单测覆盖全部边界。材质上胶囊已并入全局液态玻璃家族——共享玻璃底 + 高光棱边 + 对角水光，accent 染色按 armed-state 配方叠在玻璃上（hover/按压态因高亮层 pointer-events none 天然不适用）。
  - EN: In-input live tokens become pill badges, and the shell prefix `!` joins the highlight: line-leading `/` commands, line-leading `!`/`!!` shell prefixes, and standalone magic words now render as accent capsules instead of thin gold text, making special fields instantly distinguishable from plain text. The TUI-style `!`/`!!` shell prefix had no styling at all and now follows the line-leading rule (the pill covers the bang and the command head; `! cmd` is covered too). The pills are **metric-neutral**: horizontal padding is repaid by an equal negative margin, so the mirror layer and the textarea stay pixel-locked and the caret does not drift. Deliberately conservative semantics: `@` and `#` get no pill — nothing in the send path expands them, so a pill would promise behaviour that does not exist; nor does the second `/token` on a line — the daemon takes the first token as the command name and the rest as args (`/security /guided-goal` runs security with the rest as its argument), so only tokens that actually execute get one. The parser gains a first-wins dedupe contract (`!!workflowz` is a shell command, not a thinking trigger) with 9 new unit tests covering the edges. The pills also join the global liquid-glass family — shared glass fill + specular rim + diagonal sheen, with the accent tint layered per the armed-state recipe (hover/press states don't apply — the overlay is pointer-events none).

- **触发词高亮不再带偏光标，历史会话的模式名回归「工作模式」**：输入框里的 `ultrathink` / `workflowz` 等触发词高亮此前用等宽字体加粗渲染——字符宽度与 textarea 的实际字形不一致，镜像高亮层与透明输入层从此错位，光标和可见字符对不上；高亮 span 现在**只承载颜色**（与 TUI 同样的朴素强调），凡会改变字符宽度的属性（字体族/字重/字距/内边距）一律禁入高亮层。侧栏悬浮卡与右栏头部的模式名：守护进程未记录预设的历史会话（modeId 为空）此前回落「默认模式」，现在空 id 直接按默认预设 work 解析（与欢迎页 `modeId ?? "work"` 同一约定），显示「工作模式」；右栏快照缺失时同样不再出现「默认模式」。
  - EN: Trigger-word highlighting no longer skews the caret, and historical sessions get their real mode name back: in-input `ultrathink` / `workflowz` highlights were painted in bold monospace — wider than the textarea's proportional glyphs, so the mirror highlight layer drifted off the transparent input layer and the caret no longer met the visible characters. Highlight spans now carry **colour only** (the same plain emphasis as the TUI); anything that changes glyph advance (font family / weight / letter-spacing / padding) is banned from the highlight layer. For the mode label in the session hover card and the right-pane header: sessions whose daemon never recorded a preset (empty modeId) fell through to "Default mode"; an empty id now resolves as the default `work` preset (the same convention as the welcome composer's `modeId ?? "work"`), reading "Work mode", and a missing right-pane snapshot no longer surfaces "Default mode" either.

- **欢迎页输入框的五处修整：「+」菜单、输入重影、专注模式、玻璃收编**：「+」菜单的字号此前混杂——普通项继承 15px 基准、模式行写死 13px/12px 字面量，现在全部收进 `--ui-font-size-sm/xs` token（与 `.gui-menu-item` 同一档）；浮动菜单补上**高度钳制**——展开方向贴着窗口边时菜单就地滚动而不是戳出窗外（`useFloatingMenu` 的所有菜单统一受益）。空态输入框「两遍重叠文字」的真因是 welcome textarea 的排版挂在 Tailwind 任意值类上，而 `py-3.5` / `caret-*` / `placeholder:*` 根本不在预编译的 `tailwind.out.css` 里——与镜像高亮层逐像素错位；排版收进 `gui-composer.css` 的 `.gui-ta-welcome` 单一来源（placeholder 也随之重新可见）。专注模式开关的卡顿是一处**双驱动**：`.gui-welcome-form` 自带的 `transition: height` 与 JS 的 WAAPI morph 在同一属性上各跑一条 280ms 曲线，morph 窗口内随 textarea/input 一并抑制；专注空态不再显示底部快捷提示词 chips。发送按钮与快捷提示词 chips 并入共享液态玻璃配方——发送按钮按 armed-state 配方走 accent 染色玻璃（不再是实心色块），chips 摘掉 1px 描边换内嵌棱边，「自定义补充」chip 保持 accent 染色态。
  - EN: Five welcome-composer touch-ups: the "+" menu, the input ghost text, focus mode, and glass adoption. The "+" menu's type scale was a mix — plain entries inherited the 15px base while the mode rows hardcoded 13px/12px literals; everything now rides the `--ui-font-size-sm/xs` tokens (the same step as `.gui-menu-item`), and floating menus gain a **height clamp** — when the opening side hugs the window edge the menu scrolls in place instead of poking out of the window (every `useFloatingMenu` consumer benefits). The welcome textarea's "two overlapping texts" came from its metrics living on Tailwind arbitrary-value classes that the precompiled `tailwind.out.css` never contained (`py-3.5`, `caret-*`, `placeholder:*`), pixel-offsetting it against the mirror highlight layer; the metrics move into a single-source `.gui-ta-welcome` in `gui-composer.css` (the placeholder becomes visible again as a side effect). The focus-mode stutter was a **double driver**: `.gui-welcome-form`'s own `transition: height` and the JS WAAPI morph each ran a 280ms curve on the same property — now suppressed alongside the textarea/input for the morph window. The focused empty state no longer shows the quick-prompt chips. The send button and the suggestion chips join the shared liquid-glass recipe — the send button takes the armed-state accent-tinted glass (no more solid colour block), the chips trade their 1px border for the inset rim, and the "custom supplement" chip keeps its accent-tinted state.

- **语音输入的三个日常病灶：超时、说中文吐英文、报错一闪而过**：口述一开口就弹「request timeout」——`stt.transcribe` 压在全局 15s RPC 上限下，而首次使用要在这一次调用里完成 ~190MB 模型下载+加载，CPU 推理 15s 窗口也可能越线；现在 `request()` 支持按次 `timeoutMs`，语音链路（转写/合成）给到 180s，guest-client 同步对齐，并且口述开始时就发起模型预热（daemon 按 key 去重，下载与录音并行，转录时模型已在位）。说中文吐英文有两层：schema 默认 `stt.language: "en"` 把英文强加给所有人，改为 `""`（跟随界面语言，中文界面自动以 `zh` 起步；显式选「自动」则尊重 auto——Whisper 的语言自检对短句极不可靠，实测同一段中文 auto 判成英文、显式 `zh` 则近乎全对，所以设置页的「输入语言」才是可靠的开关）；报错也从全局 toast 挪进 composer 就地胶囊（超时/没听清/麦克风不可用/断连各有文案），口述期间按 Esc 直接丢弃重录。
  - EN: **Three everyday voice-input ailments: timeouts, Chinese speech coming back as English, and errors that flash and vanish**: dictation hit "request timeout" on first use — `stt.transcribe` rode the global 15s RPC cap while the very first call must download and load a ~190MB model, and even warm CPU inference of a 15s window could overrun. `request()` now takes a per-call `timeoutMs`: the speech paths (transcribe / synthesize) get 180s, guest-client matches, and dictation preheats the model on mic press (daemon dedupes per key, so the download overlaps the recording instead of billing the transcribe call). Chinese-in-English-out had two layers: the schema default `stt.language: "en"` forced English on everyone and is now `""` (follow the UI language — a zh UI starts dictation at `zh`; explicitly picking "auto" still means auto — Whisper's language self-detection is unreliable on short utterances: the same Chinese clip transcribed as English under auto and came back near-perfect under an explicit `zh`, so the settings "input language" control is the dependable switch). Errors also move from the global toast into an inline composer chip (distinct copy for timeout / didn't-catch-that / mic unavailable / disconnected), and Esc during dictation discards the take for a clean retry.

- **远程连接与扩展中心修复，插件清单统一**：Bot Channel 的微信等插件渠道此前两种坏法——iLink 二维码拉取没有超时，TLS 握手黑洞时 `channels.start` 永久挂起、界面毫无反馈（现在 10 秒超时并把渠道置为错误态，错误详情可见）；telegram / 飞书 / Lark 则根本没有表单定义，点「启动」只展开一个空壳（补齐 token / appId 字段，无表单渠道直接启动）。二维码与配置表单的展开收起改用全局规范的 Reveal 动效，停止状态的渠道状态点不再发绿光。扩展控制中心去掉与内部标题块重复的「扩展控制中心」外层标题，概览的来源健康度对齐 TUI 跳过内置来源——「MusePi」与「MusePi Extensions」不再并排出现。插件 tab 从只列插件包改为**统一清单**：marketplace/npm 插件包与热加载的扩展模块（`extension-module`）同屏，像技能管理一样逐项标记来源类别（内置 / 项目级 / 用户级）与类型（插件包 / 扩展模块），tab 计数为两者之和；能力中心的插件 tab 与之共用同一组件，扩展模块走共享 registry 单例、零新增轮询。空态只在两条链路都为空时出现，并说明各自从哪里来。
  - EN: **Remote-connect and extension-center fixes, one unified plugin list**: the Bot Channel's plugin transports failed two ways — the WeChat iLink QR fetch had no timeout, so a TLS black hole left `channels.start` pending forever with zero UI feedback (now a 10-second timeout that parks the channel in a visible error state), and Telegram / Feishu / Lark had no form definitions at all, so "Start" only opened an empty shell (their token / appId fields are filled in, and form-less channels start directly). The QR and config reveals now run the standard Reveal animation, and stopped channels no longer glow green. The extension control center drops its duplicated outer title, and the overview's source health skips the builtin source in line with the TUI — "MusePi" no longer sits beside "MusePi Extensions". The plugins tab becomes a **unified list**: marketplace/npm plugin packages and hot-loaded extension modules (`extension-module`) on one screen, each item tagged with its source category (builtin / project / user) and type (package / module) like skill management, with the tab count summing both; the capability center's plugins tab shares the same component, and modules ride the shared registry singleton with zero new polling. The empty state now only appears when both lanes are empty, and says where each one comes from.

- **语音输入修复（默认档的模型根本加载不起来）**：STT worker 里 transformers.js 4.3.0 一直被 bun 解析到错误的 `onnxruntime-node` 1.26.0（catalog 里为 mnemopi 钉的版本），而 1.26 的 QDQ 优化器拒绝 onnx-community Whisper 合并解码器的量化导出——`TransposeDQWeightsForMatMulNBits Missing required scale: model.decoder.embed_tokens.weight_merged_0_scale`，默认档 balanced（Whisper small, q8）与 fast（base, q8）无一幸免，GUI 口述一开口就弹「工具错误」。修复：根 package.json 显式声明 `onnxruntime-node@1.30.0`（transformers 4.3.0 的官方搭配），bun 把它提升到顶层后 worker 解析落到正确版本，q8 加载与转录实测通过；编译态二进制的 side runtime 自带完整依赖树，不受此影响。mnemopi 仍走自己的 1.26.0 嵌套链接，同样不受影响。
  - EN: **Voice input fixed (the default tier's model never loaded)**: inside the STT worker, transformers.js 4.3.0 kept resolving to the wrong `onnxruntime-node` 1.26.0 (the catalog version pinned for mnemopi), and 1.26's QDQ optimizer rejects the onnx-community Whisper merged-decoder quantized exports — `TransposeDQWeightsForMatMulNBits Missing required scale: model.decoder.embed_tokens.weight_merged_0_scale` — so both balanced (Whisper small, q8) and fast (base, q8) hard-failed, and GUI dictation surfaced a "tool error" on the first utterance. Fix: declare `onnxruntime-node@1.30.0` (transformers 4.3.0's own pairing) explicitly in the root package.json; with 1.30 hoisted to the top level the worker resolves the correct runtime, and q8 loading + transcription are verified working. Compiled binaries are unaffected (the side runtime carries its own complete dependency tree), and mnemopi keeps its own nested 1.26.0 link.

- **顶栏小球终于真正变小了**：`.gui-composer-pet` 的容器 clamp（34–46px）其实一直没生效——`PetSprite` 在内置矢量分支上给 `.gui-pet` 写了 inline 的 `width/height`，而 inline 样式的优先级高于任何样式表规则，于是小球始终按 `size × 缩放` 的创作尺寸渲染，dock 在输入框上沿时就是"一个巨大的球卡在窗口上"。其他头像预设走的是 petdex / 自绘 SVG 分支，从不经过这条路径，所以"换其他头像就正常"。现在 inline 尺寸已移除：内置球的宽度交给宿主决定（composer 用容器 clamp、头像位与悬浮宠物窗口各有自己的盒子），高度由 SVG 自身比例跟随，`size` 只在宿主既没给宽也没给高时作为兜底。顺带把伙伴大小滑杆的步进从 5% 对齐到 1%（此前 60/65/70… 与实际档位错开），并删掉 `.gui-composer-pet .gui-pet` 上的 `!important` 覆盖 hack——真因修掉后它只是在掩盖问题。
  - EN: **The composer orb is finally small**: `.gui-composer-pet`'s container clamp (34–46px) had never actually applied — `PetSprite` wrote an inline `width/height` on `.gui-pet` for the builtin vector branch, and an inline style outranks every stylesheet rule, so the pet rendered at its `size × scale` authoring size; docked on the input's top edge that is "a giant ball stuck on the window". Other avatar presets take the petdex / hand-drawn-SVG branches and never went through this path, which is why swapping the avatar made it look fine. The inline size is gone: the builtin orb's width now comes from its host (the composer's clamp, the avatar slot, the floating pet window each own their box), the height follows from the SVG's own ratio, and `size` survives only as the fallback for a host that supplies neither. The size slider's step is also aligned to 1% (it stepped by 5, so the stored value drifted off the labels), and the `!important` override on `.gui-composer-pet .gui-pet` is gone — with the root cause fixed it was only hiding it.

- **吉祥物去掉"莫名其妙的复杂化"，装饰改为可选配置项**：球体此前把所有能画的图层全开着——头顶的信标呼吸点、球下方的金色悬浮辉光、底部的反光带、地面投影、球面高光/流光。其中"地面系"（悬浮辉光 / 反光 / 投影）描述的是一具**悬浮在地面之上**的身体，而伙伴几乎总是 dock 在输入框上沿，那里根本没有地面：这些层会溢出到缝隙里，读作一条浮在输入框下的金色污渍；头顶呼吸点在任意 composer 尺寸下都细到不足一像素，只剩一个孤立的金点。现在这一族**整体删除**（不是默认关闭，是不再渲染），`VIEW` 的留白随之从约 1.28× 收到约 1.04×，球体本身在同样的盒子里变大、边缘也不再悬空。唯一保留的装饰是**球面光泽**（高光 + 流光）——它是让一个扁平深色圆读作"被照亮的球"的那一层，因此在每个场景下都成立，做成可关闭的开关（默认开）。配置走新的 `lib/pet-decor.ts`（`musepi-gui-pet-decor`，记录式、逐键降级），伙伴设置页新增「外观细节」分组并按标志表自动渲染开关；后续配饰（音符、耳机等）加一行标志 + 两个 i18n key 即可接入。
  - EN: **The mascot's gratuitous complexity is gone and its decoration is now opt-in configuration**: the orb used to render every layer the engine could draw — a beacon mast breathing on its crown, a gold hover-thrust glow under the ball, a bounce light along its bottom, a ground shadow, plus the shell's gloss and drifting sheen. The floor family (thrust / bounce / ground) describes a body **levitating over a ground plane**, but the pet is nearly always docked on an input's top edge where there is no floor: those layers spilled into the gap and read as a gold smear floating under the input, and at any composer size the beacon landed under one pixel — an orphaned gold speck. That family is now **removed outright** (not defaulted off — no longer rendered), and the rig's padding tightened from ~1.28× to ~1.04×, so the ball gets bigger inside the same box and stops hovering off its own edge. The one survivor is **surface gloss** (highlight + drifting sheen): it is what makes a flat dark circle read as a lit ball, so it is correct in every context, and it is an opt-out switch (on by default). Configuration lives in the new `lib/pet-decor.ts` (`musepi-gui-pet-decor`, a flag record that degrades key-by-key), and the companion settings page gains an "appearance detail" group rendered from that flag table — adding an accessory (notes, headphones, …) is one flag plus two i18n keys.

- **设置页的头像不再"只能切换"**：常规设置页的 Agent 头像此前是五个 38px 图标按钮排一行，身份只能靠 `title` 悬浮提示，选中态是一条发丝般的描边——五个选项在点下去之前几乎无法区分。现在改为带名称、实时预览与勾选态的卡片网格（预览 32px，是能judge 出差异的尺寸），同伴设置页同样有「外观细节」分组承接个性化配置，两处与伙伴外观网格（`.gui-pet-card`）用同一套卡片形制。
  - EN: **The settings avatar picker no longer only switches**: the agent avatar row in general settings was five 38px glyph buttons with identity available only through a `title` tooltip and a hairline ring for the selection — the five options were nearly indistinguishable until clicked. It is now a card grid with each preset's name, a live 32px preview (a size at which the difference is actually judgeable) and a check on the active one; the companion settings page gains a matching "appearance detail" group, and both reuse the companion appearance grid's card language (`.gui-pet-card`).

- **开关规范全局收编 + 四面板点阵动效移除**：`.gui-toggle` 此前有三处同名定义——gui-settings.css 的 36×20 标准真身、gui-workspace.css 的 26×15 `--sm` 变体、gui-widgets.css 里 TaskModal 自用的 38×22 复制品；聚合样式表里复制品加载在最后，把前两处的尺寸全部反向覆盖（标准开关全体变 38px 带 1px 边框；`--sm` 的 26px 小轨道被撑成 38px，而小圆点的 11px 位移仍按小轨道算——这才是扩展中心"圆点卡在轨道中间"的真根因，上一轮按 26px 轨道调位移其实修在别人的覆盖之上）。现在真身唯一：TaskModal 改用共享类与 `gui-toggle-knob`，`--sm` 变体收进真身旁（26×15 轨道、11px 圆点、11px 位移），设置页、扩展中心概览/能力清单、composer 模式开关、看板任务弹窗全部同一套开关形制。另外按反馈移除任务中心、看板、智能体中心、能力中心四个面板切换时的点阵显现动效（radial-dot mask 5px→68px 粗化），面板切换回归与其他页面一致的规范 blur 过渡（150ms 出场 / 300ms 入场），死掉的 keyframes 一并清除。
  - EN: **One toggle spec app-wide, plus the dot-matrix page-swap effect removed**: `.gui-toggle` had three same-name definitions — the 36×20 base in gui-settings.css, the 26×15 `--sm` variant in gui-workspace.css, and a TaskModal-only 38×22 copy in gui-widgets.css; loading last in the aggregate stylesheet, the copy silently re-styled both (standard switches all grew to 38px with a 1px border, and the `--sm` 26px track rendered at 38px while its 11px knob still travelled the small-track distance — the real root cause of the dot stuck mid-track in the extension center; last round's travel fix was computed against the 26px track and landed on top of that override). The base definition is now the only one: TaskModal joins the shared classes with `gui-toggle-knob`, the `--sm` variant lives beside the base (26×15 track, 11px knob, 11px travel), and settings / extension-center overview & capability list / composer mode toggles / the task widget modal all share one switch language. The dot-matrix surface reveal on the task-center / board / agents / capability-center swaps (a radial-dot mask coarsening 5px → 68px) is removed per feedback — those swaps now run the same standard blur transition as every other surface (150ms out / 300ms in), with the orphaned keyframes cleared.

- **审批胶囊外圈的颜色没了，footer 胶囊统一为液态玻璃**：审批模式胶囊此前套在审批**卡片**的警告色底里——`.gui-approval` 被审批卡（`ApprovalCard.tsx`，它画警告色底是正当的）和 footer 胶囊两个组件共用，卡片那条规则给无边框胶囊底下画了一整个彩色胶囊。现在胶囊走 `--pill` 修饰类清掉底色，并与设计风格胶囊共用一套液态玻璃配方（半透明填充 + 背景模糊 + 高光描边 + 对角水光，按下走弹簧曲线）；描边用 inset 阴影而非 border，999px 圆角上真画 1px border 就是那圈可见的环。状态（write / yolo / 已选风格）保持同一玻璃面、只让颜色承载。
  - EN: **The approval pill's stray ring is gone and the footer pills are now one liquid-glass family**: the approval-mode pill was wrapped in the approval CARD's warning surface — `.gui-approval` is shared by the card (`ApprovalCard.tsx`, where the warning tint is legitimate) and the footer pill, so the card's rule painted a full coloured capsule behind the borderless chip. The pill now uses a `--pill` modifier that drops the surface, and shares one liquid-glass recipe with the design-style pill (translucent fill + backdrop blur + specular rim + diagonal sheen, `--spring-liquid` on press); the rim is an inset shadow rather than a border, because a real 1px border on a 999px radius _is_ the visible ring. States (write / yolo / chosen style) keep the same glass surface and let colour carry the state.

- **桌宠不再渲染成黑球，脸改为白色**：黑球有两个成因，都已修——(1) SVG 壳渐变的写死回落色是冷板岩色、不是 accent 派生，色板生效前每一帧都是黑球，现在回落到达尔文本就该产出的深色 accent 石墨；(2) 主窗口只在主题/强调色变化时才推送色板（MutationObserver 首次加载从不触发），桌宠窗口在用户换主题前收不到任何 accent，现在挂载时也会先推一次。同时眼睛与嘴**不再跟随主题变色**：随主题变脸会让 ocean 主题下出现蓝眼配蓝球、脸从壳上分离不出来；现在脸在任何主题任何强调色下都是近白，守住"球上最亮的东西永远是脸"这条唯一需要的可读性规则，主题改由圈承载（error 脸仍保留唯一的那点红）。眼动幅度同步加大（脸单位 13.2/8.4 → 20.5/13.5，桌宠视距 420 → 260，头像预设 120 → 150），停靠球尺寸从 `44–60px` 收到 `34–46px`。
  - EN: **The pet no longer renders as a black ball, and the face is white**: the black ball had two causes, both fixed — (1) the SVG shell gradient's hardcoded fallback was a cold slate colour rather than accent-derived, so every frame before the palette applied showed a black ball; it now falls back to the dark accent-hued graphite the derivation actually produces. (2) The main window pushed the palette only when the theme/accent changed (a `MutationObserver`, which never fires on first load), so the pet window received no accent at all until the user switched theme; it now also pushes once on mount. The eyes and mouth likewise **no longer follow the theme colour**: a themed face meant ocean's eyes went blue on a blue-ish ball and stopped separating from the shell; the face is now near-white under every theme and accent, enforcing the one legibility rule the mascot needs — the brightest thing on the orb is always the face — with the ring carrying the accent instead (the error face keeps the single red). Gaze amplitude was raised alongside (13.2/8.4 → 20.5/13.5 face units, the pet's reach 420 → 260, the avatar preset 120 → 150), and the docked orb size narrowed from `44–60px` to `34–46px`.

- 输入框新增**审批模式按钮**（openchamber 输入框权限选择器对齐）：composer 右下角常驻 `盾牌 + 模式` 药丸，点击弹出三选菜单——**每次询问**（只自动批准只读工具）/ **自动批准写入**（读写放行，执行命令仍确认）/ **全部放行**（默认，yolo）。直接读写 `tools.approvalMode`（`settings.get`/`settings.set`）：daemon 的工具包装器每次工具调用都实时读它，切换即刻生效，无需重启会话；yolo 时按钮转警示红。会话 composer 与欢迎页 composer 都有（全局设置，无会话也可切）。
  - EN: The composer gains an **approval-mode button** (openchamber input permission-picker parity): a permanent `shield + mode` pill in the footer opens a three-way menu — **Always ask** (read-only tools auto-approved), **Auto-approve writes** (exec still confirms), **Allow everything** (the default, yolo). It reads and writes `tools.approvalMode` via `settings.get`/`settings.set`: the daemon's tool wrapper re-reads the setting on every tool call, so a flip takes effect immediately with no session restart; the pill turns warning-red while yolo. Present on both the session and the welcome composer (a global setting, usable session-less).

- 输入框**内嵌 token 高亮**（TUI 编辑器彩虹高亮对齐）：行首 `/命令` 与独立 `ultrathink` / `orchestrate` / `workflowz` 在输入框内即时着色（accent；magic 词用等宽字体），不再是纯文本。实现是透明文字 textarea + 镜像高亮层（`composer/input-highlight.tsx`）：光标经 `caret-color` 保留，命中词按 TUI 语义——只有行首命令才算命令（中部 `/btw` 不着色），magic 词用与 agent 一致的散文边界正则（`/ultrathink`、`foo.ultrathink` 不误染）；滚动经 onScroll 镜像同步，IME 组合期跟随。会话与欢迎页两处输入框都接了。
  - EN: The composer input now **highlights tokens inline** (TUI editor rainbow parity): line-leading `/commands` and standalone `ultrathink` / `orchestrate` / `workflowz` tint in accent the moment they are typed (magic words in the mono face) instead of staying plain text. Underneath sits a transparent-ink textarea with a mirrored highlight layer (`composer/input-highlight.tsx`): the caret survives via `caret-color`, matching follows TUI semantics — only line-leading commands count (a mid-text `/btw` stays plain), and magic keywords use the same prose-boundary regex as the agent (`/ultrathink`, `foo.ultrathink` stay untouched); scrolling mirrors through onScroll and IME composition tracks. Wired into both the session and the welcome composer.

- 桌宠新增**眼动跟随**（clawd-on-desk 对齐）：builtin 球的眼睛现在跟着鼠标走——桌面桌宠由主进程复用既有的 120ms 点击穿透轮询顺带算好"光标相对窗口中心"的归一化向量（420px 视距夹到 ±1、0.02 死区节流，零新增定时器）经 `pet:gaze` 推给渲染进程；引擎以 220ms 注意力渐入 / 380ms 渐出、120ms 滑动时间常数把实时目标与自带的闲视漂移混合——"注意到你"但不抽搐；挂靠左侧或拖拽翻面时写入侧做镜像补偿，视线永远对着真实光标。Petdex 精灵图为位图帧，不参与眼动。
  - EN: The pet gains **eye tracking** (clawd-on-desk parity): the builtin orb's eyes follow the cursor — the main process piggybacks cursor-vs-window-centre maths on its existing 120ms click-through poll (normalized to ±1 over a 420px reach, epsilon-throttled, zero new timers) and pushes `pet:gaze` to the renderer; the engine blends the live target with its authored idle drift through 220ms attack / 380ms release attention easing and a 120ms glide — the pet "notices" you without snapping; mirrored sprites (dock-left / walk-flip) negate x at write time so the gaze always points at the true cursor. Petdex spritesheets are baked bitmaps and sit this out.

- **Agent 头像新增"桌宠小球"预设，且表情真正绑定状态**：设置 → 常规 → Agent 头像新增 pet 预设（就是桌面那只球，眼睛同样跟随光标），并且它不参与随机效果轮播——表情直接映射会话状态。顺带修掉真正的病根：头部与聊天视图此前各自用一份重复的三元式派生 orb 状态、都漏掉了待审批这一态；现在 `orbFromSession` 是单一来源，有工具审批待处理时头像钉在新增的第 7 态 `waiting`（wave 动画、钉住不轮播——"暂停等你"），桌面桌宠同步切 waiting 表情。
  - EN: The agent avatar gains a **"pet orb" preset with real state binding**: 设置 → 常规 → Agent 头像 now offers the desktop mascot itself (eyes tracking the cursor), and it opts out of the random idle carousel — its face maps the session state directly. The actual root bug is fixed along the way: the header and the chat view each derived the orb from a duplicated ternary that ignored pending approvals; `orbFromSession` is now the single source, and pending tool approvals pin a new 7th orb state `waiting` (the wave animation, pinned — "paused for you"), mirrored by the desktop pet's waiting face.

- **桌宠配色跟随主题 accent**：球体此前是写死的石墨壳+品牌金，只在默认金色主题下成立（ocean 蓝主题下金圈金眼配冷蓝球像三种凑色）。现在 8 个绘制变量（壳三层 / 光族三层 / 边缘光 / 悬停眼辉）全部从实时 `--accent` 以 oklch 通道算术派生（chroma-js 只做解析；出 sRGB 色域的亮 tint 以 `oklch()` 字符串交给 Chromium 做色域映射、保色相），默认金色主题下与旧配色几乎重合（身份不变），mono 近中性 accent 也有染色下限；主窗口随 data-theme / data-accent 切换重算，桌宠窗口经 `petActivity {theme, accent}` 推送跟随。
  - EN: **The pet palette now follows the theme accent**: the orb's shell + gold pairing was hardcoded and only worked under the default gold accent (under ocean, a gold ring and gold eyes on a cold blue ball read as three unrelated colors). All 8 paint variables (three shell stops, three light-family stops, rim light, hover eye glow) are now derived from the live `--accent` with pure oklch channel arithmetic (chroma.js parses the token only; out-of-gamut tints ship as `oklch()` strings so Chromium gamut-maps them preserving hue); under the default brand accent the result lands within a hair of the old colors (identity preserved), mono's near-neutral accent still tints via a chroma floor, the main window re-derives on data-theme / data-accent flips, and the pet window follows via the `petActivity {theme, accent}` push.

### Fixed

- **液态玻璃铺满 footer 胶囊全家 + 扩展中心两处开关修复**：footer 的其余胶囊（圆形图标钮、专注/增强 pill、欢迎页工作区与预设 chip、会话模式开关、模型/思考胶囊）此前各画各的底——现在全部并入共享液态玻璃配方（半透明 `--glass-*` 填充 + 饱和 backdrop blur + 内嵌高光棱边 + 对角光泽，按压 `--spring-liquid`；排版尺寸仍归各自类），旧行的描边/软底死样式清除，`gui-settings.css` 里会反向覆盖配方的旧底色一并移除（该文件在配方之后加载）。扩展控制中心两处开关：概览页供应商行的开关是**自闭合空按钮**——圆点（`gui-toggle-knob`）是子元素，没渲染就没有圆点，概览与能力清单两处补上；能力清单处**圆点偏移**则是 `gui-toggle--sm` 的 ON 位移沿用 36px 大开关的 16px，把 11px 圆点推出 26px 轨道，现按小轨道改为 11px。技能市场的"来源/排序"四个**原生 `<select>`**（系统 chrome 的"毛坯"下拉）统一迁到磨砂 `GuiSelect`，死掉的 `.gui-skill-market-select` 样式一并清除。
  - EN: **Liquid glass now covers the whole footer-pill family, plus two extension-center switch fixes**: the remaining footer pills (round icon buttons, the focus/enhance pill, the welcome workspace & preset chips, the session mode toggles, the model/thinking capsule) each painted their own background — they all join the shared liquid-glass recipe now (translucent `--glass-*` fill, saturated backdrop blur, inset specular rim, diagonal sheen, `--spring-liquid` on press; layout stays in each member's own class), with the old hairline/soft-fill dead styles removed, including the ones in `gui-settings.css` that loaded after the recipe and would have overridden it. Two switches in the extension control center: the overview provider rows rendered **self-closing empty buttons** — the dot (`gui-toggle-knob`) is a child element, so no child meant no dot (overview and capability list both gained the span); the capability list's **offset dot** came from `gui-toggle--sm` reusing the 36px switch's 16px ON travel, pushing the 11px knob past its 26px track — now 11px. The skill market's four **native `<select>`** dropdowns (system chrome) move to the frosted `GuiSelect`, and the orphaned `.gui-skill-market-select` styles go with them.

- **design 预设选择此前从未真正生效**：欢迎页选了设计模式（风格 chips 已 armed、占位已切换），创建的会话却总落在工作模式——侧栏悬浮卡显示"工作模式"、会话内 composer 的 design 风格行永远不出现。根因是创建会话的 `useCallback` 依赖表漏了 `welcomeModeId`：handler 闭包里永远是旧值（stale closure），欢迎页 UI 与 create RPC 的 modeId 从此分叉。现在 handler 经同步 ref 读取最新选择，选什么落什么。
  - EN: **Picking the design preset never actually took effect**: the welcome page armed the style chips and swapped the placeholder, yet every session it created still landed on the work preset — the sidebar hover card said "work mode" and the design style row never appeared in the session composer. The create-session `useCallback` was missing `welcomeModeId` in its dependency array: the handler closure kept the initial value (a stale closure), so the welcome UI and the create RPC's modeId diverged. The handler now reads the latest pick through a synchronized ref — what you choose is what gets applied.

- **审批药丸不再自带一圈描边**：审批模式按钮此前复用模式 arm chip 的基础类，那类自带 1px 描边胶囊——静态看像凭空多了一圈背景；write 态还叠虚线 accent。现在按钮自立无描边基础类：常态纯文字，状态只上色（write=accent、yolo=警示红，各配 12% 软底），hover/菜单展开为 8% 中性软底，任何状态都不再出现边框圈。
  - EN: **The approval pill no longer ships with a stray ring**: the button reused the mode arm chip's base class, which carries a 1px hairline capsule — statically it read as an unexplained background circle, and the write state stacked a dashed accent outline on top. It now stands on its own borderless base class: plain text at rest, states colour via soft fills only (write=accent, yolo=warning red, each a 12% tint; hover/open an 8% neutral wash) — no border ring in any state.

- **设计风格选择改为 WorkBuddy 式 footer 胶囊**：会话与欢迎页输入框上方的"设计风格｜…"chips 行撤掉，改为按钮行里一颗「风格： 当前值 ∨」选择胶囊（palette 图标 + 下拉 caret），点开浮动菜单选择（跟随既有 + 五个风格，选中项打勾）。选中即把简报更新句写进输入框（契约不变）；选中风格后胶囊以 accent 软底高亮；design 会话输入框占位同步切为设计引导文案。
  - EN: **The design-style picker becomes a WorkBuddy-style footer pill**: the chips row above the input is gone on both composers, replaced by a "style: current ∨" pill in the button row (palette icon + dropdown caret) that opens a floating menu (inherit + five styles, check on the selection). Picking a style still writes the brief-update sentence into the composer (contract unchanged); a chosen style tints the pill accent, and the design-session composer placeholder now carries the design-specific hint.

- **挂靠输入框的桌宠现在真的能点**：输入框左侧那只桌宠此前是 `pointer-events:none` 的纯装饰、尺寸还写死 `size={30}`——点它会把光标从输入框里踢出去（`activeElement` 从 `#ta` 掉到 `body`），嘴型笔画只有 0.80px（亚像素，等于没画）。现在 `onPointerDown` 里 `preventDefault()` 抑制由 pointerdown 派生的兼容鼠标事件，blur 不再发生而 `click` 照常触发（摸它会跳一下，可以逗）；尺寸改由容器查询按输入框宽度自适应（`clamp(44px, 4.6cqw + 26px, 60px)`，46px 地板，过窄则降级为只留两个眼灯），挂靠态下嘴型恢复到 1.13px 可辨识；顺带修掉挂靠态浮出的天线亚像素细线、viewBox 底部约 6px 死区造成的悬空，以及**只有截图才发现的一团金色污渍**（三个地面阴影层锚在轮廓下方，溢出球底 4.7px）。
  - EN: **The composer-docked pet is actually clickable now**: that slot was a `pointer-events:none` ornament with a hard-coded `size={30}` — poking it dropped the caret out of the input (`activeElement` went `#ta` → `body`), and its mouth stroke resolved to 0.80px (sub-pixel, i.e. invisible). `onPointerDown` now calls `preventDefault()`, which suppresses the compatibility mouse events derived from pointerdown: the blur never runs while `click` still fires (poke it and it hops — it's pettable). Size now follows the composer width via a container query (`clamp(44px, 4.6cqw + 26px, 60px)`, 46px floor, degrading to two eye lamps when too narrow), bringing the mouth back to a legible 1.13px when docked; alongside that, the sub-pixel antenna hairline, the ~6px float caused by the viewBox bottom dead zone, and **a gold smear only the screenshot revealed** (three ground-shadow layers anchored below the silhouette, spilling 4.7px past the ball) are all fixed.

- **桌宠通知气泡四种类型现在能区分**：`completed` / `question` / `subtask` 此前和彼此长得一模一样，而 `question` 是唯一**阻塞 agent** 的类型——最该被看见的气泡反而最没存在感。现在每类气泡带一条 2.5px 左侧色条 + 类型标签（已完成/出错/待回答/子任务），`error` 与 `question` 额外给边框上色以示紧急。
  - EN: **The four pet bubble kinds are now distinguishable**: `completed` / `question` / `subtask` previously rendered identically, and `question` is the one kind that **blocks the agent** — the most urgent bubble had the least presence. Every kind now carries a 2.5px left rule plus a kind tag (done / failed / needs answer / subtask), with `error` and `question` additionally tinting their border to signal urgency.

- **Windows 上桌宠不再漂移、下沉或被全屏应用挡住**：拖拽桌宠时它会在松手前越飘越偏——根因是 win32 把 DIP 坐标按显示器转物理像素时会取整、贴边还会吸附，而这次修正从未回写到拖拽锚点，误差每帧都从旧假设重新累加（本机 150% 缩放，正是取整误差最大的配置）。现在位置写入统一走"先读实时位置、有差异才写"的回校通道。同时补齐四类自愈：拔插显示器/改缩放/睡眠唤醒后桌宠会回到可达位置（**但你特意放的位置不会被挪走**）、唤醒时按基准尺寸纠正被越睡越大的窗口、置顶层级每 5s 重断言且拖到屏幕边缘立即重断言，以及**全屏应用（游戏、视频、演示）前自动让位、退出后回来**——包括别人的程序，点桌面和 Alt-Tab 不算全屏。
  - EN: **On Windows the pet no longer drifts, sinks, or hides behind fullscreen apps**: while dragging, it wandered further off the longer you held the pointer — win32 rounds DIP to physical pixels per monitor and snaps at edges, and that correction was never applied back to the drag anchor, so the error re-derived from a stale assumption every frame (the dev box runs 150% scaling, exactly where that rounding is largest). Position writes now go through a read-back channel that reads the live rect and writes only when it differs. Four self-heal paths came with it: the pet returns to a reachable spot after a display is plugged/unplugged, on a DPI change, or after sleep/wake (**but a spot you placed it in deliberately is never moved**), a window ratcheted larger by repeated sleep cycles is corrected against its base size, the always-on-top band is re-asserted every 5s and immediately on a drag to a screen edge, and it **steps aside for fullscreen apps — games, video, presentations — including other people's**, returning when they exit; clicking the desktop or Alt-Tabbing does not count as fullscreen.

- **引导式目标补齐 TUI 用法**：输入框现在支持 `/guided-goal [粗略目标]` 行内参数（会话与欢迎页都行——欢迎页先自动建会话再触发；`//` 转义不受影响），`/` 菜单同时放行该命令（此前被 tuiOnly 过滤藏掉）。触发时输入框里已就位的内容**原样随行**：引用块转 `> ` 前缀、文件 chips 先上传成工作区路径再拼进目标、图片 chips 作为 wire 图片挂在隐藏 kickoff 上（`session.goal` guided 新增 `images` 参数，对齐 TUI 的 `input.images`）。daemon 侧 guided 分支补齐 TUI 同序前置校验（plan/vibe 未退、`goal.enabled` 关闭、goal 已激活/暂停），拒绝原因原文透传到 slash 提示与 pet 通知，不再只报笼统的"启动失败"。
  - EN: **Guided goal now matches the full TUI usage**: the composer accepts `/guided-goal [rough objective]` with inline args (session and welcome alike — the welcome page creates the session first; `//` escaping is untouched), and the command is un-hidden in the `/` menu (it was filtered out as tuiOnly before). Whatever is staged in the composer rides along: quotes become `> ` prefixes, file chips upload to workspace paths and prepend to the objective, and image chips attach to the hidden kickoff as wire images (`session.goal` guided gained an `images` param, TUI `input.images` parity). The daemon's guided branch also runs the TUI-ordered pre-checks (plan/vibe active, `goal.enabled` off, goal active/paused), and the rejection wording passes through to the slash notice / pet notification instead of a generic "could not start".

- **引导式目标点了没反应**：欢迎页的处理器是字面空函数 `() => {}`，会话内则被 `!rpc || !sessionId` 静默拦截、失败还被 `.catch(() => {})` 吞掉——三个入口全都不给反馈。现在欢迎页点击即**创建会话并触发访谈**（`submitNewSession` 新增 `guidedGoal` 选项：建会话后对它发 `session.goal op:"guided"`，daemon 侧以隐藏合成消息启动访谈 kickoff，输入框草稿作为粗略目标一并带走）；会话内成功后清空已带走的草稿，失败弹 slash 错误提示（新 i18n 键 `guided goal failed`），草稿保留不丢。
  - EN: **The guided-goal menu row did nothing**: the welcome handler was a literal `() => {}`, the session one was silently gated by `!rpc || !sessionId`, and failures vanished into `.catch(() => {})` — no feedback on any path. The welcome entry now **creates a session and starts the interview** (`submitNewSession` gained a `guidedGoal` option: after creating the session it fires `session.goal op:"guided"`, whose daemon side kicks off the hidden synthetic interview prompt, carrying the composer draft along as the rough objective); the session composer clears the carried draft only after success and surfaces failures in the slash notice (new i18n key `guided goal failed`), keeping the draft on error.

- `/` 菜单**不再列出 TUI 专属命令**：`commands.list` 现在带出 `tuiOnly`（无 ACP/text-mode `handle` 的内置命令），GUI 补全菜单隐藏这些条目——此前 /settings、/setup 等点了只回"该命令仅在终端中可用"。GUI 用自有面板拦截的命令（/usage /context /debug /btw /autoresearch）保留可见；技能命令与常规命令不受影响。
  - EN: The `/` menu **no longer lists TUI-only commands**: `commands.list` now carries `tuiOnly` (builtin commands without an ACP/text-mode `handle`) and the GUI completion menu hides them — previously /settings, /setup and friends could only ever answer "该命令仅在终端中可用". Commands the GUI intercepts with its own panels (/usage /context /debug /btw /autoresearch) stay visible; skill and regular commands are unaffected.

- 侧栏会话行新增**悬浮卡**（WorkBuddy 悬浮弹窗对齐 + 我们的 mode 行）：行上停 ~350ms 在行右侧浮出磨砂卡——标题（2 行截断）、fork 来源、**会话预设 chip**（accent 着色；`mode {id}` 词表命名，未知预设回落"默认模式"，与右栏头同规）、任务工作空间（basename，title 全路径）、最后活跃与创建时间；取代行原生 title 气泡。数据链同步补齐：daemon 会话 header 的 `modeId` 落进 view-store `sessions.mode_id` 列（`PRAGMA table_info` 幂等加列，ON CONFLICT 随写同步），`session.list` 逐行带出，桌面端 `sessionMeta` 全量/轮询/合并三处承接。行经模块级 bus 上报（`SessionHoverCard.tsx`），不给 memo 化的 `SessionRow` 加 props；卡片 portal 到 `document.body`，两阶段入场防毛玻璃闪变，滚动（capture）/点击/离行（120ms 跨行宽限）即收。
  - EN: Session rows in the sidebar gain a **hover card** (WorkBuddy parity plus our mode row): rest ~350ms on a row and a frosted card floats to its right — title (2-line clamp), fork source, a **session-preset chip** (accent-tinted; named via the `mode {id}` lexicon with a default-mode fallback for unknown presets, matching the right-pane header), the task workspace (basename, full path on title), and last-active + created timestamps; it replaces the row's native `title` tooltip. The data chain is completed end to end: the daemon's session-header `modeId` lands in the view-store `sessions.mode_id` column (idempotent `PRAGMA table_info` migration, synced on the ON CONFLICT update), `session.list` carries it per row, and the desktop `sessionMeta` map absorbs it in the full refresh, the 5s poll and the merge path. Rows report through a module-level bus (`SessionHoverCard.tsx`) so the memoized `SessionRow` gains no props; the card portals to `document.body`, enters two-phase to avoid the frosted flash, and dismisses on scroll (capture), click or leave (120ms cross-row grace).

- **design 预设空态**补齐（设计稿 08 欢迎页对齐）：design 预设 armed 时，欢迎页输入框与会话 composer 同款——风格 chips 行（`composer/design-styles.tsx` 共享 `DesignStyleChips` + `DESIGN_STYLES`，会话 composer 一并改用共享模块）渲染在输入卡上方，点风格即把 `design style brief update {style}` 句写进输入框；placeholder 切为 design 专属提示（`design empty placeholder`），不再轮播通用能力提示。
  - EN: The **design-preset empty state** is completed (设计稿 08 welcome parity): with the design preset armed, the welcome composer mirrors the session composer — the shared style-chips row (`composer/design-styles.tsx` exporting `DesignStyleChips` + `DESIGN_STYLES`; the session composer now consumes the same module) renders above the input and picking a style writes the `design style brief update {style}` sentence into the composer, while the placeholder switches to a design-specific hint (`design empty placeholder`) instead of the rotating capability tips.

- 画板重开（编辑已发附件）时导入的底图**变成可编辑图元**：onload 后按 `fitImage` 的落位转成一条 `tool:"image"` 笔画（points 是 `[x, y, width, height]` 两个长度而非第二个角，`src` 存原 dataUrl），并把对应的 add op 直接塞进撤销栈——打开即可 Ctrl+Z 撤掉这张图，清除、框选、平移、等比缩放、随笔画导出全部和手绘图元同一套路径。底图不再走"单独渲染的底层位图"（`baseCleared` 机制与那层 `<Layer listening={false}>` 一并删除），画板上不再有撤销/擦除模型管不到的内容。几何库的 `strokeExtent` / `shiftPoints` / `scalePoints` / `strokeBox` / `hasVisibleExtent` 补上 image 分支（平移只动原点、缩放原点仿射且边长乘 factor、包围盒不加 padding），新增 6 个单测。
  - EN: Re-opening the board (editing a sent attachment) now turns the imported base image into an **editable object**: after onload it is fitted and pushed as a `tool:"image"` stroke — `points` carries two lengths `[x, y, width, height]` rather than a second corner, `src` keeps the original data URL — and its add op is seeded straight into the undo stack, so Ctrl+Z drops the image immediately and clear, marquee select, translate, corner-scale and export all run through the exact same path as hand-drawn ink. The base image is no longer a separately painted bottom bitmap (the `baseCleared` escape hatch and its `<Layer listening={false}>` are gone), so nothing on the board sits outside the undo/erase model. The geometry library's `strokeExtent` / `shiftPoints` / `scalePoints` / `strokeBox` / `hasVisibleExtent` gained image branches (translation moves only the origin, scaling affines the origin and multiplies the side lengths, the box takes no padding), with 6 new unit tests.

- 画板选中工具补**等比缩放手柄**：选中非文本图元后四角出现 8px 手柄，拖拽即以对角为锚点等比缩放（比例 = 指针到锚点的距离比，起步距离 <2px 时保持 1:1 防抖动），放手后走与平移相同的 move op，撤销/重做零改动。缩放数学落在 `lib/sketch-geometry.ts` 的 `scalePoints`（仿射回写 points 而非 Konva 节点 scale，避免笔宽失真，factor 限幅 0.05–40，压感值透传），并把平移用的 `shiftPoints` 一并收进几何库——顺带修掉它在笔迹三元组上 x/y 增量对调、斜向拖拽扭斜笔迹的潜伏缺陷。三个函数共 17 个新单测。文本图元仍通过光标编辑，不设缩放手柄。
  - EN: The sketch board's select tool gains **corner scale handles**: with a non-text stroke selected, four 8px handles appear at the corners and dragging scales the stroke uniformly about the opposite corner (factor = pointer-to-anchor distance ratio, held at 1:1 while the grab starts within 2px of the anchor), committing through the same move op as translation so undo/redo need no new branch. The maths lives in `lib/sketch-geometry.ts` as `scalePoints` — the transform replays back into `points` rather than a Konva node scale, avoiding stroke-width distortion, with the factor clamped to 0.05–40 and pressure passed through untouched. The translation helper `shiftPoints` moved into the geometry file alongside it, fixing a latent bug where pen-triplet x/y deltas were swapped and diagonal drags sheared the ink. 17 new unit tests across the three functions; text labels keep editing through their caret and get no handles.

- 画板对齐 Codex：新增**文本工具**（画布上点一下即可输入，输入法/粘贴/选区都是原生行为，写完落成真文字层）、**形状飞出菜单**（直线/箭头/矩形/椭圆/菱形/三角形/五角星/心形，共 8 种；轨道按钮显示当前选中的形状，矩形和菱形一眼可辨）、**12 色调色盘**与**连续粗细滑杆**（1–24，取代原来只有 2/4/8/14 的四档按钮）。画板几何（拖拽方向归一化、星形/心形取点、文本度量）抽成 `lib/sketch-geometry.ts` 并配 42 个单测——Konva 离开 canvas 没法断言，抽出来才测得动。
  - EN: The sketch board now matches Codex: a **text tool** (tap the canvas and type — IME, paste and selection behave natively, and the label lands as a real text layer), a **shape flyout** (line / arrow / rectangle / ellipse / diamond / triangle / star / heart — 8 shapes; the rail button shows the armed one so rectangle vs diamond stays legible), a **12-swatch palette**, and a **continuous thickness slider** (1–24) replacing the old four-step 2/4/8/14 buttons. Board geometry (drag-direction normalization, star/heart point generation, text metrics) moved to `lib/sketch-geometry.ts` with 42 unit tests — Konva can't be asserted against without a canvas, so extracting it is what made it testable at all.

- 技能市场接入 SkillHub 与 skills.sh：`skills/marketplace-client.ts` 提供统一查询层（关键词 / 分类 / 来源 / 排序 / 分页、精选、分类表、详情与 302 下载），并新增 `skills.marketplace.query`、`categories`、`featured`、`detail` 四个 daemon RPC。SkillHub 全量公开、免鉴权，是主源（图标 / 星标 / 下载量 / 分类 / 中文描述齐备）；skills.sh 的 `/api/v1/*` 需要 Vercel OIDC token，桌面端无从签发，故只接其免鉴权的 `/api/search` 作为补充搜索源。任一源失败只记入 `failures` 而不抛出，单个市场挂掉不会清空整个商店。
  - EN: The skill marketplace now talks to SkillHub and skills.sh: `skills/marketplace-client.ts` is a unified query layer (keyword / category / source / sort / page, featured, category list, detail and the 302 download) backed by four new daemon RPCs — `skills.marketplace.query`, `categories`, `featured` and `detail`. SkillHub is the primary source: fully public, no auth, and the only one carrying icons, stars, download counts, categories and Chinese descriptions. skills.sh's `/api/v1/*` needs a Vercel OIDC token a desktop app cannot mint, so only its unauthenticated `/api/search` is wired as a supplementary source. A failing source is recorded in `failures` instead of throwing, so one dead catalog never blanks the whole store.

### Changed

- 插件市场暂缓：MusePi 的插件体系还在演进，公开市场接入整体搁置（后续可能像插件化 harness 一样整体重做）。daemon 侧已实现的 `marketplace.sources.*` 四个 RPC 随之回滚，桌面端的能力探测清单收窄为 `skills.marketplace.query`（技能市场不受影响），避免每次启动触发一次无意义的能力重启。
  - EN: The plugin marketplace is deferred: MusePi's extension story is still settling and the public-marketplace integration is shelved wholesale (it may be reworked as a full plugin system later, harness-style). The four daemon-side `marketplace.sources.*` RPCs were rolled back accordingly, and the desktop capability-probe list narrows to `skills.marketplace.query` (the skill marketplace is unaffected), so a boot no longer triggers a pointless capability restart.

### Fixed

- 用户创建的会话预设**在悬浮卡与右栏头部都显示成"默认模式"**：命名链只有 `mode {id}` 词表一步，用户/插件注册的预设没有 i18n 键，`t()` 回显 key 后直接回落。现抽共享取名链 `lib/mode-label.ts`（`resolveModeLabel`）：词表命中即用（内置四预设本地化）→ 回显则查 `modes.list` 目录的 label（用户模式真名的唯一来源）→ 仍无才回落"默认模式"；右栏 `sessionModeLabel` 尾段与悬浮卡 `modeName` 同源，两处永远显示同一个名字。catalog 由 app 的 `welcomeModes`（modes.list + modes.changed）经 SessionSidebar 与 ChatView 既有 `modes` prop 下发。
  - EN: User-created session presets **rendered as "默认模式" in both the hover card and the right-pane header**: the naming chain had only the `mode {id}` lexicon step — user/plugin-registered presets carry no i18n key, so the echoed key fell straight back. The chain is now a shared resolver, `lib/mode-label.ts` (`resolveModeLabel`): lexicon hit wins (localizes the four builtin presets), an echoed key consults the `modes.list` catalog label (the only source of a user mode's real name), and only then falls back to "default mode"; the right-pane `sessionModeLabel` tail and the hover card's mode row share it, so both surfaces always agree. The catalog rides the app's `welcomeModes` (modes.list + modes.changed) down through SessionSidebar and ChatView's existing `modes` prop.

- 画板文本工具**点画布落不下输入框**（浏览器实测定位）：不是深色底看不见，而是根本没接上焦点。Konva 在 `pointerdown` 里同步挂 textarea 并 `focus()`，但 mousedown 的默认动作会在同一手势内把焦点夺回 `<body>`（canvas 自身不可聚焦），textarea 立刻 blur、空草稿被丢弃，于是"点了没反应"。现在 pointerdown 只记坐标并 `preventDefault()`，pointerup 才落下光标，focus 推到下一帧执行；指针移出画布时清掉待落坐标。实测证据：旧实现点击后 `document.activeElement` 是 `BODY`、无光标；新实现是 `TEXTAREA`、可输入，Escape 提交后文字渲染在画布上、撤销按钮可用。顺带修掉 React 19 下 `key` 随 props 展开传进 JSX 的警告（图元 key 改为逐分支显式书写）。
  - EN: The sketch board's text tool **could not plant a caret when you clicked the canvas** — pinned in a real browser, not by reading code. It was not invisible ink on a dark ground; the focus was never actually taken. Konva mounted the textarea and called `focus()` synchronously inside `pointerdown`, but the mousedown default action reclaims focus for `<body>` within that same gesture (a canvas is not focusable), so the textarea blurred instantly, the empty draft was discarded, and the click looked like a no-op. Pointerdown now only parks the coordinates and calls `preventDefault()`; the caret lands on pointerup and the focus is deferred a frame; leaving the canvas clears the pending tap. Field evidence: the old build left `document.activeElement` on `BODY` with no caret, the new one on `TEXTAREA` with a live caret — typing then Escape renders the label on the canvas and enables undo. Also silenced a React 19 warning about `key` riding into JSX through a props spread (stroke keys are now written per branch).

- 画板修四处实测问题：①**弹窗拼色**——画布是不透明色块、四周是液态玻璃，两种材质硬拼成色差，现在画布实底只在导出快照时临时上色（浅色导出白底、否则主题色），编辑时透出玻璃，整块画板读作同一种材质；②**形状飞出菜单被画布截断**——工具栏与画布区同层且画布区在后，超出工具栏的菜单下半被盖住，工具栏提到画布之上后菜单完整浮出；③**深色主题下画笔与文字"画了/输了却看不见"**——默认墨色是给浅底准备的深墨，落在深色画布上对比度仅 1.04:1，深色开板改用浅灰默认墨色（仍在 12 色盘内）；④**重开画板（编辑已发附件）后撤销/清除失效**——内容是导入底图、不在撤销模型里，按钮全部禁用且点击毫无反馈，现在底图本身落成一条可撤销的图元（见 Added 里的「底图变成可编辑图元」），撤销/清除/框选/平移/缩放与手写内容完全一致。另外修掉选中虚线框的颜色：Konva 在 canvas 上画线，CSS 变量 `var(--accent)` 是非法 fillStyle 会被静默丢弃，改为解析成真实色值再传入。
  - EN: Four field-reported fixes on the sketch board: (1) **mismatched surfaces** — the canvas was an opaque slab while the chrome around it is liquid glass; the canvas fill is now painted on only for the export snapshot (white under light-export, else the theme colour) while editing stays transparent, so the board reads as one material. (2) **the shape flyout was clipped by the canvas** — the toolbar and the canvas sat on the same stacking level with the canvas later, so the flyout's overhang was covered; the toolbar now stacks above and the menu floats fully. (3) **ink and typed labels invisible on dark boards** — the default ink was a light-ground dark at ~1.04:1 contrast on the dark canvas; dark boards now open on the palette's light grey. (4) **undo/clear dead on a re-opened board** (editing a sent attachment) — the content was an imported base image outside the undo model, leaving every control disabled; the base image now lands as an undoable stroke of its own (see the Added entry on editable base images), so undo/clear/select/translate/scale match hand-drawn content exactly. Also fixed the selection frame colour: Konva paints on canvas where the CSS variable `var(--accent)` is an invalid fillStyle silently dropped — resolved to a concrete value instead.

- 技能详情抽屉对齐设计稿 2:665 重做：不再从视口 fixed 全屏盖下来，改为挂进「我安装的」内容壳层的 absolute 面板——顶栏与工具行不再被遮住；表面带 `--radius-xl` 圆角与液态玻璃背景；开合补齐动效（320ms 弹簧滑入、200ms 滑出，`prefers-reduced-motion` 时直切）。内容改为三 tab：概览（摘要 + 来源/级别/路径 kv + 风险提示块）、SKILL.md 正文、版本信息；动作行提供「复制调用」（复制 `/{技能名}`——设置窗口没有会话，这是当前诚实的落地）、停用/启用与卸载。
  - EN: The skill detail drawer was rebuilt to match design frame 2:665: instead of a full-viewport fixed overlay it now mounts as an absolute panel inside the "installed" content shell — the header and toolbar stay visible; the surface gets `--radius-xl` rounding and the liquid-glass background; and open/close finally has motion (320ms spring slide-in, 200ms slide-out, instant under `prefers-reduced-motion`). Content is three tabs — Overview (summary + source/level/path kv + a risk note), the SKILL.md body, and Versions — with an action row offering "copy invocation" (copies `/{skill}`; the settings window has no session, so this is the honest v1), disable/enable and uninstall.

- 扩展控制中心修三处：①双栏网格此前套在整页容器上，单子元素视图（概览/诊断等）被硬塞进 240px 左列——改为根容器单列、由 inventory 视图自带 `240px minmax(0,1fr)` 双栏；②诊断 tab 空白且"转半天"——它此前自拉 `extensions.list` + `skills.list` 全量扫描，现在改吃共享 registry 单例（切 tab 即渲染），未就绪时渲染骨架占位，加载失败也能看到错误而非空白；③移除页底的槽位注册表行——未托管槽位告警并入标题旁的状态胶囊与统计卡副文案，信息不丢但不再占一屏。
  - EN: Three fixes in the extensions center: (1) the two-column grid used to wrap the whole page, so single-child views (overview, diagnostics, …) were squeezed into the 240px left column — the root is now a single column and only the inventory view carries its own `240px minmax(0,1fr)` split; (2) the diagnostics tab was blank and "spun forever" — it used to re-fetch `extensions.list` + `skills.list` itself, now it reads the shared registry singleton (renders on tab switch) with skeleton placeholders while the first pull is pending, and errors stay visible instead of blank; (3) the slot-registry footer row is gone — unhosted-slot warnings moved into a status pill next to the title and the stat-card sublines, keeping the signal without spending a screen.

- 能力中心「我安装的」重做为设计稿 2:250 的形状：工具行是搜索 + 来源/排序双下拉 + 「批量管理」开关，下面一级别筛选 chips（全部/官方/用户级/项目级/已停用），标题行 `全部技能 · N` 带网格/列表切换。卡片补上方形彩色字形图标（无图标时取首字母，色相由技能名 hash 得出——同一技能在网格与列表两个视图永远同色）、`级别 · 来源 · 版本` 副行、启用/停用标签；内置技能注明「随客户端分发 · 不可卸载」。开启批量管理后卡片左上角出现选择角标，底部升起批量操作条（`已选 N 项 · 共 M 个技能` + 全选/卸载/批量停用/批量启用）。批量启停复用 `skills.ignoredSkills`（与详情抽屉里的开关同一条 read-modify-write 路径），批量卸载逐个走 `skills.delete`、跳过内置与扩展技能并在确认文案里如实说明跳过数量——daemon 本来就会拒绝这些删除，与其让用户点了报错，不如先说明。
  - EN: The capability center's "installed" pane now matches design frame 2:250: the toolbar is search + source/sort dropdowns + a "bulk manage" toggle, below it a level filter chip row (All / Official / User / Project / Disabled), and a `All skills · N` title row with a grid/list switch. Cards gained a square colored glyph (initial-letter fallback, hue hashed from the skill name — so one skill keeps the same color in both the grid and list views), a `level · origin · version` subline, and an enabled/disabled tag; built-ins are marked "ships with the client · cannot uninstall". With bulk manage on, cards grow a selection corner and a bottom action bar rises (`N selected · M skills total` + select all / uninstall / disable all / enable all). Bulk enable/disable reuses `skills.ignoredSkills` (the same read-modify-write path as the drawer's toggle), and bulk uninstall runs `skills.delete` per skill, skipping built-in and extension skills while stating the skipped count in the confirmation — the daemon rejects those deletions anyway, and explaining first beats letting the click fail.

- 引导界面的品牌色不是金色：三个漏网的旧翡翠绿 `#34d399` 字面量。①「品牌」色块的底色从 `var(--accent)` 取，而 `--accent` 是按 `data-accent` 分域的——色块上同时挂着 `data-accent="ocean"`，于是它解析到的是自己身上那段「海洋」定义，四个色块全变成同一个蓝；②品牌那一格另有一条字面量 `oklch(0.773 0.1538 163)`（= 翡翠绿），0.4.31 换金时因为是字面量而被扫漏；③桌宠窗口自带一份 `--color-accent` 字面量（该窗口不加载 tokens.css），同样是旧翡翠绿，工作态圆点、发送按钮、贴边光条都还是绿的。前两处修完「品牌」回到 `#d9a441`；另外引导页主按钮（下一步/开始使用）此前根本没上色——`gui-btn-primary` 只在 gui-composer.css 里为另一套组件写过，桌面 GUI 没定义，按钮一直回落到中性灰底，现在补成金底深字。新增 `packages/client-core/test/brand-accent.test.ts` 锁住这些值与「色块不得自引用 `--accent`」这条结构规则。
  - EN: The onboarding UI was not gold — three leftover emerald `#34d399` literals. (1) The "Brand" swatch painted `var(--accent)`, but `--accent` is `data-accent`-scoped and the swatch itself carries `data-accent="ocean"`, so it resolved the ocean block sitting on the same element and all four swatches came out the same blue. (2) The brand swatch also carried a literal `oklch(0.773 0.1538 163)` (= emerald), missed by the 0.4.31 re-gilding precisely because it was a literal rather than a reference. (3) The pet window ships its own `--color-accent` literal (that window never loads tokens.css) and it too was still emerald, leaving its working dot, send button and dock-edge glow green. The first two now put brand back on `#d9a441`; additionally the primer's primary CTA (Next / Get started) had never been styled at all — `gui-btn-primary` only exists in gui-composer.css for a different component family, so the desktop GUI fell back to `.gui-btn`'s neutral grey and the primer had no brand presence in any button; it now renders as gold ground with dark ink. New `packages/client-core/test/brand-accent.test.ts` pins these values and the structural rule that a swatch must never read `--accent` off itself.

- 画板弹窗并入全局液态玻璃与弹簧动效规范：此前它是一块不透明的实心面板、弹出用普通 ease，和对话框/设置页的玻璃层不是一套视觉语言。现在表面走 `--glass-*` 四件套（半透明填充 + 背景模糊 + 高光边缘 + 对角光泽）与 `--glass-shadow`，入场用 240ms `--spring-liquid` 过冲弹簧，关闭仍是 180ms 的缩小回落（两半合起来才是 Codex 那种"落回输入框"的观感）。顺带修掉心形被映射到拖拽框外的问题（原曲线 y 值域上下不对称，单除数会让底部尖端超出框 13%）。
  - EN: The sketch dialog now follows the global liquid-glass and spring-motion standard — it used to be an opaque solid panel with a plain-ease entrance, a different visual language from the dialogs and settings surfaces. The surface now uses the `--glass-*` four-piece set (translucent fill, backdrop blur, specular rim, diagonal sheen) plus `--glass-shadow`, and enters on a 240ms `--spring-liquid` overshoot; closing is still the 180ms scale-out, since the two halves together are what read as "falling back into the composer". Also fixed the heart being mapped outside its drag box (the raw curve's y range is asymmetric, so a single divisor pushed the bottom tip 13% past the edge).

- Windows 下左键单击任务栏托盘图标不弹出主窗口：修复 `tray.cjs` 将 `win32` 与 macOS 菜单栏混淆、将左键点击误绑定为 `toggle-tray-menu` 的问题。现在 Windows 下左键单击与双击均触发唤出/恢复主窗口（`show-main-window`），右键单击弹出浮动快捷菜单，对齐 Windows 平台交互规范与源码注释意图。#26
  - EN: Windows tray icon click not showing main window: fixed `tray.cjs` incorrectly treating Windows like macOS menu bar by routing left-clicks to `toggle-tray-menu`. Left-click and double-click now bring up/restore the main window (`show-main-window`), while right-click toggles the shortcut menu, matching Windows UX conventions and code comments. #26

- 待发送队列「取回重新编辑」丢附件：`session.queuedPop` 早已返回 `{text, images}`，Composer 却只消费了 `text`，图片附件被静默丢弃。现在按 `data:${mimeType};base64,…` 字符串拼装还原附件（渲染进程 CSP 的 `connect-src` 不含 `data:`，所以不能走 `fetch(dataUrl)` 解码），取回后缩略图与文本一起回到输入框。
  - EN: Retrieving a queued message for re-editing dropped its attachments — `session.queuedPop` already returned `{text, images}`, but the Composer consumed only `text` and silently discarded images. Attachments are now rebuilt as `data:${mimeType};base64,…` by string concatenation (the renderer CSP's `connect-src` has no `data:`, so `fetch(dataUrl)` cannot be used to decode), so thumbnails come back into the composer together with the text.
- 设计模式会话不显示风格 chip：SDK 的 create 只把 `modeId` 用于解析提示词 / settings / 扩展白名单，**从不写入会话头**，而 `live.modeId` 是 adopt 时从 SDK 头读的 —— 守护进程里唯一写头的地方是 `session.setMode`。于是从空态「设计模式」chip 进入的会话，`session.modes.modeId` 恒为 null，chip 永不出现（源码注释还写着"create 写入"，与实现正好相反）。现在创建路径补写 live 与快照头两级，adopt 也在 SDK 头缺失时回落到守护进程快照头。
  - EN: Design sessions never showed the style chips — the SDK's create path only used `modeId` to resolve prompts / settings / extension whitelists and **never wrote it to the session header**, while `live.modeId` is read from that header at adopt time, and the daemon's only header writer was `session.setMode`. A session opened from the empty-state "design" chip therefore always reported `modeId: null` (the source comment claimed "written on create" — the opposite of what the code did). The create path now writes both the live value and the snapshot header, and adopt falls back to the daemon snapshot header when the SDK header lacks it.
- 右侧上下文面板无论选哪个模式都显示「默认模式」：面板只读了 `goalMode` / `planMode` 两个开关，其余一律落到字面量"默认模式"。现在按 goal > plan > 会话预设 id 取名，预设 id 经 i18n 映射为「工作 / 对话 / 创作 / 设计模式」；未知预设（例如扩展提供的）仍回落到默认模式，而不是打印原始 id。
  - EN: The right-hand context panel read "默认模式" for every mode — it only looked at the `goalMode` / `planMode` flags and fell through to a literal "default mode" for everything else. It now names the session goal > plan > preset id, with preset ids mapped through i18n to Work / Chat / Creator / Design; an unknown preset (an extension-supplied one, say) still degrades to the default label rather than printing a raw id.

## [0.4.33] - 2026-09-18

### Added

- **插件面板不再加载失败，官方插件也都有图标了**：插件自带的设置面板原先一律报「模块加载失败」——界面在组件还在下载时就回收了它的地址；现在加载完成后才释放。同时每个插件项都画出了自己的标识（插件声明的图标、内置单元的类别图标，或按包名生成的彩色字母标），状态改为标识角上的小圆点，不再和图标挤在一起。
  - EN: Plugin panels no longer fail to load, and official plugins have icons too. A plugin's own settings panel always reported "module failed to load" — the UI released its address while the download was still in flight; it now waits for the load to finish. Every plugin also draws its own mark (a declared icon, a built-in unit's kind glyph, or a colored letter tile derived from the package name), and status moved to a corner badge instead of crowding the icon.
- **插件详情弹窗补齐**：点击已安装插件打开详情弹窗——大图标、版本、状态与启用开关、头部卸载按钮；插件自带的组件收进弹窗「包含的组件」逐项开关（不再与插件包重复占列表行），插件的设置界面（如账号池管理）也挂载在弹窗内。
  - EN: The plugin detail dialog completes the picture. Clicking an installed plugin opens a dialog with the large icon, version, status, enable toggle and an uninstall button in the header; the plugin's own modules collapse into per-item included-component toggles (no longer duplicated as separate list rows), and its settings surface (an account pool, for example) mounts inside the dialog.
- **插件列表支持一键卸载**：已安装插件卡片和列表行新增卸载按钮，确认后由守护进程移除插件目录并即时刷新列表；卸载失败时错误横幅提示且列表保持不变。
  - EN: One-click uninstall in the plugin list. Installed plugin cards and rows gain an uninstall button; after a confirmation the daemon removes the plugin directory and refreshes the list immediately, and a failed uninstall shows an error banner while leaving the list untouched.
- **插件图标可自定义**：插件可在清单里声明 `icon`（emoji、字符或图片 URL），列表立即采用；未声明时按包名生成确定的彩色字母标，同包同名同色。
  - EN: Customizable plugin icons. A plugin can declare `icon` in its manifest (emoji, glyph, or an image URL) and the list adopts it; without one, a deterministic colored letter mark is derived from the package name — same package, same mark.
- `/design` 斜杠命令（别名 `/ds`）：会话内一键热切换到设计预设——补齐引导页承诺的第三把入口（欢迎页 chip、`--preset design`、`/design`），复用 modes v2 switcher 与现有提示文案，预设缺失时走既有报错而非静默无操作。
  - EN: A `/design` slash command (alias `/ds`) hot-switches the session to the design preset — completing the third entry the onboarding advertises (welcome chip, `--preset design`, `/design`). Reuses the modes-v2 switcher and existing copy; a missing preset surfaces the existing failure message instead of a silent no-op.
- Artifact manifest 契约（`presets/artifact-manifest.ts`）：把设计模式的产物承诺固化成 schema + 校验——entry 相对路径（禁止绝对路径与 `..` 越出产物目录）、kind(page/component/poster/deck)、renderer(html/markdown/react-component/deck-html)、exports、可选 title/description。7 个测试覆盖越权路径拒绝与坏 JSON。设计预设模板同步点名 sidecar 文件名 `artifact.manifest.json`（模板修订 2→3，升级链支持跨版本逐级比对 v1→v2→v3，用户改过的预设仍永不覆盖）。
  - EN: The artifact manifest contract (`presets/artifact-manifest.ts`) turns the design preset's sidecar promise into schema + validation — relative entry (no absolute paths, no `..` escapes), kind, renderer, exports, optional title/description; 7 tests cover path-escape rejection and malformed JSON. The design template now names the sidecar file `artifact.manifest.json` (template revision 2→3; the upgrade chain compares across versions v1→v2→v3 and still never overwrites a user-edited preset).
- ColorPicker 新增「强调色上的文字」对比度等级（`accentInkContrast`，chroma.contrast 标准 WCAG）：与既有背景对比徽章并列，实时显示派生 `--accent-fg` 将获得的可读性——旧 sRGB 派生在品牌金上只有 2.2:1 的那类问题从此在挑选时就能看见。
  - EN: The ColorPicker now shows an "on-accent text" contrast grade next to the background grade (`accentInkContrast`, standard WCAG via chroma.contrast) — the readability the derived `--accent-fg` will get, visible at pick time. The old sRGB derivation scored 2.2:1 on the brand gold itself.
- `StateIconN` n-ary 图标切换：三态类别图标（日历/看板/列表、加载/朗读/空闲、全部/动作/任务）从普通 Icon 升级为同款弹簧形变——3 处 TODO(P1) 清零，gui-design.md 图标 morph 规则三则与色彩管线判定随文落档（中英双语）。
  - EN: `StateIconN`, an n-ary icon switch, upgrades the three tri-state kind icons (calendar/board/list, loading/speaking/idle, all/actions/list) to the same spring morph — the 3 TODO(P1) sites are cleared, and the icon-morph rules + color-pipeline verdicts land in docs/gui-design.md (bilingual).

## [0.4.32] - 2026-09-17

- **插件面板不再加载失败，官方插件也都有图标了**：插件自带的设置面板原先一律报「模块加载失败」——界面在组件还在下载时就回收了它的地址；现在加载完成后才释放。同时每个插件项都画出了自己的标识（插件声明的图标、内置单元的类别图标，或按包名生成的彩色字母标），状态改为标识角上的小圆点，不再和图标挤在一起。

Clicking an installed plugin opens a dialog with the large icon, version, status, enable toggle and an uninstall button in the header; the plugin's own modules collapse into per-item included-component toggles (no longer duplicated as separate list rows), and its settings surface (an account pool, for example) mounts inside the dialog.
- **插件列表支持一键卸载**：已安装插件卡片和列表行新增卸载按钮，确认后由守护进程移除插件目录并即时刷新列表；卸载失败时错误横幅提示且列表保持不变。
  - EN: One-click uninstall in the plugin list. Installed plugin cards and rows gain an uninstall button; after a confirmation the daemon removes the plugin directory and refreshes the list immediately, and a failed uninstall shows an error banner while leaving the list untouched.
- **插件图标可自定义**：插件可在清单里声明 `icon`（emoji、字符或图片 URL），列表立即采用；未声明时按包名生成确定的彩色字母标，同包同名同色。
  - EN: Customizable plugin icons. A plugin can declare `icon` in its manifest (emoji, glyph, or an image URL) and the list adopts it; without one, a deterministic colored letter mark is derived from the package name — same package, same mark.
- 设计模式（预设 `design`，TUI 与 GUI 都可用）从「一条 persona」补成完整设计工作流：新增工作流、简报协议、产物契约、风格边界四个提示词区块——现在会先对齐简报（目标/平台/风格基准/参考/交付物）、先做结构与主导区域判断再谈视觉、产出物带 sidecar manifest 以便预览面板识别、需要落地实现代码时明确建议切回 work 模式。风格边界把设计规范接进了 agent 行为：圆角只用 `--radius-*` 阶梯、禁止字面 px，玻璃只用 `--glass-*` 四件套且只有背后有内容的悬浮层才允许。刻意不收窄工具白名单——设计要读代码库、看现有样式、改文件。
  - EN: The design mode (preset `design`, available in both TUI and GUI) grew from a single persona line into a complete design workflow: four new prompt sections cover workflow, brief protocol, artifact contract and style boundaries. It now aligns on a brief (goal / platform / style baseline / references / deliverable) before drawing, judges structure and the dominant region before picking colours, emits a sidecar manifest so the preview panel can recognise artifacts, and explicitly recommends switching back to work mode when implementation code is needed. The style-boundary section wires the design system into agent behaviour: radii come only from the `--radius-*` ladder (literal px banned), glass only from the `--glass-*` four-piece set, and only on floating layers that have content behind them. The tool whitelist is deliberately not narrowed — design needs to read the codebase, inspect existing styles and edit files.

### Changed

- 内置预设模板现在带修订号，且只在「文件内容仍等于旧内置模板」时才升级——此前模板只在文件缺失时写入，内置模板改内容后老用户磁盘上的 preset 永远不会被替换（本次 design 就是这样被卡住的）；无条件覆盖又会抹掉用户改过的预设，所以改动过的文件一律保留，坏 JSON 也不动（交给 `modes.validate` 报告）。
  - EN: Built-in preset templates now carry a revision number and are upgraded only when the file still matches the old built-in template. Previously templates were written only when missing, so any content change to a built-in template never reached existing users (exactly what blocked the `design` upgrade here); overwriting unconditionally would instead destroy user-edited presets, so modified files are always left alone and malformed JSON is left untouched (reported by `modes.validate`).

### Fixed

- 桌面口述（麦克风输入）四条产品级失败一次修掉：①「静音阈值」设置从未接到录音入口——所有入口只传麦克风 id，说完停 2 秒仍要等满 15 秒上限，现在在 voice 层统一读 `stt.vadEndMs` 并真正驱动 VAD 判停，且静音判停只在听到过语音后才武装（否则开口前的短暂停顿会被当成「说完了」）；②桌面录音用 AudioContext 默认采样率（Windows 常为 48 kHz）且不重采样，Parakeet 按 16 kHz 解码等于三倍速、常常返回空串、输入框永不回填——现在显式请求 16 kHz 并在引擎忽略时线性重采样（对齐 guest-client 本就正确的实现）；③再点麦克风会把整段缓冲丢弃不转写——现在改为提前收音并提交转写，急着停也不再白录；④麦克风经扬声器回放抬高噪声底、干扰判停——现在经零增益节点接线（图谱仍活着但无声音）；⑤16 kHz × 15 s 的裸 float JSON 仍可能撞上 daemon 4 MiB 请求上限（`Request too large`）——现在量化到 5 位小数（约 13 bit，对 16-bit 级别的 ASR 输入无感）。#23
  - EN: Four product-level failures of desktop dictation (microphone input) fixed at once: (1) the "silence threshold" setting was never wired into the recorder — every entry point passed only a microphone id, so speech followed by a 2 s pause still waited out the full 15 s cap; the voice layer now reads `stt.vadEndMs` itself and actually drives the VAD, which only arms after some speech was heard (otherwise the pause before you start talking ends the recording). (2) The desktop capture ran at the AudioContext's default rate (48 kHz on Windows) without resampling, so Parakeet — fed at a hardcoded 16 kHz — heard 3× speed and usually returned an empty string, leaving the input box unfilled; capture now requests 16 kHz explicitly and linearly resamples when an engine ignores it (matching the guest-client implementation that was already correct). (3) Pressing the mic a second time discarded the whole buffer without transcribing; it now finishes early and submits what was said. (4) The mic was played straight back out of the speakers, raising the noise floor against the VAD; the graph now runs through a zero-gain sink. (5) Even at 16 kHz, 15 s of raw-float JSON could still exceed the daemon's 4 MiB request cap ("Request too large"); the payload is now quantised to 5 decimals (~13 bits, inaudible for 16-bit-class ASR input). #23
