- 移动端口述撞上 daemon 的 4 MiB 请求上限（`Request too large`）：上限提高到 16 MiB（与 collab relay 的 16 MiB 帧上限对齐，两个传输层不再各说各话），guest-client 的转写 payload 同步量化到 5 位小数——原先裸 float JSON 约 320 KB/s，十几秒就会失败，现在约 130 KB/s，手机上较长时间的口述也能正常转写。#23
  - EN: Mobile dictation hit the daemon's 4 MiB request cap ("Request too large"). The cap is now 16 MiB (aligned with the collab relay's 16 MiB frame limit, so the two transports finally agree), and the guest-client transcription payload is quantised to 5 decimals like the desktop — raw float JSON was ~320 KB/s and failed within seconds, now ~130 KB/s, so longer dictation on the phone transcribes normally. #23

### Fixed

- Windows 上「启动 daemon broker 的那个进程」退出会连带杀死整个 broker，连另一个项目仍在使用的全局浏览器 relay 一起带走：broker 以 `detached: false` 被拉起，而 Bun 在 Windows 上会随父进程退出终止非 detached 子进程，于是「一个项目退出不得拆掉仍被他人持有的机器级单例」这条契约被打破（relay 端口消失，另一个项目的浏览器工具随即失效）。broker 现在显式声明必须比启动它的客户端活得更久；POSIX 行为不变（那里子进程本就会被 init 收养）。#24
  - EN: On Windows, the process that happened to start the daemon broker killed the whole broker when it exited — taking the machine-global browser relay with it even while another project was still using it. The broker was spawned `detached: false`, and Bun terminates non-detached children when their parent exits, which broke the contract that one project exiting must not tear down a singleton others still hold (the relay port vanished and the other project's browser tooling broke). The broker now declares that it must outlive the client that spawned it; POSIX behaviour is unchanged, since orphans are re-parented to init there. #24

## [0.4.31] - 2026-09-17

### Added

- **插件面板不再加载失败，官方插件也都有图标了**：插件自带的设置面板原先一律报「模块加载失败」——界面在组件还在下载时就回收了它的地址；现在加载完成后才释放。同时每个插件项都画出了自己的标识（插件声明的图标、内置单元的类别图标，或按包名生成的彩色字母标），状态改为标识角上的小圆点，不再和图标挤在一起。
  - EN: Plugin panels no longer fail to load, and official plugins have icons too. A plugin's own settings panel always reported "module failed to load" — the UI released its address while the download was still in flight; it now waits for the load to finish. Every plugin also draws its own mark (a declared icon, a built-in unit's kind glyph, or a colored letter tile derived from the package name), and status moved to a corner badge instead of crowding the icon.
- **插件详情弹窗补齐**：点击已安装插件打开详情弹窗——大图标、版本、状态与启用开关、头部卸载按钮；插件自带的组件收进弹窗「包含的组件」逐项开关（不再与插件包重复占列表行），插件的设置界面（如账号池管理）也挂载在弹窗内。
  - EN: The plugin detail dialog completes the picture. Clicking an installed plugin opens a dialog with the large icon, version, status, enable toggle and an uninstall button in the header; the plugin's own modules collapse into per-item included-component toggles (no longer duplicated as separate list rows), and its settings surface (an account pool, for example) mounts inside the dialog.
- **插件列表支持一键卸载**：已安装插件卡片和列表行新增卸载按钮，确认后由守护进程移除插件目录并即时刷新列表；卸载失败时错误横幅提示且列表保持不变。
  - EN: One-click uninstall in the plugin list. Installed plugin cards and rows gain an uninstall button; after a confirmation the daemon removes the plugin directory and refreshes the list immediately, and a failed uninstall shows an error banner while leaving the list untouched.
- **插件图标可自定义**：插件可在清单里声明 `icon`（emoji、字符或图片 URL），列表立即采用；未声明时按包名生成确定的彩色字母标，同包同名同色。
  - EN: Customizable plugin icons. A plugin can declare `icon` in its manifest (emoji, glyph, or an image URL) and the list adopts it; without one, a deterministic colored letter mark is derived from the package name — same package, same mark.
- GUI 显示提示缓存命中率与本次会话花费：上下文环弹层与 GUI 原生 /context 卡片新增「缓存命中」与「会话花费」两行。命中率算法与 TUI 状态行的 `cache_hit` 段共用同一份实现（分母是整个 prompt：cacheRead + cacheWrite + input —— 只算已命中会恒为 ~100%，正好掩盖这个功能要暴露的回归），且完全没有缓存读取时留空而不显示误导性的 0%；花费仅在模型目录有价格时出现。此前 GUI 里唯一的缓存数据是轨迹检查器中的一个原始 ☍ token 计数。#8
  - EN: The GUI now shows prompt-cache hit rate and session spend, in the context-ring popover and the native /context card. The rate comes from the same helper the TUI `cache_hit` segment uses (the denominator is the whole prompt: cacheRead + cacheWrite + input — counting only hits would sit at ~100% and hide exactly the regression), and a session with no cache reads shows nothing rather than a misleading 0%; spend appears only when the catalog carries a price. The only cache number in the GUI used to be a raw ☍ token count in the trajectory inspector. #8
- 会话内用自然语言创建定时 / 闲时任务：新增 `schedule_task` 工具（`action: create|list`，覆盖 once/daily/weekly/monthly/cron、一天多时间点、闲时窗口与时区；创建走写权限审批），会话流里渲染富卡片（任务名、调度摘要、闲时窗口、下次执行时间）并提供「前往任务中心」按钮直达并选中该任务。工具不直接写任务存储，而是经 daemon 注入的句柄写入——daemon 在内存持有任务列表并整体覆写文件，工具自行写盘会被静默吞掉。#11
  - EN: Scheduled and quiet-hours tasks can now be created from the conversation: a `schedule_task` tool (`action: create|list`, covering once/daily/weekly/monthly/cron, several times a day, idle windows and timezones; creation is write-approval gated) renders a rich card in the transcript (name, schedule summary, idle window, next run) with an "open task center" button that jumps to and selects that task. The tool never writes the task store itself — it goes through a daemon-injected handle, because the daemon owns the list in memory and rewrites the file wholesale, so a tool write would be silently clobbered. #11

### Changed

- 品牌色换金：默认 accent 从翡翠绿 #34d399 换成 π 品牌金 #d9a441（GUI tokens、TUI musepi.json 主题、HTML 导出调色板、stats 面板、lightbox 主按钮全部同步），预设更名「品牌金 / Brand gold」，语义成功状态与桌宠角色保留绿色。
  - EN: Brand re-gilded: the default accent moves from emerald #34d399 to the π brand gold #d9a441 across GUI tokens, the TUI musepi.json theme, the HTML export palette, the stats dashboard and lightbox primary actions; the preset is renamed "Brand gold", while semantic success states and the pet mascot keep their green.

### Fixed

- 侧栏项目文件夹右键菜单在「复制路径」和「移除项目」之间多出一行空白可点项：不带标签/图标/动作的 `divider` 现在渲染为独立的 `role="separator"` 线，而不再同时画出分隔线和一行空按钮。#13
  - EN: The project folder context menu showed a blank clickable row between "copy path" and "remove project": a `divider` carrying no label/icon/action now renders as a standalone `role="separator"` rule instead of drawing the line and an empty button. #13
- 开启通知后真实工作仍收不到桌面提示：判定把「窗口可见」当成「用户就在眼前」，而切到浏览器/编辑器时窗口恰恰是可见的（现按焦点判断：隐藏或失去焦点），并且只有纯文本结束才派发 completion —— 带工具调用的一轮结束从不通知。现在每个完成的运行都会发一次桌面提示。#14
  - EN: Turning notifications on still produced nothing for real work: the gate treated a merely visible window as "the user is right here", yet switching to a browser or editor is exactly when the window stays visible (it now keys on focus: hidden or unfocused), and only a text-only `message_end` dispatched "completion" — a turn that ended with tool calls never notified. Every finished run now produces one desktop toast. #14
- 不同路径的同名文件夹被显示成同一个项目空间：项目块只渲染目录名，会话导入按名字分组更把两个工作区的会话真的合并成一组（两组还共用一个折叠状态）。标签与分组现在都按完整路径区分。#15
  - EN: Two folders sharing a name were shown as one project space: project blocks rendered only the directory name, and session import grouped by name — genuinely merging two workspaces' sessions into one group that also shared a single collapse flag. Labels and grouping now disambiguate by full path. #15
- 后台长任务被 30 分钟空闲回收杀掉：`lastActivity` 只记录用户交互，回收路径从不看会话真实状态——扫描器无视正在跑的工具 / Goal 循环直接关闭，紧邻的 LRU 分支反而已有忙判定，工具与流事件也从不刷新时钟。现在「会话是否忙碌」是两条回收路径共用的唯一判据。#16
  - EN: Long-running background work was killed by the 30-minute idle reaper: `lastActivity` only tracked user interaction and the path never consulted the session's real state — the scan closed anything past the timeout regardless of a tool mid-flight or a Goal loop, while the LRU branch right below it did check, and tool/stream events never refreshed the clock at all. One shared "is this session busy" predicate now guards both reclamation paths. #16

## [0.4.30] - 2026-09-16

### Added

- **插件面板不再加载失败，官方插件也都有图标了**：插件自带的设置面板原先一律报「模块加载失败」——界面在组件还在下载时就回收了它的地址；现在加载完成后才释放。同时每个插件项都画出了自己的标识（插件声明的图标、内置单元的类别图标，或按包名生成的彩色字母标），状态改为标识角上的小圆点，不再和图标挤在一起。
  - EN: Plugin panels no longer fail to load, and official plugins have icons too. A plugin's own settings panel always reported "module failed to load" — the UI released its address while the download was still in flight; it now waits for the load to finish. Every plugin also draws its own mark (a declared icon, a built-in unit's kind glyph, or a colored letter tile derived from the package name), and status moved to a corner badge instead of crowding the icon.
- **插件详情弹窗补齐**：点击已安装插件打开详情弹窗——大图标、版本、状态与启用开关、头部卸载按钮；插件自带的组件收进弹窗「包含的组件」逐项开关（不再与插件包重复占列表行），插件的设置界面（如账号池管理）也挂载在弹窗内。
  - EN: The plugin detail dialog completes the picture. Clicking an installed plugin opens a dialog with the large icon, version, status, enable toggle and an uninstall button in the header; the plugin's own modules collapse into per-item included-component toggles (no longer duplicated as separate list rows), and its settings surface (an account pool, for example) mounts inside the dialog.
- **插件列表支持一键卸载**：已安装插件卡片和列表行新增卸载按钮，确认后由守护进程移除插件目录并即时刷新列表；卸载失败时错误横幅提示且列表保持不变。
  - EN: One-click uninstall in the plugin list. Installed plugin cards and rows gain an uninstall button; after a confirmation the daemon removes the plugin directory and refreshes the list immediately, and a failed uninstall shows an error banner while leaving the list untouched.
- **插件图标可自定义**：插件可在清单里声明 `icon`（emoji、字符或图片 URL），列表立即采用；未声明时按包名生成确定的彩色字母标，同包同名同色。
  - EN: Customizable plugin icons. A plugin can declare `icon` in its manifest (emoji, glyph, or an image URL) and the list adopts it; without one, a deterministic colored letter mark is derived from the package name — same package, same mark.
- 文件面板内嵌文本编辑器：文本文件（≤2 MiB）在预览头 ✎ 进入编辑，⌘S / Ctrl+S 保存，未保存修改在 tab 上显示圆点，切换或关闭前确认丢弃；新建文件直接进入编辑器（取代旧的「无内置编辑器」提示）。
  - EN: Built-in text editor inside the file panel: text files (≤2 MiB) enter edit mode via ✎ in the preview header, ⌘S / Ctrl+S saves, unsaved changes show a dot on the tab, and switching or closing asks before discarding; a new file opens straight in the editor (replacing the old "no built-in editor" notice).
- Office 文档预览（只读）：`.docx` 用 docx-preview 内嵌渲染，`.xlsx` / `.xls` / `.csv` 用 SheetJS 渲染为转义表格（1000 行上限 + sheet 切换），损坏文件自动回退系统默认应用。
  - EN: Read-only Office preview: `.docx` renders inline via docx-preview; `.xlsx` / `.xls` / `.csv` render as an escaped table via SheetJS (1000-row cap + sheet switcher); corrupt files fall back to the system default app.

### Fixed

- Antigravity / Gemini CLI：默认系统提示、顾问提示与 Live 指令把 `<system-conventions>` 改成 `<conventions>`，避免 Cloud Code Assist 网关把开场 XML 块判成滥用并立刻返回假 `429 RESOURCE_EXHAUSTED`（30 分钟退避）。#7
  - EN: Default system, advisor, and Live prompts now open with `<conventions>` instead of `<system-conventions>` (omp 18.2.1), so Cloud Code Assist no longer false-429s the first turn. #7
- 编辑器视图：预览体补上缺失的 flex 列布局（编辑器列/文档背景/表格/图片包装都按 flex 父容器设计），textarea 不再把保存工具栏推出面板；编辑中隐藏 ✎ 与「渲染/源码」死控件，保存按钮接入真实 saving 状态。
  - EN: Editor view: the preview body regained its missing flex-column layout (the editor column, document background, tables and image wrappers all assume a flex parent), so the textarea no longer pushes the save toolbar out of the panel; while editing, the ✎ and the dead render/source controls are hidden and the save button is wired to real saving state.
- #6 Windows 任务栏缩略图全白：BrowserWindow 未设 backgroundColor，DWM 初始表面为白色；补上与应用一致的深色底色。
  - EN: #6 Windows taskbar thumbnail rendered all white: the BrowserWindow set no backgroundColor, so the DWM surface started white; it now carries the app's dark base colour.
- #9 听写录音立即返回空 PCM：recordPcm 改为等待录音真正结束（VAD 静音 / 15s 上限 / 手动停止）才交付完整缓冲，settings 的 vadEndMs 真正生效；取消竞态下麦克风即时关闭。
  - EN: #9 Dictation returned empty PCM immediately: recordPcm now waits for the recording to actually finish (VAD silence / 15s cap / manual stop) before delivering the complete buffer, settings' vadEndMs takes effect, and a cancelled race closes the microphone at once.
- #12 「已退回到」横幅：头部新增 ✕ 关闭（保持当前回退位置，分支仍可从面包屑回去）；切换会话时清空横幅（旧 undo 目标跨会话发送外会话节点 id 触发 branch failed）；面包屑/树切换节点时同步清空。
  - EN: #12 The "rewound to" banner gains an ✕ in its header (keeps the current rewind position — the branch is still reachable from the breadcrumb); the banner clears when switching sessions (a stale undo target sent a foreign session's node id across sessions and tripped branch failed), and the breadcrumb/tree node switches clear it in sync.
- 输入框卡键：合成（输入法）状态标志以前只在 `compositionend` 复位，切走窗口、取消候选、渲染进程重载都不会触发该事件，标志就此永久为真，之后每次按键都被当成「合成中」提前返回——表现为挂久了 Enter 毫无反应（点发送正常，重启才恢复）。现在失焦与获得焦点都会复位，且 Chromium 合成中的按键恒为 keyCode 229，因此一个「非 229 且非 isComposing」的按键即可判定标志过期并自愈；候选确认键仍受保护（WebKit 在 `compositionend` 之后补发 Enter）。
  - EN: Composer stuck keys: the IME composing flag was only reset on `compositionend`, which never fires when the window loses focus, the candidate list is dismissed, or the renderer reloads — so the flag stayed true forever and every later keystroke returned early as "composing" (Enter went dead after a long session; clicking send still worked and only a restart recovered). Blur and focus now reset it too, and since Chromium always reports keyCode 229 while composing, any key that is neither 229 nor `isComposing` proves the flag stale and self-heals; the candidate-confirm key stays protected (WebKit re-sends Enter after `compositionend`).

## [0.4.29] - 2026-09-16

### Added

- **插件面板不再加载失败，官方插件也都有图标了**：插件自带的设置面板原先一律报「模块加载失败」——界面在组件还在下载时就回收了它的地址；现在加载完成后才释放。同时每个插件项都画出了自己的标识（插件声明的图标、内置单元的类别图标，或按包名生成的彩色字母标），状态改为标识角上的小圆点，不再和图标挤在一起。
  - EN: Plugin panels no longer fail to load, and official plugins have icons too. A plugin's own settings panel always reported "module failed to load" — the UI released its address while the download was still in flight; it now waits for the load to finish. Every plugin also draws its own mark (a declared icon, a built-in unit's kind glyph, or a colored letter tile derived from the package name), and status moved to a corner badge instead of crowding the icon.
- **插件详情弹窗补齐**：点击已安装插件打开详情弹窗——大图标、版本、状态与启用开关、头部卸载按钮；插件自带的组件收进弹窗「包含的组件」逐项开关（不再与插件包重复占列表行），插件的设置界面（如账号池管理）也挂载在弹窗内。
  - EN: The plugin detail dialog completes the picture. Clicking an installed plugin opens a dialog with the large icon, version, status, enable toggle and an uninstall button in the header; the plugin's own modules collapse into per-item included-component toggles (no longer duplicated as separate list rows), and its settings surface (an account pool, for example) mounts inside the dialog.
- **插件列表支持一键卸载**：已安装插件卡片和列表行新增卸载按钮，确认后由守护进程移除插件目录并即时刷新列表；卸载失败时错误横幅提示且列表保持不变。
  - EN: One-click uninstall in the plugin list. Installed plugin cards and rows gain an uninstall button; after a confirmation the daemon removes the plugin directory and refreshes the list immediately, and a failed uninstall shows an error banner while leaving the list untouched.
- **插件图标可自定义**：插件可在清单里声明 `icon`（emoji、字符或图片 URL），列表立即采用；未声明时按包名生成确定的彩色字母标，同包同名同色。
  - EN: Customizable plugin icons. A plugin can declare `icon` in its manifest (emoji, glyph, or an image URL) and the list adopts it; without one, a deterministic colored letter mark is derived from the package name — same package, same mark.
- 语音设置成熟化：语音模型卡（名称 + SoTA/轻量 标签、体积、准确度/速度分档条、卡内下载进度）与可用的麦克风选择——所选设备对听写的所有入口生效。
  - EN: Voice settings maturity: speech model cards (name + SoTA/lightweight badge, download size, accuracy/speed tier bars, in-card download progress) and a working microphone picker — the chosen device feeds every dictation entry point.
- macOS：ad-hoc 签名构建的自动更新会静默失败，现在回退为手动下载安装包（dmg）并落盘更新日志。#5
  - EN: macOS: auto-update on ad-hoc–signed builds fails silently; it now falls back to a manual installer download (dmg opened after fetch) with updater logs on disk. #5
- 会话侧栏搜索改为回车提交（输入法安全），行级 memo 与首屏 RPC 并发。#5
  - EN: Session sidebar search commits on Enter (IME-safe), with memoized rows and concurrent first-paint RPCs. #5

### Changed

- 会话转写活动折叠对齐 openchamber 的 turn 模型：一轮从 prompt（或顾问条目）延伸到下一条 prompt；折叠后一行显示「orb + 活动 + 答案」，过程收进活动摘要；展开/收起有动效。
  - EN: Transcript activity fold aligned with openchamber's turn model: a turn spans from its prompt (or an advisor note) to the next prompt; a collapsed turn renders orb + activity + answer on ONE line, the process folds into the activity row, and folding animates both ways.
- 打开会话直接渲染全部已加载消息（不再有「显示更早消息」的空档），切换会话自动落在最新位置。
  - EN: Opening a session renders every loaded message (no more "show earlier messages" gap), and a session switch lands on the latest position.
- 面板宽度在最大化态也可调；折叠时间距与展开一致；最大化浮层保留圆角。
  - EN: Panel width is adjustable again in every state, including maximized; the collapsed-panel gap matches the expanded one; the maximized float keeps its curve.
- 地图模式导航轨改为聚焦定位（不再悄悄改变会话节点）；分支列表展开/收起有动效。
  - EN: Map-mode prompt rail now focuses (centers + highlights) instead of silently moving the session leaf; the branch list animates both ways.

## [0.4.28] - 2026-09-16

### Added

- **插件面板不再加载失败，官方插件也都有图标了**：插件自带的设置面板原先一律报「模块加载失败」——界面在组件还在下载时就回收了它的地址；现在加载完成后才释放。同时每个插件项都画出了自己的标识（插件声明的图标、内置单元的类别图标，或按包名生成的彩色字母标），状态改为标识角上的小圆点，不再和图标挤在一起。
  - EN: Plugin panels no longer fail to load, and official plugins have icons too. A plugin's own settings panel always reported "module failed to load" — the UI released its address while the download was still in flight; it now waits for the load to finish. Every plugin also draws its own mark (a declared icon, a built-in unit's kind glyph, or a colored letter tile derived from the package name), and status moved to a corner badge instead of crowding the icon.
- **插件详情弹窗补齐**：点击已安装插件打开详情弹窗——大图标、版本、状态与启用开关、头部卸载按钮；插件自带的组件收进弹窗「包含的组件」逐项开关（不再与插件包重复占列表行），插件的设置界面（如账号池管理）也挂载在弹窗内。
  - EN: The plugin detail dialog completes the picture. Clicking an installed plugin opens a dialog with the large icon, version, status, enable toggle and an uninstall button in the header; the plugin's own modules collapse into per-item included-component toggles (no longer duplicated as separate list rows), and its settings surface (an account pool, for example) mounts inside the dialog.
- **插件列表支持一键卸载**：已安装插件卡片和列表行新增卸载按钮，确认后由守护进程移除插件目录并即时刷新列表；卸载失败时错误横幅提示且列表保持不变。
  - EN: One-click uninstall in the plugin list. Installed plugin cards and rows gain an uninstall button; after a confirmation the daemon removes the plugin directory and refreshes the list immediately, and a failed uninstall shows an error banner while leaving the list untouched.
- **插件图标可自定义**：插件可在清单里声明 `icon`（emoji、字符或图片 URL），列表立即采用；未声明时按包名生成确定的彩色字母标，同包同名同色。
  - EN: Customizable plugin icons. A plugin can declare `icon` in its manifest (emoji, glyph, or an image URL) and the list adopts it; without one, a deterministic colored letter mark is derived from the package name — same package, same mark.
- **右侧面板改为 tab-primary:一条 tab 条承载全部视图(对齐 openchamber ContextPanel)**:面板顶部一条 tab 条承载所有已打开的视图 —— 多实例**文件 tab**、笔记、待办/计划、Git、浏览器、看板、扩展 `panel.tab.*` 槽 —— rail 降级为**启动器**(点 rail = 打开或聚焦该 surface 的 tab,不再排他切换面板体)。零 tab 时显示「从这里开始」空态导航页而非默认视图;tab 可拖拽排序、关闭激活邻居(先右后左);布局按 cwd 持久化(`musepi-gui-panel-tabs-{cwd}`),**只存标签与地址、恢复时重派生 id**,内容由所属 surface 重新物化;按 surface 分配 12 个 tab 配额,淘汰最久未触碰且非激活者(宁超预算不丢正在看的)。文件实例 tab 归面板条所有(FilePane 改受控:`activeFile` 装载 + `onOpenFile` 注册),不再有双层 tab;占位替换语义 —— rail 先开 `files::` 占位,首个真实文件打开时替换它。
  - EN: The right panel becomes tab-primary — one tab strip hosts every open view (multi-instance file tabs, notes, todos/plans, git, browser, board, extension `panel.tab.*` slots) while the rail degrades to a launcher (a rail click opens or focuses that surface's tab instead of swapping the panel body). Zero tabs shows a start-here page instead of a default view; tabs drag-reorder, closing activates the neighbour (right then left), the layout persists per cwd (`musepi-gui-panel-tabs-{cwd}`) storing labels and addresses only — ids are re-derived on restore and content re-materialises from its surface — and each surface gets a 12-tab budget that spends the oldest non-active tab (one over budget beats losing the tab in use). File instances belong to the panel strip now (FilePane is controlled: `activeFile` loads, `onOpenFile` registers), so there are no double tab rows, and the placeholder contract holds: the rail opens a `files::` placeholder that the first real file replaces.
- **审批卡备注(TUI ask 对话框 `✎ note` parity)**:审批卡新增多行备注框(Enter 换行、⌘/Ctrl+Enter 直接批准、超 5 行内部滚动),端到端打通 —— `tool.approve` / `tool.deny` 接受 `note`,`approval-bridge.resolve(requestId, approved, note)` 让 select 的 promise 携带 `{approved, note}`;**带备注的拒绝**以 `Tool call denied by user: <工具>\n\nUser note: <内容>` 抛出,wrapper 既有 catch 把它作为拒绝原因发出并 rethrow —— agent 在工具错误里读到「为什么被拒」,而不只是 denied by user。批准时保持标签契约(note 仅记录)。
  - EN: The approval card gains a multiline note field (Enter = newline, ⌘/Ctrl+Enter approves, >5 lines scroll internally), wired end to end: `tool.approve` / `tool.deny` accept `note`, `approval-bridge.resolve(requestId, approved, note)` makes the select promise carry `{approved, note}`, and a **noted denial** throws `Tool call denied by user: <tool>\n\nUser note: <text>` — the wrapper's existing catch emits that as the resolved reason and rethrows, so the agent reads _why_ it was denied instead of a bare denial. Approvals keep the label contract (the note is recorded only).
- **设置 → 记忆新增工作区记忆卡(对齐 ZCode 记忆面板)**:按工作区划分的记忆目录浏览器 —— 工作区选择器(显示项目文件夹名,基名撞车时带父段消歧)、N 条记忆计数、名称搜索、刷新;文件行显示名称与相对时间,单击在面板内查看内容(仅 `.md`、512KB 上限),悬停文件夹按钮在系统文件管理器中显示。数据源为既有按 cwd 记忆根(`memory.workspace*` 三个新 RPC);后端启停仍由下方记忆后端选择器承担,不设重复开关。
  - EN: Settings → Memory gains a workspace-memory card: a per-workspace memory directory browser — workspace selector (project folder names, parent-segment disambiguation on basename clashes), entry count, name search, refresh; rows show name and relative age, click reads the file into the in-panel viewer (`.md` only, 512KB cap), and the hover folder button reveals it in the OS file manager. Data source is the existing per-cwd memory roots (three new RPCs: `memory.workspace*`); backend on/off stays in the backend select below — no duplicate toggle.
- **图片预览新增下载按钮,操作组下移**:灯箱右上角现在是「下载 + 关闭」成对一行(比原单个关闭键更低,避开窗口部件,对齐 ZCode 预览头部);下载把图片 refetch 成 blob 保存(`data:` 与远程 URL 均可),按 mime 命名 `musepi-image-<时间戳>.<扩展名>`,fetch 失败回退为新标签打开。
  - EN: The image lightbox gains a download button, and the actions move down: download + close render as one row in the top-right. Download refetches the image into a blob (works for `data:` and remote URLs), names it `musepi-image-<stamp>.<ext>` from the mime, and falls back to opening the source in a new tab.

### Changed

- **工作区面板独立化(对齐 ZCode 工作区面板改版:会话/终端/Side Pane 拆为独立面板)**:会话列、Side Pane(面板 + rail)、终端 dock 此前共用一张圆角卡片、靠竖线分割;现三者各自为独立圆角卡片浮在玻璃基底上(`.gui-float-card` 统一 radius + shadow + clip,与设置页卡片同款),面板与 rail 的 `border-left` 竖线移除,rail 从透明条变为独立窄卡片。前置障碍已消除 —— 原「TabBar 第二导航轴」架构否决被上面的 tab-primary 模型取代。
  - EN: Workspace panels are independent now: the session column, the Side Pane (panel + rail) and the terminal dock used to share one rounded card split by vertical borders; each is now its own rounded card on the glass base (shared `.gui-float-card` radius + shadow + clip, same chrome as the settings cards), the `border-left` dividers are gone and the rail became its own slim card. The blocker is gone too: the old "TabBar as a second navigation axis" veto was superseded by the tab-primary model above.
- **tab 条取代面板标题栏**:36px 的「当前视图标题 + 最大化」头与激活 tab 的标签重复,已整体移除;最大化按钮移入 tab 条右侧控制组,并新增「+」按钮新建空白标签页(激活 surface 支持实例时开它的占位,否则开 Files —— 文件树本身就是选择器)。
  - EN: The tab strip replaces the panel title bar: the 36px "current view title + maximize" header duplicated the active tab's label and is gone; maximize moved into the tab strip's right-side control group next to a new `+` button that opens a blank tab (the active surface's placeholder when it hosts instances, Files otherwise — its tree doubles as the picker).

- **已完成轮次的折叠头改为「活动」摘要行(对齐 openchamber),并新增默认状态设置**:折叠头从「时长 + 工具/命令计数」改为活动摘要 —— 「活动 · 更改了 N 个文件 +A/−R · 探索了代码库 · 运行了 N 条命令」;新增探索统计(read/grep/glob/fetch 等调用数),时长仍由最终回复下的已工作行承载,不再重复。设置 → 聊天新增「活动默认展开」:默认折叠(原行为)或默认展开全部活动,单轮手动开关始终优先。
  - EN: Completed-round fold headers become openchamber-style 活动 (Activity) summary rows: `活动 · 更改了 N 个文件 +A/−R · 探索了代码库 · 运行了 N 条命令` replaces the old duration + bare counts, with a new explore count (read/grep/glob/fetch-style calls); the duration stays in the frozen 已工作 line under the final reply. Settings → Chat gains 活动默认展开 (activities expanded by default): collapsed (previous behavior) or expanded by default, with per-round toggles always winning.
- **输入框模型菜单对齐 openchamber 结构**:收藏与最近使用渲染为可折叠分区(标题带 chevron 与计数),新增「最近」存储(provider/id 键、上限 5、真实选择时压入);菜单打开即聚焦搜索框,↑↓ 在可见行间巡游高亮、回车选择,底部钉住「↑↓ 导航 · ↵ 选择」提示;搜索框上方新增「添加供应商」动作行直达设置供应商页(无设置入口的选择器自动省略)。
  - EN: The composer model menu matches openchamber's structure: favorites and recently-used render as collapsible sections (chevron + count headers), with a new recency store (provider/id keys, cap 5, pushed on every real pick); the search input autofocuses on open, ↑↓ moves a roving highlight through the visible rows and Enter selects, with a pinned `↑↓ navigate · ↵ select` footer; and an 添加供应商 action above the search opens the settings providers page (omitted on selectors without an opener).
- **会话侧栏对齐 ZCode 列表**:运行中会话的指示器从呼吸圆点改为旋转八芒星标(ZCode ✳ 同款);分组与项目 tab 顶部的「近期」重复区块移除(会话本就在下方分组/文件夹里,不再列两遍)。
  - EN: The session sidebar aligns with the ZCode list: the running-session indicator is now a spinning 8-ray starburst; the duplicate 近期 block is gone from the groups and projects tabs (sessions already render in their groups/folders below).
- **目标继续模式在设置面板渲染为多选列表**:该设置是数组(运行模式集合),TUI 对无选项声明的数组直接隐藏,GUI 则渲染成了逗号分隔的自由文本框。现 schema 声明成员选项(当前为 interactive —— 运行时唯一消费的模式,取消勾选即关闭目标自动继续),GUI 将带选项的数组渲染为按压芯片多选(TUI MultiSelectSubmenu 同构);无选项的数组(扩展路径等自由列表)维持文本输入。
  - EN: 目标继续模式 (goal continuation modes) now renders as a multi-select list in the settings panel. The setting is an array (a set of run modes); the TUI hides option-less arrays entirely while the GUI rendered every array as a free-form comma input. The schema key now declares its membership options (`interactive` — the only mode the runtime consults; unchecking disables goal auto-continuation) and the GUI renders declared-membership arrays as pressed chips (TUI MultiSelectSubmenu parity); option-less arrays keep the text input.
- **导入会话自动归纳为可编辑分组**:导入对话框完成复制后,按工作区把导入的会话折进侧栏的自定义分组(每工作区一组,以文件夹基名命名,同名合并)——可在分组 tab 像手动分组一样重命名/换色/拖拽;普通会话不受影响,分组 tab 底栏保持日期分组。daemon 的 `import.session` 相应返回新会话 id。
  - EN: Imported sessions are now auto-grouped into EDITABLE custom groups: after the import dialog's copy loop it folds the imported sessions into the sidebar's custom-group store — one group per workspace, folder-basename named, merged into existing groups by name — fully editable (rename/recolor/drag) like any manual group; regular sessions are untouched and the groups tab stays date-grouped. The daemon's `import.session` returns the new session id accordingly.
- **右上角状态卡改为单面板分区布局(对齐 ZCode 悬浮状态面板)**:git/子智能体/待办此前是三张独立磨砂卡纵向堆叠,折叠按钮悬在卡外左上角,折叠后的胶囊是纯静态段。现对齐 ZCode 形态:一张磨砂卡内放可折叠分区(Git 工具/智能体/待办进度,chevron 悬停浮现),内容区限高 `min(64dvh, 32rem)` 内部滚动,收起按钮移入卡内右上角;**收起 = 胶囊**:999px 圆 pill 只承载「更改 +N −N」彩色差异统计(干净回退分支 + ↑↓),与 ZCode 同款;**折叠标题卡**:transcript 列宽过窄(< 560px)时展开态自动折叠为「Git 工具 +统计 ⤡」单行卡,点击强制展开完整面板;分支按钮弹层对齐 ZCode:搜索过滤、「分支」标签、当前分支块(✓ + 未提交更改文件数)、创建并检出新分支(内联输入,`git.checkout` 新增 `create` 参数 → `checkout -b`)。Git 分区行序对齐为 更改 → 分支 → 提交或推送。
  - EN: The top-right status stack is now one sectioned panel instead of three stacked cards. Git / subagents / todo used to render as separate frosted cards with the collapse control floating outside the stack and a static pill for the collapsed state; they now match the ZCode status-panel shape: one frosted card with collapsible sections (Git tools / agents / todo progress, chevron surfacing on hover), a body capped at `min(64dvh, 32rem)` with internal scroll, and the collapse button pinned top-right inside the card. **Collapse = the capsule**: a round pill carrying exactly 更改 + colored diff stats (branch + ↑↓ when clean), ZCode-style. **Folded header card**: when the transcript column is too narrow (< 560px) the expanded state auto-folds to a one-row `Git tools +stats ⤡` card; clicking it forces the full panel. The branch popover gains ZCode parity: a search filter, a "Branches" label, a current-branch block (✓ + uncommitted-file count), and create-and-checkout via an inline input (`git.checkout` gains a `create` flag → `checkout -b`). The Git section orders rows as changes → branch → commit or push.

### Fixed

- **修复最大化时「一层阴影遮罩盖住除顶栏外全部区域」且 rail 被吞**:① 最大化锚点原为 `.gui-chat-surface` —— 那是**整张应用卡片,内部还包含面板与 rail**,所以浮起的面板把 rail 一起覆盖;现锚点改为会话列(`.gui-chat-column`),面板只浮在聊天卡片上。② `.gui-pane-maximize-backdrop` 原是 `fixed` 全窗口(顶栏以下)的 48% 变暗层,把会话侧栏与 rail 一起压暗(用户另反馈为「左面板与顶栏色差」);现遮罩与面板同取会话列矩形(`--pane-max-*` 同时写到两个元素),只压暗聊天卡片。
  - EN: Fixed the maximize overlay that "covered everything but the top bar" and swallowed the rail. (1) The maximize anchor was `.gui-chat-surface` — the whole app card, which also contains the panel and the rail — so the floated panel covered the rail too; it now anchors the session column (`.gui-chat-column`) and floats over the chat card only. (2) `.gui-pane-maximize-backdrop` was a `fixed`, viewport-wide (below the 48px header) 48% dim that darkened the session sidebar and the rail as well (reported separately as "the left pane has a tint vs the header"); the backdrop now takes the same session-column rect as the panel (`--pane-max-*` written to both elements), dimming only the chat card.
- **撤回改为回退到消息的父节点**:撤回(用户消息下的 ⤺)此前 `session.branchAt` 传的是该用户消息**自身**,消息仍留在激活路径上;按反馈语义应为「回退到撤回处的上一节点」—— 现取其 `parentId`,消息本身也退出激活路径。区别于重试(同节点重新作答)与编辑并重发(保留节点并回填输入框);被弃用的尾段作为兄弟分支留在树上(地图/轨迹仍显示),不做截断。
  - EN: 撤回 (the ⤺ action under a user message) now branches to the message's PARENT: it used to `session.branchAt` the user message itself, leaving that message on the active path; the reported semantics are "go back to the node before the rewind point", so it now takes the `parentId` and the message leaves the active path too — distinct from retry (re-answers the same node) and edit-and-resend (keeps the node and backfills the composer). The dropped tail stays on the tree as a sibling branch (still visible in map/trajectory); nothing is truncated.
- **修复 @ 引用在中文正文或标点后唤不起面板**:补全触发原要求 `@` 位于行首,「请看@文件」这类中文正文紧邻 @ 的情况完全不触发。现规则改为「@ 的前一字符不是 ASCII 词字符即触发」(行首/空白/中文/标点均可),且取行内最后一个 `@`,token 替换按 `@` 锚点进行(此前按行首计算,中段插入会算错);`foo@bar` 邮箱形态仍不触发。过滤侧本就支持中文子串,未改动。
  - EN: Fixed `@` mentions never opening after CJK body text or punctuation: the trigger required a line-leading `@`, so "请看@文件" (Chinese text directly before the @) never opened the panel. The rule is now "any `@` whose preceding character is not an ASCII word character" (line start, whitespace, CJK and punctuation all qualify), the LAST `@` in the line wins, and the token replacement runs through the `@` anchor (it was computed from line start, so mid-line mentions inserted wrongly); `foo@bar` still does not trigger. The filter already matched Chinese substrings and is unchanged.

- **修复老会话和最后一轮已完成任务不会折叠到「活动」摘要**:首版活动折叠错误地要求 daemon 提供冻结时长并跳过最后一轮完成轮次，历史快照通常没有该元数据，导致开关看似无效、工具过程全部展开。现按 openchamber 的结构性 turn 规则折叠：任何闲置的「用户消息→最后 assistant 回复」轮次只要含工具/命令工作就折叠（包括最后一轮已完成轮次），仅活跃流式尾部保持展开；设置切换通过同文档事件即时重读已有轮次。
  - EN: Fixed old sessions and the last completed turn not folding into the 活动 summary. The initial activity-fold implementation incorrectly required daemon-frozen duration metadata and skipped the last completed turn; historical snapshots usually lack that metadata, leaving all tool history expanded and making the setting appear inert. Folds now use openchamber's structural turn rule: any idle user→last-assistant round with tool/command work folds (including the last completed turn), only the active streaming tail remains expanded, and the setting's same-document event immediately reinterprets existing rounds.
- **输入框模型菜单交互细节对齐 openchamber**:搜索时去掉收藏/最近分区头以平铺命中项；收藏/最近的展开/收起走统一 Reveal 高度动效；键盘上下导航会自动把高亮行滚入可见区；「添加供应商」现在直达设置的供应商分区而非仅打开设置首页。
  - EN: Refined composer model-menu interaction to match openchamber: search flattens matches instead of repeating favorites/recent headers; favorite/recent folds use the shared Reveal height animation; keyboard ↑↓ navigation scrolls the highlighted row into view; and Add Provider now lands directly on the providers settings section rather than the settings home.
- **修复设置行长文案把开关挤到下一行 + 索引库开关空心 + 使用统计热力图不自适应**:① `.gui-settings-row` 现以 CSS 规范强制首子元素(标签块)`flex:1; min-width:0` —— 此前裸 `<div>` 按长中文描述的 max-content 计宽,浏览器与 Computer Use 面板的长描述行把开关挤到了下一行;② 索引库两个开关与此前 MCP/信息状态条同款缺 knob 问题,已补;③ 使用统计活跃热力图从固定 11px 网格改为居中的自适应图(53 周列分享剩余宽度、上限 15px、单元格 aspect-square 跟随),月份/星期标签镜像列几何保持对齐。
  - EN: Fixed three settings-page layout issues: (1) `.gui-settings-row` now enforces via CSS that the first child (the label block) takes `flex:1; min-width:0` — bare label divs sized to long CJK descriptions' max-content and wrapped their toggles onto their own line in the 浏览器与 Computer Use panel; (2) the two 索引库 toggles had the same missing-knob bug as MCP/信息状态条, now fixed; (3) the usage heatmap went from a fixed 11px grid to a centered fluid graph (53 week columns sharing the free width, 15px cap, aspect-square cells) with month/weekday labels mirroring the column geometry.
- **修复分组 tab 的全部展开/收起无效**:按钮此前只切换自定义分组,「空间」自动项目文件夹块吃的是另一份折叠状态,文件夹块占满列表后按钮读作坏的。现按「任一收起 → 全部展开,全部展开 → 全部收起」同时驱动两套折叠。
  - EN: Fixed the groups-tab expand/collapse-all doing nothing visible: it only toggled the manual custom groups while the auto 空间 folder blocks read a separate collapsed set, so once folder blocks dominated the list the button read dead. It now drives both sets with the any-collapsed → expand-all, all-expanded → collapse-all ladder.
- **修复浮动滚动条跨界面残留**:滚动条闲时改为幽灵态后,容器被卸载(如进入设置)也没人收回它,会话列表的幽灵轨永久悬在设置界面上。现轨可见期间以 ~600ms 探活容器连通性,容器卸载或尺寸归零即完全收回;settle(140ms)与 idle(1s)检查同样处理,滚动后立刻切界面也不滞留。
  - EN: Fixed the floating scrollbar ghosting across view switches: with the idle ghost state, nothing retracted the rail when its container unmounted (e.g. entering settings), so a session-list ghost hung over the settings UI forever. A ~600ms liveness probe now runs while the rail is visible and fully retracts it the moment its container disconnects or collapses to zero size; the settle (140ms) and idle (1s) passes share the check, so switching views right after a scroll also retracts promptly.
- **修复打开旧会话会把它顶到列表最前**:侧栏排序键 `updated_at` 此前在每次快照持久化时都写当前墙钟,而打开历史会话必持久化两次(激活引导 + 闲置关闭),于是「看一眼」旧会话就把它重排到所在分组/文件夹顶部。现排序键改为快照内最后条目时间(真正的最后活动语义,openchamber `time.updated` 同款;bitfun 左栏同样以「点击不跳行」为设计约束),已有会话的排序在修复后首次持久化即自愈。
  - EN: Fixed opening an old session jumping it to the top of the list. The sidebar sort key `updated_at` was stamped with the persist wall-clock on every snapshot persist, and opening a history session always persists twice (activation bootstrap + the unconditional idle-close write), so merely viewing it re-ranked it to the top of its group/folder. The key is now the snapshot's last entry timestamp — true last-activity semantics, matching openchamber's `time.updated`; bitfun's nav list carries the same "no jump on click" constraint. Existing sessions self-heal on the first persist after the fix.
- **全局浮动滚动条闲时不再消失而是变浅,吃豆人轨修复拖拽**:滚动停止 ~1s 后进度条淡为 30% 透明度的幽灵轨而不是消失,拇指保持可抓取(悬停恢复全不透明),进度随时可以按住拖动;吃豆人皮肤此前完全没有拖拽处理,现与软糖皮肤共用同一拇指拖拽契约,12px 的 pac 字形外套 24px 透明命中环便于抓取。
  - EN: The global floating scrollbar no longer disappears when idle — it fades to a 30%-opacity ghost rail with the thumb still grabbable (hover restores full opacity), so progress can be dragged at any time. The pacman skin previously had no drag handling at all; both skins now share one thumb-drag contract, with a 24px transparent hit ring around the 12px pac glyph for easier grabbing.
- **修复 MCP 服务器与「信息状态条」开关渲染为空心胶囊**:两处 `gui-toggle` 按钮都缺 CSS 所定位的 `.gui-toggle-knob` 子元素,轨道里没有滑块,读作一个空的小圆角框。补上 knob 后与全应用开关同形。
  - EN: Fixed the MCP server toggles and the 外观 → 信息状态条 toggle rendering as empty pills — both `gui-toggle` buttons lacked the `.gui-toggle-knob` child the CSS positions, leaving a bare rounded track with no knob. With the knob back they match every other toggle in the app.
- **修复「信息状态条」开关不实时生效**:开关此前只写 localStorage,而 `storage` 事件同文档不触发,状态条要重载才出现/消失。现翻转派发 `musepi-statusbar-info-changed` 同文档事件(`SessionStatusBar` 监听),外观页效果预览也在开启时渲染带示例分段的状态条,所见即所得。
  - EN: Fixed the 信息状态条 switch not applying live: it wrote localStorage only, and the `storage` event does not fire same-document, so the bar only appeared after a reload. Flips now dispatch the same-document `musepi-statusbar-info-changed` event (listened to by `SessionStatusBar`), and the appearance effects preview renders the real status-bar strip with sample segments while on.
- **修复 arm Windows 上自更新必然失败**:资产名按 `process.platform`/`arch` 拼装,于是 Windows ARM64 会去要 `musepi-windows-arm64.exe` —— 而发布矩阵**从不产出**该资产(0.4.26 的 SHA256SUMS 只有 relay 扩展 + 六个二进制,Windows 侧仅 x64),自更新必然以 `has 0 assets named musepi-windows-arm64.exe` 结束。现 Windows 上 arm64 回落到已发布的 x64 资产(Windows 11 ARM 以 x64 仿真运行);命名抽成可测的 `releaseBinaryName()` 并补 3 条用例(ARM64 取 x64、其余目标逐个对齐、不支持的平台/架构仍报错)。要发原生 arm64 版属于发布链改动(需要 windows-11-arm 作业),届时可撤掉这条回落。
  - EN: Fixed self-update being guaranteed to fail on Windows ARM64. The asset name is built from `process.platform`/`arch`, so ARM Windows asked for `musepi-windows-arm64.exe` — an asset the release matrix NEVER produces (0.4.26's SHA256SUMS carries the relay extension plus six binaries; Windows is x64 only), so every self-update ended in `has 0 assets named musepi-windows-arm64.exe`. ARM Windows now falls back to the published x64 asset (Windows 11 emulates x64); the naming moved into a testable `releaseBinaryName()` with three cases (ARM64 takes x64, every other target matches its asset, unsupported platform/arch still throw). Shipping a native arm64 build is a release-pipeline change (a windows-11-arm job) that would supersede this fallback.
- **守护进程启动失败时报出它尝试的可执行文件路径**:子进程秒退时错误此前只有 `daemon exited during startup`,看不出用的是哪个候选(随包二进制 / 开发检出 / PATH),ARM 那台报「找不到可执行程序」时无从定位。现日志与错误都带上该路径 —— 解开 arm64 安装包核对后,包内 `vendor/daemon/musepi.exe` 存在且确为 ARM64,因此这条信息用于区分「文件被隔离/装坏」与「解析到了别的可执行文件」。
  - EN: The daemon-start failure now names the executable it tried. An instantly-exiting child only reported `daemon exited during startup`, leaving no way to tell which candidate was used (packaged binary / dev checkout / PATH) — which is exactly what the ARM Windows "can't find the executable" report needed. Log and error now carry the path; inspecting the arm64 installer showed `vendor/daemon/musepi.exe` is present and genuinely ARM64, so this output separates "quarantined/broken file" from "resolved some other executable".
- **修复 TUI swarm 面板在两个成员列表之间闪烁**:`task-swarm` 这个 widget key 是全局的,但成员数据属于**某一次** `task` 调用——事件里一直带着 `toolCallId`,处理器却从没用它,于是另一次调用(或某次已结束调用的迟到 update)会用自己那份成员表覆盖在跑的这份,面板逐帧在「0/3」与「0/4」之间来回刷(用户:加了子 agent 后两个列表同时在刷新)。现 widget 绑定启动它的那次调用,异号 update/end 一律忽略(控制器注释里写着「widget 应与 tool call 绑定」,此前从未落实)。
  - EN: Fixed the TUI swarm panel flickering between two member lists. The `task-swarm` widget key is global while the member data belongs to ONE `task` call — the events always carried `toolCallId` and the handlers never read it, so a second call (or a late update from one that had already ended) overwrote the live members and the panel alternated between `0/3` and `0/4` frame by frame (user: 加了子 agent 后两个列表同时在刷新). The widget now binds to the call that started it and ignores foreign update/end events — the controller's own comment claimed the widget was call-scoped, which was never enforced.
- **修复 TUI swarm 成员网格的列宽与对齐**:id 列宽此前按**成员数量**算(`String(count).length`,四个 agent 就是 1),于是按 3 列宽排出的格子装不下真实内容(`OnboardingWiring` 这类 id 有 16 列),每行都超出终端宽度、label 被压到最小宽只剩 `◉ Re…`。现 id 列取**最长成员 id** 的可见宽度,并按它补齐到同宽,同行的进度条与标签起止列一致(参考实现的做法:先按最宽适配、再补齐);并用「每行都不得超过终端宽度」锁住该契约。
  - EN: Fixed the TUI swarm member grid's column width and alignment. The id column was sized from the member COUNT (`String(count).length`, i.e. 1 for four agents), so cells laid out as if ids were three columns wide could not hold real content (`OnboardingWiring` is 16) — every row ran past the terminal and labels collapsed to the 5-column floor (`◉ Re…`). The id column now takes the longest member id's visible width and pads to it, so a row's bars and labels start and end at the same columns (reference-implementation rule: fit the widest, then pad); a "no row exceeds the terminal width" test pins the contract.
- **修复客户端 swarm 进度条看不见、右面板子 agent 行没有进度条**:guest 侧运行态的填充**没有背景色**(只有 ok/warn/err 有),于是运行中的成员画的是一块透明条 —— 看上去就是「没有进度条」;右面板的 per-agent 行更是完全没有条。现两侧共用同一条规则(TUI 的软渐近:`n/(n+2)`,daemon 不流总量就不编造百分比),运行中按工具调用数增长、未开工时才走不确定动画,右面板行也补上同一比例的条,并把填充的默认色接回应用强调色。
  - EN: Fixed the client's swarm progress bars being invisible and the right-panel subagent rows having none. The guest fill carried no background for the running phase (only ok/warn/err had one), so a live member painted a transparent block — indistinguishable from "no progress bar"; the status rows had no bar at all. Both now share one rule (the TUI's soft asymptote `n/(n+2)` — no invented percentage while the daemon streams no total): a running member grows with its tool calls, only a not-yet-working one shimmers, the status row shows the same fraction, and the fill's default colour is bridged to the app accent.
- **修复设置页被视图包装层撑爆(扩展控制中心右侧溢出窗口)**:`.gui-view-enter` 是 `.gui-shell` 里的 flex item,`min-width: auto` 把它的自动最小尺寸定成了**内容的最小内容宽度** —— 扩展中心的 provider 标签行一宽,整页就被推到 2430px(1568 窗口实测),详情栏与半个标签行都在窗口外,连左侧导航右移。现该包装层 `min-width: 0`:横向溢出交回给标签行自己的滚动容器(实测整页 2448 → 1568,塞满 6 个长标签也只横滑不撑页)。
  - EN: Fixed the settings page being blown out by its view wrapper (the extension center's right side falling outside the window). `.gui-view-enter` is a flex item in `.gui-shell`, so `min-width: auto` resolved its automatic minimum to its content's min-content width: one wide child (the extension center's provider tab strip) pushed the whole page to 2430px in a 1568px window, putting the detail pane and half the tab strip off-screen and shifting the left nav. The wrapper now takes `min-width: 0` and leaves horizontal overflow to the tab strip's own scroll container (measured 2448 → 1568, and six long tabs now scroll instead of widening the page).
- **修复排队「立即发送」反而最后送达**:引导队列每个注入边界只取**队首**一条,而 `session.queuedSend` 把点击的消息从队列取出后又追加到队尾 —— 前面已排队的消息逐个投递完才轮到它,面板上该行一直不消失,按钮读起来像没反应。现「立即发出」把该条插到 steer 队首,下一个注入边界就发出(跳过先前排队的消息);点击后面板立刻按 daemon 的权威快照重读,不再等 3s 轮询才反映真实位置。
  - EN: Fixed the queue panel's "send now" delivering the clicked message LAST. Steering takes ONE message from the queue HEAD per injection boundary, and `session.queuedSend` re-appended the popped entry to the tail — so it waited behind every message queued before it while its row stayed on the panel, reading as an inert button. "Send now" now promotes the entry to the head of the steer queue so the next injection boundary delivers it (ahead of previously queued messages), and the panel re-reads the daemon's authoritative snapshot right after the click instead of waiting for the 3s poll.

- **修复运行中看不到 advisor 提醒卡片**:提醒一直有落库、模型也照常收到,但**实时投影**把自定义消息拍成了普通 `message`(role `custom`),渲染端那条分支落到 `null` —— 于是运行中看不到,只有重载后才出现。现投影按持久化条目同一形状输出 `custom_message`,渲染端两条路径共用同一个自定义消息渲染器。
  - EN: Fixed advisor note cards being invisible while a run is in progress. The notes were persisted and the model received them, but the live projection flattened a custom message into a plain `message` (role `custom`), which the transcript's message branch rendered as `null` — visible only after a reload. The projection now emits the same `custom_message` shape persisted sessions store, and both renderer paths share one custom-message renderer.
- **修复扩展设置页内容溢出与整页滚动**:该页从未挂上既有的 fill 契约(`.gui-settings-content--fill`),且插件/市场两个并排面板没有任何布局规则 —— 实测分别撑到 5577px / 2019px,溢出到「槽位注册表」行以下、整页滚动。现同历史会话页一样挂 fill,并给两个面板与卡片区补上下界;占位文案不再因 `min-height: 300px` 而偏心,徽标也能收缩不再被裁。
  - EN: Fixed the extensions settings page overflowing and scrolling as a whole. The page never hosted the existing fill contract (`.gui-settings-content--fill`), and the plugins/marketplace panes had no layout rule at all — measured at 5577px and 2019px, spilling past the slot-registry row. It now hosts fill exactly like the history page, the two alternate panes and the item-card block are bounded, the placeholder no longer off-centres on a `min-height` floor, and the badges shrink instead of clipping.
- **修复右面板最大化不铺满 chat 圆角容器**:最大化此前是视口相对的浮层(`position: fixed` + 视口尺寸),完全无视会话卡片的 8px 内缩与圆角,会盖住会话列与部分侧栏。现按 `.gui-chat-surface` 的实测盒写入 `--pane-max-*`,并用 ResizeObserver 跟随窗口/侧栏/拖宽变化;圆角与底色取同一 token。
  - EN: Fixed the maximized right panel not filling the chat card. Maximize was a viewport-relative overlay (`position: fixed` with viewport sizes) that ignored the conversation card's 8px insets and radius, covering the chat column and part of the sidebar. The panel now takes `.gui-chat-surface`'s measured box through `--pane-max-*`, re-measured by a ResizeObserver so window, sidebar and rail changes stay flush; radius and paint use the same tokens.
- **修复地图视图遗留的「链段折叠」与无信息节点**:地图沿用了 transcript 的折叠语义(超过阈值就折成胶囊),并因此丢掉内容 —— 节点实测只有图标+省略号+时间。现地图端到端删除折叠(含 6 个只服务该胶囊的 i18n key),节点改为 208×76 内容卡:标题(角色/工具名)+ 两行摘要,悬停操作浮到卡片右上角。
  - EN: Fixed the map view's leftover chain folding and information-free nodes. The map carried the transcript's folding semantics (chains past a threshold collapsed into a capsule) and lost its content with it — nodes showed only an icon, an ellipsis and a timestamp. Folding is gone from the map end-to-end (including the six i18n keys that only served that capsule) and nodes are 208×76 content cards: a title (role/tool name) plus a two-line summary, with hover actions floating at the card's top-right.
- **修复输入框附件不随会话暂存**:附件此前只在内存里,切会话时要么被带进另一个会话、要么跨欢迎页往返后消失。现与文本草稿同一套 per-session 暂存(`musepi-gui-draft-attachments:<sessionId>`),发送/移除时清理,欢迎页组合器保持自己的空状态。
  - EN: Fixed composer attachments not being stashed per session. Attachments lived only in memory, so a session switch either leaked them into the incoming session or lost them across a welcome-scene round trip. They now ride the same per-session stash as the text draft (`musepi-gui-draft-attachments:<sessionId>`), cleared on send or removal, with the welcome composer keeping its own empty state.
- **修复长用户消息展开/收起没有动效(硬切违反全局动效规范)**:设置里开启「折叠较长的用户消息」后,超过 240 字的消息折成两行预览,但点击展开/收起是 `-webkit-line-clamp: 2` ⇄ `display: block` 的瞬时类名翻转 —— 全局动效标准要求高度变化走 220ms spring 的 `useCollapseHeight` morph,兄弟折叠体(思考块/Advisor 卡)都是这个语言,唯独用户消息硬切。现高度在预览高度与全文高度之间按同一条 spring 过渡:展开时 clamp 先摘除、从预览高度长到全文,收起时高度缩回预览、落定才套回 clamp;`useCollapseHeight` 中央 hook 相应扩展出 `closedHeight`(收起目标是实测预览高而非 0)与 `onSettled`(落定回调,用于套回 clamp)两个可选参数,既有调用点(思考块/TTSR/Advisor/连接页/工具卡)不受影响。
  - EN: Fixed long user messages expanding/collapsing without any motion — a hard snap that violated the global motion standard. With 「折叠较长的用户消息」 enabled, messages over 240 chars fold into a two-line preview, but toggling was an instant `-webkit-line-clamp: 2` ⇄ `display: block` class flip; the motion standard requires height changes to ride the 220ms spring `useCollapseHeight` morph, which the sibling collapsibles (thinking block / advisor cards) already use. The height now transitions between the preview height and the full body on the same spring: expanding removes the clamp first and grows from the preview into the full body, collapsing shrinks back into the preview and re-applies the clamp only at settle. The central `useCollapseHeight` hook gains two optional params — `closedHeight` (collapse to the measured preview height instead of 0) and `onSettled` (fires when the transition ends, used to re-apply the clamp); existing callers (thinking / TTSR / advisor / connect screen / tool cards) are unaffected.

## [0.4.27] - 2026-09-12

### Added

- **插件面板不再加载失败，官方插件也都有图标了**：插件自带的设置面板原先一律报「模块加载失败」——界面在组件还在下载时就回收了它的地址；现在加载完成后才释放。同时每个插件项都画出了自己的标识（插件声明的图标、内置单元的类别图标，或按包名生成的彩色字母标），状态改为标识角上的小圆点，不再和图标挤在一起。
  - EN: Plugin panels no longer fail to load, and official plugins have icons too. A plugin's own settings panel always reported "module failed to load" — the UI released its address while the download was still in flight; it now waits for the load to finish. Every plugin also draws its own mark (a declared icon, a built-in unit's kind glyph, or a colored letter tile derived from the package name), and status moved to a corner badge instead of crowding the icon.
- **插件详情弹窗补齐**：点击已安装插件打开详情弹窗——大图标、版本、状态与启用开关、头部卸载按钮；插件自带的组件收进弹窗「包含的组件」逐项开关（不再与插件包重复占列表行），插件的设置界面（如账号池管理）也挂载在弹窗内。
  - EN: The plugin detail dialog completes the picture. Clicking an installed plugin opens a dialog with the large icon, version, status, enable toggle and an uninstall button in the header; the plugin's own modules collapse into per-item included-component toggles (no longer duplicated as separate list rows), and its settings surface (an account pool, for example) mounts inside the dialog.
- **插件列表支持一键卸载**：已安装插件卡片和列表行新增卸载按钮，确认后由守护进程移除插件目录并即时刷新列表；卸载失败时错误横幅提示且列表保持不变。
  - EN: One-click uninstall in the plugin list. Installed plugin cards and rows gain an uninstall button; after a confirmation the daemon removes the plugin directory and refreshes the list immediately, and a failed uninstall shows an error banner while leaving the list untouched.
- **插件图标可自定义**：插件可在清单里声明 `icon`（emoji、字符或图片 URL），列表立即采用；未声明时按包名生成确定的彩色字母标，同包同名同色。
  - EN: Customizable plugin icons. A plugin can declare `icon` in its manifest (emoji, glyph, or an image URL) and the list adopts it; without one, a deterministic colored letter mark is derived from the package name — same package, same mark.
- **对话内联 widget 卡片新增「⋯」菜单:下载 / 复制 / 查看代码**:卡片源收在一处契约 `widgetSource`——`html` 面给自身标记(下载的 `.html` 可独立打开),其余类型给 `{type,title?,data}`(即 `widget` 工具的载荷,粘回对话即可让 agent 改卡);查看器、剪贴板与下载读同一份,不会漂移。「下载为图片」只在带光栅器的宿主(桌面端)出现,纯浏览器与 HTML 导出宿主不渲染该项而非点了没反应。
  - EN: The inline widget card gains a "⋯" menu — download, copy, view source. One source contract (`widgetSource`): an `html` face yields its own markup (the downloaded `.html` opens standalone), every other type yields the `{type,title?,data}` payload the `widget` tool was called with, so it pastes back into a prompt; viewer, clipboard and download read that one representation, so they cannot drift. "Download as image" appears only on hosts with a rasterizer (the desktop renderer); plain-browser and HTML-export hosts omit the item rather than offering a dead one.
- **右侧面板浏览器设备预设补上真实视口模拟**:三档预设此前只换 UA、模拟视口从未下发,1440 桌面档因此在面板里仍按面板宽度布局。现由面板上报 `{preset, viewport}`,主进程按档下发 `Emulation.setDeviceMetricsOverride` + UA + 触摸,自适应档清空覆盖(手机档实测 `mobile=true` + 布局 393,桌面档 1440)。
  - EN: Device presets in the right-panel browser now apply a real emulated viewport. They previously swapped the UA only — no viewport override was ever sent — so the 1440 desktop preset still laid out at the panel's own width. The pane reports `{preset, viewport}`; main sends `Emulation.setDeviceMetricsOverride` + UA + touch per preset and clears it for fit (measured: phone `mobile=true`, layout 393; desktop 1440).
- **agent 操作时闪出落点元素**:agent 通道按下鼠标时,主进程用 Blink 检查器覆盖层框出落点元素,900ms 后自动清除(下一次操作移动该框而非叠加);页面零注入,高亮失败也不影响 agent 的动作。
  - EN: Agent pointer presses now flash the element under the point: main outlines it with Blink's inspector overlay and clears it after 900ms (the next action moves the box rather than stacking). Nothing is injected into the page, and a failed highlight never fails the agent's action.

### Fixed

- **修复右侧面板浏览器页面只铺满顶部、下方一片空白(「高度不正常」)**:`<webview>` 上的内联 `display: block` 覆盖了 Electron 的 `:host { display: flex }`,而 guest 内部 iframe 的高度只来自那个 flex 上下文,于是退回替换元素的 150px 默认高。删掉覆盖后页面按面板满高布局,并跟随面板最大化与收起/展开。
  - EN: Fixed the panel browser painting only the top strip with blank space below. An inline `display: block` on `<webview>` overrode Electron's `:host { display: flex }`; the guest's inner iframe takes its height only from that flex context, so it fell back to the 150px replaced-element default. Without the override the page fills the panel and tracks panel maximize and collapse/reopen.
- **修复右侧面板的层级与命中缺陷(下拉菜单被网页盖住、左缘拖不动)**:面板 chrome 位于根级页面宿主之下,地址栏下拉与「更多操作」改走 `useFloatingMenu` portal;页面卡片留 6px 内缩让出拖宽手柄;指针落到网页 guest 后主渲染进程收不到 move,故按下边缘即挂起全窗手势护盾承接。
  - EN: Fixed the right pane's tier and hit-testing defects (menus buried under the page, an undraggable left edge). The pane's chrome sits below the root-level page host, so its dropdowns now render through `useFloatingMenu`; the page card keeps a 6px inset for the width handle; and since a pointer over the page guest stops delivering moves to the main frame, holding the edge raises a viewport-wide gesture shield.
- **修复折叠面板时 agent 操作不自动露出**:可见性此前按面板 rect 推断,而折叠只把宽度收到 0、面板仍挂载(槽位实测约 1px),主进程因此从不发 `agentActivity`,自动切视图与展开永不发生。现由面板直接上报自身 `open` 状态。
  - EN: Fixed agent activity never revealing a folded panel. Visibility was inferred from the pane's rect, but folding only collapses the width to 0 with the pane still mounted (the slot measures ~1px), so main never emitted `agentActivity` and the view switch + expand never happened. The pane now reports its own `open` state.
- **修复托管浏览器桥的两条静默失败路径**:任一 `managed-browser:*` 通道被重复注册会在 `start()` 内抛出,跳过 `startServer()`——整座桥不绑定、面板所有调用回 `unknown tab`,现 `start()`/`registerIpc()` 幂等;agent 高亮需要桥自己 debugger session 上的 `DOM` 域,渲染端在别处开启不带过来,此前每次都静默失败,现显式开启。
  - EN: Fixed two silent failure paths in the managed-browser bridge. A duplicated `managed-browser:*` registration throws inside `start()` and skips `startServer()`, leaving the bridge unbound and every pane call answering `unknown tab`; `start()`/`registerIpc()` are now idempotent. The agent highlight needs the `DOM` domain on the bridge's own debugger session — the renderer enabling it elsewhere does not carry over, which made it fail silently every time; now enabled explicitly.

## [0.4.26] - 2026-09-12

### Added

- **插件面板不再加载失败，官方插件也都有图标了**：插件自带的设置面板原先一律报「模块加载失败」——界面在组件还在下载时就回收了它的地址；现在加载完成后才释放。同时每个插件项都画出了自己的标识（插件声明的图标、内置单元的类别图标，或按包名生成的彩色字母标），状态改为标识角上的小圆点，不再和图标挤在一起。
  - EN: Plugin panels no longer fail to load, and official plugins have icons too. A plugin's own settings panel always reported "module failed to load" — the UI released its address while the download was still in flight; it now waits for the load to finish. Every plugin also draws its own mark (a declared icon, a built-in unit's kind glyph, or a colored letter tile derived from the package name), and status moved to a corner badge instead of crowding the icon.
- **插件详情弹窗补齐**：点击已安装插件打开详情弹窗——大图标、版本、状态与启用开关、头部卸载按钮；插件自带的组件收进弹窗「包含的组件」逐项开关（不再与插件包重复占列表行），插件的设置界面（如账号池管理）也挂载在弹窗内。
  - EN: The plugin detail dialog completes the picture. Clicking an installed plugin opens a dialog with the large icon, version, status, enable toggle and an uninstall button in the header; the plugin's own modules collapse into per-item included-component toggles (no longer duplicated as separate list rows), and its settings surface (an account pool, for example) mounts inside the dialog.
- **插件列表支持一键卸载**：已安装插件卡片和列表行新增卸载按钮，确认后由守护进程移除插件目录并即时刷新列表；卸载失败时错误横幅提示且列表保持不变。
  - EN: One-click uninstall in the plugin list. Installed plugin cards and rows gain an uninstall button; after a confirmation the daemon removes the plugin directory and refreshes the list immediately, and a failed uninstall shows an error banner while leaving the list untouched.
- **插件图标可自定义**：插件可在清单里声明 `icon`（emoji、字符或图片 URL），列表立即采用；未声明时按包名生成确定的彩色字母标，同包同名同色。
  - EN: Customizable plugin icons. A plugin can declare `icon` in its manifest (emoji, glyph, or an image URL) and the list adopts it; without one, a deterministic colored letter mark is derived from the package name — same package, same mark.
- **TUI `/pair` 命令:一条指令把手机端绑到这台机器**:绑定手机此前只能靠 `/collab lan`——它确实会出二维码,但语义是「分享会话」,找「怎么绑定 app」的人无从下手。新增 `/pair`:已在分享则直接出码,未分享则先起局域网分享再出码,并列出各网卡可达地址与「本机浏览器打开」提示;复用既有链接格式与终端 ANSI 二维码组件,手机端扫码即绑(或改用粘贴链接)。`/collab lan|tunnel` 的启动与展示逻辑同时抽成共享函数,两条命令不再各写一份。
  - EN: New TUI `/pair` command — one command to bind the mobile app to this machine. Pairing previously meant knowing that `/collab lan` was the QR path, which reads as session _sharing_, not device pairing. `/pair` shows the code immediately when a share is already running and starts the LAN share first when it is not, listing every reachable NIC address plus the host-machine browser link; it reuses the existing link format and the terminal ANSI QR component, so the phone scans and is bound (or pastes the link instead). The LAN/tunnel start-and-present logic is now shared with `/collab` rather than written twice.

### Fixed

- **修复侧面板内置浏览器空白(页面明明已加载却一片空白)**:两个独立缺陷指向同一表象。(1) **投影被当作过期丢弃**——渲染器的布局版本号是**模块级计数器,页面每次重载即归零**,而主进程的「过期版本」水位线**跨重载存活**(主进程不重载),于是重载后渲染器发出的每一次投影都低于水位线、被静默丢弃,原生视图永远拿不到 bounds 也永远不显示,面板只剩白色底;这正是「重载一下就空白」的成因,也解释了为何视图一直停在创建默认的 800×600。(2) **遮挡判定把「存在」当成「盖住」**——原生 `WebContentsView` 恒在 DOM 之上,所以有浮层盖住槽位时必须隐藏它;但旧判定只问「文档里是否存在可见的 dialog/menu/listbox」,从不管它是否真的盖住槽位,而转录区常驻的回合导航带(24px `role="listbox"`)就紧贴面板左侧几像素,于是任何有回合的会话都会永久隐藏视图。现渲染器给每次投影盖上**页面加载 epoch**,主进程见到新 epoch 即重置水位线;遮挡判定改为**三条件**(`lib/overlay-block`:在屏 + 矩形与槽位相交 + 在该点拥有最上层——只看几何仍会把「与槽位重叠但画在面板下面」的元素误判成遮挡),并带回归测试。
  - EN: Fixed the side panel's built-in browser sitting blank with the page fully loaded — two independent defects produce that one symptom. (1) **Projections were dropped as stale**: the renderer's layout counter is module-scoped and restarts at 0 on every page load, while the main process's "stale revision" watermark survives reloads (main is not reloaded). Every projection after a reload therefore compared below the watermark and was silently discarded, so the native view never received bounds and never appeared, leaving the pane on its white backing — which is why a reload reliably produced a blank pane, and why the view stayed at its 800×600 creation default. (2) **Occlusion treated "present" as "covering"**: the page is a `WebContentsView` that always paints above the DOM, so a real overlay over the slot must hide it — but the old check only asked whether _any_ visible dialog/menu/listbox existed in the document, never whether it covered the slot, and the transcript's permanently-mounted turn rail (24px `role="listbox"`) sits a few pixels left of the panel, so every session with turns hid the view for good. Each projection now carries a **page-load epoch** that lets main reset its watermark, and occlusion now requires all three of on screen, intersecting the slot's rect, and owning the paint order at a point inside that intersection — geometry alone still mistook an element that overlaps the slot but paints beneath the panel for an occluder (`lib/overlay-block`), with a regression test.
- **配对码在有效期内可无限复用**:配对码展示在屏幕上,而解析成功后服务端并未删码 —— 10 分钟窗口内同一串数字可反复换取会话链接(拍下屏幕即可长期使用)。现改为**单次有效**:解析成功即作废,TTL 只约束从未被使用的码。该规则抽成可测的 `collab/pair-codes.ts`(单次性/过期/清除共 7 个用例)。
  - EN: Fixed pair codes being replayable for their whole lifetime. The codes are shown on screen, and resolving one did not delete it — the same six digits kept trading for the session link for the full 10-minute window (photograph the screen and keep using it). Codes are now single-use: resolving spends them, and the TTL only bounds codes that were never used. The rule lives in a testable `collab/pair-codes.ts` (7 cases: single-use, expiry, clearing).
- **协作面板:配对码无有效期、拿不到只读链接、也看不出谁在看**:面板丢弃了服务端随码返回的有效期(既无倒计时,也看不出码已失效);只读链接虽由服务端一并下发却从未渲染,只读分享成了 TUI 独占能力;`collab.status` 不含参与者,面板无法区分「空转的分享」与「已有人连上」。现配对码显示倒计时与「单次有效」提示、失效后提示重新获取;新增「复制只读链接」;新增在线参与者列表并标注仅观看者。另修「刷新二维码」名不副实(它只是重取状态、并不重铸链接),现改名为「刷新」。
  - EN: Fixed the share panel showing no pair-code lifetime, offering no watch-only link, and giving no sign of who is connected. It discarded the expiry the daemon returns with each code (no countdown, no indication a code had died); the view-only link was delivered but never rendered, making read-only sharing a TUI-only capability; and `collab.status` carried no participants, so an idle share looked identical to one with a guest attached. The pair code now shows a countdown with a single-use hint and says to mint a new one once it expires; a "copy watch-only link" action is available; and connected participants are listed with watch-only guests marked. The misleading "refresh QR code" button (it only re-read status, never re-minted the link) is now simply "refresh".
- **修复历史消息与切换会话时也「逐字重播」**:揭示引擎把「挂载」当成「有积压」——揭示位置从 0 起算,于是历史消息、切换会话后重新挂载的实时消息都被整段重新打字,收尾的定速排水又把已读内容当成待揭示积压再走一遍。现挂载即定位到该块文本末尾(只有挂载**之后**新增的文本参与动画),`resetKey` 也只在键真正变化时重置(此前 effect 首次运行就重置,等于抵消挂载规则);预览循环仍可从 0 重播。
  - EN: Fixed committed messages and session switches re-typing themselves: the reveal engine treated mounting as backlog — the reveal position started at 0, so a history message (or a live one re-mounted by a session switch) was re-typed in full, and the settle drain then treated the already-read text as backlog and replayed it. Mounting now starts at the end of the block's text (only text that grows _after_ mount animates), and `resetKey` only restarts on a real key change (the effect's first run used to reset, which cancelled the mount rule); preview loops still replay from 0.
- **修复流式期间 markdown 不成形(头段现随流增长)**:此前只有**首帧**文本里最后一个 `\n\n` 平衡边界之前的块被解析成 markdown,该边界在冻结后永不再推进——一条长消息只有开头几段是 markdown,其余全程以纯文本滚动,直到流结束才整体成形(这正是「参考 dsh 后怎么没有生效」的实际形态)。现逐帧把新成立的平衡块提升进 markdown 头段:段落、列表、闭合的代码围栏在流式过程中即成形;最后一个平衡边界之后仍是原始文本——逐字 span 动效需要一个稳定的纯文本节点,「未闭合围栏保持字面量」也是同一原因。切点前移沿用 DOM 侧既有的「清空并重挂当前尾段」路径,已显示的字符不会被重新动画。
  - EN: Fixed markdown not taking shape while a message streams (the head now grows with the stream): only the blocks before the last balanced `\n\n` boundary of the _first_ frame were parsed as markdown, and that boundary never advanced once frozen — so a long message showed markdown for its opening paragraphs and plain text for everything after them until settle. Every block that becomes complete is now promoted into the markdown head per frame: paragraphs, lists, and a code fence the moment it closes take shape while streaming, while the region after the last balanced boundary stays raw text (the per-character span effects need a stable plain node, which is also why an unclosed fence stays literal). The advancing cut reuses the DOM pass's existing "clear and re-append the current tail" path, so already-shown characters are not re-animated.
- **修复右侧面板拖宽「拖完回弹 + 一路选中文字」**:释放时的吸附读的是 **pointerdown 那次 render 的闭包 `width`**,拖到哪儿松手都会吸回起点附近的档位;吸附又**没有距离阈值**,260–300 全落 300、1200 落 800,精确宽度根本不可达;拖拽期间既不加 `body.gui-resizing`(文本选择冻结与宽度过渡逃逸都挂在它上面——而右面板那条走 `startResize("right")` 的分支**全仓没有调用方**,是死代码),也没有 pointer capture / `preventDefault`,指针一离开 4px 手柄,拖拽就变成扫过转录区选文字。现改用仓内统一的 `usePointerDrag`(capture + 过阈值后 `preventDefault` + pointerId 过滤),起止宽度都走 ref(不再读闭包),吸附补 24px 半径(超出即保留精确值),拖拽期间加 `body.gui-resizing`,并删掉 `startResize` 的死分支。
  - EN: Fixed right-panel width dragging snapping back on release and selecting text along the way: the release-time snap read the `width` captured by the **pointerdown render's closure**, so a drag anywhere snapped to a point near where it began; the snap also had **no distance threshold** (260–300 all landed on 300, 1200 on 800), making an exact width unreachable; and the drag never added `body.gui-resizing` (the class that freezes text selection and escapes the pane's width transition — the right-panel path went through `startResize("right")`, a branch with **no caller anywhere in the repo**), nor used pointer capture / `preventDefault`, so once the pointer left the 4px handle the gesture became a text selection sweep across the transcript. It now uses the repo's `usePointerDrag` (capture + `preventDefault` past the threshold + pointerId filtering), keeps both endpoints in refs (no closure read), snaps only within 24px (otherwise the exact width wins), adds `body.gui-resizing` for the duration, and the dead `startResize` branch is gone.
- **托管浏览器改用 DOM 内的 `<webview>`(消除「原生视图恒在 DOM 之上」的一整类规避)**:右侧面板的页面此前是主进程的 `WebContentsView`,它永远画在 DOM 之上,于是浮层只能靠「让位」或「隐藏页面」凑合——4px 拖拽手柄被吃掉约 3px、rail 悬浮提示被页面盖住(还得再加一条「有提示就隐藏页面」的规避)、菜单与弹窗同理。现页面改为渲染端持有的 `<webview>`(OOPIF,参与 DOM 层叠):手柄、提示、菜单、弹窗全部按普通 z-index 正常显示,`lib/overlay-block.ts` 遮挡判定、4px inset 与整套投影/重投影机制一并删除。主进程保留不可替代的部分——分区权限拒绝与 `omp-file://` 预览、9230 上的 CDP 桥(agent 的 `browser.gui` 照旧用 `puppeteer.connect` 驱动**同一批页面**)、活动台账与风险导航同意门——并新增渲染端↔主进程的 guest 生命周期握手(`guest-ready`/`guest-gone`/`active-tab`/`visibility` ↔ `create-tab`/`select-tab`/`close-tab`,建标签带 15s 超时与明确失败回包)。已用隔离 harness(Electron 43 + 真实控制器 + 真实分区 + 真实桥)实测:`/json/version` 与 `Target.*`、`Page.navigate`、`Runtime.evaluate`、`Page.captureScreenshot` 全通,浮层在页面上方赢得命中测试,agent 建标签 266ms 完成绑定。两条实测约束已写进代码与文档:① `<webview>` 一旦在 DOM 中被移动就**销毁 guest**,故元素挂在常驻宿主、永不移动/永不卸载(标签切换只切 `opacity`);② `display:none`/移出视口/零尺寸下 `capturePage()` 永不返回,故隐藏只用 `opacity`(面板关闭时宿主仍保留合成表面,agent 仍可截图),截图另有 1.5s×2 有界重试。**语义变化**:渲染进程重载(dev 热重载、窗口重载)会销毁全部 guest(此前由主进程持有、不受重载影响),标签 URL 现持久化在 localStorage 以便恢复。
  - EN: The right-pane browser is now a DOM `<webview>`, removing a whole class of "native view always above the DOM" workarounds. The page used to be a main-process `WebContentsView` that permanently painted over the DOM, so overlays had to either yield or hide the page: the 4px width handle lost ~3px to it, the rail's tooltips were buried and needed a "hide the page while a tooltip shows" rule, and menus/popups had the same problem. The page is now a renderer-owned `<webview>` (OOPIF, part of DOM stacking): the handle, tooltips, menus and dialogs all layer normally, and `lib/overlay-block.ts`, the 4px inset and the whole projection/re-projection machinery are deleted. Main keeps what only it can own — the partition's deny-all permissions and `omp-file://` previews, the CDP bridge on 9230 (the agent's `browser.gui` still drives the SAME pages through `puppeteer.connect`), the activity ledger and the risky-navigation consent gate — plus a new guest lifecycle handshake (`guest-ready`/`guest-gone`/`active-tab`/`visibility` ↔ `create-tab`/`select-tab`/`close-tab`, tab creation with a 15s deadline and an explicit error reply). Verified with an isolated harness (Electron 43 + the real controller + the real partition + the real bridge): `/json/version` and `Target.*`, `Page.navigate`, `Runtime.evaluate` and `Page.captureScreenshot` all work, an overlay wins hit-testing above the page, and an agent-created tab binds in 266ms. Two measured constraints are documented in the code: (1) moving a `<webview>` in the DOM **destroys its guest**, so elements live in an always-mounted host and are never moved or unmounted (tab switching only toggles `opacity`); (2) `capturePage()` never settles while the guest is `display:none`, off-viewport or zero-sized, so hiding is `opacity`-only (a closed panel keeps the composited surface, so the agent can still screenshot) and capture retries once within a 1.5s cap. **Behaviour change**: a renderer reload (dev hot-reload, window reload) now destroys every guest (main used to own them, so reloads were invisible); tab URLs are persisted to localStorage so the tabs come back.
- **修复托管浏览器迁移后的四个实测问题**:① **空白标签的起始页点不动**——宿主隐藏(`opacity:0`)时外层虽是 `pointer-events:none`,但标签层写的是「激活即 auto」,而子级的 `auto` 会在 `none` 父级内部**重新开启命中测试**,透明 guest 于是继续吃掉本属于起始页的点击(现改为 `visible && active` 才 `auto`)。② **网页显示尺寸/位置不对**(看起来变大并被裁、点击坐标整体错位)——宿主的 rect 只在**尺寸**变化(ResizeObserver)与 window resize/scroll 时更新,而侧栏折叠、面板宽度拖动、视图切换这类**纯位移**不触发其中任何一个,宿主便停在旧 rect;现补 200ms 低频复测 + `transitionend`,并让 `setPaneRect` 对相同 rect 直接返回(避免每秒数次无谓重渲染)。③ **`ERR_ABORTED (-3)` 红条**——根因是 `<webview src>` 被绑到**会被导航更新的 state**:guest 报回新 URL → React 重设 `src` → guest 重载并顶掉在途加载(隔离 harness 实测:被顶掉的加载正是以 `ERR_ABORTED (-3) loading '<url>'` reject,与截图里的字串一致)。现 `src` 只在元素创建时给一次(`entrySrc` 契约 + 回归测试),后续导航只走 `loadURL`;同时主进程把「被顶掉」的 ERR_ABORTED 视作非失败(页面确实正在加载),不再冒红条。④ **轨迹/转录跳转把整个界面顶上去并被遮挡**——两处都用 `scrollIntoView({block:"start"})`,它会**连带滚动所有可滚动祖先**(整个壳上移),落点又贴着容器上沿;现改为只滚自己的滚动容器、按容器坐标算落点并留 12px 顶部内缩。
  - EN: Fixed four issues found in the `<webview>` migration. (1) **The blank tab's start page was unclickable**: with the host hidden (`opacity: 0`) the outer layer is `pointer-events: none`, but the per-tab layer said "auto when active" — a child's `auto` **re-enables hit-testing inside a `none` parent**, so the transparent guest kept eating the clicks meant for the start page (now `auto` needs `visible && active`). (2) **The page rendered at the wrong size/position** (looked oversized, clipped, and clicks landed off-target): the host's rect was only re-measured on **size** changes (ResizeObserver) plus window resize/scroll, while position-only shifts (sidebar collapse, panel drag, view switching) trigger none of them, leaving the host on a stale rect — it now re-measures on a 200ms interval (and `transitionend`), and `setPaneRect` returns early for an unchanged rect so the interval costs no re-renders. (3) **The `ERR_ABORTED (-3)` banner**: `<webview src>` was bound to state that navigation itself updates, so the guest's reported URL re-pointed the element, reloading it and aborting the in-flight load (the isolated harness reproduces exactly the `ERR_ABORTED (-3) loading '<url>'` string from the report). `src` is now set once at element creation (`entrySrc` contract + regression test) and later navigations only use `loadURL`; main also treats an aborted-by-supersede load as "still loading" instead of an error. (4) **Trajectory/transcript jumps pushed the whole shell up and left the target half-hidden**: both used `scrollIntoView({ block: "start" })`, which scrolls **every** scrollable ancestor, and landed the row flush against the scroller's edge — each now scrolls only its own scroller, computed in that scroller's coordinates with a 12px top inset.
- **托管浏览器补齐参考实现(openchamber)的三处行为**:① **视口预设此前形同虚设**——预设只用 `max-width` 约束槽位,flex 一收缩就退回面板宽度,预设比面板宽时既不缩放也不裁切(「网页显示尺寸不正常」的另一半成因)。现按 openchamber `fitViewport` 的做法:页面**按预设宽度布局**(响应式检查才有意义),再整体按 `scale = min(1, 可用宽/预设宽)` 缩放居中(`lib/browser-viewport.ts` 纯函数 + 4 用例,缩放只向下不向上);② **最大化面板时页面被埋**——宿主 `z-index: 5` 低于最大化面板的 850,现提到 900(高于面板、低于应用的弹层与提示);③ **真实加载失败没有任何提示**——`did-fail-load` 只把 loading 置 false。现补错误面:仅主帧失败才报、忽略 `ERR_ABORTED (-3)`(导航被顶掉不算失败),错误描述 + 失败 URL 显示在既有错误行,成功加载后自动清除。
  - EN: Three behaviours brought in line with the reference implementation. (1) **Viewport presets did nothing**: a preset only set `max-width` on the slot, so flex shrank it back to the panel width — a preset wider than the panel was neither scaled nor cropped (the other half of "the page renders at the wrong size"). Following openchamber's `fitViewport`, the page now **lays out at the preset width** (which is what makes a responsive check meaningful) and is scaled to fit with `scale = min(1, available / preset)`, centred — a pure helper (`lib/browser-viewport.ts`) with four cases, and it never scales up. (2) **A maximized panel buried the page**: the host sat at `z-index: 5`, below the maximized panel's 850 — it is now 900 (above the panel, below the app's dialogs and tooltips). (3) **A real load failure said nothing**: `did-fail-load` only cleared the loading flag. There is now an error face — main-frame failures only, `ERR_ABORTED (-3)` ignored (a superseded navigation is not a failure), with the description and failing URL in the existing error row, cleared by the next successful load.
- **右面板拖宽不再压扁主视图 + 输入框底栏窄宽度不再重叠**:① 面板宽度此前只钳 260–1200、与窗口无关,拖到 1200 可把会话列挤成一字一行、组合器无处安放。现按窗口算预算:拖拽起点量出「面板右缘 − 会话列左缘 − 会话列最小宽(420px)」作为上限(这两条边在拖拽期间固定,所以上限稳定),窗口 resize 时重新钳制(持久化宽度也一并收进预算),吸附点同样受上限约束;判定抽成纯函数 `lib/panel-width.ts`(4 用例)。② 输入框底栏(`.gui-composer-row`)是 `space-between` 且**不换行**,两侧按钮又都是固定 26px、不可收缩,窄宽度下直接压在一起;现改为可换行(`flex-wrap` + `row-gap`),右组补 `min-width: 0` 让 chip 截断而非撑宽,并以 `margin-left: auto` 保证换行后仍靠右。
  - EN: The right-panel drag no longer squeezes the main view, and the composer's action row no longer overlaps at narrow widths. (1) The panel width was clamped only to [260, 1200] regardless of the window, so dragging it to 1200 could squeeze the chat column into one word per line with the composer nowhere to go. The budget is now derived from the window: at drag start it measures `panel right edge − chat column left edge − MIN_CHAT_WIDTH (420px)` (both edges are fixed during the drag, so the cap is stable), window resizes re-clamp the width — including the persisted one — and the snap points are clamped by the same budget. The predicate is a pure helper (`lib/panel-width.ts`, 4 cases). (2) The composer footer (`.gui-composer-row`) used `space-between` without wrapping while both button groups hold fixed 26px items that cannot shrink, so at narrow widths they simply collided; it now wraps (`flex-wrap` + `row-gap`), the right group gained `min-width: 0` so chips truncate instead of forcing the row wider, and `margin-left: auto` keeps it right-aligned once wrapped.
- **Git 状态卡:分支菜单只能开不能关 + 工作区更改折叠无动效**:分支按钮的处理函数只调 `setBranchOpen(true)`,而 `useFloatingMenu` 的文档 mousedown 监听**刻意跳过落在锚点上的按下**(它假定调用方自己切换),于是菜单无法从打开它的那个按钮关掉——其它消费者(欢迎页 composer、附件菜单)写的都是切换。工作区更改的文件列表是裸 `{cond && …}` 挂载,展开/收起是硬切;现改用条件区块动效规范 `Reveal`(240ms 高度 + 160ms 外层淡入)。
  - EN: Fixed the git status card's branch menu being open-only and its workspace-changes disclosure having no animation. The branch trigger called `setBranchOpen(true)` unconditionally, while `useFloatingMenu`'s document mousedown listener **deliberately skips presses that land on the anchor** (it expects the caller's own click to toggle) — so the menu could not be dismissed from the button that opened it, unlike every other consumer (welcome composer, attach menu), which toggle. The workspace-changes file list was a bare `{cond && …}` mount, so expanding and collapsing was a hard cut; it now uses the conditional-block animation standard `Reveal` (240ms height + 160ms outer fade).

## [0.4.25] - 2026-09-12

### Fixed

- **修复会话地图折叠:展开后卡片互相叠放 + 跳转静默失效**:折叠段展开时,布局只把节点从「隐藏集」里放出来,却没让它们参与纵向排布——段内 20 多张卡片全部落在同一个坐标(`段首 y + CHAIN_FOLD_H`),且下方节点仍占用它们「本该不占」的空间。折叠布局现按展开态计算(`layoutTree` 新增 `expandedFolds` 参数,组件把它并入 memo 依赖):展开段的节点各自获得独立行位、卡片间距不小于卡片高度,其下节点被正确推开,画布高度随之增长。另修两处与「折叠」直接相关的缺陷:地图的 Esc 处理未认领按键,于是「按 Esc 关掉聚焦卡」会连带把正在跑的回合打断(现仅在卡片开着时监听并 `preventDefault`);搜索条上下两个匹配导航按钮的 `aria-label` 都写着「清除筛选」,读屏软件会把「下一个匹配」念成「清除筛选」(现各自独立)。
  - EN: Fixed the session map's folding (expanded cards stacked on top of each other): expanding a segment only released its nodes from the hidden set — the layout never gave them vertical space, so 20-odd cards all landed on one coordinate (`head.y + CHAIN_FOLD_H`) while the nodes below kept occupying the space the hidden ones were supposed to not use. The layout is now a function of the expansion state (`layoutTree` takes `expandedFolds`, and the component folds it into the memo deps): nodes of an expanded segment get their own non-overlapping rows, following nodes are pushed down, and the canvas grows accordingly. Two further defects in the same feature: the map's Escape handler did not claim the key, so closing the focus card with Esc also aborted a running turn (it now listens only while the card is open and calls `preventDefault`), and the search bar's two match-navigation buttons both announced "clear filter" (screen readers read "next match" as "clear filter"; each now has its own label).
- **修复流式输出的收尾瞬移 + 每帧全量重分词**:`streaming` 变 false 时揭示引擎把整段剩余文本一次性显示——流式过程中平滑,最后一段却整体跳出。现改为**固定速率收尾**(`nextDrainPosition`,刻意不用按积压成比例追赶的那套:后者会在约 8 帧内吃光任意长度的剩余文本,仍是瞬移),尾段保持用户已经在看的阅读节奏。同时把渲染期的取前缀改走**已记忆化**的 `BlockUnitCounter.slice`(流式文本只追加、揭示位置单调前进,因此只需重新分词新增的后缀)——此前每帧调用无状态的 `sliceGraphemes`,把整个前缀重新分词一次。
  - EN: Fixed the streaming tail popping in one frame plus a full re-segmentation per frame: when `streaming` flipped false the reveal engine showed the entire remaining text at once — smooth while streaming, then the whole tail appeared in one go. It now **drains at a fixed rate** (`nextDrainPosition`, deliberately not the proportional catch-up step, which would eat any backlog in ~8 frames and is still a jump), so the tail keeps the reading cadence the user was already watching. The render-time prefix is also taken through the **memoized** `BlockUnitCounter.slice` (streaming text only grows by appending and the reveal target advances monotonically, so only the appended suffix is re-segmented) — it previously called the stateless `sliceGraphemes` every frame, re-segmenting the entire prefix.
- **修复消息按钮「点了没反应」(撤回/编辑/重试全部失效)**:三个按钮都走 `session.branchAt`,而该 RPC 有两处无保护解引用,任何一次点击都在返回前抛错——(1) 匹配目标消息时对 `type === "message"` 但无 `message` 字段的记录直接读 `.role`(SDK 文件混有记账记录);(2) 分支成功后用 `messageKey()` 给落点叶子取键,而叶子可以是 `thinking_level_change` / `model_change` 这类非消息记录,`messageKey` 读 `.message` 即崩。两者都被 GUI 的空 `catch` 吞掉,于是表现成「按钮是死的」:不移动、不回填、无任何提示。现补齐空值守卫,叶子键改为向上取最近的消息祖先(与流事件 rekey 同一套解析),并让历史会话按需激活(此前 `branchAt` 要求会话已 live,而 `session.abort`/`collab.start` 都会自动激活)。另修编辑回填的两个缺口:daemon 在 `navigateTree` 走 no-op 提前返回(叶子已在目标处——正是「撤回后再编辑」的状态)时用目标消息原文兜底 `editorText`;Composer 的去重守卫由「按值比较」改为「请求归空即重置」,否则同一条消息的第二次编辑会被永久判为已处理。同时把失败从静默改为可见提示(共享 toast 横幅,含 daemon 的原因),不再让用户面对一个毫无反馈的按钮。
  - EN: Fixed the message action buttons doing nothing (撤回 / 编辑 / 重试 all dead): every one of them goes through `session.branchAt`, which threw before returning on two unguarded reads — (1) matching the target message dereferenced `.role` on records that are `type: "message"` but carry no payload (the SDK file mixes in bookkeeping records), and (2) after a successful branch, `messageKey()` keyed the landing leaf, but that leaf can be a non-message record such as `thinking_level_change` / `model_change`, and `messageKey` reads `.message`. The GUI's empty `catch` swallowed both, so the buttons read as dead: nothing moved, nothing backfilled, no feedback. This adds the null guards, resolves the leaf key by walking up to the nearest message ancestor (the same resolution the stream-event rekey uses), and activates history sessions on demand (branchAt required an already-live session, while `session.abort`/`collab.start` auto-activate). Two edit-backfill gaps are fixed alongside: the daemon now falls back to the target message's own text for `editorText` when `navigateTree` takes its no-op early return (the leaf is already at the target — exactly the state 撤回 leaves behind), and the Composer's dedupe guard resets when the request clears instead of comparing by value, which made a second 编辑 of the same message a permanent no-op. Failures are also reported through the shared toast banner (with the daemon's reason) instead of vanishing, so a button can never fail silently again.
- **修复任意 Esc 都会中断正在运行的回合(空 Esc 误报「已中断」)**:窗口级 Esc 绑定无任何门控——菜单/弹层虽各自处理 Esc 却未认领该按键(未 `preventDefault`)、按键连发、以及原本属于输入框(会话搜索、文件重命名、浏览器地址栏)的 Esc,都会穿透到该绑定并触发 `session.abort`,回合以「Interrupted by user」收场,而用户根本没打算停止。现新增 `lib/escape-stop.ts`:仅当「无人认领(`defaultPrevented` 为假)+ 无 Esc 归属面(模态/菜单/非输入框字段)+ 非连发 + 确有回合在跑」时才中断;同时给所有自行处理 Esc 的面(浮动菜单、Composer 弹层、消息树、命令面板、选择工具条、AskCard、DialogFrame 模态)补上认领语义。另修复 `session.abort` 与协作中断此前传的 `"user interrupt"` 字面量与 `USER_INTERRUPT_LABEL`(`"Interrupted by user"`)不相等的问题——`abort()` 是严格比较,导致 GUI 停止从未打上 UserInterrupt 标记:转录显示原始 reason 而非中断卡,且顾问自动续跑未被抑制(用户明确停止后顾问仍可能重启回合)。
  - EN: Fixed any Escape keypress interrupting a running turn (a stray Escape reported as "Interrupted by user"): the window-level Escape binding had no gating, so an Escape that a menu/popover handled without claiming the key, a key repeat, or an Escape belonging to a field (session search, file rename, browser address bar) all reached it and called `session.abort` — ending the turn as "Interrupted by user" for a keypress the user never aimed at the turn. A new `lib/escape-stop.ts` interrupts only when nothing claimed the key (`defaultPrevented` false), no surface owns it (dialog/menu/listbox, or a non-composer field), it is not a repeat, and a turn is actually running; every surface that handles Escape itself (floating menus, Composer popovers, message tree, command palette, selection toolbar, AskCard, the always-mounted modal frame) now claims it. Also fixed `session.abort` and the collab interrupt threading the literal `"user interrupt"` while `abort()` compares against `USER_INTERRUPT_LABEL` (`"Interrupted by user"`) by strict equality — the GUI stop path never set the UserInterrupt flag, so the transcript showed the raw reason instead of the interrupt card and advisor auto-resume was not suppressed (the advisor could restart a turn the user had deliberately stopped).
